#CH 101
[ZH] 还完仪王的人情，四人在桥头瓦外分别。
[EN] Having repaid her debt to Prince Yi, the four parted ways outside Qiaotou Wa.
[ZH] 乔文仙带着仪王与佳和郡主直接回乔府享用乞巧宴，千里寻自然要赶回朱府。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian took Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe straight back to Qiao Manor for the Qiqiao Banquet, while Qianli Xun hurried back to Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 马车上，千里寻一直研究苦荞……
[EN] Inside the carriage, Qianli Xun studied Kuqiao without blinking...
[ZH] 苦荞被小姐盯得不好意思，老实招道：“小姐别这么看着我，我是喜欢白风，今日也没想着瞒您。”
[EN] Flushing under her mistress's stare, Kuqiao confessed at once: "Don't look at me like that, Miss. I do like Bai Feng, and I wasn't trying to hide it from you today."
[ZH] “什么时候的事儿？”
[EN] "When did this start?"
[ZH] “上次去西三胡同，他堵住我的马车，说了好多话，还送给我一个泥人，我才答应。”
[EN] "The last time I went to West Third Alley, he blocked my carriage, talked my ear off, and gave me a clay figurine. That's when I agreed."
[ZH] “一个泥人就哄得你答应，还是不是我的丫鬟？你想要泥人我可以给你买好多。”
[EN] "One clay figurine won you over? Are you even my maid? If you want clay figurines, I can buy you piles of them."
[ZH] “不是泥人的事，我喜欢白风，况且他还是仪王身边的侍卫，前途无量。就目前来看，对我还不错。”
[EN] "It wasn't about the figurine! I like Bai Feng, and besides, he's Prince Yi's guard with a bright future ahead. From what I've seen so far, he treats me pretty well."
[ZH] 千里寻终于轻松地笑道：“这还差不多，像我身边的丫鬟，没有被爱情冲昏头脑。”
[EN] Qianli Xun let out an easy laugh at last. "Now that sounds more like my maid—didn't let love go straight to your head."
[ZH] 苦荞大胆说：“我怎么觉得小姐您像是被爱情冲昏头脑的人。”
[EN] Kuqiao grew bold: "Funny, Miss, you seem like the one who let love go to her head."
[ZH] “你竟然敢说你家小姐我，看我不挠你！”
[EN] "You dare talk back to your own mistress? Watch me tickle you!"
[ZH] 千里寻挠得苦荞咯咯直笑，不停地喊：“小姐！饶了我吧！饶了我吧！”
[EN] Qianli Xun tickled Kuqiao until she burst into giggles, shrieking over and over, "Miss! Mercy! Spare me!"
[ZH] 正挠着，瞧到夏果在一旁努力挤出笑容，迎合她们，千里寻停下手：“你怎么了？看上去不太高兴？”
[EN] Mid-tickle, Qianli Xun caught sight of Xiaguo forcing a smile beside them to play along. She paused. "What's wrong with you? You look out of sorts."
[ZH] “小姐与苦荞姐姐都有心上人，可我……”
[EN] "Both Miss and Sister Kuqiao have someone you love, while I..."
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“你不是也有吗？说出来听听。”
[EN] Qianli Xun laughed. "Didn't you say you have someone too? Tell me who!"
[ZH] 夏果哪里敢说，隐瞒道：“当时为拒绝南双随口编的理由，小姐不要当真。”
[EN] How could Xiaguo dare admit it? She covered it up: "I just made that up on the spot to turn Nan Shuang down. Don't take it to heart, Miss."
[ZH] “原来如此，你也不用羡慕我们，你长得漂亮，再好好修炼内在，等时机成熟，我定会帮你找个好人家。”
[EN] "I see. Well, no need to envy us. You're pretty—just work on cultivating your inner self, and when the time is right, I'll make sure to find you a fine match."
[ZH] 夏果好奇地问：“小姐，内在怎么修炼？”
[EN] Xiaguo looked curious. "Miss, how do you cultivate your inner self?"
[ZH] “内在嘛，一是学识修养，二是品德。你的字已认得差不多，没事翻翻我屋子里的书，腹有诗书气自华，时间久了，内在的学识修养自然养成，至于品德，记住四个字‘正思正念’，不管处理任何事，都要想到这四个字。”
[EN] "Your inner self comes down to two things: learning and character. You already know most characters, so flip through the books in my room whenever you're free. As the saying goes, a mind steeped in poetry and books gives a person natural grace. Over time, cultivation follows. As for character, remember four words: Right Thought, Right Mindfulness. Keep them in mind in everything you do."
[ZH] 夏果似乎听明白，点点头：“我记下了。”
[EN] Xiaguo seemed to grasp it and nodded. "I'll remember."
[ZH] “我再让苦荞教你学做茶，有机会我会帮你挑个好人家。”
[EN] "I'll also have Kuqiao teach you how to whisk tea. Once an opening comes up, I'll pick out a good family for you."
[ZH] 夏果急了：“小姐不用帮我挑好人家，我只跟着您，您嫁到乔家，我就跟到乔家。”
[EN] Panic struck Xiaguo. "Miss doesn't need to pick a family for me! I only want to follow you. When you marry into the Qiao family, I'll follow you right there."
[ZH] 苦荞批评道：“夏果，你到底哪句是真？哪句是假？刚还说羡慕我跟小姐有心上人，小姐要给你挑好人家你又不愿意，怎么回事？”
[EN] Kuqiao scolded her: "Xiaguo, which word out of your mouth is true and which is false? One second ago you envied Miss and me for having sweethearts, and the next you refuse when Miss offers to find you a good match. What's going on?"
[ZH] 千里寻帮夏果解释：“还不是想跟你一样找个自己喜欢的。不过，夏果，你可以自己找，但必须过我这一关，我要说不行，你们两个都不许嫁，听到没？包括白风！”
[EN] Qianli Xun jumped in to explain for her: "She just wants to find someone she likes on her own, same as you. Still, Xiaguo, you can look on your own, but he has to pass my inspection first. If I say no, neither of you is allowed to marry—hear me? That goes for Bai Feng too!"
[ZH] 苦荞强调道：“白风好着呢，小姐不用担心。”
[EN] Kuqiao protested, "Bai Feng is a great guy. Miss doesn't need to worry."
[ZH] “你有没有听过有其主必有其仆？仪王是什么样的人我比你清楚，你还是多个心眼儿，没把白风认清之前，不准拉手，不准……”千里寻说不出口，“反正就是不能有太亲密的举动，否则到时后悔都来不及。”
[EN] "Ever heard 'like master, like servant'? I know what kind of man Prince Yi is better than you do. Stay sharp! Until you know Bai Feng inside and out, no holding hands, no..." Qianli Xun bit off the rest. "Anyway, nothing too intimate, or you'll live to regret it."
[ZH] “啊！小姐要求这么多，可我跟白风都已拉手，怎么办？”
[EN] "Ah! So many rules, Miss—what if Bai Feng and I already held hands?"
[ZH] “算了，拉手我同意，其他的一概不许做，听到没？”
[EN] "Fine, I'll allow holding hands, and nothing else—hear me?"
[ZH] “听到了，小姐放心。”
[EN] "Loud and clear, Miss. Don't worry."
[ZH] 千里寻管理千里拳庄，自然而然养成为弟子操心的习惯，也将这习惯带到宋朝，不由自主为两个贴身丫鬟未来的感情生活操心起来。
[EN] Running Qianli Fist Manor had given Qianli Xun the habit of fussing over her disciples, and she had brought that habit straight into the Song dynasty, worrying over her two personal maids' love lives without even trying.
[ZH] 回到朱府后，她们直奔乞巧宴。
[EN] Back at Zhu Manor, they headed straight for the Qiqiao Banquet.
[ZH] 下人们都已回来，朱府又热闹起来，到处张灯结彩，尤其是摆宴席处，更是亮堂，朱府所有人都聚集在此处。
[EN] The servants had all returned, filling Zhu Manor with lively cheer once more. Lanterns and silk festoons glowed everywhere, brightest of all where the banquet tables stood, and the entire household had gathered there.
[ZH] 千里寻赶到时，乞巧宴已摆上，大家还未坐在席间。
[EN] By the time Qianli Xun arrived, the Qiqiao Banquet stood laid out, though no one had taken their seats yet.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷第一个看到姑娘，上前拉住她：“姑娘终于回来，就等你。”然后向屋内喊道：“老爷，夫人，姑娘回来了！”
[EN] Matron Wei spotted her first and hurried over to take her hand. "At last you're back, Young Lady—everyone was waiting on you." Turning toward the hall, she called out, "Master, Madam, the Young Lady is back!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜、向夫人、朱景元和陈文心从屋里出来，大家按照次序在焚香桌案前站好。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan, Madam Xiang, Zhu Jingyuan, and Chen Wenxin stepped out of the hall, lining up in order before the incense table.
[ZH] 有下人将三炷香点燃，送到朱晞颜手中。他手捏三炷香，对月三拜，其他人也跟着拜，然后将三炷香一一插入香炉中。
[EN] A servant lit three sticks of incense and handed them to Zhu Xiyan. Holding them, he bowed three times to the moon while the rest followed suit, then planted the three sticks one by one into the censer.
[ZH] 拜完后，大家才落座，享用乞巧宴。
[EN] Only after the offering did everyone take their seats to enjoy the Qiqiao Banquet.
[ZH] 主人拜完，丫鬟们又拜，接着是男仆。
[EN] Once the masters finished bowing, the maids offered their prayers, followed by the male servants.
[ZH] 拜完后，丫鬟们开始拿起针线对月穿针引线，苦荞和夏果也参与其中，玩得不亦乐乎。
[EN] Afterward, the maids picked up needles and thread to try threading them by moonlight. Kuqiao and Xiaguo joined in, laughing and having the time of their lives.
[ZH] 男仆则在一个供着泥像的桌上，摆放贡品，也将笔墨纸砚摆上，一个丫鬟抱着莱宝，跪下，帮助莱宝磕头礼拜，口称：“朱家大孙子莱宝乞聪明！”连称三下。
[EN] Meanwhile, the male servants placed offerings and the Four Treasures of the Study on a table holding a clay statue. A maid cradling Laibao knelt down and helped him kowtow, chanting three times: "Eldest grandson of the Zhu house, Laibao, prays for wit and wisdom!"
[ZH] 朱家众人听了，无不笑得合不拢嘴。丫鬟帮莱宝祈求完毕，把他送到陈文心怀里。接着男仆们也在供桌前跪拜祈求聪明。
[EN] Everyone in the Zhu family burst into wide smiles. After praying for him, the maid handed Laibao back into Chen Wenxin's arms, and the male servants took their turns kneeling before the altar to pray for cleverness.
[ZH] 千里寻瞅那泥像，问身旁的韦嬷嬷：“泥像塑的是哪位？”
[EN] Eyeing the clay statue, Qianli Xun asked Matron Wei beside her, "Who is that statue modeled after?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷回道：“塑的是牛郎，男子跪拜可乞聪明。”
[EN] Matron Wei answered, "That's the Cowherd. When boys and men kneel to him, they pray for intelligence."
[ZH] 千里寻边享用乞巧宴，边想，古人真是智慧，这么普通的节日都能赋予积极的含义。哪像穿越之前的时代，好好的一个“牛郎”称谓，却包含了其他含义，真是世风日下。从这个角度来讲，历史到底是倒退还是进步？估计没有人能讲得清楚。
[EN] Eating her way through the Qiqiao Banquet, Qianli Xun marveled at the wisdom of the ancients, who gave even an ordinary festival such an uplifting meaning. Not like the era she had crossed from, where a fine title like "Cowherd" (niulang) had taken on a sleazy meaning—male escort! What a decline in morals. Looked at from that angle, was history moving forward or backward? Nobody could say for sure.
[ZH] 就像人的生命，也许最健康的应是像莱宝那样的状态。等长大了，看似懂得很多，其实内心却变得复杂，最终在复杂中走向死亡。长大看似进步，其实也是一种倒退。
[EN] Just like human life itself—maybe the healthiest state was being like little Laibao. Growing up looked like gaining wisdom, yet hearts grew tangled and complicated until people marched toward death in that tangle. Growing up looked like progress, yet it was a step backward too.
[ZH] 千里寻发现，经历一个七夕节，她都快变成哲学家，竟然思考起历史和人生的问题。
[EN] Qianli Xun realized one Qixi Festival was turning her into a philosopher, brooding over history and the meaning of life.
[ZH] 传来苦荞高兴的声音：“哇！我捉住一只蜘蛛！”
[EN] Kuqiao's thrilled voice rang out: "Whoa! I caught a spider!"
[ZH] 丫鬟们都围了上去，绿桃拿来一只木盒：“我找了半天都没找到，你真幸运，把木盒送你，明日结的网必须让我看，也让我讨个彩头！”
[EN] The maids crowded around, and Lutao brought over a small wooden box. "I hunted forever and couldn't find one—you're so lucky! Take this box, and tomorrow you have to let me see the web it spins so I can borrow some of your good luck!"
[ZH] 苦荞接过木盒：“谢谢绿桃姐姐，明日打开后，让你第一个瞧。”
[EN] Kuqiao took the box. "Thanks, Sister Lutao. Tomorrow I'll let you peek first when I open it."
[ZH] 主人们都为苦荞感到高兴，连朱晞颜也满面笑容。一方面是因过七夕节，不同往日，若还板个脸，不吉利；另一方面，女儿求仪王出面，将夏果之事顺利解决，说明仪王对朱家不错，有仪王做靠山，临安知府的职位稳如泰山。
[EN] The masters shared in Kuqiao's cheer; even Zhu Xiyan wore a broad smile. For one thing, keeping a stern face on Qixi Festival brought bad luck. For another, his daughter had asked Prince Yi to step in and settle Xiaguo's trouble, proving Prince Yi looked kindly on the Zhu family—with Prince Yi as his backing, his seat as Prefect of Lin'an stood solid as Mount Tai.
[ZH] 最最重要的是他听说乔家有意到朱家提亲，跟皇亲国戚攀上亲家，实在是意外之喜。他甚至后悔，早知道乔家公子对淑真有情，当时就不该一时心热把淑真嫁给林家。还好一切都朝着好的方向发展，朱晞颜不由与儿子朱景元多喝了几杯。
[EN] Best of all, he had heard the Qiao family meant to propose marriage to the Zhu family. Becoming in-laws with imperial relatives was a windfall beyond his dreams. He even regretted marrying Shuzhen off to the Lin family in a rush—had he known Young Master Qiao had feelings for her, he would never have done it. Thank goodness everything was turning out right. In high spirits, Zhu Xiyan drank several extra cups with his son, Zhu Jingyuan.
[ZH] 七夕节晚上，下人们闹到很晚才睡，千里寻则早早睡去。
[EN] On Qixi night, the servants stayed up reveling late, while Qianli Xun turned in early.
[ZH] 半夜时，她做了一个梦，梦中有一男子背对着她，以为是乔文仙，大喊一声：“文渊！”
[EN] In the middle of the night, she had a dream. A man stood with his back to her. Mistaking him for Qiao Wenxian, she shouted, "Wenyuan!"
[ZH] 男子转过身时，才看清是申简辰。她跑上前抓住他：“申简辰，你去了哪里？是不是也穿越而来？”
[EN] When the man turned around, it was Shen Jianchen. She ran up and grabbed him. "Shen Jianchen, where have you been? Did you cross over too?"
[ZH] 申简辰埋怨道：“你为什么不来找我？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen reproached her: "Why didn't you come look for me?"
[ZH] “我不知道该去哪里找？”
[EN] "Where was I supposed to look?"
[ZH] “你有了喜欢的人，是不是已把我忘了？”
[EN] "You have someone you love now—have you forgotten me already?"
[ZH] “我是有了喜欢的人，可我并没忘记你。”
[EN] "I do have someone I love, but I haven't forgotten you."
[ZH] “那你说，你到底喜欢他，还是喜欢我？”
[EN] "Then tell me: do you like him, or do you like me?"
[ZH] 千里寻放开他的手：“我从来没答应过你，还是那句话，除非猪能上树，老娘才会答应嫁给你！”
[EN] Qianli Xun let go of his hand. "I never said yes to you! Same answer as always: I'll marry you when pigs climb trees!"
[ZH] 申简辰消失不见，却出现仪王的身影，用调戏的目光看着她：“幽栖居士，本王喜欢你！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen vanished, replaced by Prince Yi staring at her with a teasing gleam: "Youqi Jushi, I love you!"
[ZH] 千里寻一拳打去，这一拳将她惊醒，睁开眼睛时，原来她的手臂伸在空中。
[EN] Qianli Xun swung a punch straight at him—and jolted herself awake, eyes flying open to find her arm thrust straight into the air.
[ZH] 天还未亮，外面漆黑一片，千里寻收回手臂，翻了个身，继续入睡，可怎么也睡不着，想想刚才的梦境，觉得十分可笑。
[EN] Dawn had not broken yet, and pitch darkness filled the yard outside. Qianli Xun lowered her arm, rolled over, and tried to fall back asleep, yet sleep refused to come. Thinking back on that absurd dream, she could not help laughing.
[ZH] 因梦到申简辰，不由想念他。他到底在哪里？什么时候才能与他相遇？如果遇到，他知道自己有了文渊，会不会难过？
[EN] Dreaming of Shen Jianchen left her missing him. Where on earth was he? When would she ever cross paths with him again? And if they met and he found out about Wenyuan, would it break his heart?
[ZH] 又转念一想，也许申简辰在另一个地方已有新的生活，早将她忘记，她又何必杞人忧天想那么多，还是好好睡觉。
[EN] Then again, maybe Shen Jianchen had already built a new life somewhere else and forgotten all about her. Why borrow trouble worrying over nothing? Better to get some sleep.

#CH 102
[ZH] 天刚刚放亮，朱府的下人已起床。银杏和绿桃带着几个丫鬟来到小院儿，大家围着苦荞，等待她打开木盒，都想见识一晚上蜘蛛网结得如何。
[EN] At the first hint of dawn, the servants of Zhu Manor were up and about. Yinxing and Lutao led several maids into the small courtyard, crowding around Kuqiao as they waited for her to open the wooden box, eager to see how the spider had spun its web overnight.
[ZH] 夏果毫无兴致，小姐还没起来，她无事可做，回到丫鬟房中懒懒地坐着，想她与乔公子之事。
[EN] Xiaguo had no interest at all. With her mistress still asleep and nothing to do, she sat idling in the maids' quarters, daydreaming about Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 当丫鬟们打开木盒，看到蛛网又大又圆时，一个个十分高兴，笑声将千里寻吵醒。
[EN] When the maids lifted the lid and saw a big, round web inside, cheers erupted, and their laughter woke Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 她睁开眼，发现天已亮。昨晚因为梦醒，搞得失眠，也不知后来是怎么睡着的。
[EN] She opened her eyes to daylight. Waking from that dream last night had left her tossing and turning; she had no idea when she had drifted back off.
[ZH] 丫鬟们快乐的笑声感染到千里寻，她爬起床，准备迎接美好的一天。她也想瞧瞧蛛网，讨个吉利。
[EN] Infected by the maids' bright laughter, Qianli Xun climbed out of bed ready to welcome a fine day. She wanted a look at the spiderweb too, for good luck.
[ZH] 千里寻身着睡裙，下了床，打开房门。
[EN] Still in her nightgown, she stepped out of bed and opened the door.
[ZH] 丫鬟们听到门响，都停止笑声，拜见自家姑娘。
[EN] At the sound of the door, the maids hushed their laughter and bowed to their mistress.
[ZH] 千里寻说：“你们继续，别管我，等你们看完，也把木盒拿给我瞧瞧。”
[EN] Qianli Xun said, "Carry on, don't mind me. Once you're done looking, bring the box over so I can see too."
[ZH] “我们已看完，该去忙，就不在此打扰小姐。”绿桃笑着说完，与银杏带其他丫鬟离开。
[EN] "We've already seen it and need to get to work, so we won't disturb Miss." With a smile, Lutao led Yinxing and the other maids away.
[ZH] 院子里安静下来。
[EN] Quiet returned to the courtyard.
[ZH] 苦荞手拿木盒，走近小姐：“小姐进屋看，已经入秋，早晨冷，您小心着凉。”
[EN] Carrying the wooden box, Kuqiao stepped up to her mistress. "Look at it indoors, Miss. Autumn is here and the mornings are chilly—don't catch cold."
[ZH] 千里寻从苦荞手里拿过木盒，走到屋内，打开，果然在木盒的犄角旮旯里出现一个结了一晚上的蛛网，按照木盒的大小衡量，算是又大又圆。
[EN] Qianli Xun took the box from Kuqiao, stepped inside, and opened it. Sure enough, tucked in the corner sat a web spun overnight—big and round for the size of the box.
[ZH] “你们可真会玩儿，连蜘蛛网都能玩出花样。”
[EN] "You girls sure know how to have fun, making a game out of spiderwebs."
[ZH] 苦荞边收拾床铺边说：“小姐，您仔细瞧，蛛网结得又密又细又圆，看过的人都会得巧。”
[EN] Making the bed as she spoke, Kuqiao said, "Take a close look, Miss. The web is tight, fine, and round—anyone who sees it gains skill and cleverness."
[ZH] 千里寻又仔细瞧一遍，的确如此，她把木盒放在一边：“这么说来我也得巧，可我根本不会穿针引线，更别说绣花之类。”
[EN] Qianli Xun looked again; it really was. Setting the box aside, she said, "So I get skill too? Too bad I can't thread a needle to save my life, let alone embroider."
[ZH] “每个人得巧不同，小姐是在写诗作词方面。”
[EN] "Everyone's skill is different. For Miss, it's writing poetry and ci."
[ZH] “说得有道理。”
[EN] "Fair point."
[ZH] 夏果将洗脸水端进来，听到二人聊天儿，把洗脸盆在凳子上放好，手中捧着毛巾，站在一旁。
[EN] Xiaguo carried in a basin of washing water, overheard their chat, set the basin on a stool, and stood ready with a towel in her hands.
[ZH] “小姐天生心灵手巧，加上后天学习，才能写出好词好诗，跟蛛网没什么关系，我在乡下时见过更大更圆的蛛网，也没见得到哪方面的巧，苦荞姐姐千万不要把普通的蛛网夸大其词。”
[EN] "Miss was born clever and honed it through study—that's why she writes great poetry and ci. It has nothing to do with spiderwebs. Back in the countryside I saw webs twice as big and round, and nobody gained a lick of skill from them. Don't blow an ordinary spiderweb out of proportion, Sister Kuqiao."
[ZH] 苦荞边帮小姐换衣裳，边说：“你这样说多没意思，都是老祖宗留下的习俗。”
[EN] Helping her mistress change, Kuqiao retorted, "What a killjoy you are! It's a custom handed down by our ancestors."
[ZH] 夏果继续说：“这习俗一点儿意义都没有，还搞得蜘蛛不得安宁，最好以后能被大家忘记。”
[EN] Xiaguo pressed on: "That custom makes zero sense and doesn't even leave the poor spider in peace. It'd be better if everyone forgot it."
[ZH] 换好衣裳，夏果伺候小姐开始洗脸。
[EN] Once dressed, Qianli Xun let Xiaguo help her wash her face.
[ZH] 千里寻边洗边说：“习俗能传下来，自有其道理，哪一日消失也有它的道理。习俗在时，该玩就玩，习俗没了也不必纠结，人生啊，顺其自然。”
[EN] As she washed, Qianli Xun said, "When a custom survives, it has its reason; if it fades away one day, that has its reason too. Enjoy it while it's here, and don't fret if it's gone. Go with the flow in life."
[ZH] 千里寻擦完脸，把毛巾放在夏果手中，坐到梳妆台前，两个丫鬟又为她梳妆打扮。
[EN] Drying her face, she handed the towel back to Xiaguo and sat down at the vanity while both maids styled her hair and makeup.
[ZH] 千里寻一席话搞得两个丫鬟不好再斗嘴。
[EN] Her remarks left the two maids unable to bicker any further.
[ZH] 为小姐装扮完，伺候她用过早饭。当小姐在书桌前忙碌时，苦荞做茶，夏果在一旁学习。
[EN] Once dressed and fed, Qianli Xun settled at her desk to work while Kuqiao whisked tea and Xiaguo watched to learn.
[ZH] 苦荞做茶时，只要夏果在，她都会坐在旁边仔细观察，今日她想亲自动手：“苦荞姐姐，待会儿我帮你做一盏？”
[EN] Whenever Kuqiao made tea, Xiaguo sat beside her studying every step. Today she wanted to try her own hand: "Sister Kuqiao, let me whisk a bowl for you in a moment?"
[ZH] “那敢情好，我整日为小姐做茶，从未喝过别人为我做的茶，今儿我也享受享受。”
[EN] "Sounds wonderful! I make tea for Miss all day and never get a bowl made for me. I'll treat myself today."
[ZH] 苦荞做好茶后，给小姐端到桌前放好。夏果才坐到茶桌前做起茶来，她按照记下的步骤，一点一点做，倒也像模像样。
[EN] After serving her mistress's tea at the desk, Kuqiao yielded the tea table to Xiaguo. Following the steps she had memorized one by one, Xiaguo turned out a respectable bowl.
[ZH] 做好一盏，苦荞尝后，夸道：“比我第一次做得好。”
[EN] Kuqiao took a sip and praised her: "Better than my very first try."
[ZH] 夏果很开心，第一次做茶得到夸赞，以后再练练，岂不是可以超越苦荞？
[EN] Xiaguo beamed. Getting praised on her very first bowl meant that with a bit more practice, she could surpass Kuqiao.
[ZH] 她高兴之余向小姐讨要一本书，拿到丫鬟房仔细阅读，虽然不是很懂，但她读得很认真，无非是希望未来能配得上乔公子。
[EN] Thrilled, she asked her mistress for a book to read in the maids' quarters. Even though much of it went over her head, she pored over every line, driven by one wish: to be worthy of Young Master Qiao someday.
[ZH] 千里寻趁夏果不在，将刚刚写好的书稿让苦荞送到西三胡同。苦荞收好书稿，套了马车，往西三胡同而去。
[EN] While Xiaguo was out of the room, Qianli Xun handed her latest finished manuscript to Kuqiao to deliver to West Third Alley. Kuqiao tucked it away, hitched a carriage, and set off.
[ZH] 千里寻闲来无事，正准备找嫂嫂陈文心聊天儿，却见韦嬷嬷只身前来。
[EN] With nothing pressing to do, Qianli Xun was about to go chat with her sister-in-law Chen Wenxin when Matron Wei arrived alone.
[ZH] “姑娘这是要去哪里？”
[EN] "Where are you headed, Young Lady?"
[ZH] “准备去嫂嫂院子里坐坐。”
[EN] "Thought I'd go sit with Sister-in-law in her courtyard."
[ZH] “待会儿再去，我受夫人所托，来跟姑娘确认个事儿。”
[EN] "Go in a bit. Madam sent me to confirm something with you."
[ZH] “什么事？嬷嬷屋里说话。”
[EN] "What is it? Come inside and talk, Matron."
[ZH] 两人在屋里坐好后，韦嬷嬷不见有丫鬟在身旁，问道：“苦荞和夏果呢？怎么连个人影儿都没有？”
[EN] Once seated inside, Matron Wei saw no maids around and asked, "Where are Kuqiao and Xiaguo? Not a soul in sight?"
[ZH] “苦荞出去帮我办些事，夏果在她屋里读书，是我允许的。”
[EN] "I sent Kuqiao out on an errand, and Xiaguo is reading in her room—I gave her permission."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷责备道：“不是我说姑娘，你再善良，也不该纵得丫鬟忘记自己的身份，夏果这丫头本来就不安分，你还让她读书，往后还不得拿自己当主子？”
[EN] Matron Wei chided her: "I don't mean to lecture you, Young Lady, but kind as you are, you shouldn't spoil maids until they forget their place. That girl Xiaguo is restless enough as it is; let her read books and soon she'll fancy herself a mistress!"
[ZH] “嬷嬷严重，我只不过让她多读些书，往后好帮她找个好人家。”
[EN] "You're making too much of it, Matron. I just want her to read a little so I can find her a good match later."
[ZH] “姑娘还是别为她费心，这丫头心高着呢？练兵官她都看不上，指不定会看上谁。算了，我们不说她，还是说说正事。姑娘对我说实话，是不是乔家打算到朱府提亲？”
[EN] "Don't waste your energy on her. That girl aims sky-high—she turned her nose up at a Military Training Officer, so Heaven knows who she's set her sights on. Never mind her; let's talk real business. Tell me the truth, Young Lady: is the Qiao family planning to come propose marriage at Zhu Manor?"
[ZH] 昨日文渊才告诉她此事，没想到韦嬷嬷这么快知道。
[EN] Wenyuan had only told her yesterday—how had Matron Wei caught wind of it so fast?
[ZH] “嬷嬷如何得知？”
[EN] "How did you hear about that, Matron?"
[ZH] “看来是有此事。”韦嬷嬷笑道，“昨日你出去逛街，我跟夫人也没闲着，受邀到通判家参加秦夫人举办的七巧茶宴。茶宴间，有人说乔家本来正为儿子选亲，突然停了，让媒婆把各家姑娘的生辰八字还回去，说是乔家儿媳的人选已定。”
[EN] "So it's true!" Matron Wei burst into a grin. "While you were out strolling yesterday, Madam and I didn't sit idle either—we went to the Vice-Prefect's residence for Madam Qin's Qixi Tea Banquet. Over tea, someone mentioned the Qiao family had been picking a bride for their son and halted out of nowhere, telling the matchmaker to return all the young ladies' birth horoscopes because they had already settled on their future daughter-in-law."
[ZH] 千里寻窃喜，乔家选亲虽比不上皇子选妃，可在临安也是件大事，花落谁家除了与家世有关，还得靠运气，没想到她千里寻竟然成为有运气之人。
[EN] Secret glee bubbled in Qianli Xun's chest. Even if the Qiao family's bride selection could not rival a prince choosing a consort, it was huge news in Lin'an, depending on both pedigree and luck—and she, Qianli Xun, had turned out to be the lucky one.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷继续说：“后来，提举家的娘子悄悄给夫人说，她听帮乔家办事的媒婆讲，乔家准备到朱家提亲，夫人听后不敢相信，昨晚又不好当众问你，拜托我今日过来问问。”
[EN] Matron Wei went on: "Later, Intendant An's wife whispered to Madam that she heard from the Qiao family's matchmaker that they plan to propose to the Zhu family. Madam couldn't believe her ears, and couldn't ask you in front of everyone last night, so she sent me over today."
[ZH] 此事没有必要隐瞒家人，千里寻便将昨日文渊说的话一字不落告诉韦嬷嬷。
[EN] Seeing no need to hide it from her family, Qianli Xun repeated every word Wenyuan had told her yesterday.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷拍着膝盖，直笑：“呵呵……！要我说，乔家也忒急了些，姑娘刚和离，等半年后再提亲才刚刚合适，呵呵……！也是我家姑娘优秀，乔家怕被人抢先，才急着将婚事定下来！呵呵……！”
[EN] Matron Wei slapped her knee, roaring with laughter. "Haha! If you ask me, the Qiao family is in quite a rush! You only went through your mutual divorce a short while ago—waiting half a year would be just right, haha! Just goes to show how outstanding our Young Lady is; the Qiao family is afraid someone else will snatch you up first! Haha!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的表现，让千里寻认识到，原来一两个月的时间并不算长，更不好意思表明希望乔家明日就来提亲。
[EN] Matron Wei's reaction made Qianli Xun realize a month or two was not long at all, and she felt far too shy to admit she wished the Qiao family would come tomorrow.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷又聊了一会儿，也没见夏果出现，走时叮嘱姑娘，不能太纵容下人。
[EN] Matron Wei chatted a while longer without seeing hide or hair of Xiaguo, and before leaving reminded her mistress once more not to pamper the servants.
[ZH] 千里寻猛然想起昨日与仪王之约，差点儿把这么重要的事情忘记，还好早早想起，快到午饭时间，没法再找嫂嫂聊天儿，还是改日再说。
[EN] Qianli Xun snapped to attention—she had almost forgotten her appointment with Prince Yi from yesterday! Good thing she remembered in time. With lunch approaching, visiting her sister-in-law would have to wait for another day.
[ZH] 绿桃等丫鬟按时为小姐把午饭摆上，夏果才从丫鬟房出来，伺候小姐吃饭。
[EN] Only when Lutao and the other maids set out lunch on schedule did Xiaguo emerge from the maids' quarters to wait on her mistress.
[ZH] 她站在小姐身旁，看着小姐用饭，心里很是悲戚，为何自己生来是伺候人的命？而小姐生来就是被人伺候的命？她觉得上天实在不公。
[EN] Standing beside her mistress as she ate, bitterness welled up in Xiaguo's chest. Why was she born to serve while her mistress was born to be served? Heaven was so unfair.
[ZH] 苦荞送完手稿回来，刚进屋，夏果瞧见，不满道：“苦荞姐姐就知道出去玩儿，玩儿到现在才回来，你难道不知小姐需要人伺候？”
[EN] Just then Kuqiao walked in from delivering the manuscript. Seeing her, Xiaguo snapped, "All you do is run off and play, Sister Kuqiao, only strolling back now! Don't you know Miss needs waiting on?"
[ZH] 苦荞解释道：“是小姐派我出去办事，我哪像某些人，拿本书待到丫鬟房里躲着不出来，搞得自己跟小姐似的。”
[EN] Kuqiao shot back, "Miss sent me on an errand! Unlike certain people who grab a book and hide out in the maids' quarters acting like a noble lady."
[ZH] “你！”夏果感到委屈，哭道，“我是得到小姐的同意才看书，你又不是不知道，大不了从明儿起，我不看了！呜呜……！”
[EN] "You!" Stung, Xiaguo burst into tears. "Miss gave me permission to read, and you know it! Fine, starting tomorrow I won't read a single page! Waaah!"
[ZH] 这顿饭吃得不安宁，丫鬟吵起架来比男弟子还麻烦，打也不能打，骂也不能骂，千里寻说：“今儿是怎么了？都吃枪药了？我看你们两个天生不合，要不……夏果，你还是先回望月轩待着。”
[EN] No peace even at lunch! Quarreling maids were twice the headache of male martial-arts disciples—she could neither hit them nor scold them harsh. Qianli Xun set down her chopsticks. "What's gotten into you two today—swallowed gunpowder? Looks like you two just can't get along. Tell you what, Xiaguo, why don't you go stay back at Wangyue Pavilion for now."
[ZH] 夏果“扑通”跪下：“小姐，您别赶我走，我以后再也不跟苦荞姐姐吵架，安心伺候小姐。”
[EN] With a thud, Xiaguo dropped to her knees. "Please don't send me away, Miss! I'll never fight with Sister Kuqiao again—I'll wait on you with all my heart!"
[ZH] 苦荞也说：“小姐，我保证再也不跟夏果斗嘴。”
[EN] Kuqiao chimed in: "Miss, I promise I won't bicker with Xiaguo anymore either."
[ZH] “这就对嘛，你们两个乖乖的，和睦相处。”
[EN] "That's more like it. Behave yourselves and get along."
[ZH] 千里寻不再理会二人，继续吃午饭。
[EN] Ignoring them both, Qianli Xun went back to eating.
[ZH] 两个丫鬟立刻变乖，一个夹菜，一个添汤。
[EN] The two maids turned docile on the spot, one serving dishes and the other ladling soup.
[ZH] 时间差不多时，千里寻打算出发到神柳下赴仪王之约。她只让苦荞陪着，吩咐夏果留下看院子，顺便多读些书。
[EN] When the time drew near, Qianli Xun prepared to head for the Divine Willow to meet Prince Yi. She took only Kuqiao, telling Xiaguo to stay behind, mind the courtyard, and get some more reading done.
[ZH] 夏果问小姐是不是去见乔公子，得到小姐否认后，她才安心待在朱府小院儿，继续读那些她不是很懂的书。
[EN] Only after asking whether her mistress was going to meet Young Master Qiao and hearing a firm no did Xiaguo settle back in the courtyard, returning to the books she did not quite understand.

#CH 103
[ZH] 当千里寻顺着山下的路来到神柳跟前时，她不由产生愧疚感。
[EN] Walking down the hillside path toward the Divine Willow, Qianli Xun felt a pang of guilt.
[ZH] 神柳离幽林别居不远，她来到此处，不为与文渊幽会，竟然偷偷跑来赴仪王之约。文渊一旦知道，肯定会不高兴。
[EN] The Divine Willow stood not far from Youlin Retreat. Yet here she was, not sneaking out for a tryst with Wenyuan, but meeting Prince Yi in secret. If Wenyuan ever found out, he was sure to be upset.
[ZH] 还有佳和郡主，千里寻已在心中把她当成闺中密友。佳和郡主对仪王那么痴情，如果知道仪王主动约她来此，不知会难过成什么样？
[EN] And then there was Princess Jiahe, whom Qianli Xun already regarded as a close confidante. Devoted as the princess was to Prince Yi, how heartbroken would she be if she knew he had invited Qianli Xun out here?
[ZH] 神柳上的红绸已消失不见，没有了红绸的神柳，更容易令她想起穿越前在神柳下与申简辰对打的场面。
[EN] The red silk ribbons were gone from the willow. Bare of ribbons, the tree reminded her all the more of her brawl with Shen Jianchen beneath it before crossing over.
[ZH] 想到申简辰，再瞧瞧站在神柳下的背影，千里寻感到恍惚，要不是仪王身着古装，还有站在他身旁的白风，她绝对会误认为仪王就是申简辰。
[EN] Thinking of Shen Jianchen while gazing at the figure standing with his back to her under the willow left Qianli Xun in a trance. If not for the Song robes and Bai Feng standing beside him, she would have sworn Prince Yi was Shen Jianchen.
[ZH] 白风比仪王显得更积极，行礼道：“幽栖居士，我能带苦荞去玩儿吗？”
[EN] Bai Feng proved far livelier than his master, stepping forward with a bow. "Youqi Jushi, may I take Kuqiao off to play?"
[ZH] 千里寻将白风上下打量一番：“你要是敢辜负苦荞，我饶不了你！”
[EN] Qianli Xun looked Bai Feng up and down. "If you ever dare let Kuqiao down, I won't spare you!"
[ZH] “您放一百个心，我对苦荞好着呢，您得答应我，以后不能把苦荞许给别人。”
[EN] "Put your mind at ease—I treat Kuqiao like gold! Just promise me you won't marry her off to anyone else."
[ZH] 千里寻警告道：“我没答应你娶苦荞前，不准拉她的手，不准有更亲密的举动，听到没？要是被我知道，我才不管你的主人是谁，该打照样打！”
[EN] Qianli Xun warned him, "Until I agree to let you marry Kuqiao, no holding hands and nothing closer—hear me? If word reaches my ears, I don't care who your master is, I'll still beat you up!"
[ZH] “我怕您了！”白风吐吐舌头，对苦荞使使眼色，两人一前一后走到池塘边，坐在石头上聊天儿。
[EN] "Mercy, I surrender!" Bai Feng stuck out his tongue and winked at Kuqiao, and the two headed off to sit on the stones by the pond and chat.
[ZH] 白风跟千里寻说话时，申简辰已转过身，一直深情地注视着他喜欢的人，希望从日渐熟悉的外表中看出以前那个有趣的灵魂。
[EN] While Bai Feng spoke, Shen Jianchen had already turned around, drinking in the sight of the woman he loved, searching that familiar face for glimpses of the lively soul he knew.
[ZH] 仪王不说话，只顾用眼神挑逗，千里寻很不满：“仪王又来了，今日没其他人在，我得好好说说您，我有喜欢的人，您也有喜欢的人，为何老这么盯着我？您不觉得自己很不道德吗？”
[EN] Seeing Prince Yi stay silent and just flirt with his eyes, Qianli Xun frowned. "Here you go again, Prince Yi. Nobody else is around today, so let me give you a piece of my mind. I have someone I love, and you have someone too—why do you keep staring at me like that? Don't you think it's shameless?"
[ZH] “你竟如此看我？”
[EN] "Is that how you see me?"
[ZH] 千里寻躲开仪王的眼神：“我这么看您，再正常不过，这叫吃着碗里的，瞧着锅里，实在贪心。今儿既然您约我，我不妨把话挑明，您帮我，我感激，但一码归一码，总不能因为你帮过我，让我舍弃文渊喜欢上您？我这人，感情专一，一生只爱一人，也只嫁一人。”
[EN] Qianli Xun dodged his gaze. "Of course I do! Eating from your own bowl while eyeing the pot—that's pure greed. Since you asked me here today, let's put our cards on the table. I'm grateful for your help, sure, but one thing is separate from another. You can't expect me to ditch Wenyuan for you just because you did me a favor. I'm a one-man woman: I love one person for life and marry one person for life."
[ZH] 千里寻的话句句戳在申简辰的心坎儿里，刚想说出实情，又将话咽回去。
[EN] Every word struck straight at Shen Jianchen's heart. The truth rose to his lips, only for him to swallow it back down.
[ZH] 沉默一会儿，才说道：“幽栖居士对我误解太深，我也是感情专一之人，只可惜爱我的人我不喜欢，我爱的人却要嫁给别人，还是我一手促成，你能理解我的痛苦吗？”
[EN] After a silence, he said, "Youqi Jushi misjudges me. I'm devoted to one person too. The pity is that I don't love the one who loves me, while the one I love is about to marry another man—and by my own hand. Can you understand that pain?"
[ZH] 千里寻实在佩服仪王，三言两语将自己说成可怜虫。
[EN] Qianli Xun had to hand it to him: in two sentences he painted himself as the tragic victim.
[ZH] “仪王约我来，难道想告诉我您是感情专一之人？呵呵……，拿这话哄佳和郡主她应该会相信，可我，火眼金睛，一眼看穿仪王是个花心之人。”
[EN] "Did Prince Yi summon me here just to claim you're a faithful man? Haha, spin that tale to Princess Jiahe and she might buy it, but my sharp eyes see right through you—you're a fickle playboy."
[ZH] 申简辰靠近千里寻：“幽栖居士敢不敢与我对视？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stepped closer. "Does Youqi Jushi dare look me in the eye?"
[ZH] “有什么敢不敢？我才懒得盯着您看……”千里寻向后退去。
[EN] "What's there to dare? I just can't be bothered staring at you..." Qianli Xun backed away.
[ZH] 申简辰故意说：“你该不会是喜欢上我，心虚了，不敢承认？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen baited her: "Don't tell me you've fallen for me and feel too guilty to admit it?"
[ZH] “老娘会喜欢你？实在可笑，对视就对视，你还能把老娘吃了不成？”千里寻大胆接触仪王的目光，她感到全身像触电一般，为何与文渊在一起从未有过这样的感觉？
[EN] "Me, fall for you? Hilarious! Look you in the eye? Fine—what are you going to do, eat me?" Qianli Xun locked eyes with Prince Yi—and a jolt like an electric shock shot through her whole body. Why had she never felt anything like this with Wenyuan?
[ZH] “千里寻，你愿不愿意跟我在一起？”申简辰终于借助赵朴又一次表达心声。
[EN] "Qianli Xun, will you be with me?" Speaking through Zhao Pu's lips, Shen Jianchen voiced his heart once more.
[ZH] 千里寻又开始恍惚，她脑中出现申简辰的名字，可定眼一瞧，面前只有一位花心的王爷，她没忍住，紧张之余，一拳上去，正好打在仪王的脸上，大喊出：“无耻！”
[EN] Dazed all over again, Shen Jianchen's name flashed across her mind. Blinking into focus, all she saw was a fickle royal prince—and in a rush of panic, her fist flew out, landing smack on Prince Yi's cheek as she shouted, "Shameless!"
[ZH] 苦荞跟白风听到，吓得起身跑过来。
[EN] Hearing the shout, Kuqiao and Bai Feng shot up and came running.
[ZH] 白风见王爷的左脸被打得乌青，关心道：“王爷，您没事吧？”
[EN] Seeing the bruise blooming on his master's left cheek, Bai Feng cried, "Your Highness, are you all right?"
[ZH] 苦荞见自家小姐打了仪王，立刻跪下：“王爷恕罪！”
[EN] Seeing her mistress had punched a prince, Kuqiao dropped to her knees at once. "Your Highness, spare her!"
[ZH] 千里寻也后怕，她这一拳打的可是位皇子，还是帮过她的人，他要是计较起来，恐怕还会连累朱晞颜。
[EN] A wave of delayed dread hit Qianli Xun too. She had just punched an imperial prince—one who had helped her, no less! If he pressed the issue, Zhu Xiyan could be dragged down with her.
[ZH] 却听仪王说：“你起来吧，跟白风走远些，不用理会我们，本王与幽栖居士还有事要说。”
[EN] Yet Prince Yi spoke up: "Get up, go further off with Bai Feng, and pay us no mind. Youqi Jushi and I still have things to discuss."
[ZH] 仪王如此大度，苦荞为小姐感到庆幸，她起身与白风走到池塘对岸，继续找石头坐下谈情说爱。
[EN] Amazed at Prince Yi's magnanimity, Kuqiao sighed in relief for her mistress, rose, and walked with Bai Feng to the far side of the pond to sit on the rocks and talk love.
[ZH] 千里寻说：“仪王如若不说轻浮的话，我也不会打您。”
[EN] Qianli Xun said, "If Prince Yi hadn't said something so flippant, I wouldn't have hit you."
[ZH] “以后你会慢慢清楚，我并不轻浮，说的全是真心话。”
[EN] "Sooner or later you'll understand: I wasn't being flippant. Every word came from my heart."
[ZH] “我劝仪王好好珍惜佳和郡主，乔家很快到朱府提亲，希望仪王今日说过的话以后不要再提。”
[EN] "My advice is to treasure Princess Jiahe. The Qiao family will be coming to Zhu Manor to propose soon—I hope Prince Yi never breathes another word of what you said today."
[ZH] “我知道一切已不可能，但我想让你知道我的心，知道有一个人默默爱着你……”
[EN] "I know it's impossible now. I just wanted you to know my heart—to know someone loves you in silence..."
[ZH] “知道有什么用？只能徒增烦恼。”
[EN] "What good does knowing do? It only brings headaches."
[ZH] “幽栖居士可想起我的问题？”
[EN] "Has Youqi Jushi remembered my question yet?"
[ZH] “什么问题？”
[EN] "What question?"
[ZH] 申简辰一脸失落：“你从来不把我话放在心上，算了，不与你计较，再问一遍，幽栖居士失忆后，体内新的灵魂是否记得曾喜欢过谁？”
[EN] Dejection crossed Shen Jianchen's face. "You never take my words to heart. Fine, I won't hold it against you. I'll ask again: after Youqi Jushi lost her memory, does the new soul inside her remember ever loving someone?"
[ZH] 千里寻思忖：“难道仪王猜到我是穿越而来？应该不可能，他看着也没有知过去和未来的能力。他上次说，失忆的人都是因为有新的灵魂住进身体里，也不知从哪里听到这种十分有道理的说法。”
[EN] Qianli Xun paused to think. Could Prince Yi have guessed she crossed over? Impossible—he didn't look like some prophet who saw past and future. Last time he had mentioned the theory that amnesia meant a new soul entering the body; who knew where he picked up such a plausible idea?
[ZH] 想清楚后，千里寻反问道：“仪王老问这干吗？我喜欢过谁，跟仪王毫无关系。”
[EN] Settling her thoughts, Qianli Xun shot back, "Why does Prince Yi keep harping on that? Whoever I loved has nothing to do with you."
[ZH] 申简辰继续靠近千里寻，鼓起勇气：“我想知道千里寻曾经爱过谁？”
[EN] Stepping closer still, Shen Jianchen gathered his nerve. "I want to know who Qianli Xun ever loved."
[ZH] 千里寻又开始恍惚，不由自主产生莫名的熟悉感，忍不住轻声唤道：“申……简……辰？”
[EN] Dazed once more by a wave of uncanny familiarity, she breathed out three syllables without meaning to: "Shen... Jian... chen?"
[ZH] 申简辰的脸上露出满意的笑容：“他是你曾经喜欢过的人吗？”
[EN] A satisfied smile lit Shen Jianchen's face. "Is he the one you used to love?"
[ZH] 千里寻被莫名的熟悉感迷惑，在仪王的脸上寻找着申简辰的痕迹，根本没有用心听仪王问些什么？
[EN] Trapped in that haze of familiarity, Qianli Xun searched Prince Yi's features for traces of Shen Jianchen, half deaf to his question.
[ZH] 申简辰继续说：“你不用这么看着我，我就是……”
[EN] Shen Jianchen went on: "No need to look at me like that. I am—"
[ZH] 后面的话还未说出，传来乔梦蓉的声音：“哥哥为何与幽栖居士在一起？”
[EN] Before he could finish, Qiao Mengrong's voice cut across the clearing: "Why is Brother here with Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] 千里寻瞬间清醒，一定是她太想念申简辰，又身处神柳之下，才会莫名其妙产生幻觉，把仪王当成申简辰。
[EN] Qianli Xun snapped wide awake in an instant. Missing Shen Jianchen so much while standing beneath the Divine Willow must have made her hallucinate and mistake Prince Yi for him!
[ZH] 仪王怎么可能是申简辰？申简辰那个嘚瑟样儿，实在与身份尊贵的皇子不搭。只怪仪王善于勾引女子，连她今日都感到定力不足。
[EN] How on earth could Prince Yi be Shen Jianchen? Shen Jianchen's cocky swagger didn't fit a noble imperial prince at all. Blame Prince Yi for being such a smooth seducer that even her own self-control had wobbled today.
[ZH] 好好的气氛被乔梦容破坏，申简辰还没有任何理由埋怨，只能转身问道：“梦容，你怎么来了？”
[EN] With the moment shattered by Qiao Mengrong, Shen Jianchen had no right to complain and turned around. "Mengrong, what brings you here?"
[ZH] 乔梦容看到哥哥脸上的淤青，担忧道：“哥哥脸上的伤是怎么回事？疼不疼？”
[EN] Spotting the bruise on his cheek, Qiao Mengrong gasped, "What happened to Brother's face? Does it hurt?"
[ZH] “没事，从密林过来时，不小心碰到树上。”申简辰随意编了个理由。
[EN] "It's nothing. I bumped into a tree branch walking through the woods." Shen Jianchen tossed off an excuse.
[ZH] 乔梦容敏锐地感觉到她的出现并不是哥哥所期望的：“哥哥难道不想见到我？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong sensed at once that her arrival was unwelcome. "Doesn't Brother want to see me?"
[ZH] 申简辰不知该如何回答，他不愿说欺骗梦容的话。
[EN] Shen Jianchen did not know what to say; he hated lying to her.
[ZH] 哥哥竟然不作任何解释，泪水已经开始在乔梦容的眼中打转：“哥哥真的不希望见到我？”
[EN] When he offered no denial, tears welled in Qiao Mengrong's eyes. "Brother really didn't want to see me?"
[ZH] 见此情景，千里寻不能再沉默下去，上前拜见佳和郡主：“郡主千万别误会仪王，我今日本打算到神柳下看看我挂的红绸是否还在，等看完了再去幽林别居找文渊，谁知神柳上的红绸全都消失不见，正要去尼姑庵问个究竟，仪王恰好走来，大家都是熟人，我便与他多聊两句，刚才仪王还跟我提到郡主……”
[EN] Seeing the impasse, Qianli Xun stepped in and bowed to Princess Jiahe. "Please don't misunderstand Prince Yi, Princess. I came to the Divine Willow today to check on my red ribbon before heading to Youlin Retreat to see Wenyuan, only to find every ribbon gone! Just as I was about to go ask the nunnery what happened, Prince Yi happened to walk up. Since we know each other, we chatted a bit, and Prince Yi was just talking about you..."
[ZH] 乔梦容转悲为喜：“真的？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong brightened in an instant. "Really?"
[ZH] 千里寻继续编着：“当然，仪王说他这一生最大的幸运就是遇到佳和郡主，还说你们成亲时，邀请我参加。”
[EN] Qianli Xun kept spinning: "Of course! Prince Yi said the greatest fortune of his life was meeting Princess Jiahe, and he even invited me to your wedding."
[ZH] 申简辰心里想道：“你穿越而来，编谎话的本领倒是见长。难道欺骗梦容，就一定是为她好？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen thought, "Crossing over sure sharpened your lying skills. Do you really think fooling Mengrong is for her own good?"
[ZH] 乔梦容走到哥哥身边，眼角滚下一滴泪水：“哥哥，你真的认为遇到我，是你一生最大的幸运？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong stepped up beside him, a single tear rolling from the corner of her eye. "Brother, do you really think meeting me is the greatest fortune of your life?"
[ZH] 申简辰依旧没有回答，掏出巾帕，替梦容擦掉眼角的泪水。乔梦容的到来，再次提醒他，他没有资格再喜欢千里寻。
[EN] Still silent, Shen Jianchen pulled out a handkerchief and wiped the tear from her cheek. Qiao Mengrong's arrival reminded him yet again that he had no right to love Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 帮梦容擦完泪水，申简辰抬头望望升起的明月，才意识到天快黑了。
[EN] Finishing, he glanced up at the rising moon and realized dusk was closing in.
[ZH] “幽栖居士还去看文渊吗？”
[EN] "Is Youqi Jushi still going to see Wenyuan?"
[ZH] 千里寻不假思索地回道：“我还是回朱府吧。”
[EN] Without missing a beat, Qianli Xun answered, "I'd better head back to Zhu Manor."
[ZH] 乔梦容疑惑道：“你不是打算去看乔公子吗？为何又不去？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong blinked. "Didn't you plan to see Young Master Qiao? Why not go?"
[ZH] 今日被仪王搞得心情全无，千里寻怕见到文渊，被他看出些端倪来：“我本打算找文渊，刚刚想起我爹晚上找我有事，怕回去晚了，他不高兴。”
[EN] With Prince Yi wrecking her mood, Qianli Xun feared Wenyuan would notice something off. "I did mean to see Wenyuan, then remembered my father wants to talk to me tonight. I don't want to get back late and upset him."
[ZH] “幽栖居士请便。”申简辰说完，拉着乔梦容离开。
[EN] "As Youqi Jushi wishes." With that, Shen Jianchen took Qiao Mengrong's hand and walked away.
[ZH] 苦荞已与白风分开，从池塘对岸走过来。
[EN] Having parted from Bai Feng, Kuqiao walked back from the far side of the pond.
[ZH] 千里寻望着仪王与佳和郡主的背影，叹道：“好好珍惜当下吧！别一天闲来生事！老娘再也不会单独跟你会面，说到做到！”
[EN] Watching Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe disappear down the path, Qianli Xun sighed. "Cherish what you have in front of you! Stop stirring up trouble when you're bored! I swear I will never meet you alone again—mark my words!"

#CH 104
[ZH] 时间过得真快，已过去一个多月。天气逐渐变凉，临安的女子都换上秋季的衣裳，千里寻也不例外。
[EN] Time flew past, and over a month slipped by. The air grew brisk, and women across Lin'an changed into autumn attire, Qianli Xun included.
[ZH] 一个多月来，她的日子过得相当顺遂，除与家人在一起外，要么跟文渊约会，要么与他一起参加诗会。
[EN] For over a month, her days had rolled along smooth as silk: when not with her family, she was out on dates with Wenyuan or attending poetry salons with him.
[ZH] 自神柳之下相约后，再没见到过仪王，他仿佛故意躲着她，千里寻反而开心。
[EN] Since that meeting beneath the Divine Willow, she had not seen Prince Yi once. He seemed to be avoiding her on purpose, which suited Qianli Xun just fine.
[ZH] 这就对了！说明上次她当面说教起到极好的作用。能将一位王爷改变，千里寻觉得自己实在有本事。
[EN] That was more like it! Her face-to-face lecture must have worked wonders. Turning a royal prince around—Qianli Xun felt pretty impressed with herself.
[ZH] 更开心的是，一个多月来，在朱府没见到陈浩宇。千里寻找嫂嫂聊过好几次，都没碰到他。她出去逛，也没遇到过，陈浩宇好像一下子从她的生活中消失一般。千里寻十分庆幸，说明老天正在帮她清除掉人生中的杂质。
[EN] Even better, she had not run into Chen Haoyu once at Zhu Manor all month. She had visited her sister-in-law several times without spotting him, nor had she crossed paths with him on the streets; he seemed to have vanished from her life overnight. Qianli Xun thanked her lucky stars—Heaven must be skimming the trash out of her life.
[ZH] 日子过得太过顺遂，反而感到不安，千里寻总担心会发生些什么。
[EN] Yet when days went too smooth, unease crept in, and she kept waiting for the other shoe to drop.
[ZH] 快到中秋节，乔守仁终于决定去朱府提亲，花五日的时间将聘礼准备好，给媒婆打好招呼，说好日期，也差人将帖子送到朱府。
[EN] With Mid-Autumn Festival nearing, Qiao Shouren made up his mind at last to propose at Zhu Manor. He spent five days preparing betrothal gifts, instructed the matchmaker, fixed a date, and sent an invitation card over to Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 朱晞颜见到帖子，高兴得合不拢嘴。
[EN] Seeing the card, Zhu Xiyan grinned from ear to ear.
[ZH] 到了约定的提亲之日，朱府的下人将府内上上下下收拾一新，朱晞颜也将族人请来，可左等右等，都到晌午，也不见乔家来人。
[EN] When the appointed day arrived, the Zhu Manor servants scrubbed the estate spotless from front to back, and Zhu Xiyan invited his clansmen over. Yet they waited and waited until noon, with no sign of the Qiao family.
[ZH] 向夫人着急道：“老爷，要不要派人去乔家问问？到底怎么回事？”
[EN] Madam Xiang fretted: "Husband, shouldn't we send someone to Qiao Manor to check what happened?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜点点头，吩咐一位下人前去打听。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan nodded and dispatched a servant to inquire.
[ZH] 下人刚出朱府的侧门，碰见乔家找的媒婆，拉住问道：“乔家说好今日要来提亲，为何到现在还不来？一家子人都着急等着呢？”
[EN] No sooner had the servant stepped out the side gate of Zhu Manor than he ran into the Qiao family's matchmaker. Grabbing her, he asked, "The Qiao family promised to come propose today—why aren't they here yet? The whole house is waiting on pins and needles!"
[ZH] 媒婆说：“还提什么亲？你既然来，也省得我去面见知府大人，你转告大人一声，朱家姑娘在外名声不好，乔家以后不会再上门提亲。”
[EN] The matchmaker said, "What proposal? Since you're here, you'll save me a trip facing the Prefect. Pass on word to His Lordship: Miss Zhu's reputation outside is ruined, so the Qiao family won't be coming to propose anymore."
[ZH] 下人拽住媒婆的胳膊：“你这媒婆，怎么能这么编排我家姑娘？我这就拉你去见我家老爷。”
[EN] The servant seized her arm. "You matchmaker—how dare you slander our Young Lady! I'm dragging you straight to my master!"
[ZH] 媒婆挣脱着跑开，朱府的下人只好回去照实说了。
[EN] Wrenching free, the matchmaker bolted, leaving the servant no choice but to return and report the truth.
[ZH] 朱晞颜气得拍桌子骂道：“乔家欺人太甚！是他们要来提亲，说好的日子，不按时来，还找出这么个理由！别以为他们家是皇亲国戚就了不起，我也是临安知府，这临安可是我说了算！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan slammed the table in fury: "The Qiao family has gone too far! They asked to propose, picked the date, stood us up, and cooked up this excuse! Don't think being imperial relatives makes them gods—I'm the Prefect of Lin'an, and my word still rules this city!"
[ZH] 向夫人在一旁只顾哭，好好的事怎么一下子变成这样？
[EN] Madam Xiang sat weeping beside him. How had such a happy day fallen apart in an instant?
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷劝道：“老爷别生气，等把事情搞明白再说，即使乔家真的听到什么风言风语，不愿上门提亲，也只能说明乔家人目光短浅，耳根子又软，善于听信他人之言，姑娘嫁给这样的人家，我还不放心。”
[EN] Matron Wei urged, "Don't lose your temper yet, Master—wait until we get to the bottom of this. Even if the Qiao family did hear wild gossip and backed out, it only proves they're shortsighted and gullible, ready to swallow any rumor. I wouldn't feel safe letting our Young Lady marry into a house like that anyway."
[ZH] 正说着，朱景元从外面回来，走到父亲跟前，对他耳语一番。
[EN] Just then Zhu Jingyuan walked in from outside, stepped up to his father, and whispered into his ear.
[ZH] 朱晞颜脸色立刻变黑：“你去告诉族人，让他们都回去吧。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan's face went dark as thunder. "Go tell the clansmen to head home."
[ZH] 朱景元瞧一眼正在抹眼泪的向夫人，摇摇头，出去安排。
[EN] Glancing at Madam Xiang dabbing her eyes, Zhu Jingyuan shook his head and went out to handle it.
[ZH] 朱晞颜批评道：“哭什么哭？都是你生的好女儿！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan snapped, "What are you crying for? Look at the fine daughter you raised!"
[ZH] 向夫人哭得更厉害：“呜呜……！我家淑真怎么这么命苦！跟林家和离，又被乔家如此对待！呜呜……！”
[EN] Madam Xiang sobbed all the harder: "Waaah! Why is my Shuzhen's fate so bitter! A mutual divorce from the Lin family, and now treated like this by the Qiaos! Waaah!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷扶着向夫人：“夫人别难过，老爷自有决断，我扶您回屋。”
[EN] Matron Wei steadied her. "Don't cry, Madam. Master knows what to do—let me help you back to your room."
[ZH] 乔家没来提亲的消息很快在朱府传开，苦荞急忙将消息告诉小姐。
[EN] Word that the Qiao family had canceled the proposal spread across Zhu Manor in no time, and Kuqiao rushed in to tell her mistress.
[ZH] 千里寻听后，手中的笔掉落在纸上：“乔家为何到现在还没上门？”
[EN] Qianli Xun's brush slipped from her fingers onto the paper. "Why hasn't the Qiao family come yet?"
[ZH] “下人们传，说是因小姐在外名声不好。”苦荞为小姐愤愤不平，“简直是胡说！小姐人美，心好，肯定是乔家不想来提亲，胡乱找的理由。”
[EN] "Servants are saying it's because Miss's reputation outside is ruined." Outrage burned on Kuqiao's face. "Pure nonsense! Miss is beautiful and kind—the Qiao family must just be making up excuses because they didn't want to propose!"
[ZH] 夏果听到乔家不会来提亲，比小姐还伤心，趴在茶桌上哭起来：“呜呜……！难道小姐以后不能嫁到乔家？呜呜……！”
[EN] Hearing the Qiao family would not propose, Xiaguo wept harder than her mistress, slumping over the tea table: "Waaah! Does this mean Miss can't marry into the Qiao family anymore?! Waaah!"
[ZH] 千里寻没有心情理会夏果，想着自己因何在外名声不好？难道是因那句“和衣睡倒人怀”？
[EN] With no energy to mind Xiaguo, Qianli Xun racked her brains over how her reputation had been ruined. Could it be that line, "falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed"?
[ZH] “苦荞，你去把大公子找来，他定然知道详情。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, go fetch Eldest Brother. He's bound to know the full story."
[ZH] 苦荞出去后，夏果还在哭，千里寻说：“没想到，你比我还难过，也好，你把我该流的眼泪都流了，我正好想想接下来该怎么办。”
[EN] After Kuqiao left, Xiaguo kept sobbing. Qianli Xun said, "Who knew you'd take it harder than me? Fine—cry all the tears I'm supposed to cry so I can think straight about what to do next."
[ZH] 夏果依然哭个不停，千里寻感到心烦：“你回去哭吧，大公子一会儿过来，还要与我说事。”
[EN] Xiaguo still would not stop, fraying Qianli Xun's nerves. "Go back to your room to cry. Eldest Brother will be here in a minute to talk to me."
[ZH] 夏果哭着起身回丫鬟房，一到屋内，趴在床上，哭得更伤心：“小姐不能嫁给乔公子，我该怎么办？呜呜……！”
[EN] Still sobbing, Xiaguo rose, returned to the maids' quarters, flung herself onto her bed, and wept even harder: "If Miss can't marry Young Master Qiao, what am I going to do?! Waaah!"
[ZH] 很快，朱景元进来，让苦荞把门关上。
[EN] Before long, Zhu Jingyuan entered and told Kuqiao to shut the door.
[ZH] 哥哥的表现，使得千里寻更加紧张：“哥哥，到底怎么回事？”
[EN] Her brother's grim manner tightened the knot in Qianli Xun's chest. "Brother, what happened?"
[ZH] “妹妹听了，别往心里去，你与乔文仙的事看样子成不了。”朱景元尽量小心说话，怕伤及妹妹，怎奈实情如此，再小心也无济于事。
[EN] "Don't take it too hard when you hear this, Sister. It looks like things between you and Qiao Wenxian are over." Zhu Jingyuan tried to tread with care, yet the truth hurt no matter how gentle he made it.
[ZH] “乔家……乔家都听到什么？才……突然放弃……放弃上门提亲？”千里寻头一次不由自主用颤抖的声音说话。
[EN] "What... what did the Qiao family hear... to call off the proposal out of nowhere?" For the first time, Qianli Xun heard her own voice tremble.
[ZH] “妹妹可写过一句词？”
[EN] "Did you write a certain line of ci?"
[ZH] 果然因为那句词，千里寻点点头，控制好情绪：“写过，可那时我与文渊在一起，词是写给他的。”
[EN] Just as she feared. Qianli Xun nodded, steadying herself. "I did, back when Wenyuan and I were together. I wrote it for him."
[ZH] “别人管不了那么多，都在批判你有失女德，有伤风化，还说淑真不贞……”
[EN] "Nobody cares about that. Everyone is condemning you for lacking wifely virtue and corrupting morals—even calling you unchaste..."
[ZH] “哥哥也认为是我的错？”
[EN] "Does Brother think I'm to blame too?"
[ZH] “我的态度已不重要，关键是所有人都这么认为，妹妹以后还是不要再写诗作词，否则，你只能在朱府孤独终老。”
[EN] "My opinion doesn't matter anymore. The point is everyone thinks so. Stop writing poetry and ci from now on, Sister, or you'll grow old alone inside Zhu Manor."
[ZH] 千里寻想起，朱淑真就是在朱府郁郁而终，难道她的人生也要如此？绝不！她努力站起来：“哥哥放心，我绝不允许自己在朱府孤独终老。”
[EN] Qianli Xun remembered how the historical Zhu Shuzhen had died of grief inside Zhu Manor. Was her life doomed to the same end? Never! She pushed herself to her feet. "Don't worry, Brother. I'll never let myself die an old maid inside Zhu Manor."
[ZH] 说完，千里寻打算往外走。
[EN] With that, she started for the door.
[ZH] “妹妹要去做什么？”
[EN] "Where are you going?"
[ZH] 千里寻也不知道要去做什么，只是突然之间想走出朱府：“出去走走。”
[EN] She did not even know—she just needed out of Zhu Manor. "For a walk."
[ZH] “还是别出去，外面都是关于你的流言蜚语。”
[EN] "Don't go out! The streets are overflowing with nasty rumors about you."
[ZH] “流言蜚语？”千里寻忍住即将掉落下来的泪水，转过身，“一句词还不够，还有流言蜚语？”
[EN] "Rumors?" Holding back the tears pressing at her eyes, Qianli Xun spun around. "One line of ci wasn't enough—there are rumors too?"
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] "Yes."
[ZH] “哥哥说来听听。”
[EN] "Tell me what they're saying."
[ZH] “还是算了，你好好在家待着，哪里都别去，父亲正在气头上，也别去招惹他。”
[EN] "Better not. Stay indoors and don't go anywhere. Father is furious right now—don't cross his path either."
[ZH] “哥哥不说，我出去听。”
[EN] "If Brother won't tell me, I'll go out and hear for myself."
[ZH] “好！我说！外面百姓到处在传，说你是母老虎，嫁给林家时，已经红杏出墙，跟临安才子暗送秋波，还说妹妹应该被千人所指，万人唾骂……”
[EN] "Fine, I'll tell you! Folk all over town are saying you're a tigress, that you had affairs while married to the Lin family and traded secret glances with Lin'an's scholars, and that you deserve to be pointed at by a thousand fingers and spat on by ten thousand..."
[ZH] 千里寻气得一拳砸在身边的椅子上，椅子被砸断，她的手也受伤。
[EN] Fury exploded in Qianli Xun. She slammed her fist down on the chair beside her; the wood cracked clean through, scraping her knuckles raw.
[ZH] 苦荞上前抓住小姐的手：“小姐，您没事吧？”
[EN] Kuqiao rushed forward and grabbed her hand. "Miss, are you hurt?!"
[ZH] “我说了，你都如此生气，要是出去，还不得见人就打，到时反而坐实了母老虎的传言。听哥哥的，好好在家里待着，哪里都别去，我与父亲会处理此事。”朱景元安慰完，从怀里掏出治伤之药，“我刚好买了药，你拿去先用。”
[EN] "See what I mean? You're this furious indoors—step outside and you'll punch the first person you see, proving the 'tigress' rumors right! Listen to Brother: stay put and don't go anywhere. Father and I will handle this." Trying to comfort her, Zhu Jingyuan drew a bottle of salve from his sleeve. "I happened to buy medicine. Use this first."
[ZH] 苦荞接过药瓶，待大公子走后，将小姐扶到椅子前坐下，为她把药敷上。
[EN] Kuqiao took the bottle, and once Eldest Master left, sat her mistress down and applied the salve.
[ZH] 小姐既不哭，也不闹，平静的样子反而让苦荞感到害怕，越害怕她的话越多。
[EN] Seeing her mistress neither weep nor rage frightened Kuqiao even more, and the more scared she got, the faster she talked.
[ZH] “小姐别担心，谣言之所以是谣言，就是来得快也去得快。乔公子中意的人是小姐您，乔家今日没来提亲，改日也会来，我陪小姐在朱府耐心等待。”
[EN] "Don't worry, Miss. Rumors come fast and fade fast. Young Master Qiao's heart belongs to you—if the Qiao family didn't come today, they'll come another day. I'll stay right here with you and wait."
[ZH] 小姐依然不说话，苦荞继续叨叨：“我们村当年还有人传言我不是娘亲生的，是我娘从臭水沟旁把我捡回去，我与家人根本不理会谣言，后来大家传着传着也觉无趣，渐渐忘记。我敢保证，不出半个月，大家肯定不会再议论……”
[EN] When her mistress stayed silent, Kuqiao rattled on: "Back in our village, folks once spread rumors I wasn't my mother's real child, claiming she picked me up beside a ditch. My family paid it no mind, and after a while everyone got bored and forgot. I swear, within half a month nobody will breathe another word—"
[ZH] 苦荞话还未说完，突然冲进一群下人，将书桌上的手稿，还有木盒里放的诗画，全部往出搬，又翻箱倒柜，只要见到纸张上有字，全部拿到院子里。
[EN] Before Kuqiao could finish, a throng of servants burst through the door, sweeping manuscripts off the desk and poems and paintings out of wooden boxes, ransacking chests and drawers and hauling out every sheet of paper with ink on it into the yard.
[ZH] 千里寻被眼前的情景惊住，她站起来，看着这些下人，他们此时像一群强盗，把朱淑真的诗词和画作全部往外搬。
[EN] Frozen in shock, Qianli Xun stood watching them like a band of looters stripping away every poem and painting of Zhu Shuzhen's.
[ZH] 苦荞厉声道：“你们反了天了！怎么敢在小姐屋里乱翻？”
[EN] Kuqiao barked, "Have you all lost your minds?! How dare you ransack Miss's room!"
[ZH] 一个下人说：“小姐莫见怪，老爷吩咐我们这么做，让我们把小姐所有手稿拿到院子里焚烧。”
[EN] One servant said, "Forgive us, Miss. Master ordered us to haul every single one of your manuscripts out into the yard and burn them."
[ZH] “焚烧？”千里寻念着这两个字，她记得，朱家焚烧朱淑真的手稿是在她郁郁而终之后，那时朱淑真已四十多岁。
[EN] "Burn them?" Qianli Xun repeated the words under her breath. In her memory, Zhu Shuzhen's manuscripts were burned only after she died of heartbreak past the age of forty.
[ZH] ﻿可她穿越而来，才二十岁，就发生这样的事，难道今日才是由朱淑真的命运转变为千里寻命运的关键？
[EN] Yet here she was, crossed over at just twenty, watching it happen right now. Could today be the turning point where Zhu Shuzhen's fate ended and Qianli Xun's true fate began?

#CH 105
[ZH] 屋内的手稿被洗劫一空，全部堆积在院内，一个下人掏出火折子，吹出火苗，打算点着时，苦荞冲出去，一把将火折子打落在地。火碰到冰凉的石砖，立刻熄灭。
[EN] Stripped bare from the room, the manuscripts lay heaped in the courtyard. A servant pulled out a tinder tube, blew a spark into flame, and bent to light the pile—only for Kuqiao to dash forward and knock it straight from his hand. The spark hit the cold stone tiles and died at once.
[ZH] “这些手稿都是小姐多年的心血，怎能被你们随意烧掉？”
[EN] "These manuscripts are years of Miss's heart and soul! How dare you burn them like trash?"
[ZH] 夏果从丫鬟房出来，拦在书稿前：“想烧书稿，先烧了我再说！”
[EN] Xiaguo ran out of the maids' quarters and threw herself in front of the stack. "If you want to burn the manuscripts, burn me first!"
[ZH] 见此情景，其他下人不敢随意动手。
[EN] Confronted by the two maids, the servants hesitated to move.
[ZH] 一位劝道：“拦也没用，老爷吩咐的，必须今日将小姐写的诗词全部焚烧，一首都不许留，我们也没办法。”
[EN] One urged, "Blocking us won't help. Master's orders—every poem Miss ever wrote has to burn today, not a single piece spared. Our hands are tied."
[ZH] 焚烧书稿之事不可逆转，千里寻没想过要阻拦，再说该留下的还会留下。
[EN] The burning could not be stopped, and Qianli Xun had no wish to stop it. Whatever was meant to survive would survive anyway.
[ZH] 焚书坑儒毁灭不了儒家文化，一把火又岂能烧掉朱淑真的诗词？与文字相关的玩意儿，最大的妙处就在于容易刻在脑中，凭记忆便可薪火相传。
[EN] Burning books and burying scholars had never wiped out Confucianism; how could one bonfire erase Zhu Shuzhen's verse? The beauty of words was how they etched themselves into memory, passing from mind to mind like an undying flame.
[ZH] 千里寻从屋内走出，显得异常冷静：“你们两个不要阻拦，既然是我爹的意思，烧就烧吧。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped out of the room, eerily calm. "Step aside, you two. If it's Father's wish, let them burn."
[ZH] 苦荞与夏果离开堆积的书稿，站到小姐身边，目睹下人捡起火折子，又将火苗吹出，把纸点着。纸见到火，疯狂燃烧，火势越来越大。
[EN] Kuqiao and Xiaguo moved away from the mound and stood beside their mistress as the servant retrieved the tinder tube, blew it to life, and touched flame to paper. Fire caught with a fierce crackle, leaping higher by the second.
[ZH] 纸张一点一点在火焰中化为灰烬，千里寻的内心也一点点萌生出希望，仿佛朱淑真的命运正在逐渐被火苗吞噬，剩下的只会属于千里寻。
[EN] Watching sheet after sheet crumble into ash, a strange hope took root inside Qianli Xun—as if Zhu Shuzhen's tragic fate were being devoured by the flames, leaving only Qianli Xun's own path behind.
[ZH] 正当她体验火焰和灰烬带来的快感时，朱晞颜怒气冲冲进来，一巴掌打在女儿的脸上。
[EN] Just as she felt a dark rush of relief watching paper turn to ash, Zhu Xiyan stormed into the yard and slapped her hard across the face.
[ZH] “从小我教你写诗作词，不是让你拿来辱没门风，你倒好，写出淫词滥句，败坏自己的名声不说，连带朱家也跟着被整个临安城的人非议，以后谁还会来朱府提亲！我作为临安知府，因为你！没脸见临安的官员与百姓！”
[EN] "I taught you poetry and ci from childhood—not so you could drag our family honor through the mud! And look what you've done: writing lewd lines, ruining your own name, and leaving the entire Zhu family mocked by all of Lin'an! Who will ever come propose at Zhu Manor now?! As Prefect of Lin'an, I can't even show my face before officials or commoners because of you!"
[ZH] 千里寻捂住被打疼的脸，不想做任何反抗，这一关总得过，过了便好。
[EN] Cupping her stinging cheek, Qianli Xun offered no resistance. This storm had to be weathered; once it passed, it would be over.
[ZH] 苦荞与夏果替小姐担心，可面对老爷的愤怒，她们不敢有任何言语。
[EN] Kuqiao and Xiaguo trembled for their mistress, yet before their master's wrath neither dared utter a peep.
[ZH] “呵呵……！烧得好，统统烧光，一个字都不许留着！”朱晞颜盯着火焰，难过地笑道，接着吩咐下人，“去！到屋里把所有与写诗作词有关的统统搬出来烧掉！”
[EN] "Haha! Burn them! Burn every last scrap—leave not a single character!" Staring into the fire, Zhu Xiyan laughed a bitter laugh, then barked at the servants, "Go! Haul out everything in her room tied to writing poetry and ci and burn it all!"
[ZH] 下人为难道：“老爷，难道笔也要烧？”
[EN] A servant hesitated: "Master, even the brushes?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜大声命令：“烧！还有屋子里的书，也都搬出来！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan roared, "Burn them! Drag out every book in the room too!"
[ZH] 下人们又进入屋内，把笔和所有书全部搬出来，扔进大火中。
[EN] The servants filed back inside, hauling out brushes and every single book to hurl into the blaze.
[ZH] 千里寻放下捂住脸的手，在心中说：“烧吧，把所有与朱淑真有关的都烧掉吧。”
[EN] Lowering her hand from her cheek, Qianli Xun murmured in her heart: "Burn it all. Burn everything that belonged to Zhu Shuzhen."
[ZH] 朱晞颜厉声说：“跪下！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan thundered, "Kneel!"
[ZH] 千里寻很不情愿地跪下，苦荞和夏果也跟着跪下
[EN] Against her will, Qianli Xun dropped to her knees, with Kuqiao and Xiaguo kneeling right beside her.
[ZH] 朱晞颜命令道：“拿鞭子来！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan ordered, "Bring the whip!"
[ZH] 千里寻这才注意到朱晞颜走进小院时，身后跟着一位手拿鞭子的下人。听到命令，那下人将鞭子递到老爷跟前。
[EN] Only then did Qianli Xun notice a servant carrying a whip trailing behind Zhu Xiyan. At the command, the servant stepped forward and offered it.
[ZH] 朱晞颜接过鞭子：“你们两个给我闪开！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan gripped the handle. "You two, get out of the way!"
[ZH] 苦荞与夏果被吓坏，赶紧站起来，躲到一边。
[EN] Terrified, Kuqiao and Xiaguo scrambled to their feet and backed off.
[ZH] 只见朱晞颜使出全身力气，狠狠打下一鞭……
[EN] Putting all his strength into his arm, Zhu Xiyan swung the whip down with ruthless force...
[ZH] 千里寻只觉背上被抽打得火辣辣的疼。
[EN] A searing stripe of fire tore across Qianli Xun's back.
[ZH] 刚要打下第二鞭，韦嬷嬷扶着向夫人走进来，向夫人伤心地哭着。
[EN] Just as he raised his arm for a second strike, Matron Wei hurried in supporting Madam Xiang, who was sobbing her heart out.
[ZH] “呜呜……！老爷！淑真怎么能经得起如此鞭打？您就饶了她吧！……呜呜……！”
[EN] "Waaah! Husband! How can Shuzhen survive a whipping like this? Please spare her! Waaah!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜又狠狠打下一鞭……
[EN] Zhu Xiyan brought the whip slashing down a second time...
[ZH] 向夫人立刻扑到女儿的背上：“呜呜……老爷要打就先打死我！以前的女儿被林觉打得失忆，难道老爷连失忆后的女儿都不愿给我留下？呜呜……！”
[EN] Madam Xiang threw herself straight over her daughter's back. "Waaah! If you want to beat her, beat me to death first! Lin Jue already beat our old daughter into amnesia—won't you even leave me this daughter who came back to us?! Waaah!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜的鞭子无法再落下，他以为如果女儿没有失忆，也许不会写出“和衣睡倒人怀”的句子。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan's arm froze in midair. If his daughter had never lost her memory, perhaps she would never have written "falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed."
[ZH] 朱晞颜将鞭子扔在地上，对向夫人说：“你起开，我有话与淑真说。”
[EN] He flung the whip to the ground and told Madam Xiang, "Get up. I have words for Shuzhen."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷将夫人扶到藤椅上坐下，向夫人继续哭着抹眼泪。
[EN] Matron Wei helped Madam Xiang onto a rattan chair, where she kept weeping and dabbing her eyes.
[ZH] 朱晞颜看着跪在地上忍着疼痛的女儿，想起那日她抱着他的脖子在他脸上亲了一口的情景，那时他已完全从心底接受了这个不一样的女儿。
[EN] Gazing down at his daughter kneeling in silent pain, Zhu Xiyan remembered the day she had thrown her arms around his neck and kissed his cheek—the moment he had accepted this changed daughter with his whole heart.
[ZH] 可她却写出有失女德的句子，被整个临安城的人唾骂，搞得朱家声誉扫地，若继续将她留在朱府，以后朱家的子孙都会受其牵连。
[EN] Yet she had written shameless lines, earning the scorn of all Lin'an and dragging the Zhu name into the dirt. Keeping her under his roof would taint every generation to come.
[ZH] “淑真，你走吧，以后不要再回朱府，朱家就当没有你这个女儿。”
[EN] "Leave, Shuzhen. Never set foot in Zhu Manor again. From this day on, the Zhu family has no daughter like you."
[ZH] 千里寻虽在朱府待的时间不长，可亲情已萌生，朱淑真的父亲突然赶她离开，她的眼角还是滚落下不舍的泪水。
[EN] Though Qianli Xun had not spent long in Zhu Manor, real family warmth had grown there. Hearing Zhu Shuzhen's father cast her out sent reluctant tears spilling down her cheeks.
[ZH] 向夫人急了：“老爷！你把淑真赶出去，她以后一个人怎么过？呜呜……！”
[EN] Madam Xiang panicked: "Husband! If you throw Shuzhen out, how will she survive all alone?! Waaah!"
[ZH] 千里寻双目含泪，果断地说：“父亲放心，从今日起，我搬出朱府。朱府不认我，可在我心里，这里永远是我的家，您和娘，还有哥哥嫂嫂永远是我的亲人。”
[EN] Eyes glistening with tears, Qianli Xun spoke with firm resolve: "Rest easy, Father. Starting today, I move out of Zhu Manor. Even if Zhu Manor disowns me, in my heart this will always be my home, and you, Mother, Brother, and Sister-in-law will always be my family."
[ZH] 女儿的话令向夫人哭得更伤心：“呜呜……！我家淑真的命怎么就这么苦啊！呜呜……！”
[EN] Her words made Madam Xiang sob even harder: "Waaah! Why is my Shuzhen's life so cruel?! Waaah!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜忍住泪水，往院外走，撂下一句话：“天黑前搬出朱府，否则我会派人把你的东西全部扔出去！”
[EN] Swallowing back his own tears, Zhu Xiyan strode toward the gate, tossing one parting order over his shoulder: "Be out of Zhu Manor before dark, or I'll have your belongings dumped in the street!"
[ZH] 其他下人都跟着朱晞颜出了小院。
[EN] The other servants trailed out of the courtyard behind him.
[ZH] 火还在继续燃烧，随着灰烬越来越多，火势变得缓和许多。
[EN] The fire kept crackling, sinking lower as the heap of ash grew deeper.
[ZH] 在两位丫鬟的搀扶下，千里寻努力爬起来，有意装作轻松的样子。
[EN] Leaning on her two maids, Qianli Xun struggled to her feet and put on a brave, easy front.
[ZH] “娘，您别哭，爹打我两下，根本不算什么，再说我离开朱府，又不会走远，依然会待在临安，娘想要见我，可以去望月轩。”
[EN] "Don't cry, Mother. Two lashes from Father are nothing. Besides, I'm not going far—I'll still be right here in Lin'an. Anytime Mother wants to see me, just come to Wangyue Pavilion."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷也说：“夫人放心，有我在，姑娘离开朱府生活不会差。”
[EN] Matron Wei added, "Rest easy, Madam. With me watching over her, our Young Lady won't live poorly outside Zhu Manor."
[ZH] “淑真的生活我不担心，以前的嫁妆够她用，大不了我再拿出些田产铺子给她带上，我只担心她以后没个长辈做主，婚事该怎么办？”向夫人又是一阵难过。
[EN] "I'm not worried about her livelihood—her old dowry is plenty, and I can hand over more estates and shops if need be. I just worry about her marriage, with no elders left to stand behind her!" Fresh grief shook Madam Xiang.
[ZH] 面对突如其来的变故，千里寻很痛心，却强撑着安慰向夫人：“娘不必担心，人来世间又不是只有嫁人这一件事，还有很多事可做。若没人要我，我就一个人过，做自己喜欢的事。”
[EN] Her own heart aching from the sudden blow, Qianli Xun forced herself to comfort Madam Xiang. "Don't worry, Mother. A person doesn't come into this world just to get married—there's plenty else to do. If nobody wants me, I'll live on my own and do whatever makes me happy."
[ZH] 向夫人继续抹眼泪：“话虽如此，女子生来是要嫁人的。”
[EN] Madam Xiang wiped her eyes. "Easy to say, yet a woman is born to wed."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷宽慰道：“我以后就替您和老爷当姑娘的长辈，她的婚事我定会放在心上。”
[EN] Matron Wei soothed her: "From now on, I'll serve as Young Lady's elder in place of you and Master. I'll keep her marriage close to heart."
[ZH] 向夫人才放心地点点头，对两位丫鬟说：“你们两个还不赶快扶小姐进屋给她背上上些药。”
[EN] Only then did Madam Xiang nod in relief, turning to the two maids: "Hurry up and help Miss indoors to put medicine on her back!"
[ZH] 安慰好向夫人，千里寻才感受到背部一阵刺痛，在两位丫鬟的搀扶下进屋上药。
[EN] Only after soothing Madam Xiang did sharp stings flare across Qianli Xun's back. Supported by both maids, she went inside for medicine.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷也扶着向夫人离开。
[EN] Matron Wei helped Madam Xiang back to her quarters as well.
[ZH] 苦荞帮小姐把衣裳脱下，看到背部两道鞭打的伤痕，还时不时渗出血来：“老爷怎么下这般狠手？”
[EN] Helping her mistress out of her robe, Kuqiao stared at the two angry welts oozing blood across her back. "How could Master strike so hard?!"
[ZH] 夏果把药瓶拿来，她的心里还想着乔公子：“小姐，您与乔公子该怎么办？”
[EN] Xiaguo brought over the salve bottle, her mind still stuck on Young Master Qiao. "Miss, what will happen with you and Young Master Qiao now?"
[ZH] 今日发生的事情太多，千里寻还没腾出心情考虑她与文渊之事。夏果提起，她想了想说道：“等我伤好后，跟文渊见一面，再作决定。”
[EN] Too much had piled up today for Qianli Xun to sort out her future with Wenyuan yet. At Xiaguo's question, she paused and said, "Once my wounds heal, I'll meet Wenyuan and decide."
[ZH] 苦荞接过药瓶，边给小姐上药边说：“乔公子是乔家独苗，只要乔公子愿意跟小姐在一起，乔家的长辈也没办法。”
[EN] Taking the bottle, Kuqiao dabbed salve onto her mistress's back. "Young Master Qiao is the sole heir of the Qiao line. As long as he wants to be with you, his elders won't be able to stop him."
[ZH] 夏果转悲为喜：“苦荞姐姐说这话我爱听。”
[EN] Xiaguo perked up at once: "Now that's what I love to hear, Sister Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 刚为小姐上完药，一群丫鬟进来，为首的说：“小姐，老爷吩咐，让我们过来帮您收拾东西，即刻离开朱府。”
[EN] No sooner had the medicine been applied than a troop of maids filed in. Their leader said, "Miss, Master ordered us to help pack your things and see you out of Zhu Manor at once."
[ZH] 夏果生气道：“老爷不是说天黑前吗？离天黑还早，你们急什么？”
[EN] Xiaguo bristled: "Didn't Master say before dark? It's nowhere near dusk—what's your rush?!"
[ZH] 为首的丫鬟又说：“小姐，老爷吩咐，我等只能听从。”
[EN] The lead maid replied, "Master's orders, Miss. We have to obey."
[ZH] 朱晞颜着急把她赶出去，无非是为维护朱家声誉，当所有人知道朱家已将无品无德的女儿赶出家门，才会认为朱家家风甚严，也才可保朱家子孙后代不被他人诟病。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan was in a rush to throw her out just to protect the Zhu name: once all of Lin'an saw the family expel an "unvirtuous" daughter, folk would praise the Zhu household's strict standards and spare future generations from gossip.
[ZH] 千里寻觉得甚是讽刺，她认真奉行净光法师教的“正思正念”四字，却因品行遭人诟病，又被父亲赶出家门，她自信从未做过任何违背道德之事。
[EN] Qianli Xun found it rich with irony. She had followed Master Jingguang's "Right Thought, Right Mindfulness" to the letter, never doing a single immoral deed—yet here she was, slandered for loose conduct and thrown out of her own father's house.
[ZH] 到底哪里不对？要说是因为一句“和衣睡倒人怀”，千里寻难以相信一句词会有这么大威力。再说普通百姓很少喜欢欣赏诗词，这句词最多只会在文人雅士中流传，不至于闹得人尽皆知。
[EN] Where had it all gone wrong? One line—"falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed"—could never carry that kind of blast radius on its own. Common folk didn't follow poetry; that line would have stayed among literati, not swept the whole city.
[ZH] 看来关键在流言，显然是别有用心之人故意编排。编排的人得多恨她，才诌出那些话来。
[EN] The real weapon was the smear campaign, spun up by someone with a grudge—someone who hated her enough to invent filth out of thin air.
[ZH] 提到“恨”字，千里寻想到陈浩宇，她穿越而来唯一合不来之人便是他，难道流言与他有关？
[EN] At the word "hate," Chen Haoyu popped straight into her head. He was the one person she had clashed with since crossing over. Could he be behind the rumors?
[ZH] 千里寻猛然想起在陈浩宇说过，过不了多久，送她一份大礼……
[EN] Then she remembered Chen Haoyu's parting taunt—promising to send her a "big gift" before long...
[ZH] “姑娘，您在想什么？”为首的丫鬟问道。
[EN] "Young Lady, what are you thinking about?" the lead maid asked.
[ZH] 千里寻回过神来：“哦，没什么，既然是我爹吩咐，你们收拾吧。”
[EN] Qianli Xun snapped back. "Oh, nothing. If it's my father's orders, go ahead and pack."
[ZH] 丫鬟们开始翻箱倒柜帮忙收拾整理，无须苦荞和夏果插手。她们头一次不用亲手为小姐整理衣物，清闲在一旁，也头一次体会到原来不是所有的清闲都令人愉快。
[EN] The maids busied themselves emptying chests and wardrobes without needing Kuqiao or Xiaguo to lift a finger. For the first time, the two personal maids stood idle while others packed their mistress's clothes—learning for the first time that not every spell of idleness felt sweet.

#CH 106
[ZH] 丫鬟们收拾完，将大包小包和木箱堆在屋内，叫来些下人，一起把东西搬到府外已套好的马车上。
[EN] Once the maids finished packing, they stacked bundles and wooden chests around the room and called male servants to haul everything out to carriages waiting beyond the gate.
[ZH] 千里寻不想再被丫鬟催促，主动向朱府外走去，既有不舍，也有坚决离开之意。
[EN] Unwilling to be hurried by the maids any longer, Qianli Xun walked out toward the gate on her own—reluctant to leave, yet resolute all the same.
[ZH] 焚烧书稿本发生在朱淑真香魂飘零之后，也算是她以死亡的方式离开朱府。
[EN] Burning the manuscripts was supposed to happen after Zhu Shuzhen's soul slipped away—her final exit from Zhu Manor through death.
[ZH] 现在，焚烧书稿之事提前发生，也是千里寻离开之时，只有离开，她才能走上属于千里寻的命运之路。
[EN] Now the burning had come early, and so had Qianli Xun's departure. Only by walking away could she step onto the destiny that belonged to Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 苦荞看到小姐手上有伤，又想想她背上的鞭痕，一天之内，被伤了两次，也不知道这冤家得罪了哪路神仙。
[EN] Eyeing her mistress's scraped knuckles and picturing the whip marks across her back, Kuqiao wondered which deity on earth her poor mistress had offended to get hurt twice in a single day.
[ZH] “小姐，安顿好之后，您再去尼姑庵拜拜神佛，以求护佑，或者再找找净光法师。”
[EN] "Once we settle in, Miss, go burn incense at the nunnery to pray for protection—or go see Master Jingguang again."
[ZH] 千里寻猜到苦荞的意思，她是觉得自家小姐太可怜，实在找不到其他解决办法，才想到临时抱佛脚。
[EN] Qianli Xun saw right through Kuqiao: feeling sorry for her mistress and out of ideas, she was grasping at incense sticks in a pinch.
[ZH] 苦荞提到净光法师，千里寻不由自主想到老祖宗。自从回到朱府，怕打扰她老人家清静，从未去拜访过，今日要离开，不如前去与她拜别。
[EN] Kuqiao's mention of Master Jingguang reminded Qianli Xun of the Old Ancestor. Ever since returning to Zhu Manor, she had stayed away out of respect for the elder's quiet devotion. Leaving now, she ought to say goodbye.
[ZH] 两位丫鬟见小姐走着走着，忽然停下，又转身往回走。
[EN] Halfway down the path, both maids watched their mistress stop in her tracks and turn back.
[ZH] 苦荞问道：“小姐，您这是……”
[EN] Kuqiao asked, "Miss, where are you—"
[ZH] “我去跟老祖宗道个别。”
[EN] "I'm going to bid the Old Ancestor farewell."
[ZH] 千里寻头也不回，径直往佛堂方向走去。
[EN] Without looking back, Qianli Xun headed straight for the Buddhist shrine.
[ZH] 两位丫鬟只好跟上。
[EN] Both maids hurried after her.
[ZH] 千里寻对朱府已不陌生，每一条路通向哪里，她都了然于心。刚刚习惯朱府，又要离开，多多少少会有不舍。
[EN] Qianli Xun knew every path in Zhu Manor by heart now. Just as she had grown used to calling it home, she had to leave—it was hard not to feel a tug of sorrow.
[ZH] 也许穿越而来的人生需要在漂泊中度过，从林府飘回朱府，又要从朱府飘到望月轩，也不知以后又会飘到哪里。无论飘到哪里，都比死亡幸运，只要还活着，人生便有希望。
[EN] Maybe her crossed-over life was meant to be a drifting one: from Lin Manor back to Zhu Manor, from Zhu Manor to Wangyue Pavilion, and who knew where after that. Still, anywhere beat dying; as long as she drew breath, hope remained.
[ZH] 千里寻珍惜老天给她的第二次生命，她要好好利用第二次重生的机会，走好每一步，无论遇到任何困难，她都会勇敢面对。
[EN] Qianli Xun treasured this second shot at life Heaven had handed her. She meant to make every step count and face whatever storm came head-on.
[ZH] 想着想着，已来到佛堂跟前，里面传出敲木鱼的声音，与第一次来时，并无两样。
[EN] Lost in thought, she reached the Buddhist shrine. From inside came the steady clack of a wooden fish, unchanged from her very first visit.
[ZH] 苦荞上前敲门，敲了好一阵儿，门才被打开。
[EN] Kuqiao knocked on the door for a long while before it swung open.
[ZH] 开门的依然是秀珍嬷嬷，并未像上次一般赶她离开：“原来是姑娘，不知姑娘前来有何事？”
[EN] Matron Xiuzhen stood there again, though this time she did not shoo them away. "Ah, it's the Young Lady. What brings you here?"
[ZH] 千里寻走上前：“我要离开朱府，也许再也不回来，想见见老祖宗。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped forward. "I'm leaving Zhu Manor, perhaps for good. I wanted to see the Old Ancestor before I go."
[ZH] “姑娘稍候。”秀珍嬷嬷又把门关上。
[EN] "Wait a moment, Young Lady." Matron Xiuzhen shut the door again.
[ZH] 不一会儿，敲木鱼的声音戛然而止，过了没多久，又响起。
[EN] Moments later, the clacking of the wooden fish halted, then resumed after a brief pause.
[ZH] 门又被打开，秀珍嬷嬷走到门外：“老祖宗说不必见，姑娘一路走好，她还让我叮嘱姑娘，人生只不过是一场由生到死的旅行，不可能只有一个驿站，无论走到哪个驿站，都希望姑娘懂得停下脚步，欣赏欣赏风景。”
[EN] The door opened again and Matron Xiuzhen stepped out. "The Old Ancestor says there's no need to meet, and wishes you a safe journey. She asked me to leave you with these words: life is only a journey from birth to death, and no road has just one waystation. At whichever station you reach, remember to pause and enjoy the scenery."
[ZH] 老祖宗的话既是安慰，也是人生至理，千里寻十分感激：“淑真受教，感谢老祖宗。”
[EN] Both solace and timeless wisdom—gratitude swelled in Qianli Xun's chest. "Shuzhen takes her teaching to heart. My thanks to the Old Ancestor."
[ZH] 佛堂的门又被关上，千里寻有种强烈的感觉，也许这是她最后一次见老祖宗。想到老祖宗在她最无助时伸出援助之手，感激之情油然而生，不由跪下磕头。
[EN] The shrine door closed once more. A deep certainty told Qianli Xun she might never see the Old Ancestor again. Remembering how the old woman had reached out to save her at her darkest hour, she dropped to her knees and kowtowed.
[ZH] 两个丫鬟也跪下，跟着小姐一起磕头。
[EN] Both maids knelt and kowtowed alongside their mistress.
[ZH] 朱府侧门外，已有四辆马车等待，两辆负责装载大小包袱和箱子，两辆负责载人。
[EN] Outside Zhu Manor's side gate waited four carriages: two loaded with bundles and trunks, two for passengers.
[ZH] 千里寻出来时，不见娘与哥嫂，只有韦嬷嬷站在外面等她。她猜测，定是朱晞颜不让他们出来相送。
[EN] Stepping outside, Qianli Xun saw neither her mother nor her brother and sister-in-law—only Matron Wei waiting there. No doubt Zhu Xiyan had forbidden them from seeing her off.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷见姑娘出来，迎上去：“老爷在，夫人不敢出来，等姑娘安顿好，夫人会偷偷到望月轩看你。”
[EN] Matron Wei hurried up. "With Master inside, Madam dared not come out. Once you're settled in, Madam will slip over to Wangyue Pavilion to visit you."
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，登上马车。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded and climbed into the carriage.
[ZH] 两个丫鬟跟随小姐同坐一辆，韦嬷嬷坐在后面的马车内。
[EN] Both maids rode with her while Matron Wei took the carriage behind.
[ZH] 马车启动，一步一步离开朱府，离开千里寻已在心中认定的家。老祖中说的极对，家也只是驿站，为开启下一个人生驿站，只能离开上一个。
[EN] The wheels began to turn, carrying Qianli Xun step by step away from Zhu Manor—the place her heart had come to call home. The Old Ancestor was right: even home was just a waystation, and to reach the next, one had to leave the last behind.
[ZH] 马车在街上行走，千里寻无心关注街上的情景，她心里念着文渊。乔家放弃提亲，不知他会如何？是痛苦难过？还是正在向父母祈求？
[EN] As the carriage rolled through the streets, Qianli Xun paid no heed to the bustle outside; her thoughts clung to Wenyuan. With the Qiao family calling off the proposal, how was he holding up? Was he heartbroken, or pleading with his parents right now?
[ZH] 净光法师说过，她会经历几段情路。难道她与文渊有缘无分？想到这些，千里寻更感到悲伤。
[EN] Master Jingguang had once foretold she would walk several paths of love. Were she and Wenyuan destined to meet without being meant to stay? The thought deepened her ache.
[ZH] 悲伤之余，暗暗决定，无论结果如何，绝不会轻言放弃，她要为个人的幸福努力，哪怕徒劳一场，以后想起也不会因没有努力争取而自怨自艾。
[EN] Through the grief, a quiet vow hardened in her chest: whatever the outcome, she would not give up without a fight. She would fight for her happiness so that even if it came to nothing, she would never look back in regret for failing to try.
[ZH] 马车突然停下，苦荞掀开帘子：“怎么停下？”
[EN] The carriage came to a halt. Kuqiao lifted the curtain. "Why did we stop?"
[ZH] 没等车夫回答，她已瞧见朱景元和陈文心，赶紧放下帘子：“小姐，大公子跟少夫人在外面。”
[EN] Before the driver could reply, she spotted Zhu Jingyuan and Chen Wenxin outside and dropped the curtain. "Miss, Eldest Master and Young Madam are out there!"
[ZH] 哥哥与嫂嫂前来相送，完全出乎意料。千里寻迅速走下马车，拜见完哥嫂，被他们拉到人少的巷子。
[EN] Never expecting her brother and sister-in-law to come see her off, Qianli Xun hopped down at once. After greeting them, they drew her into a quiet side lane.
[ZH] 陈文心说：“我跟你哥哥不敢在朱府外送你，怕公公知道用家法处置我们，就找了个借口出来，到半道上拦截你，送送你，也跟你聊几句。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin said, "Your brother and I didn't dare see you off at Zhu Manor's gate for fear Father would bring down the rod on us. So we made an excuse to slip out and intercept you halfway—to send you off and have a few words."
[ZH] “哥哥嫂嫂如此待我，淑真感激不尽。”
[EN] "Shuzhen is grateful beyond words for Brother and Sister-in-law's kindness."
[ZH] 朱景元说：“谢什么？我们是一家人。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan said, "What's there to thank us for? We're family."
[ZH] “哥哥和嫂嫂还愿认我？不担心我毁了朱家声誉？”
[EN] "You two still claim me? Aren't you afraid I've ruined the Zhu family's name?"
[ZH] 陈文心笑道：“别人不了解你，我还不了解？外人都是胡说，至于你写的那句词，我可是很喜欢，整首词我都能背下来，要不我背给你听？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin laughed. "Others might not know you, but don't I? Outsiders are talking rubbish. As for that line of ci you wrote, I love it! I memorized the whole lyric—want me to recite it for you?"
[ZH] “嫂嫂说过不喜欢我的词，怎么对这首情有独钟？”
[EN] "Didn't Sister-in-law say you never liked my ci? Why take a shine to this one?"
[ZH] “这首不一样，让我看到不一样的你，大胆，热情……，我跟你哥前来，是想告诉你，公公虽把你赶出朱府，但我们，还有婆婆，依然是你的亲人，有什么需要，尽管来麻烦我们。”
[EN] "This one's different—it showed me a new side of you, bold and fiery! Your brother and I came to tell you: even if Father threw you out of Zhu Manor, we—and Mother too—are still your family. Anytime you need anything, come straight to us."
[ZH] 千里寻很感动，再次行礼：“哥哥嫂嫂在淑真被全城人唾骂之时，如此关切我，令我……”她不知该说什么话表达心情，在亲情面前，任何话语都显得苍白无力。
[EN] Deeply moved, Qianli Xun bowed again. "With the whole city spitting on my name, having Brother and Sister-in-law stand by me makes me..." Words failed her; before true family love, speech felt pale and thin.
[ZH] 陈文心扶住她：“我跟你哥哥前来，又不是想让你给我们行礼。说起谣言，真是可恶，我们等你时，还听到有人议论，我上去把那人臭骂几句，你要是听到，别往心里去，都是大家乱说，主要因你在临安大小也是个名人，稍微有点儿风吹草动，会被人添油加醋议论，到最后传得面目全非。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin caught her arms. "Your brother and I didn't come out here to make you bow. As for those filthy rumors, they make my blood boil! While waiting for you, we heard some idiot gossiping and I tore him a new one. Don't take any of it to heart—it's all idle chatter. You're famous in Lin'an, so the tiniest breeze gets blown up and twisted beyond recognition."
[ZH] 嫂嫂的一番话令千里寻觉得产生怀疑陈浩宇的念头甚是对不住关切她的嫂嫂。
[EN] Hearing her sister-in-law's warmth made Qianli Xun feel guilty for ever suspecting Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] “嫂嫂不用担心，我不会把流言蜚语当回事，人正不怕影子歪，日久见人心，只要我行得端坐得正，终有一日会被大家看在眼里。”
[EN] "Don't worry, Sister-in-law, I won't let rumors get to me. An upright tree casts a straight shadow, and time reveals every heart. As long as I walk a straight path, people will see the truth one day."
[ZH] 朱景元对一旁的下人说：“把东西拿来。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan signaled a servant standing nearby. "Bring it over."
[ZH] 下人走过来，将手中的木盒呈上。
[EN] The servant stepped up and presented a wooden box.
[ZH] 朱景元接过，送到妹妹面前：“拿着，是爹让我给你的。”
[EN] Taking it, Zhu Jingyuan handed it to his sister. "Take this. Father told me to give it to you."
[ZH] “爹？”千里寻接过木盒，“里面是什么？”
[EN] "Father?" Qianli Xun accepted the box. "What's inside?"
[ZH] 朱景元说：“关于你的谣言传得满城都是，加之乔家放弃提亲，必然又引得大家议论，爹为保朱家声誉，不得已才将你赶出家门，其实，他也很难过，让我把这些地契和房契给你。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan explained, "With rumors flooding the city and the Qiao family calling off the proposal, tongues are bound to wag harder. Father had to throw you out to protect the family name, yet it broke his heart too. He told me to give you these land and property deeds."
[ZH] 千里寻实在搞不懂这位父亲：“爹不让你和嫂嫂送我，却又让你给我送地契和房契，这不是自相矛盾吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun could not make heads or tails of this father. "He forbids you and Sister-in-law from seeing me off, then sends you after me with deeds? Doesn't he contradict himself?"
[ZH] 朱景元说：“你要理解他的矛盾，若我们在家门口送你，必然被爹处置，故而找个理由来这里，恐怕爹也是这么想的。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan said, "Try to understand his bind. If we saw you off at the gate, he'd have to punish us—that's why we made an excuse to meet you here, and I bet Father counted on it."
[ZH] “真够虚伪的！”
[EN] "What a hypocrite!"
[ZH] “别这么说爹。”
[EN] "Don't speak of Father like that."
[ZH] “以前的嫁妆尽够，爹又何必给这些？我不要。”千里寻虽知朱晞颜的难处，还是忍不住想赌气，把盒子放回哥哥怀中。
[EN] "My old dowry is more than enough—why hand me these? I don't want them." Even knowing Zhu Xiyan's dilemma, stubborn pride flared in Qianli Xun, and she pushed the box back into her brother's arms.
[ZH] 朱景元又将盒子塞给妹妹：“你又何必拒绝爹的心意？他把你赶出来已伤心，你再不收下，他心里会更加难安。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan pressed it right back into hers. "Why turn away Father's goodwill? Throwing you out already tore him up; if you refuse this, his conscience will weigh even heavier."
[ZH] “原来爹是为自己安心才让你送来这些，那我就收下，谁让我叫他一声爹，作为女儿，让爹安心，也算是尽孝。”千里寻接过盒子，递给苦荞，“收好了。”
[EN] "So Father only sent these to ease his own guilt? Fine, I'll take them. Since I call him Father, putting his mind at ease counts as filial duty." Qianli Xun accepted the box and passed it to Kuqiao. "Keep it safe."
[ZH] 朱景元继续说：“爹还说，他会派人彻查散播谣言之人，他怀疑有人故意破坏朱家声誉，一旦查出，绝不轻饶。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan added, "Father also said he's sending men to hunt down whoever started these rumors. He suspects someone is targeting the Zhu family's reputation on purpose—once caught, he won't show mercy."
[ZH] 刚想打消怀疑陈浩宇的想法，哥哥又说此话，看来连父亲都觉得是有人故意为之，她又不由生起对陈浩宇的怀疑之心。
[EN] Just as she was about to dismiss her suspicion of Chen Haoyu, her brother's words stirred it right back up. Even her father suspected foul play.
[ZH] 没有任何证据，嫂嫂又善待于她，绝对不能说出心中疑虑，还是让父亲查吧。只怕查出陈浩宇来，朱陈两家将难以相处，嫂嫂夹在中间，该如何自处？
[EN] Yet with zero proof and a sister-in-law who treated her like gold, she could never voice that suspicion aloud—better to let her father investigate. If it did trace back to Chen Haoyu, relations between the Zhu and Chen families would turn ugly, leaving her sister-in-law trapped in the middle.
[ZH] 千里寻看一眼嫂嫂，她露出亲切的笑容，那笑容足以化解她内心所有不满。她又觉得是自己太过多虑，仅仅只是怀疑，说不定陈浩宇这个混世魔王跟此事毫无关系。
[EN] Qianli Xun glanced at Chen Wenxin, whose warm smile melted every ounce of resentment inside her. Maybe she was overthinking things; with no proof, that troublemaker Chen Haoyu might have nothing to do with it at all.
[ZH] “让爹费心了。”
[EN] "Tell Father I'm grateful for his trouble."
[ZH] 辞别哥嫂，千里寻又登上马车。
[EN] Bidding her brother and sister-in-law farewell, Qianli Xun boarded the carriage once more.
[ZH] 苦荞将木盒给了后面马车上的韦嬷嬷，随后也上了小姐乘坐的马车。
[EN] Kuqiao passed the wooden box to Matron Wei in the rear carriage, then climbed in beside her mistress.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷打开木盒，看到一摞厚厚的房契地契，叹道：“老爷表面心狠，内心还是关心姑娘。”
[EN] Opening the box to find a thick stack of land and house deeds, Matron Wei sighed, "Master may look hard on the outside, yet he still cares for our Young Lady inside."
[ZH] 马车又开始启动，穿过闹市，千里寻觉得外面的闹市与她内心比起来更显安静。
[EN] The carriages rolled onward through the bustling streets, yet to Qianli Xun all the clamor outside felt quieter than the storm in her chest.

#CH 107
[ZH] 乔家本已做好提亲准备，一大早起来忙活，聘礼在院内摆得整整齐齐，族中许多年轻人也赶来，准备代表乔家一起到朱家提亲。
[EN] The Qiao household had prepared everything for the proposal, rising at crack of dawn to get busy. Betrothal gifts stood arrayed in neat rows across the courtyard, and young clansmen had gathered to accompany the family to Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 乔文仙也一大早赶回乔府，今日是他与淑真的好日子，必须跟家人前去。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had hurried back to Qiao Manor at dawn too—today was the big day for him and Shuzhen, and he wouldn't miss going with his family for the world.
[ZH] 正当一切准备就绪，刚要出发时，米氏从外面回来，行色匆匆，说有要事相告，便将乔守仁与钱氏请到屋内，让丫鬟把门关上。
[EN] Just as everything stood ready for departure, Concubine Mi rushed back from outside in a fluster, claiming she had urgent news. She ushered Qiao Shouren and Madam Qian indoors and had a maid shut the door.
[ZH] 乔文仙很纳闷，到底发生何事？会不会影响他与淑真的好事？
[EN] Qiao Wenxian frowned in puzzlement. What on earth had happened? Would it disrupt his happy day with Shuzhen?
[ZH] 屋内，乔守仁批评道：“你唱的是哪一出？难道嫌我不让你跟着一起去朱府？都给你解释过，你去不合适。”
[EN] Inside, Qiao Shouren snapped, "What kind of stunt are you pulling now? Sulking because I wouldn't let you come to Zhu Manor? I already explained it isn't proper for you to go."
