[ZH] 米氏说：“老爷也太小瞧我，哪是因为此事？一大早，我身边的丫鬟说，朱家姑娘在外面名声大臭，全城人都在议论她，只因乔家到朱家提亲的消息很多都听说，不敢将听到的告诉我们。我本不相信，出去转了一圈儿，一转才知道，大街小巷茶馆酒肆都在谈论她。”
[EN] Concubine Mi retorted, "Master thinks too little of me! It's nothing like that! First thing this morning, my maid told me Miss Zhu's name is mud out on the streets—the whole city is gossiping about her! Because everyone heard the Qiao family was proposing to the Zhus, nobody dared tell us. I didn't believe it, so I went out for a turn myself, and sure enough, every lane, teahouse, and tavern is buzzing about her!"
[ZH] 钱氏担忧道：“大家如何谈论朱家姑娘？”
[EN] Madam Qian gasped in alarm, "What are they saying about Miss Zhu?"
[ZH] “可难听了！”米氏便将听到的细说一遍。
[EN] "Pure filth!" Concubine Mi laid out every word she had heard.
[ZH] 乔守仁听后，脸色变得难看，一言不发。
[EN] Qiao Shouren's face darkened to ash, and he said not a word.
[ZH] 钱氏说：“来了那么多族人，也没听大家说什么。”
[EN] Madam Qian said, "So many clansmen showed up today, yet none of them breathed a word."
[ZH] “姐姐与老爷等着。”米氏起身，打开门，对丫鬟说，“去，叫位族中的晚辈，再把公子唤来。”
[EN] "Wait right here, Sister and Master." Concubine Mi rose, opened the door, and instructed a maid, "Go fetch one of the younger clansmen, and summon Young Master too."
[ZH] 没过多久，乔文仙与一位族中的晚辈一起前来，一进门，门又被丫鬟从外面关上。
[EN] Moments later, Qiao Wenxian walked in with a young kinsman, and the maid shut the door behind them.
[ZH] 乔文仙不明所以，问道：“父亲、母亲、娘，你们为何躲在屋里？不赶快去朱家提亲？”
[EN] Baffled, Qiao Wenxian asked, "Father, Mother, Mama—why are you hiding indoors instead of setting off for Zhu Manor to propose?"
[ZH] 长辈们没有回话，任由乔文仙站在那里。
[EN] None of the elders replied, leaving Qiao Wenxian standing there.
[ZH] 族中晚辈大约十来岁，见此情景，问道：“大伯可是听说了关于朱家姑娘的传言？”
[EN] The young kinsman, barely in his teens, sized up the room and asked, "Has Uncle heard the rumors about Miss Zhu?"
[ZH] 乔文仙感觉出不对：“传言，什么传言？”
[EN] A cold chill struck Qiao Wenxian. "Rumors? What rumors?"
[ZH] 晚辈说：“文渊哥哥难道不知？朱家姑娘写了一句‘和衣睡倒人怀’传遍临安，大家都在议论。”
[EN] The boy said, "Brother Wenyuan doesn't know? Miss Zhu wrote a line—'falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed'—and it's spread all over Lin'an. Everyone's talking about it."
[ZH] 乔文仙笑道：“我当是什么，这句词我知道，淑真写时我就在身边，她是写给我的。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian laughed. "I thought it was something serious! I know that line—I was right beside Shuzhen when she wrote it. She wrote it for me!"
[ZH] “啪”的一声，桌子被乔守仁拍响：“如此放荡的女子！岂能嫁到乔家？”
[EN] With a sharp crack, Qiao Shouren slammed the table. "Such a wanton woman! How could she ever marry into the Qiao family?!"
[ZH] 乔文仙急道：“父亲，淑真不就是写了一句词？您何须以此评判她的人品？再说她是跟我在一起时写下的，前前后后儿子最清楚不过……”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian panicked: "Father, it's just one line of ci! How can you judge her character from that? Besides, she wrote it while with me—I know every detail of what happened..."
[ZH] 米氏说：“儿子，还不止这些，你是没出去听听，大家都怎么议论她？难听死了，我都开不了口。”
[EN] Concubine Mi broke in: "Son, that's not the half of it! You haven't gone out to hear how folk are tearing her apart—it's so vile I can't even repeat it!"
[ZH] 乔守仁问族中晚辈：“外面对朱家姑娘的议论，族中人可知？”
[EN] Qiao Shouren turned to the young kinsman: "Do the rest of the clan know what folk are saying about Miss Zhu?"
[ZH] 晚辈点点头：“尽知。”
[EN] The boy nodded. "Every single one."
[ZH] 乔守仁质问道：“为何不告诉我？害我差点儿铸成大错！”
[EN] Qiao Shouren barked, "Why didn't anyone tell me?! You almost let me make a disastrous mistake!"
[ZH] 晚辈小心解释：“大伯家是皇亲国戚，朱家老爷又是临安知府，又听说，仪王也从中牵线搭桥，谁敢在大伯面前说这些？”
[EN] The boy explained in a small voice, "Uncle's house is imperial kin, Master Zhu is the Prefect of Lin'an, and everyone heard Prince Yi acted as go-between. Who would dare bring that up to Uncle?"
[ZH] 乔守仁用凝重的语气说：“你去吧，告诉大家，乔家向朱家提亲之事取消，让大家回去吧。”
[EN] In a grim voice, Qiao Shouren ordered, "Go out and tell everyone the proposal to the Zhu family is canceled. Send them all home."
[ZH] 晚辈还未退出，乔文仙着急道：“爹！都说好的事，岂能反悔？”
[EN] Before the boy could leave, Qiao Wenxian cried out, "Father! You already gave your word—how can you go back on it?!"
[ZH] 乔守仁大声吼道：“有什么不能反悔的？难道你想把一个品行不端的女子娶回家？”
[EN] Qiao Shouren roared, "Why can't I go back on it?! Do you want to bring a woman of loose morals into this house?!"
[ZH] 晚辈吓得赶紧开门退出，又小心把门从外面带上。
[EN] Terrified, the boy slipped out the door and shut it behind him.
[ZH] “爹难道连仪王的面子都不给？”乔文仙想用仪王再次说服乔守仁。
[EN] "Father wouldn't even give Prince Yi face?" Qiao Wenxian tried playing Prince Yi's card again.
[ZH] “仪王的面子我当然要给，可仪王劝说我时，流言未起，若当时听到流言，我舍了这条老命，也绝不同意你娶朱家姑娘！”
[EN] "Of course I give Prince Yi face—yet when Prince Yi persuaded me, these rumors hadn't broken out! Had I heard them back then, I would have staked my old life before letting you marry Miss Zhu!"
[ZH] 昨晚乔文仙高兴得睡不着，一大早又兴奋地起来赶回乔府，期待着提亲之事。本以为万无一失，谁知会发生这等事。
[EN] Last night Qiao Wenxian had lain awake from pure joy, leaping up at dawn to hurry home for the proposal. He had thought it was a done deal—never dreaming it would crash down like this.
[ZH] 乔文仙跪下：“父亲，您别相信那些流言，儿子与淑真交好，她是什么样的人我最清楚，一定是有人诋毁她……”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian dropped to his knees. "Father, don't believe those rumors! I know Shuzhen better than anyone—someone must be slandering her..."
[ZH] 乔守仁哪里肯听？厉声说：“诋毁？苍蝇不叮无缝的蛋！她没问题，别人能平白无故议论她？我看还是她有问题！你回别居去吧，不要再跟朱家姑娘来往，若不听，我就把你锁在乔府，直到帮你挑好姑娘，成了亲再放你出来。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren would hear none of it, snapping back: "Slandering her? Flies don't buzz around a seamless egg! If she's spotless, why would folk talk about her out of nowhere? Clearly the fault lies with her! Go back to the retreat and cut off all contact with Miss Zhu. Disobey me, and I'll lock you inside Qiao Manor until we pick another bride and get you wed!"
[ZH] 乔文仙求道：“母亲！娘！你们帮我说说话，劝劝爹，淑真她是好姑娘！”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian begged, "Mother! Mama! Say a word for me—persuade Father! Shuzhen is a good girl!"
[ZH] 米氏决定将流言的内容说给儿子听，希望他死心：“儿子，你可知道外头怎么议论朱家姑娘？”
[EN] Determined to make her son give up, Concubine Mi asked, "Son, do you even know what people outside are saying about Miss Zhu?"
[ZH] 乔文仙继续表明态度：“我不管别人怎么议论，只要我知道她好便可。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian stood firm: "I don't care what outsiders say—all that matters is that I know she's good."
[ZH] 米氏果断说道：“你既是如此态度，我不妨说出来让你听听，外面人都在议论她跟林家和离前就与野男人暗送秋波，红杏出墙，当众睡到男人怀里，才写下‘和衣睡倒人怀’，还说她是母老虎，才逼得林家答应和离，啧啧啧，你听听，乔家怎能把这样的女子娶回来？”
[EN] Concubine Mi hardened her heart: "If that's your attitude, then hear it for yourself! Everyone out there says she traded secret glances with other men and had affairs before divorcing Lin Jue, sprawled in a man's arms in public to write 'falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed,' and acted like a tigress to force the Lin family into divorce! Tsk, listen to that—how could the Qiao family ever marry a woman like that?"
[ZH] “全都是胡说！娘千万别信！”
[EN] "Pure lies! Don't believe a word of it, Mama!"
[ZH] 米氏劝道：“儿子，朱家姑娘成亲后，你就离开临安，你回来后，她很快和离，你当然什么都不清楚。乖，别闹了，听你爹的话，不要再跟朱家姑娘来往。”
[EN] Concubine Mi urged, "Son, you left Lin'an right after Miss Zhu wed, and she divorced right after you returned—how could you know what happened? Be good, stop making a scene, listen to your father, and stay away from Miss Zhu."
[ZH] “母亲，您替儿子说说话，劝劝爹。”
[EN] "Mother, please put in a word for me with Father!"
[ZH] 钱氏叹口气：“唉，文渊，本来因朱家姑娘和离过一次，我们三人都不同意你跟她的事，仪王帮你出面，他的面子总是要给的。你也看到，本也要上门提亲，可谁知朱家姑娘名声如此差，怪只怪你与她不是一家人，进不了一家门，还是放弃吧。”
[EN] Madam Qian let out a sigh. "Wenyuan, none of us three wanted this match in the first place since she had been divorced. We only went along to give Prince Yi face. You saw we were ready to go propose, yet who knew her reputation was this foul? Blame fate—you two aren't meant to be under one roof. Let her go."
[ZH] 三位长辈口径一致，乔文仙清楚自己再怎么努力也无济于事。
[EN] With all three elders united against him, Qiao Wenxian saw no amount of pleading would move them.
[ZH] 他站起来，拜道：“父亲、母亲、娘，我这就回幽林别居，可你们别想让我与其他姑娘成亲，儿子这辈子非淑真不娶！”
[EN] Rising to his feet, he bowed. "Father, Mother, Mama—I'll return to Youlin Retreat right now. Don't dream of making me marry anyone else, though. In this life, I'll marry Shuzhen or no one at all!"
[ZH] 乔文仙说完，推门而出。
[EN] With that, he flung open the door and strode out.
[ZH] 聘礼还摆在院内，人已散去，喜庆的场面就像根本没发生一般。乔文仙觉得他一定是在做梦，如若不是，为何感觉如此不真实。
[EN] The betrothal gifts still stood lined up in the courtyard, yet the guests had dispersed, leaving the festive display looking like a mirage. Qiao Wenxian felt trapped in a nightmare—how else could it feel so unreal?
[ZH] 直到媒婆出现在面前：“乔公子，提亲之事真的取消了？”
[EN] Only when the matchmaker appeared before him—"Young Master Qiao, is the proposal really called off?"—did reality crash home.
[ZH] 乔文仙才清醒过来，抓住媒婆的手臂：“你是媒人，最会说合，快进去帮我说说，让父亲到朱家提亲。”
[EN] He gripped the matchmaker's arm. "You're a matchmaker, skilled at smoothing things over—go inside and help convince my father to propose to the Zhu family!"
[ZH] 媒婆说：“我前几日还在想，要不要把朱家姑娘被人议论之事告诉乔老爷，一直没敢开口，还好他知道得及时，否则这媒我做着都不安心。”
[EN] The matchmaker replied, "For days I debated whether to tell Master Qiao about the gossip surrounding Miss Zhu, never daring to speak up. Thank goodness he found out in time, or my conscience wouldn't have let me sleep arranging this match."
[ZH] “没想到连你也这么认为，我这就去大街上听听，看谁在造谣！”乔文仙的内心就被浇了一盆凉水，他放开媒婆的手臂，冲出乔府。
[EN] "Even you believe it? I'll go out on the streets right now and see who's spreading these lies!" Cold water doused Qiao Wenxian's heart. Releasing her arm, he bolted out of Qiao Manor.
[ZH] 南双担心公子出事，跟着跑出去。
[EN] Worried for his master, Nan Shuang chased after him.
[ZH] 媒婆正要进屋问个究竟，乔守仁正好出来。
[EN] Just as the matchmaker turned to head inside, Qiao Shouren stepped out.
[ZH] “麻烦你去趟朱府，告诉知府大人，朱家姑娘名声不好，乔家高攀不上。”
[EN] "Trouble yourself to go to Zhu Manor and tell the Prefect that with Miss Zhu's reputation in tatters, the Qiao family cannot presume to aim so high."
[ZH] 媒婆为难道：“只怕知府大人会打我板子……”
[EN] The matchmaker hesitated: "What if the Prefect has me beaten with boards?"
[ZH] “你只传个话，知府大人再生气，也得讲究一个‘理’字，尽管去，银子少不了你的，文渊往后的婚事还需你多费心。”乔守仁掏出一锭银子，“你的辛苦费。”
[EN] "You're only delivering a message—furious as the Prefect may be, he still listens to reason. Go on, you'll be paid well, and I'll still need your help with Wenyuan's future match." Qiao Shouren tossed her an ingot of silver. "For your trouble."
[ZH] 媒婆接过银子，满口应下：“我这就去朱府，定然把话传到。”
[EN] Catching the silver, the matchmaker agreed at once. "I'll head straight to Zhu Manor and deliver your message."
[ZH] 离开乔府，南双见自家公子连马车都不坐，只顾往人多的地方走，边走边说：“都是胡说！哪有人议论淑真？肯定是你们不想上门提亲，才胡乱找出理由！”
[EN] Outside Qiao Manor, Nan Shuang watched his master shun the carriage and stride toward the busiest streets, muttering as he walked: "All lies! Who's gossiping about Shuzhen? They just didn't want to propose and cooked up an excuse!"
[ZH] 南双上前拽住公子：“公子，我们回去吧。”
[EN] Nan Shuang hurried ahead to tug his sleeve. "Young Master, let's go back."
[ZH] 乔文仙双手抓住南双，晃着他的肩膀：“南双，你告诉我你从来没有听到别人议论淑真……”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian seized Nan Shuang by both shoulders and shook him. "Nan Shuang, tell me you've never heard anyone gossiping about Shuzhen!"
[ZH] “我整日跟公子在一起，公子没听到过，我也没听到过，我要是听到，肯定会告诉公子。”
[EN] "I'm with you all day, Young Master—if you haven't heard it, neither have I! If I had, I'd have told you."
[ZH] “你瞧，街上这么多人，大家连自己的生活都忙不过来，哪有人有闲工夫议论他人？……”
[EN] "Look at all these people on the street! Everyone's busy enough making a living—who has spare time to gossip about others..."
[ZH] 话还未说完，一边有个买菜的妇人买完菜，边付钱边说：“最近听说没？知府家千金的事儿？”
[EN] Before he could finish, a woman buying vegetables nearby handed over her coins and said, "Heard the latest about the Prefect's daughter?"
[ZH] 卖菜的妇人回道：“怎么没听说，这条街无人不知。”
[EN] The vegetable vendor replied, "Who hasn't? Everyone on this street knows!"
[ZH] 买菜的妇人说：“我一直以为大家闺秀既有修养，又有品德，没想到知府家的千金跟瓦子里那些女子没什么区别。”
[EN] The buyer said, "I always thought highborn ladies had refinement and virtue—turns out the Prefect's daughter is no better than those women in the entertainment quarters."
[ZH] “是啊，她叫什么来着？对！朱淑真！”卖菜的妇人摇摇头，“名字倒起得好，只可惜一点儿都不贤良淑德，谁娶她谁倒霉。”
[EN] "Right? What's her name again? Ah, Zhu Shuzhen!" The vendor shook her head. "Nice name, shame she hasn't an ounce of wifely virtue. Whoever marries her is in for misery."
[ZH] 一个老头儿过来批评道：“你们这些妇人，凑到一起就知道瞎胡议论，小心知府大人听到，把你们拉去打板子。”
[EN] An old man walked over and scolded them: "You women gather together and wag your tongues nonstop—careful His Lordship the Prefect hears and hauls you off for a beating!"
[ZH] 买菜的妇人说：“知府大人哪会来这种卖菜的地儿？我等小民说些闲话，他才不会知道。”
[EN] The buyer scoffed, "When does the Prefect ever set foot in a vegetable market? He'll never hear a word of our chatter."
[ZH] “老光棍，你还有脸说我们，昨个，你还说把朱家千金说得越臭越好，没人要，你就上门提亲，到时知府大人会把他的女儿当废弃的菜叶子一样扔给你，正好便宜你，呵呵……！”卖菜的妇人拿起一片腐烂的菜叶，在老光棍面前晃晃。
[EN] "You old bachelor, what nerve lecturing us! Just yesterday you said the fouler her name gets, the better—when nobody wants her, you'll go propose, and the Prefect will toss his daughter to you like wilted greens! Cheap bargain for you, haha!" The vendor waved a rotting cabbage leaf under the old bachelor's nose.

#CH 108
[ZH] 乔文仙已听不下去，面带怒色，冲到跟前，大喊：“你们一派胡言！淑真不是这样的人！再胡说！我就掀了你的菜摊！”
[EN] Unable to stomach another word, Qiao Wenxian stormed up, face dark with rage, and shouted, "Every word out of your mouths is garbage! Shuzhen isn't like that! Say one more word and I'll flip your vegetable stall!"
[ZH] 卖菜妇人被吓坏：“不敢了！不敢了！公子可别掀我的菜摊。”
[EN] Terrified, the vendor shrank back: "No more! I won't say another word! Don't overturn my stall, Young Master!"
[ZH] 买菜妇人提稳菜篮边逃离边嘀咕：“怎么突然出现个疯子？”
[EN] Clutching her basket, the buyer fled, muttering, "Where did this lunatic pop out from?"
[ZH] 老光棍并不怕，一脸调侃的表情：“看来公子与朱淑真认识，她不会是对你暗送秋波？是不是也睡倒在你的怀里？……”
[EN] Unfazed, the old bachelor sneered, "Looks like Young Master knows Zhu Shuzhen! Did she toss secret glances at you too? Fall asleep in your arms too?"
[ZH] 乔文仙一把抓住老光棍的衣领：“再胡说！我撕烂你的嘴！”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian seized the old bachelor by the collar. "Say one more word and I'll rip your mouth apart!"
[ZH] 老光棍唾沫星子乱飞：“你光对我横有什么用？整条街上的人都在议论，不信你到处听听！”
[EN] Spittle flying, the old bachelor barked back, "What's the use bullying me? Every soul on this street is saying the same thing—go listen for yourself if you don't believe me!"
[ZH] 乔文仙用厌恶的眼神向周围瞅去，所有人对他指指点点，口中不知说着什么……
[EN] Qiao Wenxian whipped his head around in disgust. People on all sides were pointing at him, whispering behind their hands...
[ZH] 他可以打一个骂一堆，却堵不住悠悠众口。一伤心，手劲儿变小，老光棍趁机逃开。
[EN] He could punch one man and curse a dozen, yet he could never silence a whole city's tongues. Heartbreak drained the strength from his fingers, and the old bachelor slipped free and ran.
[ZH] 乔文仙踉跄着，走在大街上，像只霜打的茄子。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian stumbled down the street, wilted like a frostbitten eggplant.
[ZH] 难道他与淑真再也没有机会在一起？曾经幻想两人白头偕老的场景难道只会成为幻想？
[EN] Were he and Shuzhen never going to be together? Would all his dreams of growing old beside her remain nothing but empty dreams?
[ZH] 乔文仙决定去找淑真，她在朱府肯定不好过。
[EN] He decided to go find Shuzhen—she had to be suffering inside Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 南双劝他坐马车，可乔文仙不愿意，只有将身体走得疲惫，心里才不会太难受。
[EN] Nan Shuang urged him to hire a carriage, yet Qiao Wenxian refused; only walking until his legs gave out could numb the pain in his chest.
[ZH] 待他到达朱府，门口的下人告诉他：“乔公子来晚了，我家姑娘已被老爷赶出家门。”
[EN] When he reached Zhu Manor, a gatekeeper informed him, "Young Master Qiao came too late. Master has already driven our Young Lady out of the house."
[ZH] “她去了哪里？”
[EN] "Where did she go?"
[ZH] 下人摇摇头：“不知道。”
[EN] The servant shook his head. "No idea."
[ZH] 乔文仙难过不已，淑真遭遇家人所弃，往后的日子该怎么过？他能想象到她离开朱府时哭成梨花带雨的模样。
[EN] Grief crashed over Qiao Wenxian. Cast out by her own family, how would Shuzhen get by? He could picture her walking out of Zhu Manor with tears streaming down her cheeks like rain on pear blossoms.
[ZH] 越想乔文仙的内心越痛，淑真被赶出家门，他们的婚事再没长辈做主，以后两人如何能够名正言顺在一起？
[EN] The more he imagined it, the sharper his chest ached. With her cast out and no elders to arrange their marriage, how could they ever be wed with honor?
[ZH] 乔文仙打算找知府大人，向他表明娶淑真的决心。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian meant to go see the Prefect and swear his resolve to marry Shuzhen.
[ZH] 门口的下人将他拦住：“老爷吩咐，乔家人来一律不许进。”
[EN] The gatekeeper blocked his path: "Master's orders—no one from the Qiao family is allowed inside."
[ZH] 乔文仙艰难退出，该怎么办？他想到仪王，只能继续向他求助。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian backed away with heavy steps. What could he do now? Only one person came to mind: Prince Yi. He had to beg him for help again.
[ZH] 回幽林别居肯定不能再依赖双脚，身边没有乔家马车跟着，南双在街上租辆马车，载着他们往城外而去。
[EN] He could not walk all the way back to Youlin Retreat on foot. With no Qiao carriage nearby, Nan Shuang hired a carriage on the street to carry them beyond the city gates.
[ZH] 一路上，公子一言不发。
[EN] All the way out, his master said not a single word.
[ZH] 南双也为公子与幽栖居士感到难过，他想不通，为何大家都在议论幽栖居士？还把她议论得那么难听？
[EN] Nan Shuang felt heartbroken for his master and Youqi Jushi too. He could not fathom why everyone was talking about her—and saying such ugly things.
[ZH] “公子，该不会是有人想破坏提亲，故意散播谣言？”
[EN] "Young Master, could someone have spread those rumors on purpose to wreck the proposal?"
[ZH] 公子不言语，仿佛身边的一切与他毫无关系。
[EN] His master stayed silent, deaf to the world around him.
[ZH] 南双继续猜测：“据我估计，应该是喜欢公子的人，公子若向幽栖居士提亲，喜欢公子的人以后可就没机会，才想出这么损的招数。”
[EN] Nan Shuang kept guessing: "My bet is it's some girl who likes you, Young Master. If you proposed to Youqi Jushi, she'd lose her shot, so she cooked up this dirty trick."
[ZH] 乔文仙依然不言语，谁诋毁淑真对他来讲根本不重要，他只关心两人以后该怎么办。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian still did not answer. Whoever slandered Shuzhen didn't matter to him right now—all he cared about was what the two of them would do next.
[ZH] “只怪公子太优秀，乔家又是皇亲国戚，多少姑娘盯着。公子别难过，事情总会过去。”南双边猜测边安慰。
[EN] "Blame it on you being too outstanding, Young Master, and the Qiao family being imperial kin—so many ladies have their eyes on you. Cheer up, this will blow over." Nan Shuang kept trying to comfort him as he speculated.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 自从与千里寻在神柳下相会之后，申简辰一直未走出幽林别居，活动范围仅限于幽林别居周围，最远到过神柳下。
[EN] Ever since meeting Qianli Xun beneath the Divine Willow, Shen Jianchen had not stepped out of Youlin Retreat, keeping his walks to the grounds nearby, going no further than the willow.
[ZH] 今日乔家要向朱家提亲，申简辰才发现躲着不见千里寻，根本不是忘记她的好办法，她与文渊的婚事即将成为事实，他变得更加难过。
[EN] Today was the Qiao family's proposal day, and Shen Jianchen discovered that hiding from Qianli Xun did nothing to help him forget her. With her marriage to Wenyuan about to become fact, the ache inside him only deepened.
[ZH] 从文渊一早出门，申简辰就窝在屋里不出来。午饭时，白风把饭摆好，他实在没胃口，筷子根本未拿起，白风又原封不动端出去。
[EN] Since Wenyuan left at dawn, Shen Jianchen had holed up in his room. At noon Bai Feng laid out lunch, yet he had zero appetite and never lifted his chopsticks, leaving Bai Feng to carry the dishes back untouched.
[ZH] 乔梦容来过几次，被拒之门外。他不愿让妹妹看到他心里念着其他女子的落魄样，更害怕见到梦容会忍不住说出让她伤心的话。只说要在屋里好好想想临安的军政大事，不想被打扰。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong stopped by several times, only to be turned away at the door. He could not bear letting her see him moping over another woman, and feared seeing her would push harsh words out of his mouth. He only claimed he was busy thinking through Lin'an's military and civil affairs and wanted no interruptions.
[ZH] 正当申简辰舔舐个人的痛苦时，门被推开，文渊失魂落魄地跪在他面前，带着哭腔求道：“仪王，帮帮我，只有你能帮我……”
[EN] Just as Shen Jianchen sat nursing his own misery, the door flew open. Wenyuan stumbled in, dropped to his knees before him, and begged in a choked voice: "Prince Yi, help me—only you can help me..."
[ZH] 申简辰感到十分疑惑：“怎么了？今日不是提亲吗？”
[EN] Startled, Shen Jianchen asked, "What happened? Isn't today the proposal?"
[ZH] 文渊难过得说不出口。
[EN] Wenyuan was too choked with grief to get the words out.
[ZH] 南双替公子说：“仪王有所不知，外面全是关于幽栖居士的流言蜚语，说得可难听，老爷知道后不再去朱家提亲，连幽栖居士也被知府大人赶出家门，不知去向。”
[EN] Nan Shuang spoke up for his master: "Prince Yi doesn't know—the streets are flooded with filthy rumors about Youqi Jushi! When Master found out, he called off the proposal to the Zhu family, and the Prefect even threw Youqi Jushi out of the house—nobody knows where she went!"
[ZH] “什么？”申简辰立刻明白，也来了精神，他更担心千里寻，“竟然发生这等事？”
[EN] "What?!" Realization snapped Shen Jianchen straight to attention, fear for Qianli Xun surging through him. "Something like that happened?"
[ZH] 文渊哭着说：“仪王，你帮帮我，不能跟淑真在一起，我活着还有什么意思！”
[EN] Weeping, Wenyuan cried, "Help me, Prince Yi! If I can't be with Shuzhen, what's the point of living?!"
[ZH] 申简辰上前扶起文渊：“别难过，安心待在别居，等我了解完情况再作决定。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stepped forward and hauled Wenyuan to his feet. "Pull yourself together. Stay here at the retreat while I find out what's going on and decide what to do."
[ZH] 乔文仙抱住仪王：“你一定要帮我再次说服父亲，他只听你的。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian wrapped his arms around Prince Yi. "You have to persuade my father again! He only listens to you!"
[ZH] 申简辰安慰道：“我这就去乔府见外公。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen reassured him: "I'm heading to Qiao Manor to see Grandfather right now."
[ZH] 乔文仙把仪王抱得更紧：“你是天底下最好的王爷，等事成之后，我给你送块匾额。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian hugged him tighter. "You're the finest prince under heaven! Once this works out, I'll hang an inscribed plaque in your honor!"
[ZH] “好了，别再抱着我，你抱着我，我怎么去乔府？”申简辰认为他的心里一定是出了问题，又想帮文渊，又庆幸提亲未成。
[EN] "All right, let go of me—how am I supposed to get to Qiao Manor with you clinging to me?" Shen Jianchen thought something had to be twisted in his own head: half of him wanted to help Wenyuan, while the other half felt glad the proposal had fallen through.
[ZH] 乔文仙放开仪王，擦擦脸上的泪痕。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian released him and wiped the tear streaks from his cheeks.
[ZH] 申简辰吩咐道：“南双，带你家公子去休息，顺便把白风找来。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen ordered, "Nan Shuang, take your master to rest, and go fetch Bai Feng."
[ZH] 白风就在门外站着，听到王爷找他，立即走进屋里：“王爷不用找我，我一直在外面守着。”
[EN] Standing right outside the door, Bai Feng stepped in at once. "No need to fetch me, Your Highness—I've been waiting right outside."
[ZH] “套上马车，随我去乔府。”
[EN] "Hitch the carriage. We're going to Qiao Manor."
[ZH] “太好了！王爷终于不宅在屋里！我也可以走出幽林别居。”
[EN] "Awesome! Your Highness is leaving the house at last! I get to step out of Youlin Retreat too!"
[ZH] “少废话！赶紧的！”
[EN] "Less chatter—get moving!"
[ZH] 乔文仙见仪王带白风离开，又对提亲之事充满希望。
[EN] Watching Prince Yi leave with Bai Feng, hope flickered back to life in Qiao Wenxian's eyes.
[ZH] 去乔府的路上，白风边赶马车边说：“王爷，我已经一个多月没见苦荞，今日能不能让我见见她？”
[EN] On the road to Qiao Manor, Bai Feng asked from the driver's box, "Your Highness, I haven't seen Kuqiao in over a month. Can I see her today?"
[ZH] 何止白风，申简辰也一个多月未见千里寻，发生这样的事，不知她可好？南双说她被赶出朱府，也不知去了哪里？
[EN] Not just Bai Feng—Shen Jianchen hadn't seen Qianli Xun in over a month either. With all this crashing down on her, how was she holding up? Nan Shuang said she'd been thrown out of Zhu Manor; where had she gone?
[ZH] “当然可以，只要找到幽栖居士，自然见到苦荞。”
[EN] "Sure. Once we find Youqi Jushi, you'll see Kuqiao."
[ZH] “找到？难道幽栖居士失踪了？”
[EN] "Find her? Did Youqi Jushi go missing?"
[ZH] “别问那么多，尽快赶马车去乔府。”
[EN] "Stop asking questions and drive faster to Qiao Manor."
[ZH] 白风吐吐舌头，这位王爷整日都不去府衙，也不关心政务，倒是对乔公子的事上心，真是个奇怪的王爷。
[EN] Bai Feng stuck out his tongue. Here was a prince who never visited the prefecture office and cared nothing for government business, yet threw himself into Young Master Qiao's romance—what an odd prince.
[ZH] 马车进城之后，申简辰直奔乔府。
[EN] Once inside the city gates, Shen Jianchen went straight to Qiao Manor.
[ZH] 乔守仁听到仪王前来，带领钱氏和米氏出门迎接。
[EN] Hearing Prince Yi had arrived, Qiao Shouren led Madam Qian and Concubine Mi out to welcome him.
[ZH] 仪王被迎入乔府厅堂，没等仪王问话，乔守仁直接问：“仪王可是为提亲之事而来？”
[EN] Shown into the main hall, Qiao Shouren beat Prince Yi to the punch: "Did Prince Yi come over the proposal?"
[ZH] 申简辰点点头：“文渊说提亲未成，我过来问问外公，难道仅仅是因为外面关于朱家姑娘的谣言，外公才不愿到朱家提亲？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded. "Wenyuan told me it fell through. I came to ask Grandfather—are you calling off the proposal to Zhu Manor over nothing more than street rumors about Miss Zhu?"
[ZH] 乔守仁回道：“正是，上次仪王出面已将我说服，乔家也心甘情愿与朱家联姻，谁知外面把朱家姑娘传得实在难听，乔家怎能娶如此品行不端的女子过门？”
[EN] Qiao Shouren answered, "Precisely. Last time Prince Yi talked me round, and the Qiao family agreed to the match with all our hearts. Who knew folk outside were spreading such foul talk about Miss Zhu? How could the Qiao house take in a bride of such disrepute?"
[ZH] “外公有没有想过只是谣言而已。”
[EN] "Has Grandfather considered they're just rumors?"
[ZH] “我当然想过，可苍蝇不叮无缝的蛋，为何谣言不说别人，专说朱家姑娘？也是她本人有问题。”
[EN] "Of course I have—yet flies don't buzz around a seamless egg! Why didn't rumors target anyone else, only Miss Zhu? Clearly she brought it on herself."
[ZH] 申简辰听得出来，外公铁了心不再向朱家提亲。为检验他的态度是否坚决，故意说：“文渊心里只有幽栖居士，他甚至说，没有幽栖居士，活着也没多大意思。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen heard the iron resolve in his grandfather's tone. To test how firm he was, he probed: "Wenyuan has eyes only for Youqi Jushi. He even said life has no meaning without her."
[ZH] 米氏急了：“老爷，文渊都说出此话，该怎么办？”
[EN] Concubine Mi panicked: "Master, if Wenyuan is talking like that, what do we do?!"
[ZH] 乔守仁批评道：“你个妇道人家，我与仪王说话，哪有你插话的份儿？还不赶紧退下！”
[EN] Qiao Shouren barked at her: "Know your place! When I'm speaking with Prince Yi, who gave you leave to butt in? Withdraw at once!"
[ZH] 米氏一脸不满，拜别仪王，带丫鬟离开。
[EN] Displeasure flashing across her face, Concubine Mi bowed to Prince Yi and left with her maid.
[ZH] 乔守仁才说道：“仪王不必把文渊的话当回事，他还年轻，难免会说些痴情的话，等娶了亲，自然会好。”
[EN] Only then did Qiao Shouren say, "Don't take Wenyuan's words to heart, Prince Yi. He's young and bound to talk like a lovesick fool; once he marries, he'll get over it."
[ZH] 申简辰继续试探：“如若我为文渊到官家跟前请一道赐婚的圣旨……”
[EN] Shen Jianchen pushed further: "What if I went to His Majesty and asked for an imperial marriage decree for Wenyuan..."
[ZH] 仪王话未说完，乔守仁立刻起身跪下，钱氏也跟着跪下。
[EN] Before he could finish, Qiao Shouren shot up and dropped to his knees, with Madam Qian kneeling beside him.
[ZH] “若仪王要请圣旨，我即刻撞死在仪王面前，就是拼了这条老命，也不允许朱家姑娘进乔家的门！”
[EN] "If Prince Yi asks for an imperial decree, I'll smash my head open right in front of you! Even at the cost of this old life, I will never let Miss Zhu step across the Qiao threshold!"
[ZH] 申简辰站起来，叹口气：“起来吧，此事是你们乔家的事，以后我不会再插手。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen rose and let out a sigh. "Get up. This is Qiao family business—I won't meddle in it again."
[ZH] 乔守仁松口气，与钱氏起身，对着仪王的背影齐声道：“恭送仪王！”
[EN] Exhaling in relief, Qiao Shouren and Madam Qian climbed to their feet and called out to Prince Yi's retreating back in unison: "We humbly see Prince Yi off!"

#CH 109
[ZH] 乔守仁态度坚决，申简辰没必要多加劝说，再说劝说无益。
[EN] With Qiao Shouren digging in his heels, further persuasion was pointless.
[ZH] 更重要的是申简辰不想继续劝说，既然老天让提亲之事出现阻碍，说明文渊不是千里寻的最终归宿，他申简辰还有希望。
[EN] More important, Shen Jianchen did not want to keep persuading him. If Heaven itself threw roadblocks in front of this proposal, maybe Wenyuan was not Qianli Xun's final destination—maybe Shen Jianchen still had a shot.
[ZH] 带着希望，申简辰吩咐白风赶马车去朱府。
[EN] With fresh hope stirring, Shen Jianchen told Bai Feng to drive to Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 把女儿赶出家门，朱晞颜去了趟府衙。独自坐在府衙内，思索该找谁查传播谣言之人？
[EN] After driving his daughter out, Zhu Xiyan had gone to the prefecture office. Sitting alone in his study, he mulled over whom to send after the rumor-mongers.
[ZH] 此事乃朱家私事，不宜动用官府之人。可朱家的下人伺候人尚可，查找传播谣言之人，能力尚欠缺。
[EN] This was private Zhu family business—not something to mobilize official yamen runners for. Yet Zhu Manor's servants were good enough for waiting tables, useless for tracking down schemers.
[ZH] 朱晞颜想到林觉，既是曾经的女婿，又听话，关键是手下有士兵，办事极其方便。于是他又出府衙，往练兵署而去。
[EN] Then Zhu Xiyan thought of Lin Jue: a former son-in-law, eager to please, and best of all, with soldiers under his command who could track leads with ease. Leaving the prefecture office, he headed straight for the Military Training Office.
[ZH] 到达练兵署，在士兵的引领下，来到统领办公之地，正好碰到副都统制从里面出来。
[EN] Guided by a soldier to the Commandant's quarters, he ran right into the Vice Commander-in-Chief stepping out.
[ZH] 副都统制见知府前来，甚至恭敬：“知府大人。”
[EN] Seeing the Prefect arrive, the Vice Commander-in-Chief bowed with utmost deference. "Lord Prefect."
[ZH] 朱晞颜寒暄道：“好久不见，副都统制大人近来可好？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan exchanged pleasantries: "Long time no see. How has the Vice Commander-in-Chief been keeping?"
[ZH] “还是老样子，公务繁忙，知府大人最清楚不过。”
[EN] "Same as ever—buried in official duties, as Lord Prefect knows all too well."
[ZH] “是啊，我等虽为地方官员，也是为朝廷效力，难得有清闲时间。”
[EN] "Indeed. Local officials like us serve the court; idle moments are scarce."
[ZH] “不知知府大人前来有何指教？”
[EN] "May I ask what brings Lord Prefect here?"
[ZH] “哦，我找林统领有些事。”
[EN] "Oh, I have a word for Commandant Lin."
[ZH] “林统领正在里面，知府大人请便，若有任何吩咐只管派人告知下官。”
[EN] "Commandant Lin is right inside. Please go ahead, Lord Prefect—if you need anything at all, just send word to me."
[ZH] 朱晞颜满意地点点头，管理军政的官员虽不直接受他管辖，见到他倒也恭敬。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan nodded in satisfaction. Even military officers outside his direct chain of command treated him with full respect.
[ZH] 他走进统领办公之地，林觉正在处理公务，抬头见曾经的岳丈前来，起身迎上去：“知府大人突然光临，实乃意外之喜，快上座。”
[EN] Stepping into the Commandant's office, he found Lin Jue working at his desk. Looking up to see his former father-in-law, Lin Jue jumped up to greet him: "Lord Prefect's sudden visit is a pleasant surprise! Please take the seat of honor!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜对林觉的表现甚感欣慰，这位曾经的女婿最大的优点就是对他够恭敬。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan felt pleased; his former son-in-law's biggest virtue was always his deference.
[ZH] 坐下后，林觉吩咐士兵上茶，然后恭顺地站立一旁等待发话。
[EN] Once Zhu Xiyan sat, Lin Jue ordered a soldier to serve tea, then stood waiting at attention.
[ZH] 待士兵上完茶退出后，朱晞颜看一眼旁边的士兵。
[EN] After the soldier served tea and left, Zhu Xiyan glanced at the guards still by the door.
[ZH] 林觉猜到朱晞颜前来所说之事不想被他人知晓，于是吩咐道：“你们都退下。”
[EN] Catching the hint that this was confidential, Lin Jue waved them off: "All of you, step outside."
[ZH] 士兵退出后，林觉将门关好，谨慎道：“已安全，知府大人请讲。”
[EN] Once they withdrew, Lin Jue shut the door and said with care, "All clear now. Please speak, Lord Prefect."
[ZH] 朱晞颜先恭喜道：“一段时日未见，你已从练兵官一跃成为统领，可喜可贺。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan offered congratulations first: "Only a short while since we met, and you've leaped from Training Officer to Commandant. Well done."
[ZH] 林觉谦虚道：“主要是节度使与诸位大人信任，才选择下官。”
[EN] Lin Jue replied with modesty, "All thanks to the trust of the Military Commissioner and my superiors."
[ZH] “节度使大人也知此事？”朱晞颜感到疑惑，仪王从不参与政事，林觉被提拔之事又怎与他有关？
[EN] "The Military Commissioner was involved?" Zhu Xiyan blinked in surprise. Prince Yi never touched civil or military affairs—how had he backed Lin Jue's promotion?
[ZH] 林觉笑笑说：“知府大人有所不知，有一日，节度使大人前来给士兵训话，下官正好在，接待了贵人，其他几位大人随后也都纷纷拜见，正好统领的位置空缺，大家都推举我，节度使大人也无不认可。”
[EN] Lin Jue smiled. "Lord Prefect wouldn't know. One day the Military Commissioner came by to address the troops, and I happened to be on hand to host him. The senior commanders hurried over to pay respects too; with the Commandant's post vacant, they all recommended me, and the Military Commissioner approved."
[ZH] “哦，原来是这么回事。”朱晞颜不想再问下去，还是尽快说正事，“本官来找你，是想麻烦你件事。”
[EN] "Ah, so that's how it happened." Unwilling to linger on small talk, Zhu Xiyan moved to business. "I came to ask a favor of you."
[ZH] “知府大人尽管说，只要下官能力所及，定当尽力而为。”林觉虽已放下朱淑真，可这位曾经的岳丈他可不想放弃，在官场混，不能没有靠山。
[EN] "Say the word, Lord Prefect—anything within my power, I'll give my all." Even though Lin Jue had let Zhu Shuzhen go, he was not about to let go of his former father-in-law; nobody climbed in officialdom without backing.
[ZH] 毕竟是麻烦林觉，朱晞颜不好让他一直恭顺地站着：“来，坐下说。”
[EN] Since he was asking a favor, Zhu Xiyan could not keep Lin Jue standing. "Come, sit down and talk."
[ZH] 林觉乖乖坐下。
[EN] Lin Jue sat down at once.
[ZH] 待林觉坐好后，朱晞颜犹豫片刻说道：“我找你是为私事，你肯定已听到关于淑真的谣言，我想麻烦你派些人手，帮我查查谣言是谁人传出。”
[EN] Once he was seated, Zhu Xiyan paused a beat before saying, "I came on a private matter. You must have heard the rumors about Shuzhen. I want you to send men to find out who started them."
[ZH] 提到谣言，林觉心情复杂。既觉传谣之人实在可恶，连林家都牵扯其中。又觉朱淑真被大家非议是罪有应得，谁让她嫁到林家不好好跟他过日子，一天尽想些风流才子，刚和离就扑到乔文仙怀里，实在有失大家闺秀的身份。
[EN] Mention of the rumors stirred mixed feelings in Lin Jue. On one hand, the rumor-monger was despicable for dragging the Lin family into the mud too; on the other, Zhu Shuzhen had it coming—refusing to live a quiet wife's life in the Lin house, pining for dashing scholars all day, and throwing herself into Qiao Wenxian's arms the second they divorced. Hardly noble-lady conduct.
[ZH] 知府大人为此事而来，林觉心有不满，为了前程，还是一口应下：“知府大人放心，我定派人彻查此事，您就等我的好消息。”
[EN] Annoyed as he felt, his career came first, so he agreed on the spot: "Rest easy, Lord Prefect. I'll send men to investigate from top to bottom—just wait for my good news."
[ZH] 朱晞颜说：“你虽与淑真和离，可在我眼里依然把你当自家孩子看待，以后要是看上哪家姑娘，尽管告诉我，我出面替你说合。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan said, "Even though you and Shuzhen divorced, I still think of you as my own boy. Whenever a young lady catches your eye, tell me, and I'll speak on your behalf."
[ZH] 林觉心想，他想要个丫鬟，朱晞颜都解决不了，还请仪王出面劝说。现在用上他，又说这种客套话，要不是看在他是临安知府，官场上多少会帮到自己，他才不会如此恭敬。
[EN] Lin Jue scoffed to himself: when he wanted a single maid, Zhu Xiyan couldn't handle it and sicced Prince Yi on him! Now that he needed a favor, out came the sweet talk. If not for the Prefect's clout in officialdom, he wouldn't bother bowing and scraping.
[ZH] “感谢知府大人关怀，若有中意的，定然告知。”
[EN] "Thank you for your kindness, Lord Prefect. If someone catches my eye, I'll be sure to let you know."
[ZH] 朱晞颜点头微笑，很快又笑容全无，用平静的语气说：“我没把淑真教育好，出了这等事，我已将她赶出家门，以后她与朱府再无关系。但谣言还在，我绝不允许有人故意诋毁朱家清誉，此事林统领若帮朱家办成，不出一年，统制的位置定然花落林家。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan smiled, then let his smile vanish into a cool, steady tone: "I failed to raise Shuzhen right. After this scandal, I cast her out of Zhu Manor; she has no ties to us anymore. Yet the rumors remain, and I will not tolerate anyone targeting the Zhu family's good name. Pull this off for us, Commandant Lin, and within a year the Division Commander's post will be yours."
[ZH] 又有升职机会，林觉自然高兴，立刻起身，更加恭顺道：“知府大人放心，朱家的事，也就是我的事。”
[EN] Another promotion dangling right in front of him! Thrilled, Lin Jue sprang up and bowed even lower: "Rest easy, Lord Prefect! The Zhu family's business is my business!"
[ZH] “很好，一旦查到任何线索，直接到朱府告诉我。”
[EN] "Good. The moment you find a lead, come straight to Zhu Manor and report to me."
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] "Yes, sir!"
[ZH] 办完事，朱晞颜回到朱府，还未换官服，又将儿子朱景元叫到跟前，让他找些下人装扮成喝茶的，没事待在茶馆里查探谣言来源。下人虽作用不大，但朱晞颜觉还得派些人出去，能探多少探多少。
[EN] Back at Zhu Manor, before even changing out of his official robes, Zhu Xiyan summoned Zhu Jingyuan and told him to dress several servants as ordinary tea-drinkers to hang around teahouses fishing for leads. Servants might not turn up much, yet every extra pair of ears helped.
[ZH] 刚安排好，有人来报：“老爷，仪王前来拜访，正在门外候着。”
[EN] No sooner had he arranged it than a servant ran in to report: "Master, Prince Yi is at the gate asking to see you!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜整理好官服，带上儿子匆忙向府外走去。来到侧门时，见仪王站在门外，上前拜道：“下官拜见仪王殿下，不知殿下驾到，若有不到之处，还请见谅。”
[EN] Straightening his robes, Zhu Xiyan hurried out with his son. Reaching the side gate, he saw Prince Yi waiting outside and stepped forward to bow. "This humble official greets Your Highness Prince Yi. Forgive me for failing to welcome you sooner!"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“我来得匆忙，没有时间提前通知知府大人，还请知府大人不要介意。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said, "I came in a rush with no time to send word ahead—don't mind it, Lord Prefect."
[ZH] “仪王能来拜访，朱府蓬荜生辉，又怎敢介意？仪王若不嫌弃，请到府内坐坐。”
[EN] "Prince Yi's visit brings honor to Zhu Manor! If Your Highness doesn't disdain our humble home, please step inside for a seat."
[ZH] “今日我还有事，改日再到府上叨扰。”
[EN] "I have other business today; I'll impose another time."
[ZH] 朱晞颜纳闷，仪王来了，又不愿进府，不知是为何事？于是恭敬地问：“不知仪王前来有何吩咐？”
[EN] Puzzled why Prince Yi had come without stepping inside, Zhu Xiyan asked with deference, "May I ask what instructions Your Highness has for me?"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“我从乔府而来，两家之事已清楚，希望知府大人不要因此对乔家不满。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said, "I just came from Qiao Manor and heard what happened between the two houses. I hope Lord Prefect won't hold it against the Qiao family."
[ZH] 原来是为此事，朱晞颜怎能对乔家没有意见？可当着仪王的面，他万万不会承认：“怪不得他人，只怪我没把自家闺女教育好。此事惊动仪王，下官惶恐。”
[EN] So that was it! How could Zhu Xiyan not resent the Qiaos? Yet before Prince Yi, he would never admit it. "No one to blame but myself for raising my daughter poorly. Having this trouble Prince Yi fills me with shame."
[ZH] “知府大人不必自责，依我看您把女儿教育得很好，至于外面的谣言，知府大人不必太当回事。”
[EN] "Don't blame yourself, Lord Prefect. In my eyes, you raised a fine daughter. As for the street gossip, don't pay it too much mind."
[ZH] 在全城百姓诋毁朱家姑娘的情况下，仪王如此替朱家说话，朱晞颜感激不尽，跪在地上：“仪王圣明！”
[EN] With the whole city tearing his daughter apart, hearing Prince Yi speak up for the Zhu family overwhelmed Zhu Xiyan. He dropped to his knees. "Your Highness is wise and gracious!"
[ZH] 申简辰扶起朱晞颜：“知府大人快起来，我是替文渊而来，他告诉我您把幽栖居士赶出去，不知她搬去哪里？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen helped him up. "Get up, Lord Prefect. I came on Wenyuan's behalf. He told me you sent Youqi Jushi away—where did she move?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜竟然感动得流下一滴泪，仪王不仅为朱家说话，还帮乔家那小子打听淑真，说明乔家那小子对淑真的确有真情，他的眼泪也是为淑真高兴而流。
[EN] A tear of genuine emotion slipped down Zhu Xiyan's cheek. Not only did Prince Yi defend the Zhu house, he was tracking down Shuzhen for young Qiao—proving that boy really did love Shuzhen! His tear was pure relief for his daughter.
[ZH] 朱景元见父亲激动，半天不言语，主动说：“仪王殿下，淑真新买的宅子在城东，叫望月轩。”
[EN] Seeing his father too moved to speak, Zhu Jingyuan stepped in: "Your Highness, the new house Shuzhen bought is in the east of the city—it's called Wangyue Pavilion."
[ZH] “知府大人，我还有事，改日再聊。”已大听到重要信息，申简辰不想耽搁下去，匆忙登上马车，命令白风去城东。
[EN] "I have errands to run, Lord Prefect. We'll talk another day." Having got the vital piece of news, Shen Jianchen wasted no time, climbing back into his carriage and ordering Bai Feng to head east.
[ZH] 朱晞颜只觉这位仪王来得匆忙也走得匆忙。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan watched him go, marveling at how fast this prince came and went.
[ZH] 不过仪王说的一席话，令朱晞颜的心情好了许多，世间总有像仪王这等明理之人。
[EN] Still, Prince Yi's words lifted a boulder from his chest. At least there were still reasonable men like Prince Yi in this world.
[ZH] 马车行到闹市，申简辰让白风把车停下，他打算买件礼物送给千里寻。
[EN] Passing through a busy market, Shen Jianchen told Bai Feng to pull over—he wanted to buy a gift for Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 她被百姓非议，又被赶出朱府，与文渊的婚事也泡汤，心情肯定不好。送个礼物给她，心情应该会好些。
[EN] Mocked by the whole city, thrown out of Zhu Manor, her marriage to Wenyuan blown to pieces—she had to be miserable. A little gift might cheer her up.
[ZH] 申简辰从未给女孩子买过礼物，他感到为难。一下马车，问道：“白风，你说女孩子都喜欢什么礼物？”
[EN] Never having bought a gift for a girl before, Shen Jianchen felt stumped. Stepping down, he asked, "Bai Feng, what kind of gifts do girls like?"
[ZH] “王爷要买给佳和郡主吗？”
[EN] "Is Your Highness buying for Princess Jiahe?"
[ZH] “别瞎问，你只需告诉我送女孩子什么礼物她会高兴？”
[EN] "Quit guessing—just tell me what gift makes a girl happy."
[ZH] 白风想想说：“我给苦荞送的是泥人，她挺开心的。”
[EN] Bai Feng thought a second. "I gave Kuqiao a clay figurine, and she loved it."
[ZH] “那就送给泥人吧。”
[EN] "A clay figurine it is, then."
[ZH] 正好瞧见有家店铺售卖泥人，两人走进去，里面摆放的泥人活灵活现，形态各异。
[EN] Spotting a clay figurine shop right nearby, the two stepped inside, surrounded by shelves of lifelike painted figures in every pose imaginable.
[ZH] 伙计见有人前来，迎上去，露出殷勤的表情：“客官里边请，我家店各色泥人均有，您尽管挑。”
[EN] A clerk hurried over with an eager grin. "Right this way, gentlemen! We carry every kind of clay figurine—take your pick!"
[ZH] 申简辰四下瞅瞅，将目光停留在撒尿娃娃的身上，小娃娃圆眼圆嘴圆脑袋，做出撒尿姿势，实在可爱，只看一眼，便觉开心，绝对能将千里寻逗乐。
[EN] Scanning the shelves, Shen Jianchen's gaze locked onto a peeing-boy figurine: round eyes, round mouth, round head, mid-pee—so cheeky and adorable that one glance made him grin. It was guaranteed to crack Qianli Xun up.
[ZH] “伙计，把那个泥人装起来。”
[EN] "Clerk, wrap that figurine up."
[ZH] 白风一瞅，实在想不通，王爷为何要送佳和郡主一个撒尿娃娃？要送也应该送一对喜娃娃，既温馨，寓意又好。
[EN] One look at it left Bai Feng baffled. Why on earth would His Highness give Princess Jiahe a peeing boy? Shouldn't he get a pair of lucky wedding dolls—sweet and auspicious?

#CH 110
[ZH] 伙计取下撒尿娃娃，找来一个小木盒包装之际，进来两位姑娘，她们边选泥人边聊天儿。
[EN] While the clerk fetched a small wooden box to pack the peeing-boy figurine, two young women strolled in, chatting as they browsed the clay dolls.
[ZH] “这家店铺的泥人真不错，你说我买哪个送给他比较好？”
[EN] "This shop's clay figurines are wonderful! Which one should I buy for him?"
[ZH] “真不知羞，小心到时大家都像议论幽栖居士一样议论你。”
[EN] "Have you no shame? Careful folks don't start gossiping about you the way they do about Youqi Jushi!"
[ZH] “怎能将我与她相提并论？这可是爹娘给我定的亲，三媒六聘一样不少。”
[EN] "How can you lump me in with her? My parents arranged this betrothal, with every matchmaker and betrothal gift in place!"
[ZH] “唉，没想到幽栖居士诗词写得不错，做派实在令人堪忧。”
[EN] "Sigh. Who knew Youqi Jushi writes great poetry while her conduct leaves so much to be desired?"
[ZH] “可不是？闺中女子最重名节，可她竟如此大胆，既敢写到诗词里，还敢付诸行动，令我等闺阁女子汗颜，也不知与她暗送秋波，红杏出墙的男子会作何感想？……”
[EN] "Tell me about it! Nothing matters more to a maiden of the inner chambers than her chastity, yet she's bold enough to put it in verse and act on it, putting every sheltered young lady to shame! Makes you wonder what the men she traded secret glances and had affairs with think..."
[ZH] 申简辰实在听不下去，尤其听到“暗送秋波”四字，说的不就是他吗？
[EN] Shen Jianchen could not listen to another word. Hearing "traded secret glances"—wasn't that talking about him?
[ZH] 面对他秋波暗送，千里寻根本未放在眼里，申简辰又岂能任由两位姑娘随意诋毁她？
[EN] Qianli Xun had not spared his own yearning glances a second thought, so how could he let two strangers smear her?
[ZH] 他走到两位姑娘面前：“两位姑娘长得花容月貌，刚才的话与你们的容貌实在不搭，背后议论他人小心找不到婆家，即使找到，婆家知道未来的儿媳是长舌妇，恐后悔定下的亲事，说不定还会闹到悔婚。”
[EN] He marched right up to them. "Two young ladies blessed with faces fair as flowers—pity your words don't match your looks. Keep running your mouths behind people's backs and you might never find a husband's house. Even if you do, once your in-laws learn their future daughter-in-law is a gossipmonger, they'll regret the match and might even call off the wedding."
[ZH] 两位姑娘开始听到夸自己花容月貌，实在开心，可听到后面，着实生气。
[EN] Flattered at first by his praise of their looks, both girls bristled at the sting that followed.
[ZH] 一位姑娘不满道：“公子说我们两个有什么用？满大街人都在议论，难道满大街的姑娘都找不到婆家？”
[EN] One snapped, "What's the use lecturing us? Everyone on the street is talking about her! Are all the girls in Lin'an going to end up unwed?"
[ZH] 另一位姑娘随手拿个喜娃娃，递给伙计：“给我包上，今日真是晦气，碰到个爱管闲事的。”
[EN] The other grabbed a lucky-doll figurine and handed it to the clerk. "Wrap this up. What rotten luck today, running into a meddler!"
[ZH] 伙计接过喜娃娃，把装有撒尿娃娃的木盒递给白风。
[EN] Taking her doll, the clerk handed the wooden box holding the peeing boy to Bai Feng.
[ZH] 白风接过，付了银子，嫌弃两位姑娘对王爷不恭敬，批评道：“你们两个在背后议论别人小心第二天烂嘴巴！”
[EN] Bai Feng paid the silver, annoyed at how rude the girls had been to His Highness, and snapped back, "Keep badmouthing folks behind their backs and watch your mouths rot off tomorrow!"
[ZH] 两位姑娘更加生气，一位说：“我们议论幽栖居士关你们两个什么事儿？狗拿耗子多管闲事！”
[EN] Both girls flared up hotter, one barking, "What business is it of yours if we talk about Youqi Jushi? Dogs catching mice—mind your own business!"
[ZH] 白风大声骂道：“你们两个就是长舌妇！长舌妇！”
[EN] Bai Feng yelled back, "Gossipmongers! A pair of gossipmongers!"
[ZH] 申简辰并不管，谁让她们瞎胡议论他喜欢的人。
[EN] Shen Jianchen did not stop him; they had it coming for slandering the woman he loved.
[ZH] 伙计当起老好人：“几位几位，和气生财，和气生财。客官，两位姑娘议论别人是不对，可最近大家都议论，你们还能一个个去管？怪只怪知府家的千金做派太出阁，不守闺阁女子之礼，才招致他人非议。”
[EN] The clerk jumped in as peacemaker: "Now, now, folks—harmony brings wealth! Sir, it isn't right for the young ladies to gossip, sure, yet the whole city's buzzing right now. Can you police every last person? Blame the Prefect's daughter for crossing lines no sheltered lady should cross and inviting talk."
[ZH] 申简辰问：“难道连你也议论？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen asked, "You gossip about her too?"
[ZH] 伙计笑道：“那还用说？无人不议论，我还听说知府大人为维护朱家清誉，把女儿赶出家门。”
[EN] The clerk chuckled. "Who doesn't? Everybody's talking. Heard the Prefect even threw his own daughter out to save the Zhu family's name."
[ZH] 另一位姑娘拿过装好的泥人，继续不满道：“连自家爹都不要她，我们议论两句怎么啦？哼！”
[EN] Taking her wrapped figurine, the second girl snorted, "Even her own father doesn't want her—what's wrong with us saying two words? Hmph!"
[ZH] 两位姑娘付完钱，带着情绪离开。
[EN] Paying up, both girls flounced out in a huff.
[ZH] 申简辰越发担心千里寻，不知她现在难过成什么样？他觉得谣言甚是蹊跷，仿佛有人故意为之。
[EN] Dread deepened in Shen Jianchen's chest; how hard must Qianli Xun be hurting right now? Something about these rumors smelled fishy, as if engineered on purpose.
[ZH] “伙计，不知你是如何听到关于朱家姑娘的谣言？”
[EN] "Clerk, how did you hear these rumors about Miss Zhu?"
[ZH] “我们开店，来来往往的人多，听大家议论的多，自然知道。说句心里话，平日只顾忙店铺生意，哪有时间关心什么词人诗人之类，不怕客官笑话，我连一首诗词都不会，这倒好，记住一句‘和衣睡倒人怀’，呵呵，文人雅士跟我等俗人唯一的区别就是把见不得的事说得够文雅，呵呵……”
[EN] "Running a shop, folk stream in and out all day; hear enough talk and it sticks. Honest truth, I'm busy minding the counter and never cared a fig about poets. Don't laugh, sir, I don't know a single poem by heart, yet now even I remember 'falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed'! Haha—the only difference between refined scholars and common folk like us is dressing up dirty deeds in fancy words, haha..."
[ZH] 申简辰听不下去，没有做任何回应，出了店铺，登上马车，继续往城东行驶。
[EN] Unable to stomach another word, Shen Jianchen walked out without reply, boarded his carriage, and headed east.
[ZH] 他后悔一个多月来躲在幽林别居，如若早些到街上逛逛，也许会早些发现谣言，也可早些想办法制止，不至于传得人尽皆知。
[EN] He kicked himself for holing up in Youlin Retreat for over a month. Had he strolled the streets sooner, he might have caught the rumors early and snuffed them out before they swept the whole city.
[ZH] 可要不是谣言，提亲之事已成，千里寻已是文渊的人，他哪有买好礼物去找她的机会？
[EN] Yet without those rumors, the proposal would have gone through, Qianli Xun would belong to Wenyuan, and he would not be riding east right now with a gift in hand.
[ZH] 申简辰在自己脸上轻轻扇了一下，轻声自语道：“你怎能如此想？你是有婚约之人，文渊还痛苦着，千里寻肯定更不好受，你却高兴，实在不该！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen gave his own cheek a light slap and muttered under his breath, "How can you think like that? You're an engaged man! Wenyuan is suffering, Qianli Xun must be hurting worse, and here you are feeling glad—shame on you!"
[ZH] 可他还是忍不住不高兴，只为他与千里寻还有一丝希望而高兴。
[EN] Yet he could not crush that stubborn spark of gladness—glad that one sliver of hope still survived between him and Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 来到城东，很容易找到富人居住的区域，也轻松找到望月轩。
[EN] Reaching the east quarter, it took no effort to find the wealthy neighborhood and locate Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 望月轩比起朱府来小得不能再小，好在里面布置清雅。
[EN] Compared with Zhu Manor, Wangyue Pavilion was tiny, yet its layout held an understated grace.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷完全按照自家姑娘的喜好选择宅子，先不说里面兰花修竹俱佳，只是院子里种植的各色菊花，正值开放季节，争奇斗艳，甚是引人注目。可以说望月轩兼有竹兰居和朱府小院儿的特色。
[EN] Matron Wei had picked the house straight to her mistress's tastes: graceful orchids and slender bamboo filled the grounds, and chrysanthemums of every hue stood in full bloom across the yard, vying in beauty. It combined the best touches of Zhulan Residence and her old courtyard at Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 千里寻很满意，尤其是屋内的布置跟朱府小院的闺房一模一样，让她有家的亲切感。
[EN] Qianli Xun loved it; above all, her bedroom was arranged identical to her old room at Zhu Manor, wrapping her in the comfort of home.
[ZH] 丫鬟们忙着收拾衣物，韦嬷嬷则忙着安排一切事务，给下人们立规矩。
[EN] The maids busied themselves unpacking clothes while Matron Wei took charge of household affairs and laid down rules for the servants.
[ZH] 千里寻什么都无须操心，只管躺在床上睡大觉。一日之内大起大落，她需整理心情，也许睡一觉，可以忘记所有不幸。
[EN] With nothing to fuss over, Qianli Xun collapsed onto bed to sleep. One day had dragged her through wild highs and lows; she needed to collect her thoughts, hoping sleep might wash every misfortune away.
[ZH] 正当她睡得香，梦到她写的话本受到宋代粉丝追捧，书刚刚刊印出来，便售卖一空，街头小巷，人手一本，岂不是有名载史册的节奏？
[EN] Deep in slumber, she dreamed her huaben stories had taken Song readers by storm—books selling out the instant they left the press, a copy in every hand down every lane, her name headed straight into the history books.
[ZH] “小姐，小姐，快醒醒，仪王前来拜访！”苦荞的声音将千里寻从美梦中唤醒。
[EN] "Miss! Miss, wake up! Prince Yi is here to visit!" Kuqiao's voice yanked her out of her dream.
[ZH] 她睁开眼，坐起来，瞧瞧窗外，天已蒙蒙黑：“谁来拜访？”
[EN] Opening her eyes and sitting up, she glanced out the window at gathering dusk. "Who's here?"
[ZH] 苦荞又说一遍：“仪王。”
[EN] Kuqiao repeated, "Prince Yi."
[ZH] “仪王？她怎么知道我搬到望月轩？该不会是你偷偷告诉白风？”
[EN] "Prince Yi? How did he know I moved to Wangyue Pavilion? Don't tell me you leaked it to Bai Feng!"
[ZH] “小姐冤枉，您多久没见仪王，我就多久没见白风。”
[EN] "You wrong me, Miss! However long you haven't seen Prince Yi, I haven't seen Bai Feng either."
[ZH] 千里寻想起她警告过自己，以后不会与仪王单独相见：“你去告诉仪王，我心情不好，没空见他。”
[EN] Remembering her vow never to meet Prince Yi alone again, Qianli Xun said, "Go tell Prince Yi I'm in a foul mood and have no time to see him."
[ZH] “小姐，他可是仪王，这样回，恐怕不好。”
[EN] "Miss, he's Prince Yi—turning him away like that might not go over well."
[ZH] 千里寻感到为难，见吧，怕他又给她放电；不见吧，人家身份尊贵，得罪不起。
[EN] Qianli Xun felt stuck: see him, and he would start tossing sparks at her again; refuse him, and she risked offending a royal prince.
[ZH] 苦荞为她找到一个合适的理由：“小姐还是见见仪王，顺便打听一下乔公子的消息。”
[EN] Kuqiao handed her the ideal excuse: "Better see him, Miss—you can ask him how Young Master Qiao is doing."
[ZH] 千里寻立刻下床：“快替我收拾，我要让仪王看到我状态极好。”
[EN] Qianli Xun swung out of bed at once. "Help me freshen up! I want Prince Yi to see me looking my absolute best."
[ZH] 苦荞伺候小姐穿好衣裳，装扮靓丽之后，来到厅堂。仪王正坐着等待，韦嬷嬷负责陪伴。
[EN] Dressed and polished by Kuqiao, she stepped into the main hall, where Prince Yi sat waiting with Matron Wei keeping him company.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷见姑娘进来，起身说：“仪王既与我家姑娘有话说，我先退下。”
[EN] Seeing her mistress enter, Matron Wei rose. "Since Prince Yi has words for our Young Lady, I'll take my leave."
[ZH] 申简辰点点头，将目光停留在千里寻身上，她显然精心打扮过一番，没想到，她为见自己如此用心，难道她已对他有情？
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded, his gaze lingering on Qianli Xun. She had taken great care with her dress and makeup—had she really dressed up just to see him? Could she have feelings for him after all?
[ZH] 很快，申简辰便明白过来，她不过是想用靓丽的妆容掩饰内心的悲伤。
[EN] A second later he saw through it: she was only using bright makeup to mask the ache inside.
[ZH] 路上白风已猜到木盒里的泥人是送给幽栖居士的。刚开始想不通，后来想明白，王爷只不过是帮乔公子来看望幽栖居士，空手而来不太好。可它又着实想不通，买个撒尿娃娃是几个意思？
[EN] On the ride over, Bai Feng had figured out the clay figurine was for Youqi Jushi. Baffled at first, he had rationalized it as His Highness visiting on Young Master Qiao's behalf and not wanting to arrive empty-handed—still, what on earth was the idea behind a peeing boy?
[ZH] 见到苦荞，他无心多想，把木盒放在桌上，对苦荞使了个眼色，二人悄悄退出。
[EN] Spotting Kuqiao, he dropped the puzzle, set the wooden box on the table, tossed Kuqiao a wink, and slipped out in silence with her.
[ZH] 没有其他人在旁，申简辰相当满意，他站起来：“幽栖居士可还好？”
[EN] Alone at last, Shen Jianchen felt satisfied and stood up. "How is Youqi Jushi holding up?"
[ZH] 千里寻没有回答，拜道：“不知仪王如何找到望月轩？”
[EN] Skipping an answer, Qianli Xun bowed. "How did Prince Yi find Wangyue Pavilion?"
[ZH] “只要是我想找的人，都难不倒我。”
[EN] "Anyone I want to find can't stay hidden from me."
[ZH] 千里寻想，他是仪王，此等小事如何能难倒他？她的问题问得实在没意思。
[EN] Right—he was a royal prince; what a pointless question.
[ZH] “文渊可好？”
[EN] "How is Wenyuan?"
[ZH] 没说两句，她便关心文渊，申简辰心中不快，依然回道：“提亲未成，文渊当然不好，他求我到乔府劝说外公，可外公宁可舍弃性命，也不愿成全你与文渊的姻缘，我也无能为力。”
[EN] Two sentences in and she was already asking about Wenyuan. Stung, Shen Jianchen answered anyway: "With the proposal off, Wenyuan is taking it hard. He begged me to go persuade Grandfather at Qiao Manor, yet Grandfather swore he'd give up his life before allowing your marriage. There was nothing I could do."
[ZH] 千里寻一阵心酸，心酸到任何话都说不出口。
[EN] A wave of bitterness choked Qianli Xun silent.
[ZH] 申简辰注意到她手上有伤：“幽栖居士要懂得爱惜自己。”
[EN] Spotting the bruise on her hand, Shen Jianchen said, "Youqi Jushi needs to take better care of herself."
[ZH] 千里寻沉浸在内心的酸痛中，本不想说话，可面对仪王不能不做出回应。
[EN] Swamped in heartache, she had no urge to talk, yet etiquette before a prince demanded a reply.
[ZH] “感谢仪王关怀，请仪王帮我告诉文渊，明日神柳下相见。”
[EN] "Thank you for your concern, Prince Yi. Please pass word to Wenyuan to meet me beneath the Divine Willow tomorrow."
[ZH] 在申简辰看来，神柳专属于他与千里寻：“为何要在神柳下相见？”
[EN] To Shen Jianchen, the Divine Willow belonged to him and Qianli Xun alone. "Why meet beneath the Divine Willow?"
[ZH] “为何不能在神柳下？”千里寻认为仪王管得实在太多，连她与文渊在哪里约会都要管，实在多事。
[EN] "Why not beneath the Divine Willow?" Qianli Xun thought he was meddling way too much—now policing where she and Wenyuan met? What a busybody!
[ZH] “好吧，你想怎样便怎样。”申简辰拿起木盒，“送你个礼物，保准你看后心情会好些。”
[EN] "Fine, suit yourself." Shen Jianchen picked up the wooden box. "Brought you a gift. Guaranteed to lift your spirits once you open it."
[ZH] “是文渊送的吗？”
[EN] "Did Wenyuan send it?"
[ZH] “难道不能是我送的？”
[EN] "Can't it be from me?"
[ZH] 千里寻犹豫要不要收下：“仪王不必对我如此破费。”
[EN] Qianli Xun hesitated to accept. "Prince Yi shouldn't spend money on me."
[ZH] 申简辰把礼物塞进千里寻怀中：“一点儿都不破费，给你送礼物，我高兴得很，必须收下，否则，我不帮你给文渊传话。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen pressed the box into her arms. "Hardly any money at all—buying you a gift makes me happy. Take it, or I won't pass your message to Wenyuan."
[ZH] 申简辰转身要离开。
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned to leave.
[ZH] 千里寻手握木盒，心情复杂：“仪王！”
[EN] Clutching the wooden box, tangled emotions churned inside Qianli Xun. "Prince Yi!"
[ZH] 申简辰站住，并未回头：“什么事？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen halted without looking back. "Yes?"
[ZH] “我爱的是文渊。”
[EN] "Wenyuan is the one I love."
[ZH] “我知道。”
[EN] "I know."
[ZH] “佳和郡主很好，你不能辜负她。”
[EN] "Princess Jiahe is a wonderful girl. Don't you dare let her down."
[ZH] “不用你提醒。”
[EN] "No need for you to remind me."
[ZH] 申简辰说完，出了厅堂，经过院落。白风见状，只好与苦荞分别，跟上王爷出了望月轩。
[EN] With that, Shen Jianchen strode out of the hall and across the courtyard. Seeing him leave, Bai Feng bade Kuqiao a hasty farewell and followed his master out of Wangyue Pavilion.

#CH 111
[ZH] 苦荞进入厅堂，见小姐手里拿个木盒，站着发呆。
[EN] Entering the hall, Kuqiao found her mistress standing in a daze with a wooden box in her hands.
[ZH] “小姐怎么了？难道被仪王吓住？”
[EN] "What's wrong, Miss? Did Prince Yi scare you?"
[ZH] 千里寻对仪王的做法并不认同，也不会接受他的感情。可不得不承认，她内心对仪王生出感激之情。
[EN] Qianli Xun neither approved of Prince Yi's ways nor meant to accept his feelings, yet she could not deny a warm spark of gratitude toward him.
[ZH] 乔家放弃提亲，令她多少产生自我怀疑。仪王是第一个来望月轩的人，还是身份尊贵之人，他的到来，说的那些话，还有礼物，让千里寻重拾自信。
[EN] The Qiao family calling off the proposal had stirred a sliver of self-doubt. Prince Yi was the first visitor to set foot in Wangyue Pavilion—and a royal prince at that. His visit, his words, and his gift had restored her footing.
[ZH] “他怎会吓住我？只是想，我可能是第一个收王爷礼物的草民。”
[EN] "Why would he scare me? I'm just thinking I might be the first commoner to get a gift from a prince."
[ZH] “小姐不打开看看吗？听白风说很有趣。”
[EN] "Aren't you going to open it, Miss? Bai Feng said it's funny."
[ZH] 千里寻打开木盒，不由露出笑容：“竟然送这玩意儿，哪像一位王爷的做派！”
[EN] Qianli Xun lifted the lid and broke into a smile. "He actually gave me this—what kind of prince pulls a stunt like this?!"
[ZH] “小姐，您笑了，仪王送的礼物肯定有趣，也让我瞧瞧。”
[EN] "You smiled, Miss! Whatever Prince Yi gave you must be hilarious—let me see!"
[ZH] 千里寻拿起来，举到苦荞面前：“瞧，就是这玩意儿。”
[EN] Qianli Xun lifted the figurine up before Kuqiao. "Look, it's this."
[ZH] 苦荞看到泥人娃娃光着屁股，做出撒尿的动作，还一脸得意与享受，立刻捂住眼睛：“仪王怎么送这东西，羞死人！”
[EN] Seeing the bare-bottomed clay boy mid-pee with a smug, blissful grin, Kuqiao slapped her hands over her eyes. "Why would Prince Yi give something like that—how shameless!"
[ZH] 千里寻把泥人放进木盒，盖好盖子：“你想得太多，小孩子多单纯可爱，你要用单纯的思想看他。”
[EN] Qianli Xun tucked the figurine back inside and shut the lid. "Your mind's in the gutter! Little kids are pure and adorable—look at it with pure eyes."
[ZH] “小姐说得有道理，是我不对。”苦荞放下捂住眼睛的手，接过木盒，“我帮您收起来。”
[EN] "Miss has a point; my bad." Lowering her hands, Kuqiao took the box. "I'll put it away for you."
[ZH] 千里寻忽然觉得仪王不错，只可惜他付错情。
[EN] For half a second Qianli Xun thought Prince Yi wasn't half bad, just barking up the wrong tree.
[ZH] 又转念一想：“不错个头！明明有婚约，还想勾引老娘，今日来此，不过是想乘虚而入，想得美！”
[EN] Then she caught herself: "Not half bad, my foot! He's engaged and still trying to seduce me—coming here today just to swoop in while I'm down! Dream on!"
[ZH] “苦荞，把仪王送的礼物压到箱底，我不想再看到。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, bury Prince Yi's gift at the bottom of a trunk. I don't want to see it again."
[ZH] 苦荞纳闷，小姐刚才还挺高兴，怎么突然改变态度？
[EN] Kuqiao blinked—one second ago Miss was smiling, why the sudden flip?
[ZH] “好的，一会儿回屋，我把它压到不常打开的箱子里。”
[EN] "Yes, Miss. I'll stash it at the bottom of a trunk we never open."
[ZH] “记住，不能在白风跟前提起此事。”
[EN] "And don't breathe a word of this to Bai Feng."
[ZH] “小姐放心，我虽跟白风好，也知什么话该说，什么话不该说。”
[EN] "Don't worry, Miss. Close as I am to Bai Feng, I know what to say and what to keep shut."
[ZH] “那便好。”
[EN] "Good."
[ZH] 主仆二人走出厅堂，准备回屋，在院子里碰到夏果。
[EN] Stepping out of the hall toward her room, mistress and maid ran into Xiaguo in the courtyard.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷派夏果出去买些日用之物，她刚买回来，放下东西，匆匆往小姐闺房方向走，可巧半路遇上。
[EN] Matron Wei had sent Xiaguo out to buy household supplies. Having dropped off her parcels, she was hurrying to her mistress's room when they crossed paths.
[ZH] “小姐，您猜我在外面碰到谁？”
[EN] "Miss, guess who I saw outside!"
[ZH] “谁？”千里寻很好奇。
[EN] "Who?" Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] “陈公子！陈浩宇！”
[EN] "Young Master Chen! Chen Haoyu!"
[ZH] 本以为此人已从她命运中消失，没想到又被夏果碰到。
[EN] Just when she thought he had vanished from her life, Xiaguo had spotted him.
[ZH] “怎么会在这里碰到？”
[EN] "How did you run into him around here?"
[ZH] “我买完东西往回走，快到望月轩时，见到一伙人，往不远处的一所宅子走去，好像叫永宁居。本来我没当回事，因好奇随意瞧一眼，结果借着永宁居门口挂的大灯笼，看清为首的正是陈浩宇。”
[EN] "On my way back, almost at Wangyue Pavilion, I saw a bunch of men heading into a manor nearby—the plaque read Yongning Residence. I didn't think much of it, just glanced out of curiosity, and under the big lanterns by the gate I saw Chen Haoyu leading the pack!"
[ZH] 千里寻边向闺房走，边念叨：“永宁居？难道是陈家的宅子？夏果，你去把韦嬷嬷找来。”
[EN] Walking toward her room, Qianli Xun muttered, "Yongning Residence? A Chen family property? Xiaguo, go fetch Matron Wei."
[ZH] 夏果答应而去。
[EN] Xiaguo hurried off.
[ZH] 回到闺房，苦荞把木盒压入箱底，开始做茶。
[EN] Back in her chamber, Kuqiao stashed the wooden box at the bottom of a trunk and began whisking tea.
[ZH] 千里寻坐在书桌前等待韦嬷嬷，她无心提笔，更无心翻阅桌案上的书籍。讨厌的人又出现在她身边，还离得那么近，令她内心难安。
[EN] Sitting at her desk waiting for Matron Wei, Qianli Xun had no heart to pick up a brush or open a book. Having her worst nemesis popping up right next door left her uneasy.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷与夏果进来，一进屋，说道：“过来的路上，夏果都给我说了，陈公子出现在周围不足为奇，陈家有钱，到处有宅子，估计永宁居就是陈家的。”
[EN] Matron Wei walked in with Xiaguo, speaking before even sitting: "Xiaguo told me on the way over. Seeing Young Master Chen around here is no surprise—the Chen family is rich and owns houses all over Lin'an. Yongning Residence is bound to be theirs."
[ZH] “嬷嬷坐，苦荞，看茶。”
[EN] "Have a seat, Matron. Kuqiao, serve tea."
[ZH] 苦荞刚做好一盏，给韦嬷嬷端上，继续给小姐做茶，夏果凑到跟前学习。
[EN] Kuqiao had just finished a bowl and brought it to Matron Wei before starting another for her mistress, with Xiaguo hovering close to watch.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷品完一口茶，放下茶盏：“姑娘要是嫌弃望月轩离陈家宅子近，改日我再到其他地方找找，大不了把这处卖掉，另买一处。”
[EN] Taking a sip, Matron Wei set the bowl down. "If you dislike being near a Chen house, Young Lady, I can hunt elsewhere another day—worst case we sell this place and buy another."
[ZH] 刚搬来，还未适应新环境，又谋着离开，千里寻内心更感凄凉。
[EN] She had barely moved in; uprooting again so soon felt too dreary.
[ZH] 仔细想来，买卖宅子说起来容易，行动起来也是件麻烦事。嬷嬷管理她名下产业已够辛苦，再为琐事操心，岂不累坏？
[EN] Besides, buying and selling houses sounded easy, yet handling it was a huge chore. Matron Wei already worked hard enough managing her properties—no need to pile more hassle on her.
[ZH] “不必，既然命运安排我住进望月轩，就在这里安定下来，我不想再漂泊。陈浩宇的确讨厌，不过陈家既然房产多，他不至于老住这里，估计也是偶尔过来。再说我何必怕他？应该是他怕我才对。”
[EN] "No need. If fate put me in Wangyue Pavilion, I'll put down roots here—I'm tired of drifting. Chen Haoyu is obnoxious, sure, yet with so many Chen properties he won't live here full-time; he probably just drops by now and then. Besides, why should I fear him? He's the one who ought to fear me."
[ZH] 讨厌陈浩宇的心理，悄悄滋长起担忧见到陈浩宇的想法，千里寻不愿继续受这种情绪困扰，她要努力调整。
[EN] Disliking Chen Haoyu had almost morphed into dreading him—Qianli Xun refused to let that mindset take hold.
[ZH] 哼！陈浩宇算老几！碰见又如何？要是敢横，大不了再揍他一顿！
[EN] Hmph! Who did Chen Haoyu think he was? So what if they crossed paths? If he acted cocky, she'd just beat him up again!
[ZH] “姑娘能如此想，再好不过。”韦嬷嬷又端起茶盏品起茶来。
[EN] "That's the spirit, Young Lady." Matron Wei lifted her tea bowl for another sip.
[ZH] 不一会儿苦荞又做好一盏，给姑娘放在桌上。千里寻又与韦嬷嬷闲聊一会儿，才收拾睡去。
[EN] Before long Kuqiao served her mistress a fresh bowl. After chatting with Matron Wei a while longer, Qianli Xun turned in for the night.
[ZH] 近期，临安城最高兴的莫过于陈浩宇，计划成功，大仇得报。
[EN] Right now, nobody in Lin'an felt happier than Chen Haoyu—plan executed, sweet revenge achieved.
[ZH] 最近他没事总带几个小弟在街上闲逛，听听谣言传播的情况，一点一点见证谣言如何逐渐波及开来，到今日算是达到预期效果。
[EN] Over the past few days he had strolled the streets with his lackeys, listening to the rumors snowball bit by bit until today they hit peak blast.
[ZH] 一高兴，吩咐小弟把各处的混混头目叫来，论功行赏。任务是在永宁居布置的，还在永宁居论功行赏。
[EN] Thrilled, he summoned the gang bosses from every quarter back to Yongning Residence—where he had handed out the job—to reward them.
[ZH] 永宁居的厨子做好一桌子酒菜，陈浩宇坐在主位，几个混混头目围坐桌边。
[EN] The Yongning Residence cook laid out a lavish feast. Sitting in the host's chair, Chen Haoyu presided over the gang bosses crowding the table.
[ZH] 身边的小弟拿出几袋银子，一一扔到混混头目的怀里。拿到银子，他们个个喜笑颜开。
[EN] A lackey pulled out pouches of silver and tossed one into each boss's lap, lighting up greedy grins all around.
[ZH] “谢陈公子！下次还有这等好事，尽管找我们。”
[EN] "Thanks, Young Master Chen! Anytime another sweet gig like this comes up, call us first!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“本公子今日开心，你们吃好喝好，完了，再让人带你们去春香楼。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu declared, "I'm in a great mood today—eat and drink your fill, and afterward my boys will take you all over to Chunxiang Brothel!"
[ZH] “陈公子豪爽！跟着陈公子有吃有喝，还有得玩儿，人生夫复何求！”
[EN] "Generous as ever, Young Master Chen! Food, drink, and fun—what more could a man ask from life!"
[ZH] “来！干了这杯！”陈浩宇举起酒杯与众人干了。
[EN] "Come on, bottom's up!" Chen Haoyu raised his cup and drank with the crowd.
[ZH] 一位头目饮完酒，嬉笑道：“陈公子这招真够损的……”
[EN] Draining his cup, one boss grinned: "Young Master Chen's scheme was nasty as—"
[ZH] 小弟提醒道：“怎么说话呢？”
[EN] A lackey barked, "Watch your mouth!"
[ZH] 那位头目立刻改变语气：“真够厉害的，搞得朱家姑娘被全城人骂得面目全非，以后想嫁人都难！”
[EN] The boss pivoted on a dime: "—brilliant as hell! Got the whole city tearing Miss Zhu's name to shreds—good luck finding a husband now!"
[ZH] 又一位说：“我听说乔家本来今日上门提亲，结果被陈公子搅黄，嘿嘿，朱家姑娘还被蒙在鼓里，关键是要想找到幕后主使，如同大海捞针。”
[EN] Another chimed in: "Heard the Qiao family was set to propose today and you blew it sky-high! Heheh, Miss Zhu's still in the dark, and trying to trace the mastermind? Like fishing a needle out of the sea!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“呵呵……！本公子总算出了口恶气，全城人都替本公子骂她，看她以后还敢在本公子面前横！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu crowed, "Haha! Got that venom off my chest at last! The whole city's cursing her out for me—let's see her act cocky in front of me again!"
[ZH] 旁边的小弟说：“不止，也传到乡下，乡下百姓也骂着呢！”
[EN] A lackey beside him added, "Not just the city—it's reached the countryside too! Peasants out there are cursing her too!"
[ZH] 一桌人更是高兴，喝得更欢，吃得更起劲。
[EN] Cheers rose louder around the table as everyone drank harder and dug in deeper.
[ZH] 正高兴着，一个小弟从外面匆匆回来，边往里走，边喊：“陈公子！不好了！不好了！”
[EN] Right in the middle of the celebration, a lackey burst in shouting, "Young Master Chen! Disaster! Disaster!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇见状，骂道：“怎么说话呢？本公子打败朱淑真，正好着呢？哪里来的不好？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu barked, "Watch your mouth! I just crushed Zhu Shuzhen—life's grand! What disaster?!"
[ZH] 小弟说：“回陈公子，我在春香楼碰到林觉，偶然听到他给春金巧说知府大人让他彻查传播谣言之人。”
[EN] The lackey panted, "I bumped into Lin Jue at Chunxiang Brothel and overheard him telling Chun Jinqiao that His Lordship the Prefect ordered him to hunt down whoever started the rumors!"
[ZH] 几个头目开始紧张，有人说：“陈公子，该怎么办？会不会被查出来？”
[EN] Tension snapped across the gang bosses' faces. One stammered, "What do we do, Young Master Chen? Will they trace it to us?"
[ZH] “怕什么？根本查不到！”陈浩宇放下酒杯，指着所有人，“你们给我听好，自己先不要心虚，装作什么都不知，回去把给你们的写好的纸张都烧了，一张都不许留。”
[EN] "What's there to panic about? They won't find a thing!" Setting down his cup, Chen Haoyu pointed around the table. "Listen up: don't act guilty, play dumb, and go home and burn every single rumor sheet I gave you—don't leave a scrap!"
[ZH] 有头目说：“那些纸扔的扔，丢的丢，都没了，有的还被弟兄们上茅房给用了。”
[EN] One boss piped up, "Those sheets got tossed or lost already—some of the boys even used them in the latrine!"
[ZH] “既然没了，不必去管，该怎样就怎样，就当啥事没有，今儿犒赏完你们，以后我不叫别来找我。”
[EN] "Gone? Even better! Carry on like normal, act like nothing happened. After tonight's reward, don't come looking for me unless I call."
[ZH] 陈浩宇又与大家吃喝起来，直到凌晨，才各自散去。
[EN] Chen Haoyu kept drinking and feasting with them until the small hours before they broke up.
[ZH] 其他人去了春香楼，陈浩宇则在永宁居留宿，他打算明日去朱府看望姐姐，顺便打探消息。
[EN] The rest headed for Chunxiang Brothel while Chen Haoyu stayed the night at Yongning Residence, planning to visit his sister at Zhu Manor tomorrow to fish for news.

#CH 112
[ZH] 离开望月轩，申简辰并未直接回幽林别居，吩咐白风把马车赶往林府。
[EN] Leaving Wangyue Pavilion, Shen Jianchen did not head straight back to Youlin Retreat; he told Bai Feng to drive to Lin Manor.
[ZH] 一路上他心里纠结流言蜚语，能如此大范围传开，背后定有人操控。
[EN] All the way there, those rumors nagged at him. Spreading across the whole city like wildfire meant someone was pulling strings behind the scenes.
[ZH] 临安城内，谁人如此无聊？到处诋毁幽栖居士？多半此人与朱家有仇，见不得朱家好。
[EN] Who in Lin'an had so much time on their hands as to drag Youqi Jushi through the mud? Most likely an enemy of the Zhu family who hated seeing them thrive.
[ZH] 申简辰回想刚刚见到千里寻时她的表情，尽管那张脸是朱淑真的，可他能感觉到她并不开心。
[EN] Shen Jianchen recalled Qianli Xun's expression just now. Even wearing Zhu Shuzhen's face, he could feel how heavy her heart was.
[ZH] 遇到这么大的事，又岂能开心？
[EN] How could anyone stay cheerful after a blow like that?
[ZH] 千里寻不高兴，申简辰又如何高兴？他打算帮她解决临安城的流言蜚语。
[EN] When Qianli Xun hurt, how could Shen Jianchen rest easy? He was going to stamp out these rumors across Lin'an for her.
[ZH] 来到林府门外，白风向林府下人说明来意，对方为难道：“您来得不是时候，我家三爷这个点儿不在府上。”
[EN] Outside Lin Manor, Bai Feng stated their purpose to a gatekeeper, who looked awkward: "You picked a bad time—Third Master isn't home at this hour."
[ZH] 白风问：“去了哪里？”
[EN] Bai Feng asked, "Where did he go?"
[ZH] 下人毫不避讳：“春香楼。”
[EN] The servant didn't even blink: "Chunxiang Brothel."
[ZH] 申简辰又往春香楼而去。
[EN] Shen Jianchen directed them straight to Chunxiang Brothel.
[ZH] 夜间的临安城依然热闹，应该不比京城差。
[EN] Even after nightfall, Lin'an stayed lively—no quieter than the capital.
[ZH] 仔细想想，自穿越而来，还没来得及在夜间逛汴梁街市便匆匆来到临安。在临安的时日竟然比在汴梁还要长，说明他依然与千里寻有缘，乔家提亲失败不也正说明这一点。
[EN] Thinking about it, since crossing over, he hadn't even toured Bianliang's night markets before rushing down to Lin'an. He had now spent longer in Lin'an than in Bianliang—proof fate still tied him to Qianli Xun. Wasn't the Qiao proposal collapsing proof too?
[ZH] 刚有些高兴，乔梦容唤他“哥哥”的声音出现在耳畔。
[EN] No sooner did that spark of cheer rise than Qiao Mengrong's voice calling "Brother" echoed in his ears.
[ZH] 申简辰在心中埋怨赵朴：“赵朴啊赵朴，你为何偏偏在我即将穿越时离开？搞的我一不小心跑到你的身体里，还要替你照顾妹妹？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen grumbled at Zhao Pu in his head: "Zhao Pu, oh Zhao Pu, why did you have to check out the exact second I crossed over, dumping me in your body to babysit your little fiancée?"
[ZH] 申简辰叹口气：“唉！不想那么多，车到山前必有路。”
[EN] He let out a sigh. "Ah, stop overthinking—we'll cross that bridge when we come to it."
[ZH] 白风听到王爷的声音，并未听清说些什么，以为王爷有事吩咐：“王爷，您说什么？我没听清？”
[EN] Hearing his master speak without catching the words, Bai Feng asked, "Did Your Highness say something? I didn't catch it."
[ZH] “哦，没什么，汴梁与临安相比，晚上哪个更热闹？”
[EN] "Nothing. Between Bianliang and Lin'an, which has livelier nights?"
[ZH] “在汴梁时，王爷难道从来没在晚上逛过街？”
[EN] "Didn't Your Highness ever stroll Bianliang's streets at night?"
[ZH] “我一直病着，哪有心情和精力出去逛？病刚好，还没来得及晚上出去，就跑到临安来，所以不知。”解释完，又觉何必对白风说那么多，“你小子问那么多干吗？我问什么你只管回答便是，以后不许多问。”
[EN] "I was sick the whole time—where would I get the energy or mood to go out? And the second I recovered, I came down to Lin'an before getting a night out, so I wouldn't know." Pausing, he wondered why he was explaining so much to Bai Feng. "Why all the questions? Answer what I ask and quit prying!"
[ZH] “好好好，我不会再多问王爷的事。”白风边赶马车边得意地说，“汴梁是京城，当然更繁华，晚上比白天还热闹，临安根本没法比！等王爷回到京城，晚上我陪您去矾楼，还有勾栏瓦肆，玩的，吃得，都比临安好。”
[EN] "Yes, yes, I won't pry into Your Highness's business." Driving on, Bai Feng puffed out his chest: "Bianliang is the capital, so of course it's grander—nights are livelier than days! Lin'an can't hold a candle to it! Once Your Highness returns to the capital, I'll take you out at night to Fanlou Tower and the Goulan Wasi—the food and entertainment blow Lin'an out of the water!"
[ZH] 申简辰听出白风作为汴梁小老百姓的荣耀，很是不屑：“听上去，老百姓对时下的生活很满意？只因大家吃得好玩得好？”
[EN] Hearing the hometown pride of an ordinary Bianliang lad, Shen Jianchen scoffed, "Sounds like common folk are mighty happy with life these days—just because the eating and playing are good?"
[ZH] “当然也有不满意的。”
[EN] "Well, there's plenty they're unhappy about too."
[ZH] “你倒是说说对哪些不满意？”
[EN] "Go on—tell me what they're unhappy about."
[ZH] “我说了，王爷可别治我的罪。”
[EN] "If I say it, Your Highness can't punish me."
[ZH] “畅所欲言。”
[EN] "Speak your mind."
[ZH] “汴梁瞧着繁华，老百姓还是很穷，繁华都是有钱人的事，跟我们小老百姓没什么关系，要是老百姓对时下生活满意，也不会有人造反。”
[EN] "Bianliang looks dazzling, sure, yet common folk are still dirt poor. All that glitter belongs to the rich, nothing to do with little folk like us. If common folk were happy with life, nobody would be rising in rebellion."
[ZH] “说得好！冲你这番话，回到汴梁我带你去矾楼撮一顿。”
[EN] "Well said! For that remark alone, once we're back in Bianliang I'm treating you to a feast at Fanlou Tower!"
[ZH] “谢王爷！”白风开心的同时，也疑惑，王爷是不是官家的儿子？刚才他的话显然是批评官家管理的天下有瑕疵，为何王爷听后如此高兴？真是个不一样的王爷！
[EN] "Thank you, Your Highness!" Thrilled yet baffled, Bai Feng wondered if His Highness was really the Emperor's son—he had just criticized flaws in His Majesty's realm, and His Highness loved it! What an unusual prince.
[ZH] 两人聊着天儿，已到春香楼门外。
[EN] Chatting along, they pulled up outside Chunxiang Brothel.
[ZH] 申简辰不愿进去，依旧坐在马车里，吩咐白风将林觉叫出来。
[EN] Unwilling to step inside, Shen Jianchen stayed in the carriage and sent Bai Feng in to summon Lin Jue.
[ZH] 白风也是第一次来烟花柳巷之地，既好奇又小心。
[EN] It was Bai Feng's first time stepping into a house of pleasure, leaving him equal parts curious and nervous.
[ZH] 刚走进，春香楼的妈妈迎上来：“这位小爷看着面生，不过一回生二回熟，我这儿的姑娘个顶个的好，小爷您喜欢哪种类型？”
[EN] No sooner had he crossed the threshold than the madam swept up. "Never seen this young master before—stranger the first time, regular the second! Every girl in my house is top-notch. What type strikes your fancy?"
[ZH] 白风很不习惯：“我有喜欢的人，不找姑娘，来找林觉。”
[EN] Uncomfortable, Bai Feng stepped back: "I have someone I like—I'm not here for girls. I'm looking for Lin Jue."
[ZH] “小哥还是个纯情的主。”春香楼的妈妈指向一边，“呶，林统领在那边。”
[EN] "A faithful lad, eh?" The madam pointed across the room. "There—Commandant Lin is right over there."
[ZH] 春金巧正陪林觉看台上跳舞，两人挨得很近。
[EN] Chun Jinqiao sat pressed up beside Lin Jue, watching dancers on stage.
[ZH] 白风很不好意，心想：“难怪幽栖居士跟林觉和离，瞧那样儿应是春香楼的常客。”
[EN] Awkwardness pricked Bai Feng. "No wonder Youqi Jushi divorced Lin Jue—looks like he's a fixture at Chunxiang Brothel."
[ZH] 白风走到林觉身边：“林统领，仪王有请。”
[EN] Bai Feng stepped over to Lin Jue. "Commandant Lin, Prince Yi requests your presence."
[ZH] 林觉正沉浸在玩乐中，突然身边多出声音，还提到仪王，赶紧起身，原来是仪王身边的侍卫。
[EN] Absorbed in his pleasure, Lin Jue shot up at the mention of Prince Yi, recognizing the prince's personal bodyguard.
[ZH] ﻿“仪王在何处？”
[EN] "Where is Prince Yi?"
[ZH] “外面的马车里候着。”
[EN] "Waiting in his carriage outside."
[ZH] 林觉甩下春金巧，迅速走出春香楼。
[EN] Ditching Chun Jinqiao on the spot, Lin Jue hurried out of Chunxiang Brothel.
[ZH] 过来一位商妓，拦住白风：“小哥，我陪你玩玩儿？”
[EN] A courtesan stepped into Bai Feng's path: "Stay and have a little fun with me, handsome?"
[ZH] “去去去！忙着呢！”白风推开商妓，也出了春香楼。
[EN] "Get off, get off—I'm on duty!" Pushing past her, Bai Feng headed out too.
[ZH] 商妓不满道：“什么人！既来春香楼，何必装！”
[EN] The courtesan huffed, "What a prude! Why step into Chunxiang Brothel if you're going to act saintly?"
[ZH] 春金巧安慰道：“妹妹揽人，也要看对人，那小伙子一看就是第一次来，你那股热情劲儿，不把人吓跑才怪。”
[EN] Chun Jinqiao chuckled, "Pick your targets better, sister. One look tells you that kid's a first-timer—pouncing on him like that was bound to scare him off!"
[ZH] “我哪有姐姐幸运，把着林统领不放，搞得我们都没机会，哼！”商妓表达完嫌弃的情绪，见有客人进来，又立刻换一副表情，笑脸迎上去。
[EN] "Easy for Sister to say, hogging Commandant Lin so the rest of us don't get a look-in! Hmph!" The second fresh customers walked in, her pout flipped straight into a beaming welcome.
[ZH] 林觉果然看到有辆马车停在街上，上前拜道：“拜见仪王。”
[EN] Spotting the carriage parked on the street, Lin Jue stepped up and bowed. "Greetings, Prince Yi."
[ZH] “到车上来，我有话对你讲。”
[EN] "Climb inside. I have a word for you."
[ZH] 林觉登上马车，在仪王对面坐好，他略显紧张，怕仪王批评他。
[EN] Lin Jue climbed aboard and sat across from Prince Yi, tense lest the prince scold him for visiting a brothel.
[ZH] “我来找你，有事相托。”
[EN] "I came to ask a favor."
[ZH] 仪王并未提逛春香楼之事，林觉松口气：“仪王尽管说，我无不答应。”
[EN] Relieved Prince Yi skipped mention of Chunxiang Brothel, Lin Jue exhaled. "Name it, Prince Yi—anything you ask."
[ZH] 申简辰说：“幽栖居士被全城人非议，此事蹊跷，我想派你私下查查谣言的源头，再将散播谣言之人抓来见我。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said, "The whole city is slandering Youqi Jushi, and something smells off about it. I want you to investigate the source of these rumors on the quiet and bring whoever started them straight to me."
[ZH] 原来仪王也是为此事而来，一天之内被两个人嘱托，还都是临安城地位最高之人，林觉倍感荣耀。
[EN] Prince Yi had come over this too! Entrusted in a single day by the two highest-ranking men in Lin'an filled Lin Jue with pride.
[ZH] 可也疑惑，朱晞颜嘱托当然是为朱家，仪王嘱托又为何人？仔细一想，也许是受乔文仙所托，他们两个关系好，临安有头有脸的人都知道。
[EN] Still, he wondered: Zhu Xiyan was acting for the Zhu family, so why did Prince Yi care? Probably on Qiao Wenxian's behalf—everyone of standing in Lin'an knew how tight those two were.
[ZH] “仪王放心，今日知府大人也找过我，下官已将人手安排下去，定将幕后之人揪出来。”
[EN] "Rest easy, Prince Yi. His Lordship the Prefect came to me today too, and I've already sent men out. We'll drag the mastermind into the light."
[ZH] “很好，林统领去忙吧。”
[EN] "Good. Go back to your evening, Commandant Lin."
[ZH] 仪王的话令林觉很不好意思：“下官告退。”
[EN] Embarrassed by the parting remark, Lin Jue bowed: "I take my leave."
[ZH] 林觉离开后，白风不必问王爷，自然是回幽林别居。
[EN] Once Lin Jue exited, Bai Feng didn't need to ask—he drove straight back to Youlin Retreat.
[ZH] 幽林别居外，乔文仙带着南双翘首企盼。
[EN] Outside Youlin Retreat's gate, Qiao Wenxian stood craning his neck with Nan Shuang beside him.
[ZH] 李管家劝过好几回，公子都不愿回屋，便懒得再管。他吩咐南双将厚些的披风拿出来给公子披上，已是秋季，晚上凉。吩咐完自顾回屋睡大觉去。
[EN] Butler Li had urged his young master indoors several times to no avail before giving up, ordering Nan Shuang to bring out a thick cape against the autumn night chill, then heading off to bed himself.
[ZH] 当南双刚刚为公子披好披风，便传来“嘎吱嘎吱”的马车声。
[EN] Just as Nan Shuang draped the cape over his master's shoulders, the creak of carriage wheels sounded down the road.
[ZH] 乔文仙冲上去，白风不得不将马车停下。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian bolted out into the lane, forcing Bai Feng to haul the horse to a stop.
[ZH] 乔文仙不等仪王下车，快速爬上马车，抓住仪王：“我爹可同意？”
[EN] Before Prince Yi could step down, Qiao Wenxian scrambled into the carriage and clutched his arm. "Did my father agree?!"
[ZH] 申简辰不想瞒文渊：“外公以性命相逼，我也无能为力。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen wouldn't lie to him: "Grandfather threatened his own life. There was nothing I could do."
[ZH] 乔文仙的手像失去力气，渐渐松开，双眼泛红，声音颤抖：“本来好好的，为……为何会变成这样？仪王，你……你告诉我，这一切……一切都是假的！”
[EN] Strength drained from Qiao Wenxian's hands. His eyes turned red and his voice shook: "Everything was going fine—why did it turn out like this?! Tell me, Prince Yi... tell me this is all a bad dream!"
[ZH] 申简辰并未回应他，而是说：“你放心，我会查出散播谣言之人，也会把此人揪到外公面前，说不定到时外公会改变态度。”
[EN] Ignoring the plea, Shen Jianchen said, "Don't worry—I'm going to track down whoever started these rumors and haul him right in front of Grandfather. Maybe then he'll change his mind."
[ZH] 乔文仙又充满希望，一激动，抱住仪王：“你真是我的好兄弟……”
[EN] Hope flared again, and Qiao Wenxian threw his arms around Prince Yi. "You really are my best brother..."
[ZH] 申简辰不忍见文渊继续痛苦下去，安慰道：“有件高兴的事，我找到幽栖居士，她搬到城东，居住在望月轩，她让我代为转告，约你明日神柳下相见。”
[EN] Unable to watch Wenyuan suffer, Shen Jianchen added a bright note: "Here's some good news—I found Youqi Jushi. She moved to the east side of town, a place called Wangyue Pavilion, and asked me to tell you to meet her beneath the Divine Willow tomorrow."
[ZH] 这是提亲失败后最令乔文仙开心之事，他又把仪王抱紧：“你生病时，没觉得你重要；没想到你病好后，竟然对我如此重要，这份恩情恐怕我难以偿还，只能下辈子给你作牛作马。”﻿
[EN] The best news since the proposal collapsed! Qiao Wenxian hugged him even tighter. "Back when you were sick, I never knew how much you'd mean to me; who knew once you recovered, you'd become my lifeline? I can never repay this debt—I'll serve you like an ox or a horse in the next life!"
[ZH] 文渊一席话，令申简辰愧疚，他帮文渊，多多少少还有私心：“别说得这么严重，不需要你偿还，你跟我之间何必客气，再客气我下次可不帮你。”
[EN] Wenyuan's gratitude stabbed Shen Jianchen with guilt, knowing his own motives weren't pure. "Cut the melodrama—you don't owe me anything. Since when do we stand on ceremony? Keep thanking me and I won't help next time."
[ZH] 乔文仙终于露出笑容，放开仪王：“佳和郡主找你好几次，你快去见见她。”
[EN] A smile broke across Qiao Wenxian's face at last, and he let go. "Princess Jiahe came looking for you several times—go see her."
[ZH] 莫名的沉重感再次袭来，申简辰不想再说任何话，他走下马车，向别居内走去，向他必须肩负的责任走去。
[EN] That familiar leaden weight dropped back onto Shen Jianchen's chest. Without another word, he stepped out of the carriage and walked into the retreat—toward the duty he could not escape.

#CH 113
[ZH] 天色已晚，哥哥未归，乔梦容难以入眠，一直坐在闺房内等待。她知哥哥是去帮助乔公子，可这么晚未归，会不会在外面被哪位女子绊住？毕竟临安的姑娘个个长得水灵。
[EN] Night grew deep with no sign of Prince Yi returning, and Qiao Mengrong could not sleep a wink, waiting in her chamber. She knew he had gone to help Young Master Qiao, yet staying out this late—what if some girl in town had caught his eye? Lin'an was full of fresh, pretty young women.
[ZH] 一顿胡思乱想，搞得内心难以平静，越不平静越担心，越担心越不平静，她坐立难安。
[EN] Her thoughts spiraled, stirring restlessness that fed deeper worry, leaving her pacing on pins and needles.
[ZH] 直到传来白风的声音：“佳和郡主，仪王来看您。”
[EN] At last Bai Feng's voice sounded outside: "Princess Jiahe, Prince Yi is here to see you."
[ZH] 等不及喜兰开门，乔梦容急忙推开木门。哥哥出现在面前，她像很久没见到哥哥似的，扑进他的怀里：“哥哥出去许久，害我担心不已。”
[EN] Before Xilan could reach the door, Qiao Mengrong flung it wide. Seeing him standing there, she threw herself into his arms as if they had been parted for months: "Brother was gone so long—you worried me to death!"
[ZH] 真是怕什么来什么，申简辰越担心乔梦容抱住他，越偏偏被她抱住，他还没有任何拒绝的理由。
[EN] What he dreaded most always came true: the more he feared Qiao Mengrong hugging him, the faster she rushed into his arms, leaving him zero excuse to push her away.
[ZH] “我一个大男人，又有功夫，不会有事，以后不许再瞎担心。”
[EN] "I'm a grown man who knows martial arts—nothing's going to happen to me. Quit worrying over nothing."
[ZH] 乔梦容继续抱住哥哥，不愿放开：“哥哥以后不管多晚出去，都带上我，好吗？”
[EN] Clinging tight, Qiao Mengrong refused to let go: "From now on, no matter how late Brother goes out, take me along, all right?"
[ZH] 申简辰岂能愿意？
[EN] How could Shen Jianchen agree to that?
[ZH] “我出去办事，带上你十分不便，我答应你，只要是逛街，都带上你。”
[EN] "Bringing you along on errands isn't convenient. I'll promise you this, though: whenever I go strolling the streets, I'll take you."
[ZH] 哥哥没有一口回绝，乔梦容很欣慰：“哥哥说话算话。”
[EN] Relieved he hadn't turned her down flat, Qiao Mengrong smiled: "Brother keeps his word."
[ZH] “当然。”申简辰不想再让梦容抱下去，故意说，“在外面被文渊抱，现在又被你抱，难道大家都觉得我身板硬朗，想把我当大树一样靠靠？可我这棵大树也会被人靠累。”
[EN] "Of course." Eager to slip out of her embrace, Shen Jianchen teased, "First Wenyuan hugs me outside, and now you're hugging me in here—does everyone think my frame is sturdy as a big tree to lean on? Even a big tree gets tired of being leaned on."
[ZH] 乔梦容放开哥哥，害羞道：“我抱哥哥跟乔公子不一样，我是要嫁给哥哥的人。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong released him with a shy blush: "Me hugging Brother isn't the same as Young Master Qiao—I'm the woman going to marry you."
[ZH] 乔梦容总是强调婚约，好像害怕他忘掉似的。申简辰能理解，可并不喜欢听，因每次听到，会令他觉得人生黯淡无光。
[EN] She kept bringing up their engagement as if terrified he might forget. Shen Jianchen understood why, yet hearing it always seemed to drain the color out of his life.
[ZH] “梦容，我很累，想回屋休息，你也尽快休息吧。”
[EN] "I'm beat, Mengrong—I need to get some sleep. Turn in soon too."
[ZH] 哥哥说完，转身离开。
[EN] With that, he turned and left.
[ZH] 乔梦容站在院内，盯着哥哥离开的背影，直到那背影消失在眼前，才失落地问：“喜兰，有没有觉得哥哥不喜欢我提到婚约？”
[EN] Standing in the yard watching his back vanish into the dark, Qiao Mengrong murmured in low spirits, "Xilan, doesn't it feel like Brother hates it whenever I bring up our engagement?"
[ZH] “郡主多虑，王爷出去得久，肯定是累了。”
[EN] "Princess is overthinking things—His Highness was out late and must just be worn out."
[ZH] “你说得有些道理。”
[EN] "You have a point."
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 第二天一早，陈浩宇爬起床，在永宁居用过早饭，想到知府大人调查谣言传播一事，打算先回陈府一趟，再去朱府找姐姐打探消息。
[EN] First thing the next morning, Chen Haoyu crawled out of bed, ate breakfast at Yongning Residence, and—mindful of the Prefect hunting the rumor-mongers—decided to drop by Chen Manor first before visiting his sister at Zhu Manor to fish for news.
[ZH] 陈家祖上无人当官，生意倒做得遍布各地，作为临安首富，财力不是一般富裕家庭能媲美。
[EN] No ancestor of the Chen clan had ever held public office, yet their businesses spanned the empire; as the wealthiest household in Lin'an, their fortune dwarfed ordinary rich families.
[ZH] 到了这一辈，陈家兄弟共三人，老大陈江林，老二陈江树，老三陈江木。陈家的家业主要由老大陈江林主管，老二老三辅佐。
[EN] In this generation stood three Chen brothers: the eldest, Chen Jianglin; the second, Chen Jiangshu; and the third, Chen Jiangmu. Chen Jianglin ran the family empire, assisted by his two younger brothers.
[ZH] 陈浩宇和陈文心乃陈江林所出，陈江树也有一儿子名唤陈浩东，老三陈江木也有一子，名叫陈浩飞。
[EN] Chen Haoyu and Chen Wenxin were Chen Jianglin's children; Chen Jiangshu had a son named Chen Haodong; and Chen Jiangmu had a son named Chen Haofei.
[ZH] 陈家年纪最大的便是夏氏夏老太太，已过花甲之年，在家中养尊处优，享受天伦之乐。她最心疼浩宇，只因浩宇长相酷似爷爷，老人把对死去老伴儿的情全部寄托在孙子身上，宠得陈浩宇纨绔异常。
[EN] The eldest soul in the house was Old Madam Xia, past sixty and living in pampered comfort. She doted on Haoyu above all others because he was the spitting image of her late husband, pouring all her love for her departed spouse onto her grandson until she spoiled him into an incorrigible rake.
[ZH] 按照往常的做派，陈浩宇能不着家就不着家，考虑到知府大人查传播谣言之人，担心查到自己头上，干脆近期老实些，回家留个好印象，一旦查出，还有家人出面说合。
[EN] Normally Chen Haoyu avoided home like the plague, yet with the Prefect investigating the rumors, he figured he'd better play the dutiful son at home for a stretch to build goodwill—just in case the trail reached him and he needed family cover.
[ZH] 全临安城的人都知道，陈家的府邸最大，里面的建筑和布置，在临安没有哪家能比得上。
