[EN] Everyone in Lin'an knew Chen Manor was the largest estate in town, its halls and gardens unmatched by any other family.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一进陈府大门，碰见的丫鬟小厮都高兴地打招呼：“公子终于回来！”
[EN] The second Chen Haoyu stepped inside Chen Manor's gate, maids and page boys beamed in greeting: "Young Master is home at last!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇只调侃丫鬟：“几日不见，又变漂亮！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu only flirted with the maids: "Gone a few days and you've gotten even prettier!"
[ZH] 丫鬟既高兴又不好意思。
[EN] The maids giggled, flattered yet shy.
[ZH] 正往母亲姚轻琰房中走去，碰见贴身小厮青瓜。
[EN] Heading toward his mother Yao Qingyan's courtyard, he ran right into his personal page boy, Qinggua.
[ZH] 说是贴身小厮，实则不然，陈浩宇只允许他在陈府内跟着伺候，不准走出陈府以外。就连名字青瓜也是陈浩宇随口起的，只因他进府时，陈浩宇手中正好拿着一根青瓜在吃。
[EN] Personal page boy in name only—Chen Haoyu only let him tag along inside Chen Manor, never beyond the front gate. Even the name Qinggua ("Cucumber") had been slapped on him on a whim because Chen Haoyu happened to be crunching a cucumber when the lad entered service.
[ZH] “公子可回来！我终于有人可伺候！”
[EN] "Young Master is back! I have someone to wait on at last!"
[ZH] “你怎么这么贱！就喜欢伺候人？”
[EN] "Why are you such a glutton for punishment? Love waiting on people that much?"
[ZH] “别人我不稀罕，只喜欢伺候公子。”
[EN] "Nobody else—I only like waiting on Young Master!"
[ZH] “别以为我不知道，爹娘把你安插在我身边，是想让你盯着我。”
[EN] "Don't think I don't know Father and Mother planted you beside me to spy on me."
[ZH] “公子误会，小的虽是老爷和夫人安排，心却在公子身上，只求公子以后出去带上我，我也想跟着公子出去玩玩。”
[EN] "Young Master wrongs me! Master and Madam placed me here, sure, yet my heart belongs to Young Master. Just take me along when you go out—I want to go have fun with you too!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇站住：“可是真心话？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu halted. "Mean that?"
[ZH] 青瓜也站住：“比真金白银还真。”
[EN] Qinggua stopped too. "Truer than solid gold and silver!"
[ZH] “既如此，你还在陈府跟着我。”陈浩宇继续往姚轻琰房中而去。
[EN] "Then keep following me around Chen Manor." Chen Haoyu strode on toward Yao Qingyan's rooms.
[ZH] 青瓜失落道：“说半天，公子还是不愿我跟您出去。”
[EN] Qinggua slumped: "All that talk and Young Master still won't let me go out with you."
[ZH] 陈浩宇并不理会，继续往里走。
[EN] Ignoring him, Chen Haoyu kept walking.
[ZH] 陈府很大，姚轻琰住的院子虽是主院，可从大门进入，还需走一段距离。
[EN] Chen Manor was vast; even though Yao Qingyan lived in the main courtyard, reaching it from the front gate took quite a trek.
[ZH] 走着走着，碰到二伯的儿子陈浩东。
[EN] Halfway there, he bumped into his second uncle's son, Chen Haodong.
[ZH] 陈浩宇主动打招呼：“东哥要去哪里？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu greeted him first: "Where are you off to, Brother Dong?"
[ZH] “浩宇回来了。”陈浩东招呼道，然后把他拉到一边，“你整日混在外面，吃喝玩乐，听说还去赌坊，小心大伯揍你。”
[EN] "Haoyu's home!" Returning the greeting, Chen Haodong pulled him aside. "Out carousing day and night, even hitting gambling dens—watch out Eldest Uncle doesn't tan your hide!"
[ZH] “东哥放心，我是什么样，我爹又不是不知道，他已习惯，根本不会当回事。”
[EN] "Relax, Brother Dong. Father knows how I am—he's used to it and won't bat an eye."
[ZH] “听说你在外面搞了许多黄花大姑娘，中意的放在西城的宅子里当丫鬟，不中意的卖到各大青楼，有没有这回事？”
[EN] "Heard you've been messing with young virgins out there, keeping the ones you fancy as maids in your west-city house and selling the rest to brothels—true?"
[ZH] “污蔑！纯属污蔑！倒是有几个放在西城的宅子里当丫鬟，可卖到青楼这回事，还真没有，也不知是谁到处散播谣言，搞得大家都这么认为。”
[EN] "Slander! Pure slander! Sure, I put a few in the west-city house as maids, yet selling girls to brothels? Never happened! No idea who's spreading lies to make everyone think that."
[ZH] “老实告诉我，那几个丫鬟该不会都被你……”陈浩东的脸上露出调侃的表情。
[EN] "Level with me—those maids over there, haven't you already..." A teasing smirk curled Chen Haodong's lip.
[ZH] 陈浩宇毫不掩饰：“那还用说？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu didn't even blink: "Goes without saying!"
[ZH] 陈浩东嘴角露出一丝轻蔑，很快又笑道：“弟啊，以后别再这样，陈家这份家业，大伯迟早会交到你手上，得赶紧浪子回头，好好珍惜老天赐给你的机会。”
[EN] A flash of scorn crossed Chen Haodong's mouth before turning back into a warm grin. "Little brother, knock it off already. Eldest Uncle is going to hand the Chen empire to you sooner or later—turn over a new leaf and treasure the gift Heaven dropped in your lap."
[ZH] “别！陈家又不是只有我一个男儿，有东哥，还有浩飞，你们两个够了，我只求自由自在，吃喝玩乐。不说了，还要去找我娘，回见！”
[EN] "Pass! I'm not the only male heir—there's you and Haofei, plenty between you two! All I want is freedom, good food, and fun. Gotta run see Mother—catch you later!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇带着青瓜，继续往主院走。
[EN] With Qinggua at his heels, Chen Haoyu headed on toward the main courtyard.
[ZH] 陈浩东对着堂弟的背影冷笑道：“浪荡公子，一身纨绔习气，陈家的家业交到你手上岂不玩完？不过，你这样儿倒也好。”
[EN] Watching his cousin's back, Chen Haodong let out a cold sneer. "A hopeless rake soaked in dandy habits—hand the Chen fortune to you and it'd go up in smoke! Still, staying just as you are suits me fine."
[ZH] 姚轻琰一生最大的幸福便是嫁给陈江林，不是因为他有钱，而是因为他对她一心一意。
[EN] Yao Qingyan's greatest joy in life was marrying Chen Jianglin—not for his fortune, but for his undivided devotion.
[ZH] 陈江林从不纳妾室，平时除忙自家生意外，只顾陪她，甚至把陪她看得比生意还重要，不知令多少临安贵妇羡慕。
[EN] Chen Jianglin had never taken a concubine. Outside running the family empire, he spent all his hours by her side, even putting her ahead of business deals, earning the envy of half the noblewomen in Lin'an.
[ZH] 姚轻琰最大的不幸便是生下陈浩宇，不学无术，整日浪荡。她对儿子既不满，又不忍心打他，再说有婆婆护着，她也毫无办法。
[EN] Yao Qingyan's greatest sorrow was bearing Chen Haoyu, a shiftless ne'er-do-well who drifted outside all day. Frustrated as she was, she never had the heart to beat him—and with her mother-in-law shielding him, her hands were tied anyway.
[ZH] 家中唯一能狠下心收拾陈浩宇的只有陈江林，可陈江林孝顺，只要家中老太太发话，他只能停手，加之疼爱妻子，她稍加阻拦，手中的棍子难以落下。
[EN] The only soul in the manor tough enough to discipline Chen Haoyu was Chen Jianglin, yet he was a filial son who dropped the rod whenever Old Madam Xia spoke, and a doting husband who stayed his hand the instant his wife pleaded.
[ZH] 因此，纵得陈浩宇更是无法无天，想在外浪荡多久便浪荡多久。反正陈家有的是宅子，不怕他没地方住，有的是商铺，不怕他没银子支取。
[EN] So Chen Haoyu grew wilder by the day, staying out as long as he pleased—the Chen clan had endless estates to sleep in and endless shops to draw silver from.
[ZH] 夫妻二人正在屋内闲聊，丫鬟映雪进来报告：“老爷，夫人，公子回来，在外面候着。”
[EN] Husband and wife were chatting indoors when the maid Yingxue stepped in to announce, "Master, Madam, Second Young Master is home and waiting outside."
[ZH] 陈江林正对着夫人眉开眼笑，听到不成器的儿子回来，立刻拉下脸：“让他进来！”
[EN] Beaming at his wife a second ago, Chen Jianglin's face snapped grim at mention of his wayward son. "Send him in!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“老爷别这样，儿子好不容易回来，你老给他脸色看，他会越来越不喜欢待在家里。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan chided him: "Don't be like that, Husband. Our son comes home once in a blue moon—keep glaring at him and he'll hate coming home even more."
[ZH] 陈江林立刻露出笑容：“你说的有道理。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin broke into a smile at once. "Fair point."
[ZH] 陈浩宇一进屋，扑进母亲怀里：“娘，儿子好想您。”
[EN] Bursting through the door, Chen Haoyu threw himself into his mother's arms: "Mother, I missed you so much!"
[ZH] “又骗我，你要是想娘，就不该在外面鬼混。”姚轻琰语气中略带批评，可眼神却很温柔。
[EN] "Sweet-talking me again! If you missed your mother, you wouldn't be out carousing." Though her words scolded, Yao Qingyan's eyes brimmed with warmth.
[ZH] “我不骗您，这次回来儿子打算安心待在家里。”
[EN] "Cross my heart—this time I'm staying home for good!"
[ZH] “真是太阳打西边出来，你能在家待上两日我就烧高香了。”
[EN] "The sun must be rising in the west! If you last two whole days at home, I'll burn incense in thanks."
[ZH] 陈江林还是不由板起脸：“别一回来，就往你娘身上贴，老实站好，听我问话。”
[EN] Unable to hold back, Chen Jianglin darkened his brow: "Stop hanging on your mother the second you walk in! Stand up straight and answer my questions."
[ZH] 当着儿子的面，老爷的面子还是要给的，姚轻琰说：“快去站好，好好回答你爹的问题。”
[EN] Giving her husband face in front of their son, Yao Qingyan said, "Go stand straight and answer your father."
[ZH] 陈浩宇老实站好：“爹，您问吧。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu snapped upright. "Ask away, Father."
[ZH] 陈江林语气严厉：“老实交代，最近在外面都干些什么？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin spoke in a stern tone: "Confess—what have you been up to out there?"
[ZH] “吃吃喝喝玩玩之类，也没干什么坏事。”
[EN] "Eating, drinking, having fun—nothing bad."
[ZH] “难道没欺负良家妇女？没逛青楼？没去赌坊？”
[EN] "Not harassing decent girls? Not visiting brothels? Not haunting gambling dens?"
[ZH] 父亲的问题一个个问在点子上，陈浩宇不敢承认：“爹，儿子贪玩儿，喜欢不着家，但也不可能去那些地方，肯定有人诬陷我。”
[EN] Every question hit dead center, yet Chen Haoyu dared not admit a word. "Father, I love having fun and staying out, sure, yet I'd never touch places like that—somebody's framing me!"
[ZH] “就你那样儿，还怕被人诬陷！”
[EN] "With a reputation like yours, who'd need to frame you?!"
[ZH] 说着陈江林气不打一处来，想站起来扇儿子一巴掌，被夫人的眼神阻止。他努力平复心情：“听你姐姐说，你连朱家姑娘都欺负，是也不是？”
[EN] Tempers flaring, Chen Jianglin moved to stand and box his son's ears, only for a look from his wife to stop him. Steeling his breath, he said, "Your sister told me you even picked on Miss Zhu—true or not?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇一脸不满：“是她欺负我好不好？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu pouted: "She's the one who picked on me, all right?!"
[ZH] “朱家姑娘是有名的女词人，才华横溢，以后见她要恭敬些，多跟人家学习，爹不图你满腹经纶，哪怕你以后娶一位像朱家姑娘那样知书达理，又有才华的姑娘回来，我也心满意足。”
[EN] "Miss Zhu is a famed female ci poet brimming with talent! Treat her with respect when you see her and learn from her! I don't expect you to turn into a grand scholar—even if you just marry a refined, talented girl half like Miss Zhu someday, I'll die happy."
[ZH] 陈浩宇很不屑：“爹，您哪只眼睛看到她知书达理？您出去听听，外面的人全在骂她，难听死了。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu curled his lip: "Which eye of yours sees her as refined, Father? Step outside and listen—the whole city's cursing her out! Filthy stuff!"
[ZH] “市井之谈岂能当真？我又不是没见过朱家姑娘？她是什么样的人我难道不知？再说你姐姐经常在我们面前夸她，我与你娘可以怀疑别人，也不能怀疑你姐姐！”
[EN] "Since when do we trust street gossip? Haven't I met Miss Zhu myself? Don't I know what kind of girl she is? Besides, your sister praises her to the skies—your mother and I might doubt anyone else, never your sister!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰笑道：“你爹说得对，你姐姐很少夸人，能入她眼之人肯定错不了。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan smiled. "Your father's right. Your sister rarely praises anyone; someone who wins her over can't be bad."
[ZH] 正说着，陈文心带丫鬟抱着莱宝进来……
[EN] Right on cue, Chen Wenxin walked in with a maid carrying Laibao...

#CH 114
[ZH] 淑真被全城人议论，连带朱家也被大家非议，公爹朱晞颜心情不好，整个朱府气氛实在压抑。陈文心怕一不小心惹公爹生气，又引得婆婆伤心，便想回娘家住几日。
[EN] With the whole city gossiping about Shuzhen and dragging the Zhu family down too, her father-in-law Zhu Xiyan stayed in a black mood, smothering Zhu Manor in gloom. Afraid of crossing her father-in-law or making her mother-in-law cry more, Chen Wenxin decided to go stay at her maiden home for a few days.
[ZH] 征得朱景元同意，与公爹婆婆辞别，只说奶奶年纪大了，喜欢莱宝，叫她回去住几日。
[EN] With Zhu Jingyuan's blessing, she bade her in-laws farewell, claiming her grandmother was getting on in years, missed Laibao, and had asked her back for a short visit.
[ZH] 朱晞颜与向夫人没有理由阻拦，只嘱咐她回去后向亲家问好。
[EN] Neither Zhu Xiyan nor Madam Xiang had reason to refuse, only asking her to pass their regards to the Chen elders.
[ZH] 陈文心一进屋，见弟弟站着，猜到父亲正在训斥他，故意说：“吆！你今儿倒老实在家，怎么？又惹爹生气？”
[EN] Stepping into the room to find her brother standing at attention, Chen Wenxin guessed Father was lecturing him and teased, "Well, well! Look who's behaving at home today! Did you make Father mad again?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不满道：“姐姐说话的语气是几个意思？好像你没见过我似的？我不回陈府，却常去朱府看望你和姐夫，还有莱宝。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu grumbled, "What's with that tone, Sister? Acting like you never see me! Even when I don't come back to Chen Manor, I stop by Zhu Manor all the time to see you, Brother-in-law, and Laibao."
[ZH] “这话倒没错。”陈文心走到父亲身边，“爹，您别跟他置气，好不容易一家人聚齐。”
[EN] "Fair enough." Stepping beside her father, Chen Wenxin said, "Don't waste anger on him, Father—it's rare to have the whole family together."
[ZH] 陈江林见到女儿自是开心：“看在你的面子上，我不与那臭小子计较，改日再收拾他。”
[EN] Seeing his daughter lit up Chen Jianglin's face: "For your sake, I'll let the brat off today and settle accounts another time."
[ZH] 姚轻琰招呼丫鬟把莱宝抱到她身边。
[EN] Yao Qingyan beckoned the maid to bring Laibao over to her lap.
[ZH] 孩子可爱的模样惹得外公外婆甚是喜爱，把陈浩宇扔到一边。
[EN] Charmed by their adorable grandson, both grandparents forgot all about Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 陈浩宇自然开心，姐姐又回来，省得到朱府跑一趟，便拉起陈文心：“姐，你出来，我问你个事儿？”
[EN] Delighted his sister had saved him a trip to Zhu Manor, Chen Haoyu tugged Chen Wenxin's sleeve: "Come outside a sec, Sister—got something to ask you."
[ZH] 陈文心跟弟弟走到屋外。青瓜也屁颠屁颠尾随出去，反正在陈府，公子没理由不让他跟着。
[EN] Chen Wenxin followed him out, with Qinggua trotting right behind—inside Chen Manor, Young Master had no excuse to shoo him off.
[ZH] 来到院中，陈文心问道：“为何拉我出来？”
[EN] Out in the courtyard, Chen Wenxin asked, "Why drag me out here?"
[ZH] “本来打算看完爹娘，再到朱府找你，结果你回来，省得我再跑一趟。”
[EN] "I was going to swing by Zhu Manor after seeing Father and Mother, so you saved me the trip."
[ZH] 陈文心双眼中充满怀疑的目光：“你怎么突然变得如此懂事？还知道回家看爹娘？”
[EN] Suspicion narrowed Chen Wenxin's eyes. "Since when did you grow so thoughtful, coming home to see our parents?"
[ZH] “我一直很懂事，决定近期好好待在陈府，陪陪家人。”
[EN] "I've always been thoughtful! Decided to stay put at Chen Manor for a while and keep the family company."
[ZH] 陈文心更加怀疑：“真是太阳打西边出来，怎么感觉那么不真实？你该不会是犯了什么天大的错误，跑回来求庇护？”
[EN] Suspicion sharpened further: "The sun really is rising in the west—why does this feel fake? Don't tell me you pulled some colossal stunt and ran home for shelter!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇慨叹这位姐姐实在了解他，嘴上绝不能承认：“哪有的事儿？老在外面多没意思。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu marveled at how well his sister read him, though he'd never admit a thing: "No way! Hanging around outside gets boring."
[ZH] “快说，找我什么事？”
[EN] "Spit it out—what did you want to ask?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇小心打探：“姐，为什么外面人都在议论朱淑真？还把她说得那么难听？我都快听不下去。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu probed with care: "Sister, why is everyone outside gossiping about Zhu Shuzhen? Saying such ugly stuff I can't even stand listening!"
[ZH] 陈文心气愤道：“连你都听不下去，何况我公爹和婆婆？也不知淑真得罪了谁，这么编排她，连朱家也深受牵连。我公爹怀疑有人故意诋毁朱家，正派人查探。对了浩宇，你经常在外混，认识的人多，没事帮着打听打听，到底谁这么可恶！要是找到，我定在那人脸上扇几个耳光，再把他多踹几脚！”
[EN] Indignation blazed on Chen Wenxin's face: "Even you can't stand it—imagine my father-in-law and mother-in-law! Who knows who Shuzhen offended to get smeared like this, dragging the whole Zhu family down with her! Father-in-law suspects foul play and has men investigating. Hey, Haoyu—you roam outside all the time and know loads of people. Help sniff around for whoever's behind this! Catch that rat and I'll slap their face raw and kick them black and blue!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇仿佛已感觉到脸上和身上的疼痛：“姐，没必要这么狠吧？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu practically felt the slaps and kicks landing on his own hide: "Uh, Sister, isn't that a bit harsh?"
[ZH] “哼！这还是轻的！要是被我公爹抓到，肯定要将那人打得皮开肉绽！”陈文心仿佛已抓住散播谣言之人，脸上充满恨恨的表情。
[EN] "Hmph, that's getting off light! If Father-in-law catches them, he'll flay them alive!" Scowl fierce, Chen Wenxin looked ready to shred the culprit on the spot.
[ZH] 陈浩宇更害怕，故作镇定：“依我看知府大人别费那功夫，哪有什么散播谣言之人？都是朱淑真写那句子给害的。她不胡写，会有人议论她？还有她平日喜欢跟临安才子鬼混，才搞得名声大臭，就怕知府大人抓来抓来，最后还是抓到自家闺女头上，到时让人笑掉大牙！”
[EN] Nerves jangling, Chen Haoyu put on a cool front: "If you ask me, the Prefect shouldn't waste his energy. What rumor-monger? Zhu Shuzhen brought it on herself with that line of verse! If she hadn't written trash and hung out with Lin'an's scholars all the time, would her name stink? Careful the Prefect digs and digs only to land right back on his own daughter—what a laughingstock that'd be!"
[ZH] 陈文心仔细观察弟弟的表情，觉得他很奇怪：“刚才你说话还向着淑真，怎么态度变得这么快？”
[EN] Studying her brother's face, Chen Wenxin frowned: "One second ago you were defending Shuzhen—why flip your tune so fast?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇努力掩饰：“我这人向来喜欢有什么说什么，姐姐别这么看着我，怪难为情的。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu scrambled to cover: "You know me, I speak my mind! Quit staring at me like that, Sister—it's embarrassing."
[ZH] 陈文心用手指点一下弟弟的额头：“你这小子，什么时候知道难为情？既然回来，还不快去见奶奶？她肯定想你想得不行。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin poked his forehead. "Since when does a rascal like you know embarrassment? Now that you're home, go see Grandmother—she's been missing you nonstop."
[ZH] “奶奶是要看，不过还要向姐姐打听一件事。”
[EN] "I'm going, I'm going—just tell me one more thing first."
[ZH] “说。”
[EN] "What?"
[ZH] “谣言对朱淑真影响有多大？”尽管陈浩宇已听说，还需从姐姐口中得到确切消息。
[EN] "How badly did the rumors hit Zhu Shuzhen?" Though he had already heard, he wanted confirmation straight from his sister.
[ZH] “多大？大了去了！乔家取消提亲，淑真也被公爹赶出家门，你说大不大？”
[EN] "How badly? Catastrophic! The Qiao family canceled the proposal, and Father-in-law threw Shuzhen out of the house—what do you think?!"
[ZH] 得到姐姐的肯定，陈浩宇更是开心不已，心想：“朱淑真啊朱淑真，跟我斗，怎么样，服了吧，哈哈哈……！”
[EN] Elation burst inside Chen Haoyu. "Ha! Zhu Shuzhen, try messing with me again—how does defeat taste?! Hahaha!"
[ZH] 陈文心诧异地看着弟弟：“浩宇，我怎么觉得你挺幸灾乐祸的？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin eyed him askance: "Haoyu, why do you look like you're gloating?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续掩饰：“没……哪有？我去看奶奶。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu hurried to mask his grin: "Me? No way! Going to see Grandmother now!"
[ZH] 陈文心一把拽住弟弟：“我警告你，再跟淑真合不来，也不许在这个时候欺负她，她已经够不幸的，听到没？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin caught his sleeve. "I'm warning you—no matter how much you clash with Shuzhen, don't you dare pick on her while she's down! She's suffered enough—hear me?"
[ZH] “听到听到，你弟弟我都打算在家里好好待着，近期不会跟朱淑真碰面，何谈欺负？再说即使跟她碰面，只有她欺负我的份儿，我哪敢欺负她？”陈浩宇挣脱开来，一溜烟跑掉。
[EN] "Heard you, heard you! I'm staying home anyway, so I won't even cross paths with her—how could I pick on her? Besides, whenever we cross paths, she's the one beating me up!" Breaking free, Chen Haoyu bolted off in a flash.
[ZH] “这浑小子！”
[EN] "That little menace!"
[ZH] 青瓜说：“大小姐，我去陪公子。”
[EN] Qinggua offered, "Eldest Miss, I'll go keep Young Master company."
[ZH] “去吧，把他盯紧些，别让他干坏事。”
[EN] "Go on, and keep a close eye on him so he doesn't cause trouble."
[ZH] 青瓜答应着跑开。
[EN] Qinggua nodded and scampered off.
[ZH] 陈文心笑着回屋，让丫鬟把莱宝抱出去玩儿，坐下与自家父母聊起朱家之事。
[EN] Returning inside with a smile, Chen Wenxin sent the maid out to play with Laibao, then sat down with her parents to talk over the Zhu family's troubles.
[ZH] “爹、娘，朱家的事你们应该听说了吧？”
[EN] "Father, Mother, you must have heard what happened to the Zhu family."
[ZH] 姚轻琰叹道：“唉，怎能没听说？满城风言风语的，可苦了朱家姑娘。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan sighed, "How could we miss it? Gossip's flying everywhere—poor Miss Zhu."
[ZH] 陈江林问：“你公爹打算如何解决此事？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin asked, "How does your father-in-law plan to handle it?"
[ZH] “我听景元说，公爹已安排人彻查散播谣言之人。”
[EN] "Jingyuan told me Father-in-law sent men to track down whoever started the rumors."
[ZH] 陈江林说：“如何能查出？谣言你传我，我传你，传到最后，谁还记得谁是第一个说的？我看你公爹还不如不去理会，谣言这玩意儿来得快也去得快，时间一长，大家自然忘记。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin shook his head. "How can you trace that? A rumor passes from one mouth to the next until nobody remembers who spoke first. Better for your father-in-law to ignore it—rumors flare fast and die fast. Give it time and folks will forget."
[ZH] “我只担心淑真这孩子被乔家放鸽子，以后可怎么嫁人？她怎么这么命苦？遇到一段不幸的婚姻也就罢了，好不容易有好人家上门提亲，又泡汤。”姚轻琰说着开始伤感，好像遇到此事的是自家闺女。
[EN] "I just worry about poor Shuzhen getting stood up by the Qiaos—how will she find a husband now? Why is her fate so hard? First an unhappy marriage, and just when a fine match comes along, it falls apart!" Grief misted Yao Qingyan's eyes as if it were her own daughter.
[ZH] 陈江林笑着说：“你娘就是心善，见不得别人受苦。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin smiled: "Your mother's too soft-hearted—can't bear seeing anyone suffer."
[ZH] “哪是别人？是亲家的女儿。”姚轻琰强调道，“要不改日去亲家走一趟，安慰安慰文心的公爹和婆婆？”
[EN] "Not just anyone—our in-laws' daughter!" Yao Qingyan insisted. "Why don't we pay the Zhus a visit in a day or two to comfort Wenxin's in-laws?"
[ZH] 陈文心立刻说：“还是别！现在去不合适，等等再说。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin waved her hands at once: "Better not! Going right now would be awkward—wait a bit."
[ZH] 陈江林十分赞同：“的确不合适，等谣言淡些，朱家人的情绪慢慢平缓，再把很多事情想明白，咱们再去，会更好些。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin agreed: "True. Wait until the gossip dies down and the Zhus calm down and sort things out, then we'll call on them."
[ZH] 姚轻琰听出些道理，仔细思索，也觉出不合适的意味来，便点头同意。
[EN] Weighing their words, Yao Qingyan saw the sense in it and nodded.
[ZH] 早有丫鬟向夏老太太报告二公子回府的消息，老太太拄起拐杖，就要到前边去寻孙子，丫鬟只好扶着她往出走。
[EN] Meanwhile, a maid had already carried news of Second Young Master's return to Old Madam Xia. Grabbing her cane, the old woman started out to find her grandson, her maid hurrying to steady her.
[ZH] 刚走到门口，正巧碰到孙子过来。
[EN] Right at the doorway, they bumped into Chen Haoyu walking up.
[ZH] 陈浩宇扑到老太太怀里：“奶奶，想死我了。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu threw himself into her arms: "Grandmother, I missed you to death!"
[ZH] 夏老太太边笑边批评：“又哄我，想我却不沾家，整日待在外面，还好陈家在外面宅子多，要不你只能流浪街头。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia laughed while scolding him: "Sweet-talking me again! Missing me while staying away all the time—good thing the Chen family owns houses everywhere, or you'd be sleeping in the streets!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇扶住夏老太太：“奶奶，我扶您进屋，咱们到屋里说话。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu steadied her elbow: "Let me help you inside, Grandmother—we'll talk indoors."
[ZH] 进屋后，丫鬟把老太太在榻上安置好。
[EN] Once inside, the maid settled Old Madam Xia onto the couch.
[ZH] ﻿陈浩宇也脱掉鞋，盘腿坐好，命令丫鬟：“我渴了，去，给我泡壶茶来。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu kicked off his shoes, climbed up cross-legged, and barked at a maid: "I'm parched—go brew a pot of tea."
[ZH] 丫鬟立刻去忙活，青瓜站在一旁听婆孙两个聊天儿。
[EN] The maid hurried off while Qinggua stood beside them listening to grandmother and grandson chat.
[ZH] 夏老太太抓住孙子的手：“让奶奶好好看看，瘦了，再贪玩儿也要把自个身子照顾好。”
[EN] Holding her grandson's hand, Old Madam Xia peered close: "Let Grandmother look at you—you've lost weight! Have fun all you want, just take care of your health."
[ZH] “奶奶放心，好着呢。”
[EN] "Don't worry, Grandmother, I'm fit as a fiddle."
[ZH] “你老实告诉我，在外面都忙些什么？”
[EN] "Tell Grandmother the truth: what keep you busy out there?"
[ZH] 他在外面干的那些勾当，岂能对奶奶提？陈浩宇哄骗道：“结交结交朋友，偶尔跟临安才子们参加参加诗会。”
[EN] How could he tell his grandmother about his real antics? Chen Haoyu spun a yarn: "Making friends, attending poetry salons now and then with Lin'an's scholars."
[ZH] “真不错！我孙子竟然也能参加诗会，我听说诗会不是谁都能参加，看来我孙子是越来越有学问，说不定将来还能考个功名，光耀门楣。”
[EN] "Splendid! My grandson even attends poetry salons! I heard not just anyone gets invited—my boy's turning into a real scholar! Maybe one day you'll pass the exams and bring honor to our house!"
[ZH] 被奶奶这么一说，陈浩宇更觉心虚。正好丫鬟把茶泡好，倒了一盏，给陈浩宇放在桌上。陈浩宇端起茶喝起来，掩饰心虚的表情。
[EN] Guilt prickled Chen Haoyu under her praise. Just in time, the maid brought tea and poured a cup; Chen Haoyu lifted it to his lips to hide his sheepish face.
[ZH] 夏老太太见到孙子，满心喜爱，至于其他，根本未注意，只顾说：“你堂哥已成亲，你什么时候也是给奶奶娶个孙媳妇回来？奶奶可等着抱重孙呢！”
[EN] Doting eyes glued to her favorite grandson, Old Madam Xia noticed nothing and rattled on: "Your older cousin is already wed—when are you bringing Grandmother home a granddaughter-in-law? I'm waiting to hold a great-grandson!"
[ZH] 话题转移，陈浩宇放下茶盏：“奶奶放心，迟早我会把孙媳妇给您娶回来，绝对是您喜欢的类型。”
[EN] Glad for the change of subject, Chen Haoyu set his cup down. "Don't worry, Grandmother—sooner or later I'll bring you home a granddaughter-in-law you'll adore."
[ZH] 夏老太太更开心：“难道你有中意的姑娘？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia beamed brighter: "Do you have a girl in mind already?"
[ZH] “现在还没有，等有了第一个告诉奶奶。”
[EN] "Not yet. The second I do, Grandmother will be the first to know."
[ZH] “既然没有，我会催你爹娘把你的婚事当回事，也在临安的大家闺秀中挑一挑。”
[EN] "Since you don't, I'll push your parents to get serious and look over Lin'an's noble daughters for you."
[ZH] “还是别，临安的大家闺秀一个个十分清高，说起话来扭扭捏捏，还以为自己很不错，其实都很没意思。”
[EN] "Pass! Those highborn ladies in Lin'an are stuck-up prudes who talk all coy and think they're hot stuff—total snoozefests."
[ZH] “我孙子该不会心中已有人选？呵呵……！”夏老太太眯着眼，笑得合不拢嘴。
[EN] "Sounds like my boy has someone in mind after all, haha!" Old Madam Xia squinted with glee.
[ZH] 关于未来娶谁，陈浩宇从未想过：“哪有？我还没玩够，怎会那么傻？找个人限制我自由？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu had never given marriage a single thought: "No way! I haven't had my fill of fun yet—why would I be dumb enough to shackle myself?"
[ZH] “我听说你买了很多丫鬟，还逛青楼，可别找个丫鬟或青楼女子回来，奶奶再疼你，都不会答应。”夏老太太话语充满批评，却笑容满面，谁让他的二孙子看着就让人喜欢，老人家是看也看不够。
[EN] "I heard you've been buying up maids and visiting brothels—don't you dare bring home a maid or a courtesan! Much as I spoil you, I'll never allow that." Even while lecturing, her face overflowed with affection; she could never get enough of her second grandson.
[ZH] 陈浩宇半真半假地说：“奶奶别听他人乱说，我没买丫鬟，也不可能逛青楼。将来我要娶妻也得娶自个儿喜欢的，家世必须不错，至于丫鬟和青楼女子只配陪我玩玩儿。”
[EN] Mixing half-truth with lie, Chen Haoyu vowed, "Don't listen to gossip, Grandmother. I didn't buy maids and I don't haunt brothels. When I marry, it'll be a girl I love from a solid family—maids and courtesans are only for passing the time."
[ZH] “知道就好，不许乱玩！”
[EN] "Good that you know! Don't go wild!"
[ZH] “奶奶放心，孙儿什么都明白。”
[EN] "Relax, Grandmother, I know the score."
[ZH] 夏老太太对陈浩宇的话深信不疑，觉得他既懂事，又有头脑，跟他的死去的爷爷不相上下。
[EN] Old Madam Xia swallowed every word without a trace of doubt, convinced her grandson was every bit as sharp and sensible as his late grandfather.

#CH 115
[ZH] 经过一晚上休息，千里寻的心情并不比昨日好多少。
[EN] A full night's rest left Qianli Xun in no better spirits than yesterday.
[ZH] 大家如何议论她并不重要，唯一担心今后如何才能与文渊在一起。
[EN] How folk gossiped about her didn't matter; all that weighed on her was how she and Wenyuan could ever be together now.
[ZH] 穿越而来的时代，不是相爱之人靠自由恋爱就能走到一起的时代，父母若不同意，一切只会徒劳。
[EN] This era she had crossed into was not an age of free love—without parents' consent, romance ran straight into a brick wall.
[ZH] 流言不早不晚，偏偏在提亲之日被两家知晓，难道老天根本不想让她与文渊在一起？想到这些，千里寻更加痛恨流言。越痛恨，越想去亲耳听听，再亲眼目睹大家议论的样子。
[EN] Why had the rumors hit both families on the exact day of the proposal, not a day earlier or later? Did Heaven really mean to keep her and Wenyuan apart? The more she brooded, the fiercer her hatred for those rumors burned—and the stronger her urge grew to go hear and see the gossipers with her own eyes.
[ZH] 用过早饭，还不到与文渊约定的时间，她打算去街上走走。
[EN] After breakfast, with hours to spare before meeting Wenyuan, she decided to take a turn down the street.
[ZH] “苦荞，给我找身男装。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, go find me a set of men's clothes."
[ZH] “小姐要男装做什么？”
[EN] "What does Miss want men's clothes for?"
[ZH] “别问那么多，找来便是。”
[EN] "Quit asking questions and just fetch some."
[ZH] 苦荞感到为难：“小姐的衣柜里一身男装都没有。”
[EN] Kuqiao hesitated: "There isn't a single set of men's clothes in your wardrobe, Miss."
[ZH] 千里寻想到一个好办法：“去问外面的下人要一身。”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought of a quick fix: "Go ask one of the male servants outside for a set."
[ZH] “小姐岂能穿下人的衣裳？”
[EN] "How could Miss wear a servant's clothes?!"
[ZH] “让你要就去要，别管那么多。”
[EN] "Do as I say and don't fuss."
[ZH] 苦荞只好出去找下人要身男装。
[EN] Left no choice, Kuqiao went off to borrow a man's outfit from a servant.
[ZH] 夏果并不关心小姐要男装之事，今日的早茶由她来做，正专心体验做茶的快乐，她觉得做茶时自己仿佛变成大家闺秀的模样。
[EN] Unbothered by the clothes request, Xiaguo was busy whisking morning tea, savoring the feeling that whisking tea made her look just like a refined young lady.
[ZH] 茶做好后，给小姐端到跟前，夏果小心问：“小姐今日要见乔公子，我可否跟着伺候？”
[EN] Bringing the finished bowl over to her mistress, Xiaguo asked with care, "Miss is meeting Young Master Qiao today—may I come along to wait on you?"
[ZH] “你是我的贴身丫鬟，当然得跟我一起。”茶到嘴边，千里寻不想喝一口，将茶盏放在桌上。
[EN] "You're my personal maid; of course you're coming." Raising the tea bowl to her lips, Qianli Xun lost the urge to drink and set it right back down.
[ZH] 夏果担忧地问：“小姐对我做的茶不满意？”
[EN] Alarm flashed in Xiaguo's eyes: "Isn't Miss pleased with the tea I made?"
[ZH] “不是，你别多想，我只是突然不想喝而已。”
[EN] "No, don't overthink it—I just don't feel thirsty right now."
[ZH] 早饭时，小姐也没吃多少，夏果清楚一定是因与乔公子的婚事受阻，小姐心情不佳所致。
[EN] Her mistress had barely touched breakfast either; Xiaguo knew the wrecked marriage proposal had drained all appetite from her.
[ZH] 苦荞找来一身干净的下人衣裳给小姐换上，又把她头上的发饰取下，将头发绾成男子的发髻。
[EN] Kuqiao returned with a clean set of servant's clothes, helped her mistress change, removed her hairpins, and bound her hair into a man's topknot.
[ZH] 千里寻照照镜子，很满意：“我出去一趟，你们两个不许跟着。”
[EN] Checking the mirror, Qianli Xun nodded in approval. "I'm stepping out for a bit. Neither of you is to follow."
[ZH] “小姐要去做什么？”夏果担心小姐偷偷去见乔公子。
[EN] "Where is Miss going?" Xiaguo worried her mistress was sneaking off to see Young Master Qiao alone.
[ZH] “我去街上转转，听听别人怎么议论我。”千里寻扔下两个丫鬟独自走出望月轩。
[EN] "Taking a turn down the street to hear what folks are saying about me." Leaving both maids behind, Qianli Xun walked out of Wangyue Pavilion alone.
[ZH] 苦荞叹道：“小姐真可怜，难道以后出去都要女扮男装？外面议论的人实在可恶！”
[EN] Kuqiao sighed, "Poor Miss—will she have to dress like a man every time she steps outside now? Those gossipers out there are vile!"
[ZH] 夏果想不通，小姐为何要亲自上杆子去听：“是可恶，昨个我出去买东西，听到有人议论，可难听，我都听不下去，把议论的人臭骂一顿，小姐要是亲耳听到，岂不更难过？”
[EN] Xiaguo couldn't fathom why her mistress would go hunt for insults: "Vile is right! Yesterday when I went shopping I heard folks spewing filth so awful I cursed them out on the spot. If Miss hears it with her own ears, she'll only hurt worse!"
[ZH] “那还用说。”苦荞瞧到桌上的茶盏，看样子里面的茶一口都没喝，“小姐竟然没喝茶就走，她得多伤心。”
[EN] "No kidding." Glancing at the untouched tea bowl on the table, Kuqiao sighed again: "Miss didn't even take one sip before leaving—she must be heartbroken."
[ZH] 千里寻步行来到街上，她不打算走得太远，附近逛逛即可，之所以换上男装，只是不想被人认出而已。
[EN] Walking out onto the avenue, Qianli Xun meant only to stroll nearby, dressed as a man just to avoid being recognized.
[ZH] 在大街上走了半晌，人来人往，没有听到任何议论朱淑真的言论，千里寻的心情变得轻松许多。
[EN] Walking for a good half-hour through bustling crowds without hearing a single syllable about Zhu Shuzhen eased the knot in her chest.
[ZH] 早晨未喝茶，多走几步后，倍感口渴，正好不远处有家茶肆，她决定进去坐坐。
[EN] Having skipped morning tea, thirst crept up after her long walk. Spotting a teahouse ahead, she stepped inside for a seat.
[ZH] 这家茶肆为招揽生意，也有说书表演。
[EN] To draw customers, this teahouse featured live storytelling too.
[ZH] 一走进茶肆，听到说话艺人说的正是她写的话本，心情不由自主好了许多，问伙计要壶茶，坐下边喝边听。
[EN] The instant she crossed the threshold, she heard the storyteller reciting her own huaben story! Her spirits lifted at once; ordering a pot of tea, she sat down to sip and listen.
[ZH] 只听说话艺人讲道：
[EN] The storyteller's voice carried across the room:
[ZH] 说也奇怪，自从华家生下女儿，地里便长出各色花卉，华家认为是祥兆。果然是祥兆，从此华家便以卖花为生，日子过得越来越好，成为乡下富户。
[EN] Strange to tell, the very day the Hua family welcomed their daughter, flowers of every hue sprang up across their fields—a sign of heaven's favor, they believed. And auspicious it proved! From then on the Huas sold flowers for a living, prospering year by year until they became a wealthy country household.
[ZH] 华红香长到十五岁时，城主的儿子武玄英快过十五岁诞辰，想要买些花卉装点宴席。派出去的人打听来，打听去，只有华家种植的花卉最好，而且传言只要华家姑娘在一旁，花不仅开得更艳，还不会凋谢，负责生日宴的人便向华家订下许多花卉……
[EN] When Hua Hongxiang turned fifteen, Wu Xuanying, son of the City Lord, was approaching his fifteenth birthday banquet and needed blossoms to deck the hall. His stewards searched high and low and found no flowers finer than the Huas'—and rumor said whenever the Hua girl stood nearby, blooms opened brighter and refused to wilt, so the banquet stewards placed a huge order with the Hua house...
[ZH] 千里寻瞧瞧周围听书的人，哪有人议论朱淑真？都在认真听，也是她写的故事太吸引人。
[EN] Glancing around the packed tables, Qianli Xun saw nobody gossiping about Zhu Shuzhen—everyone sat hooked on her story!
[ZH] 她向伙计招招手。
[EN] She waved a waiter over.
[ZH] 伙计来到跟前：“客官有什么吩咐？”
[EN] The waiter stepped up. "What can I get you, sir?"
[ZH] 千里寻装出男子的声音：“你们茶肆怎么也说千里寻的本子？”
[EN] Pitching her voice low like a young man, Qianli Xun asked, "Your teahouse performs Qianli Xun's scripts too?"
[ZH] 伙计夸道：“看来客官是爱听书之人，竟也知千里寻才子的美名，他写的故事很受欢迎，各大茶肆酒楼都在说。”
[EN] The waiter beamed: "Looks like young sir loves storytelling—you know the brilliant Qianli Xun's name too! His tales are all the rage; every big teahouse and tavern in town is telling them."
[ZH] “千里寻现在很有名气吗？”
[EN] "Is Qianli Xun really that famous now?"
[ZH] “小有名气。”
[EN] "Quite the rising name!"
[ZH] 听到小有名气，千里寻一开心，从怀里掏出两块碎银：“拿着，赏你的。”
[EN] Delighted to hear "rising name," Qianli Xun pulled two bits of loose silver from her sleeve: "Here, for you."
[ZH] 伙计更高兴：“谢客官！”
[EN] The waiter grinned wide: "Thank you, young sir!"
[ZH] 走进两名男子，瞧瞧周围没地方可坐，只有千里寻的桌子坐着一人，上前道：“小兄弟，我们能否跟您凑个桌？”
[EN] Just then two men walked in, scanned the packed room for empty tables, and stepped up to Qianli Xun's table: "Mind if we share your table, little brother?"
[ZH] 千里寻并不介意：“当然可以，二位随便坐。”
[EN] Qianli Xun waved them over: "Go right ahead, make yourselves at home."
[ZH] 两人坐下后，也要壶茶，品完几口，听会儿书，便没了兴致，轻声聊起天儿来。
[EN] Sitting down, the two ordered tea, listened for a few minutes, then grew bored and began chatting in low voices.
[ZH] “这本子我听过好几遍，故事再熟悉不过，华红香在生日宴上第一次遇见武玄英，可惜前世记忆已无，两人都认不出对方，还闹出些误会。”
[EN] "Heard this script three times already—know it by heart! Hua Hongxiang meets Wu Xuanying for the first time at the birthday banquet, yet with past memories erased, neither recognizes the other and misunderstandings fly."
[ZH] “不知接下来故事如何？只等千里寻才子写出来，我等才知。”
[EN] "Wonder what happens next—we'll have to wait for Master Qianli Xun to write more."
[ZH] 千里寻听后，更加高兴，谣言没听到，反而听到自己的大名逐渐被人所知：“两位朋友竟也知道千里寻？”
[EN] Delight bubbled in Qianli Xun: not a whisper of rumor, only praise for her pen name! "You two know Qianli Xun as well?"
[ZH] “那是自然，喜欢听书的人都知道。”
[EN] "Of course—everyone who loves storytelling knows him!"
[ZH] 千里寻继续问：“他现在很出名吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun fished for more: "Pretty famous, is he?"
[ZH] “小有名气，不过，再有名也盖不过朱家姑娘，呵呵……！”
[EN] "Making a name, sure—though no fame beats Miss Zhu's right now! Haha!"
[ZH] 对方的笑声搞得千里寻笑容全无，她不再说话，继续喝茶听书。书没听进多少，二人的议论倒是全部灌进耳朵。
[EN] His laugh wiped the smile clean off Qianli Xun's face. Clamping her lips shut, she sipped her tea—ignoring the storyteller as every syllable from the two men poured straight into her ears.
[ZH] “是啊，最近大家都在议论。说起朱家姑娘，实在大胆，一点儿大家闺秀的风范都没有。”
[EN] "Tell me about it—everyone's talking! That Miss Zhu sure has nerve, not an ounce of noble-lady grace."
[ZH] “我家小妹以前很喜欢她的诗词，也不知从哪里抄来许多，听到街上的传言，伤心不已。”
[EN] "My little sister used to adore her poetry and copied heaps of it from who knows where—hearing the street talk broke her heart."
[ZH] 千里寻以为有人明事理，忍不住插嘴道：“你家小妹肯定觉得外面谣言是假，替朱家姑娘鸣不平。”
[EN] Thinking someone out there had sense, Qianli Xun chimed in: "Your sister must know those street rumors are lies and feel furious for Miss Zhu!"
[ZH] 那人摇摇头：“非也，我家小妹伤心自己多年喜欢幽栖居士的诗词，实在不该，早知她品性不端，就不应想尽办法抄来那么多诗词。”
[EN] The man shook his head: "Not at all! My sister's heartbroken she wasted years admiring Youqi Jushi's verse—said if she'd known her morals were rotten, she'd never have hunted down so many poems!"
[ZH] 另一位问：“不会一把火烧了？”
[EN] The other man asked, "Don't tell me she burned them all?"
[ZH] “你怎知道？”
[EN] "How'd you guess?!"
[ZH] “猜的！我听说知府大人把幽栖居士的手稿一把火烧毁，没想到你家小妹也如此，真是巾帼不让须眉！”
[EN] "Easy! Heard the Prefect torched all of Youqi Jushi's manuscripts—your sister did the same? Now that's a heroine to rival any man!"
[ZH] 千里寻觉得好笑，烧朱淑真的手稿与巾帼不让须眉有何关系？如此赞扬，实在不恰当。
[EN] Qianli Xun almost laughed out loud—burning Zhu Shuzhen's poems made a girl "a heroine to rival any man"? What twisted praise!
[ZH] “二位有没有想过，大家议论的话只是谣言，朱家姑娘根本不像谣言说的那样。”
[EN] "Have you two ever considered those stories are just rumors, and Miss Zhu is nothing like what folk say?"
[ZH] 二人奇怪地盯着眼前的小子，一起摇摇头。
[EN] Both men stared at the strange youth and shook their heads together.
[ZH] “没想过，我等不认识幽栖居士，谁知传言是真是假。”
[EN] "Never thought about it. We don't know Youqi Jushi—how should we know what's true or false?"
[ZH] 千里寻劝说道：“那就更不该胡乱议论别人，冤枉好人岂不罪过？”
[EN] Qianli Xun pressed: "Then you have even less business slandering her! Isn't wronging an innocent person a sin?"
[ZH] “常言道：无风不起浪，何况她还是知府家千金，谁敢随意诋毁？肯定是她过于放荡，才臭名远扬，要怪只能怪她自己。”
[EN] "No smoke without fire! Besides, she's the Prefect's daughter—who'd dare slander her for nothing? She must be shameless to stink this far and wide; she has only herself to blame!"
[ZH] 千里寻已忍不下去，端起茶盏，将茶水泼在那人身上，大声吼道：“两个大男人跟个长舌妇似的，小心你生下儿子没屁眼！”
[EN] That snapped Qianli Xun's last nerve. Lifting her tea bowl, she splashed the tea straight across the man's chest and roared, "Two grown men wagging tongues like fishwives—may your sons be born without assholes!"
[ZH] 被眼前的小子侮辱，那人甚是气愤，站起来骂道：“臭小子，敢泼我茶水，看爷爷怎么教训你！”
[EN] Humiliated by a slip of a boy, the man jumped up cursing: "Little punk, dare splash tea on me? Watch your grandpa teach you manners!"
[ZH] 上前就要用拳头打千里寻，另一个也站起来帮忙。
[EN] He swung a fist at Qianli Xun, his companion surging up to help.
[ZH] 本想着两个大男人收拾一个臭小子应该不成问题，谁承想没两下被臭小子左右拳打倒在地，捂着被打成瘀青的脸直喊：“哎吆！”
[EN] Two grown men figured flattening one skinny lad would be a breeze—only for rapid left-right hooks to drop them both flat on the floor in two seconds, clutching bruised faces and howling, "Ow! Ouch!"
[ZH] 三人闹得太大，听书的没心情再听，艺人也停止说书，都看起热闹来。
[EN] The brawl brought the teahouse to a standstill; the storyteller froze and every customer turned to gawk.
[ZH] 千里寻骂道：“老娘……你爷爷我不好欺负！警告你们，以后再让我听到议论朱家姑娘，我的拳头绝不轻饶！”
[EN] Qianli Xun barked, "Your old la—your grandpa isn't someone to push around! Mark my words: catch anyone else slandering Miss Zhu, and my fists won't go easy!"
[ZH] 其他人听到这话，无不吐吐舌头，心想，刚才自己议论时多亏声音不大，没被那小子听到，否则被打的就是他们。
[EN] Every bystander stuck out their tongue in silent relief—thank goodness they'd kept their voices low while gossiping earlier, or they'd be eating dust too!
[ZH] 伙计上前扶起两人，劝道：“小哥，我们是小本经营，经不起折腾，您可别再乱打人。”
[EN] Hurrying over to haul the two men up, the waiter pleaded, "Young sir, we run a tiny shop and can't survive a brawl—please don't hit anyone else!"
[ZH] 千里寻瞧瞧周围：“也没砸坏店里的东西，再说我从不乱打人，打的都是该打之人，谁让他们胡乱议论朱家姑娘。”
[EN] Qianli Xun glanced around. "Didn't break a single table or chair. Besides, I never hit innocents—only scum who ask for it by slandering Miss Zhu."
[ZH] 伙计把碎银掏出来：“客官，还您，我也议论过，您不打我都是好的，小费我岂敢再要。”
[EN] Pulling out the two bits of loose silver, the waiter held them back: "Take your silver back, sir! I gossiped too—I'm lucky you didn't beat me up; how could I dare keep your tip?!"
[ZH] 千里寻瞧瞧两块碎银：“既给出，岂能要回？”
[EN] Qianli Xun waved the silver away: "Once given, I never take it back."
[ZH] 伙计又收起，劝道：“客官，不能怪议论的人，不信你问问，这里谁没议论过？”
[EN] Pocketing it again, the waiter sighed, "Can't blame folks for talking, young sir—ask around, who in this room hasn't talked?"
[ZH] 被打的人气愤道：“你打我们有什么用？有本事，把全城的人打一顿！我听说，乡下人也在议论，难道你也跑到乡下去一个个打不成？”
[EN] One bruised man yelled back, "What's beating us up prove?! If you've got guts, go beat up the whole city! Heard peasants in the countryside are gossiping too—going to hike out there and punch them one by one?!"
[ZH] 千里寻被问住，她的拳头再厉害，总不能见人就打，老百姓议论只图个茶余饭后开心，最可恶的是传播谣言之人。若抓住此人，定要将他大卸八块！
[EN] His retort stopped Qianli Xun cold. Tough as her fists were, she couldn't beat up all of Lin'an. Common folk only gossiped for idle amusement; the real villain was whoever planted the rumors. Once she caught that rat, she'd chop him to pieces!

#CH 116
[ZH] 离开茶肆，千里寻心情愈加沉重，大家如此议论她，乔家取消提亲实在正常不过，她与文渊该怎么办？
[EN] Leaving the teahouse with an even heavier heart, Qianli Xun saw why the Qiao family had canceled the proposal—with the city talking like this, what choice did she and Wenyuan have?
[ZH] 走着走着，看到一家裁缝铺，想到身上的男装实在不适合，不如现做两身，以备不时之需。
[EN] Walking on, she spotted a tailor shop. Her borrowed servant's outfit fit poorly; better have two proper sets of men's clothes tailored in case she needed them again.
[ZH] 她走进裁缝铺内，店主迎上来：“小哥是买成衣还是现做衣裳？”
[EN] Stepping inside, the shopkeeper greeted her: "Ready-made or custom-tailored, young sir?"
[ZH] “现做。”
[EN] "Custom-tailored."
[ZH] “男装在左边，小哥这边请。”
[EN] "Men's wear is on the left—right this way, sir."
[ZH] 千里寻毕竟是女子，不由自主瞧向右边，女装区的成衣的确不错。瞧着瞧着，无意中发现苗知瑜与一位闺秀正在挑衣裳。
[EN] Still a woman at heart, Qianli Xun glanced over to the right, where the women's ready-made robes looked lovely—and right there, browsing fabrics with a female friend, stood Miao Zhiyu!
[ZH] 还好没被她们注意到，千里寻赶紧扭过头，走到男装区。
[EN] Before they noticed her, Qianli Xun whipped her head away and ducked into the men's section.
[ZH] 过来一名伙计，负责量尺寸：“麻烦小哥把胳膊抬起来。”
[EN] An apprentice stepped up with a measuring tape: "Please raise your arms, young sir."
[ZH] 千里寻乖乖地抬起胳膊，背对着女装区，怕被苗知瑜发现。
[EN] Qianli Xun lifted her arms obediently, keeping her back turned to the women's side so Miao Zhiyu wouldn't spot her.
[ZH] 苗知瑜倒未注意男装区，与闺中密友边挑衣裳边聊天儿。
[EN] Unaware of anyone on the men's side, Miao Zhiyu chatted with her confidante while sorting through silks.
[ZH] “知瑜，这件款式不错，很适合你。”
[EN] "This style is lovely, Zhiyu—suits you down to the ground."
[ZH] “太过素雅，再看看别的。”
[EN] "Too plain. Let's look at others."
[ZH] “你不是向来喜欢素雅？怎么突然喜好有所改变？”
[EN] "Didn't you always prefer understated pieces? Why the change in taste?"
[ZH] “我想试试艳丽些的，也许更适合我。”
[EN] "Thought I'd try brighter colors—might suit me better."
[ZH] 闺中密友又说：“东方公子若组织诗会，你一定叫上我。”
[EN] Her friend added, "If Young Master Dongfang holds another poetry salon, you have to bring me along!"
[ZH] “你又不懂诗词，去了也没意思。”
[EN] "You don't even care for poetry—you'd be bored."
[ZH] “诗词我不感兴趣，我只想认识东方公子。”
[EN] "I don't care about poetry; I just want to meet Young Master Dongfang!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜叹口气：“唉！恐怕东方公子的诗会再也组织不起来。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu let out a sigh. "I doubt Young Master Dongfang will hold any more salons."
[ZH] “为何？”
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] “幽栖居士出这么大的事，他哪有心情？”
[EN] "With everything hitting Youqi Jushi, how could he be in the mood?"
[ZH] “又为何？”
[EN] "Why would that stop him?"
[ZH] “诗会没有幽栖居士，没有乔公子，举办了又有什么意思？”
[EN] "Without Youqi Jushi and Young Master Qiao, what's the point of holding a salon?"
[ZH] “原来如此。说起幽栖居士，没想到她是那样的女子，真是人不可貌相。”
[EN] "Oh, right. Speaking of Youqi Jushi—who knew she was that kind of woman? Never judge a book by its cover!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜边挑衣裳边说：“你别跟他人瞎胡议论，幽栖居士可不是谣言中说的那种人。”
[EN] Browsing robes, Miao Zhiyu spoke up: "Don't repeat wild street talk. Youqi Jushi is nothing like those rumors claim."
[ZH] 听到这话，千里寻对苗知瑜生出好感，能在众人诋毁之时还替她说话，人品必然不错。
[EN] Warmth surged in Qianli Xun toward Miao Zhiyu—defending her while the whole city spat on her name showed real character.
[ZH] 伙计边量尺寸边疑惑：“明明是个女子，却要扮成男子，还买男装，真是奇怪。”
[EN] Taking measurements, the tailor's apprentice puzzled in silence: clearly a young woman, yet dressed as a man ordering men's robes—how odd!
[ZH] 尺寸已量完，伙计说：“小哥，尺寸已记下，您再挑挑款式。”
[EN] Finishing up, the apprentice said, "Got your measurements down, sir. Now pick out your styles."
[ZH] 千里寻不着急，装作认真挑选款式的样子，尽量避开与苗知瑜对视，却忍不住竖起耳朵听她们聊天儿。
[EN] Taking her time, Qianli Xun pretended to inspect patterns while keeping her back to Miao Zhiyu, ears pricked wide open.
[ZH] 闺中密友说：“好像你很了解她似的。”
[EN] The friend teased, "You talk like you know her inside out."
[ZH] “我跟幽栖居士虽谈不上是闺中密友，可常常在诗会中碰面，对她的事再清楚不过。谣言说她是母老虎，实在过分！她参加诗会时，常常带伤而来，都是被林觉打的。后来，她虽变得厉害，可对朋友最温和不过。至于其余难听的话，更不符合实际。她和离前，每次来诗会，与东方公子他们相处最懂男女有别，行为从未逾矩。即使乔公子对她有情，她也从未有过任何……”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi and I aren't bosom friends, sure, yet we met at salon after salon, so I know her well. Calling her a tigress is outrageous! She used to show up to salons covered in bruises from Lin Jue's beatings. Later she grew tougher, yet she's always warm to friends. As for the rest of that filth, it's pure lies. Before her mutual divorce, she was scrupulous about proper distance around Young Master Dongfang and the others—never crossed a line once! Even when Young Master Qiao had feelings for her, she never..."
[ZH] 苗知瑜想起到朱家参加宴会时，那时幽栖居士还未和离，已与乔公子眉来眼去，她因此吃醋不已。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu stopped short, recalling the Zhu Manor banquet before the divorce when Youqi Jushi and Young Master Qiao had traded tender glances—sparking green jealousy inside her.
[ZH] “怎么，难道幽栖居士还是有问题？”闺中密友见苗知瑜忽然停下，故意问。
[EN] "Well? So Youqi Jushi isn't spotless after all?" her friend prodded at the pause.
[ZH] 苗知瑜强调道：“她跟乔公子在一起是和离后的事，总之，外面的谣言都是诋毁。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu insisted, "She and Young Master Qiao only got together after her divorce. Point is, those street rumors are all slander."
[ZH] 千里寻很想上前抱住苗知瑜，感谢她替她说话，瞧到自己身上的男装，还是忍住，继续装作挑选款式的样子，认真听着。
[EN] Qianli Xun almost marched over to hug Miao Zhiyu in thanks, then remembered her men's disguise and held back, keeping her ears glued to their chat.
[ZH] 闺中密友继续说：“知瑜，我估计也只有你替幽栖居士说话。你真善良，应了那句善有善报。我听说乔家又开始准备为乔公子选亲，你的机会来了。”
[EN] Her friend went on: "Zhiyu, you're probably the only soul left defending Youqi Jushi! Such a kind heart—and kindness brings good karma! Heard the Qiao family started bride selection for Young Master Qiao all over again—your chance has come!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜露出笑容，接着担忧道：“也不知乔公子现在可好？”
[EN] A smile curved Miao Zhiyu's lips before worry crept in: "I wonder how Young Master Qiao is holding up right now..."
[ZH] 闺中密友建议道：“别在这里瞎担心，不如找机会去见见他。”
[EN] Her friend urged, "Stop fretting here—find a chance to go see him!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜点头同意。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu nodded in agreement.
[ZH] 二人又挑选一会儿，终于选好衣裳。
[EN] After browsing a bit longer, they settled on their robes.
[ZH] 掌柜命人取下，包好后，将二位客人送出门。
[EN] The shopkeeper wrapped their purchases and saw both ladies out the door.
[ZH] 千里寻根本无心挑选，随便选两个款式，付完定金，走出裁缝铺。
[EN] With no heart left for shopping, Qianli Xun picked two styles at random, paid the deposit, and walked out.
[ZH] 在外面逛荡许久，也该回去，收拾收拾去见文渊，只有见到他，才知接下来的路该如何走。
[EN] Having wandered long enough, it was time to head back and get ready to meet Wenyuan—only face to face would they know what path lay ahead.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 回到望月轩，千里寻换回女装，带上两个丫鬟乘坐马车，赶往神柳下面见乔文仙。
[EN] Back at Wangyue Pavilion, Qianli Xun changed back into women's robes, took both maids, and rode out to meet Qiao Wenxian beneath the Divine Willow.
[ZH] 夏果很开心，又能见到乔公子。
[EN] Xiaguo beamed at the thought of seeing Young Master Qiao again.
[ZH] 苦荞一直观察小姐，发生这么大的事，小姐还能够坦然面对，令她钦佩。小姐沉默不语，苦荞也保持沉默，心中担忧着小姐与乔公子的未来。
[EN] Kuqiao watched her mistress in quiet awe—facing such a storm with calm courage was no small thing. Seeing her mistress silent, Kuqiao stayed silent too, fretting over what lay ahead for Miss and Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 乔文仙早已来到神柳旁，站在树下，向淑真必然出现的方向眺望，满脸期待。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had arrived beneath the Divine Willow long before, staring down the path where Shuzhen was sure to appear, longing stamped across his face.
[ZH] 神柳的叶子已发黄，一阵秋风吹过，时不时落下枯黄的柳叶，掉落在乔文仙的身上。南双见状，上前将柳叶从公子身上取下，扔在草丛中。
[EN] The willow's leaves had turned yellow; gusts of autumn wind shook withered leaves down onto Qiao Wenxian's shoulders, and Nan Shuang kept stepping up to brush them off into the grass.
[ZH] 发黄的柳叶与正在发黄的草混合在一起，分不清哪个是柳叶，哪个是草叶。周围的植物逐渐失去生气，如同乔文仙的心情一样凄凉。
[EN] Yellow willow leaves mingled with yellowing grass until neither could be told apart. All around them, nature drained of life—matching the bleak chill in Qiao Wenxian's chest.
[ZH] 昨晚他没睡好，思索一晚上与淑真的未来，仍旧未思索出结果。他更担心淑真会就此舍他而去。
[EN] He had lain awake all night searching for a future with Shuzhen and finding none, terrified above all that she might walk away from him.
[ZH] 期待已久的身影终于出现在眼前，正缓慢向他走来，乔文仙凄凉的表情中露出些许喜色。
[EN] At last the figure he longed for appeared, walking up the path at an unhurried pace, bringing a spark of light to his desolate face.
[ZH] 他已等不及，快步走上前，一把将心爱的人抱入怀里……
[EN] Unable to wait another second, he strode forward and pulled the woman he loved straight into his arms...
[ZH] 苦荞拉起夏果朝南双走去。
[EN] Kuqiao tugged Xiaguo over to join Nan Shuang.
[ZH] 夏果很不情愿，又没有任何理由待在一旁，只能不舍地回头再瞧几眼乔公子，他看上去很憔悴，夏果一阵心疼。
[EN] Xiaguo dragged her feet, casting longing glances back over her shoulder at Young Master Qiao; seeing how haggard he looked made her heart ache.
[ZH] 被文渊抱住，千里寻也伸手抱住他，这一抱，她忍不住流下泪来：“文渊，我已在临安城臭名昭著，难道你不嫌弃我？”
[EN] Held tight in Wenyuan's arms, Qianli Xun wrapped her arms around him too, and tears spilled over at last: "Wenyuan, my name is dirt across all of Lin'an—don't you despise me?"
[ZH] “我对你再清楚不过，谣言岂能影响我？”
[EN] "I know you better than anyone. How could rumors ever sway me?"
[ZH] 千里寻把文渊抱得更紧：“谢谢你信任我。”
[EN] Qianli Xun hugged him tighter. "Thank you for trusting me."
[ZH] “以后不许对我说谢谢的话。”
[EN] "Never say thank you to me again."
[ZH] “好，我不会再说。”千里寻沉默一会儿，终于问出一路思索的问题，“以后，我们……我们该怎么办？”
[EN] "All right, I won't." After a quiet beat, she asked the question she had turned over the whole way here: "What... what do we do from here?"
[ZH] 乔文仙没有立刻回答，他放开淑真，深情地盯着她的面庞：“你要等我，相信我，我会说服爹娘，让他们同意再次到朱家提亲。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian did not answer at once. Letting her go, he gazed straight into her eyes with deep tenderness: "Wait for me. Trust me—I'll persuade my parents to propose to the Zhu family again."
[ZH] 千里寻垂下眼帘：“我当然相信你，可仪王告诉我，你的父亲宁可舍弃性命也不愿到朱府提亲。”
[EN] Qianli Xun dropped her gaze. "Of course I trust you. Only Prince Yi told me your father would sooner give up his life than propose to Zhu Manor."
[ZH] “父亲可以舍弃性命，我也可以……”
[EN] "If Father can stake his life, so can I—"
[ZH] “不！千万不要！”千里寻担心不已，“如若你这样，我宁可不要这段姻缘……”
[EN] "No! Never say that!" Panic gripped Qianli Xun. "If it comes to that, I'd rather give up this marriage..."
[ZH] 乔文仙用手捂住淑真的嘴巴，不愿她继续说下去：“别这么说，我答应你，会珍爱自己，你也要相信我，等着我。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian pressed a hand over her mouth to stop her words: "Don't talk like that. I promise I'll take care of myself—just believe in me and wait for me."
[ZH] “好，我等你，只要你坚持，我便坚持，你若放弃……”
[EN] "All right, I'll wait. As long as you hold on, I'll hold on. If you ever give up—"
[ZH] “我不会放弃。”
[EN] "I'll never give up."
[ZH] “我也不放弃。”
[EN] "Neither will I."
[ZH] 两人正沉浸着体会各自坚守的话语时，传来仪王的声音：“你们两个人信誓旦旦又有何用？”
[EN] Right as they drank in each other's vows, Prince Yi's voice sliced in: "What good do all your grand vows do?"
[ZH] 千里寻心想，说仪王没眼色，还真没眼色。她与文渊约会，他又跑来凑热闹。正要回怼两句，瞧到佳和郡主跟在一旁，才控制好情绪，上前拜道：“仪王，佳和郡主。”
[EN] Qianli Xun rolled her eyes—talk about zero tact! Intruding right on her date with Wenyuan! Ready to snap back, she spotted Princess Jiahe walking beside him, swallowed her temper, and stepped up to bow: "Prince Yi, Princess Jiahe."
[ZH] 乔梦容上前扶住幽栖居士：“我与哥哥前来，有没有打扰到你们？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong hurried up to steady her: "Did Brother and I interrupt you two?"
[ZH] 千里寻心想，还用问？打扰得实在厉害！嘴上却说：“不打扰，不知郡主与仪王前来有何指教？”
[EN] "Need you ask? Huge interruption!" Qianli Xun raged inside, while smiling aloud: "Not at all. What brings Princess and Prince Yi out here?"
[ZH] 申简辰主动说：“没什么指教，我想出来走走，邀梦容陪我一起。谁知走着走着，来到此处。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen spoke up: "Nothing in particular. Felt like a walk and brought Mengrong along, and somehow our feet led us right here."
[ZH] 千里寻心里骂道：“我看你就是故意！把准了我不敢当众揭下你的面具，才如此没羞没臊，破坏我的好事！”
[EN] Qianli Xun cursed him in her mind: "On purpose, my foot! Banking on me not tearing your mask off in public so you can shamelessly wreck my date!"
[ZH] 千里寻拉起文渊：“我们去那边聊，把这里留给仪王与佳和郡主。”
[EN] She tugged Wenyuan's sleeve: "Let's go talk over there and leave this spot to Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe."
[ZH] 乔文仙点头同意：“仪王，我与淑真去那边。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian nodded: "Prince Yi, Shuzhen and I will step over that way."
[ZH] 申简辰拦道：“还是别，既然大家遇到，不如一起聊？再说你们两个待了一阵儿，重要的话也该说完，再说下去，又提那些伤心事，多没意思，不如我们四个一起，说点儿别的，你们也可开心开心。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stepped in front of them: "Why bother? Since we ran into each other, let's all chat together! You two have been out here a while—must've said everything important already. Keep dwelling on sad stuff and you'll only feel worse. Let the four of us talk about something brighter to cheer you up!"
[ZH] 乔文仙认为仪王说的有道理：“淑真，该说的我已说，不如大家一起？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian saw sense in that: "Shuzhen, we've said our piece—why not join them?"
[ZH] 千里寻正要反对，佳和郡主拉起她的手：“幽栖居士，好久不见你，我有很多话想跟你聊，不如今晚留在幽林别居，跟我聊一晚上。”
[EN] Before Qianli Xun could refuse, Princess Jiahe took her hand: "Youqi Jushi, I haven't seen you in ages and have so much to tell you! Why not stay the night at Youlin Retreat and talk all night with me?"
[ZH] 千里寻向远处瞧去，太阳快要落山，入秋后，白天正逐渐缩短，再聊一会儿天色自然越来越暗。发生这么多事，她正想找人好好聊聊，发泄发泄不满，佳和郡主无疑是最佳人选。
[EN] Glancing toward the horizon, Qianli Xun saw the sun sinking low; autumn days shrank fast, and night would fall soon. After all the storms lately, she did crave a good vent, and Princess Jiahe was the best confidante around.
[ZH] “好吧，既如此，我在幽林别居留宿一晚。”
[EN] "All right—then I'll stay the night at Youlin Retreat."
[ZH] 淑真同意住下，乔文仙很开心，也不急于一时，他把仪王拉到一边。
[EN] Hearing Shuzhen agree to stay cheered Qiao Wenxian up; with no rush now, he pulled Prince Yi aside.
[ZH] 千里寻则跟佳和郡主绕着池塘，边走边聊。
[EN] Qianli Xun walked around the pond arm in arm with Princess Jiahe, chatting as they strolled.
[ZH] 白风一见到苦荞便粘着她；南双见夏果不理睬他，去找喜兰说话；留夏果一人只顾关切地注视乔公子。
[EN] Bai Feng stuck to Kuqiao like glue the second he saw her; ignored by Xiaguo, Nan Shuang wandered off to chat with Xilan; and Xiaguo stood alone, eyes fixed in anxious care on Young Master Qiao.

#CH 117
[ZH] 夏果一直注意乔公子，小姐同意住下，他憔悴的脸上多出些笑容，拉仪王走到柳树下面，不知说些什么。
[EN] Xiaguo kept her eyes locked on Young Master Qiao. Hearing her mistress agree to stay overnight had brought a spark of smiles back to his worn face as he drew Prince Yi beneath the willow tree to talk.
[ZH] 乔文仙之所以把仪王拉到柳树下，不过希望仪王帮他出主意
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had pulled Prince Yi aside for one reason only: to beg him for a plan.
[ZH] “我与淑真都不愿放弃，可我不知接下来该怎么做，仪王可否帮我出个主意？”
[EN] "Neither Shuzhen nor I will give up, yet I have no idea what to do next. Can Prince Yi think of a way out for me?"
[ZH] 文渊憔悴的脸上充满渴望，申简辰不忍伤他。想起刚才听到千里寻愿意坚持下去的话，心里为之一痛：“幽栖居士果真不愿放弃？多难都不愿放弃？”
[EN] Facing the desperation on Wenyuan's haggard face, Shen Jianchen could not bear to crush him—yet remembering Qianli Xun's vow to hold on stabbed his own chest. "Is Youqi Jushi really refusing to give up? No matter how hard it gets?"
[ZH] “她亲口对我说，我很开心。必须做些什么，才不辜负她对我一片真情，可又不知该如何去做？”乔文仙满面愁容。
[EN] "She told me herself, and it filled me with hope! I have to do something worthy of her devotion—I just don't know what!" Gloom clouded Qiao Wenxian's brow.
[ZH] 申简辰劝道：“你有没有想过，你与幽栖居士可能是有缘无分……”
[EN] Shen Jianchen tested gently, "Have you ever thought you and Youqi Jushi might simply not be fated to end up together?"
[ZH] 没等仪王说完，乔文仙即刻否认：“不可能！我和她会走到一起，只是暂时遇到困难而已。”
[EN] Before he even finished, Qiao Wenxian cut him off: "Impossible! We will be together—this is just a rough patch."
[ZH] “既如此，我给你出个主意。”千里寻喜欢文渊，愿意坚守，申简辰艰难作出决定，不如帮帮他们。
[EN] "In that case, I've got an idea." Qianli Xun loved Wenyuan and meant to stand by him; grinding his teeth, Shen Jianchen forced himself to help them.
[ZH] 乔文仙催促道：“什么主意，快说！”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian pressed: "What idea? Tell me!"
[ZH] 文渊可怜的样子，也使得申简辰无法不说出他的想法：“你带幽栖居士离开临安，到别处生活，反正朱府已将她赶出家门，没有人会拦着她。等生米煮成熟饭，再生下孩子，过上几年，外公不得不接受，你是乔家唯一的儿子，要继承家业，必然得把你找回来，结局岂不圆满？”
[EN] Wenyuan's pitiful look wrung the plan straight out of Shen Jianchen: "Take Youqi Jushi and leave Lin'an to build a life somewhere else. Zhu Manor already cast her out, so nobody will stop her. Once the rice is cooked and you two have a child, a few years down the road Grandfather will have no choice but to accept it—you're the Qiao family's only son and heir, so he'll have to bring you home. Happy ending, right?"
[ZH] 乔文仙听后并不认同：“不行，我怎能如此做？淑真本就被人非议，我带她私奔，会让她的名声愈加受损。再说没有父母之命媒妁之言，婚姻不会受到祝福，我不希望淑真跟我受苦。还有没有别的办法？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian shook his head at once: "No! How could I do that? Folk are already slandering Shuzhen—if I elope with her, her reputation will be ruined forever! Besides, a marriage without parents' orders and matchmakers' words earns no blessings. I won't let Shuzhen suffer with me. Isn't there another way?"
[ZH] 文渊拒绝私奔，申简辰莫名喜悦，他真怕文渊听他的话带千里寻离开，那么他也会艰难地追寻他们，恐怕到时想掩饰都难。其实，要不是考虑到乔梦容与文渊会难过，申简辰才不愿意掩饰对千里寻的感情。
[EN] Hearing Wenyuan reject elopement sent a secret surge of relief through Shen Jianchen. He had half dreaded Wenyuan taking his advice and spirits-whisking Qianli Xun away—forcing him to chase after them until he couldn't even hide his feelings anymore. Truth be told, if not for hurting Qiao Mengrong and Wenyuan, Shen Jianchen would never have bothered hiding how he felt about Qianli Xun at all.
[ZH] 申简辰摇摇头：“没有更好的办法。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen shook his head. "No better way."
[ZH] “不，肯定会有更好的办法，我要好好想想。”
[EN] "No, there has to be a better way—I need to think harder."
[ZH] 乔文仙的身体因沉重的思虑变得无比沉重，他瞧到神柳下的石头，走到跟前坐下，背靠树干，仰望天空，真是秋高气爽，他的内心却爽快不起来。
[EN] Crushed under heavy thoughts, Qiao Wenxian dragged his feet over to the boulder beneath the Divine Willow, sank down against the trunk, and stared up at the clear autumn sky—crisp and bright overhead, yet bleak inside his chest.
[ZH] 申简辰眺望远处，千里寻与乔梦容已走到池塘对岸，也不知她们聊着什么。接着看到她们进入密林，应该是打算回幽林别居。
[EN] Gazing across the water, Shen Jianchen saw Qianli Xun and Qiao Mengrong reach the far bank and disappear into the woods toward Youlin Retreat.
[ZH] “文渊，回去吧，幽栖居士和梦容已进入密林。”
[EN] "Let's head back, Wenyuan. Youqi Jushi and Mengrong already went into the woods."
[ZH] 乔文仙才缓慢站起来，没有拉仪王，径自朝对岸走去。
[EN] Only then did Qiao Wenxian heave himself up and trudge off toward the far bank without waiting for Prince Yi.
[ZH] 申简辰瞧着他沉重的步伐，心想：“文渊什么都好，只是遇到事情思虑太多，要是我，什么都不要，带着心爱的女子远走高飞。”
[EN] Watching his heavy steps, Shen Jianchen thought, "Wenyuan's great in every way except he overthinks everything. If it were me, I'd drop it all and run off to the ends of the earth with the woman I love."
[ZH] 夏果见乔公子要离开，尾随在乔公子和仪王身后，其他人也都跟上。
[EN] Seeing Young Master Qiao depart, Xiaguo trailed behind him and Prince Yi, with the rest following along.
[ZH] 到达幽林别居门口，申简辰白让白风带苦荞回趟望月轩，告诉韦嬷嬷，佳和郡主与幽栖居士十分相投，邀请她在幽林别居住一晚，明日一早回去。
[EN] At Youlin Retreat's gate, Shen Jianchen had Bai Feng drive Kuqiao back to Wangyue Pavilion to inform Matron Wei that Princess Jiahe and Youqi Jushi had hit it off so well the princess had invited her to stay the night and return in the morning.
[ZH] 只要有机会跟苦荞一起，白风自然乐意。心甘情愿赶起马车，与苦荞一路上有说有笑，不知不觉来到望月轩。
[EN] Any excuse to spend time with Kuqiao delighted Bai Feng. Whipping up the carriage, he laughed and joked with Kuqiao all the way to Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 苦荞将小姐留宿幽林别居之事向韦嬷嬷汇报完，担心韦嬷嬷将她留下，很快加上一句：“小姐第一次在外留宿，我担心，还得赶过去伺候她。”
[EN] After reporting her mistress's overnight stay to Matron Wei, Kuqiao rushed to add lest the matron keep her behind: "It's Miss's first time sleeping away from home—I'm worried and need to hurry back to wait on her."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷不情愿自家姑娘在外留宿，既是佳和郡主挽留，她不好多说什么，叮嘱道：“最近胡乱议论姑娘的人很多，留宿一事只怕被人知道，又添油加醋乱说一通。明日回来时，要小心谨慎，不要被人发现。”
[EN] Matron Wei hated the idea of her mistress staying out overnight, yet with Princess Jiahe hosting, she couldn't object. "Folks are wagging tongues everywhere lately; if word leaks that you stayed out overnight, they'll spin even worse filth! Be careful coming back tomorrow so nobody spots you."
[ZH] 苦荞应答着，跟白风返回幽林别居。与白风又玩一会儿，担心自家小姐，才往郡主居住的院子而去。
[EN] Promising she would, Kuqiao rode back to Youlin Retreat with Bai Feng. After stealing a little more playtime with him, she hurried off to Princess Jiahe's courtyard to tend her mistress.
[ZH] 今晚，小姐要与郡主同睡在一张床上，还是佳和郡主强烈要求，说她从未与好友同榻而卧，想体验一回。
[EN] Tonight her mistress was sharing a bed with the princess—at Princess Jiahe's enthusiastic insistence, since she had never shared a bed with a close friend before and wanted to try it.
[ZH] 苦荞到达佳和郡主的闺房时，夏果与喜兰各自已帮主人卸好妆，宽了衣裳，无须苦荞再做什么。
[EN] By the time Kuqiao arrived, Xiaguo and Xilan had already removed their mistresses' makeup and outer robes, leaving nothing left for Kuqiao to do.
[ZH] 喜兰把苦荞和夏果安排在丫鬟房，苦荞心里不搁事，很快入睡。
[EN] Xilan settled Kuqiao and Xiaguo into the maids' quarters. With an easy conscience, Kuqiao drifted off in minutes.
[ZH] 夏果怎么也睡不着，不是担心自家小姐，而是担心乔公子。第一次离乔公子这么近，她想更近些。
[EN] Xiaguo, however, tossed wide awake—not worrying over her mistress, but over Young Master Qiao. Never had she slept so close to him, and she longed to draw closer still.
[ZH] 夏果悄悄爬起床，摸黑穿好衣裳，简单整理好头发，蹑手蹑脚走出丫鬟房。
[EN] Slipping out of bed, she dressed in the dark, tidied her hair, and tiptoed out of the maids' quarters.
[ZH] 来到院中，听见小姐与郡主聊天儿的声音，夏果并不感兴趣，轻手轻脚出了院子。
[EN] Out in the yard, she heard her mistress and the princess chatting indoors. Uninterested, Xiaguo crept out through the courtyard gate.
[ZH] 进幽林别居里时，夏果仔细观察过乔公子的住处，在心里悄悄记下如何从郡主住的小院走到乔公子的住处。
[EN] On entering Youlin Retreat earlier, she had memorized the layout and the exact route from Princess Jiahe's yard to Young Master Qiao's quarters.
[ZH] 很容易找到乔公子居住的院子，在院外，她向木质高台瞧了那么一眼，便朝院内看去。幽林别居内的院子院墙不够高，夏果能清晰地看到里面还亮着灯光。
[EN] Finding his courtyard took no effort. Outside the gate, she spared a quick glance at the wooden viewing platform, then peered over the low wall—warm lamplight still glowed inside.
[ZH] 夏果靠近院门，发现门并未关，她轻轻推开，门发出“嘎吱”声。
[EN] Stepping up to the gate, she found it unlatched and nudged it open with a soft creak.
[ZH] 传来南双的声音：“谁？”
[EN] Nan Shuang's voice rang out: "Who's there?"
[ZH] 夏果站住，怯怯地回道：“是我。”
[EN] Xiaguo froze, answering in a timid voice: "It's me."
[ZH] 南双从屋里出来，借着月光，看出是夏果，以为来找他，兴奋地问：“你怎么过来？”
[EN] Stepping outside, Nan Shuang recognized Xiaguo by moonlight and lit up, thinking she had come for him: "What brings you here?"
[ZH] 夏果回道：“我找乔公子有事说。”
[EN] Xiaguo replied, "I have something to tell Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] 南双白高兴一场。
[EN] Nan Shuang's excitement fizzled on the spot.
[ZH] 里面传来乔文仙的声音：“南双，外面是谁？”
[EN] From indoors came Qiao Wenxian's voice: "Who's outside, Nan Shuang?"
[ZH] 南双用失望的语气回道：“公子，是幽栖居士身边的丫鬟夏果。”
[EN] In a flat, disappointed tone, Nan Shuang called back, "Young Master, it's Youqi Jushi's maid, Xiaguo."
[ZH] 乔文仙以为淑真有话向他转达：“让她进来。”
[EN] Thinking Shuzhen had sent word, Qiao Wenxian answered, "Let her in."
[ZH] “听到没？公子让你进去。”
[EN] "Hear that? Young Master says go in."
[ZH] 南双一直盯着夏果走到他面前，又从他眼前经过，进到屋内，他也跟着进入。
[EN] Nan Shuang watched Xiaguo walk straight past him through the door and trailed right in behind her.
[ZH] 乔文仙睡不着，坐在桌前翻阅书籍，希望能从书籍中找到解决问题的办法，查找半天一无所获。夏果前来，说不定是淑真想到好办法。
[EN] Unable to sleep, Qiao Wenxian had been poring over books at his desk in hopes of finding a way out, turning up nothing. Xiaguo coming by meant maybe Shuzhen had hit on an idea!
[ZH] “你家小姐给我带来什么话？”
[EN] "What message did your mistress send?"
[ZH] 小姐哪里让她带话？夏果为乔公子心中无她感到伤心。
[EN] Her mistress hadn't sent any message—realizing Young Master Qiao didn't even see her stabbed Xiaguo's heart.
[ZH] “你怎么看上去不高兴？”乔文仙担心淑真想放弃他们之间的感情，尽管白天时两人已信誓旦旦，他还是担心。
[EN] "Why do you look upset?" Dread pricked Qiao Wenxian—could Shuzhen be thinking of giving up on them, despite their vows earlier today?
[ZH] 夏果鼓起勇气：“我跟乔公子说话，不能有他人在场。”
[EN] Gathering her courage, Xiaguo said, "What I have to tell Young Master Qiao can't have anyone else listening."
[ZH] 乔文仙对南双说：“你出去待会儿，把门带上。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian told Nan Shuang, "Step outside for a minute and shut the door."
[ZH] 南双很不情愿，走出屋外，把门从外面关上：“有什么了不起！不就是长得漂亮点儿！长得漂亮有什么用？还不是丫鬟命！难道你长得漂亮还能嫁给像我家公子那样的人？”
[EN] Scowling, Nan Shuang tramped outside and pulled the door shut, muttering, "Big deal! So you're a little pretty—what's pretty get you? Still a maid's fate! Think a pretty face is going to marry you to a gentleman like my master?!"
[ZH] 屋内，乔文仙继续问：“你家小姐有什么话给我转达？”
[EN] Inside, Qiao Wenxian pressed: "What word did your mistress send?"
[ZH] 夏果跪下：“我家小姐并未让我传话，是我想来看公子，公子因提亲之事未成愁苦不已，我看着心疼，特来探望……”
[EN] Xiaguo dropped to her knees. "Miss didn't send any message. I came because I wanted to see you, Young Master. Watching you grieve over the ruined proposal broke my heart, so I came to check on you..."
[ZH] 乔文仙倍感失望，也觉麻烦：“既如此，你回去吧。”
[EN] Disappointment crashed over Qiao Wenxian, mixed with annoyance: "If that's all, go back."
[ZH] “公子听我把话说完，我说完就走。”
[EN] "Hear me out, Young Master—then I'll leave."
[ZH] “不用说，你走吧。”
[EN] "No need. Leave."
[ZH] 夏果哪里肯轻易失去表白的机会：“我想让公子知道，除小姐对您真心外，我也把您装进心里。我不奢求您像爱小姐一样爱我，只求您跟小姐在一起后，让我在您身边伺候，我就心满意足。如若您跟小姐成不了，也请您把我要到您身边，我愿意一辈子伺候公子……”
[EN] Refusing to waste her one shot to confess, Xiaguo blurted out, "I want Young Master to know that besides Miss, I hold you in my heart too! I don't ask you to love me the way you love Miss—just let me stay by your side and wait on you once you two are together, and I'll be content! And if things with Miss don't work out, please take me in—I'll wait on Young Master for the rest of my life..."
[ZH] “闭嘴！出去！出去！”乔文仙既生气，又不耐烦，大喊道，“南双！”
[EN] "Shut up! Get out! Get out!" Rage and impatience flared in Qiao Wenxian as he shouted, "Nan Shuang!"
[ZH] 南双听到公子的呼唤，推门而入，见夏果跪在地上，公子又十分生气，不知发生了什么。
[EN] Hearing his master's shout, Nan Shuang pushed the door open to find Xiaguo kneeling on the floor and his master furious, baffled at what had happened.
[ZH] “南双，你负责把夏果送出去。”
[EN] "Nan Shuang, escort Xiaguo out!"
[ZH] 表白被拒绝，夏果伤心，却不后悔，起码说出心里话。今日之后，乔公子只要见到她，便会想起她今日所说。
[EN] Rejected, Xiaguo felt crushed yet unrepentant—at least she had spoken her heart. From tonight on, every time Young Master Qiao laid eyes on her, he would remember what she had confessed.
[ZH] 她站起来，准备往外走，又被乔文仙叫住：“等一下！”
[EN] Rising to leave, she froze when Qiao Wenxian called out, "Hold on!"
[ZH] 夏果兴奋地转过身，难道乔公子改变想法？
[EN] Hope flared as she spun back—had Young Master Qiao changed his mind?!
[ZH] ﻿结果却听对方说：“今日之事，我当没发生过，你也忘了吧。”
[EN] Instead, his voice fell cold as stone: "I'll pretend tonight never happened. You forget it too."
[ZH] 夏果没有做任何回应，转身离开。
[EN] Without another word, Xiaguo turned and walked out.

#CH 118
[ZH] 佳和郡主热情邀请同榻而卧，千里寻不好拒绝也不愿拒绝。
[EN] With Princess Jiahe's warm invitation to share her bed, Qianli Xun neither wanted nor had the heart to refuse.
[ZH] 两日来发生太多事，她需要找个人聊聊发泄发泄，佳和郡主无疑是最佳人选。
[EN] Too much had piled up over the past two days; she needed a sympathetic ear to vent to, and Princess Jiahe was the ideal confidante.
[ZH] 两个女孩子睡前还没聊够，躺下后，继续开聊，她们如同两个长久失联的好友，一见面有聊不完的话题。当然，关键话题还是关乎个人感情之事。
[EN] Not finished talking before bed, the two girls kept right on chatting beneath the quilts like long-lost sisters catching up—with romance, of course, taking center stage.
[ZH] “淑真……”乔梦容不再客气地称呼幽栖居士，而是直呼其名，“我听哥哥说，乔家取消提亲，你有没有想过以后跟乔公子该怎么办？”
[EN] "Shuzhen..." Dropping the formal "Youqi Jushi" for her given name, Qiao Mengrong asked, "Brother told me the Qiao family called off the proposal. Have you thought about what you and Young Master Qiao will do next?"
[ZH] “文渊说他不会放弃，我也不会放弃。”
[EN] "Wenyuan said he won't give up, and neither will I."
[ZH] “你们打算怎么做？”
[EN] "What's your plan?"
[ZH] “不知道。”千里寻认为穿越后的时代除科技不够发达外，最大的问题就是恋爱婚姻不得自由，“如果没有父母之命，媒妁之言，我与文渊的难题会迎刃而解。”
[EN] "No idea." Aside from primitive technology, Qianli Xun decided the worst flaw of this era was zero freedom in love and marriage. "If there were no parents' orders and matchmakers' words, Wenyuan's and my troubles would vanish in a snap."
[ZH] “很难想象父母不管儿女的婚事，也不需要媒人出面，会是什么样子？”
[EN] "Hard to picture a world where parents stay out of their children's marriages and nobody needs a matchmaker—what would that even look like?"
[ZH] 千里寻转向乔梦容：“年轻人自由恋爱，父母基本上不插手，除非到谈婚论嫁，父母才出面，会尊重年轻人的想法。还有很多人不愿意恋爱结婚，父母急得不行，恨不得在大街上随便拉个人把婚给结了。”
[EN] Qianli Xun rolled over to face Qiao Mengrong. "Young people fall in love freely while parents stay hands-off, only stepping in when it's time to talk wedding plans—and even then respecting what their kids want. Plenty of folks don't even want to date or marry, driving their parents so crazy they'd drag any stranger off the street to get them hitched!"
[ZH] “这么有趣，说的你好像经历过一样。”
[EN] "Sounds so wild—you talk like you've lived through it!"
[ZH] “我梦想着有这么一个时代，也相信未来会出现这样一个时代。”
[EN] "I dream of an age like that, and I believe one day it'll come."
[ZH] 千里寻幻想着带领文渊回到更先进的时代，享受恋爱的自由。她觉得人真是奇怪，拥有自由时，不认为有多重要，一旦失去，才知原来在先进时代经历的生活有多么来之不易。
[EN] Qianli Xun pictured bringing Wenyuan back to the modern world to taste the freedom to love. Funny how human beings worked: when you had freedom, you took it for granted; only after losing it did you realize how precious that modern life had been.
[ZH] 乔梦容想到哥哥：“淑真，你认识我和哥哥已有一段时日，你觉得哥哥爱我吗？”
[EN] Thoughts drifting to Prince Yi, Qiao Mengrong asked, "Shuzhen, you've known Brother and me a while now—do you think Brother loves me?"
[ZH] 仪王花心的样子出现在脑海中，他怎么可能对佳和郡主一心一意？千里寻无法说出实情：“你跟仪王天天在一起，他爱不爱你，难道感觉不出？”
[EN] Prince Yi's flirtatious glances flashed across Qianli Xun's mind. How could he be devoted to Princess Jiahe? Yet she couldn't bear telling her the blunt truth: "You're with Prince Yi every day—can't you feel whether he loves you?"
[ZH] 乔梦容叹口气：“唉！哥哥病着时，我很清楚他心里只有我，自从病好后，以前的事想不起来，连我与他的感情都忘记，我现在不是很确定他对我的感情……”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong sighed. "Back when Brother was sick, I knew for a fact he had room only for me. Since he recovered, he can't remember the past—he even forgot our love. Now I'm not so sure how he feels..."
[ZH] “什么？仪王失忆过？”千里寻产生莫名的希冀，仪王该不会与申简辰有什么关系？
[EN] "Wait—Prince Yi had amnesia?!" A strange spark of hope leaped in Qianli Xun: could Prince Yi have some tie to Shen Jianchen?
[ZH] “是的，哥哥失忆，以前的事情都不记得。”
[EN] "Yes. Brother lost his memory and remembers nothing from before."
[ZH] “他是怎么失忆的？”
[EN] "How did he lose his memory?"
[ZH] “哥哥本来已病入膏肓，咽下最后一口气，我正哭得伤心，他却奇迹般地活过来，可什么都不记得。邹嬷嬷说，哥哥到鬼门关走一趟，阎王不要他，又回来，因喝过孟婆汤，以前的记忆全无。”
[EN] "Brother was at death's door and breathed his last breath. While I was sobbing my heart out, he miraculously came back to life, remembering nothing. Matron Zou said Brother wandered to the Gates of Hell, King Yama sent him back, and because he'd drunk Mengpo's Soup, his past memories were wiped clean."
[ZH] “原来仪王是起死回生。”千里寻轻声说着。因她有过神奇的经历，对“鬼门关”和“孟婆汤”的说法深信不疑。
[EN] "So Prince Yi came back from the dead." Qianli Xun murmured under her breath. After her own wild crossing-over, tales of the Gates of Hell and Mengpo's Soup sounded believable enough.
[ZH] 她放弃了仪王可能是申简辰的想法，也能够理解为何仪王有婚约还会对她放电，原来是不记得以前的感情。
[EN] She dropped the notion that Prince Yi could be Shen Jianchen—and at last understood why an engaged prince kept flirting with her: he had forgotten his old love.
[ZH] 可再不记得，与佳和郡主的婚约实实在在，因婚约也不应对其他女子动情。
[EN] Still, forgetting or not, his engagement to Princess Jiahe stood solid as rock, giving him zero right to flirt with other women.
[ZH] “哥哥现在依然对我很好，却不像以前那么热情，你说我该怎么办？”
[EN] "Brother still treats me well, just without the warmth he used to have. What should I do?"
[ZH] 乔梦容的问题使千里寻陷入沉思，每个人的人生都有不幸，佳和郡主与仪王有婚约，在外人眼中看似完美，实则不尽然，只有身处其中，才能品出酸甜苦辣。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's question plunged Qianli Xun into silence. Every life held its hidden thorns: to outsiders, Princess Jiahe's imperial betrothal to Prince Yi looked flawless, yet only the one inside tasted its bittersweet ache.
[ZH] 她与文渊没有婚约，又因外力受阻，心却连在一起，千里寻希望她与文渊的心力最终能突破外力限制。
[EN] She and Wenyuan had no betrothal and faced walls on every side, yet their hearts beat as one. Qianli Xun prayed their shared devotion would smash through every barrier.
[ZH] “淑真，你睡着了吗？”
[EN] "Shuzhen, are you asleep?"
[ZH] “哦，没有，我是在想你跟仪王有婚约，还是官家赐婚，仪王娶你不容置疑。比起很多女子，已经很幸福，起码能嫁给喜欢的人。至于仪王的变化，不必太在意，还记得之前我教你的吗？”
[EN] "Oh, no. Just thinking—you and Prince Yi have an imperial betrothal from His Majesty, so he's guaranteed to marry you. Compared to most women, you're already lucky, marrying the man you love. Don't dwell on how he changed—remember the advice I gave you before?"
[ZH] 乔梦容早已忘记：“什么？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong had already forgotten. "What advice?"
[ZH] “我再说一遍，可要记清楚。嫁给仪王后，你肯定是正室，时刻把正室的地位巩固好，不要在乎仪王纳妾，他想纳谁让他纳，你大度些，他才会对你印象好。不要把对仪王的感情看得太重，看得越重对你越不利，要时刻保持理智，记住没？”
[EN] "I'll repeat it, so listen close! Once you marry Prince Yi, you're the primary wife. Guard your authority as primary wife tight, and don't sweat him taking concubines—let him take whoever he wants, and your graciousness will win his favor. Don't invest too much heart in him either; the heavier your heart leans on him, the worse off you'll be. Keep a cool head—got it?"
[ZH] 这回乔梦容听进心里：“你说得没错，我不能把对哥哥的感情看得太重，要保持理智。不过，我总觉得哥哥不是那种会纳妾的人。”
[EN] This time the words sank in: "You're right—I can't put too much of my heart on Brother; I have to stay levelheaded. Still, I don't think Brother's the type to take concubines."
[ZH] 千里寻很想告诉乔梦容，仪王是如何对她放电，还是忍住，只怕说出会影响两人之间的关系。于是编道：“告诉你，我亲眼见仪王盯着别的女子看。”
[EN] Qianli Xun itched to tell her how Prince Yi kept tossing sparks her own way, yet held back lest it poison their friendship. Instead she invented a tale: "Listen, I saw Prince Yi staring at another girl with my own eyes."
[ZH] 乔梦容立刻说不出话来，半天哽咽道：“你……你在……哪里见到？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong froze, choking out after a long beat: "W-where... did you see that?"
[ZH] 千里寻搜寻记忆，该说哪一次？想到七夕节那日，逛街时，街上姑娘最多，随便挑一位即可。
[EN] Racking her memory for a scene, Qianli Xun landed on Qixi Festival—crowds of pretty girls everywhere!
[ZH] “七夕那日，我们去逛街，你跟仪王走在前面，我无意中瞧见他看旁边经过的姑娘，看得相当仔细，眼神很不对劲，反正当时我感觉他就是个色狼。”
[EN] "On Qixi, when we went strolling. You and Prince Yi walked ahead, and I caught him eyeing a passing girl up and down—creepy look in his eyes, total lecher vibes!"
[ZH] 乔梦容回想那日的情景，很快破涕为笑：“哦，是那日，你误会哥哥，他是看一位女子来着，是我让哥哥看的，那女子手中的摩睺罗太像哥哥小时候，他便多看了几眼。”
[EN] Thinking back to that day, Qiao Mengrong burst into relieved laughter through her tears: "Oh, that! You misunderstood Brother! He did look at a girl, because I told him to—the Mohouluo clay doll in her hand looked just like Brother when he was little, so he looked twice!"
[ZH] 千里寻本想胡乱邹个场景，提醒佳和郡主多个心眼儿，没承想抛出去的球，又被踢回来，只好说：“可能是我看错，反正你还是小心些，以免嫁给他后……啊……啊……失望。”
[EN] Trying to spin a caution only to get her serve smashed straight back in her face, Qianli Xun muttered, "Guess I saw wrong, then. Stay sharp anyway, so after you marry him you won't... *yawn*... end up disappointed."
[ZH] 千里寻开始打哈欠，困意渐渐袭来。
[EN] Yawns rolled over Qianli Xun as sleep pulled at her lids.
[ZH] “从没有人对我说这些话，谢谢你关心我。”
[EN] "Nobody's ever talked to me like this—thank you for caring about me."
[ZH] “我是……关心你，希望你能……能有个好归宿，你……放心，我绝对不会……不会破坏……你们……的感……”千里寻断断续续说完，已进入梦乡。
[EN] "Of course I... care about you... want you to have a happy home... Don't worry, I'll never... come between... the two of..." Murmuring broken phrases, Qianli Xun drifted fast asleep.
[ZH] 乔梦容还清醒着，因千里寻说得含糊不清，并未听清她后面说些什么。好友已入睡，没有聊天儿的对象，乔梦容翻过身，也逐渐入睡。
[EN] Still wide awake, Qiao Mengrong missed her friend's mumbled last words. With her confidante asleep, she turned onto her side and slipped off to sleep too.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 两个女孩子昨晚聊得太久，天亮时，还继续睡着。
[EN] Having talked into the wee hours, both girls slept right past dawn.
[ZH] 丫鬟们很知趣，没有打扰她们。
[EN] Tactful, the maids left them to sleep undisturbed.
[ZH] 两人睡得正酣时，乔守仁带着几名力气大的下人，手拿棍子和绳子，闯进幽林别居，那架势一看就是要绑人。
[EN] Right as they slept soundest, Qiao Shouren stormed into Youlin Retreat with a crew of brawny servants carrying clubs and ropes—plainly here to haul someone away in bonds.
[ZH] 李管家迎上去：“老爷。”
[EN] Butler Li hurried out: "Master!"
[ZH] 乔守仁厉声问道：“文渊住哪里？”
[EN] Qiao Shouren barked, "Where is Wenyuan's courtyard?"
[ZH] 李管家知道事态不好，已没机会通知自家公子：“我带老爷过去。”
[EN] Sensing disaster with zero chance to tip off his young master, Butler Li bowed: "This way, Master."
[ZH] 到达乔文仙居住的院子，乔守仁一脚踹开院门，站在院中：“文渊！你给我出来！”
[EN] Reaching Qiao Wenxian's yard, Qiao Shouren kicked the gate wide open and roared into the courtyard: "Wenyuan! Get out here!"
[ZH] 乔文仙晚上没睡好，又起个大早，依旧在翻书，越是心急，越从书上翻不出所以然来。听到父亲的声音，打开门走出去。
[EN] Having lain awake most of the night and risen at dawn, Qiao Wenxian sat flipping books in vain frustration when his father's shout shook the yard. Opening the door, he stepped out.
[ZH] 从眼前的架势判断，父亲显然是想把他绑回乔府：“父亲不是让我老实待在别居吗？为何带他们前来？”
[EN] One glance at the clubs and ropes told him Father meant to haul him back to Qiao Manor in bonds. "Didn't Father order me to stay at the retreat? Why bring this mob?"
[ZH] “为何带他们？得问你自己！我让你待在别居，一来陪伴仪王，二来修身养性，可你却把不该留的人留在别居，今日我把你绑回乔府，你就老老实实在乔府待着！”
[EN] "Ask yourself why! I left you at the retreat to keep Prince Yi company and calm your mind—and here you go letting a woman who has no business here sleep overnight! Today I'm tying you up and hauling you back to Qiao Manor to stay put!"
[ZH] 听父亲的话，昨晚已有人到乔府打小报告。也难怪，幽林别居的下人都是父亲安排，必然有他的心腹，乔文仙怪自己太大意。
[EN] A spy among the retreat's staff must have run straight to Qiao Manor last night! No surprise—Father had picked every servant here. Qiao Wenxian kicked himself for getting careless.
[ZH] 还没等乔文仙再次说话，下人们蜂拥而上，手举大棒子，将他团团围住。乔文仙不会功夫，无法反抗。又上去几个下人，将他的手脚紧紧控制，用绳子捆绑住全身。
[EN] Before he could utter another word, the servants swarmed in with raised clubs. Knowing no martial arts, Qiao Wenxian couldn't fight back; several big men pinned his arms and legs and trussed him tight with rope.
[ZH] 乔文仙努力挣扎，一点儿用都没有：“父亲！你以为把我绑起来就会改变我的想法，告诉你，我依然爱淑真，非她不娶！”
[EN] Struggling in vain, Qiao Wenxian shouted, "Father! Think tying me up will change my heart?! Listen well—I still love Shuzhen, and I'll marry her or nobody!"
[ZH] 乔守仁怒道：“给我把公子抬上马车！”
[EN] Qiao Shouren barked, "Carry Young Master onto the carriage!"
[ZH] 南双跪在地上：“老爷，您就饶了公子吧，公子够可怜……”
[EN] Nan Shuang threw himself to his knees: "Have mercy on Young Master, Master—he's miserable enough!"
[ZH] 乔守仁训道：“你个小兔崽子，别以为我不知道，在中间帮文渊与朱家姑娘传递消息，今日也把你绑起来，一并关入乔府！”
[EN] Qiao Shouren snapped, "You little rascal—don't think I don't know you've been running messages between Wenyuan and Miss Zhu! Tie him up too and lock them both in Qiao Manor!"
[ZH] 下人听到，立刻把南双也给捆了。
[EN] At the command, the servants trussed Nan Shuang up as well.
[ZH] 乔守仁正要将捆绑好的儿子带走，听到仪王的声音：“外公何必如此对待文渊？”
[EN] Just as Qiao Shouren moved to haul his bound son out, Prince Yi's voice cut across the yard: "Why treat Wenyuan like this, Grandfather?"
[ZH] 原来李管家将老爷带到公子的住处后，看出老爷前来会对公子不利，赶快跑去报告仪王，。
[EN] After leading Master to Young Master's courtyard and seeing trouble brewing, Butler Li had bolted straight to warn Prince Yi.
[ZH] 申简辰起得早，正在院中教白风练习拳法，见李管家行色匆匆而来，问后才知文渊之事，匆忙带领白风前来。
[EN] Up at dawn coaching Bai Feng in martial arts, Shen Jianchen heard Butler Li's panicked report and rushed straight over with Bai Feng.
[ZH] 白风本想上前把捆绑乔公子的人打一顿，被王爷阻拦：“对付他们不需要用我教你的拳法。”
[EN] Bai Feng had itched to jump in and clobber the men tying Young Master Qiao, only for Prince Yi to hold him back: "No need to use the fists I taught you on them."
[ZH] 申简辰说完，才对乔守仁喊出话来。
[EN] With that, Shen Jianchen had called out to Qiao Shouren.

#CH 119
[ZH] 乔守仁猜到仪王会出现，他早有准备，先上前恭敬拜道：“仪王殿下，我来是想将文渊带回乔府，望殿下不要阻拦。”
[EN] Expecting Prince Yi to intervene, Qiao Shouren came prepared, stepping up with a deferential bow: "Your Highness Prince Yi, I'm here to bring Wenyuan back to Qiao Manor. Please do not stand in my way."
[ZH] 申简辰劝道：“外公带文渊回乔府，我当然不会阻拦，可您将他绑起来，毫无道理，再说文渊又不是像陈家公子那样的纨绔子弟，您又何须如此对待他？还是放开得好。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen reasoned, "Of course I won't stop Grandfather from taking Wenyuan home, yet tying him up makes no sense. Wenyuan isn't some wild dandy like Young Master Chen—why treat him like a criminal? Untie him."
[ZH] 乔守仁态度坚决：“仪王的话不无道理，可若我不用此法，他肯定不愿乖乖回乔府，我这次带他回去，自然是要将他关起来，直到他心甘情愿放下朱家姑娘。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren stood ironclad: "Prince Yi speaks sense, yet without ropes he won't come home quiet. I'm taking him back to lock him up until he willingly lets go of Miss Zhu."
[ZH] 乔文仙喊道：“仪王救我！我不想被父亲关起来！”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian shouted, "Save me, Prince Yi! I don't want Father locking me up!"
[ZH] 申简辰继续劝说：“外公关得住人，关不住心。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen tried again: "Grandfather can lock up his body, not his heart."
[ZH] 乔守仁说：“心在人身上长着，只要是关住人，心迟早会被关住。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren countered, "The heart sits inside the body—lock up the body long enough, and the heart gives in too."
[ZH] 申简辰见不得文渊受苦，还想继续阻拦：“我若强行阻止呢？”
[EN] Unable to watch Wenyuan suffer, Shen Jianchen pushed: "And what if I stop you by force?"
[ZH] 看样子仪王是非阻拦不可，一家人闹成这样，都怪朱家姑娘，若不解决，恐怕以后文渊会在错误的道路上越陷越深。
[EN] Seeing Prince Yi wouldn't back down, Qiao Shouren blamed every ounce of this family turmoil on Miss Zhu—if he didn't cut it off now, Wenyuan would sink beyond saving.
[ZH] 乔守仁从袖中取出一把匕首，对准自己的脖颈：“仪王若强行阻拦，今日便是我的祭日！”
[EN] Drawing a dagger from his sleeve, Qiao Shouren pressed the blade to his own throat: "If Prince Yi stops me by force, today becomes the day I die!"
[ZH] 没想到乔守仁会来这一招，申简辰既担心外公安危，又不愿文渊痛苦，又认为这一招使得太绝，替他找到不必插手的理由。
[EN] Never expecting Qiao Shouren to pull that stunt, Shen Jianchen felt equal parts alarm for his grandfather's life, pity for Wenyuan, and—truth be told—a handy excuse to step back.
[ZH] “外公何须出此下策？我不阻拦便是。”
[EN] "Why resort to something so extreme, Grandfather? I won't interfere."
[ZH] 乔文仙十分痛苦：“爹，您不必逼仪王，我跟您回去。”
[EN] Torn by anguish, Qiao Wenxian cried, "Don't push Prince Yi, Father—I'll go home with you."
[ZH] 乔守仁收起匕首，又恭敬道：“望仪王不要见怪，我这就带文渊离开，今日将幽林别居赠予仪王，以后文渊不会再来。”
[EN] Sheathing his dagger, Qiao Shouren bowed with full respect once more: "Forgive this old man, Prince Yi. I'll take Wenyuan away at once. As of today, I gift Youlin Retreat to Your Highness—Wenyuan won't set foot here again."
[ZH] 乔守仁带领众人，抬起文渊，迅速离开。
[EN] With that, Qiao Shouren led his men away, hauling Wenyuan off in haste.
[ZH] 申简辰站在原地，只觉文渊这一走，偏僻的别居更加冷清。越冷清，他越渴望跟爱的人在一起。
[EN] Left standing in the empty yard, Shen Jianchen felt this tucked-away retreat grow lonelier than ever with Wenyuan gone—and the lonelier it felt, the stronger his longing burned to be beside the woman he loved.
[ZH] 白风见仪王朝佳和郡主居住的院子走去，他紧跟上，王爷一路上一言不发，他也不敢多说一句。
[EN] Seeing His Highness stride toward Princess Jiahe's courtyard, Bai Feng trailed behind in silence, daring not say a word.
[ZH] 来到闺房小院的门口时，院门还紧闭着。
[EN] At the gate of her small courtyard, the wooden doors still stood bolted shut.
[ZH] 白风踮起脚尖，向院内瞧去，屋门也关着，院子里没有丫鬟的影子。
[EN] Standing on tiptoe to peer over the wall, Bai Feng saw the chamber door closed too, with not a maid in sight.
[ZH] “王爷，郡主应该还睡着，要不您先回去？”
[EN] "Princess must still be asleep, Your Highness. Want to head back first?"
[ZH] “没关系，我在门外等会儿。”申简辰猜测定是千里寻与梦容聊得太晚，才睡到太阳老高还未起床。
[EN] "No need. I'll wait outside a bit." Shen Jianchen figured Qianli Xun and Mengrong had stayed up talking past midnight and were sleeping late into the morning.
[ZH] 他想起穿越前，好不容易打听到千里寻在城里的住处，一大早买束玫瑰花，开着豪车，来到千里寻居住的小区，乘坐电梯，爬上二十二层。
[EN] His mind drifted back before crossing over, to the morning he had hunted down Qianli Xun's apartment in the city, bought a bouquet of roses at dawn, drove his luxury car to her high-rise, and rode the elevator up to the twenty-second floor.
[ZH] 找到千里寻居住的屋子，开始敲门：“咚咚咚！”
[EN] Finding her door, he knocked: *Knock, knock, knock!*
[ZH] 屋内没有任何声音，申简辰觉得纳闷，明明详细调查过千里寻今日必然在，为何没人前来开门？
[EN] Dead silence inside. Puzzled—his intel swore she was home today—why wasn't anyone answering?
[ZH] 他继续敲门，“咚咚咚！”还是没有反应。接着又敲，一连敲四五回，终于传出千里寻睡意浓浓的声音：“谁啊！大清早的，鬼敲什么？”
[EN] He knocked again—*knock, knock, knock!*—still nothing. Four or five rounds later, Qianli Xun's groggy, sleep-thick voice finally yelled through the door: "Who the hell is hammering like a ghost at crack of dawn?!"
[ZH] 听到回应，申简辰兴奋地向里大声说：“是我！”
[EN] Hearing her voice, Shen Jianchen called out in high spirits: "It's me!"
[ZH] “你是哪路神仙？大清早打扰老娘休息！”
[EN] "What deity on earth disturbs my beauty sleep this early?!"
[ZH] “我！申简辰！”
[EN] "Me! Shen Jianchen!"
[ZH] “赶紧滚！老娘昨晚码字码到深夜，困着呢！别来烦我！”
[EN] "Scram! I was writing until dawn and I'm dead tired—quit bugging me!"
[ZH] 接着，再听不到声音。
[EN] Then dead silence again.
[ZH] 既然人在，申简辰便放心。他把玫瑰花放在地上，人也坐在地上，背靠墙，掏出手机，边打游戏边等千里寻起床。
[EN] Knowing she was home put him at ease. Setting the roses on the floor, he slumped down against the hallway wall, pulled out his phone, and played games while waiting for her to wake up.
