[ZH] 申简辰已将工作安排好，今日主要用来追求千里寻，不见到本尊，誓不离开。
[EN] He had cleared his whole work schedule today for chasing Qianli Xun—no leaving until he saw her in person.
[ZH] 大约等到中午十二点时，申简辰隐约听到屋内有穿拖鞋走路的声音。立刻收起手机，捧好玫瑰花，整理整理衣装，清清嗓子，又开始敲门：“咚咚咚！”
[EN] Around noon, the faint shuffle of slippers sounded inside. Tucking his phone away, he scooped up the roses, smoothed his suit, cleared his throat, and knocked again: *Knock, knock, knock!*
[ZH] 门很快被打开，千里寻头发凌乱，瞪着一双有眼屎的大眼睛：“你怎么还没走？”
[EN] The door swung open to reveal Qianli Xun with wild bedhead, glaring out of wide eyes crusted with eye boogers: "You're still here?!"
[ZH] “怎么样？漂亮吧？”申简辰把花束举到千里寻面前。
[EN] "Well? Pretty, right?" Shen Jianchen thrust the bouquet under her nose.
[ZH] “你怎么这么俗！就知道送玫瑰花！”
[EN] "So tacky! All you ever bring are roses!"
[ZH] “俗吗？我不觉得。在外面等你几个小时，能不能看在我如此执着的份上，别把我堵在门口？让我进去？”
[EN] "Tacky? I don't think so. After waiting outside for hours, can't you reward my persistence and let me in instead of blocking the door?"
[ZH] 千里寻把门开大：“进来吧，把花放在桌上，以后不准给老娘送花！老娘最不喜欢这类俗套。”
[EN] Qianli Xun swung the door wider. "Come in, put the flowers on the table, and never bring me flowers again—I hate cheesy stuff like that!"
[ZH] 申简辰把花放在桌上，将屋内扫视一番：“装修得太简单，改日我找人重新给你设计装修一番。”
[EN] Setting the bouquet down, Shen Jianchen glanced around the apartment: "Way too bare in here. One of these days I'll hire designers to renovate it for you."
[ZH] “你脑子是不是有病？装修不得花时间，花钱，还得散甲醛味儿，老娘什么时候才能住进来！”
[EN] "Are you nuts?! Renovating takes time, money, and weeks airing out formaldehyde—when am I supposed to live in it?!"
[ZH] “实在不行，我给你买个精装修的房子，拎包入住，怎么样？”
[EN] "Fine, I'll buy you a luxury turnkey condo—just bring your suitcase. Deal?"
[ZH] “申简辰，你有几个钱就了不起！老娘也不差钱，要你买？”
[EN] "Think having a little cash makes you king, Shen Jianchen? I'm not broke—who asked you to buy me a place?!"
[ZH] 申简辰觉得千里寻不是一般的女孩儿，难以用钱摆平。
[EN] Shen Jianchen realized Qianli Xun was no ordinary girl money could win over.
[ZH] “收拾收拾，我带你出去吃大餐。”
[EN] "Go freshen up—I'm taking you out for a fancy feast."
[ZH] “吃什么大餐！搞得老娘好像什么都没吃过一样，告诉你，老娘最讨厌的就是大餐，少在我面前来这一套！”
[EN] "Fancy feast, my foot! Acting like I've never eaten decent food! Hate to break it to you, I despise fancy restaurants—save that routine!"
[ZH] 申简辰感受到前所未有的难度：“你想吃什么？我都听你的。”
[EN] Facing the toughest challenge of his life, Shen Jianchen yielded: "Whatever you want to eat—your call."
[ZH] 千里寻怼得开心，见申简辰一副无奈的样子，笑道：“呵呵……，我今儿不想出去，看在你在外面等得辛苦的份上，我给你煮碗泡面，外加一个荷包蛋。”
[EN] Pleased with shutting him down and amused at his helpless look, Qianli Xun cracked a grin: "Haha, I don't feel like going out today. Since you suffered waiting outside so long, I'll cook you a bowl of instant noodles with a poached egg."
[ZH] “煮泡面啊，我最拿手，你待着，我来煮。”申简辰很开心，亲手煮碗泡面给千里寻吃，的确比请她吃大餐要浪漫许多。
[EN] "Instant noodles? My specialty! Sit tight—I'll cook." Making her instant noodles with his own hands beat a five-star restaurant by a mile.
[ZH] 千里寻果断同意，安心去卫生间洗漱收拾，将厨房交给申简辰。
[EN] Qianli Xun agreed at once and went off to wash up in the bathroom, leaving him the kitchen.
[ZH] 申简辰打开冰箱，里面除两桶牛奶外，只有几包泡面、青菜和鸡蛋：“这日子过的，真是清贫，要是跟我在一起，绝对会把你的冰箱塞得满满当当。”
[EN] Opening her fridge to find two cartons of milk, a few packs of instant noodles, greens, and eggs, he muttered, "What a Spartan life—get with me and I'll pack your fridge to bursting."
[ZH] 申简辰取出食材，进厨房忙碌。等千里寻收拾完后，泡面已煮好，一人一碗，上面还有一个荷包蛋。
[EN] Grabbing the ingredients, he got cooking. By the time Qianli Xun came out fresh and tidy, two steaming bowls of noodles topped with poached eggs sat ready on the table.
[ZH] 千里寻边吃边夸，说申简辰比她会煮泡面。
[EN] Slurping away, Qianli Xun praised his noodles as even better than hers.
[ZH] 心爱的女孩吃得那么香，申简辰忍不住趁机表白：“我愿意给你煮一辈子泡面。”
[EN] Watching the girl he loved eat with such relish, Shen Jianchen seized the moment to confess: "I'd love to cook instant noodles for you for the rest of our lives."
[ZH] 千里寻笑着怼道：“呵呵……你想把老娘吃得腻死！”
[EN] Qianli Xun laughed right back: "Haha, trying to make me die of boredom eating noodles?!"
[ZH] 想到这里，申简辰脸上露出笑容，那一次他与千里寻待得时间最长，聊得最开心。
[EN] Remembering that day curved Shen Jianchen's lips into a warm smile—the longest, happiest afternoon he had ever spent with Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 白风发现王爷脸上笑容满满，甚是奇怪，又不敢多问。
[EN] Watching his master beam at thin air baffled Bai Feng, though he dared not ask.
[ZH] 他继续踮起脚尖，向院内瞧去，苦荞正好从丫鬟房出来，白风连忙招手，不敢将声音放都太大：“苦荞！苦荞！这边！看这边！”
[EN] Tiptoeing to peek over the wall again, Bai Feng spotted Kuqiao stepping out of the maids' quarters and waved frantic hands in a stage whisper: "Kuqiao! Kuqiao! Over here! Look here!"
[ZH] 苦荞隐约听到白风的声音，向周围瞧了一遍，终于看到院墙外白风的脑袋。
[EN] Catching his whisper, Kuqiao glanced around until she spotted Bai Feng's head bobbing above the wall.
[ZH] ﻿她走到院门跟前，把门打开，仪王的身影出现在面前。
[EN] Walking over, she unbolted the courtyard gate—finding Prince Yi standing right outside.
[ZH] 苦荞赶紧行礼：“仪王，郡主与我家小姐还睡着，我去把她们唤醒。”
[EN] Kuqiao dropped into a bow at once: "Prince Yi! Princess and my mistress are still asleep—I'll go wake them."
[ZH] “不必，我在这里等着就好。”申简辰很享受等待的过程，只因让他想起以前美好的时光。
[EN] "No need. I'm fine waiting right here." Shen Jianchen savored the wait; it brought back those bright days from before.
[ZH] “我给仪王端把椅子……”
[EN] "Let me bring Prince Yi a chair—"
[ZH] “不必，我喜欢站着，你跟白风去玩儿吧，不必管我。”
[EN] "No need, I'd rather stand. Go play with Bai Feng and leave me be."
[ZH] “谢王爷！”王爷发话，白风无不听从，关键他也这么想，便拉起苦荞，消失在王爷面前。
[EN] "Thank you, Your Highness!" Needing no second invitation, Bai Feng grabbed Kuqiao's hand and vanished around the corner.
[ZH] 申简辰亲自将院门关好，心甘情愿在门口守护，他希望千里寻起来后，看到的第一个男子便是他。
[EN] Shutting the gate himself, Shen Jianchen stood guard outside with a willing heart, hoping he would be the very first man Qianli Xun saw when she woke.
[ZH] 喜兰从她的房间出来，抬头仰望天空，太阳已老高，快到午饭时间，还是赶紧把郡主叫醒。
[EN] Stepping out of her own room, Xilan squinted up at the high sun—almost noon! Time to wake the princess.
[ZH] 郡主与幽栖居士同榻而卧，一个醒来，另一个肯定也会醒。她需伺候郡主，哪顾得上幽栖居士。喜兰推开丫鬟房的门，不见苦荞，只有夏果躺在床上呼呼大睡。
[EN] With the princess and Youqi Jushi sharing one bed, waking one meant waking both; Xilan only cared about tending her own mistress anyway. Pushing open the maids' quarters door, she saw no Kuqiao, only Xiaguo dead to the world, snoring loud.
[ZH] 夏果昨晚大胆表白，被拒绝，却也兴奋，一高兴怎么都睡不着，后来不知几时才渐渐睡去。
[EN] Thrilled yet stung after confessing last night, Xiaguo had lain awake half the night before crashing hard toward dawn.
[ZH] 喜兰唤道：“夏果，快起来，跟我一起把主子们叫醒。”
[EN] Xilan called out, "Xiaguo, get up! Come help me wake the mistresses!"
[ZH] 根本没用，夏果什么都听不到，依旧呼呼大睡。
[EN] Zero response—Xiaguo kept snoring away.
[ZH] 喜兰把手伸进被窝，在夏果的手臂上拧一下。
[EN] Reaching under the quilt, Xilan gave Xiaguo's arm a sharp pinch.
[ZH] 夏果猛然疼醒，刚要骂，用朦胧的睡眼发现是喜兰。人家可是郡主的丫鬟，自然比她身份尊贵，便硬撑着坐起来：“喜兰姐姐想唤醒我，也没必要拧人家，老疼。”
[EN] Jolting awake with a yelp, Xiaguo was about to curse when her blurry eyes made out Xilan—the princess's personal maid outranked her, so she swallowed her anger and sat up: "If Sister Xilan wants to wake me, no need to pinch—that hurt!"
[ZH] “我唤你好几声，你都没反应，苦荞丫头也不见人影儿，只好拧你，还别说，这招真灵。赶紧下床跟我把主子们叫醒，都快晌午。”
[EN] "Called you five times and you didn't budge, and Kuqiao's nowhere to be found! Works like magic, doesn't it? Drag yourself out of bed—it's almost noon!"
[ZH] 夏果打了个大大的哈欠，很不情愿地走下床，穿好衣裳，根本没时间精心收拾，就赶着去给小姐忙活。她更对自己的人生不满，什么时候能摆脱丫鬟的身份，成为被人伺候的主子？
[EN] Letting out a huge yawn, Xiaguo crawled out of bed and threw on her clothes with zero time to tidy up, hurrying off to serve her mistress—resenting her lot in life more than ever. When would she shed this maid's skin and become a mistress waited on by others?

#CH 120
[ZH] 两个丫鬟来到闺房门口，喜兰向屋内说：“郡主，日头老高，您该起床，再不起，只怕王爷一会儿来，您还睡着。”
[EN] Reaching the bedchamber door, Xilan called inside, "Princess, the sun is high—time to rise! Sleep any longer and His Highness might arrive to catch you still in bed."
[ZH] 喜兰真是懂郡主的心思，听到哥哥可能会来，乔梦容顿时清醒，从床上爬起来。
[EN] Xilan knew her mistress inside out: at the mention of Prince Yi visiting, Qiao Mengrong snapped wide awake and scrambled up.
[ZH] “我怎么只顾睡觉，忘记哥哥可能会过来，他要是过来见我还睡着，岂不笑话？淑真，你也赶紧起来，难道你不想见乔公子？”
[EN] "How did I sleep so late, forgetting Brother might drop by? If he catches me still in bed, how embarrassing! Wake up too, Shuzhen—don't you want to see Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 乔文仙如同千里寻的兴奋剂，她也坐起来：“是该起床，昨日我跟文渊没说几句，你与仪王过来，之后只顾跟你聊，今日我要与他好好说说话。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian's name worked like instant adrenaline on Qianli Xun, who sat bolt upright: "Right! Barely got two words in with Wenyuan yesterday before you and Prince Yi showed up, and then I talked with you all night—today I'm going to have a proper chat with him."
[ZH] “你们进来吧！”乔梦容向门外吩咐完，又笑着问，“难道嫌弃我与哥哥打扰你们？”
[EN] "Come in, you two!" calling to the maids outside, Qiao Mengrong added with a smile, "Holding a grudge that Brother and I interrupted you yesterday?"
[ZH] “哪敢？你们两个来不叫打扰，那叫锦上添花！跟你聊一晚，心情好很多，正能量满满。哪怕现在站在大街上，别人当面指责我，我都不会当回事，因为有身份尊贵的郡主把我当朋友，这份荣耀足以抵挡所有攻击而来的流言蜚语！”
[EN] "Not a chance! You two weren't interrupting—you were icing on the cake! Talking with you all night lifted a ton off my chest and pumped me full of positive energy. Even if folk yelled insults in my face on the street right now, I wouldn't flinch—having a royal princess as my bosom friend is armor enough against every rumor in town!"
[ZH] 丫鬟已进来，各自伺候主人穿衣梳洗。
[EN] Entering the room, the maids helped each mistress dress and wash up.
[ZH] 千里寻问：“苦荞呢？怎么没见人影？”
[EN] Qianli Xun asked, "Where's Kuqiao? Haven't seen her."
[ZH] 夏果边帮小姐穿衣裳边说：“有白风在，苦荞姐姐怎么可能闲着？”
[EN] Helping her mistress into her robe, Xiaguo said, "With Bai Feng around, when does Sister Kuqiao ever sit still?"
[ZH] 乔梦容已穿好衣裳，刚坐到梳妆台前，听到夏果提起白风，心想，苦荞不见，说明白风来过，白风既来，定少不了哥哥，难道哥哥来过？
[EN] Already dressed and seated at the vanity, Qiao Mengrong froze at the mention of Bai Feng. If Kuqiao was gone, Bai Feng had come—and wherever Bai Feng went, Prince Yi wasn't far behind! Could he be here right now?
[ZH] 她已坐不住，想要起身，见头发不整，急忙说：“喜兰，快帮我收拾，说不定哥哥就在门外。”
[EN] Unable to sit still, she started to rise, caught her untidy hair in the mirror, and urged, "Hurry and do my hair, Xilan—Brother might be right outside the gate!"
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“郡主是长了千里眼吗？一眼能看到门外？”
[EN] Qianli Xun laughed: "Since when do you have clairvoyant eyes, Princess, seeing straight through walls?"
[ZH] “我能感觉到哥哥站在门外。”乔梦容也不知自己何来如此肯定的感觉。
[EN] "I can just feel Brother standing right outside." Even Qiao Mengrong couldn't explain where that sudden certainty came from.
[ZH] 喜兰很快为郡主整理完头发。
[EN] Xilan pinned up her hair in record time.
[ZH] 乔梦容匆匆起身，快步来到院中，又快步走到院门旁，在心里默默祈祷：“如若哥哥站在门外，说明他心里有我。”
[EN] Jumping up, Qiao Mengrong hurried across the yard to the gate, whispering a silent prayer: "If Brother really is standing outside, it proves he has me in his heart."
[ZH] 喜兰要去开门，被乔梦容阻止，她要亲自验证强烈的感觉，要让祈祷成真。
[EN] Waving Xilan back from the latch, she meant to test her prayer with her own hands.
[ZH] 乔梦容小心翼翼上前将院门慢慢打开，她很害怕哥哥不在，开门的一刻闭上双眼。门打开后，她的呼吸变得紧张……
[EN] Heart pounding, she eased the wooden gate ajar, squeezing her eyes shut for fear of finding the lane empty...
[ZH] 传来喜兰的声音：“王爷，您果真站在门外！”
[EN] Xilan's voice rang out: "Your Highness, you really are waiting right outside!"
[ZH] 乔梦容欣喜地睁开双眼，哥哥高大魁梧的身影就在眼前，真实得不能再真实，看来哥哥心中真的有她。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's eyes flew open to find his tall, sturdy figure standing right before her, real as daylight—he really did hold her in his heart!
[ZH] 这一刻，乔梦容埋怨自己不该怀疑哥哥，他若心中没她，为何要站在门外候着？她忍不住又一次抱住哥哥。
[EN] Scolding herself for ever doubting him—why else would he wait outside her gate?—she rushed forward and threw her arms around him once more.
[ZH] “哥哥，你在门外站了多久？”
[EN] "How long has Brother been waiting out here?"
[ZH] 没有被心中所想之人拥抱，申简辰倍感失落。最近梦容很爱搂抱他，以后还是少给她搂抱的机会。
[EN] Embraced by the wrong woman, emptiness sank into Shen Jianchen's chest. Mengrong was getting way too fond of hugging him lately; he'd have to keep his distance better.
[ZH] “我……我没站多久，怎么只有你一人起来？幽栖居士难道还睡着？”
[EN] "Not... not long. Why are you the only one up? Is Youqi Jushi still asleep?"
[ZH] “淑真已起床，正在梳妆。不知为何，我感觉到哥哥在门外等我，匆忙出来，哥哥果然在，你说我们是不是心灵相通？”
[EN] "Shuzhen's up, just finishing her hair. Somehow I sensed Brother waiting outside, hurried out, and here you are! Doesn't that mean our hearts beat as one?"
[ZH] 申简辰没有回答，他望着院门内，希望看到喜欢的人出现在眼前。
[EN] Without answering, Shen Jianchen looked past her into the courtyard, longing for the figure he had waited all morning to see.
[ZH] 瞧了一会儿，她向他走来，申简辰的脸上露出笑容，同时为自己感到悲哀。
[EN] A moment later, she came walking out—bringing a smile to Shen Jianchen's lips and a pang of sorrow to his heart.
[ZH] 千里寻来到门口，瞧见佳和郡主搂抱住仪王，而仪王双手耷拉着，没有丝毫拥抱郡主的意思。当她走近时，又瞧见仪王那双直勾勾的眼睛，两只眼珠子都快从眼眶里蹦出来。
[EN] Reaching the gate, Qianli Xun saw Princess Jiahe clinging tight to Prince Yi while his arms hung limp at his sides—and the instant she approached, his eyes locked onto her so hard they looked ready to pop from his sockets.
[ZH] 千里寻在心里悄悄批判道：“身在福中不知福！有美人在怀，还敢用那种眼神看老娘！”
[EN] Qianli Xun scoffed inside: "Blind to the blessing in his own arms! Holding a beauty and still ogling me like that!"
[ZH] 她边想边走出院门，经过二位身边时，有礼道：“二位忙，我去找文渊。”
[EN] Stepping through the gate past them, she bowed with polite distance: "I'll leave you two—off to see Wenyuan."
[ZH] ﻿千里寻说完就要离开。
[EN] With that, she turned to go.
[ZH] “幽栖居士！等等！”申简辰迅速喊道，他将乔梦容扶开，“梦容，我有话与幽栖居士说。”
[EN] "Wait, Youqi Jushi!" Shen Jianchen called out at once, easing Qiao Mengrong off his chest. "Mengrong, I have words for Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 因为欢心，乔梦容很听话：“哥哥尽管说，我回避就是。”
[EN] Floating on air, Qiao Mengrong agreed: "Go ahead, Brother—I'll step aside."
[ZH] “你不用回避，又不是什么秘密。”
[EN] "No need to step aside. It isn't a secret."
[ZH] 哥哥的坦诚又使得乔梦容欢心，她点点头。
[EN] His openness warmed Qiao Mengrong's heart even more, and she nodded.
[ZH] 仪王真是多事，未婚妻在此，竟然敢公然叫住她！他既然不避讳，千里寻觉得她也没有必要回避，不如趁此机会让郡主知道仪王的真实面目。
[EN] What a busybody—calling her back right in front of his fiancée! Since he didn't bother hiding, Qianli Xun saw no reason to either; maybe this was her chance to expose his true colors to the princess!
[ZH] “仪王有何吩咐？”
[EN] "What does Prince Yi want?"
[ZH] 仪王向她靠近几步，又用深情的目光盯着她。
[EN] Stepping closer, Prince Yi fixed her with that same deep, ardent gaze.
[ZH] 千里寻瞥一眼仪王身后的佳和郡主，更觉她可怜。她已下定决心，在此时此刻揭露仪王的真面目，刚要说：“仪王，你怎么能当着梦容的面勾引我？”
[EN] Glancing at Princess Jiahe behind him, pity surged in Qianli Xun. She made up her mind to blow his cover right here and now—her mouth opened to say, "Prince Yi, how dare you flirt with me right in front of Mengrong?!"
[ZH] 话还未出口，却听仪王说：“幽栖居士恐怕很难再见到文渊。”
[EN] Before a syllable came out, Prince Yi spoke: "I'm afraid Youqi Jushi won't be seeing Wenyuan anytime soon."
[ZH] “什么意思？”对方所说直接将千里寻想要揭露的话语狠狠打回去。
[EN] "What do you mean?" His words knocked her accusation straight back down her throat.
[ZH] “今日一大早，幽栖居士睡得正香时，外公带人前来，将文渊五花大绑带回乔府。”
[EN] "At crack of dawn while you were sound asleep, Grandfather showed up with men, trussed Wenyuan up like a bundle, and hauled him back to Qiao Manor."
[ZH] “什么？怎么会发生这种事？你不是王爷吗？为何不拦着？为何不派人把我叫醒？我去拦也可以！”
[EN] "What?! How could that happen?! Aren't you a prince—why didn't you stop him?! Why didn't you send someone to wake me? I would've stopped him myself!"
[ZH] 申简辰第一次见为爱情而痛苦的千里寻，穿越前的她根本不是这样的人，是否会有一天，她也为他如此难过？
[EN] For the first time, Shen Jianchen saw Qianli Xun cut raw by love—nothing like the woman he knew before crossing over. Would she ever hurt like this over him?
[ZH] “我拦过，外公用匕首对着自己的脖子，显然他为此事连性命都不顾，我也无能为力。再说把你唤醒又有何用？难道幽栖居士想觍着脸求乔家娶你？”
[EN] "I tried stopping him. Grandfather pressed a dagger to his own neck—he'd die before budging, so my hands were tied. Besides, what good would waking you do? Did Youqi Jushi mean to throw away her pride and beg the Qiao family to marry her?"
[ZH] 申简辰将话说得难听，无非是想让千里寻明白她与文渊已不可能，希望她放下。只有她放下文渊，也许有那么一天，心中才会对他生出情感来。
[EN] Shen Jianchen spoke harsh on purpose, hoping to snap her out of illusions about Wenyuan—only if she let Wenyuan go might a spark ever open for him someday.
[ZH] 哥哥的话不入耳，乔梦容担心淑真心中不快，小心批评道：“哥哥怎能如此说淑真？”
[EN] Wincing at his harsh tone, Qiao Mengrong chided him soft: "How can Brother talk to Shuzhen like that?"
[ZH] 夏果也很难过，乔公子被五花大绑肯定不好难受，小姐与乔公子没有希望，她的希望也会变得渺茫。
[EN] Heartache gripped Xiaguo too—poor Young Master Qiao trussed up in ropes! If Miss's hopes died, hers grew dimmer too.
[ZH] 她见小姐明明难过，却努力控制好情绪，用稍显平静的语气说：“我虽爱文渊，可也不会觍着脸求乔家娶我，只是想不通，我有那么差吗？乔家何至于为拆散我们将文渊五花大绑带走？”
[EN] Watching her mistress swallow her grief and keep her voice steady, Xiaguo admired her nerve: "Much as I love Wenyuan, I'd never throw away my pride begging the Qiaos to take me. I just can't fathom it—am I really that awful, that the Qiao family had to tie Wenyuan up just to tear us apart?"
[ZH] 乔梦容走到幽栖居士面前：“淑真，你不差，只是世事弄人。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong stepped up beside her: "Nothing's wrong with you, Shuzhen—it's just cruel fate."
[ZH] “谢谢你梦容，我该回去。”千里寻很感激佳和郡主，走之前叮嘱仪王，“仪王，您与梦容很幸运，有一纸婚约在手，希望您懂得珍惜眼前人。”
[EN] "Thank you, Mengrong. I'd better head home." Grateful for her friend's kindness, Qianli Xun left Prince Yi a pointed parting line: "You and Mengrong are lucky to hold a betrothal in your hands, Prince Yi—treasure the one right in front of you."
[ZH] 千里寻带着夏果向幽林别居外走去，她表情凝重，眼中蓄满泪水，稍不注意便会从脸颊上滚落下来。她在心中默念着：难道我与文渊已不可能？
[EN] Leading Xiaguo toward the retreat's gate, Qianli Xun kept her face rigid, eyes brimming with tears one blink away from spilling. "Are Wenyuan and I really finished?" she whispered inside.
[ZH] 她回想昨日的情景，在神柳下，他们互相诉说不会放弃的话。千里寻决定，只要文渊不给她传来想要放弃的讯息，她绝对不会放弃。
[EN] Then yesterday beneath the Divine Willow flashed back—their matching vows never to give up. Her jaw set: until Wenyuan himself sent word he was giving up, she would never quit.
[ZH] 千里寻带着伤痛离开，申简辰心中不安：“梦容，我出去一趟。”
[EN] Watching Qianli Xun walk away in pain tore at Shen Jianchen: "Mengrong, I have to step out."
[ZH] “哥哥要去哪里？”
[EN] "Where is Brother going?"
[ZH] “文渊被绑到乔家，我很担心，想去乔家再次劝说外公。”
[EN] "I'm worried about Wenyuan hauled back in ropes—I'm going to Qiao Manor to try persuading Grandfather again."
[ZH] “已到午饭时间，哥哥用完饭再去。”
[EN] "It's lunchtime already—eat before you go."
[ZH] 申简辰揪心千里寻，哪里吃得下饭：“少一顿，无妨。”
[EN] Sick with worry for Qianli Xun, how could he swallow food? "Missing one meal won't hurt."
[ZH] 刚走出一步，又觉对不起梦容，转过身，走到梦容跟前，将她抱住：“梦容，你放心，哥哥会娶你，会给你一个满意的婚礼。”
[EN] Taking a step away, guilt tugged him back toward Mengrong. Turning around, he wrapped his arms around her: "Rest easy, Mengrong—I will marry you and give you a wedding to make you proud."
[ZH] 失忆后的哥哥第一次拥抱她，又一次主动说要娶她的话，简直是意外之喜，一直以来藏在心底的阴云立刻烟消云散，变得阳光明媚。
[EN] His very first embrace since waking from amnesia—and another promise to wed her! A windfall of joy swept every lingering cloud from her heart, leaving pure sunshine.
[ZH] “你就是我的哥哥。”
[EN] "That's my Brother!"
[ZH] 申简辰迅速放开梦容，追着千里寻而去。
[EN] Releasing her after a beat, Shen Jianchen hurried off after Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 乔梦容还沉浸在喜悦中：“喜兰，你看到吗？哥哥终于主动抱我，他的怀抱既有力又温暖。”
[EN] Still floating in bliss, Qiao Mengrong sighed, "Did you see that, Xilan? Brother hugged me on his own at last—his arms are so strong and warm!"
[ZH] 喜兰也替郡主高兴：“奴婢看得清清楚楚，王爷还说要给郡主一个满意的婚礼。”
[EN] Xilan beamed for her mistress: "Saw every bit of it, Princess! His Highness even promised you a wedding to make you proud!"
[ZH] “后天就是中秋节，离明年中秋节还有整整一年，时间过得太慢，真希望明日就能嫁给哥哥。”
[EN] "Mid-Autumn is the day after tomorrow—a whole year left until next Mid-Autumn! Time crawls so slow; I wish I could marry Brother tomorrow!"
[ZH] 千里寻懒得寻苦荞，带着夏果上了幽林别居的马车，麻烦车夫送她一程。
[EN] With no patience to hunt down Kuqiao, Qianli Xun boarded a Youlin Retreat carriage with Xiaguo and asked the driver to take her home.
[ZH] 申简辰快步走到大门口时，正好碰到白风与苦荞，他叫住白风：“幽栖居士已走，你还带着人家的丫鬟，太不像话！”
[EN] Striding up to the front gate, Shen Jianchen caught Bai Feng strolling back with Kuqiao and snapped, "Youqi Jushi already left, and here you are keeping her maid—outrageous!"
[ZH] 白风委屈道：“是王爷让我带苦荞去玩儿的，现在又来怪我。”
[EN] Bai Feng protested: "Your Highness told me to take Kuqiao off to play, and now you blame me!"
[ZH] 听到小姐离开，苦荞说：“我不能跟你继续玩儿，要去找我家小姐。”苦荞说着就往外走。
[EN] Hearing her mistress had left, Kuqiao cried, "I can't play anymore—I have to catch up to Miss!" and started out the gate.
[ZH] “别着急，我送你一程。”申简辰拦住苦荞，又吩咐白风，“愣着干什么？还不快去套辆马车，你来赶车。”
[EN] "Hold on, I'll give you a ride." Stopping Kuqiao, Shen Jianchen barked at Bai Feng, "What are you standing around for? Go hitch a carriage and drive!"
[ZH] 白风走出大门，很快套好马车，载着王爷与苦荞去追幽栖居士。他感到纳闷，王爷到底是自己追幽栖居士还是为帮苦荞？
[EN] Hurrying out, Bai Feng hitched a carriage in no time, driving Prince Yi and Kuqiao after Youqi Jushi—wondering the whole way whether His Highness was chasing Youqi Jushi for himself or helping Kuqiao.
[ZH] 若是王爷追，没有任何道理，若是帮苦荞，还有些道理。可他第一次见一位身份尊贵之人帮一个丫鬟做这种芝麻绿豆大的小事。
[EN] Chasing Youqi Jushi made zero sense; helping a maid made slightly more, yet who ever saw a royal prince running tiny errands for a maid?
[ZH] 很快，白风已想通，仪王是个不一样的王爷，在别人看来不可能的事，发生在他身上实属正常。
[EN] Before long Bai Feng shrugged it off—Prince Yi was unlike any other prince alive; impossible things were just Tuesday for him.

#CH 121
[ZH] 乔守仁把儿子五花大绑后，在赶回乔府的路上，两人坐在马车里沉默许久。
[EN] Having trussed his son up tight, Qiao Shouren sat opposite him in silence all the way back to Qiao Manor.
[ZH] 乔文仙正在生父亲的气。父亲实在过分，他大小也是临安才子，竟然一点儿面子都不给，说绑就绑。还用死威胁仪王，从小到大第一次见父亲如此。
[EN] Fury simmered in Qiao Wenxian. Father had gone way too far—he was a respected scholar of Lin'an, yet Father hadn't spared him a shred of dignity, tying him up like a thief and even threatening suicide in front of Prince Yi! Never in all his years had he seen Father like this.
[ZH] 他更加担心，面对父亲强硬的态度，恐怕想要与淑真在一起，获得长辈祝福，可能性极其微弱。
[EN] Worse, facing iron stubborness like this made winning his parents' blessing to wed Shuzhen look hopeless.
[ZH] 想起仪王提出私奔的主意，乔文仙后悔，为何昨日要拒绝！如果昨日他同意，如果昨日他带淑真离开，也不会有今日之事。
[EN] Remembering Prince Yi's suggestion to elope, Qiao Wenxian kicked himself for turning it down yesterday! Had he agreed and carried Shuzhen off yesterday, none of this would have happened.
[ZH] 儿子被捆绑，身上自然不舒服，乔守仁也疼在心里，可他顾不了那么多。
[EN] Watching his son squirm in ropes hurt Qiao Shouren's heart too, yet he had no choice.
[ZH] 昨晚，别居的心腹下人匆匆来报，说公子将朱家姑娘留在幽林别居时，乔守仁心中不安，担心儿子做出傻事。
[EN] Last night, when his trusted servant at the retreat rushed over reporting Young Master had kept Miss Zhu overnight, panic seized Qiao Shouren lest his son do something reckless.
[ZH] 一旦二人做出傻事，后悔都来不及。虽说朱家姑娘被赶出家门，若乔家儿子做出傻事，朱家肯定会不依不饶，娶朱家姑娘岂不是成定局？
[EN] If the two crossed a line together, there would be no undoing it—even cast out by Zhu Manor, the Zhus would raise hell and force the Qiaos into marriage!
[ZH] 乔守仁更怕儿子与朱家姑娘私奔，自己的儿子自己了解，可朱家姑娘是什么样，他没见识过。她被传成那样，估计什么事都干得出来，要不怎么好好的姑娘家夜不归宿住在别人家里？何况宅子里还住着两个外男？
[EN] Worse still, what if they eloped? He knew his own innocent son, yet who knew what Miss Zhu was capable of? With a reputation that dark, what wouldn't she try—sleeping overnight at another family's house with two unmarried men under the roof!
[ZH] 知府大人到底怎么教育的？竟教出这类不知羞的姑娘来！也是，她有过一段婚姻，自然比儿子经历多，又懂得多，什么都没了，自是不管不顾。可文渊还是个纯情男子，又怎能经得起她勾引？
[EN] How on earth had the Prefect raised such a shameless daughter?! Having been married once already, she knew the ways of the world and had nothing left to lose, whereas pure-hearted Wenyuan stood no chance against her charms.
[ZH] 乔守仁越想越害怕，一晚上没休息好，一大早早早起来，集合下人，将一切可能想好，做好充足准备，绑也要把他绑回乔府，关上十天半个月，等老实了，不再与朱家姑娘来往，再将他放出来。
[EN] Lying awake all night in dread, Qiao Shouren had rallied his men at dawn prepared for anything—determined to drag his son home in ropes and lock him up for ten days or half a month until he came to his senses and cut ties with Miss Zhu.
[ZH] “你别一直用那种表情对着我，今日你恨我，总有一日你会感激我。”
[EN] "Quit glaring at me like that. Hate me today if you want; one day you'll thank me."
[ZH] 乔文仙懒得理会父亲。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian refused to give his father a single word.
[ZH] “把朱家姑娘忘了吧，临安城好姑娘多得是，何必盯着她不放？依我看，外面的流言说得一点儿没错！她就不是个好姑娘！”
[EN] "Forget Miss Zhu. Lin'an is overflowing with fine girls—why cling to her? If you ask me, those street rumors didn't lie one bit! She's no decent girl!"
[ZH] 父亲批判淑真，乔文仙无法再沉默下去：“爹说我便说我，不要诋毁淑真，在我眼中她是世上最好的姑娘。”
[EN] Hearing Father smear Shuzhen broke Qiao Wenxian's silence: "Scold me all you want, Father, just don't slander Shuzhen! In my eyes, she's the finest woman alive."
[ZH] “好什么好？好人家的姑娘能随随便便夜不归宿？”
[EN] "Finest? Does a decent girl stay overnight at a man's house on a whim?"
[ZH] “佳和郡主邀请她住一晚，再说她与佳和郡主睡在一处，昨晚也派丫鬟回去告知过家里的嬷嬷。”
[EN] "Princess Jiahe invited her! She shared a bed with Princess Jiahe and even sent her maid back to tell her matron!"
[ZH] “那又如何？好人家的姑娘谁会像她一样大晚上留宿在别人家中？”
[EN] "So what? What respectable daughter sleeps under someone else's roof overnight?"
[ZH] “父亲不必劝说，在我眼里，淑真就是最好的姑娘。”
[EN] "Save your breath, Father—to me, Shuzhen is the best woman in this world."
[ZH] “你既如此认为，就在乔府好好待着，什么时候把朱家姑娘忘记，什么时候放你出来。”
[EN] "Believe that if you like—then sit tight at Qiao Manor! The day you forget Miss Zhu is the day I let you out."
[ZH] 乔文仙不再言语。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian clamped his jaw shut.
[ZH] 回到乔府，乔守仁吩咐下人将儿子抬进他常住的屋子，把窗户钉死，仅留一个送饭的方形孔来，可容碗碟出入。
[EN] Back at Qiao Manor, Qiao Shouren ordered servants to haul his son into his usual bedchamber, nail every window shut, and leave only one small square hatch just wide enough to slide meal trays through.
[ZH] 一切就绪后，乔守仁又在屋里巡视一番，发现无路可逃后，亲手给儿子松绑，又差人将南双带进来。
[EN] Inspecting the room to confirm no escape route existed, Qiao Shouren untied his son himself, then had Nan Shuang shoved inside too.
[ZH] “将你和公子关在一处，好好伺候他，没事劝劝他，什么时候他不再想朱家姑娘，什么时候放你们出去。”
[EN] "You stay locked in here with Young Master—wait on him and talk some sense into him. When he stops pining for Miss Zhu, you both come out."
[ZH] 乔守仁走到屋外，门被关上，南双清晰地听到上锁的声音。
[EN] Stepping outside, Qiao Shouren pulled the door shut, and Nan Shuang heard the heavy padlock click home.
[ZH] “公子，老爷真把我们锁起来！”
[EN] "Young Master, Master really locked us in!"
[ZH] 乔文仙显得异常冷静，倒在床上：“锁就锁，该吃吃，该喝喝，我爹锁住人，锁不住我的心。我永远不会放下淑真，看他能把我怎样！难道还想关我一辈子不成？”
[EN] Eerily calm, Qiao Wenxian flopped back onto his bed: "Let him lock us in. Eat when food comes, sleep when tired—Father can lock up my body, never my heart! I'll never give up Shuzhen—see what he can do about it! Think he'll lock me up till I die?"
[ZH] 南双笑道：“公子好心态，以后闲时间多，公子不如趁此机会教我下棋呗？”
[EN] Nan Shuang grinned: "Great attitude, Young Master! With all this free time, why not teach me chess?"
[ZH] “没问题！”乔文仙嘴上说得轻松，心里实在难过。
[EN] "Deal!" Qiao Wenxian sounded light, yet agony twisted his chest.
[ZH] 钱氏与米氏听说老爷把儿子绑回来，既高兴又担心，来到儿子居住的院子，在屋外劝说。
[EN] Hearing Master had hauled their son home in ropes, Madam Qian and Concubine Mi hurried to his courtyard, equal parts relieved and worried, calling through the hatch.
[ZH] 米氏通过方形孔，向内瞧着：“儿子，我与姐姐过来看你。”。
[EN] Peering through the square opening, Concubine Mi called, "Son, Sister and I came to see you."
[ZH] 南双听到声音，从里屋出来，透过方形孔对外说：“夫人，米姨娘，公子在里面躺着，你们快帮公子劝说劝说老爷，让他放公子出去。”
[EN] Hearing their voices, Nan Shuang stepped out from the inner chamber and spoke through the hatch: "Madam, Concubine Mi, Young Master is lying down inside—please persuade Master to let him out!"
[ZH] 钱氏批评道：“你这小子懂什么？去把公子叫来，我有话对他讲。”
[EN] Madam Qian scolded him: "What do you know, boy? Go fetch Young Master—I have words for him."
[ZH] 南双走进里屋：“公子，你也听到，夫人和姨娘要见您。”
[EN] Nan Shuang ducked back inside: "Young Master, Madam and Concubine Mi want to see you."
[ZH] 乔文仙起身，来到窗户跟前，想趁此机会说服她们帮忙劝说父亲。
[EN] Rising from bed, Qiao Wenxian walked to the window, hoping to recruit them to soften his father.
[ZH] 南双很贴心，端把椅子：“公子坐下说。”
[EN] Thoughtful as ever, Nan Shuang pulled over a chair: "Sit while you talk, Young Master."
[ZH] 乔文仙坐下后，透过方形孔，瞅到米氏的脸：“母亲，娘，你们来看我有何用？我已被父亲关起来，不得自由。”
[EN] Sitting down, Qiao Wenxian spotted Concubine Mi's face framed in the hatch: "Mother, Mama, what's the point of visiting? Father locked me up like a prisoner."
[ZH] 钱氏劝道：“文渊，听话，把朱家姑娘忘了吧，她不适合你。”
[EN] Madam Qian pleaded, "Be good, Wenyuan—forget Miss Zhu. She isn't right for you."
[ZH] “母亲不必劝我，我就是被关一辈子，也不会放弃淑真。”
[EN] "Save your words, Mother. Lock me up for life and I still won't give up Shuzhen."
[ZH] 米氏急了：“你怎么这么傻！你对她一片痴心，人家不一定这么想，外面把她传得一无是处，我昨日还听到有人说她跟东方家的公子不清不白……”
[EN] Concubine Mi fretted: "Why are you so stubborn?! You're giving her your whole heart, yet who knows if she feels the same! Her name outside is mud—yesterday I even heard folk claiming she and Young Master Dongfang carry on behind closed doors..."
[ZH] “胡说！淑真跟东方兄我最清楚不过！”
[EN] "Rubbish! Nobody knows Shuzhen and Brother Dongfang better than me!"
[ZH] “你怎么可能清楚？你又没日日与他们两个在一起？”钱氏批评道，“昨日，提举娘子邀请我到家中品茶，他家儿子也常参加你们的诗会，据说那诗会是东方公子组织的，几乎都是品鉴朱家姑娘的诗词，还听说东方公子甚是喜爱搜罗朱家姑娘的诗词，到处传播。你说说，若没私情，他会这么做？”
[EN] "How could you know? Were you glued to them day and night?" Madam Qian chimed in. "Yesterday Intendant An's wife invited me for tea—her son goes to your salons too. Word is Young Master Dongfang runs those salons just to praise Miss Zhu's verse and collects her poems to spread everywhere! Tell me, if there's nothing going on, why would he do all that?"
[ZH] 乔文仙觉得十分可笑，批判道：“欲加之罪，何患无辞！淑真与东方兄最清白不过，只因他喜欢淑真的诗词，大家就如此编排，世道人心简直是颠倒黑白，是非不分！”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian barked a bitter laugh: "Give a dog a bad name and hang him! Nothing could be cleaner than Shuzhen and Brother Dongfang—just because he admires her poetry, folk twist it into filth! This world can't tell black from white!"
[ZH] “儿啊。”米氏说，“我听说东方家公子也被关起来，东方老爷不让他以后再组织诗会，也不允许跟朱家姑娘往来。”
[EN] "Son," Concubine Mi added, "I heard Young Master Dongfang got locked up at home too—Master Dongfang forbade him from holding salons or seeing Miss Zhu ever again."
[ZH] 乔文仙慨叹道：“看来六人组的诗会再也组织不起来。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian let out a hollow sigh: "Looks like our six-person poetry circle is done for."
[ZH] 钱氏问：“文渊，你说什么？大声些，母亲听不到。”
[EN] Madam Qian asked, "What did you mutter, Wenyuan? Speak up—Mother can't hear."
[ZH] 乔文仙把声音放大：“我是说，你们有时间跟我白费口舌，不如去劝说父亲，别相信流言，好好到朱家提亲。”
[EN] Raising his voice, Qiao Wenxian called, "I said instead of wasting breath on me, go tell Father to ignore rumors and go propose to the Zhu family!"
[ZH] 乔文仙说完，起身回里屋，倒头便睡。
[EN] With that, he stood, walked back into the inner room, and collapsed onto bed.
[ZH] 钱氏与米氏呼唤多次，不见回应，只好离去，打算找乔守仁商议。
[EN] Calling over and over to dead silence, Madam Qian and Concubine Mi gave up and went off to confer with Qiao Shouren.
[ZH] 乔守仁正在院中亲自浇花，一副悠闲自在的样子，一点儿不担心。
[EN] Out in the courtyard, Qiao Shouren stood watering his flowers at a leisurely pace, calm as could be.
[ZH] 钱氏看到，将手老爷中的水瓢拿掉，交给一旁的下人：“有下人忙活，老爷何必亲自动手？赶紧想想文渊的事该怎么办？”
[EN] Madam Qian marched up, plucked the ladle from his hand, and passed it to a servant: "With servants all around, why water flowers yourself? Figure out what to do about Wenyuan!"
[ZH] 乔守仁又从下人手里拿过水瓢，从木桶里舀瓢水，继续浇花：“我养花是为修身养性，若都让他们替我浇水，还怎么修身养性？”
[EN] Taking the ladle right back and scooping more water from the wooden bucket, Qiao Shouren said, "Tending flowers calms the mind—let servants do it and where's the calm?"
[ZH] 钱氏说：“老爷修身养性我不阻拦，可文渊被关着也不是个事。”
[EN] Madam Qian protested, "Calm your mind all you like, yet keeping Wenyuan locked up solves nothing."
[ZH] 乔守仁一边浇花一边批评：“你们两个妇道人家总是沉不住气，把文渊关起来，在我们眼皮子底下，起码不用担心他再跟朱家姑娘往来。这人啊，只要不来往，感情自然渐渐会淡。”
[EN] Watering on, Qiao Shouren chided them: "You two women have zero patience. Locked under our roof, at least we know he can't sneak off to see Miss Zhu. Out of sight, out of mind—give it time and his infatuation will cool."
[ZH] 米氏说：“老爷的话有些道理，只怕文渊一时半会儿放不下。”
[EN] Concubine Mi worried: "Makes sense, Master, yet Wenyuan won't let go overnight."
[ZH] 乔守仁态度坚决：“他一个月放不下关他一个月，半年放不下关他半年，一年放不下关他一年！”
[EN] Qiao Shouren's jaw set hard: "If a month isn't enough, lock him up a month! Half a year isn't enough, lock him up half a year! A year isn't enough, lock him up a whole year!"
[ZH] 钱氏很担忧，不由流出泪来：“只怕到时候会把文渊给关傻，乔家可就他一个儿子。”
[EN] Tears sprang to Madam Qian's eyes: "What if locking him up drives him out of his mind?! He's the Qiao family's only son!"
[ZH] 米氏也应和道：“我的确听说过有人被长期关着，最后变成傻子的事。老爷，这可是真事，不是我瞎编。”
[EN] Concubine Mi nodded fast: "I've really heard of people going mad after being locked up too long! No lie, Master—it's true!"
[ZH] 两个妇道人家叨叨叨，乔守仁没心情再修身养性，把水瓢扔给下人，转身回屋。
[EN] With both women nagging his ears off, Qiao Shouren lost all mood for gardening, tossed the ladle to a servant, and headed indoors.
[ZH] 两个女人跟上，继续说：“老爷，不能就这么关着文渊。”
[EN] Both women trailed right after him: "Master, we can't just leave Wenyuan locked up!"
[ZH] 刚到屋门口，乔守仁站住，转过身：“放心，我不会把他关得太久，最多一个月，你们趁此机会尽快挑好姑娘，为文渊把亲事定了，只要亲事定下，他即使心里有别人又能如何？”
[EN] Halting at the doorway, Qiao Shouren turned around: "Relax—I won't keep him locked up long, a month at most. Use this time to pick a fine girl and nail down Wenyuan's betrothal fast! Once a marriage is set, what does it matter who sits in his heart?"
[ZH] 钱氏说：“老爷明智，我与妹妹这就去忙活。”
[EN] Madam Qian brightened: "Brilliant as ever, Master! Sister and I will get right on it!"
[ZH] 两个女人一前一后急匆匆离开，只盼亲事早些定下，文渊也好早得自由。
[EN] The two women hurried off one after the other, praying to settle a bride fast so Wenyuan could walk free again.

#CH 122
[ZH] 苦荞跟王爷坐在一辆马车里甚是不习惯，既拘谨，又不敢随便说话。
[EN] Sharing a carriage with a royal prince made Kuqiao stiff as a board, too tense to speak a word out of turn.
[ZH] 被一位王爷亲自护送，苦荞紧张拘谨之余，又倍感到荣幸，越发觉得仪王是个不错的人。
[EN] Yet being escorted home by a prince himself also filled her with pride—convincing her more than ever that Prince Yi was a fine man.
[ZH] 申简辰心里纠结千里寻，没有心情理会面前的丫鬟，这个丫鬟唯一的好处，是为他提供了一条靠近千里寻的正当理由。
[EN] Mind tangled around Qianli Xun, Shen Jianchen spared the maid no thought; her sole value right now was giving him a rock-solid excuse to approach Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 他欺骗梦容去看文渊，事实上却往望月轩而去。申简辰真正体会到，有的时候欺骗一个人实属无奈。
[EN] He had lied to Mengrong about going to see Wenyuan when he was actually heading straight to Wangyue Pavilion. Now he understood: sometimes lies came from sheer helplessness.
[ZH] 他努力想要忘记千里寻，全心全意接受梦容，一切只是徒劳。他也清楚他必须娶梦容，无法给千里寻任何承诺，还是不由自主想要靠近她，哪怕被她误解依然要靠近。
[EN] He had tried to forget Qianli Xun and give Mengrong his whole heart, and failed flat. Knowing full well he had to wed Mengrong and could promise Qianli Xun nothing, he still couldn't stop gravitating toward her—even if she hated him for it.
[ZH] 已近晌午，千里寻起床晚，腹中空空，不如给她买些吃的，申简辰想起聚香楼。
[EN] Noon was approaching; waking late, Qianli Xun had to be starving. Better bring her food—Juxiang Tower came straight to mind.
[ZH] “白风，先把车赶到聚香楼。”
[EN] "Bai Feng, swing by Juxiang Tower first."
[ZH] “好嘞！”听到聚香楼的名字，白风无不高兴，以为王爷要带他与苦荞去撮一顿。
[EN] "You got it!" At the name Juxiang Tower, Bai Feng beamed, assuming His Highness meant to treat him and Kuqiao to lunch.
[ZH] 结果到达聚香楼后，王爷并未挑桌子坐下，点完菜，说要带走，白风感到失望。
[EN] When His Highness didn't take a table and ordered everything packed to go instead, Bai Feng's face fell.
[ZH] 申简辰发觉白风的表情，笑道：“把我送到望月轩，给你银子，你带苦荞再来聚香楼吃一顿。”
[EN] Catching his droop, Shen Jianchen chuckled: "Drop me at Wangyue Pavilion, take some silver, and bring Kuqiao right back to Juxiang Tower for a feast."
[ZH] 白风立刻喜笑颜开：“我家公子就是贴心。”
[EN] Bai Feng lit up like a lantern: "Young Master thinks of everything!"
[ZH] 苦荞更觉仪王人好，像仪王这种身份尊贵之人竟然一点儿架子都没有，实属罕见。
[EN] Kuqiao grew even fonder of Prince Yi—a man of such lofty rank without an ounce of arrogance was rare indeed.
[ZH] 聚香楼的饭菜做得又快又好，伙计将饭菜放在食盒里，亲自送上马车，三人继续向望月轩而去。
[EN] Juxiang Tower's kitchen moved fast; a waiter packed the steaming dishes into food carriers, loaded them onto the carriage, and the three rolled on toward Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 幽林别居的马车将千里寻送到望月轩，她谢过车夫之后，带着沉重的心情往闺房走去。夏果跟在身后，心情并不比小姐好多少。
[EN] Dropped off at Wangyue Pavilion by the Youlin Retreat carriage, Qianli Xun thanked the driver and walked toward her bedchamber with heavy steps, Xiaguo trailing behind in just as grim a mood.
[ZH] 小姐一进闺房，将门关上，连夏果都被关在门外。夏果只好回丫鬟房，默默舔舐个人的痛苦。不过也有庆幸之事，起码乔公子已知她的心意。
[EN] Shutting her bedchamber door behind her, Qianli Xun left even Xiaguo outside. Xiaguo retreated to the maids' quarters to nurse her own bruise—though one bright spark remained: at least Young Master Qiao knew her feelings now.
[ZH] 千里寻并未躺在床上，而是坐在书桌前发呆。心情越沉重，脑子越乱，她努力在乱如麻的思绪中探寻着。
[EN] Instead of collapsing onto bed, Qianli Xun sat at her desk staring into space. The heavier her chest felt, the wilder her thoughts tangled as she searched for a thread through the maze.
[ZH] 由于穿越而来内心孤单，还有一段不属于她的姻缘，使得她想要拥有属于自己的爱情。遇到文渊，她终于找到，甚至想过穿越而来就是为了碰到文渊，现实却给她狠狠一击。
[EN] Crossing over alone and stuck in a loveless marriage had left her hungry for real love. Meeting Wenyuan, she had thought she found it—even wondered if crossing over had been fated just to bring her to him—only for reality to slam a fist in her face.
[ZH] 该怎么办？千里寻在内心寻找答案。无非是两种结果，要么放弃，要么坚守。就目前而言，她还未找到任何放弃的理由，关键她昨日才答应文渊不放弃，他们说过的话还萦绕在耳边。
[EN] What now? Only two roads lay open: give up, or hold on. So far she had no reason to give up—especially after promising Wenyuan just yesterday not to quit, their vows still ringing in her ears.
[ZH] 千里寻发现，当人生遇到难题时，谁也帮不了自己，只有自己能够帮助自己走出困境。她又想起“正思正念”四字。
[EN] When life hit a wall, nobody could save you but yourself. Those four words floated back: "Right Thought, Right Mindfulness."
[ZH] 放弃是对？还是坚守是对？她发现很多时候很多事极难用对错评判，或许是因为她还未走出爱情这片森林，有两句诗：“不识庐山真面目，只缘身在此山中。”说的正是此时此境中的她。
[EN] Was quitting right, or holding on? Too many things in life refused neat labels of right and wrong—maybe because she stood too deep inside the forest of love. Two lines of verse came to mind: "Why can't I tell the true shape of Lu-shan? Because I myself am in the mountain" [TN: Translated by Burton Watson]. That described her right this second.
[ZH] 净光法师说过，要成为朱淑真还是千里寻，是由行为决定，而行为又是由观念决定。朱晞颜焚烧书稿，让千里寻意识到她已用千里寻的观念和行为推动命运走上属于千里寻该走的道路。
[EN] Master Jingguang had said whether she became Zhu Shuzhen or Qianli Xun came down to actions, and actions sprang from mindset. Zhu Xiyan burning her manuscripts proved Qianli Xun's mindset and choices had already steered her onto Qianli Xun's own path.
[ZH] 难道与文渊感情的磨难是穿越而来的千里寻必然要经历的？看来是这样！既如此，那就勇敢面对，努力解决。
[EN] Then maybe this trial with Wenyuan was a hurdle Qianli Xun had to cross. If so, she would face it head-on.
[ZH] 千里寻面对感情向来理智，穿越即便是申简辰的强势猛攻，她从未被击败。穿越而来遇到文渊，竟然迷失自我，一味沉浸在感情中，现实的阻碍给她了一个大大的教训。
[EN] Before crossing over, she had always kept a cool head in romance—even Shen Jianchen's relentless pursuit had never rattled her. Crossing over and meeting Wenyuan had made her lose her footing in pure emotion, and reality had just dealt her a hard lesson.
[ZH] 她决定既要对得起这份感情，又要重拾理性：“我等文渊一年，若一年后乔家还是如此态度，我便……便放弃。”
[EN] She made up her mind to honor her heart while taking back her reason: "I'll wait one year for Wenyuan. If the Qiao family still won't budge after a year, I'll... let go."
[ZH] “姑娘，我听说你回来，可否吃过午饭？”外面传来韦嬷嬷的声音。
[EN] "Young Lady, I heard you got back—have you eaten lunch?" Matron Wei's voice sounded outside.
[ZH] 千里寻起身，把门打开，又坐回书桌前。
[EN] Qianli Xun rose, opened the door, and returned to her desk.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷走进后，四下瞧瞧：“怎么没见两个丫鬟？真是越来越不像话！”
[EN] Stepping in, Matron Wei scanned the empty room: "Where are those two maids? Getting lazier by the day!"
[ZH] “苦荞还未从幽林别居回来，我让夏果回丫鬟房了，想静一静，不想被人打扰。”
[EN] "Kuqiao hasn't returned from Youlin Retreat yet, and I sent Xiaguo to the maids' quarters—wanted some quiet."
[ZH] “姑娘可在幽林别居用过饭？”
[EN] "Did you eat lunch at the Qiao retreat?"
[ZH] “没有，我不饿。”
[EN] "No. Not hungry."
[ZH] “那怎么行？本以为姑娘在乔家别居用过饭才回来，厨房就没给姑娘做，我现在去安排。”
[EN] "That won't do! Assuming you ate there, the kitchen didn't cook for you—I'll go have them whip something up right now."
[ZH] “嬷嬷！不必，您坐下陪我说会儿话，您的话比饭食更滋养人。”
[EN] "No need, Matron. Sit and talk with me awhile—your words nourish me better than food."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷笑道：“姑娘不怕我打扰你？”
[EN] Matron Wei smiled: "Not afraid I'll disturb your quiet?"
[ZH] “嬷嬷见多识广，见解通透，跟您聊聊天儿，我心里会更敞亮。”
[EN] "You've seen the world and see straight through things, Matron—talking with you clears the fog in my head."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷在椅子上坐下：“姑娘可是又遇到烦心事？”
[EN] Taking a chair, Matron Wei asked, "More trouble weighing on you, Young Lady?"
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“我去乔家别居的事不知怎么被乔家老爷知道，一早带人将文渊绑回去，估计不可能再放他出来与我相见。”
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded. "Word of me staying at the retreat somehow reached Master Qiao. He showed up at dawn with men, tied Wenyuan up, and hauled him back—doubt they'll let him out to see me again."
[ZH] “姑娘，我说句你不爱听的话，你可别往心里去，昨晚不该留宿在乔家别居，会让乔家长辈更瞧不起你。”
[EN] "Forgive an old woman speaking blunt, Young Lady, and don't take offense: sleeping overnight at the Qiao retreat last night was a mistake. It only gave the Qiao elders more reason to look down on you."
[ZH] “昨日我并未多想，只因佳和郡主盛情邀请，又想与她说说心里话，便留下来，并未有逾越之举。”
[EN] "I didn't overthink it—Princess Jiahe pressed me so warm to stay and talk heart-to-heart, and nothing improper happened."
[ZH] “姑娘是什么样的人我自然清楚，可别人不这样想，尤其是乔家人。既然事情已经发生，也没必要纠结，姑娘往后注意便是。”
[EN] "Of course I know your character, Young Lady, yet outsiders—above all the Qiaos—won't see it that way. What's done is done; no use stewing, just be more careful going forward."
[ZH] “嬷嬷提醒得对，以后我定会谨言慎行。”
[EN] "Right you are, Matron. I'll watch every word and step from now on."
[ZH] “乔家是皇亲国戚，乔公子又是年轻一辈中的翘楚，临安许多大家闺秀都想嫁到乔家。姑娘经历此事，我也把乔家看透，乔家门楣高，可见识浅，风言风语就把他们给唬住，却不知我家姑娘是块美玉。哼！我看乔家不嫁也罢，定有更好的人家等着姑娘。”
[EN] "The Qiaos are imperial relatives, and Young Master Qiao is the cream of Lin'an's youth—half the noble daughters in town dream of marrying in. Still, this mess showed me the Qiaos' true size: lofty gate, shallow sight, spooked by street chatter and blind to the rare jade right before their eyes! Hmph—better off not marrying into that house! A finer match awaits you."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的话说得千里寻嘴角出现一丝笑容：“嬷嬷是自己人，才会认为我好，别人可不这么认为。”
[EN] Matron Wei's fierce loyalty brought a faint smile to Qianli Xun's lips: "You're family, Matron—of course you think I'm wonderful. Outsiders don't."
[ZH] “市井小民的议论，姑娘不必当回事，他们也是人云亦云，道途听说，谁又对姑娘了解多少？大家只图茶余饭后说个乐子而已，说多了自然无趣，又会去寻找其他乐子，到时谣言自然被抛之脑后。”
[EN] "Pay street folk's chatter no mind! They just parrot hearsay without knowing a thing about you—chasing gossip after dinner until they get bored and hunt a new thrill, tossing old rumors straight into the dust."
[ZH] “嬷嬷几句话真是拨云见日，令我内心无比敞亮。我决定老老实实待在望月轩，静待谣言消失之日。”
[EN] "Listening to you clears the clouds right out of my sky, Matron! I'll stay put inside Wangyue Pavilion until the gossip dies out."
[ZH] “要我说，没必要在乎别人，想出去就出去。不过我还是要批评批评姑娘，以后别把什么都往诗词里写。”
[EN] "If you ask me, don't let folk box you in—go out when you feel like it! One lecture I will give you, though: stop putting every private thing into poetry."
[ZH] “嬷嬷批评得对，以后该写的写，不该写的一个字都不写。”
[EN] "Fair criticism, Matron. From now on, what should be written gets written, and what shouldn't won't see a drop of ink."
[ZH] “这就对了。”
[EN] "Now you're talking."
[ZH] 千里寻还想听听嬷嬷的意见，大胆说：“嬷嬷，您说乔家如此态度，我与文渊该如何是好？”
[EN] Eager to hear Matron Wei's take, Qianli Xun asked, "With the Qiao family acting like this, Matron, what should Wenyuan and I do?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷想想说：“我相信乔公子对姑娘一片真心，姑娘对乔公子也是真心一片，可惜，光有真心没用，这世道谁还会看重真心？既然生在这样的世道，要学会适应。”
[EN] Matron Wei thought a beat: "I don't doubt Young Master Qiao loves you with all his heart, or that you love him right back. Pity—true love alone counts for nothing in this world. Born into this age, you have to learn to bend with it."
[ZH] “难道我要放下一片真心？”千里寻又忍不住露出伤感的表情。
[EN] "So I should give up on true love?" Shadow returned to Qianli Xun's face.
[ZH] “不然呢？姑娘想用自己的真心与这个世道硬碰硬不成？只能是以卵击石。”
[EN] "What else? Trying to slam your bare heart against the whole world is like an egg striking stone."
[ZH] 千里寻黯然伤神，说不出话来。
[EN] Grief silenced Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷继续劝解：“姑娘既难以辜负一片真心，不如给自己一个期限，若在期限内无法实现，果断放弃，以后想起来也不会后悔。”
[EN] Matron Wei softened her voice: "Since you can't bear throwing away a true heart, set yourself a deadline. If nothing changes by then, walk away clean—that way you'll never look back in regret."
[ZH] 千里寻这才说：“嬷嬷与我想到一处去，我给自己一年期限。”
[EN] Only then did Qianli Xun say, "Great minds think alike, Matron—I just gave myself a one-year deadline."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷高兴道：“刚说我家姑娘是块美玉，果然没错，心中早已有主意，这么好的姑娘乔家看不上是他们的损失，以后等着后悔吧。不过，我觉得一年时间太长，不如半年吧？”
[EN] Matron Wei beamed: "Just called you a rare jade, and sure enough you already have a head on your shoulders! The Qiaos are fools to miss out—they'll live to regret it! Still, a year drags too long. How about half a year?"
[ZH] 千里寻沉默一会儿，果断说：“好，半年就半年。”
[EN] After a quiet beat, Qianli Xun made up her mind: "Deal. Half a year."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷起身：“姑娘歇着，我去厨房给你安排午饭，不管想不想吃，多少还是要吃些。”
[EN] Matron Wei stood up: "Rest a minute while I have the kitchen make lunch—hungry or not, you need food in your stomach."
[ZH] 刚要出去，苦荞走进来：“小姐，嬷嬷，仪王送我回来，顺便在聚香楼给小姐买了饭菜，还热着呢，人正在外面候着。”
[EN] Just as she turned for the door, Kuqiao stepped in: "Miss, Matron—Prince Yi escorted me back and even bought hot dishes from Juxiang Tower! He's waiting outside right now."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷甚是纳闷，惊讶道：“仪王送你一个丫鬟回来？我该不会是听错？”
[EN] Matron Wei blinked in astonishment: "Prince Yi escorted a maid home?! Did my ears deceive me?"
[ZH] “嬷嬷没听错，确实如此，仪王本来要去看乔公子，见小姐先走，我又没马车可乘，才好心送我一程，又说在幽林别居没招待小姐吃午饭，便买了饭食来。”
[EN] "Not at all, Matron! Prince Yi was heading to see Young Master Qiao anyway, saw Miss had left without me and I had no carriage, so he kindly gave me a lift—and said since Youlin Retreat hadn't served Miss lunch, he brought dishes over."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷叹道：“仪王倒是心好，算起来我们搬到望月轩，朱府的人还没见过来，仪王倒是来过两回。”
[EN] Matron Wei mused aloud: "Prince Yi sure has a warm heart. Since we moved to Wangyue Pavilion, nobody from Zhu Manor has visited once, yet Prince Yi has called twice."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷眼睛毒辣，千里寻担心嬷嬷看出什么来，拒绝道：“苦荞，你告诉仪王，我不舒服，已躺下，让他赶紧走。”
[EN] Knowing Matron Wei's razor-sharp eyes, Qianli Xun feared she might catch on and snapped, "Kuqiao, go tell Prince Yi I'm under the weather and already in bed—send him off."
[ZH] 苦荞说：“仪王早已猜到小姐会这么说，他让我回小姐，如果想知道乔公子的情况，最好让他进来。”
[EN] Kuqiao said, "Prince Yi guessed you'd say that! He told me to tell Miss: if you want news of Young Master Qiao, you'd better let him in."
[ZH] 千里寻心里骂道：“真无耻！刚刚被梦容搂抱过，又想来乘虚而入！”
[EN] Qianli Xun fumed inside: "Shameless snake! Fresh out of Mengrong's hug and already swooping in while I'm down!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷劝道：“姑娘，他是仪王，谁敢拒绝一位王爷登门拜访？还是请进来吧，既然仪王买了饭食，省得我再去麻烦厨房安排。”
[EN] Matron Wei urged, "Young Lady, he's Prince Yi—who dares slam the door on a royal prince? Invite him in! And since he brought lunch, it saves me bothering the kitchen."
[ZH] 千里寻想得到文渊的消息，现在能帮她的人只有仪王。她不得已起身，带韦嬷嬷和苦荞一起到门口迎接不愿见，又不得不见的人。
[EN] Needing news of Wenyuan, Prince Yi was her only lifeline right now. Rising with heavy reluctance, Qianli Xun led Matron Wei and Kuqiao out to welcome the man she least wanted—yet had—to see.

#CH 123
[ZH] 千里寻带领众人来到望月轩门口，仪王与白风站在那里，白风手里提着两个食盒。
[EN] Leading the household out to Wangyue Pavilion's gate, Qianli Xun found Prince Yi waiting with Bai Feng, who held two food carriers.
[ZH] 千里寻上前拜道：“感谢仪王亲自将苦荞送回。”
[EN] Stepping forward, Qianli Xun bowed: "Thank you for escorting Kuqiao home yourself, Prince Yi."
[ZH] 曾提醒过她见自己不必行礼，她也答应，可千里寻每次见他依旧依礼而行，只怪仪王的身份太过尊贵。
[EN] He had told her time and again to skip bowing, and she had agreed—yet every time they met she bowed all the same, unable to forget his lofty rank.
[ZH] 申简辰本想再次说出不让她行礼的话，见千里寻平静的面色中略显严肃，便知她想拒他于千里之外，只好把想说的话咽回去。
[EN] On the tip of his tongue to tell her again not to bow, Shen Jianchen saw the cool reserve on her face—clear notice she meant to hold him at arm's length—and swallowed his words.
[ZH] “幽栖居士若想谢我，不如请我在望月轩用午饭，饭食我已带来。”
[EN] "If Youqi Jushi wants to thank me, invite me to share lunch at Wangyue Pavilion. I brought the food."
[ZH] 千里寻正犹豫要不要让仪王进入，韦嬷嬷怕得罪贵人，赶紧说：“仪王光临望月轩，实乃荣幸，我替姑娘做主，您请！”
[EN] Before Qianli Xun could waver, Matron Wei spoke fast lest they offend royalty: "Having Prince Yi honor Wangyue Pavilion is our privilege! Forgive me for speaking for my mistress—please come right in!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷既然应下，千里寻不好再说什么，她侧身站向一旁，为仪王让开道。
[EN] With Matron Wei extending the invite, Qianli Xun could not object and stepped aside to let Prince Yi pass.
[ZH] 申简辰自然开心，迈开步子走进去，白风手提食盒紧跟上。
[EN] Delighted, Shen Jianchen strode inside, Bai Feng trailing with the food carriers.
[ZH] 进入用饭厅，苦荞与白风将饭菜摆好，都是聚香楼的硬菜。
[EN] In the dining hall, Kuqiao and Bai Feng set out dish after dish of Juxiang Tower's finest specialties.
[ZH] 申简辰不请自坐：“这么多菜，我一人吃不完，幽栖居士不如陪我一起吃？”
[EN] Helping himself to a seat, Shen Jianchen said, "Too much food for one man—won't Youqi Jushi join me?"
[ZH] 千里寻拒绝道：“我没胃口，也有些累，想去休息，还是让苦荞和白风在这里陪仪王，或者嬷嬷也行。”
[EN] Qianli Xun turned him down flat: "No appetite, and I'm tired—going to rest. Let Kuqiao and Bai Feng keep Prince Yi company, or Matron Wei."
[ZH] “可惜，我已答应让白风带苦荞去聚香楼吃饭，银子都给了。至于嬷嬷，她应该有更重要的事情做，是不是？嬷嬷？”申简辰为他找的理由而得意。
[EN] "Too bad—I already gave Bai Feng silver to take Kuqiao to Juxiang Tower for lunch. As for Matron Wei, I'm sure she has more pressing tasks, don't you, Matron?" Shen Jianchen felt pretty smug about his airtight setup.
[ZH] 仪王的面子还是要给，韦嬷嬷说：“仪王、姑娘，你们用饭，我还有事，先退下。”
[EN] Unable to cross a prince, Matron Wei bowed: "Enjoy your meal, Prince Yi, Young Lady. I have chores to attend to and take my leave."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷因仪王的身份不得已退出，千里寻不好阻拦。还好，望月轩又不是只有苦荞一个丫鬟。
[EN] Forced to let Matron Wei withdraw, Qianli Xun still wasn't out of options—Kuqiao wasn't the only maid in Wangyue Pavilion!
[ZH] “仪王若嫌一人用饭寂寞，我差人把夏果找来，再多找几个丫鬟伺候您……”
[EN] "If Prince Yi hates dining alone, I'll summon Xiaguo and a few more maids to wait on you..."
[ZH] “不必，我与幽栖居士有话要说，不想其他人在此打扰。白风，还不赶紧带苦荞去聚香楼！”
[EN] "No need. I have words for Youqi Jushi and don't want onlookers. Bai Feng, take Kuqiao to Juxiang Tower right now!"
[ZH] 听到仪王命令，白风拉起苦荞迅速离开。
[EN] At the prince's order, Bai Feng grabbed Kuqiao's hand and bolted.
[ZH] 千里寻在心中埋怨苦荞：“这么快被人收买，等你回来，看我怎么批评你！”
[EN] Qianli Xun grumbled at Kuqiao in her mind: "Bought off that easy?! Wait till you get back—I'll give you an earful!"
[ZH] 周围已没其他人，申简辰很满意，他站起来，将桌边的一把椅子往外拉出一些：“幽栖居士，请坐。”
[EN] Alone at last, Shen Jianchen smiled, stood up, and pulled out a chair beside the table: "Have a seat, Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 仪王的动作，千里寻觉得熟悉。在另一个时代，与申简辰一起出去吃饭，他每次都会帮她把座椅拉出一些，为何仪王也会如此？难道……
[EN] That gesture hit Qianli Xun with a flash of deja vu. Back in her own era, whenever she ate out with Shen Jianchen, he pulled her chair out like that every single time. Why would Prince Yi do the exact same thing? Could...
[ZH] 千里寻露出疑惑和期待的表情，申简辰猜到她定是想起穿越前的事。
[EN] Catching the flicker of wonder and hope in her eyes, Shen Jianchen knew she had just remembered their past.
[ZH] 本想找机会告诉她，他就是申简辰，几次以失败告终，便不想再说。说了有何用？又不能摆脱与梦容的婚约。
[EN] He had tried telling her he was Shen Jianchen more than once and failed every time; now he didn't even want to say it. What good would it do when his betrothal to Mengrong still chained him down?
[ZH] 只要婚约还在，他是赵朴还是申简辰有什么区别？不如借着赵朴的身份与她接近，她拒绝嫌弃的也是赵朴，而非申简辰。
[EN] As long as that betrothal stood, what difference did it make whether he was Zhao Pu or Shen Jianchen? Better to approach her as Zhao Pu—that way, every rejection fell on Zhao Pu, not Shen Jianchen.
[ZH] “想什么呢？还不赶紧坐下。”
[EN] "Daydreaming? Sit down already."
[ZH] 挪出的椅子，千里寻有种想要坐上去的欲望，她遵循欲望，乖乖坐下：“仪王为何要帮我把椅子挪出来？您可是身份尊贵的王爷，无论走到哪里都有人伺候，什么时候学会做这种事？”
[EN] Drawn toward the pulled-out chair by an odd pull, Qianli Xun sat down: "Why did Prince Yi pull my chair out? A royal prince waited on everywhere he goes—where did you pick up a habit like that?"
[ZH] “这么简单的动作还用学吗？我又不是傻子。”申简辰坐回位置，“我是身份最贵的王爷，可也是一位没有架子，又亲民的王爷，为你挪挪椅子没什么，我也经常帮梦容挪椅子，可能是习惯了。”
[EN] "Does a tiny gesture like that need lessons? I'm not an idiot." Sitting back down, Shen Jianchen shrugged: "High-ranking prince I may be, yet I'm down-to-earth with zero airs. Pulling out a chair is nothing—I do it for Mengrong all the time. Force of habit."
[ZH] “哦，原来如此。”千里寻变得清醒，他怎么可能是申简辰？他就是个不折不扣的花心王爷。
[EN] "Oh, I see." Clarity snapped back into Qianli Xun's mind. How could he ever be Shen Jianchen? Just a textbook playboy prince.
[ZH] 本来因伤心没有胃口，坐到桌旁，靠美食近些，食物屡屡香气扑鼻而来，颜色更是诱人，竟然把本已消失的食欲又刺激回来。
[EN] She had thought heartache had killed her appetite, yet sitting up close to the fragrant, gleaming dishes woke her stomach right back up.
[ZH] 千里寻拿起筷子夹一口菜，品尝后甚是满意：“仪王很会点菜，色香味俱全。”千里寻大吃起来，暂时把痛苦搁置一旁。
[EN] Lifting her chopsticks, she tasted a bite and nodded: "Prince Yi sure knows how to order—looks, smells, and tastes great." Digging in with gusto, she pushed her grief aside for a spell.
[ZH] 申简辰认真欣赏，她的吃相一点儿没变，与大家闺秀的身份实在不搭，可申简辰喜欢。他最欣赏不来扭扭捏捏，故作柔弱，吃饭时还要注意形象的女子。
[EN] Shen Jianchen watched in quiet pleasure. Her hearty appetite hadn't changed one bit—hardly ladylike, yet he adored it. He couldn't stand women who nibbled like sparrows and fussed over appearances at the dinner table.
[ZH] “仪王不吃吗？”
[EN] "Aren't you eating, Prince Yi?"
[ZH] “当然吃，不能辜负美食。”申简辰也拿起筷子吃起来。
[EN] "Of course—can't waste fine food." Picking up his chopsticks, he joined in.
[ZH] 两人专注享用美食，并未聊天儿，申简辰很享受只有他与千里寻在一起的安静感。
[EN] Neither spoke as they ate, Shen Jianchen drinking in the rare peace of sharing a quiet table with Qianli Xun alone.
[ZH] 吃着吃着，千里寻用余光瞧到仪王一脸享受的表情，心里甚是不满，放下筷子：“仪王，我已吃饱，您慢慢享用。”
[EN] Mid-bite, Qianli Xun caught Prince Yi watching her with a look of sheer bliss. Annoyance flared, and she set her chopsticks down: "I'm full, Prince Yi. Enjoy the rest at your leisure."
[ZH] “我有话对你说，说完你再离开。”
[EN] "Stay until I say my piece."
[ZH] “什么话？”
[EN] "What piece?"
[ZH] “以后我会帮你和文渊传话。”申简辰之所以愿意这么做，不过是想找一个借口多见见千里寻。
[EN] "From now on, I'll pass messages between you and Wenyuan." All Shen Jianchen really wanted was a steady excuse to keep seeing her.
[ZH] 千里寻感激的同时，也充满怀疑：“仪王为何要帮我？”
[EN] Torn between gratitude and suspicion, Qianli Xun asked, "Why is Prince Yi helping me?"
[ZH] “以前你求我帮忙时，也没问过原因，为何这次要问？”申简辰放下筷子，心爱的女子不吃，他只顾一个人吃，显得太不将她当回事。
[EN] "You never asked why back when you begged favors from me—why ask now?" Shen Jianchen set his chopsticks down too; eating alone while she sat there felt rude.
[ZH] “不一样，我求仪王时，满心只希望仪王答应，根本不管什么原因。而我现在又未求仪王，您却答应我，定是有所要求，只怕我无法满足仪王。”
[EN] "That was different. When I asked for help, I only cared if you'd say yes. Now I didn't ask, yet you're volunteering—which means you want something in return, and I'm afraid I can't give it."
[ZH] “我是有所求，只想幽栖居士知道，我心中深爱着一个女子，从第一次见时就爱上她，我不奢求能与她在一起，只需她明白有一个人默默地爱着她，哪怕她偶尔会在心里想起我，我也心满意足。”
[EN] "I do want something: I just want Youqi Jushi to know I love one woman with all my heart—loved her the very first time I saw her. I don't ask to possess her; just knowing she knows someone loves her in silence—and maybe thinks of me once in a blue moon—is enough."
[ZH] 千里寻沉默半晌，冷笑道：“哼！仪王心里到底有多少女子？恐怕是见一个爱一个吧？”
[EN] After a cold pause, Qianli Xun let out a sharp laugh: "Hmph! Just how many women sit in Prince Yi's heart? Fall in love with every single one you meet?"
[ZH] “随你怎么想，我心中从来只有一人，她就是千里寻……”
[EN] "Believe what you want. Only one woman has ever held my heart, and her name is Qianli Xun..."
[ZH] 仪王的表白在千里寻看来实在可笑：“哼！仪王不是答应要替我保密吗？为何动不动会提起这个名字？小心我把你看成不守承诺之人。”
[EN] His declaration sounded absurd to Qianli Xun: "Hmph! Didn't Prince Yi swear to guard my secret? Why keep throwing that name around at every turn? Watch out I don't brand you a liar!"
[ZH] “我会替你保密，之所以提起，除了因这里只有你我二人外，还因‘千里寻’这个名字是我们之间的秘密，只属于你和我，而幽栖居士、朱淑真之类的称谓人人可称得……”
[EN] "I will guard your secret. I only say it because nobody else is here—and because 'Qianli Xun' is a secret belonging to just the two of us, whereas anyone on the street can call you Youqi Jushi or Zhu Shuzhen..."
[ZH] 千里寻继续笑道：“呵呵……，令你失望，苦荞和苦蕨早知道，我曾在林家长辈面前也提到我是千里寻，只不过林家长辈不相信而已……”
[EN] Qianli Xun smirked back: "Haha, hate to burst your bubble—Kuqiao and Kujue already know! I even told the Lin elders I was Qianli Xun; they just didn't believe me..."
[ZH] “你又何必如此噎我？我一片真心，为何总换不来你一丝感动？”
[EN] "Why do you always shoot me down like that? Why can't all my sincerity move you even an inch?"
[ZH] “我劝仪王不要白费心思，还是老老实实对待佳和郡主，我做人有底线，什么该做，什么不该做，心里清清楚楚。即使我与文渊分开，也不可能为你所谓的真心感动，你的真心在我看来一文不值。”
[EN] "Save your breath, Prince Yi, and treat Princess Jiahe right. I have lines I don't cross, and I know right from wrong clear as day. Even if Wenyuan and I part ways, your so-called sincerity will never move me—to me, your sincerity isn't worth a single copper coin."
[ZH] 千里寻的话又一次戳进申简辰的心里：“一文不值？哼，竟然一文不值？”
[EN] Her words drove a knife straight into Shen Jianchen's chest: "Not worth a copper coin? Hmph... not even one copper coin?"
[ZH] 千里寻站起来：“仪王以后不要再对我说同样的话，我不喜欢听，佳和郡主视我为闺蜜，她若听到您今日所说，会作何感想？您还是回去好好想想如何偿还佳和郡主在您病着时，悉心照料床前的恩情，不要病着时需要她，才说些甜言蜜语哄她，病一好，就变成花心大萝卜。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stood up: "Never say things like that to me again, Prince Yi—I hate hearing them. Princess Jiahe treats me like her closest friend; how would she feel if she heard you today? Go home and think about repaying her devotion when she nursed you at your sickbed! Don't whisper sweet nothings when you need her, then turn into a total playboy the second you recover!"
[ZH] 千里寻说完，要往出走，又被仪王叫住。
[EN] Spinning on her heel, she started out—only for Prince Yi to call after her.
[ZH] “你等等！”
[EN] "Hold on!"
[ZH] 千里寻站住回过头：“仪王还有什么吩咐？”
[EN] Stopping to glance back: "What else does Prince Yi want?"
[ZH] 申简辰走到千里寻身边：“幽栖居士放心，我会偿还梦容的恩情，也会娶她……”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stepped right up to her: "Rest easy, Youqi Jushi—I will repay Mengrong's kindness, and I will marry her..."
[ZH] 千里寻很不屑：“那就结了，还来招惹我干吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun scoffed: "Then that settles it! Why keep pestering me?!"
[ZH] “幽栖居士难道没听过情不自已吗？”
[EN] "Has Youqi Jushi never heard of a heart that cannot help itself?"
[ZH] “听过又怎样？我不想再听到仪王对我说这些话，希望您以后不要再来望月轩。”
[EN] "Heard it—so what? I don't want to hear another word like this from Prince Yi. Please don't come to Wangyue Pavilion again."
[ZH] 千里寻已顾不上仪王的身份，将他甩在屋内，自顾离开。
[EN] Throwing royal etiquette to the wind, Qianli Xun left him standing there and marched out.
[ZH] 申简辰对外大声说：“放心，以后我不会来望月轩，会让白风把文渊的消息传给你。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen called after her into the empty room: "Rest easy—I won't come to Wangyue Pavilion again! I'll send Bai Feng with Wenyuan's news!"
[ZH] 没有佳人在侧，一桌子美食申简辰提不起一点儿兴致，他叹口气走出屋外，很不情愿地朝望月轩的门口走去。
[EN] With her gone, the whole table of delicacies turned tasteless as sawdust. Letting out a heavy sigh, he walked out with dragging feet toward the front gate.
[ZH] 半路上碰到韦嬷嬷，嬷嬷上前拜见：“仪王可吃好？”
[EN] Halfway across the yard, he met Matron Wei, who stepped up with a bow: "Did Prince Yi enjoy his meal?"
[ZH] “还行。”申简辰觉得需叮嘱几句，“你家姑娘的脾气得改改，多亏我是个随和又明事理的王爷，否则，就她对我那态度，被罚跪多久都不为过。”
[EN] "Passable." Feeling a nudge to speak, Shen Jianchen said, "Your mistress needs to work on that temper of hers. Lucky for her I'm an easygoing, reasonable prince—pull that attitude on anyone else and she'd be kneeling in punishment for hours."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷奉承道：“临安人都知道，仪王是位心有百姓，最没架子的好王爷，我等都希望像您这样的王爷多一些。我家姑娘脾气有时是坏了些，还望仪王担待。怪只怪她最近心情不好，满城风言风语不说，乔家又出那档子事，搁谁都会有情绪。”
[EN] Matron Wei flattered smooth: "Everyone in Lin'an knows Prince Yi cares for the common folk and has zero airs—we all pray for more princes like you! Our Young Lady's temper does flare now and then; please bear with her. Between the whole city's gossip and the Qiao family's mess, anyone would be on edge."
[ZH] “算了，我不与她计较。”
[EN] "Forget it—I won't hold it against her."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷继续说：“我在这里是专程等仪王。”
[EN] Matron Wei paused, then added, "Actually, I waited here on purpose to catch Prince Yi."
[ZH] “哦，嬷嬷有话对我讲？”
[EN] "Oh? You have words for me, Matron?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷又拜一下：“近期满城都是关于姑娘的风言风语，把她说得一无是处，还望仪王考虑到我家姑娘的声誉，今后不要再来望月轩……”
[EN] Matron Wei bowed low once more: "With ugly rumors about our Young Lady still fresh across Lin'an, for the sake of her reputation, I beg Prince Yi not to visit Wangyue Pavilion again..."
[ZH] 再次被叮嘱，还是韦嬷嬷充满礼貌地叮嘱，申简辰感到落寞：“好吧，今后我不会再来望月轩。”
[EN] Warned away a second time—polite as Matron Wei's tone was, cold loneliness washed over Shen Jianchen: "Very well. I won't come to Wangyue Pavilion again."
[ZH] 申简辰既坚定又不舍地向外走去……
[EN] Turning with firm yet reluctant steps, he walked out toward the gate...
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷面向仪王的背影，再次行礼：“感谢仪王！”
[EN] Matron Wei bowed once more to his retreating back: "My thanks to Prince Yi!"

#CH 124
[ZH] 中秋之日，富贵人家都在装饰家中亭台楼阁，临安的各大酒楼早已被预订完，只待中秋晚上饮酒玩月。
[EN] On Mid-Autumn Festival, wealthy households decked out their pavilions and towers, and every major tavern in Lin'an had been booked full long ago for evening drinking and moon-viewing.
[ZH] 中秋之夜，走在大街上，时时能听到丝簧之声从各大酒楼传出；夜间的街市人头攒动，花灯绚烂；每家每户也充满笙竽之音；儿童在小巷内嬉戏，你追我赶，好不热闹。
[EN] Once night fell, music of strings and reed pipes drifted from every tavern onto the street; crowds thronged beneath dazzling lanterns, flutes and pipes echoed from home after home, and children chased each other laughing down the lanes.
[ZH] 望月轩显得冷清而又落寞。
[EN] Wangyue Pavilion, by contrast, felt quiet and forlorn.
[ZH] 尽管厨房里也做了月饼；庭院中也摆上祭拜月神的桌案，到处也被布置；丫鬟和小斯们心情也不错，聚在一起玩乐，欣赏着天上的一轮圆月。
[EN] The kitchen had baked mooncakes, an altar to the Moon Deity stood set in the courtyard with decorations all around, and the maids and page boys were in good spirits, gathering to play and admire the full moon overhead.
[ZH] 可千里寻懒懒地躺在竹椅上，怎么都高兴不起来，她的心情让望月轩中的一切失去色彩。
[EN] Yet Qianli Xun lay sprawled across a bamboo chair, unable to summon a shred of cheer; her gloom drained the color from every corner of Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 中秋节是团圆的日子，她却无家可归。望月轩不是她的家，她的家到底在哪里？是穿越前住过的二十二层？还是千里拳庄？亦或是穿越而来的朱府？它们是过，可又不是。
[EN] Mid-Autumn meant family reunion, yet she had no home to return to. Wangyue Pavilion did not feel like home—where was home, anyway? That twenty-second-floor apartment before crossing over? Qianli Fist Manor? Or Zhu Manor? Every one had been home, yet none was home now.
[ZH] 千里寻找不到答案，乔家不同意她与文渊在一起，搞得她近期伤感不已。尤其是热闹的节日，更衬托出落寞的心情。
[EN] With no answer in sight and the Qiao family blocking her and Wenyuan, sorrow weighed on her all the heavier amid the festival cheer outside.
[ZH] 一阵桂花的香气飘进闺房，千里寻才想起望月轩栽种着几棵桂树。桂花浓郁的香气，令她的心情稍微好些。
[EN] A drift of osmanthus scent floated into her chamber, reminding her several osmanthus trees grew in Wangyue Pavilion's garden. That rich fragrance lifted her spirits a fraction.
[ZH] “韦嬷嬷吩咐厨房做的，刚刚出炉，还热着呢。”苦荞端一盘月饼从外面进来，“您吃一块。”
[EN] "Fresh out of the oven, baked by the kitchen on Matron Wei's orders—still piping hot!" Carrying in a plate of mooncakes, Kuqiao urged, "Have one, Miss."
[ZH] “放下吧，我想去桂花树旁走走。”千里寻瞅着那盘月饼，毫无胃口，她从竹椅上起来，向闺房外走去。
[EN] "Set it down. I want to walk by the osmanthus trees." With zero appetite for mooncakes, Qianli Xun rose from the bamboo chair and stepped outside.
[ZH] 苦荞放好月饼，拿上披风，跟随小姐出去。
[EN] Setting the plate down, Kuqiao grabbed a cape and followed her mistress out.
[ZH] 院子里，夏果正与其他丫鬟、小厮跪拜月神，她在心里默默祈求月神保佑她有机会与乔公子在一起。
[EN] Out in the yard, Xiaguo knelt with the other maids and page boys bowing to the Moon Deity, whispering a silent prayer for a chance to be with Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 刚拜完，抬起头，发现小姐与苦荞从身边经过，赶紧爬起来跟上：“小姐，您要去哪里？”
[EN] Rising from her bow to see Miss and Kuqiao walk past, she scrambled up to follow: "Where is Miss headed?"
[ZH] 千里寻没有说话。
[EN] Qianli Xun stayed silent.
[ZH] 苦荞帮忙回答：“去桂花树旁走走。”
[EN] Kuqiao answered for her: "For a walk by the osmanthus trees."
[ZH] 小姐不说话，显然心情不好，夏果不敢再多问，默默跟着。
[EN] Seeing her mistress mute in gloom, Xiaguo dared ask no more and trailed behind in silence.
[ZH] 望月轩的园子里共栽种三棵桂树，满树的桂花密密地开着，散发出诱人的香气，越靠近，香气越浓。
[EN] Three osmanthus trees stood in Wangyue Pavilion's garden, branches crowded tight with golden blossoms pouring out rich perfume—sweeter with every step closer.
[ZH] 千里寻绕着三棵桂树走着，希望浓浓的香气能带走她莫落的心情。
[EN] Qianli Xun circled the three trees, hoping the thick scent might wash away her low spirits.
[ZH] 曾经的她并非多愁善感之人，为何穿越而来会变得不一样？她无法解释。
[EN] She had never been the sentimental type before; why had crossing over turned her into such a softy? No answer came.
[ZH] 绕着桂树走完一圈，看到旁边有座亭子，千里寻又走到亭子里。她发现站在亭子里欣赏桂树反而能将它们尽收眼底，还能清晰地嗅到飘拂而来的阵阵香气。
[EN] Completing a lap around the trees, she spotted a pavilion beside them and stepped inside. From the pavilion she could take in all three trees at a glance while waves of osmanthus fragrance drifted straight across her face.
[ZH] 她刚搬进望月轩不久，与其说是没来得及到处走动，不如说是没心情。要不是桂花香，她才懒得走出闺房。
[EN] Since moving in, it was not that she had lacked time to explore the garden—she just had not had the mood. Without that osmanthus scent, she would not have left her room tonight.
[ZH] 站在亭子处，千里寻不由自主抬头仰望夜空，今晚的月亮好大好圆，她才发现这个亭子是赏月的绝佳位置，也才真正理解为何宅子名为望月轩。
[EN] Standing in the pavilion, she lifted her eyes to the night sky. What a huge, round moon! Only now did she realize this pavilion was the prime moon-viewing perch—and why the house bore the name Wangyue Pavilion, the Pavilion for Gazing at the Moon.
[ZH] “把灯熄了。”
[EN] "Put out the lanterns."
[ZH] 两个丫鬟互相看看，不甚理解，又不敢多问，乖乖将亭子中的灯火熄灭。
[EN] Exchanging a puzzled look, the two maids asked no questions and snuffed the pavilion's lamps.
[ZH] 周围没有灯光影响，天上的月亮显得更加明亮。今晚，天公作美，不辜负人间佳节。不知朱府的人在做什么？也不知文渊是否还被关着？
[EN] With the glare gone, moonlight spilled twice as bright across the garden. Heaven had blessed this festival night with clear skies. What were the folks at Zhu Manor doing right now? And was Wenyuan still locked up?
[ZH] 正想着，韦嬷嬷走来，边走边说：“我到处找姑娘，听丫鬟说你朝这个方向来，果真在这里，快跟我到前院，有人来看姑娘。”
[EN] Just then Matron Wei hurried up calling, "Been hunting everywhere for you, Young Lady! A maid said you headed this way, and here you are—come to the front courtyard! Guests are here to see you!"
[ZH] 有人来？会是谁？千里寻在心里猜测着，应该不是仪王，他已答应不会再来望月轩，会不会是家人？
[EN] Guests? Who? Not Prince Yi—he had promised never to come back. Could it be her family?
[ZH] 千里寻急忙走下亭子：“谁来看我？”
[EN] Qianli Xun hurried down the pavilion steps: "Who came?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷卖着关子：“姑娘一会儿便知。”
[EN] Matron Wei kept the surprise: "You'll see in a minute, Young Lady!"
[ZH] 千里寻快步往园子外走去，心里猜想着来看她的人……
[EN] Quickening her steps out of the garden, Qianli Xun tried guessing who waited ahead...
[ZH] 到达前院，进入会客厅，千里寻看到向夫人和陈文心坐在那里，她竟然开心地流下眼泪，上前拜道：“娘，嫂嫂，谢谢你们来看我……”
[EN] Entering the reception hall in the front courtyard, she saw Madam Xiang and Chen Wenxin sitting there, and joyful tears sprang straight to her eyes as she bowed: "Mother, Sister-in-law, thank you for coming to see me..."
[ZH] 陈文心最怕见感动的场面，调侃道：“怎么这么见外？又是拜，又是谢，搞得我跟婆婆好像跟你不是一家人似的，要不是一家人，我们两个何必大晚上来看你？”
[EN] Allergic to tearjerkers, Chen Wenxin teased, "Why act like strangers—bowing and thanking us as if Mother and I aren't family! If we weren't family, why on earth would we trek over here at night?!"
[ZH] “我何时跟你们见外？”千里寻破涕为笑，上前拉住向夫人，“娘，我好想您，虽然搬出来时间不长，可我感觉好像很久都没见到您。”
[EN] Laughing through her tears, Qianli Xun rushed up to hold Madam Xiang's hands: "I missed you so much, Mother! Haven't been out long, yet it feels like ages since I saw you."
[ZH] 向夫人含笑的眼中闪着泪光：“淑真，娘才来看你，你不会怪我吧？”
[EN] Tears shimmered in Madam Xiang's smiling eyes: "You don't blame Mother for taking this long to visit, Shuzhen?"
[ZH] “怎么会？本应是女儿回家看望娘，女儿不孝，却让娘亲自跑来看我。”
[EN] "How could I? A daughter ought to go home to see her mother—I'm the unfilial one making Mother trek all the way out here."
[ZH] 向夫人站起来：“你瘦了，要多吃饭，娘不在你身边，要懂得爱惜自己。”
[EN] Madam Xiang stood and looked her over: "You've gotten thinner! Eat more—with Mother not beside you, take good care of yourself."
[ZH] “娘不用操心我，我吃得好，睡得好。倒是您，鬓角的白发又多几根，定要减少思虑。”
[EN] "Don't fret over me, Mother—I eat great and sleep great. Look at you, though, more silver hairs at your temples! Quit worrying so much."
[ZH] 陈文心笑道：“婆婆，您与淑真都不要站着，我们坐下聊，坐下才好说话。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin chuckled: "Mother, Shuzhen, quit standing around—sit down so we can have a proper chat!"
[ZH] 大家都坐下。
[EN] Everyone took seats.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷吩咐丫鬟把刚刚做好的月饼端来摆上。
[EN] Matron Wei signaled a maid to bring out the fresh mooncakes.
[ZH] 向夫人招呼道：“嬷嬷也坐下，我们一家人聊会儿，今儿是中秋，大家一处才热闹。”
[EN] Madam Xiang beckoned: "Sit with us too, Matron—it's Mid-Autumn, merrier with the whole family together."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷也坐下：“夫人与少夫人尝尝月饼，也让姑娘讨个吉利。姑娘独自住在外面，正好逢中秋节，她心里不好受，我尽知，可又做不了别的，只会做月饼。做月饼时，心想，若有人今晚来看姑娘，定要让他吃块月饼，只要来人吃下月饼，以后姑娘的日子肯定会圆圆满满。”
[EN] Taking a seat, Matron Wei said, "Try these mooncakes, Madam and Young Madam, and bring our Young Lady good fortune! Living out here alone on Mid-Autumn, I knew she felt lonely, yet baking mooncakes was all I could do. While baking, I wished that whoever visited tonight would eat a mooncake so our Young Lady's life ahead turns round and complete!"
[ZH] 向夫人与陈文心二话没说，各拿起一块，用心吃着，每吃一口，都在心中祝福淑真的未来顺利圆满。
[EN] Without a second thought, Madam Xiang and Chen Wenxin each picked up a mooncake and ate every bite with heart, silently blessing Shuzhen's future with fullness and peace.
[ZH] 千里寻被两位亲人吃月饼的动作感动，忽觉自己伤感实在没有必要。家在哪里？她已找到答案，不管她走到哪里，只要有家人的地方就有家。
[EN] Watching her two loved ones eat those mooncakes thawed Qianli Xun's chest, making all her earlier gloom look silly. Where was home? She had her answer now: wherever family stood, there was home.
[ZH] 陈文心第一个吃完，用丝帕擦了擦嘴角：“淑真，你可知我刚才吃月饼时想什么？”
[EN] Finishing first, Chen Wenxin dabbed her lips with a silk handkerchief: "Shuzhen, guess what I was thinking while eating that mooncake?"
[ZH] “嫂嫂的心事，我怎能猜到？”
[EN] "How could I guess Sister-in-law's thoughts?"
[ZH] “我在想，说不定跟乔家的婚事未成是件好事，肯定有更好的姻缘等着你，临安又不是只有乔家好，我们陈家也不错。”
[EN] "I was thinking maybe the Qiao match falling through is a blessing in disguise—a better match is waiting for you! The Qiaos aren't the only fine family in Lin'an; our Chen house isn't shabby either!"
[ZH] “嫂嫂难道想让我嫁到陈家？”
[EN] "Sister-in-law wants me to marry into the Chen family?"
[ZH] “未尝不可！”
[EN] "Why not?!"
[ZH] “嫂嫂说笑，陈浩宇那样的，我可瞧不上。”
[EN] "Quit joking, Sister-in-law—a guy like Chen Haoyu couldn't pass my inspection in a million years!"
[ZH] “陈家又不是只有浩宇一个男儿，我三叔的儿子浩飞不错，不爱做生意，只喜欢读书，不敢说学富五车，也是饱读诗书，你可以考虑一下。”
[EN] "Haoyu isn't the only Chen boy! My third uncle's son Haofei is a gem—hates business, loves books, well-read as can be. Worth considering!"
[ZH] “我看嫂嫂是闲得慌，竟然当起媒人来。”
[EN] "Sounds like Sister-in-law is bored stiff, playing matchmaker!"
[ZH] 陈文心对向夫人说：“婆婆，您说呢？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin appealed to Madam Xiang: "What do you think, Mother?"
[ZH] 向夫人也已吃完月饼，笑着批评道：“我全当听个笑话，你三叔的儿子可比淑真整整小五岁，呵呵……！”
[EN] Finishing her mooncake, Madam Xiang laughed while chiding her: "I'm taking it as a joke—your third uncle's boy is five whole years younger than Shuzhen! Haha!"
[ZH] 陈文心不再撮合，本就是开个玩笑：“我也是想逗淑真乐乐。”
[EN] Dropping the match, Chen Wenxin grinned: "Just trying to make Shuzhen laugh!"
[ZH] “亏嫂嫂想得出来，用这种事情逗我，就不怕我当真？”
[EN] "Leave it to Sister-in-law to joke about something like that—aren't you afraid I'll take you up on it?"
[ZH] “当真才好呢！”
[EN] "Even better if you did!"
[ZH] 嫂嫂提议不佳，用心却好，千里寻心情又好许多：“娘，您与嫂嫂过来，爹会不会不高兴？”
[EN] Bad match aside, her sister-in-law's warm intent cheered Qianli Xun up more: "Coming over here tonight, Mother and Sister-in-law—won't Father be mad?"
[ZH] 向夫人说：“傻孩子，你爹要是不高兴，我跟你嫂嫂能出来？他对你严厉，把你赶出来，都是表演给外人看，心里时刻不挂念你。”
[EN] Madam Xiang smiled: "Silly child, if your father objected, could we have walked out the gate? Acting stern and sending you out was all a show for outsiders—he worries about you every single hour."
[ZH] 陈文心也说：“是啊，你要理解公爹，他是临安知府，要面子，又是朱家的掌舵人，出这么大的事，总得有个态度，方可对他人有个交代。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin chimed in: "True! Cut Father-in-law some slack—he's the Prefect of Lin'an and head of the Zhu house. With a scandal this loud, he had to put on a stern stance to answer to outsiders."
[ZH] 其实千里寻早已不埋怨朱晞颜，她故意装作生气的样子：“爹就知道对别人有个交代，他难道不知，对自己的女儿也需有个交代？把我赶出家门，只会让大家更加嘲笑我。”
[EN] Qianli Xun had long stopped blaming Zhu Xiyan, though she put on a mock pout: "Father only cares about answering to outsiders—doesn't he owe his own daughter an answer too? Throwing me out just gave folks more to laugh at."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷听出自家姑娘已不再埋怨老爷，甚是欣慰：“古人云，福祸相依，姑娘遭此一劫，说不定是件好事，可以看作是老天给姑娘出的考题。我家姑娘面对全城人议论，面不改色，说明心态好，人又坚强；遇乔家那档子事，没有哭哭啼啼，要死要活，反而理智面对。我要是考官，绝对给姑娘打个优等，呵呵……！”
[EN] Hearing no real grudge left in her voice, Matron Wei beamed: "As the ancients say, fortune and misfortune lean on each other. Maybe this storm is Heaven's exam for you, Young Lady! Facing a whole city's gossip without flinching shows true grit; handling the Qiao mess with a cool head instead of weeping and threatening suicide shows real wisdom. If I were the examiner, I'd give you top honors! Haha!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷半开玩笑半认真的话，千里寻竟然听进去，暗暗决定，要谨慎对待人生的每一道难题，用最适合的方法解决。她与文渊之事，目前最好的解题方法只能靠等待。
[EN] Half joke, half wisdom—Matron Wei's words struck home. Qianli Xun vowed inside to tackle every test life threw at her with clear judgment. Right now, the best answer for Wenyuan was patience.
[ZH] 提到乔家，向夫人小心问：“淑真，与乔家公子之事，你是怎么想的？”
[EN] Bringing up the Qiao family with care, Madam Xiang asked, "What are your thoughts on Young Master Qiao, Shuzhen?"
[ZH] “我给自己半年时间，若半年没有进展，我便……便放弃。”
[EN] "I'm giving myself half a year. If nothing moves in half a year, I'll... let go."
[ZH] 向夫人点点头：“想得对，只是苦了你。”
[EN] Madam Xiang nodded: "Sensible—just hard on you."
[ZH] “你能用理智面对此事，嫂嫂很欣慰，像你这么好的姑娘，乔家不上门提亲，是他们的损失，以后也不知会便宜哪家小子。”陈文心想，若是自家弟弟稍微长进些，还能张得了口，谁让浩宇是那副德行，还跟淑真合不来，想将淑真说给弟弟的想法怎么都开不了口。
[EN] "I'm glad you're keeping a cool head, Shuzhen. A gem like you—the Qiaos are the losers for backing out! Lucky whichever boy wins you next." Chen Wenxin silently sighed: if only her own brother had half a spine she would pitch him right now, yet with Haoyu's wild streak and the way those two clashed, she could not even open her mouth.
[ZH] “嫂嫂是自家人，当然会夸我。”
[EN] "Sister-in-law's family—of course you praise me."
[ZH] ﻿千里寻觉得望月轩不再落寞，已充满亲人之间浓浓的情意。
[EN] Wangyue Pavilion no longer felt cold or empty; family warmth filled every corner.
[ZH] 苦荞也觉开心，小姐并不孤单，朱家的人依然关心她。
[EN] Kuqiao felt happy too—Miss was not alone, and the Zhu family still cared for her.
[ZH] ﻿夏果却高兴不起来，小姐只等半年时间，若等不来，该怎么办？难道真的要放弃乔公子？若小姐放弃乔公子，她该如何是好？
[EN] Only Xiaguo could not raise a smile. Half a year—what if nothing happened in half a year and Miss gave up Young Master Qiao? What on earth would Xiaguo do then?

#CH 125
[ZH] 一家人又聊一会儿，毕竟是中秋之夜，总不能把朱晞颜父子撂在家里，向夫人感觉差不多时，才提出离开。
[EN] They chatted a while longer until Madam Xiang realized they shouldn't leave Zhu Xiyan and Zhu Jingyuan sitting alone on Mid-Autumn night, and rose to go.
[ZH] “今日既是看你，也算认下门，娘该回去，以后再来看你。”
[EN] "Now that Mother knows the way, I'll come visit again soon."
[ZH] 千里寻和韦嬷嬷将她们送至门口，再送上马车。
[EN] Qianli Xun and Matron Wei saw them out to the gate and into their carriage.
[ZH] 马车刚要启动时，千里寻像是想起什么：“等等！”
[EN] Just as the driver flicked the reins, Qianli Xun called out, "Wait!"
[ZH] 待马车停下，千里寻上前问道：“嫂嫂，旁边有座宅子叫永宁居，可是陈家的？”
[EN] When the carriage halted, she stepped up and asked, "Sister-in-law, there's a house right nearby called Yongning Residence—does it belong to the Chen family?"
[ZH] 陈文心从马车里探出头：“陈家的宅子太多，我都记不住，你为何问这个？”
[EN] Poking her head out the window, Chen Wenxin said, "The Chen clan owns so many houses I can't keep track—why ask?"
[ZH] “哦，没什么？只是我的丫鬟偶然见陈浩宇进入永宁居，好奇，所以问问。”
[EN] "Oh, nothing. My maid happened to see Chen Haoyu walk into Yongning Residence, so I got curious."
[ZH] “既看到浩宇进入，应该是陈家的宅子。这小子整日在外面瞎混，不着家，就是仗着陈家宅子多。不过，近日他倒老实，竟然回陈府，说是以后要老老实实在家待着，真是太阳打西边出来，呵呵……”
[EN] "If Haoyu went in, it's bound to be a Chen house. Having estates everywhere is why that rascal never sleeps at home! Lately he's turned over a new leaf, though—moved back to Chen Manor swearing he'll behave and stay home! Sun's rising in the west, haha..."
[ZH] “谢谢嫂嫂告知。”听到陈浩宇老实回到陈府，千里寻轻松许多，他既然回陈府，以后应该不会与他碰面。
[EN] "Thanks for telling me, Sister-in-law." Hearing Chen Haoyu had holed up at Chen Manor eased Qianli Xun's mind; they probably wouldn't bump into each other anymore.
[ZH] 向夫人不敢再看女儿，她怕流泪的样子被发现，反而搞得淑真心情不好。
[EN] Madam Xiang dared not look back at her daughter lest her tears show and dampen Shuzhen's mood.
[ZH] 朱府的马车又开始启动，车轮碾压着马路，渐渐消失在夜色中。
[EN] The Zhu carriage rolled off, wheels rumbling down the lane until it vanished into the night.
[ZH] 听不到朱家马车的声音时，千里寻刚要带众人回屋，却传来一个声音：“幽栖居士！等等！”
[EN] Just as the clatter faded and Qianli Xun turned to head inside, a voice hailed her: "Wait, Youqi Jushi!"
[ZH] 中秋之夜的月光下，一个身影走到她面前，千里寻认出是东方尘泽身边的小厮化雨。
[EN] Stepping out of the Mid-Autumn moonlight came a familiar boy—Dongfang Chenze's attendant, Huayu.
[ZH] “你怎么找到这里？”
[EN] "How did you track me down here?"
[ZH] “我家公子说中秋之夜，幽栖居士肯定会写诗作词，他被老爷关在家里，不得自由，没办法亲自出来，派我前来讨要一首。我本不知您住这里，跑了趟幽林别居，想求乔公子帮忙，结果没找到乔公子，反而打听到您的新住处，赶来碰碰运气。”
[EN] "My master swore Youqi Jushi would compose poems on Mid-Autumn night! Old Master locked him indoors with no freedom to come out himself, so he sent me to beg a poem. Didn't know where you moved, so I ran to Youlin Retreat to ask Young Master Qiao—didn't find him, got your new address instead, and rushed over to try my luck!"
[ZH] “你家公子为何会被关起来？”
[EN] "Why did your master get locked up?"
[ZH] 化雨照实说：“外面人议论幽栖居士，我家老爷担心连累公子，才把他关起来。”
[EN] Huayu told the plain truth: "With everyone gossiping about Youqi Jushi, Old Master worried my master would get dragged in, so he locked him up."
[ZH] 原来东方尘泽被关与她有关：“你随我来，我写两首，都送给你。”
[EN] So Dongfang Chenze got grounded over her! "Come inside—I'll write two poems for you to take back."
[ZH] 化雨高兴不已，总算没白白跑出来一趟。
[EN] Huayu beamed—his trek hadn't gone to waste!
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷不愿多熬夜，早早回屋睡下。苦荞把化雨安排在会客厅等待，夏果则跟小姐回闺房伺候笔墨。
[EN] Unwilling to stay up late, Matron Wei turned in early. Kuqiao seated Huayu in the reception hall while Xiaguo followed her mistress to the bedchamber to tend ink and brush.
[ZH] 回闺房的路上，千里寻心中已知要写朱淑真的哪两首诗。
[EN] Walking back, Qianli Xun already knew which two Mid-Autumn poems of Zhu Shuzhen's to write.
[ZH] 墨研好后，再给笔尖吸饱墨汁，夏果双手捧上。
[EN] Grinding the ink fresh and soaking the brush tip, Xiaguo presented it with both hands.
[ZH] 千里寻接过，提笔写下两首关于中秋之诗：
[EN] Taking the brush, Qianli Xun wrote down two Mid-Autumn poems:
[ZH] 秋来长是病，不易到中秋。欲赏今宵月，须登昨夜楼。
[EN] Since autumn came I've lingered sick in bed, / No easy thing to reach Mid-Autumn's night. / To gaze upon the moon that shines tonight, / I climb the tower of last night once more.
[ZH] 露浓梧影淡，风细桂香浮。莫做寻常看，嫦娥亦解愁。《中秋》
[EN] Thick dew dims shadows of the wutong tree, / Fine breezes float the sweet osmanthus scent. / Do not regard it with a casual eye: / Even Chang'e knows what sorrow means. —"Mid-Autumn" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 独占秋光盛，天工信有偏。清晖千里共，皓魄十分圆。
[EN] It claims the fullest splendor of the fall; / Heaven's hand does show favor, after all. / Its pure light reaches out a thousand li, / A bright white orb rounded in perfection.
[ZH] 兔影寒犹弄，蟾蜍老更坚。只愁看未足，一去又经年。《中秋玩月》
[EN] Through chill the jade hare works its pestle still; / Grown old, the moon toad stands yet stronger still. / I fear I cannot drink my fill of it, / For once it passes, a whole year must turn. —"Enjoying the Moon at Mid-Autumn" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 小姐写诗时，夏果站在一旁欣赏，诗中的字她全部认得，其中的意味却不懂。她决定，要继续认真读书，直到能看懂小姐写的诗词，还能说出几句评价之语，未来才可配得上乔公子。
[EN] Watching her mistress write, Xiaguo recognized every character yet missed the deeper layers. She vowed to keep studying hard until she could grasp every poem and even offer critiques—worthy of Young Master Qiao someday.
[ZH] 待小姐撂下笔，夏果把纸张拿起一一吹干，折好后，来到会客厅。
[EN] Once her mistress laid down the brush, Xiaguo blew the ink dry, folded the sheets neat, and carried them to the reception hall.
[ZH] 化雨边吃月饼，边与苦荞聊天儿。
[EN] Inside, Huayu was munching mooncakes while chatting with Kuqiao.
[ZH] “苦荞姐姐，听说你跟白风在一起……”
[EN] "Sister Kuqiao, heard you and Bai Feng are an item..."
[ZH] 苦荞很害羞：“你个小孩子！别瞎说！”
[EN] Kuqiao flushed red: "You little kid—quit talking nonsense!"
[ZH] “是白风说的。”
[EN] "Bai Feng told me himself!"
[ZH] “他这人怎么什么都说！等我下次见到，定要好好教训他。”
[EN] "Why does that blabbermouth tell everyone?! Wait till I see him next—I'll teach him a lesson!"
[ZH] “看苦荞姐姐的样子，应该是了？”化雨边吃月饼边乐。
[EN] "Judging by your face, Sister Kuqiao, it's true!" Huayu grinned through a mouthful of mooncake.
[ZH] “小孩子懂什么？吃你的月饼！”
[EN] "What does a kid know? Eat your mooncake!"
[ZH] “我不是小孩子，我跟南双一样大，他都能喜欢夏果，我为何不能说说苦荞姐姐和白风？”
[EN] "I'm no kid! Same age as Nan Shuang—if he can crush on Xiaguo, why can't I tease Sister Kuqiao and Bai Feng?"
[ZH] 夏果正好进来，本来脸上挂着笑容，听到化雨提到她与南双，一脸不高兴：“别拿我跟南双说事儿，我根本瞧不上他，他最好死了那份心。”
[EN] Walking in just in time with a smile on her face, Xiaguo snapped cold at the mention of Nan Shuang: "Keep my name out of Nan Shuang's mouth! I wouldn't look twice at him—tell him to drop it!"
[ZH] 化雨咽下最后一口月饼，站起来，走到夏果跟前，笑嘻嘻地说：“夏果姐姐觉得我怎么样？”
[EN] Swallowing his last bite, Huayu hopped up and sidled over with a cheeky grin: "What does Sister Xiaguo think of me, then?"
[ZH] “再胡说，小姐写的诗我可不给你。”
[EN] "Say one more silly word and you don't get Miss's poems!"
[ZH] 化雨不敢再胡说：“夏果姐姐快给我，我家公子被关在家里，心情不好，可指着幽栖居士的诗高兴呢！”
[EN] Huayu backed off at once: "Hand them over, Sister Xiaguo! My master's locked indoors moping—he's counting on Youqi Jushi's poems to cheer him up!"
[ZH] “以后不准再胡说！”
[EN] "No more nonsense from you!"
[ZH] “不敢了，不敢了。”
[EN] "Never again, I swear!"
[ZH] 夏果把纸张递给化雨：“拿着，若是碰到南双，告诉他，别癞蛤蟆想吃天鹅肉。”
[EN] Xiaguo handed over the folded sheets: "Here. And if you see Nan Shuang, tell him to stop acting like a toad dreaming of swan meat."
[ZH] 化雨接过，为难道：“夏果姐姐这话我可不敢传，南双怎么能是癞蛤蟆？听白风说他最近可怜着呢，跟他家公子一起被关在乔府，我连面儿都见不上。”
[EN] Taking the papers, Huayu winced: "Can't deliver that message, Sister Xiaguo! How is Nan Shuang a toad? Bai Feng told me poor Nan Shuang is locked up in Qiao Manor right alongside his master—haven't even laid eyes on him."
[ZH] “行了行了，拿着我家小姐写的诗赶紧走吧，记住，不准跟外人瞎胡议论我家小姐。”
[EN] "Enough chatter! Take Miss's poems and go—and don't you dare gossip about Miss to outsiders!"
[ZH] “我不是那样的人，再说我家公子知道也会批评我。”化雨边说边往外走。
[EN] "Not my style—besides, my master would skin me if he heard!" Huayu called back as he headed out.
[ZH] 送走化雨后，两个丫鬟回到闺房伺候自家小姐。
[EN] After seeing Huayu off, both maids returned to help their mistress get ready for bed.
[ZH] 家人送来亲情，好友也没舍弃她，在众人唾骂之际，还派下人来要诗，千里寻又觉得她很幸运，何必在月圆之夜纠结于不良情绪。
[EN] Warm family love, loyal friends sending for her poems even while the city jeered—Qianli Xun felt lucky all over again, refusing to stew in gloom beneath a full Mid-Autumn moon.
