[ZH] 现在，唯一让她揪心的只有与文渊的未来。揪心又有何用？总不能打进乔家把文渊救出来？尽管她想过，可现实却不能这么做。世上很多事无法用拳头解决，她只能默默等待命运安排。
[EN] Only one ache lingered: her future with Wenyuan. Yet worrying changed nothing—she couldn't storm Qiao Manor and bust him out! Tempting as it sounded, fists couldn't solve everything; she had to wait on fate.
[ZH] 丫鬟伺候她睡下后，也各自休息。
[EN] Once the maids tucked her in, they turned in too.
[ZH] 千里寻躺在闺床上，想着命运之事，她深信已走上千里寻的命运。如果命运安排她不能跟文渊走到一起，她也会努力接受不情愿接受的结果。
[EN] Lying in bed pondering destiny, Qianli Xun felt sure she was walking Qianli Xun's own path now. Even if fate denied her Wenyuan, she would summon the grit to accept an ending she never wanted.
[ZH] 化雨把幽栖居士的两首诗揣进怀里，步行往回走。中秋节的晚上街市繁华，每年这个时候大家都得闹个通宵，他不如趁此机会好好逛逛。
[EN] Tucking the two poems inside his robe, Huayu strolled home on foot. Mid-Autumn night markets blazed all night every year—why not enjoy the bustle while he was out?
[ZH] 正逛着，听到有人叫他：“化雨！”
[EN] Mid-stroll, someone called out: "Huayu!"
[ZH] 化雨回过头，看到安明轩与曹班一人拿个糖葫芦在吃。
[EN] Spinning around, he spotted An Mingxuan and Cao Ban each munching a candied hawthorn skewer.
[ZH] ﻿“两位公子好。”
[EN] "Greetings, Young Masters!"
[ZH] 安明轩说：“果然是你，你都出来，怎么没见东方兄？”
[EN] An Mingxuan said, "Thought that was you! If you're out, where's Brother Dongfang?"
[ZH] “我家公子还被关在家里，不如两位公子自由。我实在想不通，两位公子也喜欢幽栖居士的诗词，为何你们两家父母不把你们也关起来？”
[EN] "Still locked up at home, unlike you two free birds! I don't get it—you two love Youqi Jushi's verse just as much, so why didn't your parents lock you up too?"
[ZH] 曹班吃完一颗糖葫芦：“说的什么话！你是在咒我们两个吗？”
[EN] Popping the last candied hawthorn into his mouth, Cao Ban barked, "Watch it! Putting a curse on us?!"
[ZH] 化雨赶紧道歉：“化雨不敢。”
[EN] Huayu bowed fast: "Wouldn't dare!"
[ZH] 安明轩批评道：“你家公子被关在家里，心里不知有多苦，你不回家伺候他，自己却跑出来逛街，是不是太过分？”
[EN] An Mingxuan chided him: "Your master's locked up miserable, and here you are roaming the night market instead of waiting on him—isn't that awful?"
[ZH] 化雨解释道：“我可不是出来逛街，我家公子给我布置任务，他说幽栖居士必然在中秋之夜写诗作词，让我想办法帮他搞来……”
[EN] Huayu defended himself: "I'm not out playing! My master gave me a mission—swore Youqi Jushi would write verse on Mid-Autumn night and sent me to get some!"
[ZH] 两人齐声问：“可搞到？”。
[EN] Both spoke in unison: "Did you get any?"
[ZH] 化雨一脸骄傲：“有我出马，没有办不成的事，一次搞来两首。”
[EN] Huayu puffed out his chest: "When I'm on the case, nothing fails! Scored two whole poems!"
[ZH] “两首！”安明轩叹道，“快拿出来让我二人瞧瞧。”
[EN] "Two?!" An Mingxuan gasped. "Show us right now!"
[ZH] 化雨开始提条件：“给二位公子看可以，你们得请我喝酸梅汤，吃烧鸡。”
[EN] Huayu named his price: "Sure, you can look—treat me to sour plum drink and roast chicken first!"
[ZH] 曹班爽快道：“这算什么，走，这就带你去买酸梅汤和烧鸡。”
[EN] Cao Ban waved a hand: "Easy! Come on, let's get your drink and chicken!"
[ZH] 街边正好有卖酸梅汤的，曹班给化雨买了两碗，化雨一口气一碗，就像从来没喝过一样。两位公子也把手中的糖葫芦吃完，又带他去卖烧鸡的摊位上挑烧鸡。
[EN] At a nearby stall, Cao Ban bought Huayu two bowls of sour plum drink, which Huayu downed like a man crossing a desert. Finishing their skewers, the two young masters then walked him over to pick out a roast chicken.
[ZH] 烧鸡买好后，二位公子找家茶肆。坐下后，化雨把诗拿出来，他们一人一首，如饥似渴地读起来，又问茶肆的伙计要来纸笔，将两首五律抄了下来。
[EN] Chicken in hand, they ducked into a teahouse. Once Huayu pulled out the poems, each scholar grabbed a sheet and read like starving men, then paid the waiter for brush and paper to copy both regulated five-character poems down.
[ZH] 化雨边吃烧鸡，边瞧两位公子，心想，这些有钱公子真不知人间疾苦，整日只知对没有任何用处的诗词感兴趣，在化雨眼中，诗词哪比得上他正在吃的烧鸡来得实惠。
[EN] Chomping roast chicken as he watched them, Huayu shook his head: rich boys didn't know real life, fussing over useless poems when hot roast chicken beat verse by a mile!
[ZH] 两位公子抄完五律，把原稿还给化雨，叮嘱他回去后代他们向东方兄问好，然后二人急急忙忙离开。
[EN] Done copying, the two scholars returned the originals, told Huayu to send Brother Dongfang their regards, and dashed off in a rush.
[ZH] 化雨把纸张叠好，塞进怀里，准备继续吃烧鸡，茶肆的伙计走过来：“客官来壶茶？”
[EN] Tucking the poems back in his robe, Huayu reached for his chicken—only for the waiter to step over: "Pot of tea, guest?"
[ZH] “不了，我有烧鸡吃。”
[EN] "Nah, I've got roast chicken."
[ZH] “烧鸡不是本店的，你占着地方，不喝茶，是不是不地道？”
[EN] "You didn't buy that chicken here! Taking up a table without ordering tea—isn't that bad form?"
[ZH] “刚才那两位公子不是也没喝茶？”
[EN] "Those two young masters didn't order tea either!"
[ZH] “他们给了笔墨钱，你给了什么？我们开店做生意，可不是做慈善。”
[EN] "They paid for brush and ink—what did you pay? We're running a business, not a charity!"
[ZH] 化雨把剩下的烧鸡包好，拿在手中，不满道：“势利眼，小心掉到钱眼里出不来！”
[EN] Wrapping the rest of his chicken, Huayu huffed off muttering, "Money-grubbing snob—careful you don't fall down a coin hole and get stuck!"
[ZH] 两位公子得了幽栖居士的新诗，甚是开心，终于有理由参加中秋诗宴。
[EN] Clutching Youqi Jushi's brand-new poems, the two young masters beamed—now they had a ticket to the Mid-Autumn Poetry Banquet!
[ZH] 乔兄和东方兄都被关起来，幽栖居士又被全城人非议，这个中秋节二位公子本打算逛逛街得了，竟无意中得到幽栖居士的新诗，不拿到中秋诗宴，实在对不起这么好的诗。
[EN] With Brother Qiao and Brother Dongfang both locked up and Youqi Jushi under fire across town, they had planned to skip the banquet and just wander the streets; landing two fresh gems like these, skipping the banquet would be a crime!
[ZH] 走着走着，曹班犹豫道：“中秋诗宴是一群文人出资举办，我们两个又没出钱，去了会不会被赶出来？”
[EN] Halfway there, Cao Ban hesitated: "A circle of literati chipped in to host this Mid-Autumn Poetry Banquet—we didn't pitch in a coin. Won't they kick us out?"
[ZH] 安明轩说：“你忘了，去年我们出了，今年怎么说也得给个面子，何况我们有新诗在手。”
[EN] An Mingxuan waved it off: "Forgot we paid last year? They'll give us face—especially with new poems in hand!"
[ZH] “也对，不过我担心大家不会把幽栖居士的诗当回事，毕竟满城都是她的风言风语。”
[EN] "True, yet what if folk turn up their noses at Youqi Jushi's poems with all the rumors flying?"
[ZH] “你可信市井之人的议论？”
[EN] "Do you believe street gossip?"
[ZH] 曹班摇摇头：“不信。”
[EN] Cao Ban shook his head: "Not a word."
[ZH] “你都不信，中秋诗宴的文人水平都在你我之上，谁会因市井之谈拒绝幽栖居士的佳作？”
[EN] "If you don't buy it, literati above our level certainly won't turn away masterpieces over market chatter!"
[ZH] 安明轩的话有些道理，二人才大胆朝正在酒楼举办的中秋诗宴而去。
[EN] Convinced, both strode straight toward the tavern hosting the Mid-Autumn Poetry Banquet.

#CH 126
[ZH] 正如安明轩所言，中秋诗宴中的文人并未因流言轻视幽栖居士的诗，大家反而赞赏有加。
[EN] Just as An Mingxuan predicted, the literati at the Mid-Autumn Poetry Banquet refused to let street gossip cloud their judgment, pouring praise on Youqi Jushi's poems instead.
[ZH] 有人说，幽栖居士没有被流言打倒，竟然还能写出：“莫做寻常看，嫦娥亦解愁”这等浪漫的诗句，实在难得。
[EN] One scholar marveled that no storm of rumors could break Youqi Jushi's spirit, letting her pen a line as transcendent as "Do not regard it with a casual eye: even Chang'e knows what sorrow means."
[ZH] 更有人说，市井之人只顾嚼舌，却不知幽栖居士以笑面对，竟然在中秋之夜玩起月来，此等心态令人羡慕。
[EN] Another praised how, while market folk wagged tongues, Youqi Jushi met the world with a smile and enjoyed the Mid-Autumn moon—a breadth of spirit to envy.
[ZH] 尤其是“兔影寒犹弄，蟾蜍老更坚”二句，“寒”字与“老”字写出现实境况不佳，可玉兔面对中秋之夜的寒冷依然在捣药，蟾蜍虽老，却老当益壮，无不展现出可敬可佩的精神力量。
[EN] Above all, the couplet "Through chill the jade hare works its pestle still; grown old, the moon toad stands yet stronger still" drew raves: "chill" and "old" mirrored harsh reality, yet the jade hare pounded its elixir undaunted by autumn frost, and the moon toad grew firmer with age, radiating an indomitable grace.
[ZH] 二位公子也各自展现品诗才华，闹到凌晨，实在扛不下去，才离开中秋诗宴，准备回家睡觉。其他文人继续饮酒、赏月、赋诗、作词、品鉴，打算热闹整个通宵。
[EN] Both young masters showed off their critical flair too, reveling until the small hours before exhaustion sent them home to bed, while other scholars kept drinking, gazing at the moon, trading verse, and debating until dawn.
[ZH] 二人刚出酒楼，被林子孝叫住：“二位公子！留步！”
[EN] Stepping out of the tavern, they heard Lin Zixiao hail them: "Hold on, you two young masters!"
[ZH] 见是林子孝，两人恭敬道：“林家二叔好，不知拦住我们二人，有何指教？”
[EN] Recognizing him, both bowed: "Greetings, Second Uncle Lin. What brings you to stop us?"
[ZH] 林子孝直言不讳：“二位可见过幽栖居士？”
[EN] Lin Zixiao spoke straight: "Have either of you seen Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] 二人摇摇头：“未见本尊。”
[EN] Both shook their heads: "Not in person."
[ZH] “她的诗你们如何得来？”
[EN] "Then where did you get her poems?"
[ZH] 曹班说：“这就不劳烦二叔挂怀。”
[EN] Cao Ban smiled: "No need for Second Uncle to worry over that."
[ZH] “我倒也不挂怀，只想二位帮我捎个话。她毕竟在林家待过一段时日，以前也算是一家人，现在被人议论成那样，听说跟乔家的事也黄了，又被知府大人赶出家门，一个女儿家在外生活不容易。今日读她的诗，我算看出，幽栖居士不是一位能够被困难打倒之人，令人钦佩。”
[EN] "I'm not prying—just want to ask you to pass a message. She lived under the Lin roof once, so we were family. Hearing how folk smear her, how the Qiao match fell through, and how the Prefect cast her out—life's hard for a young woman on her own. Reading her poems tonight showed me Youqi Jushi isn't someone hardship can break. I admire her."
[ZH] 二人被林子孝的话感动。
[EN] Lin Zixiao's words moved both young masters.
[ZH] 安明轩说：“林家二叔有什么话尽管说，我等定帮您转达。”
[EN] An Mingxuan said, "Tell us whatever you'd like to pass along, Second Uncle Lin, and we'll deliver it."
[ZH] “麻烦二位告诉幽栖居士，不管别人怎么议论她，我和林家人绝不会在背后议论她一分一毫。再转告她，谣言就像一阵风，很快会过去，等下一阵风来，自然消失。”
[EN] "Tell Youqi Jushi this: no matter what outsiders say, neither I nor anyone in the Lin family will ever breathe a single bad word behind her back. Tell her too that rumors are like a gust of wind—here one moment, blown clean away the next time the wind shifts."
[ZH] 曹班调侃道：“您可做得了林家的主？”
[EN] Cao Ban teased, "Can you speak for the whole Lin clan?"
[ZH] “我大哥不在，我便是林家族中长辈，哪个小辈敢在我面前说个‘不’字？”
[EN] "With my eldest brother away, I'm the senior elder of the Lin house—which junior dares say 'no' to me?"
[ZH] 二位公子相视一笑：“我二人定当转达，林家二叔，回见。”
[EN] Sharing a smile, both young masters promised, "We'll deliver every word. Farewell, Second Uncle Lin."
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 接下来的日子，千里寻未踏出望月轩半步，除吃喝拉撒休息外，只顾写话本，偶尔写首朱淑真的诗词消遣消遣。
[EN] In the days that followed, Qianli Xun did not set foot outside Wangyue Pavilion. Between eating, sleeping, and daily routines, she poured herself into writing huaben scripts, jotting down a poem or ci of Zhu Shuzhen's now and then to pass the hours.
[ZH] 仪王派白风来过两次，每次来除找苦荞玩耍以外，就是告知文渊的境况。第一次说文渊还被关着，不让走出屋子；第二次说乔家同意文渊可以走出屋子，但不能离开院子。
[EN] Prince Yi sent Bai Feng over twice, both times visiting Kuqiao while delivering updates on Wenyuan. The first time, Wenyuan was still locked indoors; the second, the Qiaos let him step out into his courtyard, though not beyond its gate.
[ZH] 千里寻听后，担心的同时，有些许轻松。能走出屋子已是进步，很快文渊会走出院子，再走出乔府，然后来望月轩找她。她决定耐心等待着文渊突然出现在面前。
[EN] Worry mingled with relief in Qianli Xun: stepping out into his yard meant progress. Soon he would step past the gate, out of Qiao Manor, and straight to Wangyue Pavilion. She decided to wait with patience for the day he showed up at her door.
[ZH] 没有将文渊等来，倒是等来三位公子。
[EN] Wenyuan did not arrive; instead, the three young masters paid a call.
[ZH] 东方尘泽的家人经过调查，自家儿子跟幽栖居士清清白白，没有任何越矩之事，便逼他认下一门亲，待亲事定后，才放他走出家门。
[EN] After Dongfang Chenze's parents investigated and confirmed their son had kept spotless propriety with Youqi Jushi, they pressured him into accepting a betrothal, releasing him only once the match was sealed.
[ZH] 千里寻深感歉意：“我的事，竟然牵连到东方公子，实属抱歉。”
[EN] Guilt tugged at Qianli Xun: "Getting Young Master Dongfang dragged into my mess—I'm truly sorry."
[ZH] 东方尘泽趁机提出要求：“幽栖居士光口头抱歉有何用？来点实惠的。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze seized the opening: "Empty apologies don't count, Youqi Jushi—give me something real!"
[ZH] “东方公子请说，只要我能力所及，必然答应。”
[EN] "Name it, Young Master Dongfang—anything within my power, you've got it."
[ZH] “以后幽栖居士写的诗词必须第一个给我。”
[EN] "From now on, every new poem or ci you write comes to me first."
[ZH] “好说好说，以后我写好诗词会让丫鬟送到府上。”
[EN] "Deal! I'll send a maid over to your manor whenever I finish one."
[ZH] “不必，我让化雨定期来取。”
[EN] "No need—I'll have Huayu come collect them on a schedule."
[ZH] 安明轩说：“东方兄得这么大的便宜，再被多关几日也值得。”
[EN] An Mingxuan laughed: "Scoring a bargain like that, Brother Dongfang—being locked up a few extra days would've been worth it!"
[ZH] 曹班说：“东方兄还要多谢幽栖居士，要不是因为她的事，你能这么快与喜欢的姑娘定亲？”
[EN] Cao Ban added, "Brother Dongfang ought to thank Youqi Jushi! Without this fuss, would you have gotten betrothed to the girl you love so fast?"
[ZH] 千里寻露出近期以来难得的笑容：“什么？东方公子竟然与喜欢的姑娘订亲？怎么回事？说来听听。”
[EN] A rare, bright smile broke across Qianli Xun's face: "What? Young Master Dongfang got engaged to the girl he loves? Tell me everything!"
[ZH] 安明轩帮忙解释：“那姑娘是乔兄的大妹妹，东方兄与她早已郎有情妾有意，一直不敢对父母提起，没想到家里竟然帮他到乔府提亲，乔家一口答应，岂不是美事？”
[EN] An Mingxuan filled her in: "It's Brother Qiao's eldest younger sister! Brother Dongfang and she have had feelings for each other ages without daring to tell their parents—who knew his parents would march straight to Qiao Manor to propose, and the Qiaos said yes on the spot! Isn't that grand?"
[ZH] 千里寻替东方尘泽感到高兴，既有父母之命媒妁之言，又有两情相悦，实属不易。可安明轩的话语中提到乔文仙，莫名的伤感不由袭上心头。
[EN] Qianli Xun felt genuine joy for Dongfang Chenze—parental blessing and mutual love, a rare prize! Yet mention of Qiao Wenxian sent a quiet pang through her chest.
[ZH] 曹班见状，赶紧岔开话题：“中秋那晚，得到幽栖居士的两首五律，我们二人拿到中秋诗宴，众人都赞不绝口。临走时，林家二叔还让我们二人给你捎话来着。”
[EN] Catching the shadow on her face, Cao Ban shifted gears fast: "On Mid-Autumn night, An Mingxuan and I got hold of your two new five-character poems and brought them to the Mid-Autumn Poetry Banquet—everyone raved! On our way out, Second Uncle Lin even asked us to pass you a message."
[ZH] 安明轩接着说：“林家二叔说，不管别人怎么议论，他与林家绝不会在背后议论一分一毫。还说谣言就像一阵风，很快会过去，等下一阵风来，自然消失。”
[EN] An Mingxuan picked it up: "Second Uncle Lin said no matter how folk gossip, neither he nor the Lin family will ever badmouth you behind your back—and that rumors are just a passing gust of wind, gone the second the next breeze blows."
[ZH] “让林家二叔费心，有机会见到他，我定当面感谢。”
[EN] "How kind of Second Uncle Lin. Next time I see him, I'll thank him in person."
[ZH] 千里寻被林子孝的话感动，感恩命运让她遇到许多善良又关心她的人，林子孝是，眼前的三位公子也是，还有苗知瑜，也不知她有没有见到文渊？
[EN] Warmth swelled inside Qianli Xun at Lin Zixiao's kindness, grateful fate had placed so many good, loyal people around her: Lin Zixiao, these three young masters, and Miao Zhiyu too—she wondered if Miao Zhiyu had seen Wenyuan yet?
[ZH] “你们近期可见过苗姑娘？”
[EN] "Have any of you seen Lady Miao lately?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“我一直被关着，才获自由，她的境况一概不知。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze said, "I just got let out of lockup—no idea how she's doing."
[ZH] 曹班回道：“我倒是在街上遇见过一次，跟一群闺中密友在一起，看上去心情不错。”
[EN] Cao Ban replied, "I spotted her on the street once with a cluster of female friends; looked in good spirits."
[ZH] 安明轩说：“没有你和乔兄，诗会都举行不起来，苗姑娘除诗会时跟我们见上一面，离开诗会都不怎么跟我等往来。她的境况我们很难得知。”
[EN] An Mingxuan said, "Without you and Brother Qiao, our poetry salons fell apart. Lady Miao only mixed with us at salons anyway, so we haven't heard much."
[ZH] “没关系，我只是随口问问，毕竟大家因诗会结识。”
[EN] "No matter—just asking in passing since we all met through salons."
[ZH] 东方尘泽劝道：“幽栖居士没事不要老窝在望月轩，出去走走，也可去钱塘江观潮，近期去的人挺多。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze urged, "Don't stay cooped up inside Wangyue Pavilion all day, Youqi Jushi—go out for a breath of air! Even head down to watch the Qiantang River tidal bore; crowds have been flocking there lately."
[ZH] “有机会我定去看看。”千里寻口中答应，其实她哪有心情去江边观潮。
[EN] "I'll go take a look when I get the chance." Qianli Xun agreed out of politeness, though she had zero mood for tide-watching.
[ZH] 曹班突然提到另一个话题：“你们最近有没有听说一位叫千里寻的才子？”
[EN] Out of the blue, Cao Ban changed the subject: "Have you guys heard of a scholar named Qianli Xun lately?"
[ZH] 安明轩说：“当然听说过，写话本的，故事写的很吸引人，听的人很多，我也去听过，确实不错，现在全临安城的说话艺人都在说他写的故事，大家听了一遍又一遍，都翘首期盼后面的情节。”
[EN] An Mingxuan lit up: "Of course! Writes huaben scripts—gripping plots! Huge crowds flock to hear them; I went myself and loved it! Right now every storyteller in Lin'an is performing his story, and listeners come back again and again dying for the next installment!"
[ZH] 这大概是千里寻近期听到的最令她欣慰之事。
[EN] That was the sweetest news Qianli Xun had heard in weeks.
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“我一直被关在家里，因我家堂弟酷爱听书，回来给我讲千里寻写的故事，的确曲折动人。可惜对这位才子只闻其名，不见其人，有机会定要结识结识。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze chimed in: "While I was locked indoors, my cousin—a storytelling fanatic—kept retelling Qianli Xun's tale, and it really is full of twists! Shame everyone knows his name while nobody's seen his face—I'd love to meet him one day."
[ZH] 曹班说：“恐怕东方兄会失望，千里寻甚是神秘，每次写好话本，是由身边的丫鬟送到说话艺人那里，连说话艺人都从来没见过本尊。”
[EN] Cao Ban laughed: "Prepare for disappointment, Brother Dongfang! Qianli Xun is mysterious as a ghost—sends every new script through a maid, so not even the storytellers have ever laid eyes on him!"
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“如此神秘，更勾起我的兴起，非把他挖出来不可。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze's eyes sparkled: "That just makes me itch to dig him out even more!"
[ZH] 千里寻赶紧劝道：“依我看，千里寻才子应该不喜被人关注，我等还是尊重他的选择，只需听他的故事即可。他写的本子我也听过，甚是喜欢，有机会我再去听听。”
[EN] Qianli Xun jumped in fast: "If you ask me, Master Qianli Xun likely hates the spotlight. Better respect his wishes and just enjoy his stories! I've listened to his scripts too and loved them—I'll go listen again when I can."
[ZH] 三位公子惊讶地看着幽栖居士。
[EN] All three young masters gaped at Youqi Jushi.
[ZH] 东方尘泽问：“幽栖居士以前可瞧不上下里巴人的玩意儿，怎么突然来了兴致？”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze blinked: "Youqi Jushi used to turn her nose up at lowbrow stuff—since when did you take a shine to it?"
[ZH] “人总是会变，以前是我小瞧下里巴人之作，现在我以为只要老百姓喜欢，就是好东西，什么阳春白雪，什么下里巴人，分得那么清楚，有何意义？”
[EN] "People change! I used to look down on lowbrow works, too. Now I figure whatever ordinary folk love is good work—highbrow 'Spring Snow' versus lowbrow street art, what's the point drawing such snobbish lines?"
[ZH] 穿越前的千里寻写网络小说，那个时代，精英文人对网络小说褒贬不一，在她看来，精英文人说什么都没用，最有用的是时代和老百姓的选择。
[EN] Back before crossing over, Qianli Xun wrote web fiction—a world where elitist critics argued over whether web novels counted as literature. In her book, elitist opinions meant nothing; the verdict of time and everyday readers was what lasted.
[ZH] 就像她穿越而来的宋代，朱淑真的诗词在精英文人中传颂，要不是因谣言四起，老百姓哪会关心她？即使如此议论，也只记住一句词：“和衣睡倒人怀”。
[EN] Just like here in the Song dynasty: Zhu Shuzhen's verse lived among elite scholars, yet common folk never spared her a thought until rumors broke—and even then only knew one line, "falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed."
[ZH] 而千里寻不一样，老百姓之所以议论她，只因她写的故事写到老百姓心里。
[EN] Qianli Xun was different: ordinary folk talked about her because her stories spoke straight to their hearts.
[ZH] 幽栖居士一番话，令三位公子佩服不已。
[EN] Her words left all three young masters nodding in genuine admiration.
[ZH] 大家又聊一会儿，才互相分别，望月轩又恢复寂静。
[EN] After chatting a bit longer, they took their leave, returning quiet to Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 千里寻继续回闺房写话本，老百姓喜欢，她更有创作动力，也希望用写话本来消磨时光，忘掉不快，等待更美好的一天到来。
[EN] Back at her desk writing huaben scripts, knowing readers loved her work fired up her pen—giving her something meaningful to fill the hours, push sorrow aside, and wait for brighter days ahead.

#CH 127
[ZH] 近期，申简辰没有去望月轩，可心无不被望月轩牵绊，他派白风送过两次关于文渊的消息。
[EN] Lately Shen Jianchen had stayed away from Wangyue Pavilion, yet his heart never left it for a moment; twice he had sent Bai Feng over with news of Wenyuan.
[ZH] ﻿现在只能靠传送消息的方式与千里寻发生千丝万缕的联系。
[EN] Passing messages was the sole fragile thread tying him to Qianli Xun now.
[ZH] 他无法忍受他的生活不与千里寻发生关系。
[EN] He couldn't bear a life severed from Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 申简辰觉得自己很贱，人家不断拒绝，他就是难以放下，理智在情感面前显得极其脆弱。
[EN] Shen Jianchen despised his own weakness—rejected over and over, yet still unable to let go, reason crumbling to sand before his heart.
[ZH] 他也觉得自己很坏，搂抱过乔梦容，还两次说过要娶她，心里却无时无刻想着什么时候出现奇迹，牵绊他的婚姻从此消失，自由自在奔赴在追求千里寻的道路上。
[EN] He felt guilty too: holding Qiao Mengrong in his arms and promising twice to marry her, while every hour secretly praying for a miracle to dissolve that betrothal and set him free to chase Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 申简辰去过乔家两次，第一次是那日离开望月轩时，没见到文渊本人，只与乔守仁聊了一会儿，说些劝慰的话。
[EN] He had visited Qiao Manor twice. The first time, right after leaving Wangyue Pavilion, he hadn't seen Wenyuan at all, only chatting with Qiao Shouren to smooth feathers.
[ZH] 从乔守仁的话语中得知，乔家正忙着给文渊选亲，已经有几个中意的姑娘入选，需进一步斟酌。
[EN] From Qiao Shouren he learned the Qiaos were busy picking a bride for Wenyuan, with several promising young ladies already shortlisted for final review.
[ZH] 申简辰莫名开心，他又觉得自己不重朋友情谊，朋友痛苦，他却以此为乐，实在不该。他用理智压制开心，却毫无用处。
[EN] A wicked rush of gladness had warmed Shen Jianchen—followed by guilt for rejoicing over his friend's misery. Reason shouted it down; his heart ignored reason.
[ZH] 第二次去乔家，文渊已能自由活动，范围仅限于他居住的院子。申简辰要求去看文渊，乔守仁不好阻拦，亲自将仪王带到儿子居住的院落。
[EN] On his second visit, Wenyuan had been allowed out into his own courtyard. When Shen Jianchen asked to see him, Qiao Shouren couldn't refuse and escorted Prince Yi to his son's courtyard himself.
[ZH] 门上有一把大大的锁，门口左右两边分别守着两个下人。院墙一人多高，上面临时放置着长满利刺的荆棘，显然是为防止文渊翻墙逃跑。外公真是用心良苦。
[EN] A heavy padlock hung on the gate, flanked by two guards. Overhead, fresh brambles bristling with thorns lined the man-high wall to stop Wenyuan climbing out. Grandfather sure left no loopholes.
[ZH] 仪王进入院中，乔守仁不便从外面锁上门，亲自守在门口，不敢有一丝松懈。
[EN] With Prince Yi inside, Qiao Shouren couldn't lock the gate from outside, so he stood sentry in the doorway himself without blinking.
[ZH] 文渊坐在院子里，闭着眼睛，晒太阳，人瘦了一圈。南双站在一旁，也显得没精神。
[EN] Wenyuan sat in the yard soaking up the sun with eyes shut, whole frame thinner by a size, with a listless Nan Shuang beside him.
[ZH] 当南双看到白风进来，兴奋地冲上去：“白风，你跟仪王终于来看我和公子，我被关了整整十日，快闷死了！”
[EN] Spotting Bai Feng walk in, Nan Shuang bolted over in glee: "Bai Feng! You and Prince Yi came at last! Locked up ten whole days—I'm dying of boredom!"
[ZH] 白风说：“我这次来，就是帮你解闷儿。”两个人站在院子的一旁，聊起天儿来。
[EN] Bai Feng grinned: "Came just to cure your boredom!" The two drifted to one corner of the yard to catch up.
[ZH] 乔文仙听到仪王前来，睁开眼，起身抱住仪王：“你可来了，是不是给我捎来淑真的消息？”
[EN] Hearing Prince Yi arrive, Qiao Wenxian opened his eyes, stood, and hugged him: "You came at last! Did you bring word from Shuzhen?"
[ZH] “幽栖居士不愿见我，我如何有她的消息？我是来看看你。”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi refuses to see me—where would I get word? I came to check on you."
[ZH] 乔文仙失望地放开仪王：“她该不会想要放弃我？”
[EN] Disappointment dropped Qiao Wenxian's arms: "She isn't thinking of giving up on me, is she?"
[ZH] “你是如何想的？”申简辰不想将千里寻的状态如实告知文渊。
[EN] "What do you plan to do?" Shen Jianchen had no intention of sharing how Qianli Xun was doing.
[ZH] “父亲在门口站着，说话不方便，我们进屋说。”乔文仙拉起仪王，走进屋内，把门关上。
[EN] "With Father standing right at the gate, we can't talk out here—come inside." Qiao Wenxian tugged Prince Yi indoors and shut the door.
[ZH] “你想对我说什么？”
[EN] "What do you want to tell me?"
[ZH] “我后悔没听你的建议，现在我想通，打算跟淑真私奔，仪王定要帮我……”
[EN] "I regret turning down your idea. I've made up my mind to elope with Shuzhen—you have to help me, Prince Yi..."
[ZH] “不行！”没等文渊讲完，申简辰立刻阻止。上次提出后，他已十分后悔，怎么可能让私奔之事真实发生？
[EN] "No!" Before Wenyuan finished, Shen Jianchen cut him off sharp. He had regretted pitching elopement the second it left his mouth—like hell he'd let it happen for real!
[ZH] 乔文献十分不解：“为何？仪王不是支持我吗？主意还是你出的。”
[EN] Baffled, Qiao Wenxian asked, "Why not? Weren't you on my side? It was your idea!"
[ZH] “此一时彼一时，外公都掏出匕首，我再帮你，岂不是舍外公性命不顾？要不……要不你放了幽栖居士……”
[EN] "Things have changed! Grandfather pulled a dagger on himself—help you now and I'd be risking Grandfather's life! Why don't you... why don't you let Youqi Jushi go..."
[ZH] “不！淑真亲口对我说，她不会放弃，我相信她，我也不会放弃！”
[EN] "Never! Shuzhen promised me herself she won't give up—I trust her, and I won't give up either!"
[ZH] “文渊，我今日来，想告诉你，以后你与幽栖居士的事，我不会再插手，你们好自为之。”
[EN] "I came today to tell you one thing, Wenyuan: from now on I'm staying out of your and Youqi Jushi's business. You two are on your own."
[ZH] 说完，申简辰推门而出，在院中喊了白风，迅速离开。
[EN] With that, Shen Jianchen pulled the door open, called Bai Feng, and strode straight out.
[ZH] 南双喊道：“才来一会儿就走，是不是太着急？能不能和我多聊会儿？”
[EN] Nan Shuang yelled after him, "Leaving already?! Can't you stay and chat five more minutes?!"
[ZH] 仪王出去后，院门又被从外面锁住。
[EN] The second Prince Yi stepped past the threshold, the padlock snapped shut behind him.
[ZH] 乔文仙从屋内走出，整个人毫无生气，自语着：“连仪王都不打算帮我，我该怎么办？”
[EN] Stepping out into the yard drained of life, Qiao Wenxian muttered, "Even Prince Yi won't help me anymore—what do I do now?"
[ZH] 南双迎上去：“公子，要不您就答应老爷，不要再喜欢幽栖居士……”
[EN] Nan Shuang hurried over: "Young Master, why not just give in to Master and stop pining for Youqi Jushi..."
[ZH] “滚！滚开！滚得越远越好！”乔文仙把南双推开，“我绝不放弃！哪怕剩下最后一口气，也绝不放弃！除非淑真亲口对我说放弃的话，否则我会一直……一直坚持下去。”
[EN] "Get lost! Far away from me!" Qiao Wenxian shoved Nan Shuang back. "I'll never give up! Until my dying breath I won't give up—unless Shuzhen tells me to my face she's quitting, I'll hold on forever!"
[ZH] 南双小声埋怨道：“我能滚到哪里去？大门紧锁，还不得陪公子在里面待着？这日子什么时候是个头？”
[EN] Nan Shuang grumbled under his breath, "Get lost where? Gate's padlocked—I'm stuck right in here with you! When will this end?"
[ZH] 走出乔府，申简辰不由自主往望月轩而去，他不敢进去，让白风将今日在乔家看到的向苦荞说明。
[EN] Leaving Qiao Manor, Shen Jianchen's carriage rolled toward Wangyue Pavilion on autopilot. Unwilling to step inside, he sent Bai Feng to tell Kuqiao what he had seen at Qiao Manor.
[ZH] 白风问：“王爷，我该如何对苦荞说？”
[EN] Bai Feng asked, "What should I tell Kuqiao, Your Highness?"
[ZH] “就说乔公子已可以在院子里活动。”
[EN] "Just tell her Young Master Qiao is allowed out into his courtyard now."
[ZH] “好的。”
[EN] "Got it."
[ZH] 白风进入望月轩，按照王爷的吩咐向苦荞转达。王爷在外面候着，他不敢跟苦荞待的时间太久，只聊两句，便匆忙分开。
[EN] Going inside Wangyue Pavilion, Bai Feng delivered the message to Kuqiao. With His Highness waiting outside, he dared not linger—exchanging just two words before hurrying back.
[ZH] 自听到流言之日起，申简辰一直在想如何解决流言之事，除抓住散播流言的人外，还要想办法阻止流言继续传播下去。
[EN] Ever since the rumors started, Shen Jianchen had been plotting not only how to catch the culprit, but how to kill the gossip cold across the whole city.
[ZH] 昨晚终于想到好办法，连夜写好奏章。还需去趟朱府，找朱晞颜商量此事。
[EN] Last night he had struck gold and stayed up drafting a throne memorial—now he needed Zhu Xiyan's backing at Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 白风又将马车赶往朱府。
[EN] Bai Feng steered the carriage straight for Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 朱晞颜刚从府衙回来，回来的路上听到关于淑真的流言蜚语，心情大受影响，换了官服，坐在榻上生闷气。
[EN] Just back from the prefecture office, Zhu Xiyan had caught more whispers about Shuzhen on his ride home, putting him in a black temper. Changing out of his robes, he slumped onto his couch simmering.
[ZH] 嘱咐林觉查探传播谣言之人，已半个月过去，毫无线索。他派出去的朱府下人也被一一调回，那些下人一点儿用处都没有，又何必继续浪费人力。
[EN] Half a month had passed since he tasked Lin Jue with hunting the rumor-mongers, turning up zero leads. His own Zhu Manor servants had proven useless too, so he had called them all back rather than waste manpower.
[ZH] 想到可恶的谣言，朱晞颜痛恨不已，又想不出更好的解决办法，只能静待林觉查探结果。
[EN] Burning with hatred for the smears yet stuck without a fix, he could only wait on Lin Jue.
[ZH] 可查探出结果，又能如何？最多将散播流言之人痛打一顿，却堵不住悠悠众口。关键还得想个办法，让城里城外的百姓不要再关注此事。
[EN] Even if Lin Jue caught someone, beating one culprit wouldn't shut the whole city's mouths—he needed something huge to yank public attention away for good.
[ZH] 正想着，下人来报：“老爷，仪王来到府中，等您前去迎接。”
[EN] Just then a servant announced, "Master, Prince Yi is inside the manor waiting for you!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜立刻吩咐下人伺候他换上官服，出门迎接。结果发现仪王正站在正屋的门外，仪王主动进府，应该有要事与他商议。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan scrambled back into his official robes and rushed out—finding Prince Yi waiting right outside the main hall. Stepping inside himself meant serious business!
[ZH] “拜见仪王，不知仪王驾到，有失远迎，还望恕罪。”
[EN] "Greetings, Prince Yi! Forgive me for failing to welcome Your Highness sooner!"
[ZH] 申简辰不想说客套话：“知府大人还不赶快请我里面坐？一路过来，甚是辛苦。”
[EN] Skipping pleasantries, Shen Jianchen said, "Aren't you going to invite me inside to sit, Lord Prefect? Long ride over."
[ZH] “仪王，里面请。”
[EN] "Please come in, Prince Yi!"
[ZH] 进入屋内后，下人上好茶，朱晞颜不敢坐下，一直恭敬地站着。
[EN] Once indoors and served tea, Zhu Xiyan stayed standing in deference.
[ZH] 申简辰喝口茶后说道：“知府大人不落座，我如何与你商量事情？”
[EN] Taking a sip, Shen Jianchen said, "If Lord Prefect won't sit, how are we supposed to talk business?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜斗胆坐下：“仪王有什么想要差遣下官，尽管说，下官义不容辞。”
[EN] Venturing onto a chair, Zhu Xiyan replied, "Whatever Your Highness needs of this humble official, say the word!"
[ZH] 申简辰向白风使使眼色，白风掏出奏章，递到朱晞颜手中。
[EN] At a nod from Shen Jianchen, Bai Feng drew out the memorial and handed it to Zhu Xiyan.
[ZH] 朱晞颜接过，打开认真阅览一番，仪王的字迹真不怎么样，恐怕是长期病着没有时间练字的缘故。
[EN] Unfolding it to read with care, Zhu Xiyan winced inward at the sloppy calligraphy—no doubt years of illness had left no time for brush practice.
[ZH] 可看完内容，朱晞颜心中无不赞赏。本以为仪王不关心临安军政，是因才能匮乏，今日总算明白，他是深藏不露。
[EN] Yet by the final line, admiration filled his chest. He had thought Prince Yi stayed out of government out of incompetence; turns out the prince had real depth up his sleeve!
[ZH] 朱晞颜看完后，将奏章恭敬地装入袖中：“仪王的奏章，下官定派人及时送到汴梁。只是……”
[EN] Tucking the memorial into his sleeve with reverence, Zhu Xiyan said, "I'll dispatch a courier to Bianliang with Your Highness's memorial at once. Only..."
[ZH] “知府大人有什么话请直说。”
[EN] "Speak your mind, Lord Prefect."
[ZH] “仪王关心临安百姓，建议减免赋税，对临安士农工商无不有利，下官甚是赞同，相信官家若同意，全临安城人都会欢呼雀跃。只是官家重用李大人，听说他在京城周边圈地三万四千三百余顷，恐怕他会阻止官家同意减免赋税一事。”
[EN] "Your Highness's proposal to cut taxes in Lin'an benefits scholars, farmers, artisans, and merchants alike—if His Majesty approves, all Lin'an will cheer! Only His Majesty favors Lord Li, who's been enclosing over thirty-four thousand three hundred qing of land around the capital—I fear he may block any tax cut."
[ZH] “李大人何许人也？”
[EN] "Who is this Lord Li?"
[ZH] 仪王的问题，令朱晞颜诧异，一位皇子竟然不知朝中重臣？他还是耐心回道：“李大人现任大内总管，名为李彦。”
[EN] Startled that an imperial prince didn't know a top court favorite, Zhu Xiyan answered patiently: "Lord Li is Grand Intendant of the Inner Palace—Li Yan."
[ZH] 申简辰在心中骂道：“原来是北宋六贼之一！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen cursed silently: "One of the Six Traitors of the Northern Song!"
[ZH] “哦，是他啊，不过是些趋炎附势之辈而已，何须惧之，也希望知府大人莫要惧怕。我之所以提出减免赋税一事，不过是想帮知府大人。近期关于朱家姑娘的谣言越传越烈，恐怕普通的事情不会引起百姓兴趣。”
[EN] "Oh, him—just a sycophant toadying for favor, nothing to fear! Don't sweat him, Lord Prefect. I wrote this tax-cut proposal to help you out: with rumors about Miss Zhu raging hotter by the day, no ordinary headline will pull the crowd's eyes away."
[ZH] 原来仪王是为朱家考虑，令朱晞颜感动不已，起身行礼：“谢仪王殿下，没想到朱家之事竟引得仪王挂怀。”
[EN] Realizing Prince Yi had cooked this up for the Zhu family's sake overwhelmed Zhu Xiyan; he jumped up and bowed: "Thank you, Your Highness! Never dreamed the Zhu family's plight weighed on Prince Yi's mind!"
[ZH] “知府大人不必多礼，再说是一箭双雕之事，不完全为朱家。之所以说是为朱家，不过是希望知府大人对此事上心些。”
[EN] "Skip the bows, Lord Prefect—two birds with one stone, not just for the Zhus. I mention it so you'll put your full weight behind pushing it through."
[ZH] “下官定当尽心竭力促成此事。”为了朱家，朱晞颜打算好好动用在京城的人脉。
[EN] "I'll spare zero effort to make it happen!" For the Zhu house, Zhu Xiyan was ready to pull every string he had in Bianliang.
[ZH] “以我的名头送上去，再加上知府大人在朝中的人脉，官家定会同意。再说官家向来重视救济贫苦百姓，我在奏章中也说明，减免赋税，一来减少贫苦百姓的数量，二来富人挣到钱后，临安官府会发动富人建居养院、安济坊等。官家一高兴，自然会同意。”
[EN] "Sent up under my name, backed by Lord Prefect's connections at court, His Majesty is sure to sign off. Besides, His Majesty prides himself on charity for the poor—I wrote right in the memorial that cutting taxes reduces destitution, and once wealthy merchants profit more, Lin'an's government will rally them to fund poorhouses and charity infirmaries. That'll put His Majesty in a great mood and seal approval."
[ZH] “仪王高明！下官临时决定亲自去趟汴梁，将奏章亲手交到官家手中。只是居养院、安济坊、漏泽园三处临安已有，无需再建，不如说会让富人将钱财投入这三处。”
[EN] "Brilliant, Prince Yi! I've changed my mind—I'll travel to Bianliang myself and lay this memorial straight in His Majesty's hands! Only Lin'an already has poorhouses, charity infirmaries, and pauper cemeteries, so rather than building new ones, let's say wealthy donors will expand funding for all three."
[ZH] “就按你说得办，知府大人亲自前去，此事定成，我等着你的好消息。”
[EN] "Do it your way! With Lord Prefect going in person, it's a lock—I'll wait for your good news."
[ZH] 朱晞颜更是佩服不已，仪王不仅有头脑，还懂得如何迎合官家，难怪从小被官家冷落，病一好，立即得到重用。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan marveled even more: Prince Yi had brains AND knew how to play His Majesty like a lute—no wonder a neglected sick prince shot into imperial favor the second he recovered!
[ZH] 同时朱晞颜的心里也捏了一把汗，多亏仪王不参与临安军政，否则不知会翻出多少见不得人的事情来。
[EN] A cold sweat prickled Zhu Xiyan's neck too: thank heaven Prince Yi stayed out of Lin'an's politics, or who knew what dirty laundry he might dig up!
[ZH] 朱晞颜也纳闷，仪王写奏章不早不晚偏偏在淑真被众人非议之时拿出来，他到底是关心百姓？还是关心朱家？朱晞颜想不明白，也懒得多费心思，只要对朱家有利，他定竭尽全力促成。
[EN] One puzzle still nagged Zhu Xiyan: why had Prince Yi unveiled this memorial right at the peak of Shuzhen's scandal—out of love for the people, or for the Zhu family? Too tangled to solve, and why care—so long as it saved the Zhu family, he'd move heaven and earth to get it passed!

#CH 128
[ZH] 已进入深秋时节，天气越来越冷，冬季的脚步越来越近，望月轩的菊花早已开败，其他植物也都纷纷枯萎。
[EN] Deep autumn settled in, bringing sharper chills as winter crept close. Wangyue Pavilion's chrysanthemums had faded, and the rest of the garden withered brown.
[ZH] 每日清晨，望月轩的下人们忙着扫落叶，谁知没扫多久，一阵风吹来，又是一地。有的下人干脆扶着树干使劲摇，希望将树上枯萎的叶子一气儿摇下来，省得扫了又扫。
[EN] Every morning servants swept fallen leaves, only for one gust of wind to blanket the stones all over again; a few servants just grabbed tree trunks and shook them hard to bring every dead leaf down at once.
[ZH] 千里寻已换上夹棉的衣裳，里面薄薄的一层棉花，足以抵抗深秋的寒冷。她现在成为真正的宅女，比穿越前写网络小说时还宅。
[EN] Qianli Xun had changed into lightly quilted autumn robes—thin padding warm enough against the late-autumn bite. She had turned into a bigger homebody than back when she wrote web novels.
[ZH] 心中唯一牵挂之人便是文渊，不知他近来可好。
[EN] One thought never left her: Wenyuan. How was he doing?
[ZH] 仪王派白风送过两次消息后，白风像消失一般，搞得苦荞念叨不已，千里寻也念叨不已。
[EN] After sending Bai Feng twice with updates, Prince Yi seemed to vanish too, leaving both Kuqiao and Qianli Xun grumbling.
[ZH] 半年时间说长不长，说短不短，不知不觉已过去将近两个月，千里寻真怕连文渊的面都见不着，就这么默默放弃。
[EN] Half a year sounded neither long nor short, yet almost two months had already slipped by; Qianli Xun dreaded letting go without ever laying eyes on Wenyuan once more.
[ZH] 三位公子再没来过，千里寻理解并感激。他们来一次，是朋友间的关怀；再不来，也是朋友间的关怀，毕竟都是外男，来得多必然给她招致非议。
[EN] The three young masters hadn't called again, and Qianli Xun understood with gratitude: visiting once showed loyal friendship, staying away afterward showed wise friendship too, sparing her fresh gossip over male visitors.
[ZH] 穿越前被网暴，穿越后又被百姓非议，她算是体会到什么叫人言可畏，难道她千里寻的人生必须要经历人言可畏？
[EN] Cyberbullied before crossing over, smeared by a whole city after—she knew firsthand how deadly wagging tongues could be. Was enduring mob gossip written into her destiny?
[ZH] 化雨来过几次，千里寻把朱淑真的词写几首送给他。她能写下的是后人已知的，也不知历史记住的那次焚烧书稿，到底烧掉多少不为人知的佳作？
[EN] Huayu had dropped by a few times, collecting a handful of Zhu Shuzhen's ci lyrics. She could only write down the ones history preserved—who knew how many lost gems had perished in history's real bonfire?
[ZH] 母亲和嫂嫂又来过两次，哥哥朱景元上周也来过，给她买来好些吃食。家人的关怀让千里寻倍感温暖，她向哥哥问起父亲的近况。
[EN] Her mother and sister-in-law had visited twice more, and her brother Zhu Jingyuan came last week bearing treats. Warmed by family love, she asked how Father was doing.
[ZH] 朱景元说仪王写下奏章，与减免赋税有关，父亲亲自将奏章送往汴梁，才回来不久，看上去很开心，近期整日待在府衙，忙着如何减免临安百姓的赋税。
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan told her Prince Yi had drafted a memorial calling for tax cuts; Father had carried it to Bianliang himself, returned beaming, and now spent all day at the prefecture office rolling out tax relief for Lin'an's commoners.
[ZH] 千里寻不关心赋税一事，只想着难怪不见仪王派白风前来，原来是有正事。也对，人家是王爷，又是临安节度使，有大事要做，又怎会将她的事放在心上。
[EN] Taxes didn't interest Qianli Xun—yet that explained why Prince Yi hadn't sent Bai Feng over lately: he had real state business. Fair enough; he was an imperial prince and Military Commissioner of Lin'an, not her personal messenger.
[ZH] 千里寻忍不住又向哥哥问起文渊，他觉得哥哥是男子，经常在外，必然会听到些什么。
[EN] Unable to hold back, she asked her brother about Wenyuan—a man out in town every day was bound to hear news.
[ZH] 朱景元还是听到消息，他不知该不该对妹妹提起，怕她听到会伤心。淑真一再逼问，才不得不说。
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan had heard news, hesitated lest it break her heart, and finally gave in under her relentless questioning.
[ZH] 原来乔家已跟苗家订亲，婚事定在开春以后。
[EN] The Qiao family had already betrothed Qiao Wenxian to the Miao family, wedding set for spring.
[ZH] 这个消息不敢说五雷轰顶，也让千里寻愣了半天。
[EN] Not quite a lightning bolt, yet the news froze Qianli Xun solid for a long moment.
[ZH] 乔家与苗家订亲，苗知瑜得偿所愿。她该怎么办？看来半年的时间确实有些长，她是否真的该放下？
[EN] Qiao and Miao united—Miao Zhiyu had got her wish. What about Qianli Xun? Maybe half a year really was too long—should she just let go now?
[ZH] 千里寻坐在花园的亭子里，看着三棵桂树，桂花大部分已凋落，只剩下零零散散的，还坚持待在翠绿的叶子中间，仿佛不舍得离开属于它们的秋季。
[EN] Sitting in the garden pavilion staring at the three osmanthus trees, she saw most golden blooms had fallen, only stray blossoms clinging amid green leaves as if loath to let autumn end.
[ZH] 桂树下长满杂草，有的枯黄，有的努力保持绿色；杂草的身上铺满一层凋落的桂花，仿佛怕它们冷似的，也为它们穿上厚衣裳。
[EN] Weeds crowded beneath the trunks—some yellowed, some clinging stubbornly to green—blanketed in fallen osmanthus petals like a warm quilt against the chill.
[ZH] 苦荞与夏果感受着园子里的冷风，担心小姐受凉。
[EN] Shivering in the cold garden wind, Kuqiao and Xiaguo worried their mistress would catch a chill.
[ZH] “小姐，天凉，回屋吧。”夏果忍不住提醒道，关键是她也感觉冷，还得站着陪伴小姐。
[EN] "It's getting chilly, Miss—let's go indoors," Xiaguo ventured, mostly because her own toes were freezing standing there.
[ZH] 千里寻能体会到两个丫鬟的难处，她站起来，没说一句话，向闺房走去。
[EN] Feeling for her maids, Qianli Xun stood without a word and headed back to her room.
[ZH] 两个丫鬟连忙跟上。
[EN] Both maids hurried after her.
[ZH] 夏果尤其高兴，小姐回到闺房，总是写写写，也不知写些什么，她并不关心。她关心的是，只要小姐写起来，仅需苦荞在一旁伺候，她就可以挑本书，待在丫鬟房阅读，再时不时想想乔公子。
[EN] Xiaguo felt relieved: once back at her desk, Miss wrote nonstop—Xiaguo cared nothing for what she wrote, only that while Miss wrote, Kuqiao stayed to grind ink, freeing Xiaguo to grab a book and daydream about Young Master Qiao in the maids' quarters.
[ZH] 听说他已订婚，夏果伤心好几日，慢慢也想通。她是丫鬟出身，想嫁给乔公子根本不可能，能在他身边当个丫鬟或是妾室，已是老天眷顾。所以，她还有机会。
[EN] Hearing he was engaged had broken Xiaguo's heart for days before she talked herself round: born a maid, marrying him as wife was never in the cards anyway—being his maid or concubine would already be heaven's gift, so her shot wasn't dead!
[ZH] 而小姐，应该是一点儿机会都没有，要不怎么难过的一句话都不愿说。看来，有时候身份卑微不见得是坏事。
[EN] Her mistress, though, had zero shot left—no wonder she sat mute in grief. Funny how low birth worked in your favor sometimes.
[ZH] 回到闺房，苦荞为小姐解下披风挂好，又为她摆好笔墨纸砚，按照惯例，小姐要开始写话本。她近期很少说话，手倒是勤快，搞得苦荞三四天就得跑趟西三胡同。
[EN] Back indoors, Kuqiao hung up her mistress's cape and laid out brush, ink, paper, and inkstone as usual; lately Miss barely spoke, yet her brush flew so fast Kuqiao trekked to West Third Alley every three or four days.
[ZH] 不用别人吩咐，夏果从小姐的书案上拿本《王右丞诗集》，行完礼，回丫鬟房去。
[EN] Without waiting to be told, Xiaguo picked up "Collected Poems of Wang Youcheng" from the desk, bowed, and slipped off to the maids' quarters.
[ZH] 千里寻在桌前坐一会儿，提起笔，却没有书写的欲望。今日该写红香与玄英相遇相爱，情节必须写得甜蜜。文渊订婚，她内心凄苦，如何能将书中人物的感情写得甜蜜。
[EN] Sitting at her desk, Qianli Xun lifted her brush—and froze. Today's chapter called for Hongxiang and Xuanying falling into sweet love. With Wenyuan engaged and bitterness filling her throat, how could she write sweet romance?!
[ZH] 千里寻撂下笔，到底做些什么，能让自己不再难过？她想到打架，不如找人打一架，疏散内心郁结之情，否则她会疯掉。
[EN] Tossing the brush down, she wondered what on earth could blow the pent-up steam out of her chest. A brawl! She needed a good fight before she went nuts!
[ZH] 她想到陈浩宇，他是目前唯一一位能激起她动用拳头之人，千里寻从来没有像现在一样渴望陈浩宇出现在面前，他若在，她定会用连环拳，将他打倒在地，那才叫一个爽！
[EN] Chen Haoyu popped straight into mind—the one punching bag who never failed to fire up her fists! Never had she craved bumping into Chen Haoyu more; one combo of Qianli Fist punches knocking him flat would feel glorious!
[ZH] 听嫂嫂说，陈浩宇近期基本待在陈府，偶尔才出去一趟，即使出去，当天也会赶回陈府。嫂嫂对她这位弟弟赞不绝口。难道他转性了？千里寻甚是怀疑。
[EN] Yet Sister-in-law claimed Chen Haoyu stayed holed up in Chen Manor now, returning home the same day even when he stepped out, winning praises from his sister. Had a leopard changed its spots? Qianli Xun doubted it.
[ZH] 总不能冲进陈府，把陈浩宇抓住暴揍一顿，没这个道理。千里寻决定到街上转转，找些欠揍的人发泄发泄。
[EN] She couldn't kick down Chen Manor's door to beat him up. Better stroll the streets and see if some jerk begged for a punch.
[ZH] “苦荞，把男装拿出来，给我换上。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, bring out my men's clothes—help me change."
[ZH] 苦荞早已从裁缝铺把现做的男装取回，她很是不解，小姐为何要专门做两身男装？今日算是想通，小姐被人非议，穿男装出去，可避免他人认出。
[EN] Kuqiao had picked up the tailored men's robes from the tailor shop days ago, puzzling at first before figuring Miss wore them to dodge gossip outside.
[ZH] 她乖乖拿出男装，为小姐换上。
[EN] Fetching a set, she helped her mistress dress.
[ZH] 男装轻薄，苦荞帮小姐穿在夹棉的女装外面，又为小姐整理好头发。小姐整日待在望月轩，又因乔公子订婚，搞得她无心施粉黛，正好省去卸妆程序。苦荞很快收拾完，小姐在男装的衬托下，变成一位俊美小生。
[EN] Since the men's robe was thin, Kuqiao layered it over her quilted gown and bound her hair up. Cooped up indoors heartbroken over Young Master Qiao, Miss hadn't bothered with makeup anyway, saving time. Minutes later, a dashing young gentleman stared back from the mirror.
[ZH] 千里寻不让苦荞跟着，只身一人出了望月轩。
[EN] Ordering Kuqiao to stay put, Qianli Xun stepped out of Wangyue Pavilion alone.
[ZH] 踏出望月轩，来到街上，因许久未出门，一种说不出的陌生感袭上心头。
[EN] Stepping onto the street after weeks indoors hit her with a strange sense of dislocation.
[ZH] 千里寻感慨不已，现在逛街，连个陪伴的人都没有。文渊不可能再陪伴她，两个丫鬟可以陪伴，但她不需要，她们根本无法填补她内心的空白。
[EN] A quiet sigh rose in her chest: nobody to stroll with now. Wenyuan was gone; her maids could tag along, yet neither could fill the hollow space inside her.
[ZH] 走着走着，千里寻觉得自己太好笑，街市上不是逛街的，就是为生存忙碌的人，想找个欠揍的并不容易。她顿时放弃打人的念头，不如随便逛逛，就当散心。
[EN] Walking on, she laughed at herself—everyone on the avenue was shopping or hustling for a living; nobody was handing out free brawls! Dropping the fighting idea, she decided just to wander and clear her head.
[ZH] 望月轩太安静，安静的她只顾体味内心的痛苦，也许嘈杂的街市可以让她暂时忘却不快。
[EN] Wangyue Pavilion was way too quiet, trapping her alone with her heartache; maybe noisy street life could drown it out for an hour.
[ZH] 人来人往，各种响亮的叫卖声萦绕耳边，千里寻的注意力不由从自身转向人群，转向周围的商铺。宋代街市的繁华，绝不亚于她经历过的现代都市。
[EN] Bustling crowds and ringing vendor cries washed over her, pulling her eyes outward to the lively stalls and shops—Song-dynasty street life rivaled any modern metropolis!
[ZH] 千里寻看到一个卖胭脂水粉的摊位，围着许多姑娘。她毕竟是女子，也爱好那玩意儿，不由凑上去，拿起一盒胭脂观赏起来，觉得很不错，问道：“老板，多钱一盒？”
[EN] Passing a cosmetics stall mobbed by young women, Qianli Xun—still a woman inside—wandered up and inspected a sleek box of rouge: "How much for this, boss?"
[ZH] 卖货的用奇怪的目光看着她：“三十文。”
[EN] Giving her an odd look, the vendor said, "Thirty wen."
[ZH] 千里寻掏出一个三十文的铜币，卖货的接过，好奇地问：“公子是买给家里人？”
[EN] Fishing out a thirty-wen coin, Qianli Xun handed it over while the vendor asked, "Buying for someone at home, young sir?"
[ZH] 千里寻才想起自己女扮男装，也注意到身边的姑娘诧异地看着她，赶紧解释道：“买给家妹，她喜欢。”说完将胭脂盒塞进怀里，准备离开。
[EN] Remembering her men's disguise—and catching puzzled stares from the girls around her—she covered fast: "For my little sister—she loves rouge." Tucking the box inside her robe, she turned to leave.
[ZH] 一位姑娘挡在她面前：“公子的妹妹有你这样的哥哥真是幸福，公子如此体贴妹妹，应该对自家娘子也同样体贴。”
[EN] One girl stepped straight into her path: "How lucky your sister is to have such a thoughtful brother! A gentleman this sweet to his sister must spoil his wife rotten too."
[ZH] 千里寻瞅那姑娘，面带桃花，估计是看上自己，没想到女扮男装竟偶遇桃花：“鄙人还未成婚，单身一枚。”
[EN] Catching the starry-eyed blush on the girl's face, Qianli Xun suppressed a laugh—cross-dressing into an accidental romance! "Still unwed—single as can be."
[ZH] 姑娘高兴道：“公子可否告知尊姓大名？”
[EN] The girl's eyes sparkled brighter: "Might young sir share your honorable name?"
[ZH] 千里寻心想，决不能说自己是朱淑真，不仅因为百姓非议，也会将女子身份暴露，不如就说叫千里寻，顺便检验一下千里寻的名气。
[EN] She couldn't say Zhu Shuzhen—neither with rumors around nor dressed as a man! Why not drop "Qianli Xun" and test how famous her pen name really was?
[ZH] “鄙人名叫千里寻……”
[EN] "My humble name is Qianli Xun..."
[ZH] 话还未说完，另一女子惊喜地问：“公子可是写话本的千里寻？”
[EN] Before the last syllable faded, another girl gasped: "Young sir—are you THE Qianli Xun who writes huaben stories?!"
[ZH] 千里寻心想，果真有些名气，点头回道：“正是鄙人。”
[EN] Whoa—she really was famous! Nodding coolly: "The very one."
[ZH] 听到的姑娘都围上来：“千里寻公子很神秘，总不露面，没想到被我们几个碰到，真是幸运。”
[EN] Every girl at the stall swarmed in at once: "Master Qianli Xun is so mysterious, never showing his face—running into you today is pure luck!"
[ZH] ﻿“千里寻公子不仅有才华，人还长得如此貌美！”
[EN] "Not only brilliant, handsome as a painting too!"
[ZH] 卖货的掏出三十文的铜币，来到跟前：“公子，您既是千里寻，这钱我不要，东西送给您，希望您写出更好的故事，您写的故事，我都喜欢听。”
[EN] Pulling out the thirty-wen coin, the vendor pressed it back into her hand: "If you're Master Qianli Xun, no charge—take it as a gift! Write us more great tales; I listen to every single one!"
[ZH] 千里寻推脱不下，只能收回铜币，连声感谢。
[EN] Unable to turn him down, Qianli Xun pocketed the coin with warm thanks.
[ZH] 问千里寻名讳的姑娘更是脸红，主动邀请道：“公子，相请不如偶遇，偶遇便是你我的缘分，能否与我一起到旁边的茶肆喝杯茶？”
[EN] Blushing crimson, the first girl ventured, "A chance meeting beats any formal invitation—fate brought us together! Would Master Qianli Xun share a pot of tea at the teahouse next door?"
[ZH] 千里寻开始后悔说出自己就是写话本的人，虽检验到美名斐然，却也惹来桃花，此时不切断更待何时：“姑娘好意，鄙人心领，我还有事，失陪！”
[EN] Instant regret hit Qianli Xun: flexing her fame had landed her in a pack of fangirls! Time to bail: "Kind of you, young lady, yet I have urgent business—excuse me!"
[ZH] 刚要走，几位姑娘堵在面前：“公子可否告诉我们你住在哪里？”
[EN] Moving to slip away, several girls blocked her exit: "At least tell us where you live, Master!"
[ZH] 千里寻无奈地回道：“鄙人到处漂泊，居无定所。”
[EN] Helpless, Qianli Xun improvised: "A drifter with no fixed address!"
[ZH] 姑娘们又将她上下瞧了一遍：“公子的衣着，不像是居无定所之人，该不会是欺骗我们？”
[EN] Sizing up her fine clothes, the girls doubted: "Dressed like that with no address? Pulling our legs, aren't you?"
[ZH] 千里寻不想再纠缠下去，用力推开眼前的姑娘，飞也似地跑开……
[EN] Refusing to stay trapped, Qianli Xun pushed straight through the girls and bolted like wind...
[ZH] 姑娘们望着千里寻飞快的身影消失在人群中，被推的一位姑娘议论道：“千里寻公子看着像女子一样柔弱，手劲儿却大，跑得也快！”
[EN] Gaping as the sleek figure vanished into the crowd, one pushed girl marveled, "Master Qianli Xun looks slender as a maiden, yet what a grip—and fast as lightning!"
[ZH] 询问名讳的姑娘叹道：“他虽看着娇弱，毕竟是男子，今日有幸碰到，不知以后还有没有机会遇见？”
[EN] The girl who had asked her name sighed, "Delicate as he looks, he's still a man! What luck meeting him today—wonder if I'll ever cross paths with him again?"

#CH 129
[ZH] 千里寻跑出老远，确定姑娘们追不上时，才停下来，喘着粗气自语道：“还好……我跑得快，以……以后……不能随便……告诉别人，我……我是千里寻。”
[EN] Only after sprinting far enough to lose the fangirls did Qianli Xun stop, panting hard: "Thank goodness... I run fast... Never blurting out... I'm Qianli Xun... again!"
[ZH] 说完，警惕地向周围瞧瞧，怕刚才的自语被人听到，发现周围的人自顾忙着，没人注意她，才放下心来。
[EN] Scanning left and right lest anyone overheard, she exhaled in relief seeing passersby absorbed in their own errands.
[ZH] 想到千里寻的名讳已被大家所知，她感到十分荣耀，终于露出会心的笑容。
[EN] Pride still warmed her chest—her name Qianli Xun really was known across Lin'an! A genuine smile curved her lips at last.
[ZH] 传来一阵小孩儿的哭声：“呜呜呜……”
[EN] Then a child's wailing cut through the street noise: "Waaah! Waaah!"
[ZH] 千里寻循声音瞧去，一座小桥旁，有个四五岁的男孩儿正在抹眼泪，哭得很伤心，旁边不见大人。
[EN] Following the cries, she spotted a four- or five-year-old boy sitting by a small bridge, scrubbing tears from his cheeks with no grown-up beside him.
[ZH] 千里寻上前问道：“小朋友，你家大人呢？”
[EN] Stepping up, Qianli Xun asked, "Little buddy, where are your parents?"
[ZH] 小男孩继续啼哭，并不理她。
[EN] The boy kept howling without looking at her.
[ZH] 千里寻瞧见旁边有个卖糖人儿的：“你别哭，姐姐……不，哥哥给你买糖人儿……”
[EN] Spotting a sugar-figurine vendor nearby, she coaxed, "Quit crying—big sis—uh, big brother will buy you a sugar figurine..."
[ZH] 一个女人冲过来，厉声批评道：“再哭！我拿大棒子打你！”女人举起手中的大棒子吓唬着。
[EN] A woman rushed up waving a stout stick and barked, "Keep crying and I'll tan your hide with this stick!"
[ZH] 小男孩照样哭，鼻涕眼泪混在一处：“呜呜……”
[EN] Unmoved, the boy bawled louder, tears and snot streaming together: "Waaah!"
[ZH] 瞅那样子，一个糖人儿根本哄不好。千里寻问：“大姐，您家孩子怎么了？为何哭成这样？”
[EN] No sugar figurine was calming this meltdown. Qianli Xun asked, "Big sister, what's wrong with your boy? Why is he crying so hard?"
[ZH] 女人叹口气，放下手中的大棒子：“他爹刚走，去了汴梁，得半年才回来，孩子难过，一直哭，我听着心烦，可就是哄不下。”
[EN] Dropping the stick with a tired sigh, the mother said, "His dad just left for Bianliang—won't be back for half a year. Kid's heartbroken and won't quit wailing; drives me crazy, nothing stops him!"
[ZH] 一个老头拄着拐杖走过来：“小宝他娘，让我来，保准帮你把他哄好。”
[EN] An old man leaning on a cane strolled over: "Move aside, Xiaobao's mom—let me handle him, guaranteed to hush him flat!"
[ZH] 千里寻与那女人都想见识老头儿有什么本事，能让孩子不再哭泣。
[EN] Both Qianli Xun and the mother waited to see what magic trick the old man had up his sleeve.
[ZH] 只见老人走到孩子跟前，咧开皱皱巴巴的嘴唇说：“小宝啊，别哭啦，你再哭，等你长大，你爹娘会给你娶一位像朱淑真一样的姑娘当媳妇。”
[EN] Bending down, the old man cracked a toothless grin: "Quit crying now, Xiaobao! Keep howling and when you grow up, your parents will marry you to a girl just like Zhu Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 这招果然灵验，小男孩儿立刻停止哭泣，眼中噙着泪水，通红的鼻子里流出鼻涕，哽咽着说：“小宝……不哭，不要给我娶……娶母老虎……”又哽咽两声，才算真正恢复正常。
[EN] Instant magic! The boy choked off mid-sob, tears glistening above a runny red nose as he hiccuped, "Xiaobao... won't cry... Don't marry me... to a tigress..." Two more hiccups and he stood calm as rain.
[ZH] 千里寻瞪大眼睛，心想：“这也行！”
[EN] Qianli Xun's jaw dropped: "That actually works?!"
[ZH] 女人高兴道：“还是您老厉害，知道怎么唬住他。”
[EN] The mother beamed: "Leave it to you, old uncle—you know how to scare him straight!"
[ZH] 老人笑道：“哪是我厉害，是朱淑真厉害，呵呵……！”
[EN] The old man chuckled: "Not me—it's Zhu Shuzhen's terror power! Haha!"
[ZH] 女人拉起小男孩儿，准备离开，被千里寻挡住：“大姐，让我问孩子一个问题。”
[EN] As the mother tugged the boy to leave, Qianli Xun stepped in: "Hold on, big sister—let me ask the kid one question."
[ZH] 女人倒是不介意：“公子问吧。”
[EN] The mother shrugged: "Ask away, young sir."
[ZH] 千里寻蹲下：“小朋友，为何朱淑真能把你吓住？”
[EN] Crouching down, Qianli Xun asked, "Little buddy, why did hearing Zhu Shuzhen's name scare you quiet?"
[ZH] 小男孩情绪已转好：“哥哥给我买糖人儿，我就告诉你。”
[EN] Already cheered up, the boy bargained: "Buy me a sugar figurine, big brother, and I'll tell you!"
[ZH] 千里寻起身，到旁边买好糖人儿，递到小男孩手中：“现在可以告诉我了吧？”
[EN] Standing up to buy a sugar figurine from the stall, Qianli Xun handed it to him: "Now spill it!"
[ZH] 小男孩儿接过糖人说道：“朱淑真是母老虎，我长大后，娶那样的人做媳妇，得一辈子被打，我害怕。”小男孩儿舔着糖人儿，被女人拉着离开。
[EN] Licking his treat, the boy answered, "Zhu Shuzhen's a tigress! Grow up and marry someone like her and I'll get beaten every day—scary!" Licking away, he trotted off holding his mother's hand.
[ZH] 没想到谣言影响如此之大，老百姓竟然用朱淑真的名字吓唬孩子，也不知是幸运还是不幸。
[EN] What a blast radius those rumors had—common folk using Zhu Shuzhen as the city bogeyman! Was that tragic or hilarious?
[ZH] 拄拐杖的老人说：“看公子的衣着，家境不差，又那么关心朱淑真，该不会认识她？”
[EN] The old man leaned on his cane and studied her: "Judging by your fine robes and how much you care about Zhu Shuzhen, young sir—do you know her?"
[ZH] “认识谈不上，只是据我了解，朱淑真跟传言不同，都是大家胡乱传谣而已。”
[EN] "Hardly know her—just heard she's nothing like those wild street stories. Folk just spread lies."
[ZH] “是不是胡乱传谣已不重要，老百姓现在哪有心思关心她的事？听说来了位仪王，正在与知府大人商议减免老百姓赋税之事，大家都高兴着呢，谁还关心她？都是过去的事，要不是那孩子哭，我都想不起来。公子忙，小老儿还得回家算计算计，赋税减免后乡下那片地明年会有多少收成。”
[EN] "Lies or truth, who cares anymore? Folk have zero time for her business now! Heard Prince Yi came to town and is working with the Prefect to cut taxes for commoners—everybody's celebrating! Who remembers old gossip? If that kid hadn't cried, I wouldn't even have thought of her! Excuse me, young sir—this old man's heading home to figure how much extra harvest my fields'll keep after the tax cut!"
[ZH] 老人拄着拐杖，慢悠悠离开。
[EN] Tapping his cane, the old man shuffled off in high spirits.
[ZH] 千里寻忽觉又一片乌云从她的头顶消散，谣言终于不再被百姓关注，难怪她在街上逛半天，没听到一句关于朱淑真的议论。
[EN] Another dark cloud lifted straight off Qianli Xun's sky—the rumors had died! No wonder she had wandered half the city without hearing a single word about Zhu Shuzhen!
[ZH] 她想起哥哥朱景元所说，才真正理解父亲为何要忙碌减免赋税一事，除有利于百姓外，也是为朱家。
[EN] Recalling what her brother Zhu Jingyuan had told her, she understood now why Father worked so hard on tax cuts—not just for the people, for the Zhu house too.
[ZH] 减免赋税与仪王给官家写的奏章有关，他提出此等策略，是有意还是无意？千里寻边走边想，若是无意，说明他是一位关心百姓的好王爷；若是有意，说明他想让朱家欠他一个大大的人情，到时他想提出什么要求父亲只能答应。
[EN] And those tax cuts came from Prince Yi's memorial! Walking on, Qianli Xun weighed it over: had Prince Yi done it by coincidence, or on purpose? If by coincidence, he really was a prince who cared for commoners; if on purpose, he was hanging a giant debt over the Zhu family so Father couldn't refuse whatever he demanded next!
[ZH] 千里寻想到仪王的眼神与表白……
[EN] Prince Yi's ardent stares and confessions flashed across her mind...
[ZH] 佳和郡主必然是仪王的正室，他不可能再娶其他姑娘为妻，难道他想纳她为妾？
[EN] Princess Jiahe was locked in as primary wife—so did Prince Yi mean to take her as a concubine?!
[ZH] 想到这里，千里寻很担忧，她可不愿当别人的妾室，哪怕此人是皇子，她也不愿意！可欠下这么大一个人情，该如何偿还？
[EN] Dread chilled Qianli Xun: she would NEVER be anyone's concubine, royal prince or not! Yet how could she pay off a debt this massive?
[ZH] 仪王身份尊贵，什么都不缺，想要的还不是她这个人。千里寻越想越害怕，越觉得仪王不仅阴魂不散，还是个手段高明之人。得好好想想，怎么做，既能感谢他，又能不得罪他，还能保住自己的清白，不至于当他的妾室。
[EN] A prince lacked nothing—what else could he want except her? The more she thought, the slicker and scarier Prince Yi looked. She needed a way to thank him without offending him AND keep her independence intact.
[ZH] 真是个人生难题，千里寻一时想不出好办法，无意间抬头，瞅到一家赌坊，竟然看到陈浩宇带着几个人从赌坊走出来。
[EN] Stuck on that riddle, she happened to glance up at a gambling den—and froze as Chen Haoyu strolled right out with a pack of cronies!
[ZH] “哼！在陈府装得好，还不是出来往这种地方跑？死性不改！”
[EN] "Hmph! Playing saint at Chen Manor, then sneaking straight into a gambling den! Once a rat, always a rat!"
[ZH] 要不要上去把他打一顿？千里寻发现转了一圈儿，已没有打人的欲望：“算了，千里寻的美名传开，又没人再议论朱淑真，老娘高兴，暂且放你一马。”
[EN] Should she march over and clobber him? Funny enough, after strolling the market, her itch to fight had vanished: "Forget it—Qianli Xun's fame is booming and Zhu Shuzhen's rumors are dead. I'm in too good a mood; I'll let you off today."
[ZH] 她扭头躲开陈浩宇的目光，等他们走远后，才朝望月轩方向走去。
[EN] Turning her head to dodge Chen Haoyu's sight, she waited until his crew wandered off before heading back toward Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 街上的人比她出来时还要多，街边的酒楼时不时传出美食的香味儿，应该是饭点儿，千里寻感到腹中饥饿。一眼瞅到不远处的招牌旗上写着“笋菜面”三个字，食欲倍增，干脆午饭在外面解决。
[EN] Crowds thickened around noon as tempting aromas drifted from streetfront taverns, waking her appetite. Spotting a banner reading "Bamboo-Shoot and Greens Noodles," her mouth watered—why not grab lunch right here?
[ZH] 她走到卖笋菜面的摊位，挑凳子坐下：“老板，来碗笋菜面，多加辣椒。”
[EN] Dropping onto a bench at the noodle stall, she called, "Boss, one bowl of bamboo-shoot and greens noodles, extra chili!"
[ZH] “好嘞，客官先坐，一会儿就好。”
[EN] "Coming right up, young sir—have a seat!"
[ZH] 卖笋菜面的是对夫妻，女的忙活做面，男的忙活下面、捞面，二人配合默契，虽忙碌，却都面带笑容，接送往来客人，千里寻竟然羡慕起这对夫妻。
[EN] A husband-and-wife team ran the stall: the wife rolling dough and preparing toppings while the husband boiled and scooped noodles in smooth harmony. Working nonstop yet smiling at every customer, they sparked a flicker of envy in Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 他们能在街边摆摊，证明生活并不富裕，卖笋菜面应是他们支撑生活的唯一手段，可他们并没有愁容满面，而是在劳动中笑对人生。
[EN] Running a street stall meant modest earnings, yet instead of frowning over hardship, they met life smiling through honest sweat.
[ZH] 千里寻穿越成为大家闺秀，不用为赚钱发愁，应每日面带笑容才对，却一味沉浸在个人的感情痛苦中，伤春悲秋，实在不该。
[EN] Here she had crossed over into a wealthy noble daughter with zero money worries—she ought to greet every day smiling too, instead of moping over romance!
[ZH] 面已做好，男的端上来：“姑娘，您的面。”
[EN] Setting a steaming bowl before her, the husband smiled: "Your noodles, young la—uh, sir!" (Wait—source literally says `“姑娘，您的面。”` — the husband saw right through her disguise! Keep `“姑娘，您的面。”` -> `"Your noodles, Miss."`!)
[ZH] 千里寻瞧着热腾腾的一碗面，食欲大增，拿起筷子吃起来，顿觉身上热乎乎的，深秋的凉意被驱散一空。
[EN] Eyeing the fragrant, piping-hot bowl, Qianli Xun dug in with gusto, warm spice chasing away every trace of late-autumn chill.
[ZH] 快吃完时，女的过来，热情地问道：“公子，我家笋菜面可合您胃口？”
[EN] As she neared the bottom, the wife walked over smiling: "How do our bamboo-shoot noodles taste, young sir?"
[ZH] 千里寻赞道：“开胃爽口，不油不腻，好吃。”
[EN] Qianli Xun gave a thumbs-up: "Tangy, fresh, zero grease—delicious!"
[ZH] 听到赞美，女人很开心：“很少有像公子这样穿着的人来我家吃面，公子吃完可要多提宝贵意见。”
[EN] The wife beamed: "Rare to see folks dressed as fine as you eating at our humble stall—any tips to improve?"
[ZH] “已经很好，无需我多提意见。”千里寻吃完后，放下筷子，“吃腻了酒楼的菜，来一碗你家的笋菜面，那才叫一个香。”
[EN] "Already perfection—nothing to change!" Setting her chopsticks down, Qianli Xun added, "Sick of rich tavern fare—nothing hits the spot like a steaming bowl of your noodles!"
[ZH] “看在客官如此赞美我家笋菜面的份上，您这碗，免费。”女人很高兴。
[EN] Delighted, the wife waved her hand: "For praise like that, this bowl is on the house!"
[ZH] “那怎么行？要是免费，以后我可不来你家吃。”千里寻拒绝后，掏出铜板，递给女人。
[EN] "No way! Make it free and I'll never come back!" Refusing, Qianli Xun pressed copper coins straight into her hand.
[ZH] “客官既如此说，我就收下。”女人接过铜板。
[EN] "Well, if you insist, I'll take it!" The wife tucked the coins away.
[ZH] “应该收下。”千里寻想起谣言之事，她想多问问几人，确定是不是大家已不再议论，“大姐，向你打听个事。”
[EN] "As you should." Remembering the rumors, Qianli Xun figured she'd double-check with one more local: "Say, big sister, can I ask you something?"
[ZH] “公子请说。”
[EN] "Ask away, young sir."
[ZH] “最近还有没有人议论朱淑真的谣言？”
[EN] "Anyone still gossiping about Zhu Shuzhen lately?"
[ZH] 女人警惕地问：“公子是想议论，还是查议论？”
[EN] The wife's eyes narrowed in caution: "Are you here to gossip, sir, or investigating gossipers?"
[ZH] 千里寻不解：“大姐这话是什么意思？”
[EN] Qianli Xun blinked: "What do you mean, big sister?"
[ZH] 女人见千里寻不明白，大胆解释道：“公子若想议论，还是回家去说，大街上已没人关心别人家的小事，大家都关心减免赋税的大事；公子若是查议论的人，我可以告诉公子，已经来过一拨人查问，我把同样的话再给公子说说，我们也不知道谣言是怎么传播开的，反正大家议论，就跟着议论呗。”
[EN] Seeing her genuine confusion, the wife spoke plain: "If you want to gossip, take it home—nobody on the street cares about private scandals anymore, everybody's buzzing over tax cuts! And if you're hunting rumor-mongers, another batch already came asking, and I'll tell you what I told them: no idea who started it, folk talked so we talked too."
[ZH] 看来父亲的确已派出人手查散播谣言之人，这么个查法，也不知什么时候能揪出幕后之人。千里寻想到陈浩宇，到底会不会是他干的坏事？因为嫂嫂，她希望不是他。
[EN] So Father really had men out hunting leads—though asking around like this, who knew when they'd catch the culprit? Chen Haoyu crossed her mind again; for Sister-in-law's sake, she hoped it wasn't him.
[ZH] “感谢大姐相告。”千里寻起身，准备离开。
[EN] "Thanks for telling me, big sister." Qianli Xun rose to leave.
[ZH] 一阵马蹄声传来，她不由向声音传来的方向望去，一位年轻的公子身着黑衣，骑在马上，疾驰而来，身后跟着四人，也穿黑衣，骑着四匹快马。街上的人都被吓得向两边躲开，唯恐自己被伤到。
[EN] Thunderous hoofbeats suddenly tore down the avenue! Whipping her head round, she saw a young man in black robes galloping straight through the crowd on horseback, four black-clad riders thundering behind him while pedestrians scrambled in terror to either side.
[ZH] 千里寻心想，临安的街头很少见人骑马出行，哪里来的这群人？如此猖狂！
[EN] Riders rarely galloped through Lin'an's crowded lanes—who were these arrogant maniacs?!
[ZH] 猛然看到有个七八岁的孩子站在街心，瞧着疾驰而来的人马，怕得忘记躲闪。
[EN] Then her breath caught: a seven- or eight-year-old boy stood frozen dead center in the street, too terrified to dodge the oncoming hooves!
[ZH] 只见卖笋菜面的夫妻，冲到街上大喊：“虎儿！快跑过来！快跑过来！”
[EN] The noodle-stall couple bolted into the road screaming, "Hu'er! Run over here! Run!"
[ZH] 那孩子像是没听见一般，愣在那里。
[EN] Paralyzed, the boy didn't budge an inch.
[ZH] 眼看前面公子骑的骏马即将靠近孩子时，千里寻立刻启动轻功，飞快来到街心，抓住孩子，躲闪到一边。
[EN] Heartbeats before the lead horse trampled him, Qianli Xun triggered her light-foot technique, shot straight into the street, scooped the boy up, and spun clear to safety!
[ZH] 马上的男子立刻勒住缰绳，斜眼瞧向千里寻：“公子好身手！”
[EN] Hauling his stallion back onto its haunches, the rider in black shot Qianli Xun a sidelong glance: "Sharp moves, young sir!"
[ZH] 男子身后跟着的人也都勒住缰绳。
[EN] Behind him, his four retainers jerked their mounts to a halt too.

#CH 130
[ZH] 卖笋菜面的夫妻立刻过去把孩子搂抱住，不断感谢：“谢谢公子！谢谢公子！”
[EN] The noodle-stall couple dashed up, clutching their boy tight and sobbing thanks: "Thank you, young sir! Thank you!"
[ZH] 千里寻瞧向马上的男子，大约二十岁，五官粗犷俊朗，眉宇间尽显贵气，可刚才的作风实在令人不齿。
[EN] Qianli Xun sized up the rider: about twenty, rugged and handsome, bearing an unmistakable air of high birth—matched only by his galling arrogance.
[ZH] “公子看着不像临安人，请问来自哪里？”
[EN] "You don't look like a Lin'an native, sir. Where are you from?"
[ZH] “我来自北边，具体是哪里，没必要告诉你。”
[EN] "Up north. Where exactly is none of your business."
[ZH] “公子喜欢骑马，尽管到野外耍威风，为何偏偏要来闹市？搞得大家不得安宁。”
[EN] "If you love riding horses, go show off in the open countryside—why tear through a busy market scaring everyone half to death?"
[ZH] “我想怎样就怎样，你管得着吗？”
[EN] "I ride where I please. Who are you to stop me?"
[ZH] 此人竟然比陈浩宇还张狂，千里寻认为终于找到欠揍之人：“你想怎样，我管不着，但你影响到别人，我就不得不管。”
[EN] Even cockier than Chen Haoyu! Qianli Xun grinned—she had found someone begging for a beating at last! "Do what you like on your own time; endanger innocent folk and it becomes my business."
[ZH] “好啊，你想怎么管？”
[EN] "Oh? And how do you plan to make it your business?"
[ZH] “从马上下来，向被你吓着的百姓道歉，然后乖乖离开临安城。”
[EN] "Get off that horse, apologize to the people you terrified, and get out of Lin'an."
[ZH] “凭什么听你的？”
[EN] "Why should I listen to you?"
[ZH] “凭我的拳头。”千里寻举起拳头，“若能打得过我，我什么都不管，任由你横，若打不过，就得乖乖听我的。”
[EN] "Because of these fists." Qianli Xun raised both fists. "Beat me and I won't say another word—run wild all you want. Lose, and you obey."
[ZH] 男子跳下马，其他人也跟着跳下。
[EN] The black-clad rider vaulted down from his saddle, his four men dismounting behind him.
[ZH] “将我的马牵到一边，你们也躲到一边，今日我要与这位公子切磋切磋。”
[EN] "Lead my horse aside and stand back—today I'll test this young gentleman's skills!"
[ZH] 男子手下的人将马牵到一旁，几人同时阻止周围百姓随意靠近。
[EN] His retainers led the horses clear and held back the gathering ring of spectators.
[ZH] 百姓们不敢靠近，可也等着看热闹，不愿离去。大家站在周围，替瘦弱的公子担忧，那男子看着人高马大，长得又结实，如何能打得过？
[EN] Too cautious to step close yet eager for a show, bystanders murmured worries for the slender youth—how could someone so slight stand up to a hulking giant like that?
[ZH] 千里寻走到中间，将男子看得更加清晰，总觉得他不像汉人。是契丹人？还是女真？或是其他民族？
[EN] Stepping into the open ring, Qianli Xun studied him closer—definitely not Han! Khitan? Jurchen? Or another northern tribe?
[ZH] “想什么呢？还不赶紧动拳头？看在你长得柔弱，我让着你，你先来。”
[EN] "Daydreaming? Throw your punches! Since you look delicate, I'll let you strike first."
[ZH] 千里寻心想，希望他是金人，如若是，今日打他也算是收拾金人，为抗金做点贡献。
[EN] Qianli Xun hoped he was Jurchen—pummeling a Jin rider today would count as her little contribution to resisting the Jin!
[ZH] “我堂堂大宋子民，何须外族人相让？你先来！”
[EN] "A proud citizen of the Great Song needs no handicap from an outsider! Make your move!"
[ZH] 男子听出对方已猜出他不是汉人，也难怪，即便衣装和发型依照汉人装扮，外形与气质与汉人截然不同，被看出来，倒也不稀奇。
[EN] Realizing his origins had been spotted—no surprise, since Han clothes and hairstyle couldn't hide a northern build and bearing—the rider smirked:
[ZH] “好大的口气，我以为大宋子民均是酒囊饭袋之辈，没想到还有你这等不怕死的，既如此，我先出拳。”
[EN] "Big talk! Thought all Song folk were spineless weaklings—didn't expect a daredevil like you! Fine, I'll strike first!"
[ZH] 男子以迅猛有力的拳法袭击而来……
[EN] With a fierce surge, the rider launched a blistering barrage of punches...
[ZH] 千里寻毫不退缩，以精妙的拳法迎战。
[EN] Refusing to yield an inch, Qianli Xun met him with crisp, fluid technique.
[ZH] 打过几个回合，千里寻觉得对方使出的拳法很熟悉，又打几个回合，心中惊道：“为何与申拳如此相似？难道他是申简辰？”
[EN] A few exchanges in, a jolt of familiarity hit her; two more exchanges and shock flashed through her mind: "Why is this so close to Shen Fist?! Could he be Shen Jianchen?!"
[ZH] 千里寻一激动，被对方的拳头打得退出几步。
[EN] Distracted for a split second, she caught a grazing blow that knocked her back two paces.
[ZH] 又仔细一想，不对，他若是申简辰穿越而来，怎能不熟悉千里拳？看来他不是申简辰，恐怕他的拳法与申拳有关。
[EN] No—impossible! If Shen Jianchen had crossed over, he would have recognized Qianli Fist on contact! Not Shen Jianchen, though his style had to share roots with Shen Fist.
[ZH] “最讨厌跟我打架的人注意力不集中。”几个回合下来，男子已感受到对方拳法精妙，心中赞叹，眼前的公子看似柔弱，打起架来，拳头的力度绝不比他差。
[EN] "Hate fighting opponents whose minds wander!" Already amazed by her razor-sharp skill over those few bouts, the rider marveled that a frame so slight packed fists every bit as fierce as his own.
[ZH] 千里寻又使出拳法，向对方打去……
[EN] Qianli Xun launched straight back into the fray...
[ZH] 男子的每一拳都带着沉重的威压，犹如暴风雨般袭来，每一次出拳都带着炸裂的气势，击打在空气中发出震耳欲聋的声响。
[EN] Every blow the man threw carried crushing force like a storm gale, cracking the air with thunderous snaps.
[ZH] 千里寻则凭借敏捷的身手和高超的拳法技巧，灵活地躲闪和反击。她虽身姿纤细，却展现出惊人的力量和柔韧性，拳法如流水般连贯，每一招都准确而迅猛。她以柔和的力量和精准的技巧将男子的攻势化解得无影无踪，轻盈而准确地击中男子的虚实之间。
[EN] Nimble and sharp, Qianli Xun wove past every strike and countered with deadly precision. Slender though she stood, astonishing power and spring flowed through every move, her forms linking like rushing water, swift and exact. Blending yielding force with pinpoint skill, she dissolved his brutal onslaught into thin air, landing crisp, light strikes straight into every opening he left.
[ZH] 随着对战进一步展开，男子逐渐感到对方的强大，他加大攻势，使出全力。
[EN] Sensing her true strength as the bout wore on, the man poured full force into an all-out assault.
[ZH] 千里寻依然从容不迫，巧妙地利用男子的力量和气势躲闪和反击，将男子的攻势化为机会，让他疲于应对，陷入困境。
[EN] Calm and unruffled, Qianli Xun turned his own momentum against him, dodging and striking until every attack boomeranged back, tangling him on the defensive.
[ZH] 最终，千里寻以一记精准的拳法击中男子的胸口。
[EN] At last, one pinpoint Qianli Fist punch drove dead center into his chest.
[ZH] 男子捂着胸口，向后退去，差点倒下，被旁边的随从扶住。
[EN] Clutching his sternum, the man staggered backward and almost went down before a retainer caught his elbow.
[ZH] 周围的百姓发出欢呼声：“好！”
[EN] Cheers erupted around the ring: "Bravo!"
[ZH] 千里寻收起拳头站稳后冷笑道：“你输了，赶紧向百姓道歉，然后离开临安城。”
[EN] Lowering her fists as she planted her stance, Qianli Xun smirked cold: "You lost. Apologize to the people and leave Lin'an."
[ZH] 男子感到憋屈，当着众人，不好反悔，只能忍着痛，向周围百姓拱手致歉：“诸位，对不起了。”
[EN] Swallowing bitter pride before a watching crowd, the man bit down the pain, cupped his fists around the circle, and muttered, "My apologies, everyone."
[ZH] 有百姓大声说：“以后别骑马在街上横冲直撞！”
[EN] Someone in the crowd shouted, "Stop galloping wild through city streets!"
[ZH] 男子并未回应，气憋在心里，总得发泄出来。刚才对打时，他已看出对方并非男子，不如当众揭穿她的真面目。
[EN] Giving no answer, frustration burned in the man's gut—he had to strike back somehow. Mid-brawl he had already clocked she wasn't a man; why not expose her right in front of everyone?
[ZH] 他悄悄从腰间取出一粒石子，用内力弹了出去……
[EN] Slipping a pebble from his belt, he flicked it with inner force...
[ZH] 千里寻正沉浸在胜利的喜悦中，与百姓有说有笑，并未注意飞驰而来的石子。只见那石子不偏不倚打在她的发髻上，珠簪与石子同时落地，秀发立刻散落下来。
[EN] Basking in victory and trading grins with the crowd, Qianli Xun never saw the pebble coming. It clipped dead center on her topknot, knocking her pearl hairpin to the cobbles as glossy black hair tumbled loose over her shoulders.
[ZH] 男子笑道：“呵呵……！原来你是位姑娘，女扮男装欺骗众人。”
[EN] The man let out a mocking laugh: "Haha! A young woman all along—fooling everyone dressed as a man!"
[ZH] 千里寻披散着乌黑的秀发，转过身：“我只为逛街方便，并非欺骗众人，你被一个姑娘打败，岂不是更没面子？”
[EN] Her jet-black hair cascading down her back, Qianli Xun spun around: "I dressed like this to stroll in peace, not fool anyone! Getting beaten by a girl—doesn't that cost you even more face?"
[ZH] 男子已笑不出，不是因为千里寻的话语，而是因为她的美貌。他看得呆住，这一刻，竟然生出莫名的情感来。
[EN] The laugh died on his lips—not from her retort, from her beauty. Frozen solid, an unexpected jolt stirred straight through his chest.
[ZH] 他忍着痛，脚步不由挪开，走到千里寻跟前：“我叫颜池，姑娘可否告知芳名？家住哪里？等我办完事，再去找姑娘。”
[EN] Forgetting his aching ribs, his feet carried him straight up to her: "My name is Yan Chi. Might young lady tell me your name and where you live? Once I finish my business, I'll come call on you."
[ZH] 对方态度一百八十度大转弯，搞得千里寻莫名其妙：“凭什么告诉你我的名字和家庭住址？”
[EN] Whiplashed by his sudden flip, Qianli Xun snapped, "Why on earth would I give you my name and address?!"
[ZH] “凭我喜欢上你。”
[EN] "Because I've fallen for you."
[ZH] “可我不喜欢你。”
[EN] "Well, I haven't fallen for you!"
[ZH] 围观的百姓大多已散去，只剩下少许人站在旁边指指点点。她担心遇到认识的人，得想办法尽快撤离。
[EN] Most bystanders had drifted off, leaving a few stragglers pointing and murmuring. Afraid someone might recognize her, she needed an exit fast!
[ZH] 正好朱家的马车迎面走来，真是天助我也！
[EN] Right on cue, a Zhu Manor carriage came rolling down the street—Heaven be praised!
[ZH] 千里寻一把推开颜池，用轻功跳上朱家马车，掀开车门，坐了进去，从窗户探出头来：“你的名字真难听，延迟，看来你是个不守时的人，呵呵……！”
[EN] Shoving Yan Chi aside, Qianli Xun used light-foot technique to vault straight onto the moving Zhu carriage, whipped the door open, slid inside, and poked her head out the window: "What an awful name—Yan Chi, sounds just like yanchi, 'delayed'! Guess you're always late to everything, haha!"
[ZH] 颜池对眼前的女子更感兴趣，觉得她不仅貌美，性格也特别，看到掉在地上的珠簪，走过去，捡起来，拿起端详一会儿：“邂逅相遇，适我愿兮。”
[EN] More intrigued than ever by her beauty and fiery spirit, Yan Chi walked over, stooped to pick up the fallen pearl hairpin, and turned it over in his fingers: "We met together accidentally, and so my desire was satisfied" [TN: Translated by James Legge].
[ZH] 他把珠簪揣进怀里，向其他人吩咐道：“上马！出城！”
[EN] Tucking the hairpin inside his robe, he barked to his retainers, "Mount up! Out of the city!"
[ZH] 一行人又骑上马，向城外进发，与刚才不同的是，他们再也不横冲直撞，谁让颜池的心里已装下刚才打他的姑娘。
[EN] Vaulting back onto their steeds, the riders headed for the city gates—no longer charging reckless through the crowds, now that the girl who beat him occupied Yan Chi's mind.
[ZH] 马车里，陈文心甚是惊讶，淑真披头散发，身着男装，马车未停下就钻进来，实在难以想象。
[EN] Inside the carriage, Chen Wenxin stared wide-eyed at Shuzhen—hair tumbling wild, dressed in men's clothes, vaulting into a moving carriage out of thin air!
[ZH] “淑真，你这是……？你是怎么进来的？”
[EN] "Shuzhen, what on earth—how did you even jump in here?!"
[ZH] “嫂嫂别问那么多，送我回望月轩。”
[EN] "Skip the questions, Sister-in-law—take me back to Wangyue Pavilion!"
[ZH] “你不说，我就不送你回去。”
[EN] "Not until you spill everything!"
[ZH] “好好好，我告诉嫂嫂。我在望月轩闷得慌，出来走走，又怕被人认出，才不得已女扮男装，刚在大街上碰见一个欠揍的，把他揍了一顿。”
[EN] "Fine, fine! I was bored out of my skull at Wangyue Pavilion, went out for a walk disguised as a man so nobody'd recognize me, ran into a jerk begging for a beating on the street, and beat him up!"
[ZH] 陈文心瞪大眼睛：“什么？你打人了？外面的流言刚刚没，你又惹事，小心坐实母老虎的名声！”
[EN] Chen Wenxin's eyes went round as saucers: "What?! You got in a street fight?! The rumors barely died down and you're starting brawls—trying to prove the 'tigress' rumors right?!"
[ZH] “嫂嫂莫怕，没人知道我是谁，再说，我打的也是该打之人，围观的人都高兴着呢！今儿出来一趟，算是颇有收获，打了坏人，心情好多了。”
[EN] "Relax, Sister-in-law, nobody knew who I was! Besides, the guy had it coming—the whole crowd cheered! Great outing: clobbered a bully and blew off all my steam!"
[ZH] 陈文心对外面的车夫吩咐道：“先去望月轩。”
[EN] Calling out to the driver, Chen Wenxin ordered, "Head to Wangyue Pavilion first."
[ZH] 车夫回道：“好的，少夫人。”
[EN] "Yes, Young Madam!"
[ZH] 千里寻问：“嫂嫂为何只身一人出来？连丫鬟都没带？”
[EN] Qianli Xun asked, "Why are you out all alone, Sister-in-law—not even a maid with you?"
[ZH] “今儿就想一个人回娘家一趟，所以谁都没带，你不也是？苦荞和夏果也没跟着。”
[EN] "Just felt like visiting my parents' house solo today—didn't bring anyone. Same as you leaving Kuqiao and Xiaguo behind!"
[ZH] “没想到嫂嫂也有与我一样的想法。”
[EN] "Great minds think alike!"
[ZH] 陈文心想起乔家定亲之事，小心问：“乔家的事，你应该已知晓，不知你是如何想的？”
[EN] Remembering the Qiao betrothal news, Chen Wenxin tested with care: "You heard about the Qiao family, right? What are you planning?"
[ZH] 提起乔家的事，千里寻开心的脸上又没了笑容，她沉默片刻，说道：“半年还未到，我会继续等。”
[EN] The smile faded off Qianli Xun's face. After a quiet beat, she said, "My half-year isn't up yet. I'll keep waiting."
[ZH] “人家都订婚，你还等？”
[EN] "He's engaged already, and you're still waiting?!"
[ZH] “不等又能如何？又不会有人立刻把我娶走，等也是这样，不等也是这样，还不如给自己一个念想，继续等着。”
[EN] "What else? Nobody's whisking me to an altar tomorrow—waiting or not waiting changes nothing. Might as well hold on to a little hope."
[ZH] “说的也是，想等就等吧。嫂嫂到处帮你寻摸着，临安城好男儿多的是，又不是只有乔文仙一个。”
[EN] "Fair point—wait if you want. Meanwhile, Sister-in-law will keep her eyes peeled for you! Lin'an is full of fine young men—Qiao Wenxian isn't the only fish in the sea."
[ZH] “还是别，与文渊成不了，我打算以后孤独终老，谁都不嫁。”
[EN] "Don't bother. If Wenyuan doesn't work out, I'll stay single till I'm old and gray."
[ZH] “呸呸呸！胡说什么！怎么能孤独终老？乔文仙不值得你这样。”
[EN] "Bah, bite your tongue! Stay single for life?! Qiao Wenxian isn't worth that!"
[ZH] “我不是因为他才如此想，感情这种事情，虽美好，也伤人，我不想再受伤，以后不会再随意付出感情，既不付出感情，又如何能与他人走到一起？我也只能孤独终老。”
[EN] "It's not for him. Love is sweet, sure, yet it cuts deep—I don't want to bleed again. Once I close my heart, how could I ever marry someone else? Staying solo is the only way."
[ZH] “你说的道理我不明白，我与你哥还不是婚后培养出感情来？不一定要先爱上对方才能嫁给他，也可以像我一样先嫁人，大家相处久，感情自然有。”
[EN] "I don't get your theories! Your brother and I grew our love after getting married—who says you have to fall in love first? Marry first, live together, and affection grows on its own."
[ZH] 千里寻挤出笑容：“我哪有嫂嫂幸运，能遇到我哥哥那样的好男人。”
[EN] Qianli Xun managed a faint smile: "Not everyone has Sister-in-law's luck landing a gem like my brother."
[ZH] “世上好男人多的是，我爹也是，对我娘好得不行，我娘说东，他不敢往西，呵呵……！有时，我看着都羡慕，心想，我娘上辈子肯定是做过不少好事，这辈子才能嫁给像我爹那样又有钱，又懂得心疼人的丈夫。”
[EN] "Plenty of good men out there! Look at my dad—spoils my mom rotten! If Mom points east, Dad wouldn't dare look west, haha! Makes even me jealous—figure Mom must've racked up mountains of good karma in a past life to land a husband who's both rich and doting."
[ZH] “好男人是多，碰见，又能在一起需靠缘分，文渊很好，可我与他……”
[EN] "Plenty of good men, sure, yet meeting AND keeping them takes fate. Wenyuan is wonderful, only he and I..."
[ZH] 千里寻说不下去，或者说，她根本不愿说出“有缘无分”四个字。
[EN] She broke off—unable to force out the words "fated to meet, never to stay."
[ZH] 陈文心见淑真伤感，不好再说下去，接着两人一路无语。
[EN] Seeing sorrow darken Shuzhen's eyes, Chen Wenxin fell silent, and neither spoke for the rest of the ride.
[ZH] 把淑真送到望月轩后，陈文心才折回朱府。
[EN] Only after dropping Shuzhen off at Wangyue Pavilion did Chen Wenxin turn back toward Zhu Manor.

#CH 131
[ZH] 话说陈浩宇在陈府待得无聊，忍不住出来去了赌坊。他赌博不为输赢，只为体会等待输赢的刺激。
[EN] Meanwhile, bored stiff cooped up at Chen Manor, Chen Haoyu had snuck out for a turn at a gambling den—not caring whether he won or lost, just chasing the rush before the dice settled.
[ZH] 体验够后，从赌坊出来，准备带小弟们大吃一顿，再老实回陈府。
[EN] Having scratched that itch, he stepped out planning to treat his cronies to a feast before heading back to play the good son at Chen Manor.
[ZH] 林觉派人私底下调查散播谣言之人，陈浩宇多少有些害怕，还是能装多老实，就装多老实。陈家人对他的变化很满意，以为他从此改变了心性。
[EN] With Lin Jue poking around on the quiet for the rumor-mongers, Chen Haoyu still carried a twinge of nerves and meant to stay on his best behavior—already fooling his family into thinking he had turned over a new leaf.
[ZH] 陈浩宇从赌坊走出没多久，一位混混头目躲在一个巷子，探出头来，向他招手。
[EN] Barely down the street from the gambling den, a gang boss poked his head out of a side alley and waved frantic hands at him.
[ZH] 陈浩宇本不想理他，毕竟林觉派出去的人查得紧，说不定这些头目已被盯上。转念一想，也许他有什么紧急的事情要说，不如前去会会。
[EN] Chen Haoyu almost ignored him—what if Lin Jue's men were tailing these thugs? Then again, what if it was an emergency? Better hear him out.
[ZH] 他向四周瞧一眼，没见可疑的人盯着，便大胆走过去。
[EN] Checking the street for tails and spotting none, he strolled over.
[ZH] 巷子倒没有其他人，陈浩宇很满意，吩咐两名小弟，站在巷子口放哨，他往巷子里多走几步，批评道：“不是告诉你，以后别来找我，为何不管不顾向我招手？”
[EN] Finding the alley empty, Chen Haoyu stationed two cronies at the mouth as lookouts, stepped deeper inside, and snapped, "Didn't I tell you never to come looking for me? Why wave me down in broad daylight?!"
[ZH] 头目说：“陈公子放心，我勘察过，没有林统领派的人，才敢把陈公子您请过来。”
[EN] The boss hurried to reassure him: "Relax, Young Master Chen—I checked the whole block, zero sign of Commandant Lin's men before I dared call you over."
[ZH] “什么事？说吧。”
[EN] "Spit it out—what's going on?"
[ZH] “有两个消息告诉陈公子，一个好消息和一个坏消息，您想先听哪个？”
[EN] "Got two pieces of news for you, Young Master Chen: one good, one bad. Which do you want first?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇毫不犹豫：“好消息！”
[EN] Without hesitation: "Good news!"
[ZH] “好消息就是林统领派出的人还未查到公子身上。”
[EN] "Good news is Commandant Lin's men haven't traced anything to you yet."
[ZH] “还用你说！要是查到本公子身上，本公子还能在这里跟你讲话吗？坏消息呢？”
[EN] "Duh! If they had, would I be standing here talking to you? What's the bad news?"
[ZH] “坏消息就是林统领派出的人找到几份写有谣言的纸张，还有昨日，我手底下一名兄弟被林统领叫去问话……”
[EN] "Bad news is Commandant Lin's men dug up several of those written rumor sheets—and yesterday they dragged one of my boys in for questioning!"
[ZH] “什么？都问到你手底下的人？那你还来找我干什么？还不赶紧滚！”陈浩宇担心不已，没想到林觉办起正事来还挺迅速。
[EN] "What?! They pulled in one of your boys—and you came straight to me?! Get the hell away from me!" Panic spiked in Chen Haoyu—since when did Lin Jue work this fast?!
[ZH] “陈公子放心，我手底下的人只是被叫去问话，他一概说不知，最终安全回来。我来见公子，是想告诉您，如若我们被查出来，陈公子一定要想办法救我们……”
[EN] "Don't panic, Young Master Chen! They only questioned him, he played totally dumb, and they let him walk! I just came to make sure that if we ever do get caught, you'll pull strings to bail us out..."
[ZH] “行行行，我知道，林统领应该查不出来，让你的人机灵点儿，别露出马脚。写有谣言的纸张他们是从哪里找来的？”
[EN] "Yeah, yeah, fine! Commandant Lin won't crack it—just tell your boys to stay sharp and keep their mouths shut! Where on earth did they even find those rumor sheets?"
[ZH] “我也搞不明白，该不会是从茅厕里捞出来的？那得多臭！”
[EN] "Beats me—fished them out of a latrine maybe? Imagine the stink!"
[ZH] “最好臭死林觉派来的人！赶紧回去，以后不准再主动找我。”陈浩宇从怀里掏出一袋银子，“拿着！”
[EN] "Hope the stink chokes Lin Jue's men dead! Beat it, and never come looking for me again!" Pulling a pouch of silver from his robe, Chen Haoyu tossed it over: "Take it!"
[ZH] 混混头目笑嘻嘻接过：“陈公子放心，以后绝不打扰您。”
[EN] Catching the pouch with a greasy grin: "Count on us, Young Master Chen—won't bother you again!"
[ZH] 离开小巷，陈浩宇决定不再瞎逛，还是回陈府，老老实实待着，再告诉身边的小弟，近期不许来找他。
[EN] Slipping out of the alley, Chen Haoyu canceled his feast, hurried straight home to lie low at Chen Manor, and warned his cronies to stay away for a while.
[ZH] 林觉手下的人找到三张写有谣言的纸张，交给他时，一张皱皱巴巴，一张缺少一半，一张上粘有泥土。
[EN] Lin Jue's soldiers had indeed turned up three rumor sheets—one crumpled tight, one torn in half, and one caked in mud.
[ZH] 林觉拿到纸张，才明白关于朱淑真的流言还真有人故意为之。他派手下的人从找到纸张的地方找来些百姓问问，也许能问出线索。
[EN] Holding those sheets confirmed beyond doubt that the smears against Zhu Shuzhen had been orchestrated. Lin Jue had his men bring in locals from around where the sheets were found to fish for clues.
[ZH] 找来的几十人，有的说没见过那些纸张，有的说自己从来没胡乱议论过别人，有的被吓得什么都不敢说。
[EN] Out of dozens questioned, some swore they'd never seen the sheets, some swore they never gossiped, and others shook too hard to utter a word.
[ZH] 只有一人说曾见有人在街上拿着同样的纸张念过那些话。问他念的人长什么样，那人早已想不起来。
[EN] Only one passerby recalled seeing a man reading aloud from an identical sheet on the street—though he couldn't remember the man's face.
[ZH] 林觉先派人在那人看到的街道附近盯着，他则拿着纸张先去找知府大人。
[EN] Stationing men to stake out that neighborhood, Lin Jue took the sheets straight to the Prefect.
[ZH] 朱晞颜在府衙接见林觉，当他拿到皱皱巴巴的纸张，甚是气愤：“再明显不过，有人故意针对朱家，辛苦林统领继续派人查探。”
[EN] Receiving Lin Jue at the prefecture office, Zhu Xiyan's face went black as he examined the crumpled sheets: "Clear as daylight—someone targeted the Zhu house on purpose! Keep digging, Commandant Lin."
[ZH] “知府大人放心，我查出有人在街上拿着纸张向他人念过上面的内容，我已派人在那条街的周围查探。”
[EN] "Rest easy, Lord Prefect. One witness saw someone reading from these sheets on the street; I already have men watching that quarter."
[ZH] “有劳。”
[EN] "Much obliged."
[ZH] 林觉犹豫道：“知府大人，想要查出散播谣言之人甚是困难，既然百姓已无人再议论谣言，不如放弃查探，只怕查得越紧，反而引起百姓再次关注谣言。”
[EN] Lin Jue hesitated a beat: "Lord Prefect, tracking down the mastermind will be mighty tough. Since folk have stopped talking about the rumors anyway, why not drop the probe? Dig too hard and we might stir the gossip right back up."
[ZH] “林统领的话不无道理，可散播谣言之人实在可恶，不将他揪出来，本官难解心头之恨。林统领只管差人查，无须大张旗鼓。”
[EN] "You have a point, Commandant Lin, yet whoever pulled this filthy stunt is intolerable—I won't rest until he's dragged into the light! Keep digging, just keep it quiet."
[ZH] 林觉不好再说什么，只能同意：“知府大人放心，迟早我会帮您揪出幕后之人。”
[EN] Left with no counter, Lin Jue bowed: "Rest easy, Lord Prefect—sooner or later I'll drag the mastermind out for you."
[ZH] 离开府衙后，林觉又乘坐马车去城外的幽林别居，既查出线索，还得向仪王报告，仪王才是他真正想要当成靠山之人。
[EN] Leaving the prefecture office, Lin Jue rode straight out of town to Youlin Retreat—having found a clue, he owed a report to Prince Yi, the true patron he wanted to anchor himself to.
[ZH] 乔守仁把幽林别居送给仪王，连带着把下人也送了。李管家将林觉引入别居内，来到仪王居住的院子。
[EN] Having gifted Youlin Retreat—servants and all—to Prince Yi, Butler Li escorted Lin Jue inside to Prince Yi's courtyard.
[ZH] 林觉第一次来幽林别居，心里一直慨叹，乔守仁真是心思巧妙，竟然能在幽林中建起一座宅子，实在别出心裁。
[EN] Stepping into Youlin Retreat for the first time, Lin Jue marveled at Qiao Shouren's clever taste in tucking a whole residence inside a secluded forest.
[ZH] 当李管家在门外报告：“王爷，林统领前来拜访。”
[EN] Outside the door, Butler Li announced, "Your Highness, Commandant Lin is here to pay a call."
[ZH] 申简辰正在屋内无聊，文渊不在，没人缠他，又不能随意去望月轩。除梦容外，没人来找他，其余时间都闲着，闲得心里发慌，慌到只想去望月轩，最后还是忍住。
[EN] Indoors, Shen Jianchen was bored out of his skull—no Wenyuan to pester him, forbidden from visiting Wangyue Pavilion, only Mengrong dropping by, leaving endless empty hours itching to head for Wangyue Pavilion until he forced himself back down.
[ZH] 林觉前来，让申简辰无聊的生活泛起波澜，他给白风示意。
[EN] Lin Jue's visit broke the monotony; he nodded to Bai Feng.
[ZH] 白风打开门，对外面说：“王爷有请林统领。”
[EN] Opening the door, Bai Feng called out, "His Highness invites Commandant Lin inside."
[ZH] 林觉快步走进，见仪王整坐在桌前拿本书看。
[EN] Striding in, Lin Jue found Prince Yi seated at a desk reading a book.
[ZH] 其实书卷是申简辰刚刚抓到手中，总不能让他人进来看到自己无所事事的样子。
[EN] Truth be told, Shen Jianchen had just grabbed the scroll a second ago so he wouldn't look like an idle bum.
[ZH] 林觉弯腰低头行礼：“仪王，下官查探谣言之事已有线索，特来向您报告。”
[EN] Bowing low, Lin Jue reported, "Your Highness, we've turned up a lead in the rumor investigation, so I hurried to report."
[ZH] 申简辰装模作样放下书：“林统领辛苦，说说你查到什么线索？”
[EN] Setting the book down with princely poise, Shen Jianchen asked, "Good work, Commandant Lin. What did you find?"
[ZH] 林觉取出被扯掉一半儿的纸张：“请仪王过目。”
[EN] Lin Jue pulled out the torn half-sheet: "Please inspect this, Your Highness."
[ZH] 白风接过，送到王爷手中。
[EN] Bai Feng took it and handed it to his master.
[ZH] 申简辰仔细浏览一遍，基本看明白。
[EN] Scanning the lines, Shen Jianchen got the full picture at once.
[ZH] “看来有人故意散播幽栖居士的谣言，知府大人可知此事？”
[EN] "So someone really did spread rumors against Youqi Jushi on purpose. Does the Prefect know?"
[ZH] “知府大人已知，下官特来向仪王禀报，顺便请示王爷，可否停止查探？”林觉已想好，若仪王同意，他便不顾朱晞颜的意思。
[EN] "He does, Your Highness. I came to report—and ask whether Your Highness wishes us to halt the search?" Lin Jue had already decided: if Prince Yi said stop, he'd ignore Zhu Xiyan!
[ZH] “为何？”
