[EN] "Why halt?"
[ZH] “寻找有意散播谣言之人如同大海捞针，现在谣言已不被百姓重视，再查下去只怕百姓又开始议论。”
[EN] "Hunting down a rumor-monger is like fishing a needle out of the sea. Nobody cares about the gossip anymore—keep poking around and folk might start talking again."
[ZH] 散播谣言之人的确难查，申简辰看出来，林觉嫌麻烦，不愿再查下去。可谁让谣言诋毁他心爱的姑娘，定要查出幕后黑手，将那人好好收拾收拾，让那人明白，他喜欢的人不能任由他人欺侮。
[EN] Shen Jianchen saw straight through Lin Jue: the man was just dodging tedious work! No chance—nobody smeared the woman he loved and walked away clean; he meant to catch the mastermind and pound him into understanding who was off-limits!
[ZH] “我知此事麻烦，如同大海捞针，再难，也请林统领帮我把传谣之人揪出来，不管多久，我都会耐心等待。至于会不会引起百姓再次关注谣言，我看未必，若百姓知道官府出面调查，自然更会紧闭嘴巴，一个字不敢再提，林统领尽管放宽心，继续派人查探。”
[EN] "I know it's a needle in a haystack, Commandant Lin—no matter how tough, drag the culprit out for me. I'll wait as long as it takes. As for stirring gossip back up, quite the opposite: once folks see authorities investigating, they'll clamp their mouths twice as tight. Keep your men digging."
[ZH] 仪王发话，林觉更没什么可说的，接着查便是。
[EN] With Prince Yi's word laid down, Lin Jue had zero wiggle room left—keep digging it was.
[ZH] 从这件事，林觉算是彻底明白，仪王对朱家真是好，恐怕不仅仅是因乔文仙的原因。
[EN] One thing dawned clear as glass on Lin Jue now: Prince Yi cared way too much about the Zhu family for it to be just about Qiao Wenxian!
[ZH] “下官谨遵仪王吩咐！”
[EN] "I will carry out Prince Yi's orders to the letter!"
[ZH] 林觉拜别仪王，离开幽林别居，坐在马车里，一路在想，仪王为何对朱家这么好？想来想去想不明白。
[EN] Bowing out and climbing into his carriage, Lin Jue racked his brains all the way back to town: why on earth was Prince Yi this devoted to the Zhu house?
[ZH] 按说乔文仙已订婚，仪王不可能再能将朱淑真放在眼里，肯定不会再管谣言之事。可仪王还对朱家如此好，到底因为什么？以前也没听说他与朱晞颜有任何关系。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian was already betrothed to someone else, so Prince Yi had zero reason to care about Zhu Shuzhen or old rumors—yet he stayed dead set on hunting the culprit! And Prince Yi had never had ties to Zhu Xiyan before!
[ZH] 林觉越想越迷惑，想得脑袋发胀也没想明白。不过想到乔家不要朱淑真，他心里还是有些许得意，她离开自己又能如何？还不是没人要！
[EN] Lin Jue thought until his head throbbed without cracking the puzzle. Still, knowing the Qiaos had dumped Zhu Shuzhen gave him a petty thrill: leaving him hadn't gotten her far—nobody wanted her now!
[ZH] 申简辰每日都想去见千里寻，有好几次，他乘坐马车走到半路，又折回。白风甚是纳闷，王爷到底想干什么？为何犹犹豫豫？他又不敢多问。
[EN] Every single day Shen Jianchen itched to go see Qianli Xun; more than once his carriage got halfway to town before he ordered it turned back, leaving Bai Feng baffled yet silent.
[ZH] 申简辰想到一个好办法，来到佳和郡主的院子。
[EN] Then an idea sparked, and he strolled over to Qiao Mengrong's courtyard.
[ZH] 乔梦容正准备看望哥哥，两人在院子里相遇。乔梦容以为哥哥心里念着她，很是开心：“我正要找哥哥，哥哥就来了。”
[EN] Just stepping out to visit him herself, Qiao Mengrong lit up at meeting him in the yard, thinking he had missed her: "I was just coming to find Brother, and here you are!"
[ZH] “你一直待在幽林别居，都没怎么出去过，今日我带我去逛街。”
[EN] "You've been cooped up in Youlin Retreat forever without going out—today I'll take you out strolling the streets."
[ZH] “哥哥与我想到一处。”
[EN] "Brother read my mind!"
[ZH] 乔梦容更是开心不已，哥哥越来越与她合拍。
[EN] Joy bubbled over in Qiao Mengrong—they were growing more in sync every day!
[ZH] 他们乘坐马车进了城，来到闹市，乔梦容逛得甚是开心。
[EN] Riding into the city's bustling heart, Qiao Mengrong strolled in pure delight.
[ZH] 申简辰心里装着千里寻，根本没有心思继续逛下去。他瞅准时机说：“梦容，我们逛得开心，可文渊心情并不好。”
[EN] Mind locked onto Qianli Xun, Shen Jianchen had zero heart for shopping. Picking his moment, he said, "Here we are having fun, while poor Wenyuan is miserable."
[ZH] “哥哥不是说乔公子可以自由出入了吗？”
[EN] "Didn't Brother say Young Master Qiao is allowed out again?"
[ZH] “他能自由出入，是因为乔家已给他定亲。”
[EN] "Only because the Qiao family engaged him to someone else."
[ZH] “定的可是淑真？”
[EN] "To Shuzhen?"
[ZH] “是苗家姑娘苗知瑜。”
[EN] "To the Miao family's daughter, Miao Zhiyu."
[ZH] “淑真岂不难过？”
[EN] "Won't Shuzhen be heartbroken?!"
[ZH] “是啊，我不敢让白风把消息告诉她，就是怕她难过，可她迟早会知道，你跟她关系好，不如把消息传给她，再安慰安慰她。”
[EN] "Right—I didn't even dare send Bai Feng to tell her for fear of hurting her. Still, she's bound to hear sooner or later. Since you two are close, why not go break the news gently and comfort her?"
[ZH] “说起来，我也好久没见她，应该去看看，不如就今日。”
[EN] "Come to think of it, I haven't seen her in ages—I should visit! Let's go today!"
[ZH] 申简辰既开心又觉得自己实在“卑鄙”，为了解千里寻的近况，竟然利用梦容的善良。可他又忍不住不这么做。
[EN] Equal parts glad and disgusted with himself for playing Mengrong's kind heart like a pawn, Shen Jianchen couldn't stop himself.
[ZH] 本来要派白风去，可白风每次去，只知传递消息，最多从苦荞口中了解一二。再说这家伙心思在苦荞身上，根本没把幽栖居士当回事，每次回来说些只言片语，根本无法满足他。
[EN] Sending Bai Feng never gave him enough—the lad only dropped off messages and spent the rest of the time flirting with Kuqiao, bringing back useless scraps.
[ZH] 不得已，才想到梦容，她们两个是闺中密友，无话不谈，自然能了解得更多更详细，尤其是关于千里寻内心的想法。
[EN] Only Mengrong, Qianli Xun's close confidante, could draw out every real thought hiding inside Qianli Xun's heart.

#CH 132
[ZH] 到达望月轩门口，乔梦容见哥哥没有要下马车的意思，不解地问：“难道哥哥不打算跟我一起看望淑真？”
[EN] Pulling up outside Wangyue Pavilion, Qiao Mengrong saw no sign of Prince Yi stepping down and asked in puzzlement, "Isn't Brother coming in with me to see Shuzhen?"
[ZH] 申简辰当然想，可他已答应千里寻不再来望月轩，又岂能食言？
[EN] Shen Jianchen burned to go in, yet he had promised Qianli Xun never to enter Wangyue Pavilion again; how could he break his word?
[ZH] “你们之间有女孩子的话题要聊，我去不大合适；再说我是外男，探望幽栖居士对她影响不好。我在车上等你，不用担心我等得久，今日无事，专程陪你，你与幽栖居士想聊多久都可以。”
[EN] "You girls have girl talk—me tagging along would be awkward. Besides, I'm an unmarried man; visiting Youqi Jushi wouldn't look right for her reputation. I'll wait right here in the carriage. Take all the time you want; my whole day is yours."
[ZH] 哥哥如此体贴，乔梦容甚是满意，尤其是哥哥对别的女子懂得男女有别，更令她欣慰，以后嫁给哥哥可以放一百个心。
[EN] Such thoughtfulness—above all, keeping proper distance from another woman—melted Qiao Mengrong's heart, leaving her at complete ease about marrying him.
[ZH] 乔梦容没有让望月轩的下人报告主人，她想给淑真一个惊喜，在望月轩下人的带领下，朝淑真的闺房走去。
[EN] Asking Wangyue Pavilion's servants not to announce her so she could surprise Shuzhen, Qiao Mengrong followed a servant straight to the bedchamber courtyard.
[ZH] 到达闺房门外，夏果正好在院中，见到郡主，刚要拜见，郡主将手指放在嘴边，“嘘”了一声，她便明白郡主想给小姐一个惊喜，便默声参拜完，帮郡主撩开帘子。
[EN] Spotting Xiaguo in the yard about to bow and announce her, Qiao Mengrong pressed a finger to her lips with a quiet "Shhh!" Catching on, Xiaguo bowed in silence and lifted the door curtain for her.
[ZH] 快要进入冬季，望月轩所有屋门上挂起布帘，既可装饰，亦可抵御冷风。
[EN] With winter nearing, heavy cloth curtains hung over every doorway in Wangyue Pavilion, both neat and snug against drafts.
[ZH] 千里寻正在桌前写话本，苦荞站在一旁伺候笔墨。
[EN] Inside, Qianli Xun sat writing her huaben script while Kuqiao tended ink beside her.
[ZH] 逛了一趟街，没人再议论她，又收拾了一个欠揍的，郁结之情得到疏散后，千里寻开始大胆提笔写男女主人公甜蜜的恋情。
[EN] After that market stroll—hearing rumors had died and beating up an arrogant bully—her gloom had lifted enough to start writing sweet romance for her hero and heroine again.
[ZH] 正写得专注，传来乔梦容的声音。
[EN] Lost in her script, she heard Qiao Mengrong's bright voice break the hush.
[ZH] “在忙什么？好久没去看我，我只好过来探望你。”
[EN] "What are you busy with? You never came to visit me, so I had to come hunt you down!"
[ZH] 乔梦容到来，无疑让望月轩多出些许生气。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's arrival injected instant warmth into Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 千里寻放下笔，起身迎上去：“你既来，也不说一声，我好出去迎接。”
[EN] Laying down her brush, Qianli Xun jumped up to greet her: "You didn't even send word so I could come welcome you at the gate!"
[ZH] “迎接多没意思，不如我进来给你个意外之喜。”
[EN] "Where's the fun in formal welcomes? Much better popping in as a surprise!"
[ZH] 二人说话之际，苦荞将小姐没写完的手稿收起来，走到书架跟前，打开一个木盒子，小心放入。
[EN] While they greeted each other, Kuqiao gathered her mistress's unfinished manuscript, walked over to the bookshelf, opened a wooden box, and tucked it safe inside.
[ZH] 千里寻拉起乔梦容在椅子上坐下，吩咐夏果做两盏茶。
[EN] Pulling Qiao Mengrong into a chair, Qianli Xun told Xiaguo to whisk two bowls of tea.
[ZH] 夏果做茶的技术已拿得出手，她高兴地应答着，走到茶桌前，忙碌起来。
[EN] Confident in her tea-whisking skills now, Xiaguo answered with a bright smile and moved to the tea table.
[ZH] 喜兰站在郡主身旁，扫视幽栖居士的闺房，书香四溢，心中慨叹，难怪她能成为江南女词人。
[EN] Standing beside her princess, Xilan took in the scholar's charm filling Youqi Jushi's chamber—no wonder she was known as the Poetess of Jiangnan!
[ZH] “淑真，有机会再去幽林别居陪我住几日，别居又大又冷清，以前还有乔公子在，他一走，更冷清。”
[EN] "Come stay a few days at Youlin Retreat again sometime, Shuzhen! That big house feels so empty—and twice as lonely since Young Master Qiao left."
[ZH] 提到乔文仙，千里寻心中一痛，怕郡主瞧出来，掩饰道：“我已答应韦嬷嬷，以后不会在外留宿，再说别居有仪王在，他对我来讲，是外男，甚是不便。”
[EN] A sharp twinge pierced Qianli Xun at mention of Qiao Wenxian; masking it fast, she replied, "I promised Matron Wei no more staying out overnight. Besides, Prince Yi lives at the retreat—an unmarried man, so staying there isn't proper."
[ZH] “怪我，只想着跟你多待些时日，没考虑周全，倒是你跟哥哥考虑全面，他跟我一起来，说不便进来，待在外面的马车里候着。”
[EN] "My fault! Just wanted to spend more time with you and didn't think it through. You and Brother both think ahead—he came with me today, said stepping inside wouldn't look right, and stayed waiting in the carriage outside."
[ZH] “什么？仪王跟你一起来？让他待在外面岂不是慢待他？”千里寻犹豫要不要出去迎接。
[EN] "What?! Prince Yi came with you?! Leaving him waiting outside is awful manners!" Qianli Xun wavered over whether to go welcome him.
[ZH] “哥哥说不用管他，还说我们想聊多久都可以。”
[EN] "Brother said leave him be—stay and chat as long as we like!"
[ZH] 千里寻本也不想见仪王，他既然懂事，不随便进来，再好不过。
[EN] Truth be told, Qianli Xun had zero urge to see Prince Yi; if he had the sense to stay outside, all the better!
[ZH] 苦荞听到仪王在外面等着，已呆不住，走到小姐跟前，大胆说：“小姐，我能不能出去跟白风说两句话。”
[EN] Hearing Prince Yi was outside, Kuqiao could not sit still and ventured, "Miss, may I step outside for two words with Bai Feng?"
[ZH] “去吧，早知道你想见他。”千里寻答应得干脆。
[EN] "Go on—knew you were itching to see him!" Qianli Xun waved her off at once.
[ZH] “谢谢小姐！”苦荞兴奋地跑出去。
[EN] "Thank you, Miss!" Kuqiao darted out beaming.
[ZH] 乔梦容想起乔公子订亲之事，犹豫如何开口告诉淑真。
[EN] Remembering Qiao Wenxian's betrothal, Qiao Mengrong hesitated how to break it to Shuzhen.
[ZH] “淑真，有件事，我想对你说，只怕说出来，你会伤心……”
[EN] "Shuzhen, there's something I need to tell you—afraid it'll hurt you..."
[ZH] 千里寻猜到梦容想说什么：“你是想说文渊订亲一事？”
[EN] Qianli Xun guessed right away: "You mean Wenyuan getting engaged?"
[ZH] “原来你已知，害我担心半天。”
[EN] "You already know?! Here I was sweating bullets over how to tell you!"
[ZH] “我哥哥来时已告诉我。”
[EN] "My brother told me when he visited."
[ZH] 乔梦容观察淑真的表情，她看上去并没有想象中那么难过。
[EN] Studying Shuzhen's face, Qiao Mengrong saw far less devastation than she had braced for.
[ZH] “淑真，我相信乔公子心中还有你，只是造化弄人，别想那么多，一切向前看。”
[EN] "I'm sure Young Master Qiao still holds you in his heart, Shuzhen—just cruel fate. Don't brood; keep looking forward."
[ZH] “你放心，不管乔家订多少门亲事，只要开春前，文渊不亲口对我说放弃的话，我绝不放弃。”
[EN] "Don't worry. No matter how many matches the Qiao family arranges, until spring comes, unless Wenyuan tells me to my face he's giving up, I won't give up."
[ZH] “开春后呢？”
[EN] "And after spring?"
[ZH] “开春后……开春后，他若依然不说，我……我便放弃。”
[EN] "After spring... if he still stays silent, I'll... let go."
[ZH] “看来，你已想清楚。我听哥哥说，乔家给乔公子订下亲后，不再关着他……”
[EN] "Sounds like you've thought it clear. Brother told me once the Qiaos sealed the betrothal, they stopped locking Young Master Qiao up..."
[ZH] 千里寻听到文渊不再被关着，忍不住流下眼泪：“他既已自由，为何不来找我？”
[EN] Hearing Wenyuan walked free now brought sudden tears spilling from Qianli Xun's eyes: "If he's free, why hasn't he come to see me?!"
[ZH] “哥哥没告诉我，我猜想，应是乔公子有什么难处。”
[EN] "Brother didn't say. Maybe Young Master Qiao has his hands tied."
[ZH] “梦容，你帮我拜托仪王，让他给文渊捎句话，就说我在望月轩等他。”
[EN] "Mengrong, ask Prince Yi to pass a message to Wenyuan for me—tell him I'm waiting for him at Wangyue Pavilion."
[ZH] 乔梦容点点头：“放心，我会转告哥哥。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong nodded: "Don't worry, I'll tell Brother."
[ZH] 夏果已做好两盏茶，给郡主与小姐端上。
[EN] Having finished two bowls of whisked tea, Xiaguo served them to Princess Jiahe and her mistress.
[ZH] 她站在一旁听二人聊天儿，得知乔公子已获自由，为他高兴的同时，想着什么时候找机会见见他。小姐与乔公子已毫无希望，她要为自己的人生搏上一把。
[EN] Standing beside them listening to their chat, Xiaguo felt her pulse quicken at news that Young Master Qiao was free; with Miss's hopes gone, Xiaguo meant to gamble on her own future!
[ZH] 申简辰坐在马车里等得悠然自得，等得心甘情愿。表面上等的是梦容，事实上等的是千里寻，等关于她的消息，与等她这个人又有什么区别。
[EN] Out in the carriage, Shen Jianchen waited in complete content—ostensibly waiting for Mengrong, in truth waiting on Qianli Xun; waiting for news of her felt almost as good as waiting for her in person.
[ZH] 马车外，白风与苦荞在聊天儿。苦荞埋怨白风为何这么久才来望月轩，白风说王爷不让他随便来，他也没办法。
[EN] Outside the carriage, Bai Feng and Kuqiao chatted away, Kuqiao scolding him for taking so long to visit while Bai Feng pleaded His Highness would not let him come over whenever he liked.
[ZH] “难道王爷不愿再给我家小姐送消息？”
[EN] "Doesn't Prince Yi want to send my mistress updates anymore?"
[ZH] “怎么不愿？王爷对这事儿热心着呢？”
[EN] "Of course he does—he cares tons!"
[ZH] “既热心，你为何才送过两次消息，后面再不见来？”
[EN] "Then why only two updates and then radio silence?"
[ZH] “都不是什么好消息，王爷担心幽栖居士伤心，所以不让我送。”
[EN] "Nothing but bad news—His Highness didn't want to make Youqi Jushi cry, so he held me back."
[ZH] “原来是这样，害得我好久没见到你。”
[EN] "Oh... still made me miss you forever!"
[ZH] “我已想好，以后每隔三日，来看你，你在申时到门外等我就是。”
[EN] "Got it all planned: every three days, I'll come see you—meet me outside the gate at the hour of shen!"
[ZH] “说好了，不能变卦。”
[EN] "Promise—no backing out!"
[ZH] “放心，绝不变卦，变卦我是小狗。”
[EN] "Never! Back out and I'm a puppy!"
[ZH] 两人说得无比开心。
[EN] Both grinned ear to ear.
[ZH] 品完一盏茶后，千里寻的情绪平静许多，文渊不来找她，也许是有他的难处，她还是继续耐心等待。
[EN] Finishing her tea, Qianli Xun regained her calm: maybe Wenyuan really did have some bind keeping him away; she would keep waiting.
[ZH] 乔梦容放下茶盏说道：“听得出，关于你跟乔公子之事，你的心中已有主意，我便放心。就目前情形来看，他已订亲，你想与他在一起，从礼俗上讲，已……已不可能，除非……”
[EN] Setting down her bowl, Qiao Mengrong said, "Glad you've got a clear head about Young Master Qiao. Realistically, now that he's betrothed, proper etiquette leaves no path for you two—unless..."
[ZH] 千里寻果断说：“我不可能给文渊做妾，我等的不是他娶我或不娶我，而是等他一句话。”
[EN] Qianli Xun cut in firm: "I'm never becoming Wenyuan's concubine. I'm not waiting to see if he'll marry me—I'm just waiting for one straight answer from him."
[ZH] “你也要为自己重新打算，不要为等乔公子一句话浪费大好光阴，我们女子耗费不起。”
[EN] "Still, plan your own future too! Don't burn away your best years waiting on one sentence from Young Master Qiao—women can't afford to waste time like that."
[ZH] 千里寻盯着梦容，一个遇见仪王便失去理智的郡主，安慰起别人来，却头头是道。
[EN] Qianli Xun could not help marveling at Mengrong: loses all logic the second Prince Yi enters the room, yet hands out rock-solid advice to everyone else!
[ZH] “别光顾说我的事，说说你，跟你那位王爷怎么样？他最近有没有出去勾搭别的姑娘？”
[EN] "Enough about me—how about you and that prince of yours? Been sneaking off to flirt with other girls lately?"
[ZH] 乔梦容被逗乐：“‘勾搭’这个词用在哥哥身上，实在不恰当。”
[EN] Qianli Xun's slang cracked Qiao Mengrong up: "Using 'flirt' on Brother sounds so wrong!"
[ZH] “恰当不恰当，现在还难说。”
[EN] "Wrong or right remains to be seen!"
[ZH] “也不知你为何对哥哥有成见？他可是帮过你不少忙，为帮你和乔公子送消息，两头忙，今日愿意跟我来望月轩，估计又是想给乔公子送消息。”
[EN] "Why do you hold such a grudge against Brother? He's helped you so much—running back and forth with news for you and Young Master Qiao, and likely brought me here today just to get news for Young Master Qiao too!"
[ZH] “你这位哥哥真奇怪，放着临安的大事不忙，倒对我和文渊的事热心，也不知他是真热心还是假热心。”千里寻想起仪王对她表白的话，想起减免赋税之事，更觉仪王心机深沉。
[EN] "Funny prince you've got—ignoring Lin'an's affairs to meddle in Wenyuan's and my love life! Hard to tell if he's truly helpful or up to something." Remembering his confessions and the tax cuts, Qianli Xun thought Prince Yi ran deep as a well.
[ZH] 乔梦容故作严肃道：“淑真，看在你跟我关系好，我不计较，若是别人说同样的话，我可不高兴。”
[EN] Putting on mock sternness, Qiao Mengrong scolded, "Only because you're my best friend do I let that slide—anyone else talks about Brother like that and I'd be furious!"
[ZH] “好好好，我道歉，你的哥哥是天底下最好的男子，谁都比不上。”
[EN] "All right, all right, I apologize! Your Brother is the greatest man under heaven—nobody holds a candle to him!"
[ZH] “你也别用故意夸他的话噎我，反正我哥哥就是好，他近期除忙你跟文渊的事，一直在幽林别居陪我，怕我在别居待得心烦，才想着带我出来逛逛，还心甘情愿在外面等我，哪怕我跟你聊到天黑，他都愿意等下去。你说，有几个男子能做到？”
[EN] "Quit teasing me with fake praise! Brother really is wonderful. Outside helping you and Wenyuan, he stays right beside me at Youlin Retreat all day, brought me out strolling today so I wouldn't get bored, and waits out here in the carriage without a single complaint even if we talk till dark! How many men would do that?"
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“我要是告诉你仪王对我表白过，看你还能说出几句夸赞他的话。”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought, "Wait till I tell you he confessed his love to me—let's see how sweet your praise sounds then!"
[ZH] 千里寻更觉仪王不仅心机深沉，还是情场老手。一边把单纯的郡主玩得滴溜转，一边对其他女子放电；更无耻的是，还能把自己的不道德说成是满腔痴情。
[EN] In her eyes, Prince Yi was not just scheming—he was a grandmaster player, spinning an innocent princess around his little finger with one hand while tossing sparks at other women with the other, and shameless enough to paint his cheating as tragic devotion!
[ZH] 如果她千里寻是个有貌无脑之人，估计早已因仪王的身份、地位，还有他看似痴情表白的话而倾倒。
[EN] Any pretty airhead would have melted straight into his lap over his royal title, wealth, and silky confessions.
[ZH] “你说得对，你哥哥的作派确实没几个男子能做到。”
[EN] "True enough—not many men operate quite like your Brother."
[ZH] 淑真敷衍的认可，乔梦容并未当回事，只以为淑真太过关心她，怕她在感情上受到伤害，有句话说得甚好，关心则乱。所故而，她误会哥哥，在所难免。
[EN] Missing the half-hearted edge of Shuzhen's agreement, Qiao Mengrong thought nothing of it, assuming her friend cared so much she feared seeing her hurt—just like the saying that deep concern clouds the eye, making misunderstanding inevitable.
[ZH] 别说淑真会误会哥哥，就连她乔梦容也将哥哥误会许久。
[EN] Never mind Shuzhen misunderstanding Prince Yi—even Qiao Mengrong herself had misunderstood him for ages.
[ZH] 淑真既已清楚该如何去做，乔梦容没必要多加劝说。陪她又东拉西扯，胡乱聊了半个时辰，发现淑真的心情又好很多，觉得没必要再待下去，关键她担心哥哥在外面等得太久，便起身告辞。
[EN] Seeing Shuzhen had her head screwed on straight, Qiao Mengrong dropped the lectures and chatted about random trifles for another hour until Shuzhen's spirits brightened, then rose to leave lest Prince Yi wait too long outside.
[ZH] 千里寻将佳和郡主送至望月轩门外的马车上，便向马车里的仪王行礼问好，心里再对他不恭敬，表面功夫也要做到位。
[EN] Walking Princess Jiahe out to the carriage at the gate, Qianli Xun bowed with full courtesy toward the curtained coach—whatever she felt inside, surface etiquette stayed spotless.
[ZH] “感谢仪王送梦容前来陪我说话，我的心情好了很多。”
[EN] "Thank you for bringing Mengrong to keep me company, Prince Yi. My spirits are much lighter."
[ZH] 申简辰并未掀开帘子，尽管他很想掀开，还是忍住，担心亲眼看到她，会不愿离开。
[EN] Refusing to lift the curtain lest one look at her make leaving impossible, Shen Jianchen answered from inside: "No need to thank me—Mengrong wanted to visit, I just tagged along."
[ZH] “幽栖居士不必谢我，梦容想来，我陪她而已。”
[EN] Deciding to test the waters on the tax cuts, Qianli Xun added, "One more thing Shuzhen owes Prince Yi thanks for."
[ZH] 关于减免赋税一事，千里寻想要趁机探探虚实：“还有一事，淑真要谢过仪王。”
[EN] "If it's about checking on Wenyuan, forget it."
[ZH] “若是关于帮你打探文渊消息一事，我看就免了。”
[EN] "About the tax cuts. Because of them, everyone's attention shifted and the street gossip died clean out—Shuzhen is grateful beyond words."
[ZH] “是关于减免赋税一事，由于此事，百姓关注的焦点发生转移，街上无人再议论我，流言蜚语已过去，淑真对仪王感激不尽。”
[EN] "How do you plan to thank me?"
[ZH] “你打算怎么谢我？”
[EN] "How do you plan to thank me?"
[ZH] 千里寻被仪王的问题给问住，怎么谢？她还没想好，只是探探虚实而已，了解仪王帮她是否有所求。还想趁佳和郡主在时，仪王不敢乱提要求，用良好的态度谢过他，此事也可了结。却不曾想仪王会反问她。
[EN] His question tripped her up—she had not planned anything! She only wanted to probe whether he wanted payback, figuring with Princess Jiahe right beside him he would not dare demand anything weird, letting her clear the debt with polite words. Never dreamed he would bounce the ball right back!
[ZH] 马车外一阵沉默，申简辰猜道千里寻感到为难，他最见不得她为难，便说道：“减免赋税是为临安百姓考虑，跟街上的流言毫无关系，在我眼中，幽栖居士岂可与临安百姓相提并论？”
[EN] Hearing silence outside and guessing she felt cornered—the last thing he ever wanted—Shen Jianchen spoke cool and clean: "I proposed tax cuts for the people of Lin'an, nothing to do with street gossip. In my eyes, how could Youqi Jushi compare with all the commoners of Lin'an?"
[ZH] 马车开始启动，千里寻松口气，不用再担心欠仪王一个大大的人情，也不用担心因为这个人情会被仪王纳为妾室。
[EN] As the carriage pulled away, Qianli Xun breathed a huge sigh of relief—no giant debt hanging over her head, and no risk of being strong-armed into becoming a royal concubine!
[ZH] 离望月轩远些后，申简辰忍不住问：“梦容，文渊订亲，幽栖居士可难过？”
[EN] Once down the lane, Shen Jianchen could not hold back: "Mengrong, how hard is Youqi Jushi taking Wenyuan's betrothal?"
[ZH] 乔梦容叹道：“怎能不难过？不过淑真想得明白，她说只要乔公子不说放弃的话，她便等着。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong sighed: "Of course she's hurt. Still, she has a clear head—said as long as Young Master Qiao doesn't tell her he's giving up, she'll wait."
[ZH] 申简辰担忧不已：“文渊成亲了，她还要等吗？”
[EN] Alarm flared in Shen Jianchen: "Even after Wenyuan marries?!"
[ZH] 乔梦容解释道：“淑真说开春后，若乔公子不来找她，她便放弃。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong explained: "Shuzhen said after spring comes, if Young Master Qiao still hasn't come for her, she'll let go."
[ZH] 申简辰不由轻松许多：“还好，她不会傻到永远等下去。”
[EN] A quiet breath escaped Shen Jianchen: "Thank goodness she isn't foolish enough to wait forever."
[ZH] 乔梦容想起淑真的嘱托：“对了哥哥，淑真想麻烦你给乔公子捎句话，她在望月轩等着乔公子。”
[EN] Then Qiao Mengrong remembered her errand: "Oh, Brother—Shuzhen asked if you'd pass word to Young Master Qiao that she's waiting for him at Wangyue Pavilion."
[ZH] 申简辰没有作出回应，他第一次不想帮心爱的姑娘，既然对方已订亲，见了又有何用？
[EN] Shen Jianchen gave no reply. For the very first time, he refused to help the woman he loved—with Wenyuan already engaged, what good would meeting do?

#CH 133
[ZH] 时间一点一点过去，已过立冬，寒冬的冷气越发刺骨，大家都换上冬季衣裳。千里寻还是没等到文渊前来，难道仪王没有将她的话转达给文渊？
[EN] Days slipped past the Start of Winter, bringing biting cold that sent everyone into heavy winter coats. Still no sign of Wenyuan—had Prince Yi never passed along her message?
[ZH] 白风每隔几日会来望月轩，均是在大门外与苦荞相见。苦荞很懂小姐的心思，几次向白风打探乔公子的情况，白风只说不清楚，王爷已好久没去过乔府。
[EN] Every few days Bai Feng stopped outside the gate to see Kuqiao. Reading her mistress's heart, Kuqiao kept fishing for news of Young Master Qiao, only for Bai Feng to shrug that His Highness hadn't visited Qiao Manor in ages.
[ZH] 千里寻从充满期待到慢慢失望，再到努力逼迫自己什么都不要去想。可一点用处都没有，总是自觉或不自觉地想起文渊。
[EN] Hope faded to disappointment, and disappointment turned into Qianli Xun ordering herself to stop thinking about him—useless, since Wenyuan drifted straight back into her mind at every turn.
[ZH] 哥嫂来过一次，她试图从哥嫂的口中打探文渊的消息，他们则劝她不要再想乔家公子，其余一概未提。
[EN] Her brother and sister-in-law visited once; when she tried fishing for word of Wenyuan, they only urged her to forget him and said not a word more.
[ZH] 七八日未提笔写话本，只因又怕写缠绵悱恻的爱情。既然没有事情可打发时间，为转移内心的痛苦，不如继续动笔，毕竟有那么多听众等着。
[EN] For seven or eight days she hadn't touched her huaben script, dreading writing tender love scenes. With empty hours stretching ahead, she forced herself back to the desk to distract herself—and for all those waiting listeners.
[ZH] 千里寻坚持提笔接着上次的内容，写下红香与玄英爱情甜蜜的故事，可写着写着，她竟然改变初衷，给她们之间添加了一段虐恋。
[EN] Picking up where she left off on Hongxiang and Xuanying's sweet romance, her brush veered off course on its own, plunging them straight into bitter heartbreak.
[ZH] 听众还未被虐到，她竟边写边流泪，趁着写作，也把积压在内心为文渊为自己流的眼泪通通流出来。
[EN] Before a single listener had a chance to weep, tears streamed down Qianli Xun's own cheeks—crying out all the grief she had bottled up for Wenyuan and herself.
[ZH] 苦荞递上丝帕：“小姐今儿写的故事肯定感人，把您都感动得哭了。”
[EN] Handing over a silk handkerchief, Kuqiao murmured, "Today's chapter must be a tearjerker, Miss, making even you cry."
[ZH] 千里寻接过丝帕，将脸上和眼睛周围的泪水沾干净：“是感人，我能被自己写的情节感动，听书的人肯定也会被感动。”
[EN] Dabbing her wet cheeks and eyes, Qianli Xun managed a nod: "It is. If my own scene makes me cry, listeners will bawl their eyes out too."
[ZH] 夏果掀开帘子，惊喜地喊道：“小姐，下雪了！快出来瞧瞧！”
[EN] Pushing aside the door curtain, Xiaguo burst in beaming: "Miss, it's snowing! Come out and see!"
[ZH] 夏果进屋为小姐取下厚披风。
[EN] Hurrying inside, Xiaguo reached for her mistress's thick winter cape.
[ZH] 千里寻充满好奇，毕竟是她穿越而来遇到的第一场雪。
[EN] Curiosity stirred in Qianli Xun—her very first snowfall since crossing over!
[ZH] 苦荞很机灵，趁夏果不注意，把手稿收起来，从旁边拿本书，装模作样走到书架旁：“小姐别急，我帮您把书放好，再给您拿手炉。”
[EN] Quick-witted, Kuqiao swept the manuscript out of sight while Xiaguo's back was turned, grabbed a book to walk casually toward the shelf, and said, "No rush, Miss—let me put this book away and grab your hand warmer."
[ZH] 千里寻会心笑道：“不着急，我等你。”
[EN] Qianli Xun tossed her a knowing smile: "Take your time."
[ZH] 当夏果走到书桌跟前时，桌上已无纸张。她平日不怎么关心小姐写些什么，反而喜欢小姐坐在书桌前忙碌，因为这时，她都不用近身伺候，可以拿本书躲在丫鬟房，边读边想象与乔公子在一起的情景。
[EN] By the time Xiaguo turned to the desk, not a loose page remained. Never caring what her mistress scribbled, Xiaguo actually loved her desk hours—freeing her to grab a book, hole up in the maids' quarters, and daydream about Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] “小姐，我把披风给您披上。”
[EN] "Let me put your cape on, Miss."
[ZH] 千里寻放下手中的丝帕，站起来，任由夏果为她披好披风。披风是藕粉色，又厚实又暖和，帽子的周围毛茸茸的，摸上去很舒服。
[EN] Setting down the handkerchief, Qianli Xun stood as Xiaguo draped the plush lotus-pink cape over her shoulders, its furry hood soft and snug.
[ZH] 苦荞把手炉拿来，递给小姐。
[EN] Kuqiao hurried back with a warm brass hand warmer.
[ZH] 千里寻接过，手炉的热气通过手掌传遍全身，顿觉暖洋洋的，暂时将文渊没来之事搁置一边。
[EN] Heat radiated straight from her palms through her whole body, easing the chill—and pushing Wenyuan's absence aside for a breath.
[ZH] “走吧，我们去赏雪。”
[EN] "Come on, let's watch the snow."
[ZH] 夏果掀开帘子，随着一股凉气袭来，千里寻已看到外面飘着鹅毛大雪。她迈开脚走出去，站在院中，雪刚下不久，还没将望月轩覆盖。
[EN] As Xiaguo lifted the curtain, crisp winter air rushed in along with huge goose-feather snowflakes drifting down. Stepping into the yard, Qianli Xun saw the snow had only just begun, not yet carpeting Wangyue Pavilion white.
[ZH] 苦荞替小姐把披风上的帽子戴好，乖乖地站立一旁，跟夏果欣赏飘落而来的雪花。
[EN] Kuqiao pulled the furry hood up over her mistress's head, then stood beside Xiaguo admiring the falling flakes.
[ZH] 千里寻抬起头，望着空中的雪花，大片大片向地面落下；她又看向地面，很多雪花刚落在地上便消失得无影无踪。
[EN] Tipping her face skyward, Qianli Xun watched fat flakes float down in sheets, then looked down as flake after flake melted into nothing the instant it kissed the stones.
[ZH] 她慨叹雪花的生命如此短暂，难道它们从天上落下只为消失？
[EN] How brief a snowflake's life—drifting down from heaven just to vanish?
[ZH] 她想到自己，穿越前的她已死亡，就像雪花，本以为从天空落下会更精彩，谁知却是奔向死亡，一场地震将她拥有的一切断送。
[EN] She thought of herself before crossing over—dead like a melted flake, thinking life was climbing toward its peak only for an earthquake to snuff everything out in a blink.
[ZH] 有幸穿越而来，获得新生，她很珍惜，珍惜到甚至担心是死后的自己做的一场离奇的梦。直到遇见文渊，她才确定不是梦。
[EN] Grateful for this second shot at life, she treasured it so fierce she sometimes feared it was only a bizarre dying dream—until meeting Wenyuan made it feel undeniable.
[ZH] 为什么文渊明明已自由，却不来找她？难道一切真的只是梦？如果是梦为什么她看到的如此真切？苦荞和夏果真实不虚，还有眼前飘落的雪花，还有手炉，里面热气满满。
[EN] Then why, if Wenyuan walked free now, hadn't he come? Was this all a dream after all? Yet everything felt too real: Kuqiao and Xiaguo beside her, cold flakes brushing her nose, the hot brass hand warmer burning against her palms.
[ZH] 千里寻十分珍惜来之不易的生命，珍惜到想要走好每一步。和离是第一步，走得艰难，结果倒也圆满。与文渊的感情是第二步，本以为万无一失，却困难重重，难到无人能够帮助自己，难到她只能窝在望月轩等待上天掉下一个大大的馅饼……
[EN] Treasuring this hard-won life, she wanted every step to count. Her mutual divorce had been step one—rocky, yet a clean victory. Falling for Wenyuan had been step two—looking airtight until walls closed in from every side, leaving her trapped in Wangyue Pavilion waiting on a miracle from the sky...
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷冒雪走来，见自家姑娘站在雪中，批评道：“你们两个丫头，平时我是怎么教你们的？怎能让姑娘在外面待着？小心着凉！”
[EN] Striding in through the snow, Matron Wei scolded both maids: "How do I train you two?! Letting Young Lady stand out in the snow—want her catching cold?!"
[ZH] 两个丫鬟笑而不语。
[EN] Both maids ducked their heads with sheepish grins.
[ZH] 千里寻边欣赏飘落的雪花，边说：“嬷嬷莫怪她们，是我要出来赏雪。”
[EN] Watching flakes whirl down, Qianli Xun said, "Don't scold them, Matron—I wanted to watch the snow."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷不再批评，倒也没说让姑娘进屋的话：“姑娘，门外来了两个人，你要不要见见？”
[EN] Dropping the lecture without dragging her indoors, Matron Wei said, "Two guests are at the gate asking for you, Young Lady—want to see them?"
[ZH] 千里寻不解道：“嬷嬷今日说话奇怪，还没说来人是谁？却问我见不见？”
[EN] Qianli Xun blinked: "Odd way to ask, Matron—haven't even told me who they are!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“乔家人。”
[EN] "The Qiaos."
[ZH] “难道是文渊？”千里寻充满期待。
[EN] "Wenyuan?!" Hope leaped in her chest.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷为难道：“不是乔家公子，是乔家老爷和夫人。”
[EN] Matron Wei hesitated: "Not Young Master Qiao—Master Qiao and Madam Qiao."
[ZH] 千里寻感到失望：“他们来做什么？”
[EN] Disappointment dropped heavy in her stomach: "What do they want?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷回道：“并未对我说起，只说不见到姑娘今日就不离开。我看未必是好事，姑娘不见也罢，他们想在外面等多久，让他们等去。”
[EN] "Wouldn't say—only swore they won't leave today until they see you. Smells like bad news to me; skip seeing them if you want, let them freeze outside as long as they like."
[ZH] 千里寻犹豫片刻：“嬷嬷尽管请他们进来，我在会客厅等着，不管他们为何而来，起码我能得知文渊的近况。”
[EN] Pausing a beat, Qianli Xun decided: "Show them into the reception hall, Matron. Whatever they came for, at least I'll hear how Wenyuan is."
[ZH] 夏果听到是乔家来人，充满期待，小姐多久没见到乔公子，她就有多久没见到，她对乔公子的思念并不比小姐差，便与苦荞紧跟小姐来到会客厅。
[EN] Hearing Qiao Manor visitors had arrived lit a spark in Xiaguo too—she missed Young Master Qiao every bit as much as Miss did—and she followed close behind with Kuqiao into the reception hall.
[ZH] 到达会客厅，千里寻脱掉披风，放下手炉，正襟危坐。
[EN] Slipping off her cape and setting down her hand warmer, Qianli Xun sat upright in the host's seat.
[ZH] 说心里话，她多少有些紧张，要见她的二位差一点成为她的婆婆和公爹，也不知他们前来拜访有何目的？难不成想亲自求她嫁给文渊？
[EN] Truth be told, nerves prickled inside her: the two elders coming through that door had almost become her father- and mother-in-law. Why show up now? Surely not to beg her to marry Wenyuan?
[ZH] 千里寻立刻否认了后面的想法，乔家已定下苗知瑜，怎么可能再让文渊娶她？而她又万万不愿给他人当妾室。
[EN] She killed that fantasy in half a second—he was already betrothed to Miao Zhiyu, and she'd rather die than be a concubine.
[ZH] 带着忐忑的心情终于等来两位长辈。
[EN] Heart thumping, she waited as the two elders approached.
[ZH] 在屏风外，乔守仁与钱氏脱掉厚披风，交给一旁的丫鬟。韦嬷嬷带他们拐过屏风，向里走来。
[EN] Behind the entry screen, Qiao Shouren and Madam Qian handed their snowy capes to waiting maids before Matron Wei led them around into the hall.
[ZH] 千里寻犹豫要不要站起来迎接，以显示她是知礼之人，可想到走来的二位切断她与文渊一切往来，气就不打一处来，干脆坐着，拿出主人的款儿来。
[EN] Qianli Xun wavered over standing up out of politeness—then remembered these two had severed every thread between her and Wenyuan! Temper flaring, she stayed seated in full host's pride.
[ZH] 谁知乔守仁与钱氏话还未说，竟然跪下。这倒把千里寻吓住，她已坐不住，只好站起来，惊得说不出一句话。
[EN] Before a single word left their mouths, Qiao Shouren and Madam Qian dropped straight to their knees! Shock jolted Qianli Xun straight out of her chair, struck speechless.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷见情势不妙，上前扶道：“二位这是做什么？想折我家姑娘的寿吗？什么话都没说，就跪下？快起来！”
[EN] Alarmed, Matron Wei rushed forward to haul them up: "What stunt is this?! Trying to curse our Young Lady's lifespan—kneeling before saying a word?! Get up at once!"
[ZH] 二人不愿起身。
[EN] Neither elder budged.
[ZH] 乔守仁说：“我们二人前来，是想请朱家姑娘放了我家文渊，若朱家姑娘不答应，我二人绝不起身。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren spoke: "We came to beg Miss Zhu to let our Wenyuan go. Unless Miss Zhu agrees, we will not rise."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷气道：“二位说的什么话！我家姑娘自搬进望月轩，几乎没出过门，何来放不放过乔公子？再说你们乔家已与苗家定亲，还来求我家姑娘做什么？若再不起来，我可不管什么皇亲国戚，直接拿大棒子赶人！”
[EN] Fury exploded in Matron Wei: "What garbage is that?! Since moving to Wangyue Pavilion our Young Lady has barely stepped out the door—who's holding Young Master Qiao hostage?! Your house already engaged him to the Miaos—why come hounding our Young Lady?! Get up right now, imperial kin or not, or I'll grab a broomstick and chase you out!"
[ZH] 钱氏哭道：“呜呜……！我家文渊为了朱家姑娘连命都不要，你说我们不来求她，又能求谁？呜呜……！”
[EN] Madam Qian burst into sobs: "Waaah! Our Wenyuan is throwing his own life away over Miss Zhu—who else could we beg?! Waaah!"
[ZH] 千里寻已忍不住，问道：“文渊他……他怎么了？”
[EN] Qianli Xun couldn't hold back: "Wenyuan... what happened to him?!"
[ZH] 乔守仁说：“自从与苗家定亲，文渊一直闹着退婚。婚姻大事，岂可由他说了算？我当然不同意。想着他闹一阵儿慢慢会好，谁知近日他开始用绝食逼迫我们，到今天已绝食三日，他的身体怎能受得了！我和他母亲都跪着求他，他说除非朱家姑娘说放弃的话，他才会同意娶苗家姑娘。姑娘，我们两个年纪加起来已超过百岁，跪在这里，求求你，放了我家文渊，否则乔家就要绝后了！”
[EN] Qiao Shouren's voice cracked: "Ever since the Miao betrothal, Wenyuan has raised hell demanding we break it off! How can a son dictate his own marriage? Of course I refused. Thought he'd blow off steam and settle down—instead he started starving himself to force our hand! Three full days without food—how can his body take it?! His mother and I even knelt begging him, and he swore only if Miss Zhu herself tells him she's giving up will he marry Miss Miao! Miss Zhu, between us two old bones we're past a hundred years old—kneeling here begging you, spare our Wenyuan, or the Qiao line dies out!"
[ZH] 乔守仁说完，不由老泪纵横，钱氏更是哭得伤心。
[EN] Old tears streamed down Qiao Shouren's weathered cheeks as Madam Qian wept harder still.
[ZH] 千里寻此刻才明白，文渊没来找她，不是因他放弃她，而是正在用他的方式努力挣扎，争取他们的未来。千里寻立刻原谅了文渊，更加心疼他的付出。
[EN] Clarity crashed over Qianli Xun: Wenyuan hadn't abandoned her—he had been fighting for their future in the only way he knew! Every ounce of blame dissolved into raw heartache for him.
[ZH] “你们起来吧，被长辈跪，我承受不起。”
[EN] "Please stand up. Having elders kneel to me is more than I can bear."
[ZH] 乔守仁坚持道：“姑娘若不答应放了文渊，我二人就长跪不起！”
[EN] Qiao Shouren held firm: "Until Miss Zhu promises to let Wenyuan go, we stay on our knees!"
[ZH] 本以为世上最可怕的是刀剑，今日才领略到守礼的话语和行为才真正逼得人无路可退。
[EN] She had thought blades were the deadliest weapons in this era—only now did she learn polite humility and filial duty could back a person further into a corner than any sword.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷生气道：“你们二人如此逼迫我家姑娘，是何道理？乔家公子绝食是乔家的事，与我家姑娘何干？你们还是回去好好劝说自家儿子，赶紧跟苗家成亲，不要再想着我家姑娘，什么皇亲国戚，我家姑娘可不稀罕！”
[EN] Matron Wei snapped in rage: "Bullying our Young Lady like this—where's the justice?! Your son starving himself is Qiao family business, nothing to do with our Young Lady! Go home and lecture your own son to marry Miss Miao and forget us—who cares about your imperial-relative prestige?!"
[ZH] 钱氏哭着说：“呜呜……！嬷嬷别用这话噎我们，朱家姑娘不答应放了文渊，我与老爷绝不起来，呜呜……！哪怕你用大棒子打我们，也绝不起来！呜呜……！”
[EN] Madam Qian wailed: "Waaah! Save your lectures, Matron—until Miss Zhu lets Wenyuan go, Master and I won't rise! Beat us with clubs and we still won't rise! Waaah!"
[ZH] 本以为等文渊半年太短，才过两个多月，情势逼得她不得不放弃。
[EN] Half a year had felt too short; barely two months in, fate had forced her hand.
[ZH] 想嫁给文渊已不可能，除乔家与苗家订婚一事阻挡外，乔家长辈已从心里对她生起的怨气，也令千里寻不敢向前踏出半步。爱依然在，面对现实，不得不放弃。
[EN] Marrying Wenyuan was dead—not just because of the Miao engagement, but because the bitter grudge these Qiao elders now bore against her left zero room to step forward. Love still burned, yet reality demanded she let go.
[ZH] 她不想在乔家人面前表现得痛苦难过，努力平复心情：“若二位再跪下去，我绝不答应放弃文渊。”
[EN] Refusing to show a crack of pain in front of the Qiaos, she steadied her breath: "Stay on your knees one second longer, and I swear I will never let Wenyuan go."
[ZH] 钱氏立刻停止哭声，与乔守仁四目相对，互相搀扶着爬起来。
[EN] Madam Qian choked off mid-sob, traded a glance with Qiao Shouren, and both elders scrambled to help each other up.

#CH 134
[ZH] 千里寻见二位长辈站起来，心中略感轻松，他们若继续跪下去，她岂不是真要折寿？
[EN] Seeing both elders rise took a tiny weight off Qianli Xun—if they had stayed kneeling, she might really have lost ten years off her lifespan!
[ZH] 二人要么不出现，一出现便伤在她的要害处。
[EN] Never showing their faces until today, and the second they walked in, they struck straight at her softest spot.
[ZH] 尽管问了也没有任何用处，她还是想问问藏在心底的问题：“乔老爷，我与文渊相爱，你为何要因不实的流言拆散我们？”
[EN] Pointless as asking was, one question burned inside her: "Master Qiao, Wenyuan and I love each other—why tear us apart over false rumors?"
[ZH] 乔守仁直言不讳：“没有流言时，因姑娘和离过一次，我本就不同意。怎奈仪王出面，将我说服，才同意到朱家提亲，谁知流言又起，我更是万万不能同意，即使流言是假，文渊也万万不能娶一位被全城百姓非议之人。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren spoke blunt: "Even before rumors broke, I opposed the match because you had been through a mutual divorce. Only Prince Yi talked me round into proposing to the Zhu house—then rumors exploded. True or false, Wenyuan can never marry a woman mocked by the whole city."
[ZH] 仪王又在此事中帮过她与文渊，看来此事不成，并非人力使然，是她与文渊有缘而无分。
[EN] Prince Yi had stepped up for her and Wenyuan back then too. So no human villain had ruined them—they were fated to meet and never meant to stay.
[ZH] “你们走吧，告诉文渊，明日未时，城外神柳下相会，我定给二位一个满意的答复。”
[EN] "Leave now. Tell Wenyuan to meet me beneath the Divine Willow outside the city tomorrow at the hour of wei. You two will have your answer."
[ZH] 乔守仁很不乐意儿子与朱家姑娘单独见面，又没有更好的办法，只好同意：“还请姑娘记住今日答应我的事。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren hated letting his son see Miss Zhu alone, yet had no better option: "See that Miss Zhu keeps the word you gave us today."
[ZH] 千里寻苦笑一下，没有回应。
[EN] Qianli Xun offered only a dry, bitter smile.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷气道：“我家姑娘话已说得如此明白，乔老爷还不信任她？二位赶紧走吧，望月轩不欢迎你们，下次不要再来。”
[EN] Matron Wei snapped, "Our Young Lady spoke plain as day—still don't trust her, Master Qiao? Leave at once! You're not welcome at Wangyue Pavilion; don't show up here again!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的话说到这个份上，乔守仁与钱氏不好再待下去，起身离开。
[EN] Driven out by Matron Wei's sharp tongue, Qiao Shouren and Madam Qian had no face to linger and left.
[ZH] 千里寻全身像失去力气一般，瘫坐在椅子上，一言不发。
[EN] Every drop of strength drained from Qianli Xun; she sank limp into her chair without a word.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷很是心疼：“姑娘，回屋睡会儿，床上比椅子舒服。”
[EN] Heart aching for her, Matron Wei urged, "Go lie down in your room, Young Lady—a bed beats a hard chair."
[ZH] 两个丫鬟上前扶住小姐，千里寻才使出全身的力气站起来，向闺房走去。一进屋，躺倒在床上，用被子把全身裹起来……
[EN] Supported by both maids, Qianli Xun summoned the last of her strength to walk back to her chamber, collapse onto bed, and pull the quilt over her head...
[ZH] 与苗家订亲后，乔文仙重获自由，可他无脸面对淑真，想要等退了与苗家的婚约再去见她，便开始闹着退婚。
[EN] After the Miao betrothal freed him from lockup, Qiao Wenxian could not face Shuzhen until he broke off the Miao engagement, so he had waged war demanding to annul it.
[ZH] 无论怎么闹，长辈都不同意，父亲甚至再次以死相逼，他便消停下来。父亲不关他，他倒自己把自己关在屋里，一刻也不愿出去。
[EN] Yet neither argument nor pleading moved his elders; when Father threatened suicide again, he fell quiet—locking himself in his own room without needing Father's padlock.
[ZH] 与苗知瑜的婚礼定在开春以后，眼看天气越来越冷，离春天越来越近，乔文仙也越来越急，才想出绝食的法子。
[EN] With the spring wedding to Miao Zhiyu looming closer as the winter chill deepened, desperation drove him to a hunger strike.
[ZH] 绝食纯粹是表演给父母看，私底下南双会偷偷给他找些吃的，不至于身体抗不过去。淑真叮嘱过，要他爱惜身体，他也不希望成功后让淑真看到不健康的他。
[EN] Truth be told, it was half theater for his parents—Nan Shuang slipped him snacks on the quiet so his health would not collapse, remembering Shuzhen's plea to take care of himself so she would not see a wreck once he won.
[ZH] 偷偷吃的东西哪能与饭食相比？乔文仙又比之前瘦了一圈儿，加之他在父母跟前装出虚弱的样子，他们心疼得泪流满面。
[EN] Still, stolen snacks could not replace hot meals; he dropped another size thinner and played up his frailty in front of his parents until tears streamed down their faces.
[ZH] 乔文仙于心不忍，可为了与淑真的幸福，他狠心坚持住没有后退。
[EN] Guilt tugged at him watching them weep, yet for a life with Shuzhen, he steeled himself and refused to budge.
[ZH] 闹到此等境况，父亲依然不松口，强调婚姻已定下，岂可退婚？退婚后苗家姑娘怎么见人？并劝他忘了朱家姑娘。
[EN] Even pushed this far, Father would not yield—how could they break a sealed betrothal and ruin Miss Miao's reputation?—ordering him to forget Miss Zhu.
[ZH] 乔文仙不愿意，装作有气无力的样子，坚定地说：“除非淑真亲口告诉我她要放弃，我才会努力忘记她。”
[EN] So Qiao Wenxian lay weak and resolute: "Unless Shuzhen tells me to my face she's giving up, I won't stop."
[ZH] 这才有了乔守仁与钱氏拜访望月轩之事。
[EN] And that had sent Qiao Shouren and Madam Qian trudging through snow to Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 乔守仁从望月轩回来，与钱氏一起来到儿子居住的院子，走进屋内，脱掉披风，身边的丫鬟负责拿着。
[EN] Back at Qiao Manor, Qiao Shouren and Madam Qian entered their son's courtyard, stepped inside, and handed their snowy capes to waiting maids.
[ZH] 二人拐到里屋，看到躺在床上一脸憔悴有气无力的儿子，叹道：“你如此景况，可值得？朱家姑娘看上去比你精神许多！”
[EN] Entering the inner bedchamber and eyeing their gaunt, listless son sprawled on the bed, Qiao Shouren sighed: "Look at the state of you—was it worth it? Miss Zhu looked livelier than you by a mile!"
[ZH] 父亲提到淑真，乔文仙有了精神，欠起身：“父亲见过淑真？”
[EN] At Shuzhen's name, life sparked in Qiao Wenxian as he propped himself up: "Father saw Shuzhen?!"
[ZH] 没等乔守仁说话，钱氏流着泪上前坐在床边，一副心疼儿子的样子：“我与你爹见过她，她看上去一点儿都不伤心，我看她心里根本没有你，她让我们捎话，说明日未时让你去城外的神柳下与她相见。”
[EN] Before Qiao Shouren spoke, Madam Qian sank tearful onto the bedside: "Your father and I saw her. Not a trace of sadness on her—it's clear she doesn't care about you at all! She sent word for you to meet her beneath the Divine Willow tomorrow at the hour of wei."
[ZH] 乔文仙坐起来：“淑真要见我？难道爹娘已同意退掉与苗家的亲事？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian bolted upright: "Shuzhen wants to see me?! Did Father and Mother agree to cancel the Miao betrothal?!"
[ZH] 乔守仁说：“退亲不可能，允许你去见她，是因为爹娘看明白，她心里根本没有你，让她把话与你说清楚，好让你死心。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren cut cold: "Breaking the betrothal is out of the question! We're letting you see her because we saw straight through her—she has no feelings left for you! Go hear it from her own lips and snap out of it."
[ZH] 乔文仙担忧道：“你们对淑真说了什么？”
[EN] Alarm flashed across Qiao Wenxian: "What did you say to Shuzhen?!"
[ZH] 钱氏哄骗道：“我与你父亲是何等身份之人？岂能哄她？只是说看在你对她痴心一片，希望她也念在你们二人有情，答应做你的妾室，可她根本不愿意，说与你的情到此为止，约你明日相见，将话说明白……”
[EN] Madam Qian lied smooth: "With our standing, would we ever bully her? Seeing how devoted you are, we even offered—for love's sake—to let her enter as your concubine! Yet she refused flat, said everything between you two is finished, and called you out tomorrow to make a clean break..."
[ZH] “不！你们怎能让她做我的妾室？淑真是生气才会说无情的话，我这就去找她！”乔文仙推开钱氏，从床上下来，刚走几步，发现没有穿外衣，“南双，伺候我穿衣，把那件红色的衣裳拿来，不能让淑真看到我憔悴的样子……”
[EN] "No! How dare you ask her to be a concubine?! Shuzhen only spoke cold out of anger—I'm going to find her right now!" Pushing past Madam Qian, Qiao Wenxian swung out of bed, caught himself half-dressed, and barked, "Nan Shuang, dress me—bring my red robe! I can't let Shuzhen see me looking like a ghost..."
[ZH] 南双正要给公子取衣裳，乔守仁大喊一声：“够了！你要闹到什么时候？停止吧！你怎么连个女人都不如？朱家姑娘像个冰块一样冷静，你却在这里又是绝食，又是退婚，还担心人家见到你的样子伤心，你还是不是乔家的儿子？是不是临安城让人羡慕的才子？”
[EN] Just as Nan Shuang reached for the robe, Qiao Shouren thundered: "Enough! How long will this circus drag on?! Stop! Are you weaker than a woman?! Miss Zhu sat cool as ice, while here you are starving yourself, demanding annulments, fretting she'll cry seeing you pale—are you even a son of the Qiao house, a scholar admired across Lin'an?!"
[ZH] 父亲的话令乔文仙痛苦，加之绝食导致气力不足，他快要倒下。
[EN] His father's lash combined with days of half-starvation made Qiao Wenxian sway, near collapse.
[ZH] 南双见状，上前扶住公子，把他扶到椅子跟前坐下。
[EN] Nan Shuang jumped in to catch him and ease him into a chair.
[ZH] 钱氏哭道：“儿啊！你为她这样不值得！呜呜……！”
[EN] Madam Qian sobbed: "My boy! She isn't worth wrecking yourself over! Waaah!"
[ZH] 乔文仙面无表情，冷冷地说：“还不都是被你们逼的。”
[EN] Face blank as stone, Qiao Wenxian murmured chill: "You two forced me to this."
[ZH] 乔守仁命令道：“别哭了，跟我走，让他一个人好好想想。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren ordered, "Quit crying and come with me—leave him alone to think straight."
[ZH] 钱氏边抹眼泪边往外走。
[EN] Dabbing her eyes, Madam Qian walked out.
[ZH] 乔守仁刚走到外屋又拐回来，用缓和的语气说：“退婚不可能，朱家姑娘已放下你，你也该知道做些什么。我与你母亲后退一步，如若明日你能说动她当你的妾室，开春后正室妾室一起进乔府。”
[EN] Reaching the outer room, Qiao Shouren paused, stepped back in, and softened his voice: "The Miao wedding stands. Miss Zhu has already moved on, so act like a man. Still, your mother and I will take one step back: if you can talk her into being your concubine tomorrow, both wife and concubine enter Qiao Manor together come spring."
[ZH] 说完乔守仁甩下儿子，穿上披风，毅然走出屋外，钱氏也跟上。
[EN] With that, Qiao Shouren threw on his cape and strode out into the snow, Madam Qian trailing behind.
[ZH] 外面的雪越下越大，地上已经积了一层，留下乔守仁与钱氏踩过的脚印。
[EN] Snow fell thicker outside, blanketing the stones where Qiao Shouren and Madam Qian left crisp footprints.
[ZH] 到院外后，钱氏迎着雪擦擦眼泪，忍不住问：“老爷，你真的想让朱家姑娘给文渊当妾室？”
[EN] Beyond the courtyard gate, Madam Qian wiped tears against the swirling flakes and asked, "Husband, would you really let Miss Zhu enter as Wenyuan's concubine?"
[ZH] 乔守仁站住：“她想给文渊当妾室，下辈子吧，名声坏成那样！”
[EN] Qiao Shouren stopped dead: "Her, Wenyuan's concubine? In her next life! With a reputation that filthy?!"
[ZH] 钱氏不解：“老爷为何要说刚才的话？”
[EN] Puzzled, Madam Qian asked, "Then why tell him that?"
[ZH] 乔守仁笑道：“一来安慰文渊，给他点儿希望，不至于太难过，也不至于怕被朱家姑娘拒绝而不愿赴约。二来，据我观察，朱家姑娘根本不可能答应给文渊当妾室，此话在文渊跟前说了也不怕。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren smiled sly: "First, toss Wenyuan a scrap of hope so he doesn't collapse or duck tomorrow's meeting out of fear. Second, from what I saw of Miss Zhu today, she'd die before accepting concubine—safe as houses making that promise!"
[ZH] “还是老爷高明，只是苦了文渊。”钱氏又不由流出几滴眼泪。
[EN] "Nobody outsmarts you, Husband—just breaks my heart for Wenyuan." Fresh tears brimmed in Madam Qian's eyes.
[ZH] 公子的样子令南双伤心：“公子，我到厨房给您端些吃的，您吃后才能精神些，明日好去见幽栖居士。”
[EN] Inside, aching for his master, Nan Shuang urged, "Let me fetch food from the kitchen, Young Master—get some strength back before seeing Youqi Jushi tomorrow."
[ZH] 乔文仙每日想着淑真，没有一日不想见到她，忍到现在，无非是想将一切事情摆顺之后，再奔赴到她面前，兴奋地告诉她：“淑真，我们成亲吧。”
[EN] Not a day had passed without Qiao Wenxian yearning for Shuzhen; he had held off visiting only hoping to fix everything first, then rush to her beaming, "Shuzhen, let's get married!"
[ZH] 事情还未摆顺，就要面见她，很可能明日一见，他与淑真的感情也会到此为止。
[EN] Now he had to face her with nothing fixed—knowing tomorrow's meeting might end everything between them.
[ZH] 乔文仙既渴望见面，又怕见面，他很矛盾。想到已有的婚约，冷笑着，自嘲道：“哼，你已有婚约，还有什么理由让她等你？她此时不放弃更待何时？难道你想让她等你一辈子，为你孤独终老？”
[EN] Equal parts desperate to see her and terrified to see her, he let out a bitter laugh at himself: "Hmph—engaged to another woman, what right do you have asking her to wait? If she doesn't quit now, when would she? Expect her to grow old alone waiting on you?"
[ZH] 南双听不明白公子的自语，继续问：“公子想吃什么？我去吩咐厨房做。”
[EN] Not catching his master's muttering, Nan Shuang asked again, "What do you feel like eating, Young Master? I'll tell the kitchen."
[ZH] 乔文仙不想再绝食，明日要见到淑真，状态不能太差：“随便端些吃的来。”
[EN] Dropping the hunger strike—he could not look like a corpse facing Shuzhen tomorrow—Qiao Wenxian waved a hand: "Whatever's ready."
[ZH] 南双很开心：“好嘞！我这就到厨房给公子端饭！”
[EN] Nan Shuang lit up: "Right away! I'll run to the kitchen!"
[ZH] 南双刚走出屋外，在院子里碰到米姨娘，一个丫鬟负责撑伞，为她挡雪，一个丫鬟手中提着食盒。
[EN] Stepping into the yard, Nan Shuang bumped right into Concubine Mi arriving beneath an umbrella held by one maid while another carried a food carrier.
[ZH] 米氏批评道：“你这小子，急急匆匆干什么去？”
[EN] Concubine Mi chided him: "Where are you racing off to?"
[ZH] 南双赶快解释：“米姨娘来了，公子要吃东西，我去厨房给他端些。”
[EN] Nan Shuang answered fast: "Concubine Mi! Young Master wants food—I was running to the kitchen."
[ZH] “不用忙活，没看我身边的丫鬟提着食盒。”
[EN] "Save your legs—didn't you see my maid carrying a food carrier?"
[ZH] 米氏说着走进屋内，撑伞的丫鬟收了伞，放在门口，又替米姨娘解下披风，拿在手中。
[EN] Stepping indoors, Concubine Mi had the umbrella-holding maid fold the umbrella by the doorway and take her snowy cape.
[ZH] 南双只好作罢，继续进屋伺候公子。
[EN] Left with nothing to fetch, Nan Shuang trailed right back indoors to wait on his master.
[ZH] 米氏走进里屋，见儿子颓废的样子，双眼自然变红：“文渊，不要再折磨自己，娘炖了碗燕窝粥，给你补补身子。”
[EN] Entering the inner chamber and seeing her worn-out son, Concubine Mi's eyes reddened at once: "Quit torturing yourself, Wenyuan. Mama stewed bird's-nest porridge to build your strength back."
[ZH] 乔文仙抬头，瞅一眼生他的人：“娘，我是您十月怀胎生下，您应该希望我好，为何连您也反对我跟淑真在一起？”
[EN] Lifting his eyes to the woman who gave him birth, Qiao Wenxian asked, "Mama, you carried me ten months—you ought to want me happy. Why are even you against me marrying Shuzhen?"
[ZH] “傻孩子，正因为我把你生下，才希望你好，希望你好，才不会让你把一个又和离，名声又不好的女子娶回来。”
[EN] "Silly boy, it's because I gave birth to you that I want the best for you—and that means never letting you marry a divorced woman with a ruined name!"
[ZH] “可我只想娶淑真。”
[EN] "Only Shuzhen is the one I want to marry."
[ZH] “傻孩子，你还年轻，根本不懂婚姻是怎么回事，乖，听大人的不会有错。”
[EN] "You're too young to grasp what marriage really is. Listen to your elders—we won't steer you wrong."
[ZH] “我再年轻，也结交甚广，在汴梁去过不少地方，还参加过官家的宴会，什么没见过？要娘哄骗。”
[EN] "Young? I've traveled wide, seen Bianliang, attended His Majesty's banquets—what haven't I seen? Don't treat me like a child."
[ZH] “你再见多识广，只要没成亲，就谈不上成熟。朱家姑娘为何比你冷静，不过是因为她结过一次婚，又离过一次，自然比你更明白婚姻是怎么回事。”
[EN] "See the whole world and you're still green until you've been wed. Why is Miss Zhu twice as coolheaded as you? Because she's been married and divorced once—she knows what marriage really takes."
[ZH] 米氏的话刺痛乔文仙的内心，难道淑真对他的感情并非他感受到的那样浓烈？
[EN] Concubine Mi's remark pierced straight through Qiao Wenxian—could Shuzhen's love never have burned as fierce as he believed?
[ZH] “好了，别想那么多，吃些东西，明日见到朱家姑娘，与她做个了断，回来后，好好过自己的日子。”
[EN] "Enough brooding. Eat up, make a clean break with Miss Zhu tomorrow, and come home to live a good life."
[ZH] 乔文仙的内心更是一阵刺痛：“好，我吃，也许吃饱，不会这么难过。”
[EN] Another twist of the knife inside him: "Fine, I'll eat. Maybe a full stomach hurts less."
[ZH] 丫鬟把燕窝粥从食盒中取出来，放在桌上。南双将公子扶到桌前，把他安顿好。
[EN] Setting the steaming bird's-nest porridge on the table, the maid stepped back while Nan Shuang helped his master to a chair.
[ZH] 乔文仙拿起勺子边吃边回忆与淑真甜蜜的过往，不由露出笑容。他不相信淑真不再爱他，定是父母欺骗他，只有明日见过才知真伪。
[EN] Lifting his spoon, memories of sweet days with Shuzhen rose up, bringing a faint smile to his lips. He refused to believe Shuzhen had stopped loving him—his parents had to be lying! Tomorrow, face to face, he would know the truth.

#CH 135
[ZH] 雪下到半夜才停，望月轩内积了厚厚一层，一早起来，下人们忙着清扫道路上的积雪。
[EN] Snow fell past midnight before ceasing, laying a thick white blanket across Wangyue Pavilion. At dawn, servants set to sweeping the garden paths clear.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷知道姑娘内心苦，吩咐苦荞和夏果不要催促她起床，想躺多久就躺多久，只需将饭食按时端进去，以防姑娘饿了想吃。
[EN] Knowing her mistress's heartache, Matron Wei told Kuqiao and Xiaguo not to wake her—let her lie abed as long as she wanted, just keep warm meals coming in case hunger struck.
[ZH] 千里寻早已醒来，只是不想起床，不想让大家看到她红肿的眼泡。昨日一躺在床上不断告诫自己：“不要哭，不要哭，千万不要哭。”还是不由自主哭起来。
[EN] Qianli Xun had been wide awake for hours, refusing to get up so nobody would see her puffy, swollen eyes. All night under the quilt she had ordered herself—"Don't cry, don't cry, whatever you do don't cry"—and sobbed her eyes out anyway.
[ZH] 第一次投入感情，如此结局，到底哪里做错？
[EN] Her very first real dive into love, ending like this—what on earth had she done wrong?
[ZH] 她自认为没有错，只怪散播流言之人，若抓住此人，定要将他暴揍十日，不！整整一年，以泄心头之恨！
[EN] Not a single thing! All blame belonged to the rat behind those rumors—catch him and she would pummel him ten days straight, no, a whole year straight!
[ZH] 千里寻开始怀疑净光法师所说，她坚持正思正念，自认行为无误，为何还会招来如此非议？搞得她与文渊无法在一起。
[EN] Doubt crept in over Master Jingguang's teaching: she had clung to Right Thought and Right Mindfulness, walking clean and straight—why still get smeared until she and Wenyuan were ripped apart?
[ZH] 是不是人的命运背后有一双无形的手在操纵？如果有，千里寻想要抓住它，将那双手变为自己的，然后将命运掌握在自己手中。
[EN] Was some invisible hand pulling the strings of destiny? If so, Qianli Xun wanted to grab that hand, make it hers, and seize control of her own fate.
[ZH] 今日虽不下雪，天气却阴沉，积雪依然悄悄融化，给人的感觉比昨日还冷。
[EN] Though snow had stopped, leaden clouds hung low and melting snow made the air feel even colder than yesterday.
[ZH] 千里寻躺在被窝里也觉出一丝冷意，睡得肌肉酸痛，她爬起来，掀开床幔，向火盆望了望，火烧得很旺，难道这丝冷意来自内心？
[EN] A chill crept right under her quilt; aching from lying too long, she sat up, parted the bed curtains, and saw the brazier glowing bright red—so the chill came from inside her own chest.
[ZH] 得到确证后，千里寻不想再睡下去，被窝的暖意根本无法驱散她内心的寒冷，继续躺着又有何用？
[EN] Realizing no warm quilt could thaw the frost inside her, lying there felt pointless.
[ZH] 她唤了声：“苦荞！”
[EN] She called out: "Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 苦荞听到呼唤，端着刚做好的茶，进到里屋，见小姐双眼红肿，立刻明白她昨晚定是哭了许久。
[EN] Hearing her, Kuqiao hurried into the inner chamber carrying fresh hot tea—and one look at those puffy red eyes told her Miss had cried half the night.
[ZH] “小姐醒了，先喝盏茶，刚刚做好，还热着。”
[EN] "You're awake, Miss. Drink a bowl of hot tea first—just whisked."
[ZH] 千里寻接过茶盏，苦荞则将床幔向两边挂好。
[EN] As Qianli Xun took the tea bowl, Kuqiao hooked back the bed curtains on both sides.
[ZH] 一盏热茶下肚，体内暖暖的，刚才的寒意消失一些。未时还要与文渊相见，需鼓足精神。
[EN] Hot tea slid down, warming her core and pushing back the chill. She had to face Wenyuan at the hour of wei—time to pull herself together.
[ZH] 苦荞接过茶盏，放在一边，取来衣裳给小姐穿上，边穿边说：“刚才端过来的饭已凉，一会儿厨房会端来热的饭食，我伺候小姐梳洗完，您吃过饭再出去不晚。”
[EN] Taking the empty bowl, Kuqiao brought fresh robes and helped her dress: "The morning meal went cold, so the kitchen will send hot dishes right over. Wash up, eat something, and we'll head out with plenty of time."
[ZH] 千里寻才知她赖在床上的时间太长，只怪天阴，搞得她忘记时间。
[EN] Only then did Qianli Xun realize how late she had lain in bed, the overcast sky hiding the hour.
[ZH] 梳洗完后，要求苦荞为她施粉黛，想以此遮盖红肿的双眼。
[EN] After washing up, she had Kuqiao apply powder to mask her swollen eyes.
[ZH] 苦荞细心将粉黛施好，却一点儿用处也没有。
[EN] Careful as Kuqiao brushed it on, the puffiness refused to hide.
[ZH] “你去厨房找个煮熟的鸡蛋，要热的。”
[EN] "Run to the kitchen and bring back a hot hard-boiled egg."
[ZH] “是。”苦荞明白小姐的意思，跑去厨房拿鸡蛋。
[EN] "Right away!" Catching on, Kuqiao darted off.
[ZH] 没多久怀里揣着热鸡蛋回来，拿在手中，温度合适后，分别在小姐的两只眼睛上放置片刻。
[EN] Returning with a piping-hot egg tucked inside her sleeve, Kuqiao tested the heat until just right, then held it in gentle turns over each of her mistress's closed eyelids.
[ZH] 千里寻闭上眼，享受鸡蛋带给她的温度，温暖而舒适。她能感觉到双眼因痛哭淤积的血液一下子活跃开来，向周围扩展。
[EN] Eyes shut, Qianli Xun drank in the comforting warmth, feeling the congested puffiness loosen and fade.
[ZH] “小姐，这方法真管用，您的眼睛周围一下子好多了。”苦荞说着收了鸡蛋。
[EN] "Works like magic, Miss—the swelling around your eyes went right down!" Kuqiao put the egg aside.
[ZH] 千里寻向铜镜里瞧瞧，确实恢复如初。
[EN] Checking the bronze mirror, Qianli Xun saw her eyes looked normal again.
[ZH] “挑个颜色鲜艳的唇脂。”
[EN] "Pick out a bright lip rouge."
[ZH] 千里寻担心面对文渊时，她会流露出痛苦的表情，也许鲜艳的色彩会掩饰部分情绪。
[EN] Afraid pain might bleed onto her face before Wenyuan, maybe bold color would mask the cracks.
[ZH] 苦荞挑选好鲜艳的红色给小姐涂在唇上。
[EN] Kuqiao chose a vivid crimson and painted her mistress's lips.
[ZH] 厨房的丫鬟将饭食端来，在桌上摆好，再将放凉的饭食搁入盘中，行完礼离开。
[EN] Kitchen maids brought fresh hot dishes, laid the table, cleared the cold platters, and bowed out.
[ZH] 尽管昨晚没吃晚饭，今日一早也没吃早饭，却一点儿饥饿感都没有。千里寻看向冒着热气的饭食，毫无食欲。
[EN] Despite skipping both dinner last night and breakfast today, not a hint of hunger stirred; the steaming dishes held zero appeal.
[ZH] 她决定多少吃些，只怕到时没有力气说出伤害文渊的话。
[EN] Still, she had to force some down—she would need strength to speak the cruel words ahead.
[ZH] 她走到桌前，拿起筷子吃起来，每一口如同嚼蜡，还是努力吃着，第一次感受到吃饭竟然也有艰难的时候。
[EN] Sitting down, she lifted her chopsticks and forced down bite after bite, every mouthful tasting like dry wax—learning for the first time how hard swallowing food could be.
[ZH] 将饭菜吃过一半，实在吃不下去，也该到出发的时间。穿上暖和的披风，握好手炉，带上苦荞往出走。
[EN] Halfway through, she could not stomach another bite, and the hour had come. Putting on her warm winter cape and clutching her hand warmer, she stepped out with Kuqiao.
[ZH] 刚到院中，夏果从丫鬟房出来：“小姐！也带我去吧？”
[EN] Out in the yard, Xiaguo popped out of the maids' quarters: "Miss! Take me along too!"
[ZH] 千里寻没有说话，只顾向外走。夏果自然跟上，她想乔公子想得发疯，今日有机会见到，岂能放过。
[EN] Saying nothing, Qianli Xun walked straight on, and Xiaguo hurried right after—wild with longing for Young Master Qiao, refusing to miss a single chance to lay eyes on him.
[ZH] 街市的积雪已被清扫到道路两旁，做小买卖的依然冒着寒冷叫卖，闹市的繁华并未受到天气影响。
[EN] Snow stood piled along both edges of the city streets as hawkers braved the freezing air, market bustle undimmed by winter.
[ZH] 与往日唯一不同的是街道两边的房屋和树上铺上一层厚厚的雪，整个城市银装素裹。
[EN] Only the thick white blankets capping every roof and bare bough turned Lin'an into a silver-and-jade landscape.
[ZH] 千里寻没有心情欣赏城市的雪景，她只觉天气再寒冷也冷不过她的内心。
[EN] Qianli Xun had zero eye for snow scenery; no winter frost could match the cold inside her chest.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 到达山脚下，走下马车，脚下是厚厚的积雪。这种天气，上山的人少，积雪上没有留下多少脚印。
[EN] Stepping down from the carriage at the foot of the hill, deep snow met her boots. Few folk climbed the hill in weather like this, leaving the white drift near spotless.
[ZH] 千里寻手握暖炉，脚踩在积雪上，发出咯吱咯吱的声音。她一路听着声音向神柳处走去，山脚下的枯草被雪覆盖，不见踪影，高一些的植物也因冬季天气寒冷收敛起来。她只觉视线也变得开阔，远远的，已看到神柳。
[EN] Clutching her hand warmer, snow crunched beneath every step as she walked toward the Divine Willow. Dead grass lay buried deep, bare shrubs huddled tight, opening a clear vista—and far ahead stood the Divine Willow.
[ZH] 眼前白茫茫的一片，连神柳也穿上白色的冬装，从远处望去，分外妖娆。
[EN] Amid unbroken white, the willow wore its own cloak of frost, stark and breathtaking against the winter sky.
[ZH] 一片白色中，有一个红色的身影显得鲜艳夺目。那身影，千里寻再熟悉不过，自幽林别居分开，它不断出现在她的脑海。
[EN] And against that sea of white stood one vivid crimson figure—a silhouette Qianli Xun knew down to her bones, haunting her mind ever since Youlin Retreat.
[ZH] 她缓慢向身影走去，既想见又怕见。
[EN] Slowing her steps as she drew near, she both ached to reach him and dreaded reaching him.
[ZH] 乔文仙专门穿上红色的厚披风，他想让自己显得气色好些，不管淑真今日对他说什么，他都希望在她面前表现出最好的一面。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had chosen his thick crimson cape on purpose to lend color to his pale face—whatever Shuzhen had to say today, he meant to show her his best self.
[ZH] 南双看到走来的三个人影，在雪地里十分显眼。
[EN] Nan Shuang spotted three silhouettes moving toward them, standing out stark against the white snow.
[ZH] “公子，幽栖居士来了。”
[EN] "Young Master, Youqi Jushi is here."
[ZH] 乔文仙看似观察神柳上的积雪，心无时无刻不在身后，听到淑真到来，他还是兴奋地转过身，爱还在，见到爱人，无法不兴奋。
[EN] Though seeming to study snow-laden willow branches, Qiao Wenxian's whole soul had been tuned behind him. Hearing she had arrived, he spun round with an irrepressible rush of joy—love still burned, impossible to hide!
[ZH] 他忍不住迎上去，想要像上次一样抱住她，可淑真却向后退去。
[EN] Stepping forward to pull her into his arms like last time, he stopped dead as Shuzhen stepped back.
[ZH] 他失望地放下手臂：“淑真，我……我很想你……”
[EN] His arms dropped empty: "Shuzhen, I... I missed you so much..."
[ZH] 千言万语，乔文仙不知该如何表达。
[EN] A thousand words surged in his throat, none able to cross his lips.
[ZH] 文渊消瘦许多，尽管一脸笑容，衣着鲜艳，也难掩一脸憔悴。千里寻感到心疼，一段感情若无法给两个人带来幸福感，放弃也许是最好的选择。
[EN] Wenyuan had wasted so thin; bright robes and a forced smile could not hide the hollow weariness in his face. Pain twisted inside Qianli Xun—when love brought neither soul peace, letting go was the kindest cut.
[ZH] “文渊，你真傻，不值得为我拿自己的性命开玩笑。以后要吃好，喝好，把自己照顾好。你看我，今日要来见你，反而吃得更多。”
[EN] "How foolish you are, Wenyuan—gambling your own life over me isn't worth it! Eat well, drink well, and look after yourself from now on. Look at me: knowing I'd see you today, I ate extra!"
[ZH] 苦荞在心里叹道：“小姐怎么说瞎话？她明明吃得不多。”
[EN] Kuqiao sighed inside: "Why lie, Miss? You ate half a bowl at best!"
[ZH] 夏果满心满眼都在乔公子身上，他比上次见到时瘦了整整两圈，看得夏果心酸不已。
[EN] Eyes glued solely to Young Master Qiao, Xiaguo felt her heart twist seeing him two sizes thinner.
[ZH] 南双站在公子身后，盯着夏果看，她的美貌跟幽栖居士不相上下，如果能喜欢上自己那该多好。
[EN] Standing behind his master, Nan Shuang stared at Xiaguo—pretty enough to rival Youqi Jushi! If only she would look his way...
[ZH] 淑真故作轻松的语气和神态，让乔文仙感到不安。
[EN] Shuzhen's breezy tone sent dread rippling through Qiao Wenxian.
[ZH] “淑真，我知道你怨我，与苗家的亲事是父母背着我定下，我不得已才绝食逼他们退婚……”
[EN] "Shuzhen, I know you're angry—my parents arranged the Miao betrothal behind my back! I only starved myself to force them to cancel it..."
[ZH] “文渊！苗知瑜是好姑娘，又有才华，人品也好，在我被人唾骂时，她还能替我说话，你娶她，我……我也放心……”
[EN] "Wenyuan! Miao Zhiyu is a fine girl—talented and good-hearted. Even when the whole city cursed me, she stood up for me! Knowing you'll marry her puts my mind at ease..."
[ZH] “你为何要对我说这样的话？我想娶的人是你！你一直都知道！……淑真……”乔文仙抓住淑真握着暖炉的手，“我带你离开临安，我们去天涯海角，去哪里都行，只要能跟你在一起，在哪里都无所谓。”
[EN] "Why say things like that?! You're the one I want to marry—you've always known that! Shuzhen..." Catching her hands wrapped around the hand warmer, Qiao Wenxian pleaded, "Come away from Lin'an with me! To the ends of the earth, anywhere—as long as I'm with you, nothing else matters!"
[ZH] 这一刻，千里寻真的想答应，可理智再一次战胜情感，她抽回手，继续放在暖炉上，用平静的语气说道：“恐怕你以后会后悔。”
[EN] For one wild heartbeat Qianli Xun almost said yes—then reason clamped down hard. Pulling her hands back onto her hand warmer, she kept her voice flat: "You'd regret it sooner or later."
[ZH] 淑真抽回手，乔文仙更担心，他不想听到拒绝的话，果断说：“我永远不会后悔！”
[EN] Her pulled-back hands sent panic surging through him; refusing rejection, he swore, "I'll never regret it!"
[ZH] 千里寻装作冷笑的样子：“文渊，我今日才发现你是个极其自私的人，只顾自己开心，你有没有想过，离开后你的父母该怎么办？苗知瑜该怎么办？”
[EN] Qianli Xun forced a cold laugh: "Just realizing today how selfish you are, Wenyuan—thinking only of your own happiness! Did you ever stop to think what happens to your parents if you run off? Or to Miao Zhiyu?"
[ZH] 乔文仙靠近淑真，用微红的双眼盯着她：“那你说我们该怎么办？”
[EN] Stepping closer with reddened eyes locked onto hers: "Then tell me—what are we supposed to do?!"
[ZH] 千里寻不敢再看文渊，怕自己忍不住答应与他私奔。她狠心走向一边，瞧着神柳，从神柳的枝桠上落下一团雪，与地上的雪融为一体。
[EN] Unable to hold his gaze lest she cave and run off with him right now, Qianli Xun turned aside toward the Divine Willow, watching a clump of snow slip from a bough and vanish into the drift below.
[ZH] “文渊，我们分手吧。”
[EN] "Let's break up, Wenyuan."
[ZH] 还是听到分开的话，乔文仙闭上双眼，两滴泪从眼角落下，他转过头，瞧向淑真：“我不同意！”
[EN] Hearing the word he dreaded most, Qiao Wenxian squeezed his eyes shut as two tears rolled down, then whipped his gaze back to her: "I don't agree!"
[ZH] 千里寻努力挤出笑容：“真可笑，你有什么资格不同意？你连婚约都有，难道想让我嫁给你做妾室不成？”
[EN] Qianli Xun forced a thin smile: "Hilarious—what right do you have to disagree? You're an engaged man! Expect me to marry into your house as your concubine?"
[ZH] “你可愿意？”乔文仙充满期待，目前只有这个办法能解决所有问题。
[EN] "Would you?!" Hope flared sharp in his voice—it was the one bridge left!
[ZH] 千里寻努力做着心理建设，拒绝文渊是她今日来此的目的。感觉到自己的眼神足够坚定时，才大胆面向文渊：“我这辈子即使不嫁人，孤独终老，也绝不给人当妾室。”
[EN] Steeling every ounce of resolve—ending things clean was why she came today—she waited until her gaze felt cold as steel before facing him square: "Even if I never marry and grow old all alone, I will never be anyone's concubine."
[ZH] 乔文仙被刺痛，他失去理智：“淑真，你等着，我现在就去苗家退婚！”
[EN] Stung raw, reason snapped inside Qiao Wenxian: "Wait right here, Shuzhen—I'm marching straight to the Miao house to break off the betrothal right now!"
[ZH] 乔文仙说完，就往池塘对岸走去，南双不得已跟上，想要去扶自家公子，却被他用手甩开。
[EN] Spinning on his heel, he started off toward the far side of the pond. Nan Shuang hurried after to steady him, only to get shoved away.
[ZH] 池塘周围被雪覆盖，池塘里的水已结成厚厚的冰，冰封住水底的生命，也冰封住乔文仙的爱情。
[EN] Snow ringed the pond while thick ice sealed its surface—freezing the water beneath and freezing Qiao Wenxian's love along with it.
[ZH] 当文渊在雪中踉跄着靠近池塘岸边时，千里寻大喊道：“文渊！没用的！且不说你退不了婚，即使退了，我也不会嫁给你！”
[EN] As he stumbled across the snow toward the far bank, Qianli Xun cried out behind him: "It's useless, Wenyuan! Never mind you can't break it off—even if you did, I still wouldn't marry you!"
[ZH] 乔文仙停下踉跄的脚步，痛苦地转过身，大声问：“为什么？你为什么要说出这么狠心的话？”
[EN] His stumbling steps froze. Whipping back in raw agony, he shouted, "Why?! How can you say something so cruel?!"
[ZH] 千里寻努力抬起脚，踩着雪，靠近文渊：“因为……因为我不想为自己的幸福让他人痛苦，你若退婚，你的家人会痛苦，苗知瑜会更痛苦。……谢谢你文渊，谢谢你带给我一段美好的爱情，现在……是该结束的时候，你我……一别两宽，各自安好。”
[EN] Lifting heavy boots through the snow, Qianli Xun walked closer: "Because... I refuse to build my happiness on other people's pain! Break that betrothal and your family breaks, and Miao Zhiyu breaks even worse. Thank you, Wenyuan—thank you for giving me a beautiful romance. Now... it's time to end it. Let us part clean and wish each other peace."
[ZH] 千里寻说完，毅然转过身，泪如雨下，她控制住不去抽泣，怕文渊看到她颤抖的背影。
[EN] Turning away before he could answer, tears poured down her face like rain; she locked her shoulders tight against sobs so he would not see her back shaking.
[ZH] 两个丫鬟目睹自家小姐痛苦的表情，也是心如刀割。尤其是夏果，既为小姐，又为乔公子。小姐要离开，她只能与苦荞跟上。
[EN] Watching their mistress break apart tore at both maids—above all Xiaguo, aching for Miss and Young Master Qiao alike. When Miss walked away, they had no choice but to follow.
[ZH] 乔文仙听到“一别两宽，各自安好”的话语，再也忍不住，跪在雪地里，痛哭起来。
[EN] Hearing "part clean and wish each other peace" broke the last dam inside Qiao Wenxian: he collapsed to his knees in the snow and sobbed his heart out.
[ZH] 千里寻的背后留下文渊悲伤的哭声……
[EN] Behind Qianli Xun echoed Wenyuan's ragged, heartbroken weeping...

#CH 136
[ZH] 千里寻径直往回走，速度比来时快得多，她担心自己忍不住会回头。
[EN] Qianli Xun marched back far faster than she had come, terrified one breath slower would make her turn around.
[ZH] 直到听不见文渊的哭声时，她再也忍不住，手炉落在地上，周围的雪因热气开始融化……
[EN] Only once Wenyuan's weeping faded behind the hills did her strength give out; the hand warmer tumbled from her grip into the snow, melting a wet ring where it landed...
[ZH] 眼看小姐要倒下，两个丫鬟正要去扶，仪王不知从哪里出现，将小姐扶住。
[EN] Just as her knees buckled and both maids lunged to catch her, Prince Yi stepped out of nowhere and caught her steady in his arms.
[ZH] 千里寻稍微缓一会儿，一把推开仪王，继续往回走。
[EN] Catching her breath for a second, Qianli Xun shoved Prince Yi off and kept walking.
[ZH] 苦荞捡起手炉，向仪王行完礼，跟上自家小姐。
[EN] Scooping up the hand warmer, Kuqiao bowed quick to Prince Yi and hurried after her mistress.
[ZH] 夏果以更快的速度赶上，堵在小姐面前，跪在雪地里，哭道：“小姐，乔公子太可怜，我想替您安慰安慰他。”
[EN] Racing ahead even faster, Xiaguo blocked her mistress's path, dropped to her knees in the snow, and wept: "Miss, Young Master Qiao is so pitiful—let me go comfort him for you!"
[ZH] 千里寻这才明白夏果的心思，用无力而又冷静的声音说：“你想做什么就去做吧，与我无关。”
[EN] Everything clicked at last for Qianli Xun. In a drained, cool tone she answered, "Do whatever you want—nothing to do with me."
[ZH] 夏果磕头感谢：“谢小姐！”
[EN] Xiaguo kowtowed in thanks: "Thank you, Miss!"
[ZH] 千里寻绕过夏果继续往回走，申简辰在她身后默默跟着。
[EN] Stepping around Xiaguo, Qianli Xun trudged on while Shen Jianchen followed in silence behind her.
[ZH] 他很羡慕文渊，竟然能让他难以追求的女子如此痛苦。他在心里埋怨道：“你若喜欢我，跟我在一起，我绝不会让你这么痛苦。”
[EN] Jealousy burned inside him for Wenyuan—that the one woman he could never win would bleed like this for another man! "If you loved me and chose me," he grumbled silently, "I'd never let you hurt like this."
[ZH] 走到马车旁，不等苦荞搀扶，千里寻径自登上马车。
[EN] Reaching the carriages, Qianli Xun climbed straight into hers without waiting for Kuqiao's arm.
[ZH] 仪王的马车也停在那里，白风守在一旁。
[EN] Prince Yi's carriage stood parked right beside it, Bai Feng waiting at the reins.
[ZH] 苦荞好久没见到白风，很想上前与他说话，可小姐的状态搞得她心情全无，根本顾不上白风，跟着小姐就要上马车，却被仪王挡住。
[EN] Much as Kuqiao had missed Bai Feng, seeing her mistress wrecked left her zero mood for romance. Stepping up to follow Miss aboard, Prince Yi stepped in her way.
[ZH] “你去跟白风坐一辆，我与幽栖居士有话要说。”
[EN] "Ride with Bai Feng. I have words for Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 对方是仪王，身份尊贵，他的话不得不听。
[EN] Against a royal prince's command, Kuqiao couldn't argue.
[ZH] 苦荞递上手炉：“麻烦王爷将手炉给我家小姐。”
[EN] Holding out the hand warmer: "Please give this back to my mistress, Your Highness."
[ZH] 申简辰接过，登上千里寻的马车。
[EN] Taking it, Shen Jianchen climbed into Qianli Xun's carriage.
[ZH] 白风见状，满心欢喜，上前拉起苦荞坐上仪王的马车。
[EN] Overjoyed, Bai Feng took Kuqiao's hand and helped her up into Prince Yi's carriage.
[ZH] 申简辰并未立刻将手炉送给千里寻，他担心一旦给她，她会立刻将他赶下马车。于是握在自己手中，坐在她对面，一句话不说，默默陪伴。
[EN] Holding onto the hand warmer instead of handing it right over—afraid she'd boot him straight back out—Shen Jianchen sat across from her in total silence, keeping quiet company.
[ZH] 千里寻视若无睹，她没有心情批判仪王，没有力气将他赶下车，任由他坐在对面，用目光照顾她。她只顾垂下眼帘，体会内心的伤痛。
[EN] Qianli Xun acted as if he were thin air, too drained to scold him or shove him out, letting him watch her while she kept her eyes lowered over her raw ache.
[ZH] 小姐走后，夏果从雪地里爬起来，向乔公子跑去。
[EN] The moment her mistress departed, Xiaguo scrambled out of the snow and ran straight back toward Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 到达神柳旁时，乔文仙依然跪在雪地里流泪。
[EN] Beneath the Divine Willow, Qiao Wenxian still knelt in the snow weeping.
[ZH] “公子，起来吧，地上的雪太冰冷，小心膝盖受凉。”南双一边劝说，一边去扶公子，被乔文仙甩开，只好继续无奈地站着，抬眼瞧见夏果。
[EN] "Get up, Young Master—that snow's freezing, you'll ruin your knees!" Nan Shuang tried hauling him up, got shoved off again, and stood helpless until spotting Xiaguo running up.
[ZH] “公子，夏果来了，肯定是幽栖居士有话捎来。”
[EN] "Young Master, it's Xiaguo! Youqi Jushi must have sent word!"
[ZH] 乔文仙抬起泪眼，模模糊糊中夏果向他走来。
[EN] Lifting blurred, tear-soaked eyes, Qiao Wenxian saw Xiaguo hastening toward him.
[ZH] 他努力爬起，冲到夏果跟前：“淑真是不是后悔刚才说的话？她是不是同意跟我私奔？”
[EN] Scrambling up, he stumbled straight to her and caught her arms: "Did Shuzhen take it back?! Did she agree to elope with me?!"
[ZH] 夏果的眼中充满怜爱：“小姐并未让我捎话，我见乔公子可怜，想过来看看您。”
[EN] Tenderness brimmed in Xiaguo's eyes: "Miss didn't send me. Seeing Young Master hurting broke my heart, so I came back to check on you."
[ZH] 乔文仙放开夏果，一脸失落，转过身，往回走。
[EN] Dropping her arms in hollow disappointment, Qiao Wenxian turned and began trudging away.
[ZH] 夏果追上去，挡在乔文仙面前：“乔公子，世上女子千千万，不止小姐一个，她放弃您，还有别人值得您喜欢……”
[EN] Xiaguo ran ahead to block him: "Young Master Qiao, there are ten thousand women in this world besides Miss! She gave up on you, yet someone else right here loves you..."
[ZH] 乔文仙绕开夏果，继续踉跄着脚步。
[EN] Stepping around her, Qiao Wenxian stumbled on.
[ZH] 夏果继续追上去，堵在面前：“乔公子看看我，我跟小姐长得有几分相似，小姐不愿意给您当妾室，我愿意！”
[EN] Xiaguo circled ahead again: "Look at me, Young Master Qiao! Everyone says I look a little like Miss! Miss refused to be your concubine—I'm willing!"
[ZH] 公子还未惊讶，一旁的南双倒是惊讶不已，原来夏果看不上他，是因为眼光高，瞧上自家公子。
[EN] Before his master even reacted, Nan Shuang's jaw hit the snow—so THAT was why Xiaguo turned up her nose at him! Aiming straight for his master!
[ZH] 乔文仙双眼通红，往日未仔细瞧，今日瞧来，夏果与淑真的确有几分相似，他苦笑道：“相似又如何？你又不是她。”
[EN] Eyes rimmed raw red, Qiao Wenxian glanced at her—never noticed before, yet yes, she did carry a faint shadow of Shuzhen. A hollow laugh escaped him: "So what if you look like her? You aren't her."
[ZH] “我已识字，也学会做茶，还读过些书，《王右丞诗集》我也读过，以后我还会读很多书……”夏果强调自身的优点，希望乔公子在失望之际愿意接受她。
[EN] "I can read now, I know how to whisk tea, I've read books—even 'Collected Poems of Wang Youcheng'! And I'll read heaps more..." Desperate to win him while his heart lay shattered, Xiaguo rattled off every credential she had.
[ZH] “你走吧，不要烦我。”乔文仙冷冷地回应道，再一次绕过夏果踉跄着向池塘对岸走去。
[EN] "Go away and leave me in peace." Speaking chill as ice, Qiao Wenxian brushed past her once more, stumbling toward the far bank.
[ZH] 夏果还想上前拦住，被南双挡在面前：“我家公子不稀罕你，你还是不要再拦他，赶紧走吧。”
[EN] As Xiaguo moved to block him again, Nan Shuang stepped straight in front of her: "My master doesn't want you! Stop hounding him and scram!"
[ZH] 夏果停下来，对着乔文仙的背影大喊道：“乔公子！我不会放弃！相信总有一天，你会答应我！”
[EN] Halting, Xiaguo shouted after Qiao Wenxian's retreating back: "I won't give up, Young Master Qiao! One day you'll say yes to me!"
[ZH] 南双不满道：“你能嫁给我这样儿的都算不错，竟然想找我家公子那样的，别痴心妄想！”
[EN] Nan Shuang scoffed: "You'd be lucky to marry someone like me—dreaming of my master? Quit hallucinating!"
[ZH] 夏果怼道：“是你别痴心妄想才对！改日我成乔公子身边的妾室，看你还敢这么对我说话！”
[EN] Xiaguo snapped back: "You're the one hallucinating! Watch when I become Young Master Qiao's concubine—see if you dare talk to me like that then!"
[ZH] 南双“哼”一声，转身去追自家公子。
[EN] With a loud "Hmph!", Nan Shuang ran off to catch his master.
[ZH] 马车已到大街上，申简辰确定千里寻不会将他赶下去，才把手炉塞进她怀里：“天气冷，你拿着，手会暖和些。”
[EN] Once the carriage rolled onto the city avenues and Shen Jianchen felt sure Qianli Xun wouldn't kick him out, he tucked the hand warmer into her lap: "It's freezing—hold this to warm your hands."
[ZH] 千里寻没有拒绝，乖乖接过，揣在手中。也许她太需要有人关怀，仪王出现得恰如其分。尽管如此，她心中还是清楚，仪王不过是想乘虚而入，她再难过，也会守好防线，不会让他有机可乘。
[EN] Without pushing it back, Qianli Xun took it and curled her fingers around the warm brass. Maybe she really did crave a scrap of warmth right now, and Prince Yi's timing hit dead on. Even so, she stayed sharp—knowing he was fishing for an opening while her guard sat low, refusing to let him slip past her defenses.
[ZH] 苦荞与白风在一起虽开心，可因担心小姐，一直无法轻松下来。
[EN] Happy as Kuqiao was sitting beside Bai Feng, worry for her mistress kept her tense.
[ZH] 白风安慰道：“不用为幽栖居士担心，我家王爷说，她难过一阵儿，很快会从痛苦中走出来。”
[EN] Bai Feng comforted her: "Don't worry over Youqi Jushi—His Highness says she'll grieve a spell and bounce right back."
[ZH] 苦荞诧异道：“仪王何时如此了解我家小姐？”
[EN] Kuqiao blinked: "Since when does Prince Yi understand my mistress that well?"
[ZH] 白风想想说：“幽栖居士跟郡主是好朋友，王爷应该是从郡主那里了解到的。”
[EN] Bai Feng thought a second: "Youqi Jushi is Princess's best friend—His Highness probably heard it from Princess."
[ZH] 苦荞更感到纳闷：“白风，你不觉得奇怪吗？”
[EN] Kuqiao frowned deeper: "Don't you think it's weird, Bai Feng?"
[ZH] “什么？”
[EN] "What's weird?"
[ZH] “我家小姐与郡主是好朋友，今日受伤，郡主没来，反而你家王爷来，你说说，到底怎么回事？”
[EN] "My mistress is Princess's best friend—yet when Miss gets heartbroken today, Princess didn't come, Prince Yi came instead! Tell me what's up with that!"
[ZH] “能为什么？还不是因为我家王爷心疼郡主，这么冷的天儿，怕她出来受凉，便帮她前来安慰幽栖居士。”
[EN] "Simple! His Highness spoils Princess—freezing snow out here, didn't want her catching cold, so he came to look after Youqi Jushi in her place!"
[ZH] “哪有这道理？仪王对我家小姐来讲可是外男，难道郡主不忌讳？”
[EN] "What kind of logic is that?! Prince Yi's an unmarried man to my mistress—doesn't Princess mind?"
[ZH] “倒把我问住，别管那么多，我们两个好不容易见面，说点儿别的。”
[EN] "Stumped me there! Forget all that—rare enough we get time alone, talk about something nicer!"
[ZH] 苦荞还是没有心情说别的，叹道：“乔公子与小姐本是郎才女貌，天生一对儿，我就是想不通，为何两人走不到一起？最后只能伤心分开。”
[EN] Still unable to shake her gloom, Kuqiao sighed: "Young Master Qiao and Miss were a match made in heaven—why on earth couldn't they end up together, ending in tears like this?"
[ZH] “王爷说是因为父母之命媒妁之言所致，你放心，我父母都不在，你跟我之间，只有你情我愿。”
[EN] "His Highness says it's all because of parents' orders and matchmakers' words. Don't worry about us—I've got no parents, so between you and me it's pure free will!"
[ZH] 苦荞忽然觉得自己比小姐幸运许多，故意说：“你虽无父无母，可还有仪王，如若他强迫你娶别的女子，你会怎么办？”
[EN] Realizing she was luckier than her own mistress, Kuqiao teased, "No parents, sure, yet you've got Prince Yi! What if he orders you to marry someone else?"
[ZH] “王爷要是强迫我，我就不给他当侍卫，带你离开临安，去汴梁，那里我熟人多，跟着王爷也攒了些银子，到时做点小买卖，日子不会太差。”
[EN] "If His Highness ever forces me, I'll quit being his guard and run away with you to Bianliang! Got plenty of buddies there and savings stacked up from serving His Highness—we'll open a little shop and live cozy!"
[ZH] 苦荞害羞道：“我才不跟你去汴梁，我还要伺候小姐。”
[EN] Kuqiao blushed: "Who said I'm running to Bianliang with you? I have to wait on Miss!"
[ZH] “没事，你不想去汴梁，我就陪你在临安，大不了我给幽栖居士当侍卫。”
[EN] "Easy—don't want Bianliang, I'll stay right here in Lin'an and sign on as Youqi Jushi's guard instead!"
[ZH] 苦荞笑着埋怨：“说得好像仪王已强迫你娶别人一样。”
[EN] Kuqiao laughed while chiding him: "Talking like Prince Yi already forced you into a wedding!"
[ZH] “说说而已，我家王爷很开明，绝对不会强迫我。”
[EN] "Just saying! His Highness is open-minded—he'd never force me."
[ZH] 马车已到望月轩门口，车夫将马车停下。
[EN] The carriage pulled up outside Wangyue Pavilion and came to a halt.
[ZH] 千里寻没有立刻下来，呆坐了一会儿，才说道：“感谢仪王一路陪伴，我该回去，还望仪王不要再做同样的事，徒劳无益。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stayed seated a silent beat before speaking: "Thank you for riding along, Prince Yi. I'm heading inside—please don't do things like this again. It's a waste of effort."
[ZH] 申简辰并未接话，关切地问：“你可好些？”
[EN] Ignoring the warning, Shen Jianchen asked with gentle care: "Feeling any better?"
[ZH] “我好不好，与仪王无关。”
[EN] "How I feel is none of Prince Yi's concern."
[ZH] “怎么与我无关？你难受，我心里也不好受。”
[EN] "How is it none of my concern? When you hurt, I hurt too."
[ZH] 千里寻只觉仪王不愧是情场高手，一路陪伴，又说些甜言蜜语，对一个受情伤的女子来讲，若理智稍微松懈，便成为他囊中之物。
[EN] What a grandmaster player, Qianli Xun marveled: quiet escort, silky lines—any heartbroken girl whose armor slipped an inch would fall straight into his lap!
[ZH] 还好，千里寻并非一般女子，岂能被他的甜言蜜语轻易蛊惑？
[EN] Lucky she wasn't any ordinary girl to melt under sweet talk.
[ZH] “仪王是官家之子，何等尊贵，却陪草民一路，还说些甜言蜜语，难道想娶梦容后，再纳草民为妾？我把今日拒绝文渊时说过话送给仪王，这辈子我即使不嫁，孤独终老，也绝不给人当妾室。”
[EN] "Prince Yi is the Emperor's son, high above all—yet here you are escorting a commoner and whispering sweet nothings. Planning to marry Mengrong and take me as your concubine on the side? Let me give you the exact answer I gave Wenyuan today: even if I grow old alone without ever marrying, I will never be anyone's concubine."
[ZH] 千里寻说完，推开马车的门，将仪王一个人甩在马车里。
[EN] Pushing open the carriage door, she hopped down, leaving Prince Yi sitting alone inside.
[ZH] 申简辰苦笑道：“我什么时候想让你给我当妾室？你竟如此看我？我只是忍不住想要关心你，因为……我想娶的人只有你。”
[EN] A bitter laugh escaped Shen Jianchen: "When did I ever want you as a concubine? Is that really how you see me? I just can't stop caring about you—because you're the only woman I want to marry."
[ZH] 苦荞早已站在马车外，见小姐下车后直奔望月轩，赶紧与白风分别，跟着往回走。
[EN] Already waiting outside, Kuqiao saw her mistress march straight into Wangyue Pavilion, bade Bai Feng a hurried goodbye, and chased inside.
[ZH] 王爷半天不下来，白风等得不耐烦，提醒道：“王爷，该回去了。”
[EN] When His Highness still didn't climb down, Bai Feng nudged, "Time to head back, Your Highness."
[ZH] 申简辰如同大梦初醒，从马车上下来，朝望月轩内瞧了瞧，瞧不出所以然，又不敢贸然闯进去，尽管他很想。
[EN] Jolting as if from a trance, Shen Jianchen stepped out, gazed into Wangyue Pavilion's gate seeing nothing, and fought down the urge to stride right through.
[ZH] 最终只能叹口气，登上自己的马车，回幽林別居。
[EN] Letting out a sigh, he climbed back into his own carriage for Youlin Retreat.
[ZH] 千里寻回到闺房，一进屋，将门关上。
[EN] Inside Wangyue Pavilion, Qianli Xun marched straight into her bedchamber and shut the door.
[ZH] 苦荞慢了一步，被关在外面，她不用亲眼目睹便知，小姐定是又躺在床上，舔舐内心的伤口。
[EN] One step behind, Kuqiao stood outside the closed door knowing without looking that Miss was curled back under the quilt nursing her broken heart.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷走来：“姑娘回来了？”
[EN] Matron Wei walked up: "Back already?"
[ZH] 苦荞点点头：“小姐比昨日还难过。”
[EN] Kuqiao nodded: "Even sadder than yesterday."
[ZH] “不难过才不正常，这个坎儿总得过，过了便好。不要打扰姑娘，过一阵到门口听听，她若唤你就进去，不唤，你正好乐得轻松，该干什么干什么，又不是发生什么大事，姑娘大不了睡个三天三夜，也就恢复如初。”韦嬷嬷朝周围瞅瞅，“夏果呢？”
[EN] "Wouldn't be human if she weren't! Every hurdle has to be crossed once, then it's behind her. Leave her in peace—check the door later; if she calls, go in, if not, enjoy a breather. Nothing catastrophic happened; three days of sleep and she'll bounce right back." Scanning the yard, Matron Wei frowned: "Where's Xiaguo?"
[ZH] 苦荞靠近嬷嬷，小声说：“我今日才知，夏果喜欢乔公子，小姐正难受着，她竟然跪下，请小姐同意让她去安慰乔公子。”
[EN] Stepping close, Kuqiao whispered, "Found out today Xiaguo's got a crush on Young Master Qiao! Right while Miss was falling apart, she dropped to her knees begging to go comfort Young Master Qiao!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷不满道：“我早知道她不安分，没想到心气儿如此高，竟瞧上乔公子，还不顾姑娘心痛，主动要求去安慰，真不知羞耻！等她回来，看我怎么批评她。”
[EN] Matron Wei bristled: "Knew that girl didn't know her place—didn't dream she'd aim all the way up at Young Master Qiao, ditching a heartbroken mistress to chase him! Shameless! Wait till she gets back—I'll give her a proper dressing-down!"
[ZH] 苦荞劝道：“嬷嬷还是算了，小姐都不跟她计较，您又何必管她？”
[EN] Kuqiao put in a mild word: "Forget it, Matron—even Miss didn't hold it against her."
[ZH] “姑娘不与她计较，那是心胸宽广，我与她计较，是作为长辈教她怎么做人。等她回来，你让她来我屋里一趟。”韦嬷嬷说完离开。
[EN] "Young Lady lets it go out of generosity; I step in as an elder to teach her basic decency! Send her straight to my room the second she walks through the gate." Turning sharp, Matron Wei marched off.
[ZH] 小姐无需人伺候，苦荞进入丫鬟房，一边为小姐担心，一边拿出白风送的泥人，念叨着：“我家小姐人好、心好、又有才华，失忆前感情之路不顺，为何失忆后还是如此？”
[EN] With her mistress needing no tending, Kuqiao returned to the maids' quarters, worried for Miss as she took out Bai Feng's clay figurine and murmured to it: "Miss is kind, good, and brilliant—why was her love life cursed before amnesia and cursed all over again after?"
[ZH] 念叨完，又甚觉可笑，泥人又不是菩萨像，对着它说有何用？只怪最近难得见到白风，总喜欢把泥人拿出来睹物思人，才养成对着泥人说话的习惯。
[EN] Mid-murmur she caught herself and laughed—a clay doll wasn't a Bodhisattva, what use praying to it? Seeing Bai Feng so rarely lately had turned talking to his figurine into a silly habit.

#CH 137
[ZH] 夏果被乔公子拒绝，伤心不已。小姐乘坐马车离开，山下也无其他车可坐，她只好步行进入城内。又冷又累，还好身上带有碎银，便租辆马车回望月轩。
[EN] Crushed by Young Master Qiao's rejection, Xiaguo found herself stranded at the foot of the hill with Miss's carriage gone and no rides in sight, forced to trudge all the way back into town on foot. Freezing and exhausted, she thank goodness had loose silver on her and hired a carriage back to Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 也许是因为人多的缘故，城里比山下暖和一些，周围的雪明显正在融化，路边的树上不时掉下一坨快要融化的雪来，时不时听到屋檐上落下雪水的声音: 滴答！滴答！
[EN] Warmer inside the bustling city than out by the hills, snow was already melting off eaves and bare boughs—plops of wet slush dropping from branches while steady drip, drip, drip echoed from rooftops.
[ZH] 到达望月轩，夏果将银子给了车夫，直奔丫鬟房，一进屋，趴在床上只顾哭泣：“呜呜呜……！”
[EN] Paying the driver outside Wangyue Pavilion, Xiaguo marched straight into the maids' quarters, flung herself onto bed, and burst into sobs: "Waaah!"
[ZH] 苦荞早已收起泥人，正在绣花，见夏果回来，一句话不说，大哭起来，便批评道：“小姐那么难过，你舍她而去，看样子没落上什么好，也不瞧瞧自己的身份，乔公子怎么可能看上你？”
[EN] Having put away her figurine to embroider, Kuqiao watched Xiaguo bawl without a word and snapped, "Ditching Miss right while she was breaking down—doesn't look like it got you anywhere! Look in a mirror at your station; how could Young Master Qiao ever look twice at you?!"
[ZH] 夏果哭着回道：“苦荞姐姐少对我说风凉话，呜呜！我喜欢乔公子有什么错？难道因为你我是丫鬟，就不能喜欢像乔公子那样的人？呜呜……”
[EN] Sobbing back, Xiaguo cried, "Quit rubbing salt in it, Sister Kuqiao! Waaah! What crime is loving Young Master Qiao?! Just because we're maids, aren't we allowed to love a man like him?! Waaah!"
[ZH] 夏果提到丫鬟的身份，苦荞感同身受，不好再批评她：“我是嫌你不顾小姐才说你，小姐刚对乔公子说过伤人的话，你就扑上去，她得多难受，你相当于给她伤口上撒把盐。”
[EN] Mention of a maid's lot struck a chord in Kuqiao, softening her edge: "I only scolded you for ditching Miss! She'd just forced out cruel words to break up with Young Master Qiao, and you jump right onto him—how do you think Miss felt? Pure salt in her wound!"
[ZH] “呜呜……！我当时没想那么多，呜呜！我现在找小姐，跪在她面前请罪。”夏果哭着要去找小姐。
[EN] "Waaah! I didn't think straight! Waaah! I'll go kneel at Miss's feet and beg forgiveness right now!" Xiaguo scrambled up toward the door.
[ZH] 苦荞拦住她：“小姐一回来，把自己一个人关在屋里，你还是不要打扰她。韦嬷嬷让你回来去她屋里一趟，有话对你说。”
[EN] Kuqiao blocked her: "Miss locked herself indoors the second she got back—don't bother her. Matron Wei told you to report to her room the minute you returned."
[ZH] 夏果猜到，定是她没跟着小姐回来，韦嬷嬷问起，苦荞照实说了，才叫她前去训话。
[EN] Guessing Kuqiao had spilled everything when Matron Wei asked why she was not back, Xiaguo wiped her eyes on her sleeve: "Honest to a fault, aren't you, Sister Kuqiao—couldn't cover for me once!"
[ZH] 夏果努力控制好情绪，用衣袖擦擦眼泪：“苦荞姐姐真老实，也不知替我撒个谎。”
[EN] Kuqiao did not blink: "I'm still mad at you—why on earth would I lie for you? Go get a proper lecture from Matron Wei!"
[ZH] 苦荞毫不掩饰：“我心里怨着你呢？怎么可能替你撒谎？还是让你被韦嬷嬷好好说道说道！”
[EN] Sulking and aggrieved, Xiaguo dragged herself out toward Matron Wei's quarters.
