[ZH] 夏果一边不满，一边委屈地走出丫鬟房，往韦嬷嬷的屋子而去。
[EN] Matron Wei was half the master of Wangyue Pavilion, inspiring equal parts envy and dread in Xiaguo.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷算半个望月轩的主人，夏果既羡慕又害怕。
[EN] Maids at Zhu Manor whispered Matron Wei had started out as an ordinary maid in her youth—how had she climbed into half a mistress whom even Master Zhu treated with deference?
[ZH] 听朱府的丫鬟私下议论过，年轻时韦嬷嬷也是丫鬟。夏果想不通，她为何能一步步走到半个主子的地位？在朱府时，连老爷也会给她几分薄面。
[EN] Outside Matron Wei's door, Xiaguo called in a small voice: "Matron, Xiaguo is here."
[ZH] 来到韦嬷嬷的屋外，夏果怯怯地说：“嬷嬷，夏果前来拜见。”
[EN] From inside came Matron Wei's voice: "Come in."
[ZH] 里面传出韦嬷嬷的声音：“进来吧。”
[EN] Head hung low, Xiaguo stepped inside—more nervous than she had ever been before her mistress.
[ZH] 夏果低下头，小心走进去，她在小姐面前都没这么谨慎过。
[EN] Sitting on the couch, Matron Wei took one look at Xiaguo's swollen red eyes and knew she had cried up a storm.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷坐在榻上，瞧到夏果进来，见她双眼红肿，便知哭的时间不短。
[EN] Once Xiaguo halted, Matron Wei asked, "Just got back, or came back with Young Lady?"
[ZH] 待夏果站定后，韦嬷嬷说：“你是刚回来？还是跟姑娘一道回来的？”
[EN] Xiaguo thought to herself: "She knows already and still asks?"
[ZH] 夏果心想：“都知道，还问？”
[EN] Swallowing her resentment, she answered in a meek voice: "Just got back."
[ZH] 想归想，还是小心回道：“刚回来。”
[EN] "Why didn't you come back with Young Lady? You're the personal maid she picked herself!"
[ZH] “为何不跟姑娘一起回来？你可是她亲自收的贴身丫鬟。”
[EN] Xiaguo lied: "Miss told me to stay behind and comfort Young Master Qiao for her."
[ZH] 夏果编道：“小姐让我留下帮她安慰乔公子。”
[EN] Matron Wei's voice turned steel: "Bold-faced lie! Young Lady went today to make a clean break with Young Master Qiao—why would she ask you to comfort him?!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷语气严厉：“满嘴胡言！姑娘今日见乔公子本是与他做个了断，既了断又何须你去替她安慰？”
[EN] Xiaguo dropped to her knees: "Is loving Young Master Qiao a crime, Matron?!"
[ZH] 夏果跪下：“嬷嬷，我喜欢乔公子难道有错？”
[EN] Xiaguo dropped to her knees. "Matron, is it wrong for me to like Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] “还敢反过来问我？今日就让你明白到底错在哪里。其一，姑娘对你有恩，你不该将恩人喜欢的人装在心里；其二，你是姑娘的贴身丫鬟，不该在姑娘受伤时去顾别人的主子，这叫不守本分；其三，你出身卑贱不该有非分之想，别以为有几分姿色就能混进上流社会，要不是姑娘收你当丫鬟，你哪有机会认识这些公子哥？认识也就罢了，偏偏把不该想的人装进心里，还觍着脸要给人做妾室，结果如何？还不是被人拒绝？”
[EN] "Daring to talk back?! Let me count your sins for you! First, Young Lady saved you from ruin, yet you coveted the man your benefactor loved! Second, as her personal maid, you abandoned your wounded mistress to chase another household's master—forgetting your place! Third, born humble, you harbored wild delusions—thinking a pretty face buys your way into noble society! Without Young Lady taking you in, would you ever have met gentlemen like him? Meeting him wasn't enough—you offered yourself as his concubine! And look what it got you—thrown straight back in your face!"
[ZH] 夏果慨叹，韦嬷嬷难道是顺风耳，怎么知道她想给乔公子当妾室？
[EN] Xiaguo gaped—did Matron Wei have supernatural ears?! How did she know she had offered to be his concubine?!
[ZH] “嬷嬷怎么知道得如此清楚？”
[EN] "How did Matron know all that?!"
[ZH] “怎么知道？用脚趾头都能猜到你会对乔公子说些什么，你太给姑娘丢脸！我给些你银子，回乡下去吧。”
[EN] "A child could guess what nonsense you spouted to Young Master Qiao! You've shamed our Young Lady enough—I'll give you some silver and send you back to the countryside!"
[ZH] 夏果不满道：“嬷嬷凭什么赶我走？我是小姐身边的丫鬟，要走也得小姐赶我，只要是小姐赶我，我二话不说回乡下。”
[EN] Indignation flared in Xiaguo: "What right does Matron have to fire me?! I'm Miss's maid—only Miss gets to send me away! If Miss says leave, I'll pack up for the countryside without a peep!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷继续严厉地说：“姑娘把管家的权交给我，我当然有权处置任何丫鬟，相信姑娘不会说半个不字。”
[EN] Matron Wei's tone stayed iron: "Young Lady handed management of this house to me—I hold full authority over every maid, and Young Lady won't breathe one word against my decision."
[ZH] 夏果大哭道：“呜呜……！嬷嬷绕我一回，以后我再也不敢有非分之想，呜呜……！即使嬷嬷想赶我走，也要等小姐出来，我与她道别后再说，毕竟与小姐主仆一场，呜呜……！”
[EN] Xiaguo burst into frantic sobs: "Waaah! Spare me this once, Matron! I'll never harbor wild ideas again, waaah! Even if Matron sends me away, let me wait till Miss comes out so I can say goodbye—we've been mistress and maid together! Waaah!"
[ZH] “算你有良心。”
[EN] "At least you have a shred of conscience left."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷话音刚落，有丫鬟在外面报告：“嬷嬷，佳和郡主前来拜访。”
[EN] Right then a maid called from outside: "Matron, Princess Jiahe is here to visit!"
[ZH] 贵人到来，韦嬷嬷不便继续处理夏果：“起来吧，先回丫鬟房，贵人前来，我懒得跟你掰扯，等姑娘好了，再说你的事。”
[EN] With royalty at the gate, Matron Wei set Xiaguo aside: "Get up and go back to the maids' quarters. No time to argue with you while a noble guest waits—once Young Lady feels better, we'll settle your fate."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷从榻上下来，带人前去迎接。
[EN] Stepping off the couch, Matron Wei hurried out to welcome the princess.
[ZH] 夏果松口气，从心里感激佳和郡主来的甚是时候。
[EN] Xiaguo let out a shaky breath, blessing Princess Jiahe for showing up right in the nick of time!
[ZH] 话说申简辰担心千里寻，回到幽林别居后左思右想，只能求梦容出面。
[EN] Worried sick over Qianli Xun, Shen Jianchen had racked his brains back at Youlin Retreat and seen only one lifeline: ask Mengrong to step in.
[ZH] 乔梦容本不知今日淑真与乔公子相见之事，听哥哥说后，很是担心，主动提出要去望月轩安慰淑真。
[EN] Unaware of today's breakup beneath the willow, Qiao Mengrong grew worried the instant he told her and volunteered right away to go stay with Shuzhen and comfort her.
[ZH] 申简辰开心不已，将梦容送到望月轩门口，见到是韦嬷嬷带人前来迎接，便知千里寻受伤不轻，他更揪心不已，恨不得亲自冲进去安慰她。
[EN] Relieved, Shen Jianchen escorted Mengrong to Wangyue Pavilion's gate—and seeing Matron Wei come out to receive them told him Qianli Xun was hurting bad, making him itch to march straight inside himself.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷行礼道：“有劳仪王与佳和郡主，我家姑娘今日不便见人，恐二位贵人白跑一趟。”
[EN] Bowing low, Matron Wei said, "Forgive the wasted trip, Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe—our Young Lady isn't up to seeing guests today."
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“怎能是白跑？今日我要在望月轩睡一晚，跟淑真好好聊聊。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong smiled: "Not a wasted trip at all! I'm staying overnight at Wangyue Pavilion tonight to have a good heart-to-heart with Shuzhen."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷感激不尽：“我家姑娘有郡主这等好友真是三生有幸。郡主，里面请。”
[EN] Gratitude beamed on Matron Wei's face: "Our Young Lady is blessed three lifetimes over to have a friend like Princess! Right this way, Princess!"
[ZH] 乔梦容担心哥哥，犹豫要不要也请哥哥一起进去坐坐。
[EN] Worrying about Prince Yi, Qiao Mengrong glanced back, wondering whether to invite him in for a cup of tea.
[ZH] 申简辰猜到梦容的心思：“今晚，我住在乔府，陪文渊。”
[EN] Reading her mind, Shen Jianchen said, "I'm staying at Qiao Manor tonight to keep Wenyuan company."
[ZH] 乔梦容这才放心，带着喜兰进入望月轩。
[EN] Reassured, Qiao Mengrong stepped inside Wangyue Pavilion with Xilan.
[ZH] 看不到梦容的身影后，申简辰才登上马车赶往乔府。
[EN] Only once Mengrong vanished inside did Shen Jianchen climb back into his carriage for Qiao Manor.
[ZH] 马车上，申简辰又开始自责，他利用梦容的善良达到目的，实在卑鄙。她要是知道实情，不知会如何想他？
[EN] All the way there, guilt chewed at him for exploiting Mengrong's sweet heart again—if she ever learned the truth, what would she think of him?
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷引领佳和郡主来到闺房门外，门依然紧闭。她向里面说道：“姑娘，佳和郡主来访，想要与你住一晚。”
[EN] Guiding Princess Jiahe to the still-closed bedchamber door, Matron Wei called through: "Young Lady, Princess Jiahe is here and wants to stay the night with you."
[ZH] 千里寻听到梦容前来，从床上爬起，瞧向窗外，天已黑，老天在冬季仿佛怕冷似的，总是早早隐去亮光。
[EN] Hearing Mengrong had come, Qianli Xun scrambled off bed, glancing at the window—already pitch dark! Winter skies snuffed daylight out so early.
[ZH] 她走下床，绕到前厅，打开门，掀开厚厚的帘子，顾不上没穿抵寒的外衣，抱住梦容。
[EN] Stepping out of bed into the outer room, she unlatched the door, flung back the thick curtain, and wrapped her arms around Mengrong without even a warm coat on.
[ZH] “你可来了，再不来，我连夜去幽林别居找你的心都有。”
[EN] "You're here at last! One more hour and I would've trekked through the dark to Youlin Retreat myself!"
[ZH] 闺房院内，灯笼的光芒微弱，韦嬷嬷还是发现姑娘的眼睛又红又肿。
[EN] Even in the dim glow of courtyard lanterns, Matron Wei spotted her mistress's puffy, reddened eyes.
[ZH] “赶紧进屋，外面冷，你穿这么少会着凉。”乔梦容说完，迅速拉起淑真进入屋内，喜兰也跟着进去。
[EN] "Get back inside—it's freezing and you're barely dressed!" Pulling Shuzhen straight indoors, Qiao Mengrong stepped in with Xilan right behind.
[ZH] 佳和郡主到来，千里寻不好再阻止丫鬟进入。
[EN] With Princess Jiahe here, Qianli Xun could no longer bar her maids.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷在门外喊道：“苦荞！夏果！赶紧过来伺候郡主和姑娘！”
[EN] Out in the yard, Matron Wei called, "Kuqiao! Xiaguo! Come wait on Princess and Young Lady!"
[ZH] 夏果听到韦嬷嬷喊她，心里高兴，说明嬷嬷已无心再赶她走，便与苦荞走出丫鬟房，来到闺房内伺候。
[EN] Hearing Matron Wei call her lifted a boulder off Xiaguo—maybe Matron would not kick her out after all! Hurrying out with Kuqiao, she stepped inside to serve.
[ZH] 夏果查看火炉，再填些炭火；苦荞帮小姐穿上外衣。
[EN] Xiaguo checked the brazier and added fresh charcoal while Kuqiao draped a warm robe over her mistress.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷请示道：“姑娘，吃些东西吧？不能让郡主饿着。”
[EN] Matron Wei suggested, "Eat some dinner, Young Lady—can't let Princess go hungry."
[ZH] 乔梦容一听便知淑真还未用晚饭：“我来得匆忙，在幽林别居没吃晚饭，不如在你这里蹭一顿？”
[EN] Catching on at once that Shuzhen had not eaten, Qiao Mengrong jumped in: "I rushed over without dinner at Youlin Retreat—mind if I mooch a meal here?"
[ZH] 喜兰心想，郡主明明用过晚饭，却要装作未用，明显是为幽栖居士考虑，她对闺中密友实在好。
[EN] Xilan smiled inside: Princess had eaten a full dinner already, pretending just to make Youqi Jushi eat—what a loyal friend!
[ZH] 千里寻没有胃口，考虑到郡主，点头答应：“厨房做了什么？”
[EN] No appetite, yet unable to starve her guest, Qianli Xun nodded: "What did the kitchen make?"
[ZH] “做了姑娘爱吃的小鸡双色莲子羹，还有蔬菜包。我这就让厨房端来。”韦嬷嬷回完，赶去厨房安排。
[EN] "Your favorites—two-color lotus seed chicken soup and wild vegetable steamed buns! I'll have them brought right over." Matron Wei hurried off to the kitchen.
[ZH] 千里寻刚穿越而来时在林府吃的就是这两样，美味可口。以后每隔一段时间，韦嬷嬷都会吩咐厨房做，她仿佛永远也吃不腻。
[EN] Those were the exact two dishes she had eaten on her first day waking up in Lin Manor—delicious enough she never tired of them whenever Matron Wei had them cooked.
[ZH] 不一会儿，厨房的丫鬟将饭食端来，为二人摆好。
[EN] Moments later kitchen maids brought the dishes in and laid the table for the two of them.
[ZH] 夏果在外面做茶，一会儿郡主与小姐吃完饭肯定要喝茶，她需提前预备着。
[EN] Out in the outer room, Xiaguo whisked tea ahead of time, knowing Princess Jiahe and her mistress would want tea after supper.
[ZH] 苦荞与喜兰则在饭桌前伺候。
[EN] Kuqiao and Xilan stood beside the dining table waiting on them.
[ZH] 乔梦容并不饿，象征性地喝了半碗羹，蔬菜包一个未动。
[EN] Not hungry at all, Qiao Mengrong took only half a bowl of soup for show and left the buns untouched.
[ZH] 千里寻看出梦容已吃过晚饭，她感动道：“都吃过，还装。”
[EN] Seeing straight through her, warmth welled in Qianli Xun: "Already ate and still putting on a show!"
[ZH] “看出来了？还不是因为你，要是好好吃饭，我也不至于装，希望是第一次，也是最后一次。”
[EN] "Busted! Only because of you—if you ate right, I wouldn't have to fake it! Make this the first and last time, okay?"
[ZH] “放心，绝对是最后一次，我已决定再也不涉足爱情。”
[EN] "Deal—the very last! I've sworn off romance for good."
[ZH] “什么话！总不能一朝被蛇咬十年怕井绳？还有一个词，对！因噎废食！”
[EN] "Nonsense! Once bitten by a snake, scared of rope for ten years? What's that other saying—right, refusing to eat for fear of choking!"
[ZH] “你说什么都没用，我现在越来越觉得另一个我实在明智，不恋爱，不结婚，只顾忙事业。以后，我会恢复原本的状态。”
[EN] "Save your proverbs. Every day I realize the other me had it right—no dating, no marriage, pure focus on career! Back to my original self from now on."
[ZH] 佳和郡主十分不解：“另一个你？说法很奇怪。”
[EN] Princess Jiahe tilted her head: "The other you? Strange way to put it."
[ZH] “一点儿不奇怪，难道郡主心里没有另一个你？”
[EN] "Not strange at all—doesn't Princess have another self inside her too?"
[ZH] “另一个我？”
[EN] "Another me?"
[ZH] 梦容用心理解，她内心确实有两个声音，一个说哥哥还像以前一样爱她；另一个声音却说，他不是以前的哥哥。
[EN] Turning inward, Qiao Mengrong felt two distinct voices in her own chest: one insisting Brother still loved her just as before, the other whispering this man was not the Brother she used to know.
[ZH] 乔梦容似乎对淑真说的另一个我有所领悟：“好像真有另一个我，淑真，你真厉害，竟能说出这么深奥的话。”
[EN] A glimmer of awe crossed her eyes: "Maybe I really do have another self... You say the deepest things, Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 梦容的夸赞，并不能立刻消除千里寻内心的悲伤：“你怎知我今日心情不好？”
[EN] Her friend's praise could not melt the ache just yet: "How did you know I was having a rough day?"
[ZH] “我本不知，哥哥告诉我你今日与乔公子彻底了断，还说乔公子很受伤，我想你肯定难过，所以赶过来陪陪你。”
[EN] "I didn't—Brother told me you made a clean break with Young Master Qiao today and said Young Master Qiao was devastated. Figured you had to be hurting too, so I rushed over."
[ZH] 仪王欺骗人的手段实在高，明明一直赖在马车里陪她，却对郡主说文渊很受伤，搞得他好像一直陪着文渊似的。
[EN] Prince Yi sure knew how to spin a yarn! Sitting right across from her in the carriage all the way home, yet telling Mengrong how devastated Wenyuan was as if he had been holding Wenyuan's hand the whole time!
[ZH] 千里寻想着就来气，顾不上个人悲伤：“梦容，听你的意思，仪王一直在安慰文渊？”
[EN] Annoyance pushed aside her grief: "Sounds like Prince Yi was comforting Wenyuan today?"
[ZH] “哥哥是安慰过乔公子，要不怎么知道乔公子的状况？”
[EN] "Of course he comforted Young Master Qiao—how else would he know how broken up he was?"
[ZH] “说不定仪王是骗你。”
[EN] "What if Prince Yi lied to you?"
[ZH] “怎么会？哥哥从来不骗我，再说这种事情他没必要欺骗我。”
[EN] "No way! Brother never lies to me—besides, why would he lie about something like that?"
[ZH] 千里寻更觉仪王厉害，在心中警告自己往后更要时刻警惕，千万不能被仪王这等情场高手蛊惑，否则只怕做出伤害梦容之事。
[EN] More convinced than ever that Prince Yi was a top-tier smooth operator, Qianli Xun warned herself inside to keep her shields locked tight so he could never tangle her up and hurt Mengrong.
[ZH] 千里寻继续边聊边吃，为回馈佳和郡主的好意，将一碗羹喝完，又吃两个包子。
[EN] Talking on, she honored Mengrong's kindness by finishing her whole bowl of soup and eating two steamed buns.
[ZH] ﻿闺蜜到来，为她提供倾诉的机会，今晚她打算向梦容诉尽衷肠。
[EN] With her closest confidante right beside her, tonight she meant to pour out every ounce of her heart.

#CH 138
[ZH] 两个人用过晚饭，担心饭食搁在肚内，不敢立刻睡去，围坐在火盆旁，品着夏果做好的茶，把丫鬟们支开，自顾聊着。
[EN] After dinner, reluctant to sleep on a full stomach, the two sat around the glowing brazier sipping the tea Xiaguo had whisked, dismissing all maids for private talk.
[ZH] 喜兰走时，担心一会儿没人伺候郡主，千里寻说：“你放心休息，我会伺候梦容。”
[EN] When Xilan worried who would wait on Princess later, Qianli Xun waved her off: "Go rest—I'll wait on Mengrong myself."
[ZH] “我有手有脚，哪需你伺候。”乔梦容强调道，又转向喜兰，“你只管睡去，明儿一早来即可。”
[EN] "I've got two hands and two feet—who needs waiting on?" Qiao Mengrong laughed, turning to Xilan: "Go sleep, come back in the morning."
[ZH] 郡主已发话，喜兰不得不听从，跟在夏果和苦荞后面，离开闺房，把门从外面关好。
[EN] With Princess's order laid down, Xilan trailed Xiaguo and Kuqiao out and shut the door behind them.
[ZH] 乔梦容仔细观察，发现一顿饭功夫，淑真的眼圈已没刚见到时红肿，她说话的语气虽像平常一样轻松，却有明显的故作轻松之态。
[EN] Studying Shuzhen's face, Qiao Mengrong saw the redness around her eyes had faded over dinner, though her breezy tone still sounded forced.
[ZH] “淑真，你要是难受，在我面前哭一场，哭完不要再想乔公子，好好过自己的日子。”
[EN] "If it hurts, Shuzhen, cry it all out right in front of me—then stop thinking about Young Master Qiao and live your own life."
[ZH] “怎么？你还想让我再哭一回？我已经哭够，以后绝不会为此事再流一滴泪。”
[EN] "Want me bawling a second time? Already cried my tank dry—not another single tear for this mess."
[ZH] “我当然不愿你再哭一回。”乔梦容端起茶盏品一口茶，“茶做得不错，你的丫鬟做茶的水平完全超越我。”
[EN] "Of course I don't want you crying again!" Lifting her tea bowl for a sip, Qiao Mengrong praised, "Great tea—your maid whisks rings around me."
[ZH] 茶是夏果做的，千里寻想起她跪在地上要求前去安慰文渊的情景。
[EN] Xiaguo had made that tea—bringing back the image of her kneeling in the snow begging to go comfort Wenyuan.
[ZH] 这丫头什么时候对文渊动起心思，她竟然一无所知，是夏果掩饰得好？还是自己太笨？
[EN] When had that girl fallen for Wenyuan right under her nose without Qianli Xun having a clue? Was Xiaguo that good at hiding, or was she herself that blind?
[ZH] 不管是什么，千里寻觉得她竟比不上一个丫鬟，夏果可以不管不顾想着跟文渊在一起，可她却要顾忌许多。
[EN] Either way, Qianli Xun felt an odd twinge: a maid could chase Wenyuan with zero chains, while she stood hemmed in by a hundred walls.
[ZH] 爱一个人没有任何道理，不爱一个人，却可以有千千万万条理由，只要一条足以在文渊与她之间筑起一道铜墙铁壁。
[EN] Loving someone needed zero reason; not being able to love someone brought ten thousand reasons—and any single one built an iron wall between them.
[ZH] “梦容，你可否愿意给仪王做妾室？”
[EN] "Mengrong, would you ever agree to be Prince Yi's concubine?"
[ZH] 乔梦容被突如其来的问题问住，她从未想过。
[EN] Caught off guard, Qiao Mengrong froze—she had never once imagined such a thing.
[ZH] 千里寻见梦容一脸茫然，苦笑道：“你这种被一道圣旨眷顾的人，哪里能体会到我等平民百姓的苦楚。”
[EN] Seeing her blank stare, Qianli Xun gave a crooked smile: "Blessed by an imperial marriage decree, how could you fathom commoners' woes?"
[ZH] “难道你想过要给乔公子当妾室？”
[EN] "Did you ever think about becoming Young Master Qiao's concubine?"
[ZH] 千里寻摇摇头：“从未想过，今日文渊试探我，我没答应。”
[EN] Qianli Xun shook her head: "Never once. Wenyuan tested me today, and I turned him down flat."
[ZH] “做得对！乔家如此对待你，为何要觍着脸去做妾？岂不是太便宜他们？”
[EN] "Good for you! After the way the Qiaos treated you, stooping to concubine would be letting them off way too easy!"
[ZH] 梦容向着她，千里寻感到欣慰：“你姓乔，为何不向着乔家？”
[EN] Warmed by her fierce loyalty, Qianli Xun teased, "Your surname is Qiao too—why aren't you siding with the Qiaos?"
[ZH] “此乔非彼乔，再说我跟乔家没有任何血缘关系，我只依理而言。唉！你本与乔公子天生一对儿，就这样被拆散，实在令人惋惜。”
[EN] "Different Qiao—I have zero blood relation to their Qiao family! I only side with what's right. Sigh... you and Young Master Qiao were a match made in heaven; tearing you apart is such a crime."
[ZH] 千里寻垂下眼帘，瞧着盆中的炭火，说出她最不愿说出的话。
[EN] Lowering her eyes to the glowing embers in the brazier, Qianli Xun spoke the line she hated saying most.
[ZH] “我与文渊有缘无分，走到这一步，实属必然。谁让我们眼界有限，无法预知未来。若能预知那该多好，可以避免在错误的道路上越陷越深。”
[EN] "Wenyuan and I were fated to meet, never meant to stay. Reaching this end was bound to happen. Shame human eyes can't see the future—know ahead of time and you'd spare yourself walking down a dead end."
[ZH] 乔梦容算是听出，淑真虽痛苦，却已心甘情愿放弃乔公子，也将一切想得一清二楚，无需要他人过多劝解，她只需认真听淑真倾诉即可。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong saw that beneath the pain, Shuzhen had made peace with letting go—no need for empty lectures, just a loyal pair of ears.
[ZH] “难道你后悔跟乔公子有过感情？”
[EN] "Do you regret ever loving Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 千里寻摇摇头：“不后悔，文渊是我的初恋，我会永远把这份感情珍藏在心底。”
[EN] Qianli Xun shook her head: "Not a bit. Wenyuan was my first love—I'll keep that memory tucked warm in my heart forever."
[ZH] 乔梦容开玩笑道：“你的初恋？我怎么听说你嫁给林觉之前喜欢过一位徐公子。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong grinned: "First love? Heard you had a thing for some Young Master Xu before marrying Lin Jue!"
[ZH] “我失忆过，早已把他忘得一干二净，对失忆后的我来讲，文渊是我第一个爱上的人。初恋之所以美好，在于它是第一次，如同昙花一现，也像烟花瞬间即逝，美好都留在回忆中。”
[EN] "Amnesia wiped him clean—to the post-amnesia me, Wenyuan was the first man I ever loved. First love shines bright because it's the first: brief as a night-blooming epiphyllum, dazzling as fireworks—gone in a flash, leaving the glow behind in memory."
[ZH] “没想到，你与哥哥一样都失忆过。”
[EN] "Funny how both you and Brother went through amnesia."
[ZH] 梦容的话让千里寻再次不由自主怀疑仪王与申简辰的关系。
[EN] Mengrong's remark stirred that old suspicion in Qianli Xun all over again about some tie between Prince Yi and Shen Jianchen.
[ZH] 很快，她又觉得自己是太想念申简辰才会胡思乱想。
[EN] A second later, she chalked it up to missing Shen Jianchen so much her thoughts were running wild.
[ZH] “我怎么会与仪王一样？他是起死回生，我可是被林觉打得失忆。”
[EN] "Hardly the same! He came back from the dead; I got beaten into amnesia by Lin Jue."
[ZH] 千里寻以为乔梦容会立刻接她的话，可对方却不言语。
[EN] Expecting Mengrong to chime right back, Qianli Xun met silence instead.
[ZH] 乔梦容陷入沉思，淑真刚刚关于初恋的话题，使得她想到自己。
[EN] Shuzhen's talk of first love had plunged Qiao Mengrong into her own thoughts.
[ZH] 她的初恋是哥哥，她要嫁的人也是哥哥，说起来她比淑真幸运许多。
[EN] Prince Yi was her first love and the man she was going to wed—luckier than Shuzhen by far.
[ZH] 她又十分认可淑真所言，哥哥起死回生，不记得以前的感情，她的初恋也如同昙花一现。
[EN] Yet Shuzhen had hit a raw nerve too: ever since coming back from death with his memory wiped, her own first love felt just like a vanishing night flower.
[ZH] “梦容，你在想什么？”千里寻隐约猜到，梦容应该是想起仪王，不由在心中慨叹：唉！梦容未来嫁给花心的仪王，以后的日子可怎么过？
[EN] "What are you brooding over, Mengrong?" Guessing she was thinking of Prince Yi, Qianli Xun sighed inside: how would sweet Mengrong survive married to a playboy prince?
[ZH] 千里寻不得不承认，仪王追求女孩子的手段真是高明，既愿意花时间，又愿意花心思，还会说甜言蜜语，关键是不求回报。千里寻都想给他胸前挂个牌子，写上“北宋第一撩女高手”。
[EN] Still, she had to tip her hat to Prince Yi's game: endless patience, thoughtful gestures, silky lines, asking nothing back—she was tempted to hang a sign around his neck reading "Northern Song's Number-One Heartbreaker"!
[ZH] “哦，我想哥哥这会儿跟乔公子在聊些什么。”
[EN] "Oh, just wondering what Brother and Young Master Qiao are talking about right now."
[ZH] 千里寻想让梦容知道实情，不愿她被仪王欺骗下去，试探道：“梦容，今日我与文渊分开，是仪王一路护送我回来，他并未与文渊在一起……”
[EN] Hating to watch her best friend get played, Qianli Xun poked a hole in the lie: "Actually, Mengrong, after Wenyuan and I broke up today, Prince Yi escorted me all the way home—he wasn't with Wenyuan..."
[ZH] 乔梦容竟然一点儿都不诧异：“我知道，哥哥对我讲过，他说怕你想不开，做出傻事，看在你是我闺蜜的份上，替乔公子看着你。他送完你后，在城门口正好碰到乔公子，又将他安慰一番，哥哥为你们两个真是操碎心。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong did not even blink: "I know! Brother told me already—said he worried you might do something reckless, so for my sake he looked after you on Young Master Qiao's behalf. Then right after dropping you off, he bumped into Young Master Qiao at the city gate and comforted him too! Brother really knocks himself out for you two."
[ZH] 千里寻瞪大眼睛，嘴巴微张，心想，仪王真是高明，早已解决后顾之忧，把梦容骗得够深。
[EN] Qianli Xun's eyes bulged and her jaw dropped—Prince Yi had covered every track in advance, playing Mengrong like a fiddle!
[ZH] “仪王护送别的女子，难道你不介意？”
[EN] "Prince Yi escorting another woman—doesn't that bother you?!"
[ZH] “什么别的女子？你可是我最好的朋友。哥哥送你，我很放心。”
[EN] "What 'other woman'? You're my best friend! Brother looking after you puts my mind at ease."
[ZH] 千里寻既感动又心疼梦容，她已顾不上痛苦，或者说她的痛苦在关起门后，已随泪水倾泻而出。
[EN] Torn between warmth and heartache for Mengrong, Qianli Xun's own grief slipped right into the background—already cried clean out behind closed doors earlier.
[ZH] “梦容，仪王长得不赖，多金，身份地位又高，你以后千万不要再让他单独护送别的姑娘，搞不好别的姑娘会喜欢上他。”
[EN] "Mengrong, Prince Yi's handsome, rich, and royal—stop letting him escort other girls alone, or some girl might fall for him!"
[ZH] “你可喜欢上我哥哥？”
[EN] "Did you fall for Brother?"
[ZH] “开玩笑，我怎么会喜欢他？”
[EN] "Are you kidding?! Not in a million years!"
[ZH] “所以哥哥帮你，我很放心，也很乐意。”
[EN] "See? That's why I'm happy to let Brother help you!"
[ZH] 千里寻着急道：“你是不是傻啊！就不怕仪王喜欢上我？”
[EN] Exasperated, Qianli Xun blurted, "Are you naive?! Aren't you afraid Prince Yi might fall for me?!"
[ZH] “你能这样问，说明你们之间无比敞亮，再说你是我闺中密友，我信任你。”
[EN] "Asking that straight out proves there's nothing shady between you two—besides, you're my best friend, I trust you."
[ZH] 千里寻心想，梦容实在不懂得防火防盗防闺蜜的道理。
[EN] Poor Mengrong—zero clue about the modern warning to guard against fire, thieves, and best friends!
[ZH] “我当然值得你信任，不过，仪王以后无论找什么理由照顾我，希望你别答应，要多个心眼儿，再说我不需要仪王帮忙。”
[EN] "Of course you can trust me. Still, whatever excuse Prince Yi cooks up to look after me next time, say no—stay sharp! Besides, I don't need his help."
[ZH] “淑真，你人真好，懂得替我着想，我会听你的。不过，你真的不需要哥哥帮忙吗？你可是麻烦过他很多次。”
[EN] "You're so sweet looking out for me, Shuzhen—I'll listen! Sure you don't need Brother's help, though? You've asked him for favors plenty of times."
[ZH] “为了你，我以后不会再麻烦他。”
[EN] "For your sake, I won't bother him ever again."
[ZH] 困意渐渐袭来，千里寻拉着梦容睡到床上，两人本想再聊一会儿，头一挨枕头，没说几句，都呼呼大睡。
[EN] Drowsiness rolled over them; Qianli Xun pulled Mengrong into bed, meaning to chat a bit more, yet the second their heads hit the pillows, both dropped fast asleep.
[ZH] 乔文仙从神柳处离开后，并未进幽林别居，他没有心情。对现在的他来讲，失去淑真，住在哪里都无所谓。
[EN] Meanwhile, after leaving the Divine Willow, Qiao Wenxian had skipped Youlin Retreat—with Shuzhen gone, who cared where he slept?
[ZH] 他忽然觉得人生变得恍惚，恍惚中不清楚来到这个世界的意义到底是什么。不能为个人婚姻做主，他觉得自己枉为男子。他想过死亡，肩负的家族责任使得他无法轻易舍弃性命。
[EN] A hollow fog settled over him, making him wonder why he even walked this earth. Unable to choose his own bride, what kind of man was he? Death crossed his mind, only for duty to his family to chain him back down.
[ZH] 以后的日子该怎么过？难道要苟延残喘下去？
[EN] How was he supposed to drag through the years ahead—just breathing like an empty shell?
[ZH] 回到乔府后，他把自己关在屋子里，回想与淑真相处的点点滴滴；回想在神柳下，在冰冷的雪地里她态度坚定，说出决绝的话，越想越心痛。
[EN] Back at Qiao Manor, he shut himself in his room, replaying every sweet hour with Shuzhen—then her iron stance in the snow ending it all, twisting the knife deeper every second.
[ZH] 他后悔表现出想要纳她为妾室的想法，既不尊重淑真，又小觑了他们之间的感情。
[EN] He hated himself for even hinting at making her a concubine—an insult to her pride and to their love.
[ZH] 天刚刚变黑，仪王赶来，从聚香楼买来酒菜。白风与南双将酒菜摆好后，到别的房间去聊天儿。
[EN] At dusk Prince Yi arrived bearing wine and dishes from Juxiang Tower. Setting out the feast, Bai Feng and Nan Shuang slipped off to another room to talk.
[ZH] 申简辰先在桌前坐好，对文渊喊道：“我买了皇都春，过来与我饮几杯，一醉解千愁。”
[EN] Taking a seat first, Shen Jianchen called out, "Brought Capital Spring wine—come have a few cups with me and drown your sorrows!"
[ZH] 乔文仙躺在床上，双目呆滞，以为沉浸在个人痛苦中。听到有酒，才爬起来，拐到前厅，在桌前坐下，拿起酒壶，自顾满上，端起来就喝。
[EN] Lying dull-eyed on his bed lost in misery, Qiao Wenxian dragged himself up at mention of wine, shuffled into the outer room, dropped into a chair, filled his own cup, and downed it in one gulp.
[ZH] 要倒第二杯时，被仪王挡住：“慢慢喝，我来是帮你疏散胸怀，你一下子喝醉，我怎么帮你疏散？”
[EN] As he reached to pour a second, Prince Yi caught the pot: "Pace yourself! I came to help you let off steam—pass out cold and how do we talk?"
[ZH] “疏散有何用？又不能立刻取消婚约，让我与淑真回到从前。”乔文仙继续斟一杯喝下，然后把酒杯重重放在桌上，哭起来，“呜呜……！”
[EN] "What's talking fix?! Won't undo the betrothal or bring Shuzhen back!" Pouring another cup and tossing it back, he slammed the cup down and broke into sobs: "Waaah!"
[ZH] “男儿有泪不轻弹，你怎么说哭就哭？”
[EN] "A real man doesn't shed easy tears—why start bawling?"
[ZH] 乔文仙哽咽着：“婚姻不能自己做主，算什么男人？我就该哭！呜呜……！”乔文仙又倒一杯，一饮而下。
[EN] Choking through tears: "Can't even choose my own wife—what kind of man am I?! Why shouldn't I cry?! Waaah!" Pouring another cup, he drained it whole.
[ZH] “你与幽栖居士分开已成事实，与其沉溺其中，不如尽快走出来，承担自己该承担的责任和义务。”
[EN] "You and Youqi Jushi are over—face it. Instead of wallowing, climb out and shoulder the duties waiting for you."
[ZH] 乔文仙忍住哭声：“责任和义务？哼！要不是因为想到责任和义务，我死的心都有。”
[EN] Checking his sobs, Qiao Wenxian spat bitter: "Duties?! Hmph—if not for family duty, I'd be dead already!"
[ZH] “忘了幽栖居士吧，你们两个注定不能在一起。”
[EN] "Forget Youqi Jushi. You two were never meant to be."
[ZH] “忘？怎么能忘？我一直喜欢她，没那么容易忘记。”
[EN] "Forget? How?! I've loved her forever—how do you erase that overnight?!"
[ZH] “只有忘记，你才能开始新生活。”
[EN] "Only by letting go can you start a new life."
[ZH] “新生活？哼！我一点儿期待都没有，没有淑真，跟谁在一起都一样。”
[EN] "New life? Hmph! Couldn't care less—without Shuzhen, every woman's the same!"
[ZH] 申简辰能体会到文渊内心的痛苦，他得不到千里寻也会如此，于是拿起酒壶为文渊和自己满上：“来，你我干一杯，我们都是可怜人。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen knew that ache down to his marrow—losing Qianli Xun felt no different. Filling both cups, he raised his: "Come on, drink with me—two poor fools in the same boat."
[ZH] 乔文仙端起酒杯，痛苦的表情中充满不解：“仪王时刻佳人在侧，还有赐婚，令人羡慕不已，何来可怜？”
[EN] Lifting his cup, confusion pierced Qiao Wenxian's misery: "With a beauty right beside you and an imperial betrothal everyone envies, how are you a poor fool?"
[ZH] 申简辰无法向文渊说明对千里寻的感情：“来，干一杯，为我们喜欢而又得不到的姑娘。”
[EN] Unable to tell Wenyuan the truth about Qianli Xun, Shen Jianchen clinked his cup: "Here's to the women we love and can never have."
[ZH] 申简辰喝完后，瞧见文渊端着饮完的空杯，充满疑惑地看着他。
[EN] Draining his cup, Shen Jianchen looked up to find Wenyuan holding an empty cup and staring at him in puzzlement.
[ZH] “为何这么看我？”
[EN] "Why stare at me like that?"
[ZH] 乔文仙放下酒杯，醉意微起：“得不到？佳和郡主对仪王来讲唾手可得，为何要说得不到？难道仪王另有新欢？”
[EN] Setting down his cup as wine warmed his cheeks, Qiao Wenxian slurred, "Can't have? Princess Jiahe is right in your palm—who can't you have? Don't tell me Prince Yi has another woman in his heart?"
[ZH] “另有新欢？我倒是想，可我做不到，心里一直想着她。”
[EN] "Another woman? Wish I could swap—can't do it. My heart's stuck on her alone."
[ZH] “仪王说话云里雾里，让人搞不懂。”
[EN] "Talking in riddles, Prince Yi—makes no sense."
[ZH] 乔文仙无心想仪王的事，他内心的伤痛还未消失，只顾一杯一杯地喝着，几乎一口菜都未吃，醉意很快袭来，开始说些他期待之事。
[EN] Too drowned in his own pain to puzzle over Prince Yi, Qiao Wenxian kept pouring cup after cup without touching a morsel of food until drunkenness rolled in and his wishes spilled loose:
[ZH] “仪王，你知道……吗？淑真……拒绝我，都是……都是假的，她肯定……肯定太……太生气，才那样……那样说……”
[EN] "Know what, Prince Yi?... Shuzhen turning me down... was all fake... She was just... too mad... talking out of anger..."
[ZH] “你怎知幽栖居士说的是假话？”申简辰明知文渊说的是醉话，还是不由担心起来。
[EN] Even knowing it was drunken rambling, unease pricked Shen Jianchen: "How do you know Youqi Jushi didn't mean it?"
[ZH] “她曾对我……说，想……想嫁给我，给我……给我……生很多……很多孩子，还坐在我……腿上，呵呵，和衣睡倒人怀……”文渊趴在桌上睡着。
[EN] "She told me once... wanted to marry me... give me... lots and lots of kids... even sat right on my lap... haha, falling asleep in my arms, fully dressed..." Slumping over the table, Wenyuan passed out cold.
[ZH] 申简辰叹道：“睡着也好，省得继续痛苦，明日起来又是新的一天。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen let out a quiet sigh: "Better asleep—no hurting tonight, and tomorrow brings a fresh day."
[ZH] 申简辰喊来白风和南双，两人将乔公子扶到床上安顿好。
[EN] Calling Bai Feng and Nan Shuang in, they hauled Young Master Qiao onto his bed.
[ZH] 天色已晚，仪王想在乔府住下，乔守仁当然求之不得，立刻吩咐下人将住处安排妥当。
[EN] With night grown deep, Prince Yi stayed overnight at Qiao Manor—delighting Qiao Shouren, who had guest quarters made up at once.

#CH 139
[ZH] 第二天，申简辰一早在望月轩外面等待乔梦容。昨晚，他已明白，文渊不可能忘记幽栖居士，恐怕一辈子会把她放在心里。
[EN] First thing next morning, Shen Jianchen waited in his carriage outside Wangyue Pavilion for Qiao Mengrong. Last night had shown him Wenyuan would never forget Youqi Jushi—carrying her in his heart for life.
[ZH] 他很想知道千里寻是怎么想的？难道她也会把文渊永远放在心里？
[EN] What about Qianli Xun? Would she carry Wenyuan in her heart forever too?
[ZH] 当乔梦容被众人送出望月轩时，他从马车窗户向外瞧，只看到韦嬷嬷带着一群丫鬟相送，没看到千里寻的身影，难道她还在难过？
[EN] Peering out the carriage window as Wangyue Pavilion's household saw Qiao Mengrong off, he saw only Matron Wei and a cluster of maids, no Qianli Xun—was she still heartbroken?
[ZH] 乔梦容上马车后，刚坐好，申简辰急切地问：“幽栖居士可好？”
[EN] The second Qiao Mengrong climbed inside and sat down, Shen Jianchen blurted out, "How is Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] 乔梦容想起淑真昨晚叮嘱过她的话，小心问道：“哥哥为何如此关心淑真？”
[EN] Remembering Shuzhen's warning last night, Qiao Mengrong eyed him with caution: "Why is Brother so concerned about Shuzhen?"
[ZH] 申简辰赶紧掩饰：“我不是关心她，是关心文渊，昨晚我算是看出，他根本忘不了幽栖居士，估计一辈子会把她放在心里，如此下去，以后若成婚，也好不到哪里去。所以，我想知道幽栖居士是如何想的，难道她也会把文渊一辈子放在心里？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen scrambled to cover: "Not for her—for Wenyuan! Saw last night he'll never get over Youqi Jushi, probably carry her in his heart his whole life, which'll wreck his marriage later. Just wanted to know where Youqi Jushi's head is—if she's going to pine for him forever too."
[ZH] 哥哥的解释，乔梦容很满意，不再警惕：“淑真虽难过，但她已将事情想明白，以后应该不会再纠结与乔公子的感情……”
[EN] Satisfied by his excuse, Qiao Mengrong dropped her guard: "She hurts, sure, yet she's sorted it all out—won't stay tangled up over Young Master Qiao anymore..."
[ZH] 申简辰忍不住嘴角露出不易被人察觉的微笑。
[EN] A faint, hidden smile tugged at Shen Jianchen's mouth.
[ZH] “可她也说，不再涉足男女之情，怕伤人，她打算一个人过下去。”
[EN] "Though she also swore off men and romance for good—said love hurts too much, so she's living solo from now on."
[ZH] 申简辰又高兴不起来，千里寻是个说到做到的人，恐怕以后想要打动她，更是难上加难。
[EN] His smile died on the spot. Qianli Xun was a woman of her word—melting her heart now would be ten times harder.
[ZH] 他不由叹口气：“唉！”
[EN] A long sigh slipped out: "Sigh..."
[ZH] “哥哥为何叹气？”
[EN] "Why sighing, Brother?"
[ZH] 申简辰掩饰道：“两个相爱的人不能在一起，令人惋惜。”
[EN] Covering smooth: "Just a shame watching two people in love torn apart."
[ZH] 乔梦容想到自己：“我与哥哥很幸运。”
[EN] Thinking of themselves, Qiao Mengrong smiled soft: "Brother and I are so lucky."
[ZH] 申简辰没有回应，马车开始启动，要离开望月轩，他很是不舍。
[EN] Without replying, Shen Jianchen felt a heavy reluctance as the carriage pulled away from Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 这个冬季对千里寻来讲，注定是个更加寒冷的冬季。她已打定主意，每日将火盆烧旺，窝在望月轩，耐心等待春天到来。
[EN] For Qianli Xun, this winter promised to bite twice as cold. She made up her mind to keep her braziers roaring, hole up in Wangyue Pavilion, and wait for spring.
[ZH] 离半年时间还早，她与文渊之间的感情已经有了结果。对她来讲，这段感情来得意外，去得也意外。
[EN] Her half-year mark wasn't even close, yet her story with Wenyuan had already hit the final page—arriving out of nowhere, ending just as abrupt.
[ZH] 她在心中自问，真的这么快不再爱文渊？答案是否定的。她告诫自己，再爱也要装作不爱，假装的时间久了，自然成习惯，这叫弄假成真。
[EN] Asking herself honestly: had she stopped loving Wenyuan overnight? No. Yet she ordered herself to act like she had—fake it long enough and habit turned act into truth.
[ZH] 没有爱情，该搞搞事业，千里寻决定除去继续写话本，她要让望月轩变成第二个千里拳庄。
[EN] No romance? Time to pour into her career! Besides writing huaben scripts, she meant to turn Wangyue Pavilion into a second Qianli Fist Manor.
[ZH] 徒弟该怎么找？她不想大张旗鼓，到处张贴招徒弟的告示，目前的形势不允许，心境也不允许，干脆从望月轩的下人中寻些根骨差不多的，教他们练习拳法，以此消磨时光。
[EN] Where to find students? Posting flyers to recruit apprentices wasn't realistic right now; better pick servants inside Wangyue Pavilion with decent martial aptitude and train them to keep busy.
[ZH] 苦荞与夏果她再熟悉不过，一点儿根骨都没有，根本不适合练习拳法。
[EN] She knew Kuqiao and Xiaguo too well—zero martial bones between them.
[ZH] 她很快把下人们召集而来，经过仔细观察，挑出十个，三女七男。
[EN] Gathering the whole staff and sizing them up, she picked ten: three maids and seven male servants.
[ZH] 吩咐道：“你们十个以后放下手头的事，一心跟我练习千里拳，听清没？”
[EN] "From today on, drop your chores and train in Qianli Fist with me—clear?"
[ZH] 十人高兴不已，齐声回道：“听清了！”
[EN] Overjoyed, all ten shouted in unison: "Clear!"
[ZH] “好！现在跟我去后院！”
[EN] "Good! Follow me to the back courtyard!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷并不管束，姑娘太苦，只要她高兴，怎样都行。
[EN] Matron Wei didn't interfere—poor Young Lady had suffered enough; whatever kept her smiling went.
[ZH] 自神柳下回来，夏果还未与小姐说话。她担心小姐对她不满，会像韦嬷嬷一样赶她回乡下。
[EN] Since returning from beneath the Divine Willow, Xiaguo hadn't dared speak a word to her mistress, terrified Miss would pack her back to the countryside like Matron Wei threatened.
[ZH] 与苦荞跟着小姐往后院走的路上，夏果快走几步，跪在地上：“小姐，您要打要骂都可以，千万不要赶我走。”
[EN] Walking toward the back courtyard alongside Kuqiao, Xiaguo rushed ahead and dropped to her knees: "Beat me or scold me all you want, Miss—just don't send me away!"
[ZH] 千里寻愣住：“跪着干吗？地上太冷，小心着凉。”
[EN] Qianli Xun halted: "What are you kneeling for? Ground's freezing—you'll catch cold!"
[ZH] “不，小姐答应不赶我走，我才起来。”
[EN] "Not getting up until Miss promises not to send me away!"
[ZH] “我什么时候说过要赶你走？”
[EN] "When did I ever say I was sending you away?"
[ZH] “小姐虽没说过，但我做错事，赶我走是迟早的事？”
[EN] "Miss didn't say it, yet I messed up—sooner or later you'll kick me out..."
[ZH] 千里寻想起夏果对文渊的心思，昨日她太难过，根本没有心情关注此事，夏果提起，她心中多少有些不满。夏果是她的丫鬟，竟然对她爱的人偷偷动心思，搁谁多少都会不舒服。
[EN] Recalling Xiaguo's crush on Wenyuan—too numb to care yesterday—now that Xiaguo brought it up, a prickle of annoyance did surface. Having her own maid moon over her boyfriend would irk anyone.
[ZH] “什么时候的事？”
[EN] "How long has this been going on?"
[ZH] 夏果毫不隐瞒：“小姐还未和离时，到西湖边见乔公子，我便喜欢上他。”
[EN] Xiaguo held nothing back: "Ever since before your divorce, Miss, when you met Young Master Qiao by West Lake—that's when I fell for him."
[ZH] 那时千里寻对乔文仙一见钟情，她如何能忘记。
[EN] The very day Qianli Xun had fallen for Qiao Wenxian at first sight—how could she forget?
[ZH] “没想到，你有这心思也挺早。”
[EN] "Started pretty early, didn't you."
[ZH] “本想小姐嫁给乔公子，我以后有机会伺候他，说不定还能被乔公子纳为妾室……”
[EN] "I figured once Miss married Young Master Qiao, I'd get to wait on him—maybe even become his concubine someday..."
[ZH] 苦荞阻止道：“夏果！你不要再说！……”
[EN] Kuqiao snapped, "Xiaguo, shut your mouth!"
[ZH] 千里寻控制住内心介意的情绪，她又有什么好介意的？都已跟文渊了断：“说下去！”
[EN] Swallowing her annoyance—what was there left to guard now that she and Wenyuan were finished?—Qianli Xun said, "Keep talking."
[ZH] “小姐住在幽林别居那晚，我偷偷找过乔公子，向他表白，可他拒绝我；昨日，他也拒绝我。小姐，我本可以瞒着您，想到您对我好，我不应瞒着，今日才鼓起勇气说明，希望小姐不要赶我走。”
[EN] "The night Miss stayed at Youlin Retreat, I snuck over and confessed to Young Master Qiao, and he turned me down. Yesterday he turned me down again. I could've hidden it from you, Miss, yet you've treated me too well to lie—so I worked up the nerve to confess today. Please don't kick me out!"
[ZH] 千里寻想要责怪夏果，又责怪不起来，那丫头喜欢谁又与她有什么关系？那是她的自由。总得说些什么，才能让别人知道她已彻底放下文渊。
[EN] Qianli Xun wanted to scold her, then stopped—who the girl crushed on was her own business! Still, she needed to say something to show she was over Wenyuan for good.
[ZH] “你是我的丫鬟，怎么一点骨气都没有？竟然主动表白？还求着给人当妾室？以后不许再到处给我丢脸！”
[EN] "You're my maid—where's your backbone?! Throwing yourself at a man and begging to be a concubine?! Quit embarrassing me out there!"
[ZH] 千里寻继续向后院走去，走出几步，又停下来：“还跪着干什么？赶紧起来与苦荞跟我去后院。”
[EN] Walking on toward the back courtyard, she paused two steps later: "Still kneeling? Get up and come to the back yard with Kuqiao!"
[ZH] “谢小姐！”夏果高兴地爬起来，跟上小姐。
[EN] "Thank you, Miss!" Jumping up thrilled, Xiaguo hurried after her.
[ZH] 此后，千里寻不是训练挑选的十人练习拳法，便是写话本，日子过得紧张而充实。渐渐她不会在白天想起文渊，不想心中也就没有痛苦。
[EN] From then on, between drilling her ten martial students and writing huaben scripts, Qianli Xun's days filled tight and brisk. Before long, Wenyuan stopped crossing her mind during daylight—and not thinking meant not hurting.
[ZH] 可到晚上，一个人躺在床上，还是会想起，好在想起来已没以前那么痛，只是为自己倍感惋惜，多完美的一段感情就这么结束。
[EN] At night alone in bed he still crept back, though the sting had dulled to a quiet wistfulness—what a shame such a lovely romance had ended so soon.
[ZH] 跟她练习千里拳的十人，根骨比苦荞和夏果好些，但还是达不到最高要求。千里寻不想计较那么多，无非是为让自己忙碌起来打发时间而已，又不是收徒传承千里拳。
[EN] Her ten trainees had decent enough build, though short of true master potential. Qianli Xun didn't mind—she was keeping busy, not looking for a grandmaster heir to Qianli Fist.
[ZH] 那十人练得十分起劲，不到一个月的时间，个个已掌握基本功。
[EN] Throwing themselves into practice with fierce energy, all ten nailed the basics within a month.
[ZH] 千里寻很满意，开始教他们真正的拳脚功夫，想以后安排七个男仆做护院，三个丫鬟贴身跟着当保镖，这架势，哪个临安的大家闺秀能比得上。
[EN] Pleased, Qianli Xun moved them onto real combat moves—envisioning seven male guards watching her gates and three bodyguard maids trailing her everywhere. What noble lady in Lin'an could top that entourage?!
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 腊八节这日，朱晞颜正在喝腊八粥，林觉来访，说是抓到一个散播谣言之人。
[EN] On Laba Festival, while Zhu Xiyan sat eating Laba porridge, Lin Jue arrived with news: they had caught a rumor-monger.
[ZH] 朱晞颜换件衣裳，未带任何下人，跟着林觉来到城内偏僻处一座废弃的房子里。
[EN] Changing into plain clothes with no servants, Zhu Xiyan followed Lin Jue to an abandoned house tucked in a lonely corner of town.
[ZH] 门口安排有士兵把守，见知府大人与林统领前来，士兵把门打开。
[EN] Soldiers guarded the door, swinging it open as the Prefect and Commandant Lin stepped up.
[ZH] 朱晞颜走进后，看到一个人被五花大绑，嘴里塞着布子。
[EN] Inside lay a man trussed tight with rope, a rag stuffed in his mouth.
[ZH] 林觉吩咐士兵把那人口中的布子取下来。
[EN] Lin Jue signaled a soldier to yank the gag out.
[ZH] 刚一取，那人便大喊：“老爷饶命！我没有散播谣言！都是别人胡说！”
[EN] The instant his mouth cleared, the prisoner yelled, "Mercy, Your Lordship! I didn't spread any rumors—somebody framed me!"
[ZH] 林觉说：“我的人能把你抓来，必然有道理，你若不老实承认，只能让你尝尝大棒子的滋味。”
[EN] Lin Jue sneered: "My men don't nab folks without proof! Refuse to confess and you'll taste a stout cudgel."
[ZH] “老爷，我什么都没做过，您让我承认什么？”
[EN] "I didn't do anything, Your Lordship—what's there to confess?!"
[ZH] 林觉命令道：“来人！先让他尝尝杀威棒的厉害！”
[EN] Lin Jue barked, "Men! Give him a round of warning strokes!"
[ZH] 进来一个士兵，手里拿个大棒子，另两名士兵将那人按倒在地，摆好姿势后，拿大棒子的士兵使劲打起来，被打之人不断喊叫着。
[EN] A soldier strode in hefting a thick wooden staff while two others pinned the man face-down on the floor; heavy blows rained down as the prisoner howled in agony.
[ZH] 林觉请朱晞颜在椅子上坐下：“知府大人且耐心等待，待会儿定会从他口中撬出重要信息。”
[EN] Offering Zhu Xiyan a chair, Lin Jue assured him, "Wait a minute, Lord Prefect—we'll pry the truth right out of him."
[ZH] 朱晞颜点点头，对林觉更加满意。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan nodded, more impressed with Lin Jue by the minute.
[ZH] 二十棍后，被打之人已受不了，求道：“老爷！饶命！我说！我说！”
[EN] Twenty strokes in, the prisoner broke, shrieking, "Mercy, Your Lordship! I'll talk! I'll talk!"
[ZH] 林觉抬手，不让士兵再打下去。士兵收起大棒，站立一旁。
[EN] Raising a hand, Lin Jue halted the beating; the soldier lowered the club and stepped back.
[ZH] “老爷，我若说，您要答应放我一马。”
[EN] "If I talk, Your Lordship has to promise to spare me!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜说：“你先说，我会视情况而定。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan said, "Talk first; I'll decide once I hear it."
[ZH] 那人说道：“我也是听人吩咐，只知拿着那些纸张到处给人念。”
[EN] Panting, the man spilled: "I was just following orders—paid to walk around reading off those rumor sheets!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜继续问：“是谁吩咐你？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan pressed: "Who gave you orders?"
[ZH] “我们头儿。”那人便将混混头目说了，并告诉朱晞颜那头目此时应在春香楼。
[EN] "Our boss!" Naming the gang boss, he added the boss was probably lounging at Chunxiang Brothel right now.
[ZH] 林觉立刻派人去春香楼抓人，去的人倒也麻利，很快将一位混混头目捉住，正是在小巷内见陈浩宇的那人。
[EN] Lin Jue dispatched men to Chunxiang Brothel at once; moving fast, they hauled back the exact gang boss who had met Chen Haoyu in the alley.
[ZH] 来到废弃的小黑屋后，见手下被打得屁股上全是血渍，知道已瞒不下去，再看看旁边士兵手中的大棒子，二话不说跪下。
[EN] Dragged into the dark abandoned room, one look at his underling's blood-soaked rear and the soldier's thick club killed any urge to lie—he dropped straight to his knees.
[ZH] “老爷，我都交代，只求您饶了我，我也是收钱办事，我对朱家姑娘没有半点不满。”
[EN] "I confess everything, Your Lordship—just spare my hide! I only took silver to do a job; I've got zero grudge against Miss Zhu!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜黑着脸，严厉地问：“你倒说说，是谁给你钱，让你办这等缺德事？”
[EN] Face dark as thunder, Zhu Xiyan demanded, "Spit it out—who paid you to pull this filthy stunt?!"
[ZH] 混混头目说：“说起来，此人跟朱家还有关系……”
[EN] The gang boss swallowed: "Truth be told, he's even tied to the Zhu house..."
[ZH] 听到与朱家有关系，朱晞颜更生气：“到底是谁？”
[EN] Hearing a tie to his own family spiked Zhu Xiyan's rage: "Who?!"
[ZH] “陈家公子，陈浩宇！”
[EN] "Young Master Chen—Chen Haoyu!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜惊得站起来，他难以置信：“你确定没有胡说？”
[EN] Shock shot Zhu Xiyan straight to his feet: "Are you sure you're not lying?!"
[ZH] “给我十个胆儿，我也不敢欺瞒您，陈公子对朱家姑娘不满，想报复她，便想到散播谣言这招，那些纸张都是他差人提前写好给我们的，还给我们不少银子。”
[EN] "Give me ten extra guts and I wouldn't dare lie to you, Your Lordship! Young Master Chen held a grudge against Miss Zhu, cooked up this smear campaign for revenge, had his own people write out the sheets for us, and paid us heaps of silver!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜不得不信，他又坐回椅子上，一言不发。
[EN] Forced to believe it, Zhu Xiyan sank back into his chair in dead silence.
[ZH] “老爷，我说的是实情，此事与我们无关，还望您恕罪！”混混头目继续央求。
[EN] "Every word's the gospel truth, Your Lordship—nothing personal from us, please forgive us!" the boss begged.
[ZH] 朱晞颜不想因为朱家的事牵扯更多的人，再说这些人罪不至死，他摆摆手。
[EN] Unwilling to widen the scandal—and knowing these hired thugs didn't merit death—Zhu Xiyan waved a hand.
[ZH] 林觉对两人说：“知府大人心善，不与你们计较，还不赶紧滚！”
[EN] Lin Jue snapped at both men: "His Lordship is merciful and letting you off—get lost before he changes his mind!"
[ZH] 混混头目一听，赶紧起身，扶起已被松绑的手下，往外走。
[EN] Scrambling up, the boss hoisted his untied underling and started for the door.
[ZH] 朱晞颜突然命令道：“等等！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan barked: "Hold on!"
[ZH] 二人吓得站住，以为又要治罪，转过身跪地求饶：“老爷饶命！”
[EN] Both froze in terror, dropping back to their knees: "Mercy, Your Lordship!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜叮嘱道：“你们二人回去，不准向人提起今日之事，尤其是陈家公子，否则我定不饶恕！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan warned them cold: "Walk out that door and breathe not one word of today to a living soul—above all not to Young Master Chen—or I will show no mercy!"
[ZH] 混混头目说：“我们二人就当今日之事从未发生过。”
[EN] The boss swore, "Today never happened!"
[ZH] 林觉厉声道：“还跪着干什么！滚！”
[EN] Lin Jue roared, "Still kneeling?! Scram!"
[ZH] 混混头目又爬起来，扶着手下离开。
[EN] Scrambling up again, the boss dragged his underling out into the lane.
[ZH] 散播谣言之人竟是陈浩宇，林觉自知此事为难，请示道：“知府大人，要不要把陈公子抓来？”
[EN] With the culprit turning out to be Chen Haoyu, Lin Jue sensed the tricky bind and asked, "Shall I arrest Young Master Chen, Lord Prefect?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜毫不犹豫，坚决地说：“抓！不要让陈家人知道此事。辛苦林统领，希望林统领对此事保密。”
[EN] Without a blink, Zhu Xiyan ordered steel-firm: "Grab him! Keep the Chen family in the dark. Much obliged, Commandant Lin—and keep your lips sealed tight on this."
[ZH] “知府大人放心，下官绝不声张。”
[EN] "Rest easy, Lord Prefect—not a syllable leaks from me."
[ZH] 回朱府的路上，朱晞颜怎么都想不明白，在他的印象中，陈浩宇与淑真从未来往过，为何他会对淑真不满？以至于想到这种损人的招数破坏淑真的名声，顺带着也影响到朱家的声誉。
[EN] All the way back to Zhu Manor, Zhu Xiyan couldn't wrap his head around it: as far as he knew, Chen Haoyu and Shuzhen never even crossed paths—why on earth did that brat hate Shuzhen enough to wreck her name and taint the whole Zhu house?!
[ZH] 定要把这小子抓起来，好好审问审问，顺便帮陈家教育不成器的东西。
[EN] Once he got that little menace in his hands, he'd grill the truth out of him AND teach him the discipline the Chens failed to give him!

#CH 140
[ZH] 这天，陈浩宇从陈府溜出来，身边未带任何人，他担心太过显眼，最近听说林觉查得更紧，还是低调些为好。
[EN] That day Chen Haoyu slipped out of Chen Manor solo without a single lackey, worried a crowd would attract attention now that Lin Jue was digging harder—better keep a low profile.
[ZH] 他准备去赌坊玩玩儿，走到半道，突然被人用袋子蒙住头，眼前漆黑一片，感觉到被人用绳子捆起来。
[EN] Halfway to a gambling den, a burlap sack dropped out of nowhere over his head, plunging him into pitch black as strong ropes bound him tight.
[ZH] 陈浩宇大喊：“你们是什么人？也不到处打听打听，敢绑你陈爷爷，小心我手底下的人断了你们手脚！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu yelled, "Who the hell are you?! Ask around before tying up Grandpa Chen—my boys'll break your arms and legs!"
[ZH] 结果听到林觉的声音：“陈公子不要大喊大叫，知府大人请你走一趟。”
[EN] Then Lin Jue's voice answered cool: "Save your lungs, Young Master Chen. His Lordship the Prefect requests your presence."
[ZH] 陈浩宇立刻意识到散播谣言之事已败露，便不再叫唤，乖乖被扛上马车，还是好好想想怎么对付朱晞颜，不至于被他治罪。
[EN] Realizing his smear campaign had blown wide open, Chen Haoyu shut his mouth and let them hoist him onto the carriage, racking his brains how to wriggle past Zhu Xiyan without getting flayed.
[ZH] 想了一会儿，陈浩宇实在想不出好办法，问道：“林觉，你可在马车上？”
[EN] Drawing a blank after a minute, he called out, "Lin Jue—you in here?"
[ZH] “在又如何？”
[EN] "What if I am?"
[ZH] “把头上的套子帮我拿下来，我又不是不认识你，何必套着？”
[EN] "Pull this sack off my head! We know each other—what's the point keeping me blindfolded?"
[ZH] 林觉伸手为陈浩宇取下，把套子扔到一边。
[EN] Reaching over, Lin Jue yanked the sack off and tossed it aside.
[ZH] 陈浩宇眯会儿眼，等光线适应后，问道：“你带我去哪里？”
[EN] Squinting until his eyes adjusted to the dim light, Chen Haoyu asked, "Where are you hauling me?"
[ZH] “去见知府大人。”
[EN] "To see His Lordship the Prefect."
[ZH] “他准备将我怎样？”
[EN] "What's he going to do to me?"
[ZH] “将你怎样我不知，不过你得先告诉我，为何要诋毁朱淑真？”
[EN] "No idea. First tell me why you smeared Zhu Shuzhen."
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“林觉，我估计你也吃过不少她的苦头，怎么样？我帮你报了仇，是不是应该感谢我？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu smirked: "Bet you've eaten plenty of dirt from her too, Lin Jue! Look at it this way—I avenged you! Shouldn't you be thanking me?"
[ZH] 林觉想起被朱淑真五花大绑关了一天一夜之事：“我是吃过她的苦头，可也不像你，把人害得那么惨，被全城人非议不说，连好好的婚事也没了，以后想嫁人都难。”
[EN] Remembering the day and night Zhu Shuzhen had kept him trussed in ropes, Lin Jue muttered, "Sure, I got burned by her too—yet even I wouldn't sink as low as you, getting the whole city cursing her and wrecking her marriage so nobody wants her."
[ZH] 陈浩宇故意调侃道：“我怎么觉得你有点儿幸灾乐祸？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu poked at him: "Funny—you sound like you're secretly enjoying it!"
[ZH] “有吗？你从哪里看出我幸灾乐祸？我要是幸灾乐祸，还能抓你去见知府大人？”
[EN] "Me?! What makes you think that? If I were enjoying it, would I be hauling you to the Prefect?"
[ZH] “你啊，人在官衙身不由己，整日想着往上爬，当然得帮朱晞颜。可我不一样，自由自在草民一个，我可不怕朱晞颜。不如我们做个交易，你爱权，也应爱财，我给你钱，再说我诋毁朱淑真也是替你报仇，交易再加上恩情，是不是应该把我给放了？”
[EN] "Come on—stuck in officialdom chasing promotions, of course you jump when Zhu Xiyan snaps his fingers! Not me—I'm a free commoner with zero fear of Zhu Xiyan. Let's cut a deal: you love power, so you must love silver too. I'll pay you off—plus I avenged you against Zhu Shuzhen! Cash plus favors—how about letting me walk?"
[ZH] “哼！我是爱权又爱财，你也确实帮我报复了朱淑真，可我就是不放你，因为你诋毁朱淑真时，也让大家嘲笑我。”
[EN] "Hmph! Sure I love power and silver, and sure you gave Zhu Shuzhen a taste of hell—yet I'm still not letting you go! Because while smearing Zhu Shuzhen, you made a laughingstock out of ME too!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不解：“我何时在谣言里嘲笑过你？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu blinked: "When did my rumors mock you?!"
[ZH] “你说朱淑真红杏出墙，难道不是嘲笑我无能管不住老婆？”
[EN] "Claiming Zhu Shuzhen had affairs behind my back—isn't that mocking me as a spineless husband who couldn't control his wife?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“你这人长得五大三粗，心思倒细腻，仔细想想好像是有这意思，只怪我当时光想报复朱淑真，没想那么多，还请见谅。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu chuckled: "Big tough guy with delicate feelings! Fair point—I was so busy sticking it to Zhu Shuzhen I forgot about you. My bad!"
[ZH] “哼！我见谅你有什么用？等见到知府大人，有你小子苦头吃！”
[EN] "Hmph! What good's 'my bad'? Wait till you face the Prefect—you're in for a world of pain!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜仔细寻思几日，谣言与陈家有关，他定要谨慎处理，不仅仅因为两家是亲家，更因为陈家是临安首富，掌管临安一大半产业，陈家稍有动静，必然影响临安的经济命脉。
[EN] For several days Zhu Xiyan had turned the Chen angle over with utmost care: not only were the two families in-laws, the Chens were Lin'an's wealthiest clan, controlling half the city's commerce—rattle the Chens and Lin'an's entire economy shook.
[ZH] 陈家什么都好，就是有个不争气的儿子，祸害陈家也就罢了，却跑来祸害朱家，朱晞颜难以忍受，必须让陈家和陈浩宇付出些什么才行。
[EN] Everything about the Chen house was sterling except one rotten son—wrecking his own house was their problem, yet wrecking the Zhu house crossed every line. Both Chen Haoyu and the Chen clan had to pay!
[ZH] 打定主意后，接到林觉传来的消息，换好衣裳，拿了鞭子，没带任何人，也没与朱家其他人说起此事，只身乘坐马车来到废弃的黑屋子，坐在椅子上等待陈浩宇到来。
[EN] Decision made, the instant Lin Jue sent word, Zhu Xiyan changed robes, grabbed a whip, left every servant behind without breathing a word to his family, and rode solo to the abandoned dark house to wait for Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 黑屋子靠墙的地方立着一个大布袋，鼓鼓囊囊，应该装有废弃的稻草。
[EN] Leaning against the wall stood a bulging burlap sack—looked stuffed with old straw.
[ZH] 朱晞颜走到跟前，把大袋子搬到地上放好。搬的过程中，感觉出并非稻草，应该装的是粮食。他想不通为何在一座废弃的房子里会有一袋粮食。
[EN] Walking over to lay the sack flat on the floor, Zhu Xiyan felt its heft: grain, not straw! Odd to find a sack of grain left in a deserted shack.
[ZH] 问题还没想通，听到外面有动静，朱晞颜赶紧在椅子上正襟危坐，严肃的表情中带有威严。
[EN] Before he could wonder further, footsteps sounded outside. Zhu Xiyan stepped back into his chair and sat rigid, face dark with magisterial sternness.
[ZH] 门被打开，林觉将陈浩宇推进屋内，又把门关上。
[EN] The door swung open; Lin Jue shoved Chen Haoyu inside and closed it again.
[ZH] 陈浩宇借着微弱的光芒，看清朱晞颜坐在椅子上，面无表情。
[EN] Peering through the gloom, Chen Haoyu made out Zhu Xiyan sitting stone-faced in his chair.
[ZH] 陈浩宇装作若无其事的样子：“知府大人找我，让我姐去陈家叫我就行，为何要让林觉把我带到这里来？”
[EN] Putting on a breezy front, Chen Haoyu said, "If Lord Prefect wanted to see me, just send my sister to fetch me from Chen Manor—why have Lin Jue haul me into a shack?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜没有理会，对林觉说：“林统领去忙吧，这里交给我。”
[EN] Ignoring him, Zhu Xiyan turned to Lin Jue: "Go attend to your duties, Commandant Lin—I'll take it from here."
[ZH] 林觉猜出，知府大人已将此事看作是家事，他不便在此多打扰，还得赶去向仪王汇报查找的结果。
[EN] Reading between the lines that this was now private family business—and eager to go report the breakthrough to Prince Yi—Lin Jue bowed:
[ZH] “下官还有事忙，知府大人有任何吩咐，尽管差人到林府找我。”林觉行完礼，推门而出。
[EN] "I'll take my leave, Lord Prefect. Send word to Lin Manor anytime you need me." Bowing out, he shut the door behind him.
[ZH] 在林觉推开门那一刻，陈浩宇借着亮光，看到朱晞颜满脸怒气，心想：“今日算是栽了，也不知知府大人会怎么惩罚我？”
[EN] In that brief flash of daylight as the door opened, Chen Haoyu caught the volcanic rage on Zhu Xiyan's face and thought, "Cooked! Wonder what punishment he's cooking up..."
[ZH] “跪下！”
[EN] "Kneel!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜声音不大，却很有力度。
[EN] Soft-spoken, yet heavy as a gavel.
[ZH] 陈浩宇本就做错事，被这声音吓得跪在地上，不敢说一句话。
[EN] Guilty to the bone, Chen Haoyu dropped straight to his knees without a peep.
[ZH] “朱家与陈家是亲家，关系向来不错，你为何要用下三滥的手段破坏淑真的清白和朱家清誉？”
[EN] "Our two houses are in-laws on warm terms—why on earth would you sink to gutter tricks to ruin Shuzhen's honor and smear the Zhu name?!"
[ZH] “朱伯伯！”陈浩宇开始套近乎，“我没想过要破坏朱家清誉，只因您家女儿欺人太甚，我气不过，想整整她，没承想事情搞得有些大，您也知道，谣言一旦传开，拦也拦不住……”
[EN] "Uncle Zhu!" Chen Haoyu turned on the charm fast. "I never meant to touch the Zhu family's name! Your daughter bullied me too hard, so I lost my head wanting to mess with her—didn't dream it'd blow up so huge! You know how rumors are—once loose, nobody can stop them..."
[ZH] 朱晞颜越听越气，已听不下去，起身一鞭子打在旁边的布袋上：“你还知道谣言传开拦不住！那你为何费尽心思搞这种事？我看你就是欠揍！”
[EN] Rage boiling over, Zhu Xiyan shot up and cracked his whip straight across the burlap sack beside him: "So you KNEW rumors can't be stopped?! Then why pull a dirty stunt like that?! Begging for a beating, aren't you!"
[ZH] 说着朱晞颜又在布袋上打两下。
[EN] Two more ferocious cracks lashed the grain sack.
[ZH] 虽未打在身上，陈浩宇还是很害怕，听那鞭子的力度，知府大人得多生气，要真打在身上，估计一鞭子下去就得皮开肉绽。
[EN] Even untouched, Chen Haoyu flinched at the sheer whistling force of that whip—one hit on bare flesh would rip skin clean off!
[ZH] “都怪朱淑真欺负我，她要不欺负我，我也不会想到报复她，您要打不如回家打自己的女儿！”
[EN] "Blame Zhu Shuzhen for picking on me! If she hadn't bullied me, I never would've hit back! Want to whip someone, go home and whip your own daughter!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜气得又打在一旁的布袋上，布袋被打烂，里面的粮食从断裂处流出来，一股发霉的味道传来，原来袋子里全是发霉的粮食。
[EN] Furious, Zhu Xiyan whipped the sack again until the burlap split open, spilling moldy grain across the dirt floor with a sharp musty stench.
[ZH] 陈浩宇被吓坏，布袋都被打烂，若真向他打来，别说皮开肉绽，估计连小命都保不住。他吓得不敢再说话。
[EN] Terror froze Chen Haoyu's tongue—shredding a heavy burlap sack like paper! One stroke on him would kill him dead! He clamped his mouth shut tight.
[ZH] 朱晞颜打得有些累，坐回椅子上，叹口气：“唉，不管什么原因，错事你已做下，既是男子汉，就好好想想接下来该怎么挽回你的过错。”
[EN] Winded from swinging, Zhu Xiyan sank back into his chair with a heavy sigh: "Whatever set you off, the damage is done. If you're a real man, think how you're going to fix the mess you made."
[ZH] 陈浩宇听不明白，小心问道：“朱伯伯，您这话是什么意思？”
[EN] Baffled, Chen Haoyu ventured cautious: "What do you mean, Uncle Zhu?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜抬眼看向陈浩宇：“你先说说，你想不想解决因你有意散播谣言给淑真和朱家造成的麻烦？”
[EN] Lifting sharp eyes to him, Zhu Xiyan asked, "First tell me—do you want to clean up the mess your rumors brought on Shuzhen and the Zhu family?"
[ZH] 在陈浩宇看来，朱淑真遭殃他高兴还来不及，岂会想着解决？至于对朱家有什么影响，他并不当回事。可当着朱晞颜的面，不敢照实说，再说他确实理亏。
[EN] In truth, Chen Haoyu couldn't be happier watching Zhu Shuzhen crash and burn, and couldn't care less about the Zhu house—yet kneeling under a raised whip, he wasn't suicidal!
[ZH] “想，当然想解决。不过，现在已没有人关心谣言，朱淑真和朱家的麻烦已解决，朱伯伯不必再忧心，要不放过我？我向您保证以后不会再犯同样的错误。”
[EN] "Yes! Of course I want to fix it! Good news is nobody cares about the rumors anymore, so Shuzhen's and the Zhu family's trouble is already over! No need to stress, Uncle Zhu—let me off this once, and I swear I'll never pull a stunt like that again!"
[ZH] “哼！放你？没那么容易！是没人再议论谣言，可淑真跟乔家好好的婚事被你搅黄，她被我赶出家门，一个人住在外面，整日以泪洗面，以后想找个婆家都难！”朱晞颜说着，不由流出两滴泪来。
[EN] "Hmph! Let you off? Dream on! Sure the gossip died down, yet you blew up Shuzhen's fine match with the Qiao family, forced me to cast her out alone to weep her eyes out, and left her with zero prospects for a decent marriage!" Two genuine tears rolled down Zhu Xiyan's cheeks.
[ZH] 陈浩宇见状，安慰道：“朱伯伯，这事好解决，我给您出个主意。”
[EN] Seizing the opening, Chen Haoyu offered smooth: "Easy fix, Uncle Zhu—hear my plan!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜立刻充满期待：“什么主意？”
[EN] Hope flickered in Zhu Xiyan: "What plan?"
[ZH] “您把朱淑真再接回家，跟家人在一起，她肯定不会太难过，再凭您知府大人的身份，想给女儿找个好人家，应该不是件难事……”
[EN] "Bring Zhu Shuzhen back home—with family around she'll cheer right up! And with your clout as Prefect of Lin'an, landing your daughter a fine match won't be hard at all..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇的话没说到朱晞颜心里，他站起来，拿起鞭子向他打去，到半空中停下，又无奈地打在一旁的布袋上，更多发霉的粮食涌出来。
[EN] That wasn't the line Zhu Xiyan wanted. Jumping up, he swung the whip toward Chen Haoyu, checked himself mid-air, and slashed the split grain sack again, sending another flood of moldy grain pouring across the floor.
[ZH] 陈浩宇被吓坏，他自认没有说错话，为何知府大人又动起鞭子？
[EN] Jolted half out of his skin—what had he said wrong?!—Chen Haoyu yelped:
[ZH] “朱伯伯，我是真心实意悔过，帮您出主意，您为何又要动鞭子？”
[EN] "I'm genuinely repenting and offering ideas, Uncle Zhu—why swing the whip again?!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜手握鞭子，指着陈浩宇：“既然淑真的婚事被你破坏，你要是男人，就娶了她！也算是挽回朱家和淑真的名声！”
[EN] Pointing the whip handle straight at Chen Haoyu's nose, Zhu Xiyan declared: "Since you wrecked Shuzhen's marriage, man up and MARRY HER YOURSELF! Restore both Shuzhen's and the Zhu family's honor!"
[ZH] 原来这几日，朱晞颜左想右想，想出这么个好主意，既可亲上加亲，亦可惩罚陈浩宇承担起该承担的责任，更可为女儿找个好婆家，真是一举三得！
[EN] Over the past few days, Zhu Xiyan had cooked up this masterstroke: double the tie between in-laws, force Chen Haoyu to own his crime, AND secure a wealthy home for his daughter—three birds with one stone!
[ZH] 今日把陈浩宇绑来，无非是想通过吓唬的手段，让他先将此事答应下来。
[EN] Hauling Chen Haoyu into this dark shack was pure shock-and-awe to scare him into agreeing first.
[ZH] 陈浩宇听到朱晞颜的建议，惊得眼睛瞪得老大，知府大人真会想，把没人要的女儿往他身边塞，他陈浩宇是一百个，一千个，一万个不乐意！
[EN] Chen Haoyu's eyes nearly popped out of his skull—what a sly old fox, trying to dump his unwanted daughter straight onto him! Not in a hundred, a thousand, ten thousand years!
[ZH] “朱伯伯千万不要抬举我，我可不是男人，娶不了您家女儿，再说朱淑真肯定不会同意。”
[EN] "Don't flatter me, Uncle Zhu—I'm no real man! Can't possibly marry your daughter! Besides, Zhu Shuzhen would never say yes!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不同意，朱晞颜早已料到，他还有后招，撸起右边的袖子，左手拿了鞭子，右手抓住陈浩宇：“同不同意，由不得你，现在与我一同见你的父母，当着长辈的面，把你跟淑真的婚事说定。”
[EN] Expecting refusal, Zhu Xiyan had his next move ready. Rolling up his right sleeve, whip gripped in his left hand, he seized Chen Haoyu by the scruff: "Whether you agree or not isn't your call! Come with me to face your parents right now and seal your betrothal to Shuzhen before your elders!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇被五花大绑，反抗不得，求道：“朱伯伯！朱大人！朱老爷！我错了还不行吗？您拿鞭子把我打一顿，打得皮开肉绽，我也毫无怨言，再不济，您把我在临安的牢房里关起来，也别逼我父母同意订下跟朱淑真的婚事，我求求您……”
[EN] Trussed helpless in ropes, Chen Haoyu wailed: "Uncle Zhu! Lord Prefect! Master Zhu! I admit I'm scum—beat me raw with that whip, lock me in Lin'an's dungeon for a year, anything—just don't force my parents to betroth me to Zhu Shuzhen! I'm begging you!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜哪里肯听，抓起陈浩宇，将门踢开，在车夫的帮忙下，将陈浩宇拽上马车，向车夫吩咐道：“去陈府！”
[EN] Deaf to his pleas, Zhu Xiyan kicked the door open, hauled Chen Haoyu out, heaved him into the carriage with his driver's help, and barked: "To Chen Manor!"
[ZH] 马车启动后，陈浩宇安静许多。看朱晞颜这架势，今儿是必须逼陈家同意订亲之事。陈浩宇多少有些后悔，要能预知有今日之事，他绝不会散播谣言。
[EN] Once the wheels rolled, Chen Haoyu fell silent. Looking at Zhu Xiyan's iron jaw, the old fox really meant to bully the Chens into a betrothal today! Regret bit hard—had he seen this coming, he'd never have paid a single copper for rumors!
[ZH] 到陈府还有一段距离，不如趁此机会想想，该怎么阻止朱晞颜把嫁不出去的女儿硬塞给他。
[EN] With a few blocks left to Chen Manor, he racked his brains for any trick to stop Zhu Xiyan shoving his unwanted daughter down his throat.

#CH 141
[ZH] 到达陈府大门口，朱晞颜一手拿鞭子，一手拽着陈浩宇冲进陈府。
[EN] Reaching Chen Manor's main gate, Zhu Xiyan stormed straight inside—whip in one hand, bound Chen Haoyu hauled by the other.
[ZH] 门口的下人见状，猜想公子定是犯下大错，才被知府大人捆绑。一个个顾不上行礼，拦道：“知府大人，您有什么事好好说，别绑着我家公子。”
[EN] Assuming Second Young Master had pulled some colossal disaster to get tied up by the Prefect, gatekeepers skipped bowing and stepped forward: "Hold on, Lord Prefect—talk things out calm, why tie up our Young Master?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜厉声说：“去！给陈江林报告一声，我来访，替他教育儿子！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan snapped, "Run tell Chen Jianglin I'm here to help discipline his son!"
[ZH] 下人见状，不敢再说，一路小跑去向主人报告。
[EN] Not daring another word, a servant sprinted off to report.
[ZH] 朱晞颜对陈府不熟，盯着下人跑去的方向走。
[EN] Unfamiliar with Chen Manor's maze of paths, Zhu Xiyan followed the direction the servant had bolted.
[ZH] 半路上被青瓜碰见，他忙上前拦道：“知府大人，我家公子怎么得罪您？您生这么大气？还拿鞭子？还绑他？”
[EN] Halfway in, Qinggua ran up blocking his way: "Lord Prefect, how did my master offend you to get you this furious—bringing a whip and tying him up?!"
[ZH] “少废话，赶紧带路！”朱晞颜正好抓到一个带路的。
[EN] "Shut up and lead the way!" Perfect—a free guide!
[ZH] 青瓜向公子请示：“公子，我能带路吗？”
[EN] Qinggua glanced at his bound master: "Should I lead the way, Young Master?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇生气地吼道：“知府大人在此！你问我干什么？带！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu roared, "The Prefect's standing right here—why ask me?! Lead!"
[ZH] 青瓜回道：“是，公子，我带，可是您说的，事后别怨我。”
[EN] Qinggua answered: "Yes, Young Master, leading now—your orders, so don't blame me later!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇上身被捆绑，下身倒灵活，伸起一脚，要踢青瓜：“再说！小心我踢死你！”
[EN] Bound above the waist yet nimble below, Chen Haoyu swung a kick at Qinggua: "One more peep and I'll kick you dead!"
[ZH] 被朱晞颜拽着，陈浩宇不得自由，加之青瓜向一旁躲闪，没踢着。
[EN] Tugged along by Zhu Xiyan while Qinggua hopped clear, the kick missed clean.
[ZH] 青瓜“嘿嘿”一笑，忙起带路之事：“知府大人，这边请。”
[EN] Letting out a cheeky "Heheh," Qinggua trotted ahead: "This way, Lord Prefect!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜继续抓住陈浩宇往里走，半路上，陈江林与姚轻琰带着丫鬟迎上来。
[EN] Dragging Chen Haoyu deeper inside, Zhu Xiyan met Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan hurrying out with maids.
[ZH] 两人本来正在屋里聊天儿，听到下人报告，知府大人把公子五花大绑带回来，而且手中还拿着鞭子，看样子公子这次闯下大祸。
[EN] Chatting indoors when the servant burst in saying the Prefect had hauled their son home trussed up with a whip in hand, both knew in a flash their boy had stirred up giant trouble.
[ZH] 二人立刻明白为何近期儿子如此乖巧，经常待在家里，原来是闯下大祸，回家寻求庇护。
[EN] Both realized at once why their wild son had played angel at home lately—running home for cover after a massive screw-up!
[ZH] 见儿子被绑，姚轻琰既心疼又生气，跟着陈江林先是拜见：“知府大人。”
[EN] Heart aching yet furious at seeing her son bound, Yao Qingyan joined Chen Jianglin in bowing: "Lord Prefect."
[ZH] 朱晞颜说：“今日没有临安知府，只有朱家和陈家，你家儿子犯下大错，我把他抓回来，想与二位教训他的同时，商议一下，如何让你家儿子挽回错误，保住朱家颜面。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan declared, "No Prefect of Lin'an today—only the Zhu house and the Chen house! Your son committed an outrageous wrong; I brought him here so we can discipline him together and settle how he'll make amends and restore the Zhu family's face."
[ZH] 陈江林听出来，亲家是想私了，也算是给足陈家面子。
[EN] Chen Jianglin caught the hint at once: his in-law wanted to settle this in private, giving the Chen house full face.
[ZH] “亲家。”既然朱晞颜不愿用知府大人的身份，陈江林自然用更亲切的称呼，“不知浩宇犯下什么错？以至于您亲自将他绑来？您告诉我，我定好好收拾他。”
[EN] "In-law." Since Zhu Xiyan preferred setting aside his official title, Chen Jianglin switched to warmer terms: "What on earth did Haoyu do to make you tie him up yourself? Tell me, and I'll tan his hide proper!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜回道：“此处说话不便，进屋去说。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan said, "Not out here—indoors."
[ZH] “亲家，这边请。”
[EN] "This way, In-law!"
[ZH] 陈江林带着众人进入陈府的会客厅。
[EN] Chen Jianglin led everyone into Chen Manor's reception hall.
[ZH] 朱晞颜依然抓着陈浩宇不松手，还未落座，要求道：“下人在此甚是不便，让他们都出去吧。”
[EN] Still gripping Chen Haoyu tight without sitting, Zhu Xiyan requested, "Servants get in the way—clear the room."
[ZH] 姚轻琰吩咐道：“你们都下去吧。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan waved them out: "All of you, step outside."
[ZH] 下人们全部往出走，青瓜边往出走，边瞧向公子。
[EN] Filing out, Qinggua cast a worried glance back at his master.
[ZH] 陈浩宇趁机向青瓜使眼色，青瓜很快领会，走出门，往夏老太太的住处跑去。
[EN] Chen Haoyu flashed Qinggua a sharp wink; catching on at once, Qinggua slipped past the door and sprinted straight for Old Madam Xia's quarters.
[ZH] 身边已没其他人，朱晞颜一把将陈浩宇按倒在地：“跪下！”
[EN] Alone in the hall, Zhu Xiyan shoved Chen Haoyu straight down onto the floor: "Kneel!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇很不情愿地跪下，马车上他思忖一路，也没想出好对策，只能见机行事，反正他坚决不会娶朱淑真。
[EN] Scowling, Chen Haoyu dropped to his knees—no plan cooked up during the ride, forced to wing it, sworn on his life never to marry Zhu Shuzhen.
[ZH] 陈江林瞧着不成器的儿子既生气又失望，当着知府大人的面儿，不好发作，先忍着。
[EN] Looking at his hopeless son filled Chen Jianglin with fury and shame, swallowing his temper before the Prefect.
[ZH] “亲家，请坐下说。”
[EN] "Have a seat and tell us, In-law."
[ZH] 三人坐下，姚轻琰不便开口，看向陈江林。
[EN] All three sat, Yao Qingyan glancing to her husband to speak.
[ZH] 陈江林接收到夫人的眼神，又问道：“亲家，赶紧说说浩宇到底做下什么错事？我好替亲家收拾他。”
[EN] Catching her look, Chen Jianglin pressed, "Tell me straight, In-law—what crime did Haoyu commit? I'll settle accounts for you right now!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜把鞭子放在桌上：“什么错？你儿子犯下弥天大错！让他自己说！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan slapped the whip onto the table: "What crime? A monstrous one! Let him confess himself!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰向儿子问道：“你到底做了什么错事？把亲家气成这样？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan demanded of her son, "What did you do to push our in-law this far?!"
[ZH] 已瞒不下去，陈浩宇打算尽数交代：“外面关于朱淑真的谣言，是我买通人散播的……”
[EN] Cornered with zero escape, Chen Haoyu laid it out: "I paid thugs to spread those rumors about Zhu Shuzhen..."
[ZH] 陈江林与姚轻琰几乎异口同声：“什么？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan gasped in near unison: "What?!"
[ZH] 陈江林二话不说，上前一巴掌打在儿子的脸上：“你个混蛋！在外不干好事，竟然欺负到朱家姑娘头上！你难道不知姑娘家的清誉有多重要？搞得她被全城百姓非议，与乔家好好的婚事也被你搅黄，你！你！……”
[EN] Without another word, Chen Jianglin leaped up and cracked his palm across his son's cheek: "You worthless bastard! Raising hell outside wasn't enough—you targeted Miss Zhu?! Don't you know a maiden's honor is her life?! Got the whole city cursing her and wrecked her fine match with the Qiao family—you... you...!"
[ZH] 陈江林越说越气，瞅到桌上的鞭子，一把抓过去，打在陈浩宇的身上。
[EN] Blind with rage, Chen Jianglin snatched the whip off the table and brought it slashing across Chen Haoyu's back.
[ZH] 陈浩宇吱哩哇啦乱叫：“哎哟！疼死我了！你是不是我的亲爹？朱淑真欺负我！我还不能报复报复她？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu howled bloody murder: "Ow! Killing me! Are you even my real dad?! Zhu Shuzhen bullied me—can't I even hit back?!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜并不阻拦，陈家如此不成器的小辈，是该好好收拾。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan sat watching unmoved—a spoiled brat like this needed a real thrashing.
[ZH] 陈江林又打两下，要打第四下时，姚轻琰已看不下去：“老爷！小心打坏儿子！”
[EN] Two more lashes landed; as Chen Jianglin raised his arm for a fourth, Yao Qingyan broke in: "Husband, watch you don't cripple our son!"
[ZH] 陈江林放下举起来的手，叹口气，走到桌边，把鞭子放好，看向夫人，她双眼已红，显然不忍心儿子受罪。他坐下后双手扶着头，不知如何是好。
[EN] Dropping his arm with a ragged sigh, Chen Jianglin set the whip back on the table, saw his wife's reddened eyes, sat down, and buried his face in his hands.
[ZH] 父亲的鞭打显然没用多大力道，陈浩宇还是假装痛，大声喊道：“娘！您看看！爹是不是把我身上打烂了？火辣辣地疼！”
[EN] Though his father had spared real force, Chen Haoyu hammed it up loud: "Mother! Look—Father ripped my back to shreds! Burning like fire!"
[ZH] 亲家在，姚轻琰不好护着儿子，训道：“你做下这等错事，你爹打你都是轻的，你身上的疼痛，岂能与朱家姑娘内心受的伤痛相比！”
[EN] With their in-law sitting right there, Yao Qingyan refused to coddle him: "Pulling a wicked stunt like that, your father went easy on you! Can a few bruises compare to the agony Miss Zhu suffered?!"
[ZH] 爹娘不偏袒他，陈浩宇赌气不再言语。
[EN] Seeing neither parent taking his side, Chen Haoyu sulked into silence.
[ZH] 朱晞颜终于开口：“亲家即使把他打死，也解不了我心头之恨，更无法挽回我家淑真的名声。”
[EN] At last Zhu Xiyan spoke: "Even beating him to death won't ease the rage in my chest—or give my Shuzhen back her good name."
[ZH] 陈江林放下手，瞧向朱晞颜：“亲家您说，该如何做才能解你心头之恨？”
[EN] Lowering his hands, Chen Jianglin looked across at Zhu Xiyan: "Tell me, In-law—what will ease your heart?"
[ZH] 正问到朱晞颜的心坎儿里，今日他定要为女儿说下这门亲事：“淑真与乔家公子的亲事被陈家公子破坏，好名声也没了，以后想找好婆家没那么容易，若陈家愿与朱家亲上加亲，我便既往不咎。”
[EN] Right on cue! Zhu Xiyan dropped his terms: "Your son wrecked Shuzhen's match with Young Master Qiao and tarnished her name, leaving her hard-pressed to find a worthy house. If the Chen family agrees to double our bond through marriage, I'll wipe the slate clean."
[ZH] 陈江林来了精神，与姚轻琰对视一番。姚轻琰沉思片刻，点头同意。
[EN] Chen Jianglin perked up at once, trading a glance with Yao Qingyan; after a quiet beat, she nodded.
[ZH] 陈江林立刻说：“亲家好主意，我陈家愿意亲上加亲。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin jumped in: "Splendid idea, In-law! The Chen family welcomes doubling our bond!"
[ZH] “好！此事说定，陈家不许反悔！七日后来朱家提亲。”朱晞颜岂能放过这么好的机会？定要将一切安排妥当。
[EN] "Done! No going back on your word—come propose at Zhu Manor seven days from today!" Refusing to let a golden deal slip, Zhu Xiyan nailed down the date on the spot.
[ZH] 陈江林满口答应，这是目前解决问题的最好方法。再说自家儿子不成器，能娶到饱读诗书的江南女词人，也是三生有幸。
[EN] Chen Jianglin agreed with all his heart: the cleanest fix possible, and having his wild son marry Jiangnan's celebrated poetess was a stroke of luck beyond merit!
[ZH] 加之女儿陈文心经常在他们面前夸赞淑真，好印象早已留下，自然愿意与朱家再次结亲。
[EN] Plus, Wenxin praised Shuzhen to the skies every visit, leaving both parents delighted to welcome her into the family.
[ZH] “亲家放心，七日就七日，三媒六聘，陈家一样都不会少。”
[EN] "Rest easy, In-law—seven days it is! Every matchmaker and every betrothal gift, down to the last detail!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇急了，顾不上装疼痛，大喊道：“爹！娘！我跟朱淑真合不来，成亲后日子肯定过不下去，再说她和离过一次，还比我大三岁，我坚决不同意！”
[EN] Dropping his injury act in sheer panic, Chen Haoyu yelled, "Father! Mother! Zhu Shuzhen and I clash like fire and water—we'd murder each other married! Plus she's divorced and three years older than me—I refuse!"
[ZH] 陈江林批评道：“成亲后成一家人自然合得来，至于和离，真宗皇帝都不计较，你又何必计较？大你三岁，也大得甚好，女大三抱金砖，难道你没听过？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin snapped back: "Once wed under one roof you'll get along! As for divorce, even Emperor Zhenzong didn't mind—who are you to turn up your nose?! And three years older? Ever hear 'marry a wife three years older, hug a brick of solid gold'?!"
[ZH] 正说着，夏老太太被丫鬟扶着进来，见孙儿被绑，跪在地上，背上衣衫已破烂，显然是被鞭打过。还好，冬季衣裳厚，没伤到皮肉。
[EN] Right then Old Madam Xia shuffled in supported by a maid—spotting her grandson bound on his knees with his outer robe slashed to ribbons! Luckily thick winter padding had saved his skin.
[ZH] 尽管如此，老太太依然心疼，扑到跟前：“身上可疼？”
[EN] Even so, heartbreak swept the old lady as she rushed to his side: "Does it hurt, my boy?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇假装道：“奶奶！疼死我了！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu laid it on thick: "Hurts to death, Grandmother!"
[ZH] 老太太更是心疼，对青瓜说：“还愣着干什么？快帮公子把绳子解开！”
[EN] Furious, Old Madam Xia barked at Qinggua: "Standing around like a post?! Untie Young Master right now!"
[ZH] 青瓜急忙上前解绳子。
[EN] Qinggua darted forward to loosen the knots.
[ZH] 老太太走到儿子跟前，指着陈江林，开口骂道：“你个不孝之子，哪有个当爹的样子？教育儿子也不能把他绑起来，拿鞭子打他，你年轻的时候，你爹何时这么教育过你？你要是把他打个三长两短，我跟你没完！”
[EN] Marching up to her son, Old Madam Xia pointed a trembling finger in Chen Jianglin's face: "Unfilial wretch! Call yourself a father?! Tying your own son up and whipping him—when did your father ever beat you like this?! Hurt one hair on my grandson and I'll never forgive you!"
[ZH] 陈江林上前扶住老太太，只管回话：“母亲，您别生气，我错了还不行吗？以后我再不这么教育他，您快坐下。”
[EN] Stepping up to steady her elbow, Chen Jianglin scrambled to appease: "Calm down, Mother—my fault, all right? I'll never discipline him like that again! Come sit down."
[ZH] 老太太甩开儿子：“我没你这样的儿子，你要再敢打我孙子一下，我就打你十下，看你还敢不敢欺负他！”
[EN] Shoving him off, Old Madam Xia snapped, "I have no son like you! Strike my grandson once more and I'll strike you ten times back!"
[ZH] 老太太不坐，陈江林与姚轻琰也不敢继续坐着，站起来又哄又赔笑脸。
[EN] With Old Madam Xia standing in fury, neither Chen Jianglin nor Yao Qingyan dared sit, hovering with sheepish smiles to coax her down.
[ZH] 陈浩宇身上的绳子被解开，站在一旁看热闹。
[EN] Freed from his ropes, Chen Haoyu stood off to one side enjoying the show.
[ZH] 朱晞颜算是领略到陈浩宇是怎么被培养成纨绔子弟，他必须开口说话，于是站起来，有礼道：“老太太，您不要责怪江林，是我把浩宇绑起来，他犯下错误，必须接受惩罚，现在问题都已解决，朱家和陈家亲上加亲，您老就等着乐吧。”
[EN] Seeing firsthand how Chen Haoyu got molded into a hopeless rake, Zhu Xiyan stood and offered a courteous bow: "Don't blame Jianglin, Old Madam—I tied Haoyu up myself. He made a grave mistake and had to face the music. Everything's settled now, though: the Zhus and Chens are doubling our marriage bond, so sit back and enjoy the good news!"
[ZH] 老太太这才顾上关注知府大人，简单行礼后说：“知府大人原谅民妇没有先向您行礼，主要是因为我这儿子太不争气，整日欺负我孙儿，搞得他不愿在家里待，逛荡在外面，最近才好些，他又打他，我看他就是见不得我孙儿好！”
[EN] Only then noticing the Prefect, Old Madam Xia offered a quick nod: "Forgive an old woman not greeting Lord Prefect first—this hopeless son of mine keeps bullying my grandson until the poor boy dreads coming home! Just started staying home and he whips him again—can't stand seeing my boy happy!"
[ZH] 夏老太太越说越气，猛然想起“亲上加亲”，平复心情后，问道：“知府大人刚说亲上加亲是什么意思？”
[EN] Mid-rant, "doubling our marriage bond" registered; catching her breath, she asked, "What did Lord Prefect mean by doubling our marriage bond?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜正要回话，陈浩宇走过来，搀扶住老太太：“奶奶，您可得替我做主，知府大人，还有我爹娘都逼我娶朱家姑娘，您不知道，朱家姑娘是母老虎，孙儿要是娶她，以后就等着过苦日子吧，求求您，帮帮孙儿。”
[EN] Before Zhu Xiyan could answer, Chen Haoyu stepped in to hold her arm: "Stand up for me, Grandmother! The Prefect and my parents are forcing me to marry Miss Zhu! You don't know—Miss Zhu is a total tigress! Marry her and my life turns into daily torture—please save me!"
[ZH] 老太太被孙子求得心花怒放，满眼爱意，笑道：“你净胡说，奶奶虽没见过朱家姑娘，也听过她的名头，读过她写的诗词，还听你姐姐提起过她，温柔可人不说，知书达理，满腹才学，你要是能娶到她，奶奶替你高兴。”
[EN] Melted to butter by her grandson clinging to her, Old Madam Xia beamed: "Quit talking nonsense! Even if Grandmother hasn't met Miss Zhu, I know her fame, read her poems, and hear your sister rave about her—gentle, lovely, refined, and brimming with learning! Landing her makes Grandmother thrilled for you!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜更加开心：“老太太，七日后，两家定亲，您可同意？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan grinned wider: "Seven days from now we seal the betrothal—does Old Madam approve?"
[ZH] 夏老太太头点得跟拨浪鼓似的：“同意！同意！浩宇也该成亲，早日定下，早日成亲。可我就是不明白，明明是好事，为何要将我孙子绑起来，又是训又是打的？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia nodded like a pellet drum: "Approve! Approve! High time Haoyu settled down—seal it fast and wed fast! Only thing I don't get is why on earth you tied my grandson up and whipped him over happy news?!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰走到老太太身边，搀扶着她：“母亲，我扶您回屋，把前因后果向您说个明白。”
[EN] Stepping up to take Old Madam Xia's other arm, Yao Qingyan said, "Let me walk you back to your room, Mother, and explain the whole story."
[ZH] 老太太点头同意，走时吩咐道：“青瓜，带公子下去换身衣裳，再仔细瞧瞧有没有伤到皮肉，若伤到，记得上药。”
[EN] Agreeing, Old Madam Xia paused at the door to order: "Qinggua, take Young Master to change clothes and check his skin—if there's a bruise, apply salve!"
[ZH] 青瓜回道：“老太太放心，我这就带公子去换衣裳。”
[EN] Qinggua bowed: "Right away, Old Madam!"
[ZH] 众人走后，陈江林拱手道：“亲家不计前嫌，还愿把女儿嫁到陈家，陈某不知该说些什么感谢的话，您放心，淑真一旦嫁进陈府，我们会把她当女儿一样看待。”
[EN] Once they cleared out, Chen Jianglin cupped his hands to Zhu Xiyan: "Holding no grudge and still giving your daughter to our house leaves me speechless with gratitude, In-law. Rest assured, the day Shuzhen steps into Chen Manor, we'll cherish her like our own daughter."
[ZH] 朱晞颜打心里乐呵，坏事变成好事，淑真有的嫁，朱家面子保住，又与临安首富亲上加亲，几个月来郁结在内心的情绪一下子疏散开来。
[EN] Joy bubbled inside Zhu Xiyan—calamity flipped into triumph: Shuzhen secured a rich house, Zhu face stood intact, and he had doubled ties with Lin'an's richest clan! Months of gloom blew clean away.
[ZH] “一家人不说两家话，我也该回去。”
[EN] "Family doesn't talk like strangers. Time for me to head back."
[ZH] 朱晞颜抓起桌上的鞭子，往出走。陈江林立刻跟上，将亲家送至大门外。
[EN] Snatching his whip off the table, Zhu Xiyan strode out, Chen Jianglin escorting him all the way to the front gate.

#CH 142
[ZH] 陈浩宇悻悻地离开，回到自己屋中，继续思忖着如何拒绝这门亲事。怎么都没想到，整来整去，他竟然成朱淑真的接盘侠，真是越想越气。
[EN] Fuming back to his own quarters, Chen Haoyu racked his brains over how to kill this betrothal. After all his scheming, winding up as Zhu Shuzhen's fallback husband made his blood boil!
[ZH] 青瓜很快拿来衣裳，帮公子将打烂的衣裳脱下，在他背上仔细查看，没有发现任何损伤。
[EN] Bringing fresh clothes, Qinggua helped him shed his slashed robe and checked his back carefully—not a single welt!
[ZH] “老爷还是心疼公子，手下留情，没伤到皮肉，只将衣裳打烂而已。”
[EN] "Master still loves you, Young Master—pulled his strikes so only the robe tore!"
[ZH] “你想冻死本公子？还不赶紧给我把衣裳穿好！这么多废话！”
[EN] "Trying to freeze me to death?! Put my clothes on and shut up!"
[ZH] 青瓜边给公子穿衣裳边说：“总盼公子娶个少奶奶回来，好有人看着您，没想到公子要娶朱家姑娘，她在外名声可不怎么好，还离过婚，也不知老爷和夫人是怎么想的。”
[EN] Dressing him, Qinggua prattled on: "Always hoped Young Master would wed a Young Madam to keep you in line—never guessed it'd be Miss Zhu, with her bad name and a divorce behind her! Wonder what Master and Madam were thinking."
[ZH] 陈浩宇第一次夸青瓜：“你这话说到我心里，连你都觉得不妥，我爹娘却被知府大人蛊惑，让我娶朱家没人要的姑娘，你说我得有多惨。”
[EN] For the very first time, Chen Haoyu praised Qinggua: "Hit the nail on the head! Even you see it's crazy, yet my parents got hoodwinked by the Prefect into dumping his unwanted daughter on me—how miserable is my luck?!"
[ZH] 青瓜越想越不对，担忧道：“公子，您被知府大人绑着回来，该不会在外面非礼朱家姑娘，被知府大人抓住，硬要把女儿嫁给您？老爷和夫人不得已才应下此事？”
[EN] Thinking harder, alarm lit Qinggua's face: "Wait—the Prefect hauling you home in ropes... don't tell me you forced yourself on Miss Zhu outside, got caught red-handed, and got strong-armed into marrying her?! That why Master and Madam caved?!"
[ZH] 衣裳已穿好，陈浩宇转过身，在青瓜的身上踢了一脚：“胡说什么！你家公子我是喜欢漂亮姑娘，可也不至于轻薄一只母老虎！”
[EN] Fully dressed, Chen Haoyu spun and aimed a kick at Qinggua's shin: "Shut your trap! Sure I love pretty girls, yet molesting a tigress?! Not in a million years!"
[ZH] 青瓜向一旁躲开，还是被公子踢到，不是很疼，继续多嘴道：“公子就该娶个厉害的少夫人回来好好管管您，要不您老欺负我。”
[EN] Dodging half clear so the kick barely grazed him, Qinggua kept running his mouth: "You really do need a tough Young Madam to rein you in, Young Master—otherwise all you do is bully me!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇又去踢青瓜：“我欺负你怎么了？本公子欺负你是瞧得起你，别人想让我欺负，我还不一定瞧得上。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu swung another kick: "So what if I bully you? Bullying you means I like you—plenty of folks beg to get bullied by me and don't make the cut!"
[ZH] 青瓜继续向一旁躲去，陈浩宇没踢着，也懒得再踢。
[EN] Qinggua hopped clear again, leaving Chen Haoyu too lazy to chase.
[ZH] 青瓜觍着笑脸说：“公子还是不要瞧得上我。”
[EN] Flashing a cheeky grin, Qinggua said, "Better skip liking me then, Young Master."
[ZH] 陈浩宇果断说道：“好，以后不许跟着我。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu snapped cool: "Fine—stop following me from now on."
[ZH] “那怎么行？公子还是踢我吧。”青瓜撅起屁股。
[EN] "No way! Go ahead and kick me, Young Master!" Qinggua stuck his rear out.
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“你怎么这么贱！本公子才懒得踢你。走，跟本公子出去走一趟。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu cracked a laugh: "What a shameless little bootlicker—not even worth my foot! Come on, you're coming out with me."
[ZH] 青瓜喜出望外：“公子，我没听错吧？您要带我出去？”
[EN] Qinggua's eyes lit up like lanterns: "Did I hear right, Young Master?! Taking me outside?!"
[ZH] “怎么？不想出去？那我走了。”陈浩宇说着往出走。
[EN] "Don't want to go? Fine, I'm off." Chen Haoyu started for the door.
[ZH] 青瓜赶紧跟上，嬉皮笑脸：“能跟公子出去，是我的荣幸，我还听说，跟公子混，吃香的喝辣的。”
[EN] Scrambling after with a huge grin: "My honor to tag along, Young Master! Heard rolling with you means non-stop gourmet feasts and high living!"
[ZH] “知道就行，以后只要你一心向着我，保准让你想逛青楼逛青楼，想去赌坊去赌坊，想吃什么就吃什么，人生快快乐乐。”陈浩宇边往外走边说。
[EN] "You bet! Stay loyal to me and I'll treat you to brothels, gambling dens, fine food—pure paradise!" Chen Haoyu boasted as they walked out.
[ZH] 青瓜兴奋道：“公子，我还没去过青楼，啥时候带我去？”
[EN] Excited, Qinggua bounced: "Never even seen inside a brothel—when are you taking me, Young Master?"
[ZH] “办完正事，就带你去。先出去套好马车，与本公子去趟望月轩，解决完大事，带你去春香楼快活快活。”
[EN] "Right after we handle urgent business! Go hitch a carriage—we're swinging by Wangyue Pavilion first, and once this big mess is sorted, straight to Chunxiang Brothel for fun!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇认为，因他散布谣言，父母理亏，不敢驳了知府大人的面子，才答应与朱家的亲事。而奶奶长期待在陈府，什么都不了解，自然认为这门亲事再好不过。说到底，陈家没人能帮到他。
[EN] Chen Haoyu had done the math: guilty over his rumor stunt, his parents wouldn't dare defy the Prefect; sheltered inside Chen Manor, Grandmother thought this match was heaven-sent—nobody inside Chen Manor could bail him out.
[ZH] 他打算去找朱淑真，凭她的脾气，听到两家要订婚肯定会被气得炸毛，不用费吹灰之力，婚事会被朱淑真搅黄。
[EN] His best weapon was Zhu Shuzhen herself! With her volcanic temper, tell her about the betrothal and she'd blow the roof off Zhu Manor—killing the engagement without him lifting a finger!
[ZH] 能跟着公子出去，青瓜当然开心：“公子，望月轩是什么地方？”
[EN] Thrilled just to step past the gate, Qinggua asked, "What's Wangyue Pavilion, Young Master?"
[ZH] “废话真多，只管跟我走就是，少瞎问。”
[EN] "Quit yapping and follow orders!"
[ZH] 到达陈府门外，青瓜让车夫把马车套上，他亲自赶车，带公子去望月轩。
[EN] Outside Chen Manor, Qinggua had the stablehands hitch up a carriage and took the reins himself to drive his master over.
[ZH] 有公子指路，青瓜很容易找到望月轩，停好马车后，等公子下来，才去门口，向一位小厮说：“陈公子来访，麻烦通报。”
[EN] Guided by Chen Haoyu, Qinggua located Wangyue Pavilion fast, pulled up, waited for his master to step down, and went to tell a gate boy: "Young Master Chen is calling—go announce him."
[ZH] 小厮说：“稍等，我这就去通报。”
[EN] "Wait a moment; I'll report."
[ZH] 小厮来到主人的闺房门外，向里说：“姑娘，门外来了位陈公子，说要见您。”
[EN] Reaching the bedchamber courtyard, the boy called through: "Young Lady, a Young Master Chen is at the gate asking to see you."
[ZH] 千里寻指挥下人训练完千里拳，刚坐下写话本，思路也刚打开，听到“陈公子”的称谓，第一个猜到陈浩宇。心想：“他来做什么？哼！肯定没好事！”
[EN] Fresh from drilling her servants in Qianli Fist and just catching her flow on a new huaben chapter, Qianli Xun guessed "Young Master Chen" meant Chen Haoyu—nothing good ever followed him!
[ZH] 千里寻并不理会，继续写话本。
[EN] Refusing to budge, she kept right on writing.
[ZH] 苦荞明白小姐的意思，掀开帘子，走到屋外：“你去告诉陈公子，小姐今日没空。”
[EN] Reading her mistress's mood, Kuqiao lifted the curtain and told the boy: "Tell Young Master Chen Miss has no time today."
[ZH] 小厮答应后，跑到门口，回道：“我家姑娘今日忙，没空见陈公子。”
[EN] Trotting back to the gate, the boy relayed: "My mistress is busy today and can't see Young Master Chen."
[ZH] 陈浩宇一听，十分不满：“你以为本公子愿意见她？你去告诉朱淑真，她要是不想嫁给本公子，就老老实实出来见我。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu bristled: "Think I'm dying to see HER?! Go tell Zhu Shuzhen if she doesn't want to marry me, she'd better drag herself out here right now!"
[ZH] 小厮不明所以，又不敢多问，只能回去继续禀报。在闺房外，小心说道：“姑娘，陈公子说，您要不想嫁给他，就老老实实出去见他。”
[EN] Mystified yet scared to ask, the boy sprinted back to whisper outside her door: "Young Lady, Young Master Chen says if you don't want to marry him, come out and see him right now."
[ZH] 千里寻听了，甚觉蹊跷，又充满担忧，搁下笔，站起来：“苦荞，随我出去会会陈浩宇。”
[EN] Alarm flashing with suspicion, Qianli Xun set down her brush and stood: "Come on, Kuqiao—let's see what Chen Haoyu's up to."
[ZH] 苦荞取下披风，替小姐披好，跟随她来到望月轩门口。
[EN] Draping a cape over her shoulders, Kuqiao followed her out to the gate.
[ZH] 千里寻瞧到陈浩宇欠揍的样子，不满道：“找老娘干什么？有话快说！有屁快放！”
[EN] Taking one look at Chen Haoyu's punchable smirk, Qianli Xun snapped, "What do you want?! Spit it out and get moving!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇跨过门槛，站到里边，调侃道：“朱淑真，你爹老奸巨猾，自己女儿没人要，却来祸害我，逼着我爹娘到你家提亲，七日后亲事就要定下，本公子可不想娶你，你肯定也不想嫁给本公子，赶紧回朱府好好劝说你爹，不要把你嫁到我们陈家。”
[EN] Stepping across the threshold, Chen Haoyu sneered: "That sly old fox father of yours couldn't offload his unwanted daughter, so he strong-armed my parents into proposing! In seven days our betrothal gets sealed! I don't want to marry you, and you sure don't want to marry me—march straight back to Zhu Manor and talk your dad out of marrying you to the Chens!"
[ZH] 千里寻既震惊又不敢相信：“你该不会是骗我吧？怎么可能发生这种毫无道理的事情？”
[EN] Stunned in disbelief, Qianli Xun stared: "Are you pulling my leg?! How on earth could something that insane happen?!"
[ZH] “本公子看着有这么无聊吗？你回朱府问问你爹便知，我可不想拿自己的幸福开玩笑，跟你成亲，咦……，想着浑身都起鸡皮疙瘩。你呢，不管用任何方法，负责劝说你爹，我呢，负责劝说我爹娘，还有奶奶。我保证，只要这件事情成功，我以后跟你井水不犯河水，以后见到你，二话不说，立刻滚蛋，你看如何？”
[EN] "Look like I've got time to joke about my own wedding?! Go ask your dad yourself! Marrying you—ugh, gives me goosebumps just picturing it! Here's the deal: you work on your dad by any means necessary, I work on my parents and grandmother. Pull this off, and I swear I'll stay out of your lane for life—spot you on the street and I'll bolt on sight! Deal?"
[ZH] 千里寻见陈浩宇说得认真，不像欺骗她的样子。再说他没有任何理由用个人的婚姻大事欺骗她，看来八成是真。
[EN] Watching Chen Haoyu's dead-serious panic, she knew he wasn't joking—nobody joked about his own forced wedding! Eighty percent chance it was real.
[ZH] “此事我还需详细了解，若正如你所言，你觉得我会同意嫁给你？”千里寻说完，扭头离开，“不谢！再也不见！”
[EN] "I'll verify this myself. If you're telling the truth—do you honestly think I'd say yes to marrying YOU?!" Turning sharp, she tossed back: "No thanks, and never darken my door again!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇对着她的背影喊道：“再也不见说得有些早！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu yelled after her: "Bit early to say 'never again'!"
[ZH] 喊完后，带着得意的表情自语道：“本公子太聪明，有你出马，订婚之事必败无疑！”
