[EN] Turning away with a smug grin, he patted himself on the back: "Pure genius! Unleash her on old Zhu, and this betrothal is toast!"
[ZH] 然后对身旁的青瓜说：“走！本公子带你去春香楼快活快活，现在终于不用绷着，可以开荤！”
[EN] Clapping Qinggua's shoulder: "Come on! Taking you to Chunxiang Brothel to celebrate—no more holding back, time to break my fast!"
[ZH] 青瓜高兴地答应着：“好嘞！跟公子混就是好！”
[EN] Qinggua cheered: "Woohoo! Rolling with Young Master rocks!"
[ZH] 话说林觉与朱晞颜分别后，来到幽林别居，向仪王说明情况。
[EN] Meanwhile, after parting from Zhu Xiyan, Lin Jue had ridden straight to Youlin Retreat to report the culprit to Prince Yi.
[ZH] 申简辰万万没想到，背后散播谣言之人竟然是陈浩宇。在他的印象中，陈浩宇跟千里寻基本没来往过。
[EN] Shen Jianchen never guessed Chen Haoyu was the mastermind behind the smears—as far as he knew, Chen Haoyu and Qianli Xun barely crossed paths!
[ZH] 他想起在朱府参加宴会时，无意中看到的一幕，才明白二人早已有过节。
[EN] Then that glimpse at Zhu Manor's banquet clicked back into memory: those two had bad blood from way back.
[ZH] 申简辰虽跟陈浩宇没打过交道，但在聚香楼一遇，已看出此人纨绔无比，能做出散播谣言之事，倒也符合他的做派。
[EN] Even without dealing with Chen Haoyu directly, one run-in at Juxiang Tower had shown him the kid was an incorrigible rake—a smear campaign fit his playbook to a tee.
[ZH] 申简辰不参与临安政务，可对临安的情况已有所了解。陈家虽无从政之人，但经济实力不可小觑，大到可以影响整个临安的经济命脉。
[EN] Hands-off though he stayed in local politics, Shen Jianchen knew Lin'an's balance of power: the Chen clan held no offices yet controlled half the city's lifeblood.
[ZH] 陈家与朱家又是亲家，朱晞颜才单独处理此事，看来他不想让此事宣扬开来，估计是想私了。
[EN] Plus the Chens and Zhus were in-laws—explaining why Zhu Xiyan handled it behind closed doors to keep things quiet.
[ZH] 考虑到陈家在临安的地位，申简辰也觉此事不宜公开，
[EN] Weighing the Chens' clout, Shen Jianchen agreed dragging it into court made no sense.
[ZH] “林统领办事迅速，这么快抓到幕后之人，能力不可小觑。”
[EN] "Fast work, Commandant Lin—nabbing the mastermind this quick proves real skill."
[ZH] 被仪王夸赞，林觉自知升职有望：“王爷谬赞，能为王爷做事，下官不胜荣幸。”
[EN] Glowing with visions of another promotion, Lin Jue bowed: "Your Highness flatters me—serving Prince Yi is my greatest honor."
[ZH] 申简辰点点头，问道：“有多少人知道此事？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded and asked, "How many people know?"
[ZH] “王爷您，知府大人与下官，还有几个负责此事的手下，不过他们都是我的心腹，必然守口如瓶。”
[EN] "Only Your Highness, the Prefect, myself, and a handful of my most loyal men—every one sworn to silence."
[ZH] “你先回去，让我仔细想想。”
[EN] "Head back for now; let me think this over."
[ZH] 林觉拜别后，申简辰认为没有什么可想的，欺负他心爱之人，只有一个字：打！
[EN] Once Lin Jue bowed out, Shen Jianchen decided there was nothing to think over—bully the woman he loved, and only one word applied: BEAT!
[ZH] “白风！”
[EN] "Bai Feng!"
[ZH] 白风从外面进来：“王爷有何吩咐？”
[EN] Stepping in from outside: "Your orders, Your Highness?"
[ZH] “你去把陈浩宇给我抓来，带到神柳下，我要好好教训他。”
[EN] "Go grab Chen Haoyu and haul him beneath the Divine Willow—I'm teaching him a lesson."
[ZH] 王爷要打人，白风无不高兴，他练拳法以来，还未打过人，不如用陈浩宇练练手。
[EN] A royal order to beat someone up thrilled Bai Feng—he'd been drilling punches for months without a live punching bag! Chen Haoyu made great practice!
[ZH] “是！王爷！我这就去把他抓来！”
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness! Bringing him right back!"
[ZH] 白风一溜烟跑出去，赶了马车，到城里后，先去陈府外转一圈儿，没发现人。向陈府门外的下人一打听，才知道陈公子不在家。
[EN] Bolting out, Bai Feng drove into town, swung past Chen Manor, and learned from gatekeepers Second Young Master wasn't home.
[ZH] 白风心想，像陈浩宇这类人要么去青楼，要么去赌坊。他去过春香楼，不如去那里碰碰运气。
[EN] Where did a rake like Chen Haoyu go? Either a brothel or a gambling den! Having been to Chunxiang Brothel before, he checked there first.
[ZH] 白风刚将马车赶到春香楼外不远处，瞧见陈浩宇从马车里下来。他立刻启动功力，利用轻功迅猛之力，以迅雷不及掩耳的速度抓住陈浩宇……
[EN] Sure enough, pulling up near Chunxiang Brothel, he caught Chen Haoyu stepping straight out of his carriage! Launching his light-foot technique at blinding speed, Bai Feng swooped in like lightning and snatched Chen Haoyu clean off his feet...
[ZH] 近期，王爷开始给他教轻功，白风学得很快，加上拳法的功力，他比以前厉害许多。
[EN] Recently coached in light-foot work by Prince Yi on top of his boxing drills, Bai Feng had leveled up fast.
[ZH] 青瓜只觉一股强风袭来，迅速闭上眼睛，等睁开时，公子已不见人影，他四下瞅瞅，才发现公子被人抓上一辆马车，要去追时，马车已迅速启动。
[EN] Blinded by a sudden blast of wind, Qinggua squeezed his eyes shut—opening them to empty air where his master had stood! Whipping his head round, he saw his master tossed into a strange carriage that tore off down the lane before he could even yell!
[ZH] 青瓜吓得瘫坐在地上：“这可怎么办？我怎么给老爷和夫人交代！”
[EN] Qinggua collapsed onto his backside in panic: "What do I do?! How do I face Master and Madam?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇坐在马车里，身上被点了穴位，既不能喊叫，也不能动弹。
[EN] Inside the carriage, struck on his acupoints, Chen Haoyu sat frozen stiff and mute.
[ZH] 他见过白风，知道是仪王身边的侍卫。心想抓他的人肯定是仪王，他与仪王毫无过节，他为何要抓他？
[EN] He recognized Bai Feng as Prince Yi's guard—meaning Prince Yi had snatched him! Having zero grudge with Prince Yi, why on earth was he kidnapped?!
[ZH] 能确定的是，仪王不会害他性命，因为大家都说仪王人好，心好。
[EN] At least Prince Yi wouldn't kill him—everyone swore Prince Yi was kindhearted.
[ZH] 一路上陈浩宇带着疑惑，被带到城外的山脚下。
[EN] Riddled with confusion, he was driven straight out past the city gates to the foot of the hills.
[ZH] 白风停好马车后，先用绳子把陈浩宇绑起来，再点开穴位。
[EN] Stopping the carriage, Bai Feng tied Chen Haoyu's wrists first, then tapped open his acupoints.
[ZH] “陈公子，下车吧。”
[EN] "Out you get, Young Master Chen."
[ZH] 能说话后，陈浩宇大声问：“你凭什么抓我？”
[EN] Finding his voice, Chen Haoyu barked, "By what right do you kidnap me?!"
[ZH] 白风笑道：“凭王爷吩咐我这么做！赶紧下车，王爷还在神柳下等你。”
[EN] Bai Feng grinned: "By His Highness's order! Move it—His Highness is waiting beneath the Divine Willow."
[ZH] 陈浩宇很不情愿地走下马车，一日之内被绑两次，实在晦气！边走边说：“仪王这么有身份的人找我，我敢不去？何至于将我绑起来？”
[EN] Climbing down in foul disgust—kidnapped TWICE in one day, talk about cursed luck!—he grumbled as he walked: "If a royal prince summons me, wouldn't I come on my own? Why tie me up?!"
[ZH] 白风说：“少废话！一会儿见到王爷，有你好果子吃！”
[EN] Bai Feng sneered: "Quit yapping—you're about to get a taste of what's coming!"
[ZH] 白风押着陈浩宇顺着山脚下向神柳处走去。周围的雪早已融化，满眼是冬季的萧瑟。
[EN] Marching Chen Haoyu along the base of the hills toward the Divine Willow, melted snow left bare winter earth all around.
[ZH] 陈浩宇第一次来这里，他早听说此处有一棵神柳，经常有青年男女把红绸系在柳树上。他觉得甚是无聊，也不相信神柳真的像大家传言的那么神奇。
[EN] Chen Haoyu had never set foot out here before, having heard endless talk of young lovers tying red silk ribbons to some magic willow—total nonsense, he figured.
[ZH] 他只觉眼前神柳除粗壮些，年代久远些，与普通柳树毫无区别。
[EN] Up close, aside from a thick trunk and old age, it looked like any plain willow.
[ZH] 申简辰站在柳树下，瞧见白风将陈浩宇带来。没等陈浩宇行礼，直接命令道：“白风，把陈公子身上的绳子解开，你只管用拳头打他，避开要害处即可。”
[EN] Standing beneath the branches as Bai Feng marched Chen Haoyu up, Shen Jianchen skipped waiting for bows and barked: "Untie him, Bai Feng, and beat him with your bare fists—just avoid anything fatal."
[ZH] 白风二话不说，为陈浩宇解身上的绳子。
[EN] Without a second thought, Bai Feng slipped the ropes off Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 陈浩宇急了：“仪王！仪王！您再身份尊贵，也不能随便打人……”
[EN] Panic seized Chen Haoyu: "Prince Yi! Prince Yi! Royal rank or not, you can't beat people for nothing—"
[ZH] 话还未说完，只见白风对着陈浩宇一阵拳打脚踢……
[EN] Before he finished the sentence, Bai Feng's fists and boots rained down in a relentless storm...

#CH 143
[ZH] 白风不愧被王爷训练过，成功避开陈浩宇的要害，还把他打得鼻青脸肿，浑身是伤，那叫一个惨！
[EN] True to Prince Yi's training, Bai Feng dodged every vital spot while pounding Chen Haoyu black, blue, and swollen from head to toe—a total wreck!
[ZH] 申简辰怒气已消，命令道：“可以了，再打下去恐怕打残，让他知道得罪本王没什么好下场即可，以后最好眼睛放亮些，看清楚哪些人该欺负，哪些人不该欺负，别不小心踩到老虎尾巴。”
[EN] His temper cooled at last, Shen Jianchen called off the beating: "Enough—keep going and you'll cripple him. Just make sure he remembers crossing me doesn't end well. Open your eyes wider next time, watch who you mess with, and don't step on a tiger's tail."
[ZH] 白风停下手，生平第一次打有钱人，打得甚是痛快。
[EN] Lowering his fists, Bai Feng beamed—his first time pummeling a rich dandy, and it felt fantastic!
[ZH] 陈浩宇趴在地上，浑身是伤，疼痛难忍，心里着实想不明白，到底哪里得罪仪王。
[EN] Sprawled groaning on the frozen dirt, aching in every bone, Chen Haoyu still had zero clue how he had offended Prince Yi.
[ZH] 他忍着疼痛问道：“我……与仪王……无冤无仇，您……为何……要让手下……打我？”
[EN] Gasping through swollen lips, he asked, "I've got... no grudge... with Prince Yi... Why have your man... beat me?!"
[ZH] 申简辰靠近陈浩宇：“陈公子仔细想想近期做过什么坏事，又欺负过谁，自然便知。”
[EN] Stepping close, Shen Jianchen looked down cold: "Think back on the filthy stunts you pulled lately and who you bullied—you'll figure it out."
[ZH] 陈浩宇想到谣言一事：“难道……是……是因为……我散播……朱淑真的……谣言？”
[EN] The rumor campaign flashed into Chen Haoyu's head: "Because... I spread... rumors about... Zhu Shuzhen?!"
[ZH] “你倒不笨，一下猜中，的确因为此事。”
[EN] "Not as dumb as you look—bull's-eye."
[ZH] 申简辰担心陈浩宇胡乱猜测他与千里寻的关系，从而给千里寻带来不利，解释道：“因为你散播朱家姑娘的谣言，文渊不能与心爱的人在一起，他痛苦万分，打你都是轻的。”
[EN] Careful not to let Chen Haoyu suspect his own feelings for Qianli Xun and risk hurting her, Shen Jianchen added a cover: "Because of your smears against Miss Zhu, Wenyuan lost the woman he loves and suffered hell—beating you is getting off light!"
[ZH] “原来……王爷是……为乔文仙……打我，我……还以为……是为……朱淑真……”
[EN] "So... Your Highness beat me... for Qiao Wenxian... Thought it was... for Zhu Shuzhen..."
[ZH] “哼！是为文渊，顺便也替朱家姑娘教训你，她好好的姑娘家被你害惨，以后怎么嫁人？本王打你都是轻的。”
[EN] "Hmph! For Wenyuan—and teaching you a lesson for Miss Zhu too! You wrecked an innocent girl's life and marriage prospects; you deserved twice this beating!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇苦笑道：“她的婚事……知府大人……操心着……我可就……惨了……既被王爷打……还要……”
[EN] Chen Haoyu forced a bloody grimace: "Her marriage... Prefect Zhu's already... handled that... I'm the poor sucker... beaten by Your Highness... AND forced to..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇不想说下去，全身疼得难受。
[EN] Every rib throbbing, Chen Haoyu gave up talking.
[ZH] 申简辰叮嘱道：“今日本王打你之事，若说出去，我见你一次打你一次。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen warned him cold: "Breathe a single word that I had you beaten today, and I'll beat you every single time I lay eyes on you."
[ZH] “放心……我就是死……也不说……不说……这么丢人……的事。”
[EN] "Relax... kill me... and I still won't... brag about... something this humiliating..."
[ZH] “知道就好，白风，我们走，让陈公子一个人好好在这里想想他都做过哪些该打之事。”
[EN] "Smart boy. Come on, Bai Feng—leave Young Master Chen out here alone to reflect on his sins."
[ZH] 仪王带领白风离开，留下陈浩宇在地上继续趴着。地上冰凉，他忍痛努力爬起，刚站稳，又倒下，如此三四回，才站住，迈开腿向前走，只觉整个身体像灌了铅一样沉重。
[EN] Prince Yi strode off with Bai Feng, leaving Chen Haoyu flat on the freezing ground. Shivering against the cold earth, he gritted his teeth and fought to stand—collapsing three or four times before dragging himself upright and hobbling forward, every limb heavy as lead.
[ZH] 不知走了多久，天已蒙蒙黑，才走到山脚下的入口处，继续向城里走去，不小心被地上的石头绊倒，他竟然感受不到疼痛，只因身上被打的伤痛太过严重。
[EN] Stumbling on until twilight darkened the hills, he finally reached the road back toward town—tripping over a stone and crashing down without even feeling the fresh scrape over the symphony of pain already screaming across his body.
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续爬起来，向前走。他的人生再次感到憋屈，憋屈到无处可说，无法报复。
[EN] Crawling back up, he limped onward, swallowing a choke of bitter rage he dared neither speak aloud nor avenge.
[ZH] “公子！我可找到你！”
[EN] "Young Master! Found you at last!"
[ZH] 传来青瓜的声音，陈浩宇第一次觉得青瓜如此可爱。
[EN] Qinggua's voice rang out of the dusk—making Qinggua look downright angelic to Chen Haoyu for the first time in his life!
[ZH] 公子被人抓走后，青瓜在地上坐一会儿，立刻清醒过来，赶紧起身，赶马车追去。
[EN] After watching his master snatched off the street, Qinggua had sat stunned for half a minute before snapping out of it, hopping into the driver's seat, and whipping the horses in pursuit.
[ZH] 青瓜认为抓走公子的人应该是朝城外而去，便追到城外。当他到达城外后，不知该往哪个方向去，干脆每个方向都找找，最后才来到山脚下。
[EN] Betting the kidnappers headed outside the city gates, he had driven straight out—uncertain which road they took, searching trail after trail until reaching the foot of the hills.
[ZH] 天已蒙蒙黑，本以为根本不可能再找到，无意中看到黑暗中有个身影像公子，待靠近后，发现的确是。
[EN] With dusk closing fast and hope nearly gone, he spotted a limping silhouette in the gloom—and sure enough, it was his master!
[ZH] 青瓜出现，陈浩宇再也坚持不下去，向地上倒去。
[EN] At the sight of Qinggua, Chen Haoyu's last scrap of grit snapped and he pitched forward.
[ZH] ﻿青瓜快速上前扶住：“公子，谁这么大胆？把您打成这样？”
[EN] Qinggua lunged to catch him: "Who had the nerve to beat you like this, Young Master?!"
[ZH] “别……别问……那么多，去……永宁……居，等伤……好了，再……再回……陈府。”
[EN] "Quit... asking... Take me to... Yongning... Residence... Won't go home... to Chen Manor... till I heal..."
[ZH] 青瓜总算放心，公子虽满身是伤，可毕竟找到人，回陈府也好有个交代。他把公子扶上马车，回城赶往永宁居。
[EN] Relieved at least to have found his master alive so he wouldn't face Master's wrath empty-handed, Qinggua hoisted him into the carriage and drove back into town to Yongning Residence.
[ZH] 到达永宁居后，把公子扶进屋，吩咐下人找来大夫。大夫看完后，开了口服和涂抹的药物。
[EN] Helping him into bed at Yongning Residence, Qinggua sent servants running for a physician, who prescribed both herbal drafts and topical salves.
[ZH] 丫鬟把药给陈浩宇涂抹在全身的伤口上后，他才觉得舒服些，趴在床上，吩咐道：“青瓜，你过来。”
[EN] Once maids smoothed cooling ointment across his bruises, relief eased his groans. Lying face-down on the mattress, he called, "Qinggua, come here."
[ZH] 青瓜走到床边：“公子有何吩咐？”
[EN] Stepping to the bedside: "Yes, Young Master?"
[ZH] “回趟陈府，告诉我爹，我在永宁居住段时日，不要向他们提今日之事。”
[EN] "Go back to Chen Manor and tell Father I'm staying at Yongning Residence for a spell. Not a word about today."
[ZH] 青瓜答应完，不满道：“等公子好了，一定要把打公子的人好好收拾一顿！”
[EN] Promising he would, Qinggua bristled: "The second you heal, Young Master, we're going to hunt down whoever beat you and pay them back!"
[ZH] “行了，此事以后不许提起。”
[EN] "Shut it—never mention today again."
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续趴好，感受伤口涂抹药后带来的舒适感，他从未像现在一样觉得舒适感如此舒适。
[EN] Settling back onto the pillows, Chen Haoyu savored the cool sting of the salve—never in his life had plain comfort felt this sweet.
[ZH] 青瓜不敢再多说什么，老老实实回陈府，把公子的话一句不差给陈江林说了。
[EN] Not daring another peep, Qinggua rode back to Chen Manor and relayed his master's message word for word to Chen Jianglin.
[ZH] 陈江林生气儿子死性不改，又不着家，却也无可奈何，想到与朱家的亲事，倒十分高兴，盼着把朱家姑娘娶回陈府，能有人替他约束儿子。
[EN] Exasperated at his son running off again yet helpless to stop him, Chen Jianglin cheered right up thinking of the Zhu betrothal—eager to welcome Miss Zhu into Chen Manor to whip his boy into shape.
[ZH] 千里寻在陈浩宇离开后，对两家要订亲之事怎么都想不通，更担忧成为事实。她决定回趟朱府问个明白。
[EN] Meanwhile, after Chen Haoyu left Wangyue Pavilion, Qianli Xun could not shake the dread that this insane betrothal might actually be real, deciding to go straight to Zhu Manor for the truth.
[ZH] 自从搬出朱府，还是第一次回去，不是不想回，既被赶出，哪有再回去的道理？
[EN] It was her first time setting foot back inside Zhu Manor since being driven out—after all, why return to a house that threw her out?
[ZH] 朱家又要为她订亲，说明在朱晞颜心里还把她当女儿看待，既如此，她为何不能回去问个究竟？
[EN] Yet if Zhu Xiyan was arranging her marriage again, he still counted her as his daughter—giving her every right to demand answers!
[ZH] 到达朱府后，千里寻没有让下人给向夫人报告，直奔向夫人房中。
[EN] Arriving at Zhu Manor, she skipped having servants announce her and walked straight to Madam Xiang's quarters.
[ZH] 向夫人正在榻上靠着假寐，忽听女儿叫娘的声音，睁开眼一瞧，果然是女儿。
[EN] Dozing on her couch, Madam Xiang opened her eyes at the call of "Mother" to find her daughter standing before her.
[ZH] 她起身坐好：“淑真，你回来了？是不是你父亲让你回来的？”
[EN] Sitting up at once: "You're back, Shuzhen? Did your father summon you?"
[ZH] 千里寻没有回答，直接问：“娘可知父亲要为我与陈家订亲一事？”
[EN] Skipping pleasantries, Qianli Xun cut straight in: "Mother, do you know anything about Father betrothing me to the Chen family?"
[ZH] 向夫人诧异道：“与陈家订亲？没听说过，你是打哪里听来的？”
[EN] Madam Xiang blinked in genuine surprise: "Betrothing you to the Chen family? Never heard a word! Where did you pick that up?"
[ZH] 母亲的表现令千里寻紧绷的心弦松弛下来，难道是陈浩宇胡说？
[EN] Her mother's bewildered face eased the tight spring in Qianli Xun's chest—had Chen Haoyu been messing with her?
[ZH] 千里寻坐在向夫人身旁：“娘，今日陈浩宇来望月轩亲口对我说两家要订亲之事，女儿甚是疑惑，才回来问个究竟。”
[EN] Sitting beside Madam Xiang, she explained, "Chen Haoyu showed up at Wangyue Pavilion today claiming our two families are getting engaged—it sounded so wild I came straight here to check."
[ZH] “陈家公子说的？可我没听你爹提起过。”向夫人担心老爷有事瞒着她。
[EN] "Young Master Chen said that? Your father hasn't breathed a word to me!" Unease flickered in Madam Xiang lest her husband was hiding something.
[ZH] “娘，要不您帮我问问爹？”
[EN] "Mother, could you go ask Father for me?"
[ZH] “好，娘这就去问问，你跟我一起去，顺便见见他。”
[EN] "Of course—come along with me and see your father too."
[ZH] 千里寻担心见到朱晞颜说不了几句话会吵起来：“我还是不去了，省得爹批评我回朱府，干脆在娘房中等消息。”
[EN] Fearing two sentences with Zhu Xiyan would spark another shouting match, Qianli Xun shook her head: "Better I wait here in Mother's room so Father doesn't yell at me for stepping back into Zhu Manor."
[ZH] “既如此，你等着，娘去问个明白。”
[EN] "Wait right here then; Mother will get to the bottom of it."
[ZH] 向夫人带上丫鬟来到朱晞颜的书房，朱晞颜正在看书。
[EN] Taking a maid, Madam Xiang headed to Zhu Xiyan's study, finding him reading a book in peace.
[ZH] 既解决了谣言，又为女儿说下一门亲；既能把女儿嫁个好人家，还保住朱家颜面。朱晞颜心境大好。
[EN] Rumors crushed, a rich marriage lined up for his daughter, Zhu honor restored—Zhu Xiyan hadn't felt this chipper in months!
[ZH] 他甚至觉得陈浩宇策划散布谣言之事，是命中注定要发生的，如果没有此事，他哪有理由要求陈家娶自家姑娘。
[EN] He even fancied Chen Haoyu's smear campaign had been heaven-sent; without it, what leverage would he ever have had to make the Chens take his daughter?
[ZH] 与陈家订亲之事，朱晞颜不想张扬，担心有人破坏。尤其是淑真，一旦知道，肯定会闹得天翻地覆，说不定一段好好的婚事就这么泡汤。因此，他连向夫人也未提起。
[EN] Determined to keep the betrothal under wraps until sealed—knowing Shuzhen would raise hell and wreck it if wind reached her—he hadn't even breathed a word to Madam Xiang.
[ZH] 又担心儿媳陈文心知道后，会告诉淑真。朱晞颜从陈府回来，便把儿子叫到跟前，从书房找一摞书让儿子抱回去，告诉儿媳，她字写的不错，帮忙把这些书抄上一遍。
[EN] Afraid his daughter-in-law Chen Wenxin might visit Chen Manor and spill the beans to Shuzhen, the minute Zhu Xiyan got home from Chen Manor he had summoned Zhu Jingyuan and loaded him up with a tall stack of books for Chen Wenxin to copy out in her neat hand.
[ZH] 朱景元虽想不通，还是答应。
[EN] Mystified, Zhu Jingyuan had carried them over anyway.
[ZH] 想要抄完那些书，需要十天半个月。这段时日，陈文心绝对没有时间回陈府，又怎么会知道两家要订亲的消息。
[EN] Copying that stack would tie Chen Wenxin down for two weeks—zero free hours to visit Chen Manor or hear about the engagement!
[ZH] 他还给守门的下人打招呼，要是陈浩宇来府上，一律挡住，不准进，就说是他的命令。
[EN] He had also ordered gatekeepers to bar Chen Haoyu from setting foot inside Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 一切安排好后，坐等陈家前来提亲。
[EN] With every loophole sealed, all left was waiting for the Chens to arrive with betrothal gifts.
[ZH] 向夫人进来后，说道：“老爷，淑真回来了……”
[EN] Stepping into the study, Madam Xiang announced, "Husband, Shuzhen came back..."
[ZH] “她回来做什么？”朱晞颜没有放下书，装作不高兴的样子。
[EN] "What's she doing back here?" Without setting down his book, Zhu Xiyan put on a frosty scowl.
[ZH] “淑真回来问，是不是陈家要上门提亲？”
[EN] "She came asking whether the Chen family is coming to propose."
[ZH] 朱晞颜放下书，警惕地问：“夫人可听说此事？”
[EN] Lowering his book, sharp alertness flashed in Zhu Xiyan's eyes: "Has Madam heard anything about that?"
[ZH] 向夫人摇摇头：“没有，是陈家公子对淑真这么说。”
[EN] Madam Xiang shook her head: "Not a word—Young Master Chen told Shuzhen himself."
[ZH] “陈家公子无比顽劣，他的话岂能轻信？”朱晞颜又拿起书，“若此事是真，夫人可希望淑真嫁到陈家？”
[EN] "That boy is a wild prankster—how can anyone trust a word out of his mouth?" Lifting his book again, Zhu Xiyan tested casually, "If it were true, would Madam want Shuzhen marrying into the Chen family?"
[ZH] “若真有此事，倒也不错，只是淑真恐怕不愿意。我听文心说，淑真与她的弟弟不合，两人一见面都能打起来。”
[EN] "If it were true, it wouldn't be bad at all—only Shuzhen would probably fight it. Wenxin told me Shuzhen and her brother clash like cats and dogs, ready to throw punches the second they meet."
[ZH] 朱晞颜更为他的谨慎隐瞒感到满意：“你去告诉淑真，与陈家订亲一事，纯属空穴来风，让她回去吧，没事儿别回朱府。”
[EN] Pleased his secrecy had paid off, Zhu Xiyan waved a hand: "Go tell Shuzhen a Chen betrothal is pure hot air—send her back and tell her not to wander into Zhu Manor without cause."
[ZH] “是，老爷。”
[EN] "Yes, Husband."
[ZH] 向夫人回去后，把情况向女儿说明。
[EN] Returning to her chamber, Madam Xiang passed the denial straight to her daughter.
[ZH] 千里寻还是不大相信，她觉得陈浩宇再纨绔，也不可能拿这种事情胡闹，决定再去问问嫂嫂。
[EN] Still half skeptical—even a reckless dandy like Chen Haoyu wouldn't joke about his own wedding!—Qianli Xun decided to double-check with her sister-in-law.
[ZH] 来到哥嫂居住的院子，嫂嫂正坐在桌前忙碌，见淑真前来，招呼她坐下，手中的笔并未停。
[EN] Walking over to her brother's courtyard, she found Chen Wenxin buried at her desk writing nonstop, beckoning Shuzhen to sit without lifting her brush.
[ZH] “嫂嫂在写什么？这么忙碌？”
[EN] "What keeps Sister-in-law writing so furiously?"
[ZH] “唉！公爹突然给我布置任务，让我抄书，你看，一大摞，这段时日不能去望月轩看你，可别怪我。”
[EN] "Sigh! Out of nowhere Father-in-law dumped this mountain of books on me to copy! Look at this stack—can't even visit you at Wangyue Pavilion for weeks, so don't hold it against me!"
[ZH] “我怎么会怪嫂嫂？我哥哥呢？他去了哪里？”
[EN] "How could I? Where's my brother?"
[ZH] “你们真是兄妹情深，见面就问你哥，他刚出去，至于忙什么，我没工夫问他，一大摞书够我抄的。”
[EN] "Always inseparable—asking for your brother the second you walk in! Just stepped out on errands; too buried in copying to ask where."
[ZH] “嫂嫂，你有没有听说一件事？”
[EN] "Say, Sister-in-law—have you heard a certain piece of news?"
[ZH] “什么事？”陈文心继续抄书。
[EN] "What news?" Chen Wenxin asked, brush still flying.
[ZH] “陈浩宇今日来望月轩，说陈家要与朱家订亲……”
[EN] "Chen Haoyu showed up at Wangyue Pavilion today claiming the Chens and Zhus are getting engaged..."
[ZH] 陈文心停下手，抬起头：“有这等好事？”
[EN] Her brush froze mid-stroke as Chen Wenxin looked up beaming: "Is there really news that wonderful?!"
[ZH] 千里寻生气道：“看来嫂嫂不知，说明你弟弟欺骗我，竟然编出这等谎言戏弄我，看我怎么收拾他！”
[EN] Irritation spiked in Qianli Xun: "Looks like you know nothing—meaning that brother of yours made the whole thing up just to mess with me! Wait till I get my hands on him!"
[ZH] 陈文心继续抄书：“没准儿浩宇是喜欢你，故意这么说，想探测你的心意。”
[EN] Resuming her copying, Chen Wenxin grinned: "Maybe Haoyu actually likes you and cooked that up to test your reaction!"
[ZH] “嫂嫂更是胡说，陈浩宇怎么可能喜欢我？他要是喜欢我，我跳钱塘江的心都有。”
[EN] "Now Sister-in-law's talking crazy! Chen Haoyu liking me?! If he ever likes me I'll jump straight into the Qiantang River!"
[ZH] “我弟弟有那么差吗？”
[EN] "Is my brother really that awful?"
[ZH] “差得不是一点点！”
[EN] "Worse than awful!"
[ZH] 陈文心并不生气，依旧抄书：“放心，两家要订亲之事我没听说过，等我见到浩宇，非揍他一顿不可，竟然用这种事欺骗你。”
[EN] Unbothered, Chen Wenxin kept writing: "Relax—I haven't heard a syllable about any betrothal. Next time I catch Haoyu, I'll box his ears for teasing you with nonsense like that."
[ZH] 千里寻终于放心：“嫂嫂这么说，我就不用担心，我先回望月轩，省得碰见爹惹他生气。”
[EN] Weight lifted off her shoulders at last, Qianli Xun smiled: "Hearing Sister-in-law say that puts my mind at rest. I'll head back to Wangyue Pavilion before Father spots me and blows his top."
[ZH] “我太忙，没法送你，改日去望月轩看你。”
[EN] "Too swamped to see you out—I'll visit Wangyue Pavilion once I dig out from under these books."
[ZH] 千里寻笑笑，起身离开。
[EN] Smiling goodbye, Qianli Xun rose and left.
[ZH] 马车上，夏果忍不住问：“小姐，陈公子为何要用这种事情骗您？”
[EN] Riding home in the carriage, Xiaguo wondered aloud, "Why would Young Master Chen lie to you about something like that, Miss?"
[ZH] “谁知道他是怎么想的，别让我再碰见他，再碰见，必然一拳上去把他打倒在地。”
[EN] "Who knows what rattles inside his skull? Better hope he doesn't cross my path again, or one punch puts him flat on the pavement."
[ZH] 苦荞在一旁偷乐。
[EN] Beside her, Kuqiao hid a quiet giggle.
[ZH] 千里寻质问道：“你笑什么？”
[EN] Qianli Xun raised a brow: "What are you snickering at?"
[ZH] 苦荞说：“小姐又恢复到从前，我替小姐高兴。”
[EN] Kuqiao beamed: "Miss sounds just like her old self again—makes me happy!"
[ZH] 千里寻不再说话，并不是想起与文渊分手的痛苦，而是因苦荞的话，她开始品味自己的内心，痛苦依然在，只是没有之前那么强烈。
[EN] Qianli Xun fell silent—not from fresh agony over Wenyuan, but pausing to take her own pulse at Kuqiao's words. The ache still lingered, yet its jagged edge had softened.
[ZH] 时间真是个好东西，可以慢慢擦拭掉人生经历的一切，相信不久，她心中的痛苦会完全消失。
[EN] Time truly was the finest medicine, sanding down even the sharpest scars; before long, she trusted the pain would fade away for good.

#CH 144
[ZH] 七日之内，千里寻除写话本依旧是训练下人练习拳法。
[EN] Over the next seven days, Qianli Xun split her hours between writing huaben scripts and drilling her household staff in Qianli Fist.
[ZH] 而陈浩宇遍体鳞伤，想要好，也得十天半个月。他一直躲在永宁居养伤，不敢出来。
[EN] Meanwhile, bruised from crown to heel and needing two full weeks to heal, Chen Haoyu stayed holed up inside Yongning Residence nursing his wounds.
[ZH] 关于订亲一事，陈浩宇自认为凭朱淑真一己之力，便可阻止。他身上有伤，无暇顾及，便将此事抛诸脑后。
[EN] Figuring Zhu Shuzhen's fiery temper alone would blow the engagement sky-high, he dismissed the whole matter from his mind while recuperating.
[ZH] 七日时间过得很快，陈家果然不食言，陈江林与姚轻琰以及媒人，带上丰厚的聘礼，热热闹闹来到朱府。
[EN] Seven days flew by—and true to his word, Chen Jianglin arrived at Zhu Manor with Yao Qingyan, a matchmaker, and a festive procession of lavish betrothal gifts.
[ZH] 一早起来，朱晞颜吩咐下人准备，向夫人以及儿子和儿媳才知道订亲之事属实，高兴之余，充满担忧。
[EN] Only when Zhu Xiyan roused the household at dawn to deck the halls did Madam Xiang, Zhu Jingyuan, and Chen Wenxin discover the betrothal was real—thrilled, yet nervous all the same.
[ZH] 朱晞颜安慰道：“婚姻之事向来父母做主，淑真不同意根本没用。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan waved their worries away: "Parents rule marriages—Shuzhen objecting changes nothing."
[ZH] 于是一家人也热热闹闹迎接了陈家人。陈家人给的聘礼丰厚，朱家十分满意。
[EN] So the Zhu household rolled out a grand welcome for the Chens, beaming over the generous betrothal gifts.
[ZH] 六礼中，纳彩、问名、纳吉、纳征、请期“五礼”已成，只差亲迎一礼，待来年春暖花开之日再迎娶。
[EN] Out of the Six Rites of marriage, five—proposing, exchanging names, divining auspiciousness, presenting betrothal gifts, and setting the wedding date—were sealed in one sweep, leaving only the final rite of escorting the bride home once spring flowers bloomed next year.
[ZH] 申简辰心里念着千里寻，找了个借口，说是到朱府与知府大人商量政务。乔梦容很理解哥哥，自然不会多想。
[EN] Pining for Qianli Xun, Shen Jianchen cooked up an excuse about discussing local affairs with the Prefect—Qiao Mengrong trusted him without a second thought.
[ZH] 进了城，申简辰吩咐白风去望月轩。
[EN] Once inside the city gates, however, Shen Jianchen ordered Bai Feng straight to Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 白风心中嘀咕：“王爷不是要去朱府吗？为何去望月轩？”
[EN] Driving along, Bai Feng wondered silently: "Didn't Your Highness say we were going to Zhu Manor—why Wangyue Pavilion?"
[ZH] 不过，白风也开心，他每隔三四日去趟望月轩，在大门外与苦荞相见，次数根本不够。昨日刚见过，今日再见她，那才更好。
[EN] Not that Bai Feng complained! Visiting Kuqiao outside the gate every three or four days never felt enough—having just seen her yesterday, getting an extra visit today was pure gold!
[ZH] 可王爷到达望月轩门口，既不下马车，也不允许他找苦荞，大约待了一刻钟，又吩咐去朱府。
[EN] Yet arriving outside Wangyue Pavilion, His Highness neither stepped down nor let Bai Feng summon Kuqiao; after sitting mute for a quarter-hour, he ordered them on to Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 朱府正门外张灯结彩，看上去像是有喜事。
[EN] Red lanterns and silk festoons decked Zhu Manor's main gate like a wedding feast.
[ZH] 申简辰纳闷，下了马车，来到侧门。
[EN] Puzzled, Shen Jianchen stepped down and walked to the side gate.
[ZH] 门口的下人认识他，主动上前行礼：“仪王今日来得不巧，陈家与朱家正结亲，估计老爷脱不开身。”
[EN] Recognizing him, the gatekeeper bowed: "Your Highness picked an awkward day—the Chen and Zhu families are sealing a betrothal right now, so Master may be tied up."
[ZH] 申简辰并未强求，再说他也没有什么重要的事，只想从朱晞颜口中探得千里寻一二，既然他忙，还是算了。
[EN] Having no real business anyway—just hoping to glean a scrap of news about Qianli Xun from Zhu Xiyan—Shen Jianchen almost left.
[ZH] 可也因好奇问道：“不知两家为谁结亲？”
[EN] Still, curiosity nudged him: "Which two are getting betrothed?"
[ZH] “为姑娘和陈家公子。”
[EN] "Our Young Lady and Young Master Chen."
[ZH] 申简辰担忧地问：“可是幽栖居士与陈浩宇？”
[EN] Alarm pierced Shen Jianchen: "Youqi Jushi and Chen Haoyu?!"
[ZH] “正是。”
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness."
[ZH] 申简辰感到震惊，他好不容易等到千里寻与文渊分开，没想到这么快朱家又与陈家结亲。
[EN] Shock struck Shen Jianchen cold: no sooner had Qianli Xun broken clean from Wenyuan than the Zhus turned right around and engaged her to Chen Haoyu?!
[ZH] “告诉知府大人，我在门外等他。”
[EN] "Tell the Prefect I'm waiting outside for him."
[ZH] 下人感到奇怪，仪王刚才还好好的，为何突然变得不高兴？既然仪王硬是要见老爷，只能去叫：“是，王爷稍等片刻。”
[EN] Startled at how fast Prince Yi's sunny mood had turned stormy, the gatekeeper dared not stall: "Right away, Your Highness—please wait a moment."
[ZH] 不多时，朱晞颜满面笑容而出，见到仪王赶紧行礼：“仪王，请里面坐，今日朱府有喜事，您能来真是喜上加喜。”
[EN] Moments later Zhu Xiyan hurried out beaming and bowed low: "Please step inside, Prince Yi! Happy occasion at Zhu Manor today—Your Highness's arrival doubles our joy!"
[ZH] “朱家因喜事忙碌，我就不进去添乱，只是我不明白，为何知府大人要与陈家结亲？难道不忌讳……？”
[EN] "With Zhu Manor busy celebrating, I won't intrude. One thing puzzles me, though: why on earth is Lord Prefect marrying your daughter to the Chens? Don't you remember...?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜清楚仪王是说陈浩宇散播流言一事：“那件事下官不想张扬，只想私了，结亲是最好的私了办法，关键陈家长辈对淑真甚是满意，才有今日订亲之事。”
[EN] Knowing Prince Yi meant Chen Haoyu's rumor stunt, Zhu Xiyan explained, "I wanted to keep that scandal quiet and settle it privately—a marriage alliance is the cleanest fix, and the Chen elders think the world of Shuzhen, so we sealed the betrothal today."
[ZH] “幽栖居士可愿意？”
[EN] "Does Youqi Jushi agree?"
[ZH] 堂堂王爷竟然关心自己女儿是否乐意一门亲事，朱晞颜觉得奇怪。很快又想明白，也许是因为乔文仙的缘故。
[EN] Surprised a royal prince cared whether his daughter liked the match, Zhu Xiyan figured it had to be over Qiao Wenxian.
[ZH] 他听人议论过，乔家自从与苗家定亲后，乔家公子整个人像打了霜的茄子一样。仪王这样问，估计是担心乔文仙知道会伤心。
[EN] He had heard street talk that young Qiao looked dead on his feet since getting betrothed to the Miaos—Prince Yi must be worrying this news would crush his buddy further.
[ZH] “子女婚姻大事，历来由父母做主，淑真愿不愿意不重要。下官知道王爷与乔家公子关系要好，还请王爷多加规劝，既然乔家公子已订亲，以后还是不要再念着淑真。”
[EN] "Parents decide marriages, Your Highness—whether Shuzhen likes it doesn't matter. I know Your Highness is close with Young Master Qiao; please advise him to move on now that he's engaged himself."
[ZH] 被朱晞颜误会，申简辰没有必要过多解释，他忍不住表达道：“我本想到朱家提亲，看来已没有机会。”
[EN] Not bothering to correct Zhu Xiyan's guess, frustration pushed the truth right past Shen Jianchen's lips: "I had meant to come propose to the Zhu house myself. Looks like I missed my chance."
[ZH] 朱晞颜被惊住，才明白仪王看上淑真，难怪他会帮忙消除流言蜚语，可今日亲已定下，无法挽回。
[EN] Thunderstruck, Zhu Xiyan froze—so Prince Yi himself had his eye on Shuzhen?! No wonder he had moved mountains to kill the rumors! Yet the betrothal was already sealed—too late!
[ZH] “下官惶恐，不知仪王心意，还望王爷恕罪。”
[EN] "This humble official is mortified! Had I known Your Highness's heart—please forgive me!"
[ZH] 申简辰装作轻松的样子：“知府大人不必惶恐，我开个玩笑而已，只是想让知府大人知道，幽栖居士不愁嫁，您又何必着急给她订亲？”
[EN] Forcing an easy shrug, Shen Jianchen said, "Don't panic, Lord Prefect—just teasing. Only pointing out Youqi Jushi isn't desperate for suitors; no need to rush her into a match."
[ZH] 朱晞颜不知该如何接话，只顾弯腰行礼，沉默着。
[EN] At a total loss for words, Zhu Xiyan only stayed bowed low in silence.
[ZH] “我还有事，不打扰你，希望幽栖居士对这门亲事满意。”申简辰扔下朱晞颜，上马车离开。
[EN] "I have errands to run; won't keep you. Hope Youqi Jushi loves her new match." Leaving Zhu Xiyan standing there, Shen Jianchen climbed back into his carriage and rolled off.
[ZH] 朱晞颜站在原地，半晌才缓过神来，仪王说话时显然对他不满，看来他果真看上淑真。朱晞颜埋怨自己为何如此愚钝，不早些发现仪王的心思。
[EN] Long after the wheels faded, Zhu Xiyan stood stunned—Prince Yi's sharp edge left zero doubt he truly wanted Shuzhen! Zhu Xiyan kicked himself for being blind as a bat!
[ZH] 仪王即使已与佳和郡主有婚约，若淑真能被仪王纳为妾室，也比嫁给普通人家荣耀，他们朱家也算是皇亲国戚。唉，失去绝好机会。
[EN] Even with Princess Jiahe locked as primary wife, entering Prince Yi's Manor as a concubine beat marrying any merchant clan—making the Zhus imperial kin! What a golden chance blown!
[ZH] 朱晞颜叹口气，只怪朱家没有成为皇亲国戚的命。他很快恢复正常，陈家也不错，还是回去继续陪亲家。
[EN] Letting out a long sigh, Zhu Xiyan chalked it up to fate—the Chen clan was still a rich prize anyway—and walked back inside to host his in-laws.
[ZH] 白风一边赶马车，一边纳闷。王爷刚才说想到朱家提亲，真是将他吓坏，要是郡主听到，岂不伤心？
[EN] Driving away, Bai Feng's head spun: His Highness talking about proposing to the Zhu house had scared him half to death! What if Princess Jiahe ever heard?!
[ZH] 还好王爷说是开玩笑，白风才替郡主松口气。可他就是想不通，为何王爷偏偏开这种玩笑？而且一点儿都不好笑。
[EN] Thank goodness His Highness laughed it off as a joke—still, what a weird, unfunny joke!
[ZH] “白风，去望月轩。”
[EN] "Bai Feng, back to Wangyue Pavilion."
[ZH] 听到王爷命令，白风只能扭转马头。又去望月轩，真是猜不透王爷的心思，难道王爷真的喜欢上幽栖居士？白风不敢想下去。
[EN] Swinging the reins around, Bai Feng wondered silently: back to Wangyue Pavilion again?! Could His Highness really be in love with Youqi Jushi? He dared not finish that thought.
[ZH] 到达望月轩，申简辰没有下马车，吩咐道：“去，告诉门口的小厮，就说我今日见不到幽栖居士绝不离开，让她出来，到马车上见我。”
[EN] Pulling up outside Wangyue Pavilion, Shen Jianchen stayed inside and ordered: "Go tell the gate boy I'm not leaving today until I see Youqi Jushi—tell her to come out to my carriage."
[ZH] 王爷语气严厉，白风不敢违拗，只好跳下马车，给门口的小厮说了。
[EN] Hearing iron in his master's voice, Bai Feng hopped down and delivered the ultimatum to the gate boy.
[ZH] 小厮小跑着，朝闺房而去，半路上撞到夏果。
[EN] Sprinting toward the bedchamber courtyard, the gate boy crashed straight into Xiaguo.
[ZH] “你这小子，只顾跑，也不看人，撞得我生疼。”夏果揉着撞疼的手臂。
[EN] "Watch where you're going, idiot—nearly broke my arm!" Xiaguo rubbed her bruised elbow.
[ZH] 小厮解释道：“夏果姐姐担待些，仪王在外面等姑娘，我要赶去向姑娘报告。”
[EN] "Sorry, Sister Xiaguo! Prince Yi is outside demanding to see Young Lady—gotta report fast!"
[ZH] “小姐不在闺房，她在后院，你去那里找她。”
[EN] "Miss isn't in her room; she's in the back courtyard. Run there."
[ZH] 小厮跑向后院，夏果则朝前院走去，仪王肯定知道乔公子的状况，不如向他打听打听。
[EN] As the boy sprinted toward the back courtyard, Xiaguo headed toward the front gate—Prince Yi was bound to know how Young Master Qiao was doing; why not ask?
[ZH] 白风看到夏果从大门出来，上前问：“苦荞呢？”
[EN] Spotting Xiaguo step out, Bai Feng hurried up: "Where's Kuqiao?"
[ZH] 夏果很不满：“就知道找苦荞，我要是没记错，你们两个昨日才见过。”
[EN] Xiaguo rolled her eyes: "All you care about is Kuqiao—if I remember right, you two just met yesterday!"
[ZH] 白风向夏果吐吐舌头，不再理他。
[EN] Bai Feng stuck out his tongue and ignored her.
[ZH] 夏果走到马车跟前行礼道：“仪王，我家小姐在后院训练下人练习拳法，一会儿过来。”
[EN] Stepping up to the carriage, Xiaguo bowed: "Prince Yi, my mistress is in the back courtyard training servants in martial arts; she'll be right out."
[ZH] “知道了。”
[EN] "Understood."
[ZH] 夏果大胆问：“仪王，您与乔公子关系好，不知他近期可好？”
[EN] Working up her nerve, Xiaguo asked, "Since Prince Yi is close with Young Master Qiao, may I ask how he's been lately?"
[ZH] “是幽栖居士让你问的？”
[EN] "Did Youqi Jushi tell you to ask?"
[ZH] “不是，是我想问。”
[EN] "No—I wanted to ask myself."
[ZH] “既是你问，我不妨告诉你，文渊近期不错，吃胖了一些。”
[EN] "Since you asked, Wenyuan's doing fine—even put a little weight back on."
[ZH] “不知乔公子什么时候会走出乔府？”
[EN] "Any idea when Young Master Qiao will step out of Qiao Manor?"
[ZH] “这个……说不准，他的确好久没出来，也许是因为天冷。”
[EN] "Hard to say—he hasn't come out in ages, maybe just the cold weather."
[ZH] “谢仪王告知。”
[EN] "Thank you for telling me, Prince Yi."
[ZH] 夏果刚问完，千里寻带着苦荞走来……
[EN] Right as Xiaguo finished, Qianli Xun walked out with Kuqiao...
[ZH] 当小厮来到后院，千里寻正在训练下人练习拳法，经过多日训练，十人已基本掌握千里拳的要领。
[EN] Back in the rear yard, Qianli Xun had been drilling her ten students—every one now solid on Qianli Fist fundamentals.
[ZH] 其中有一名叫红豆的丫鬟，最为突出。千里寻决定，再训练些时日，出去也将红豆带在身边，若碰到欠揍的，就由她出面，省得自己亲自动手。
[EN] One maid named Hongdou stood out sharpest of all. Qianli Xun planned to train her a bit more, then bring Hongdou along as a personal bodyguard whenever she went out—no more dirtying her own hands on jerks!
[ZH] 正训练得开心，小厮前来报告：“姑娘，仪王在门外等您，他说不见到姑娘绝不离开。”
[EN] Mid-session, the gate boy ran up panting: "Young Lady, Prince Yi is outside the gate swearing he won't leave until he sees you!"
[ZH] 千里寻根本不想再与仪王碰面，可挡不住他前来骚扰，干脆先将这尊大佛哄走，于是带着苦荞来到门外。
[EN] Dreading another encounter with Prince Yi yet knowing he'd camp at her gate all day if ignored, she figured she'd better shoo this nuisance off fast and walked out with Kuqiao.
[ZH] 白风自然不管不顾，把苦荞拉到一边，苦荞也心甘情愿。千里寻并不理会，径自走到马车旁。夏果赶紧退到一边。
[EN] Bai Feng shamelessly tugged Kuqiao aside right away, Kuqiao tagging along gladly. Ignoring them, Qianli Xun walked straight to the carriage while Xiaguo stepped clear.
[ZH] “仪王，淑真这厢有礼，不知您今日前来，有何指教？”
[EN] "Greetings, Prince Yi. What brings Your Highness to my door?"
[ZH] 千里寻语气平静，难道她对订亲一事甚是满意？申简辰越想越不耐烦：“上车，我有话要问你。”
[EN] Hearing her cool, casual tone—wait, was she actually HAPPY about the betrothal?! Impatience flared in Shen Jianchen: "Climb aboard. I have questions for you."
[ZH] 对方什么都不说，直接让她上车，千里寻自然不乐意：“仪王有什么话尽管说，淑真无不听从。”
[EN] Refusing to play along, Qianli Xun stood firm: "Whatever Prince Yi has to ask, ask right here—Shuzhen is listening."
[ZH] “你要不上来，我会天天来望月轩烦你。”
[EN] "Climb in, or I'll camp outside Wangyue Pavilion pestering you every single day."
[ZH] 仪王竟然如此赖皮，看来今日不上马车是不行，为了以后的清静日子，不如上去，听听对方到底想说什么。
[EN] What a shameless pest! Seeing no easier way to buy future peace, she climbed inside to hear what on earth he wanted.
[ZH] 小姐登上马车，夏果在心里嘀咕：“原来仪王对小姐有意思，以前竟没瞧出来。”
[EN] Watching her mistress enter Prince Yi's carriage, Xiaguo muttered inside: "So Prince Yi really DOES have a thing for Miss—never noticed before!"
[ZH] 她瞧向苦荞，与白风聊得甚欢，马车内又有一对暧昧的。夏果甚觉无趣，想到自己孤身一人，便不想在此处待下去，不如回屋继续读书，在想象中与乔公子会面。
[EN] Glancing from Kuqiao laughing with Bai Feng to the cozy carriage, Xiaguo felt left out and bored—better head back indoors to her books and daydream about Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 马车上，千里寻坐定后，顾不上礼节，不满道：“仪王有话快说，有屁快放，你我孤男寡女，单独坐一辆马车上甚是不合适，我本就名声不好，您再这样下去，只怕我的名声会更不好。”
[EN] Seated inside the carriage, Qianli Xun dropped all ceremony and snapped, "Spit it out fast, Prince Yi! A single man and woman shut inside a carriage together looks awful—my name's bruised enough without you making it worse!"
[ZH] 申简辰并不生气，只要千里寻在他面前，他都不知道气为何物？
[EN] Nothing Qianli Xun said could ever make Shen Jianchen mad—just having her sit across from him melted every trace of temper.
[ZH] “你怎么能对一位王爷如此无礼？就不怕我治你的罪？”
[EN] "Talking so rude to a royal prince—aren't you afraid I'll punish you?"
[ZH] “王爷都不顾礼俗，我只好以无礼回赠。”
[EN] "When a prince tosses propriety out the window, I return the favor."
[ZH] “算了，我不跟你计较。我来是想问你，陈家与朱家订亲之事，你可知道？难道你就这么着急嫁人？”
[EN] "Fine, won't argue with you. Tell me—did you know the Chen and Zhu families just sealed a betrothal? Are you really that desperate to get married?!"
[ZH] “王爷这话是什么意思？什么时候朱家与陈家订亲？”千里寻担忧不已。
[EN] "What are you talking about?! When did the Zhus and Chens seal a betrothal?!" Alarm jolted Qianli Xun upright.
[ZH] 原来她并不知道，申简辰既开心又担忧：“我刚去过朱府，跟办喜事一般，一问才知道陈家前来提亲，看那样子，你跟陈家公子成亲的日子已定……”
[EN] So she truly had no clue! Equal parts relieved and urgent, Shen Jianchen told her: "I just came from Zhu Manor—red lanterns everywhere like a wedding! Asked the gatekeeper and learned the Chens just came to propose, and your wedding date with Young Master Chen is practically set..."
[ZH] 千里寻已坐不住，就要下马车，被仪王一把抓住手臂：“你去做什么？”
[EN] Shooting up to jump out of the carriage, Qianli Xun felt Prince Yi catch her arm: "Where are you going?"
[ZH] “去阻止两家订亲。”
[EN] "To stop that betrothal!"
[ZH] “现在去一点儿用处都没有，不如坐下来商量商量对策。”
[EN] "Going right now won't change a thing—sit down and let's think of a real countermove."
[ZH] 千里寻见仪王一脸真诚，也许他真的有好办法，便继续坐下。
[EN] Seeing dead-serious concern on his face and hoping he might actually have a workable fix, she sat back down.
[ZH] 申简辰放开抓住千里寻的手：“你真的不愿嫁给陈浩宇？”
[EN] Releasing her arm, Shen Jianchen asked, "You truly refuse to marry Chen Haoyu?"
[ZH] “那还用说？”
[EN] "Need you even ask?!"
[ZH] “既如此，我帮你。”
[EN] "Then I'll help you."
[ZH] “怎么帮？”
[EN] "How?"
[ZH] 申简辰犹豫片刻，大胆说道：“只要你答应跟我在一起，我便能帮你。”
[EN] Pausing a tense heartbeat, Shen Jianchen gambled everything: "Agree to be with me, and I'll stop it."
[ZH] 千里寻二话不说，又起身打算离开，又被仪王一把抓住：“你只要答应做我的妾室，凭我的身份，让两家取消婚约是很容易的事。”
[EN] Without a word, Qianli Xun rose again to leave, only for him to catch her wrist once more: "Just agree to enter my manor as a concubine—with my royal status, making both houses cancel this betrothal takes a snap of my fingers!"
[ZH] 千里寻转过头：“这就是仪王的办法？跟没说有什么两样？我不会给您当妾室，也希望仪王以后不要再管我的事。”
[EN] Qianli Xun whipped her head back cold: "That's your grand plan?! Might as well have said nothing! I will NEVER be your concubine—and stay out of my business from now on!"
[ZH] 她甩开仪王的手，走下马车，没有心情再回望月轩，喊了苦荞，坐上望月轩的马车直奔朱府。
[EN] Wrenching her wrist free, she hopped down from the carriage, skipped going back inside, called Kuqiao over, boarded her own Wangyue Pavilion carriage, and raced straight for Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 申简辰既开心又落寞，开心的是千里寻根本不愿嫁给陈浩宇，落寞的是她依然不愿意接受他的感情。
[EN] Left behind, Shen Jianchen felt equal parts relief and hollow ache: glad to his bones she despised marrying Chen Haoyu, crushed that she still shut every door against his heart.

#CH 145
[ZH] 千里寻到达朱府时，陈家刚刚离开。
[EN] Qianli Xun arrived at Zhu Manor just after the Chen procession departed.
[ZH] 她跳下马车，顾不上形象，冲进朱府，下人们也不去阻拦，毕竟是朱家姑娘，再被赶出去，也是朱家人。
[EN] Vaulting from her carriage without a thought for poise, she charged straight through the gate—none of the servants daring to block her since, cast out or not, she was still Master's daughter.
[ZH] 千里寻直奔正屋，瞧到坐在榻上的朱晞颜，顾不上行礼，质问道：“父亲为何瞒着我与陈家订亲？也不问问我是否愿意！”
[EN] Storming straight into the main hall to find Zhu Xiyan lounging on his couch, she skipped bowing and demanded, "Why did Father seal a betrothal with the Chen family behind my back—without even asking if I agree?!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜对女儿的无礼甚是不满，放下手中的书：“何须问你？只要两家大人说定即可，你只管等开春后嫁过去就是。”
[EN] Annoyed at her brashness, Zhu Xiyan set down his book: "Why ask you? Both families' elders agreed—all you have to do is wait till spring and climb into the wedding sedan."
[ZH] “父亲将女儿强行嫁给林觉，最后闹到和离，难道还想让女儿再和离一次？”
[EN] "Father pushed me into marrying Lin Jue and look how that ended in divorce—want to push me straight into a second divorce?!"
[ZH] “你嫁给林觉时，我征求过你意见，你是同意的，我也没想到，最后闹到和离；现在你因流言坏名声在外，以后还想嫁什么好人家？陈家愿意上门提亲，那是你的造化，不偷着乐，还跑来质问我，是何道理？”
[EN] "When you married Lin Jue I asked your consent and you said yes—nobody foresaw a divorce! Now rumors ruined your name across Lin'an; what fine house would come calling? Having the Chens propose is pure fortune—instead of thanking your lucky stars, you storm in grilling me?!"
[ZH] “我宁可不嫁人，也不会嫁给陈浩宇！”
[EN] "I'd rather stay single till I die than marry Chen Haoyu!"
[ZH] “你嫁不嫁人，要嫁给谁，不是由你说了算，婚事已定下，没有反悔的余地，闹也没用。”
[EN] "Whether you marry—or whom you marry—isn't up to you! The betrothal is sealed, zero room to back out—raising hell won't change a thing!"
[ZH] “婚事定下，是你们的事，我坚决不嫁，到时只怕爹抬到陈家的是我的尸体！”
[EN] "You sealed it, not me! I refuse to marry him—come spring, the only thing Father will deliver to Chen Manor is my corpse!"
[ZH] 只听“啪”的一声，朱晞颜用手拍在桌上，桌上的茶盏被震翻，里面的茶水流了一桌。
[EN] With a sharp crack, Zhu Xiyan slammed the table so hard his tea bowl flipped, spilling hot tea across the wood.
[ZH] “你别用死吓唬我，你就是死了，也是陈家的鬼！”
[EN] "Don't threaten me with death! Even dead, your ghost belongs to the Chen family!"
[ZH] 陈文心与朱景元搀扶向夫人进来，见父女两个吵得凶，都不敢大声说话。
[EN] Right then Chen Wenxin and Zhu Jingyuan walked in steadying Madam Xiang; hearing father and daughter roaring at each other silenced them all.
[ZH] 向夫人把女儿拉到一边：“坐下，跟你爹好好说话。”
[EN] Madam Xiang hurried to tug her daughter aside: "Sit down and speak calm with your father."
[ZH] 千里寻根本不愿坐，继续生着气。
[EN] Too furious to sit, Qianli Xun stood rigid, chest heaving.
[ZH] 朱晞颜不想再听女儿反对订婚的话，从榻上下来，对向夫人说：“你好好劝劝她。”然后走出门。
[EN] Refusing to listen to another word against the match, Zhu Xiyan stepped off his couch, told Madam Xiang, "Talk some sense into her," and marched out the door.
[ZH] 公爹不在，陈文心才敢开口说话：“淑真，我们陈家可是临安首富，嫁过去后不会亏待你。”
[EN] Once Father-in-law cleared out, Chen Wenxin dared speak: "Shuzhen, the Chen family is the wealthiest house in Lin'an—marry over and you'll never suffer a day!"
[ZH] “嫂嫂这是什么话？好像我是爱钱之人，我说的是你那弟弟，我跟他实在不合，为何偏偏要让我嫁给他？”千里寻过去抓住陈文心的手，“嫂嫂，你人最好，能不能回陈府，劝说您父母，让他们取消婚约？”
[EN] "What kind of talk is that, Sister-in-law—acting like I'm after money?! It's your brother I'm talking about! We mix like oil and fire—why force me to marry him?!" Grabbing Chen Wenxin's hands, Qianli Xun pleaded, "You're the kindest soul, Sister-in-law—please go back to Chen Manor and beg your parents to call off the betrothal!"
[ZH] 向夫人批评道：“说的什么话？女子被退婚，以后很难在人面前抬起头，听你爹的话，安心嫁到陈家。”
[EN] Madam Xiang scolded her: "Watch what you say! A woman dropped from a betrothal can never lift her head in public again! Obey your father and marry into the Chen house in peace."
[ZH] 朱景元也劝道：“淑真，浩宇只是顽劣些，等成亲后，肯定会好，再等你们将来有个孩子，他当上爹，为给孩子做表率，自然会痛改前非。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan joined in: "Haoyu's just a bit wild, Shuzhen—once wed he'll settle down, and once you have kids he'll straighten right up to set a good example."
[ZH] 全家没人站在自己这边，千里寻放开嫂嫂的手，生气道：“娘，嫂嫂，我上次回来问两家是否要订亲，娘说没这回事，嫂嫂也说不知道，该不会都是故意骗我？”
[EN] Not a single soul in the room on her side! Dropping her sister-in-law's hands, Qianli Xun glared: "Mother, Sister-in-law—last time I came asking about a Chen betrothal, Mother swore there was nothing and Sister-in-law swore she knew nothing! Were you both in on fooling me?!"
[ZH] “当时我真不知，今日才明白，原来公爹怕坏事，故意瞒着婆婆，还让我抄书，不过是为防止我回到陈府得知消息，忍不住告诉你。还别说，公爹这事处理得相当好。”陈文心笑着说完，安慰道，“你放心，以后嫁给浩宇，他若欺负你，只管来找我，我替你收拾他。”
[EN] "I really had no clue back then! Only realized today Father-in-law kept Mother in the dark and buried me in copying books so I wouldn't visit Chen Manor and leak it to you! Gotta hand it to Father-in-law—slick move!" Laughing, Chen Wenxin patted her hand: "Relax! Once you marry Haoyu, if he ever dares give you grief, come straight to me and I'll tan his hide for you!"
[ZH] 千里寻将三位亲人扫视一遍，他们都面带笑容，显然对两家结亲之事十分满意，看来只剩下自己一人孤军奋战。
[EN] Scanning all three relatives—every single face beaming over the match—Qianli Xun realized she stood all alone.
[ZH] 她想到陈浩宇，他自然反对，要不怎么第一个跑到望月轩告诉她订亲之事？当时误解他，不如现在去寻他，让他劝说陈家退婚。
[EN] Then Chen Haoyu flashed into mind! He hated this match just as fierce—why else had he sprinted to Wangyue Pavilion to tip her off?! She had misjudged him earlier; time to go hunt him down and get him working on the Chens to break it off!
[ZH] “娘、哥哥、嫂嫂你们忙，我还有事。”千里寻带着苦荞迅速离开。
[EN] "Excuse me, Mother, Brother, Sister-in-law—got urgent business." Calling Kuqiao, Qianli Xun bolted straight out.
[ZH] 向夫人望着女儿出去的背影叹道：“希望淑真不要再闹出事来，顺利嫁到陈家，以后的日子也好有个着落。”
[EN] Watching her daughter rush off, Madam Xiang sighed: "Pray Shuzhen doesn't stir up fresh trouble and settles into the Chen house without a hitch so she has a real anchor in life."
[ZH] “婆婆说的是，之前我偶尔瞎想过，若是淑真能嫁给浩宇那该多好，没想到竟然美梦成真，呵呵，浩宇也不知几辈子修来的福气，竟然能娶到江南第一女词人。”陈文心满脸笑容。
[EN] "Right you are, Mother! Truth be told, I used to daydream how lovely it'd be if Shuzhen married Haoyu—never dreamed it'd come true! Haha, Haoyu must've saved a kingdom in a past life to land Jiangnan's foremost female ci poet!" Chen Wenxin beamed.
[ZH] 向夫人见儿媳对这段姻缘满意，也甚是欣慰：“陈家不愧是大户人家，眼界与别家不同，不会将市井之人的议论当回事。”
[EN] Seeing her daughter-in-law thrilled comforted Madam Xiang too: "The Chens really have the breadth of a grand family—paying zero heed to street gossip."
[ZH] 朱景元说道：“娘，文心嫁到朱家，就是朱家人，别人不了解淑真，她还能不了解？她只要一回娘家，就对家人说起淑真的好，陈家当然不会把外人的话当回事。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan smiled: "Once Wenxin married into the Zhu house, she became one of us—nobody knows Shuzhen better than her! Every visit home she sings Shuzhen's praises, so of course the Chens ignore outsiders' chatter."
[ZH] 向夫人对这个儿媳更是满意：“说起来，淑真失忆后才与你来往甚密，难道以前你也替她说话？”
[EN] Pleased with her daughter-in-law all the more, Madam Xiang remarked, "Come to think of it, Shuzhen only grew close to you after losing her memory—you spoke well of her even before that?"
[ZH] 陈文心解释道：“那是自然，以前淑真对我爱理不理，只因才高，不愿与我等俗人往来，但人极好。婆婆您不知道，我身边的丫鬟月桂一次走路没踩稳，脚扭伤，正好碰见淑真，她便让苦荞把月桂扶起来，先去她的小院，涂上药，再让苦荞把月桂送回来。月桂是我从娘家带回来的，从小与我一起长大，关系自然亲近些，总是在我面前对淑真赞不绝口，我自然对她印象好。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin explained, "Of course! Before amnesia Shuzhen kept aloof out of sheer poetic pride, yet her heart was pure gold. Mother wouldn't know—once my maid Yuegui slipped and sprained her ankle right in front of Shuzhen. Shuzhen had Kuqiao help Yuegui into her own courtyard, rub medicine on her ankle, and escort her all the way home! Yuegui grew up beside me at Chen Manor, so hearing her praise Shuzhen nonstop won me right over."
[ZH] 向夫人夸道：“原来如此，也是淑真幸运，遇到你这样的好嫂嫂。”
[EN] Madam Xiang praised warm: "So that's why! Shuzhen truly struck gold getting a sister-in-law like you."
[ZH] 一路上，千里寻寻思如何才能找到陈浩宇，自上次见过，再没遇见他。他既不愿与她订亲，为何今日这么大的事不见人影？千里寻决定到永宁居碰碰运气。
[EN] Rolling along in her carriage, Qianli Xun wondered where to track down Chen Haoyu—had not seen hide or hair of him since his warning, not even today on his own betrothal day! Might as well try Yongning Residence first.
[ZH] 到达永宁居门口，千里寻下了马车后，吩咐车夫先回去，不用在外面等，毕竟离得不远。
[EN] Stepping out before Yongning Residence, Qianli Xun sent her driver home—no need waiting when Wangyue Pavilion stood just down the block.
[ZH] 她瞧着“永宁居”三个大字，正要上前敲门，一位下人朝这边走来。
[EN] Looking up at the plaque reading "Yongning Residence," she raised her hand to knock just as a servant walked up the lane.
[ZH] 来人正是青瓜，出去给公子买药，大夫开的治伤药快用完，得提前买些备上。
[EN] It was Qinggua returning from the apothecary with fresh salves—the physician's ointments were running low and needed restocking.
[ZH] 也不知是哪个杀千刀的把公子打成那样，过去一周，才好一小半儿。还好，脸上的伤倒是痊愈，至于身上，只要不脱衣裳别人也发现不了。
[EN] Who knew what ruthless bastard had pounded his master so hard—a whole week later and barely half healed! At least his face looked normal again, and winter clothes hid the bruises across his torso.
[ZH] 青瓜见有位漂亮姑娘站在门口，担心是公子在外惹的烂桃花，上前小心问：“姑娘找谁？”
[EN] Spotting a gorgeous young lady standing at the gate—worried she might be some romantic fling Young Master picked up outside—Qinggua approached cautious: "Who is Young Lady looking for?"
[ZH] 千里寻见青瓜手中拎的药包和药瓶，心想，看来永宁居有人病得不轻，希望不是陈浩宇。
[EN] Seeing packets and bottles of medicine in Qinggua's hands, Qianli Xun wondered who inside was sick—better not be Chen Haoyu!
[ZH] “陈浩宇在这里住过，我找他。”
[EN] "Chen Haoyu stays here—I'm looking for him."
[ZH] 青瓜这才认出眼前的漂亮姑娘正是朱家姑娘：“不知姑娘找我家公子做什么？”
[EN] Recognition dawned on Qinggua—this beauty was Miss Zhu! "May I ask what business Young Lady has with my master?"
[ZH] 苦荞说：“我家小姐找你家公子有事商议，如果人在，麻烦通报一声。”
[EN] Kuqiao spoke up: "My mistress has something to discuss with your master. If he's in, go announce her."
[ZH] 青瓜犹豫着：“姑娘还是说说到底什么事？我好回我家公子。”
[EN] Qinggua hesitated: "Could Young Lady tell me what it's about so I can report?"
[ZH] 苦荞生气道：“你个下人问那么多干什么？只管通报便是！”
[EN] Kuqiao snapped, "Since when does a servant cross-examine guests?! Just go announce her!"
[ZH] 青瓜只觉眼前的丫鬟说话如此厉害，估计朱家姑娘也不是好惹的，再说两家要订亲，以后她嫁给公子，就是自己的女主人，得罪不起，还是老实相告为好。
[EN] Wincing at how sharp even the maid's tongue was, Qinggua figured Miss Zhu had to be twice as fierce—and since she was about to become his future Young Madam whom he dared not offend, better just tell her the truth.
[ZH] “我家公子在，稍等片刻，我进去通报。”
[EN] "My master is inside—please wait a moment while I announce you."
[ZH] 经过几日治疗，陈浩宇已无需躺在床上，可以到处活动。
[EN] After several days of treatment, Chen Haoyu was off bedrest and roaming the yard again.
[ZH] 身上的伤还未痊愈，想去春香楼开荤需等些时日，只好耐心在永宁居待着，等身上肌肤完好无损，再继续以前浪荡的生活。
[EN] Still too bruised to visit Chunxiang Brothel, he had no choice but to sit tight in Yongning Residence until every mark faded before resuming his wild streak.
[ZH] 他正在屋外逗鸟，鸟被关在笼子里叽叽喳喳地叫。青瓜拎着一大堆药从外面匆匆回来，看上去很着急。
[EN] Busy outside teasing a chirping songbird in its cage, he watched Qinggua rush in weighed down with medicine packets, looking frantic.
[ZH] “买个药，急什么？难道急着投胎？”
[EN] "Buying medicine—what's the rush, racing to reincarnate?"
[ZH] “公子，未来的少夫人要见您，人正在外面候着。”
[EN] "Young Master, your future Young Madam is at the gate asking to see you!"
[ZH] “什么未来的少夫人？把话说明白些。”
[EN] "Future Young Madam who?! Speak plain!"
[ZH] “是朱家姑娘，今日是两家订亲的日子，她不就是未来的少夫人？”
[EN] "Miss Zhu! Today's your betrothal day—doesn't that make her future Young Madam?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇仔细想想正是今日，一着急，一脚踢在青瓜的身上：“闭上你的臭嘴！把人给我叫进来！”
[EN] Realizing today was the seventh day, Chen Haoyu swung a sharp kick into Qinggua's shin: "Shut your filthy mouth and bring her in!"
[ZH] 因劲儿使得较大，身上的伤口疼起来，陈浩宇努力忍着。
[EN] Twisting too hard sent fresh pain stabbing across his bruised ribs; Chen Haoyu clenched his teeth to hide it.
[ZH] 青瓜捂着被公子踢疼的地方，对一旁的下人说：“没听见吗？快去把人叫进来！”
[EN] Rubbing his shin, Qinggua barked at a nearby servant: "Deaf?! Go bring her in!"
[ZH] 下人匆匆而去。
[EN] The servant sprinted off.
[ZH] 疼痛有所缓解后，青瓜把公子扶到椅子上坐下：“公子，您在，我把药放好，再来伺候您。”
[EN] Once the throb eased, Qinggua helped his master into a chair: "Sit tight, Young Master—I'll put this medicine away and come right back."
[ZH] 千里寻来到院中，永宁居的确比望月轩大，布置得更精巧奢华。
[EN] Stepping into the courtyard, Qianli Xun noted Yongning Residence dwarfed Wangyue Pavilion, decked out with lush, pricey flair.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一眼瞅到朱淑真，装出悠闲舒适的样子：“朱淑真，今日是两家定亲的日子，是不是让你给搅黄了？专程来向本公子报喜？”
[EN] Spotting Zhu Shuzhen walk in, Chen Haoyu leaned back putting on breezy nonchalance: "Well, Zhu Shuzhen—today's betrothal day! Blew the whole thing up already and rushed over to bring me the good news?"
[ZH] “让你失望了，我晚去一步，两家已将婚事定下。”
[EN] "Hate to disappoint you—I arrived one step too late. Both families already sealed the betrothal."
[ZH] “什么？都已定下？枉我高看你一回，本以为你可以把订亲之事搅黄，没想到你这么无用，难道你想嫁给本公子不成？”
[EN] "WHAT?! Sealed already?! And here I gave you credit thinking you'd nuke it in two seconds—turns out you're useless! Don't tell me you actually WANT to marry me?!"
[ZH] “闭上你的臭嘴！老娘嫁给谁都不会嫁给你！你既不愿意，为何今日躲在永宁居不前去阻拦？”
[EN] "Shut your rotten mouth! I'd marry a turnip before marrying you! If you hate it so much, why were you hiding out in Yongning Residence instead of stopping it today?!"
[ZH] “一言难尽，本公子近几日受重伤，需要治疗，才老实躲在这里。你倒好，大活人一个，为何不及时前去阻止？”
[EN] "Long story—got hurt bad a few days back and had to hole up here healing! What's YOUR excuse, walking around healthy as an ox?!"
[ZH] “我爹欺骗我，说两家根本不会订亲，我信以为真，谁知竟然在今日瞒着我把亲事定下，我来找你，是想让你回陈府劝说陈家人尽快退婚。”
[EN] "My dad lied straight to my face swearing there was no betrothal, and I fell for it! Then he sealed it behind my back today! I came to tell you to get back to Chen Manor and talk your family into breaking it off right now!"
[ZH] “你就不怕退婚对你影响不好？”
[EN] "Not worried a broken engagement will smear your name?"
[ZH] “哼！流言蜚语已让我名声大臭，还怕退婚？你尽管劝说，在老娘看来，退婚后被人嘲笑，也比嫁给你强！”
[EN] "Hmph! Rumors already dragged my name through every gutter in Lin'an—what's a broken betrothal? Go talk them round! Being laughed at beats marrying you ten times over!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇听着刺耳，却不愿反驳，谁让他也不愿娶眼前的母老虎。朱淑真提到流言，他谨慎地问：“你爹没告诉你散播流言的人是谁？”
[EN] Her jab stung, yet sharing the exact same wish kept him from snapping back. At mention of the rumors, he probed cautious: "Didn't your dad tell you who started the rumors?"
[ZH] 千里寻谨慎地问：“你问这干吗？难道流言是你散播的？”
[EN] Suspicion flashed in Qianli Xun's eyes: "Why ask? Don't tell me YOU started them?!"
[ZH] 如此看来，朱晞颜并未告诉女儿他陈浩宇正是散播流言之人。
[EN] Aha—Zhu Xiyan had not told his daughter Chen Haoyu was the culprit!
[ZH] 真是老奸巨猾！既不想得罪陈家，还想给女儿找个下家，害得自己还未遇到喜欢的姑娘，就得娶一只母老虎。
[EN] Sly old fox—keeping peace with the Chens while offloading his daughter onto him, saddling him with a tigress before he even met a girl he liked!
[ZH] “怎么可能是我散播的？我是觉得散播流言之人十分可恶，如果抓到他，你会怎么做？”
[EN] "Me?! No way! Just saying whoever spread them is pure scum—if you ever caught him, what would you do?"
[ZH] “如果抓到他，老娘抽了他的筋，剥了他的皮，然后再将他暴揍一顿。”千里寻边说边来气，仿佛散播谣言之人正在眼前。很快她缓和下来，“估计根本抓不到，现在我已不在意此事，反正老百姓也不再议论。对了，你准备什么时候回家劝说你父母？”
[EN] "Catch him and I'd yank out his tendons, peel off his hide, and beat him flat!" Fire blazed in Qianli Xun as if the rat stood right before her, before cooling down: "Doubt they'll ever catch him anyway, and I don't care anymore since folk stopped talking. Back to business: when are you going home to talk your parents round?"
[ZH] “为了我的幸福，现在就回去。”
[EN] "For my own future? Heading back right this second!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇答应得爽快，千里寻第一次觉得他还不错，不由关心道：“你的伤好些了吗？”
[EN] Surprised at his swift cooperation, Qianli Xun felt Chen Haoyu was not hopeless for once, even asking: "How are your injuries holding up?"
[ZH] “还没完全好，但不碍事，放心，过不了几日，你就会收到退婚的好消息。”陈浩宇信誓旦旦。
[EN] "Not all healed yet, no big deal. Relax—in a few days you'll get the sweet news that our betrothal is off!" Chen Haoyu promised loud and sure.
[ZH] 千里寻当然开心：“好，我在望月轩等你的好消息。”
[EN] Smiling in relief: "Great—I'll wait for your good news at Wangyue Pavilion."
[ZH] 两个人第一次以缓和的态度对话，只因二人有共同的目标。
[EN] For the very first time, the two arch-enemies spoke without throwing a single punch—united by one shared mission.

#CH 146
[ZH] 青瓜为公子换过身上的药后，公子执意要回陈府，青瓜套好马车，载着陈浩宇回家。
[EN] After Qinggua changed his dressings, Chen Haoyu insisted on heading straight back to Chen Manor; Qinggua hitched the carriage and drove him home.
[ZH] 因身上伤未痊愈，陈浩宇回到陈府走起路来没有之前风风火火。他小心翼翼来到父母居住的院子，还未进屋，脑海里已浮现父母恩爱的场景。
[EN] Still sore from healing bruises, Chen Haoyu shed his usual swagger as he walked with care toward his parents' courtyard—already picturing the sweet domestic scene waiting inside.
[ZH] 说句心里话，陈浩宇对自己的父母羡慕不已，也想找个姑娘，与她成亲，白头偕老。谁知老天不遂人愿，偏偏让他娶朱淑真。
[EN] Truth be told, Chen Haoyu envied his parents' marriage more than anything, wishing he too might meet one woman to grow old beside—only for Heaven to stick him with Zhu Shuzhen instead!
[ZH] 果然，一进屋，父亲正在与母亲下棋，他们眼中只有对方，根本未注意到他。
[EN] Sure enough, stepping inside, he found Father and Mother engrossed in a chess game, having eyes only for each other.
[ZH] 丫鬟见公子进来，刚要唤，被他阻止，只好默不作声，陈浩宇羡慕地听父母聊天儿。身上不舒服，他今日变得安静许多。
[EN] Waving a maid silent before she could announce him, Chen Haoyu stood watching with quiet envy—sore bruises making him uncommonly mellow today.
[ZH] “每次跟你下棋都让我，很没意思。”姚轻琰幸福地批评着。
[EN] "Every single game you let me win—takes all the fun out!" Yao Qingyan teased, beaming.
[ZH] 陈江林继续让一步：“我这辈子谁都不让，只让夫人。”
[EN] Making another generous move, Chen Jianglin smiled: "I don't yield an inch to anyone in this world—only to my wife."
[ZH] “你既然让我，我就不客气，输了别跟我置气。”
[EN] "Well, if you insist, I won't hold back—don't pout when you lose!"
[ZH] “哪里话？我何时如此小气？又何时生过夫人的气？等下完棋，我带你去逛街，陈家裁缝铺来了位新师傅，衣裳做得甚好，我让他给你新做了两身，胭脂水粉铺也进了新品，我已吩咐人包好，一会儿去取。”
[EN] "When have I ever been petty or mad at my wife? Once we finish this game, I'm taking you shopping! Our Chen tailor shop hired a master tailor who's already made two new outfits for you, and our cosmetics shop got fresh stock—packed and waiting for us to pick up."
[ZH] “让人送来就好，何必你我亲自跑一趟？”
[EN] "Why not have a servant deliver them instead of trekking out ourselves?"
[ZH] “别人送来多没意思，我想带夫人亲自去取，既可以陪你散散心，我也好顺便带你去铺子里瞧瞧。”
[EN] "Where's the fun in delivery? I want to take my wife out for fresh air and show you around the shops myself!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉得父亲是天底下最会哄女人的男人，什么时候有个女人也能让他心甘情愿去哄？
[EN] Father had to be the slickest wife-whisperer alive, Chen Haoyu thought—would he ever meet a woman he wanted to spoil like that?
[ZH] 姚轻琰放下最后一步棋，高兴地说：“我赢了！”
[EN] Dropping her winning stone, Yao Qingyan cheered: "I win!"
[ZH] 陈江林说：“夫人高兴我便高兴。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin smiled warm: "Your smile is my win."
[ZH] 见父母已下完棋，陈浩宇才说道：“爹、娘，我找你们有事。”
[EN] Seeing the match conclude, Chen Haoyu stepped forward: "Father, Mother—I need to talk to you."
[ZH] 陈江林瞧向儿子，立刻拉下脸：“这几日又跑到哪里去鬼混？”
[EN] Glancing up, Chen Jianglin's face snapped stern: "Where did you vanish to carousing these past few days?!"
[ZH] “儿子没鬼混，一直住在城东的宅子。”
[EN] "Didn't go carousing—just stayed at our east-city house."
[ZH] “你有这么老实？”陈江林继续严厉地问。
[EN] "Behaving yourself like that?!" Chen Jianglin pressed hard.
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“他都是订婚的人，别这样对他说话。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan nudged him: "He's an engaged man now—don't talk to him like a little boy."
[ZH] “夫人说得对。”陈江林又和颜悦色，“既回来，坐下与我和你娘说会儿话。”
[EN] "Fair point, Wife." Softening at once, Chen Jianglin gestured: "Sit down and talk with your mother and me."
[ZH] 陈浩宇小心坐下。
[EN] Chen Haoyu lowered himself onto a chair with care.
[ZH] 姚轻琰问：“你今日怎么看上去不对劲儿？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan frowned: "Why do you look off today?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇万万不能承认被仪王暴揍之事。
[EN] He would die before admitting Prince Yi had pounded him flat!
[ZH] “没……没什么，听说爹娘已给我定下朱家姑娘，我与朱家姑娘实在合不来，娶她只有遭殃的份儿，想请爹娘将这门亲事给退了……”
[EN] "N-nothing... Heard Father and Mother betrothed me to Miss Zhu. We clash like mortal enemies—marrying her means daily hell! Please go break off this betrothal..."
[ZH] 陈江林先吩咐青瓜外面待着，待青瓜出去后，生气道：“胡闹！定好的亲，怎能随便退？退了对人家姑娘不好。再说你做下那等事，毁人姑娘名声，你不娶她，让谁去娶？要怪只能怪你太顽劣！”
[EN] Sending Qinggua outside first, Chen Jianglin exploded the second the door shut: "Nonsense! How do you break off a sealed betrothal—ruining the girl's name?! You pulled that dirty stunt wrecking her reputation—if you don't marry her, who will?! Blame your own reckless hide!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“朱家姑娘人美，又有才华，你能娶到她，几辈子修来的福气，好好珍惜，以后别再说退婚的事。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan added, "Miss Zhu is stunning and brilliant—marrying her is three lifetimes of luck! Treasure her and never mention breaking it off again."
[ZH] 爹娘都不同意，陈浩宇急了：“爹！娘！我不想娶我不喜欢的姑娘。”
[EN] With both parents shutting him down, panic spiked in Chen Haoyu: "Father! Mother! I don't want to marry a girl I don't love!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰笑道：“怎么？你有喜欢的人？要是有，带回来让我与你爹瞧瞧，若是好，就让她给你当妾室。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan chuckled: "Oh? Got a girl you love already? Bring her home for your father and me to inspect—if she's decent, take her as a concubine!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇摇摇头：“暂时还没有，我不喜欢朱淑真，只怕以后跟她的日子没法过下去。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu shook his head: "Not yet. I just can't stand Zhu Shuzhen—we'll never last under one roof!"
[ZH] 陈江林脸上掠过不满：“喜欢不喜欢不重要，我与你娘结婚前连面都没见过，婚后还不是相亲相爱？去吧，不要在此烦我们，这婚不可能退。”
[EN] Irritation crossed Chen Jianglin's brow: "Love doesn't matter! Your mother and I never even laid eyes on each other before our wedding, and look at us now! Go on—quit pestering us. This betrothal stays."
[ZH] 劝说父母根本不奏效，不如去找奶奶：“爹娘继续下棋，我就不在此打搅。”
[EN] Seeing zero crack in his parents, he pivoted to Grandmother: "Enjoy your chess, Father and Mother—won't bother you anymore."
[ZH] 姚轻琰觉得儿子今日有些奇怪，行动十分谨慎，不由问道：“浩宇，是不是哪里受伤？为何行动如此谨慎小心？”
[EN] Noticing his stiff, ginger posture as he rose, Yao Qingyan asked in concern: "Are you hurt somewhere, Haoyu? Why are you moving with such care?"
[ZH] 提到伤，陈浩宇灵机一动：“爹、娘，我是受伤，都是被朱淑真打的，她知道我散播流言一事，气不过，把我打得遍体鳞伤，我这几日没回家，躲在永宁居疗伤，怕你们伤心，一直瞒着。不信，可以问青瓜。”
[EN] A lightbulb flashed over Chen Haoyu's head! "I am hurt, Father and Mother—Zhu Shuzhen beat me! Found out I spread the rumors, lost her mind, and pounded me black and blue! Hid out at Yongning Residence healing so I wouldn't make you cry—ask Qinggua if you don't believe me!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰起身，走到儿子身边，将儿子身上的衣裳扒开，在背上瞧瞧，果然青一块紫一块：“我的娘！怎么被打成这样？真是朱家姑娘打的？她有这么大的劲儿？”
[EN] Jumping up, Yao Qingyan pulled his collar back to inspect his back—covered in fading purple and yellow bruises! "Heavens above! How did you get beaten like this?! Really by Miss Zhu?! How does a girl hit that hard?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇装作委屈样子：“娘，您不知道，她会功夫，拳头厉害着呢，以后我要是娶她，岂不是天天被她家暴？”
[EN] Putting on a pitiful whimper, Chen Haoyu lied: "You don't know, Mother—she knows martial arts and hits like a hammer! Marry her and I'll be getting battered by my wife every single day!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰不大相信：“我以前也见过朱家姑娘，柔柔弱弱，根本不像会功夫的人，再说也没听你姐姐提起过，你该不会在外面跟人打架受伤，为退婚，嫁祸到朱家姑娘身上？”
[EN] Skeptical, Yao Qingyan shook her head: "I've met Miss Zhu—slender and delicate, nothing like a fighter! Wenxin never mentioned martial arts either. Didn't you get in a street brawl and blame Miss Zhu just to wiggle out of marrying her?!"
[ZH] 陈江林应和道：“我看是这样，即使真是被朱家姑娘打的，打得极好，这个家没人管得了你，娶个厉害的媳妇回来，正好替我管教你！”
[EN] Chen Jianglin snorted: "Bet my fortune on it! And even if Miss Zhu did beat you—bravo to her! Nobody in this house can handle you; a tough wife is just what you need to whip you straight!"
[ZH] “爹！我是不是您的亲儿子？”这招不行，陈浩宇更急。
[EN] "Father—am I even your real son?!" With that bluff flopping too, panic rose higher.
[ZH] 姚轻琰向门外喊道：“青瓜！”
[EN] Yao Qingyan called toward the door: "Qinggua!"
[ZH] 青瓜听到声音进来，姚轻琰吩咐道：“带公子回屋，把府中上好的跌打损伤药取来，给公子重新涂抹上。”
[EN] Stepping in at her call, Qinggua received orders: "Walk Young Master back to his room and rub our manor's finest bruise salve over his injuries."
[ZH] “是。”青瓜上前去扶好自家公子。
[EN] "Right away!" Stepping up, Qinggua reached out to steady his master's arm.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一把甩开：“不用你扶，我能自己走，只怕娶个悍妇回来，以后想下地走路都难！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu shrugged him off: "Don't touch me—I can walk! Wait till I marry that shrew, though—I won't even be able to crawl out of bed!"
[ZH] 儿子出去后，姚轻琰还是担忧道：“老爷，不如把文心叫回来问问，如若朱家姑娘真是那样，浩宇以后岂不是没好日子过？”
[EN] Once their son stomped out, worry lingered on Yao Qingyan's face: "Husband, should we ask Wenxin back to check? What if Miss Zhu really is violent and Haoyu suffers?"
[ZH] 陈江林安慰道：“夫人不必担忧，据我观察，那小子没说实话，他身上的伤不知道在哪里被打的，故意赖到朱家姑娘身上，好劝我们给他退婚。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin patted her hand: "Relax, Wife. Look at that brat—lying through his teeth! Picked a fight somewhere else and pinned it on Miss Zhu to kill the betrothal."
[ZH] 姚轻琰本也如此猜测，对丈夫的话深信不疑：“我也怀疑，只是不知他在外得罪谁？被打成那样？我去儿子房中照顾照顾他，顺便问问到底是谁把他打成那样。”
[EN] Having suspected the same, Yao Qingyan nodded: "True... still, who on earth did he cross to get beaten that bad? I'll go look after him in his room and pry the truth out."
[ZH] “夫人去吧，我就不陪你，看见那小子就来气，趁你不在，去找江树商量一下绸缎庄的事。”陈江林将夫人送至门外，去了二弟院中。
[EN] "Go ahead—just looking at that rascal spikes my temper. While you're busy, I'll go talk silk-shop expansion with Jiangshu." Walking his wife to the door, Chen Jianglin headed for his second brother's yard.
[ZH] 当陈浩宇到达他的院子门口时，夏老太太正好拄拐杖赶来，见到孙子，满脸堆笑：“浩宇啊，奶奶听说你回来，立刻来瞧你，怎么七八日没见，你瘦了？”
[EN] Right as Chen Haoyu reached his own courtyard gate, Old Madam Xia arrived tapping her cane, beaming ear to ear: "Heard you came home, Haoyu, so Grandmother rushed straight over! Seven or eight days away and you've lost weight!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇上前扶好奶奶：“奶奶先进院子，到屋里坐。”
[EN] Stepping forward to steady her arm: "Come inside out of the cold, Grandmother."
[ZH] 扶着老太太进去院子，又走进屋里，用了些力道，手臂上的伤还未痊愈，竟然钻心地疼，陈浩宇不由吸一口气，发出声音：“嘶……”
[EN] Supporting her weight through the doorway pulled his sore shoulder, wringing a sharp hiss between his teeth: "Tss..."
[ZH] 老太太关心不已：“怎么了？哪里疼？快让奶奶瞧瞧。”
[EN] Alarm flashed in Old Madam Xia's eyes: "What's wrong? Where does it hurt? Show Grandmother!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇想利用奶奶取消婚约，装出一副可怜样：“奶奶，您是不是最疼我？”
[EN] Time to deploy Grandmother against the betrothal! Putting on his saddest puppy eyes: "Grandmother, do you love me most in the world?"
[ZH] “那还用说。”
[EN] "Goes without saying!"
[ZH] “孙儿被人打得遍体鳞伤，都过七日，还没好利索，您就知道有多严重。”
[EN] "I got beaten black and blue all over—seven whole days later and I'm still sore! That's how brutal it was!"
[ZH] “哪个杀千刀的打你？告诉奶奶，我让你爹把那人抓来，好好收拾收拾。”
[EN] "What heartless villain touched my grandson?! Tell Grandmother—I'll have your father drag him here and tear him apart!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇哪敢说仪王？继续哄骗：“不是别人，正是您未过门的孙媳妇，朱家姑娘。”
[EN] Never daring to breathe Prince Yi's name, Chen Haoyu spun his lie: "Nobody else—your future granddaughter-in-law, Miss Zhu!"
[ZH] 青瓜在一旁偷乐，为退婚，公子什么谎言都编得出。
[EN] Behind them, Qinggua muffled a snicker—Young Master would tell any whopper alive to dodge this wedding!
[ZH] “有这等事？你长得人高马大，怎么会被一个姑娘打？”夏老太太甚是疑惑。
[EN] Baffled, Old Madam Xia frowned: "How could a big strapping lad like you get beaten by a young lady?"
[ZH] “奶奶您不知，她会功夫，拳头很厉害，孙儿打不过。”
[EN] "You don't know, Grandmother—she knows kung fu! Hits like iron; I couldn't even fight back!"
[ZH] “她为何要打你？”老太太还是难以相信。
[EN] "Why on earth would she beat you?" Old Madam Xia still struggled to picture it.
[ZH] 青瓜与其他丫鬟在，陈浩宇不便提出散播流言一事：“奶奶，我们进屋说。”
[EN] Unwilling to mention the rumors in front of Qinggua and the maids: "Come inside and I'll tell you, Grandmother."
[ZH] 陈浩宇给青瓜使个眼色，青瓜等公子与夏老太太进屋后，将所有丫鬟堵在门外，顺便把门从外面关上。
[EN] Catching his master's wink, Qinggua ushered both inside, held every maid outside, and shut the door firm.
[ZH] 等奶奶在屋里坐好后，陈浩宇开始胡诌：“朱淑真知道我散播谣言一事，气得不行，几日前把我抓进一个小黑屋里打了一顿，不信，奶奶您瞧……”
[EN] Once Old Madam Xia sat, Chen Haoyu spun fast: "Zhu Shuzhen found out I spread rumors about her, went ballistic, dragged me into a dark shack a few days ago, and beat me half to death! Look for yourself, Grandmother..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇扒开衣裳，老太太看到孙子胸前青一块紫一块，无比心疼。
[EN] Parting his lapel, he revealed his purple-mottled chest, sending horror straight to Old Madam Xia's heart!
[ZH] “她个姑娘家怎么这么大劲儿？把我孙子打成这样，这还敢娶回来？不行，我得去找你爹娘说说。”
[EN] "Where does a girl get strength like that?! Beating my grandson like a criminal—how dare we bring her under this roof?! No way—I'm marching straight to talk to your parents!"
[ZH] 夏老太太刚要起身，姚轻琰推门进入，见婆婆在，上前去扶：“母亲怎么跑来？”
[EN] Just as Old Madam Xia rose, Yao Qingyan pushed the door open and hurried to steady her elbow: "What brings Mother over here?"
[ZH] “我来看我孙子，难道不行？”夏老太太又坐下，“你来得正好，回屋告诉江林，朱家姑娘娶不得，看把我孙子打成什么样？”
[EN] "Can't I visit my own grandson?!" Sitting back down, Old Madam Xia barked, "Good timing—go tell Jianglin Miss Zhu is out! Look what she did to my grandson!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰过去先将门关好，陈浩宇散播谣言一事没几人知道，朱家和陈家都不想闹得尽人皆知。
[EN] Yao Qingyan walked back to shut the door tight first—only a handful knew about Chen Haoyu spreading the rumors, and neither Zhus nor Chens wanted it spreading further.
[ZH] 关好门后，又走回老太太身边：“母亲，不能娶也得娶，谁让浩宇干下缺德事。”
[EN] Once the door stood closed, she returned to Old Madam Xia's side: "Like it or not, Mother, he has to marry her—Haoyu brought it on himself pulling that wicked stunt."
[ZH] “你瞧瞧她把我孙子打成什么样？娶回来，还不得把整个陈府闹得底儿朝天？”老太太依然坚持，浩宇是她的心头肉，她决不允许他被人欺负。
[EN] "Look at what she did to my boy! Bring her home and she'll flip Chen Manor upside down!" Old Madam Xia would not budge—Haoyu was the apple of her eye, untouchable!
[ZH] 儿子哄骗老太太，姚轻琰解释也没用，夏老太太什么都好，只是对孙子的话深信不疑。
[EN] Yao Qingyan knew explaining was useless; Old Madam Xia believed every fairy tale out of her favorite grandson's mouth.
[ZH] “母亲，文心对朱家姑娘赞叹有加，肯定不会像浩宇说的那样，您放心。”
[EN] "Wenxin praises Miss Zhu to the skies, Mother—she can't be half as wild as Haoyu claims!"
[ZH] “我不放心！我孙子都被打成这样，让我怎么放心？既然婚不能退，就耗着！什么时候朱家姑娘把她的性子改了，什么时候成亲！”
[EN] "Don't care! Look at my grandson's bruises—how could I rest easy?! If we can't break the betrothal, then drag it out! Until Miss Zhu tames that temper, no wedding!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇央求道：“奶奶，我还是想退婚，您就让爹娘到朱家给我退了这门婚事吧？好不好？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu pleaded, "I'd still rather break it off, Grandmother—tell Father and Mother to cancel it with the Zhus, please?!"
[ZH] 夏老太太耐心安慰：“奶奶也想给你退，可亲事刚定下，立刻退不合适，你娘说得也没错，谁让你做下错事，既做错就得承担责任。再说，她毕竟是知府大人的女儿，哪有那么容易说退就退，目前最好的办法就是耗着。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia patted his hand: "Grandmother wants to cancel it too, sweetie, yet canceling right after sealing it won't fly—your mother's right, you did mess up and owe amends. Plus she's the Prefect's daughter, not easy to dump overnight. Stalling is our best play right now."
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“母亲，恐怕耗着也不是办法，眼看要过春节，离定下的日子也就两个来月，怎么耗？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan fretted: "Stalling won't work either, Mother—New Year is right around the corner, barely two months until the wedding date! How do we stall?"
[ZH] “放心，到时我自有办法。”夏老太太看上去似乎已想到妙计。
[EN] "Leave that to me—I'll have a plan ready when the time comes." Old Madam Xia looked downright smug.
[ZH] “儿媳觉得耗着没用，恐惹知府大人不快，最后还得娶朱家姑娘，不如痛痛快快按照订好的日子娶回来，皆大欢喜”
[EN] "Dragging it out will only anger the Prefect, Mother—and we'll still end up marrying her anyway. Better wed on schedule and keep everyone smiling!"
[ZH] “你甭想那么多，浩宇怎么说也有两下子，竟然被一个姑娘家打，我已有个整日让着老婆的儿子，可不愿再有个把夫君拿捏在手里的孙媳妇！”
[EN] "Quit lecturing me! Haoyu's no weakling, yet a girl beat him raw! One henpecked son letting his wife walk over him is plenty—I refuse to watch a granddaughter-in-law wrap my grandson around her little finger too!"
[ZH] 老太太的话，将姚轻琰噎住，她不敢再说下去。她暂时还无法理解，为何老太太明明经常叮嘱几个儿子要对娘子好，却有事没事嫌弃江林老是让着她？婆婆的心思实在难以猜度。
[EN] That jab froze Yao Qingyan silent—how could her mother-in-law keep preaching to her sons to cherish their wives, yet keep sniping that Jianglin spoiled her too much? A mother-in-law's mind was an unsolvable riddle.
[ZH] “一切听从母亲安排。”
[EN] "Whatever Mother decides."
[ZH] 陈浩宇努力半晌，才换来耗着的办法，他并不知足，可又没有更好的办法，不由慨叹道：“我要是再有个漂亮的姐姐该多好！”
[EN] After all that acting, scoring only a stalling tactic felt thin to Chen Haoyu, though nothing better existed. Letting out a sigh, he muttered: "Wish I had one more gorgeous older sister!"
[ZH] 夏老太太不解：“什么意思？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia blinked: "What for?"
[ZH] “再有一个，可以让官家纳在身边，陈家也会成皇亲国戚，就不用怕知府大人，想退婚就退婚，想不娶谁就不娶谁，多牛！”
[EN] "Then His Majesty could take her into the palace, making the Chens imperial kin! Wouldn't have to fear any Prefect—break any engagement we want, marry whoever we please! How awesome would that be?!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰在陈浩宇头上点一下：“胡说什么！你以为皇亲国戚有多好？朱家虽不敢惹乔家，这梁子是结下了。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan tapped his forehead: "Quit talking crazy! Think being imperial relatives is paradise? The Zhus may not dare touch the Qiaos, yet bad blood is already brewed between them."
[ZH] 姚轻琰拉起儿子：“快去床上躺着，我刚让青瓜去拿药，家里有上好的药不知道回来用，净在外面瞎找大夫。”
[EN] Tugging her son toward the inner bed: "Go lie down—sent Qinggua for our manor's best salve. Top-notch medicine sitting right at home and you go hiring street doctors!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰边往里屋走，边向门外喊：“青瓜！还不赶快进来，把药给公子涂上！”
[EN] Walking inside, she called toward the door: "Qinggua! Get in here and put salve on Young Master!"
[ZH] 青瓜应声推门而入，拜见过老夫人，进里屋忙活。
[EN] Pushing open the door, Qinggua bowed to Old Madam Xia and hurried in to tend his master.
[ZH] 丫鬟进来，搀扶夏老太太回屋，看过孙子，她心满意足，该回去歇歇。
[EN] A maid stepped in to escort Old Madam Xia back; satisfied from seeing her boy, she headed off to rest.

#CH 147
[ZH] 青瓜为公子上药，姚轻琰在一旁将儿子身上的伤尽数看遍，的确严重。打得这么重，竟然小命还在，实在难得。
[EN] As Qinggua rubbed ointment across her son's torso, Yao Qingyan saw the full extent of the bruising—fierce enough it was a miracle nothing had broken!
[ZH] “青瓜，老实告诉我，公子身上的伤是谁打的？”
[EN] "Tell me the truth, Qinggua—who gave Young Master these bruises?"
[ZH] 青瓜边上药边支支吾吾不敢言语。
[EN] Applying salve, Qinggua stammered, unable to meet her eye.
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“我都说了，是朱淑真，娘为何不信？还要问青瓜？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu jumped in: "Already told you it was Zhu Shuzhen, Mother—why interrogate Qinggua?!"
[ZH] “我才不信是朱家姑娘打的，肯定是你在外得罪什么人，老实告诉娘是谁打的？我让你爹出面处理此事。”
[EN] "Don't buy Zhu Shuzhen for one second! Who did you cross outside? Tell Mother straight and I'll have your father handle it!"
[ZH] “娘爱信不信，反正就是朱淑真打的。”
[EN] "Believe it or don't—it was Zhu Shuzhen."
[ZH] 姚轻琰从儿子口中问不出所以然，便不再继续追问，目睹青瓜一点点涂完药，为儿子穿好衣裳后，把青瓜叫到一边。
[EN] Getting nowhere with her stubborn son, Yao Qingyan waited until Qinggua finished dressing him, then pulled the boy aside.
[ZH] “青瓜，你今儿不给我说出公子身上的伤是谁打的，我就把你送回老家。”
[EN] "Tell me who beat Young Master right now, Qinggua, or I'll pack you back to your home village!"
[ZH] 青瓜立刻跪下：“夫人，千万不要送我回老家，……其实我也不知道公子是被谁打的，我在城外找到他时，公子已被人打得遍体鳞伤。”
[EN] Qinggua hit his knees at once: "Please don't send me home, Madam! Honest, I don't know who beat him either—by the time I tracked Young Master down outside the city, he was already beaten black and blue!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇从床上下来：“娘，您别吓唬青瓜，他真的不知道。”
[EN] Hopping off bed, Chen Haoyu protested, "Quit scaring Qinggua, Mother—he really doesn't know."
[ZH] 姚轻琰只好说：“起来吧，以后跟着公子，小心伺候，别让他再被人伤到。”
[EN] Relenting, Yao Qingyan told him, "Get up. Watch closer over Young Master from now on and don't let him get hurt again."
[ZH] 青瓜谢过夫人后起身，一高兴说道：“夫人有所不知，抓公子的人很厉害，我还没反应过来，公子就被他抓上马车……”
[EN] Scrambling up relieved, Qinggua's loose tongue slipped: "You didn't see it, Madam—the guy who snatched Young Master moved like lightning! Before I could blink, he yanked Young Master straight into a carriage..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇踢青瓜一脚：“滚！滚出去！尽瞎说！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu kicked Qinggua's shin: "Get out! Quit making up fairy tales!"
[ZH] 青瓜意识到失言，边往出走边改口：“夫人，我是说着玩的。”
[EN] Catching his slip, Qinggua scrambled for the door calling back: "Just joking, Madam!"
[ZH] 因踢青瓜，又一阵疼痛，陈浩宇忍着，尽量不被母亲看出：“娘，您就别瞎问，儿子还能骗您？爹不是说要带您去取衣裳还有胭脂水粉吗？您赶紧去吧，省得爹等得着急。”
[EN] Swinging his leg woke a sharp twinge in his ribs, which Chen Haoyu masked fast: "Stop playing detective, Mother—would I lie to you? Didn't Father promise to take you shopping for robes and rouge? Go already before Father gets impatient!"
[ZH] 儿子显然是赶人，姚轻琰不好再待下去：“近期不准再往外跑，好好在家休养，听到没？”
[EN] Taking the clear hint he was shooing her out, Yao Qingyan warned: "Stay indoors healing—no sneaking out, hear me?"
[ZH] “听到了，听到了，赶紧去吧。”陈浩宇将姚轻琰往外推。
[EN] "Heard you, heard you—have fun!" Chen Haoyu nudged her right out the door.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 陈家的好药的确管用，不出三日，陈浩宇身上的伤已痊愈，精神头一起来，在陈府待不下去，打算出去玩儿。
[EN] Chen Manor's top-grade salve worked miracles: within three days Chen Haoyu's bruises cleared, his vigor snapped back, and boredom drove him right back out the gate.
[ZH] 为报复朱淑真，发誓吃素半年，半年未到，报复已结束，自己的幸福还可能被搭进去，实在没必要坚持下去。
[EN] Swearing off women for six months had been his revenge vow against Zhu Shuzhen; revenge was over and his own bachelorhood was on the chopping block—zero point keeping celibate now!
[ZH] 上次打算开荤，还没踏入春香楼，被仪王的侍卫给绑了，真是晦气！不如今日出去开荤，顺便驱赶驱赶晦气。
[EN] Last time he aimed for Chunxiang Brothel, Prince Yi's guard had bagged him right outside the door—rotten luck! Today he'd go celebrate and wash the bad luck off.
[ZH] 陈浩宇决定开荤前，先去趟望月轩，与朱淑真通通气，商量接下来该怎么对付两家人。
[EN] First, though, better swing by Wangyue Pavilion to sync plans with Zhu Shuzhen against both their families.
[ZH] 他带青瓜刚走出陈府大门，瞧见常跟着他的小弟在外面晃荡，显然在等他。
[EN] Stepping out Chen Manor's gate with Qinggua, he spotted his usual pack of cronies loitering outside waiting for him.
[ZH] “青瓜，你回去吧，不必跟着我。”
[EN] "Head back indoors, Qinggua—don't tag along."
[ZH] 青瓜失望道：“公子，我想去春香楼。”
[EN] Qinggua pouted: "Young Master, I want to see Chunxiang Brothel!"
[ZH] “本公子仔细一想，你实在不适合，还是老老实实待在陈府，等我回来再跟着我。”
[EN] "Thought it over—not your scene. Stay home at Chen Manor and wait on me when I get back."
[ZH] “公子为什么说我不适合？我可是想去得很。”
[EN] "Why isn't it my scene? I'm dying to go!"
[ZH] “问那么多干吗？本公子就不想带你，你要想去自己去，不要跟着我！”
[EN] "Quit arguing—said I'm not taking you! Wanna go, go on your own dime!"
[ZH] “去春香楼那种地方得花不少银子，我可舍不得，要不公子把银子给我，我自己去。”
[EN] "Chunxiang Brothel costs a fortune—I can't afford that! Give me the silver and I'll go solo!"
[ZH] 还没等公子答应，小弟们瞧见陈浩宇，都靠拢过来：“陈公子，可见着您，我们想您想得天天晚上睡不着觉。”
[EN] Before Chen Haoyu could answer, his cronies swarmed over grinning: "Young Master Chen! Missed you so much we couldn't sleep at night!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇已顾不上青瓜：“你们几个不就是想跟着本公子到处干坏事，还不用自己花银子，放心，本公子好久没干坏事，手痒痒，这就带你们去。”
[EN] Forgetting Qinggua completely, Chen Haoyu grinned: "Just itching to raise hell on my tab, aren't you?! Good news—my hands are itching too! Come on!"
[ZH] 青瓜眼睁睁看着公子坐上马车离开，瞧着那几个他没见过的小弟，不满道：“一看都是些混吃混喝的地痞流氓，我跟他们的确不是一类人，难怪公子不喜欢带我，还是老实待在陈府，别想那些乱七八糟的事。”
[EN] Watching his master climb into a carriage surrounded by those loud ruffians, Qinggua curled his lip: "Bunch of freeloading street thugs—glad I'm nothing like them! Better off staying cozy inside Chen Manor anyway."
[ZH] 陈浩宇先来到望月轩，跟小弟们一下马车，在门口碰到化雨。
[EN] Stopping first at Wangyue Pavilion, Chen Haoyu hopped out with his pack and bumped straight into Huayu at the gate.
[ZH] “喂！你是不是东方尘泽身边的跟班？叫什么来着？”
[EN] "Hey! Aren't you Dongfang Chenze's little sidekick—what's your name?"
[ZH] 化雨上前见礼：“陈公子好，我是跟着我家公子，名叫化雨。”
[EN] Huayu bowed: "Greetings, Young Master Chen. I serve my master—name's Huayu."
[ZH] “你为何在望月轩门口？”
[EN] "What's got you hanging outside Wangyue Pavilion?"
[ZH] “来替我家公子讨要幽栖居士的诗词。”
[EN] "Collecting new poems from Youqi Jushi for my master."
[ZH] “东方尘泽脑子是不是有问题？整日就喜欢朱淑真写的那些没用的玩意儿。”
[EN] "Is Dongfang Chenze loose in the head? Obsessing all day over Zhu Shuzhen's useless scribbles!"
[ZH] 正说着，苦荞拿着手稿出来：“化雨，一共两首，都是我家小姐现写的。”
[EN] Right then Kuqiao walked out holding folded sheets: "Here you go, Huayu—two brand-new pieces Miss just wrote."
[ZH] 化雨上前接过手稿，折好揣进怀里：“感谢苦荞姐姐。”
[EN] Taking and tucking them safely inside his robe: "Thanks, Sister Kuqiao!"
[ZH] “我家小姐麻烦你给东方公子捎句话，如果哪里举行诗会，记得来邀请她，小姐整日待在望月轩，闷得慌。”
[EN] "Miss also asked you to pass word to Young Master Dongfang—if any poetry salon pops up, invite her! Cooped up in Wangyue Pavilion all day is driving her nuts."
[ZH] “苦荞姐姐放心，我定将幽栖居士的话转达给我家公子。”
[EN] "Rest assured, Sister Kuqiao—I'll tell my master word for word!"
[ZH] 得到幽栖居士的佳作，化雨很满意，笑着离开。
[EN] Pleased with his prize, Huayu smiled and hurried off.
[ZH] 苦荞早已瞧见陈浩宇，懒得理他，准备回去，被陈浩宇叫住。
[EN] Spotting Chen Haoyu and wanting zero part of him, Kuqiao turned back inside—only for Chen Haoyu to yell after her:
[ZH] “喂！你这丫头！看见本公子也不理会，如此没礼貌！赶紧的，给我把朱淑真叫出来，本公子有话跟她说。”
[EN] "Hey, little maid! Walking right past me without a greeting—no manners! Move it and summon Zhu Shuzhen; I need to talk to her!"
[ZH] 苦荞很不情愿地答道：“陈公子稍候。”
[EN] Scowling reluctantly, Kuqiao replied: "Wait here, Young Master Chen."
[ZH] 等待朱淑真的过程中，一个小弟问：“陈公子，您今日来找朱淑真，该不会是找她麻烦？”
[EN] While waiting, one crony asked, "Here to settle scores with Zhu Shuzhen today, Young Master Chen?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“你们几个以后得经常跟我见面，否则都不了解本公子的情况。告诉你们，本公子与朱淑真已订亲……”
[EN] Chen Haoyu smirked: "You boys need to keep up—haven't heard the news? Zhu Shuzhen and I just got engaged..."
[ZH] “什么什么？竟然有这等事？”几位小弟感到又意外又不解。
[EN] "WHAT?! No way!" Every crony's jaw dropped.
[ZH] 看着他们惊讶的样子，陈浩宇有些得意：“想不到吧？本公子怎么可能愿意？朱淑真也不乐意，所以本公子现在与她也算是一根绳上的蚂蚱，今日找她来商议商议怎么对付两家长辈。”
[EN] Enjoying their shock, Chen Haoyu chuckled: "Wild, right? Like hell I want her—and she hates it too! So we're two grasshoppers tied to one string now; came over to cook up a plan against our parents."
[ZH] 一位小弟说：“婚事定得蹊跷，陈公子能不能给我们几个详细说说？”
[EN] One crony probed: "Sounds fishy—how on earth did that betrothal happen, Young Master Chen?"
[ZH] “去去去！你们几个没资格知道，滚远些！别影响我跟朱淑真商量正事！”
[EN] "Buzz off! Above your pay grade—stand back so you don't ruin our strategy talk!"
[ZH] 几个小弟都听话地躲到远处，谁让陈浩宇是他们的大财神，得罪不起。
[EN] Knowing better than to cross their meal ticket, the cronies scattered down the lane.
[ZH] 千里寻很快出来，瞧见陈浩宇，大声问道：“陈浩宇，事情办得如何？”
[EN] Qianli Xun marched out soon after, demanding loud: "Well, Chen Haoyu—how'd it go?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇一副得意的样子：“本公子出马，怎么说也八九不离十。”
[EN] Puffing out his chest: "When I step up, consider it almost done!"
[ZH] 千里寻兴致倍增：“你家长辈同意退婚？”
[EN] Hope flared in Qianli Xun: "Your elders agreed to break off the betrothal?!"
[ZH] “退婚倒没同意……”
[EN] "Well, not break it off..."
[ZH] “没同意！你那么得意干什么？等同意了再来找老娘。”千里寻说完准备转身离开。
[EN] "Not break it off—and you're grinning like that?! Come back when they agree!" Spinning on her heel, she started back inside.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一把将她抓住：“着急干吗？我的话还没说完。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu caught her sleeve: "Hold your horses—haven't finished talking!"
[ZH] 千里寻甩开陈浩宇：“别抓着老娘，有什么话赶紧说，我的时间很宝贵。”
[EN] Shaking his hand off: "Hands off! Talk fast—my time is precious!"
[ZH] “我虽没说动家人同意退婚，可也有进展，我奶奶说既然婚退不了就耗着……”
[EN] "Couldn't get them to cancel it outright, yet I scored real progress: Grandmother ordered them to stall it..."
[ZH] “耗着？什么意思？”千里寻十分不解。
[EN] "Stall it? What's that mean?" Qianli Xun frowned.
[ZH] “意思就是到成亲的日子，我奶奶应该会想办法拖着，让婚事不能如期举行。”
[EN] "Means when our wedding date rolls up, Grandmother's going to drag her feet and block the ceremony."
[ZH] “拖着有什么用？最终还不得嫁到陈家？”
