[EN] "What good does stalling do if I still end up married to you eventually?!"
[ZH] “怎么没用？陈家拖着，你这边再搞些事情出来，你爹没办法，说不定拖着拖着这婚自然就退了。”
[EN] "Plenty of good! My family stalls from our side while you raise hell from yours—push your dad long enough and the whole betrothal collapses on its own!"
[ZH] “我搞些事情？怎么搞？”
[EN] "Me raise hell? How?"
[ZH] “我给你出个主意，你可不能打我，你要是打我，我就不说，转头就走，让你以后嫁给我天天受苦。”
[EN] "Got a plan—just swear you won't hit me! Hit me and I walk right now, leaving you stuck married to me in misery!"
[ZH] 千里寻举起拳头：“说不说？不说我可真打了。”
[EN] Qianli Xun cocked a fist: "Spill it or eat knuckles right now!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇抬手将千里寻紧握的拳头压下去：“别动不动举拳头，哪像个大家闺秀。”
[EN] Reaching over, Chen Haoyu gently pushed her raised fist down: "Stop waving fists around—nothing like a noble lady!"
[ZH] “挪开你的臭手！赶紧说！”
[EN] "Get your dirty paw off me and talk!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇乖乖将手挪开，千里寻也收了拳头。
[EN] Pulling his hand back fast as she lowered her fist, Chen Haoyu began:
[ZH] “前一段时间，我被人打，受了重伤……”
[EN] "A few days ago I got jumped and beaten half to death..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇话还未说完，千里寻笑道：“呵呵……，没想到临安城内还有人和老娘一样讨厌你，改日可得结识结识。”
[EN] Before he finished, Qianli Xun burst out laughing: "Haha! Glad to know someone else in Lin'an hates your guts as much as I do—gotta meet that hero someday!"
[ZH] “你能不能好好听人说话？不要随便打断我？”陈浩宇很不满，继续讲，“我被人打得受伤，就骗家人是你打的……”
[EN] "Will you let me finish without interrupting?!" Scowling, he pressed on: "While nursing my bruises, I told my family YOU beat me up..."
[ZH] 千里寻又举起拳头，对准陈浩宇：“你怎能随意污蔑老娘？”
[EN] Her fist shot straight back toward his nose: "How dare you frame me?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇向一旁躲开：“我要不这么说，怎能说动家人？我奶奶又怎能想到耗着的办法？”
[EN] Ducking back a step: "How else was I supposed to freak my family out?! That's why Grandmother decided to stall!"
[ZH] 千里寻又放下拳头：“看在你是为了退婚，老娘暂且饶了你，继续讲！”
[EN] Lowering her fist again: "Since it's for breaking off this wedding, I'll let it slide. Go on!"
[ZH] “我奶奶说，朱家姑娘什么时候把性子改了，什么时候成亲。你要做的就是别改你的性子，以后当着众人的面，尤其当着你爹的面见我就打，保准这婚结不了。”
[EN] "Grandmother swore no wedding until Miss Zhu fixes her violent temper! So all you have to do is stay wild—every time you see me in public, above all in front of your dad, beat me on sight! Betrothal dies guaranteed!"
[ZH] “放心，我天生的性子，改不了，在众人面前打你，求之不得。只是得制造些能当着我爹的面打你的机会。”
[EN] "Piece of cake! Born with this temper, never changing—and beating you in public is pure pleasure! Just need chances right in front of my dad."
[ZH] “不着急，以后我会帮你制造机会。”
[EN] "Leave setting up the stage to me!"
[ZH] “看在你如此配合的份儿上，老娘不与你计较污蔑我的事，不过你得告诉我是谁打的你，我想与他结识结识。”
[EN] "Since you're playing ball, I'll forgive the framing. Tell me who beat you up, though—I want to shake their hand!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不愿将仪王说出：“问那么多干什么？跟你又没关系，本公子还有正事，不跟你瞎耽误工夫。”
[EN] Refusing to breathe Prince Yi's name: "None of your business! Got real fun waiting—later!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不再继续耽搁下去，带着小弟们离开。
[EN] Waving off, Chen Haoyu rounded up his cronies and strolled off.
[ZH] “哼！你不告诉老娘，老娘迟早会知道。”
[EN] "Hmph—keep secrets all you want, I'll find out sooner or later!"
[ZH] 今日也算是收到好消息，陈家既然愿意拖着，千里寻自然配合。
[EN] Still, today counted as good news: if the Chens meant to stall, Qianli Xun would happily pile on fuel!
[ZH] 她已想好，以后不谈恋爱，不结婚，做喜欢做的事，再交几个知心好友，若有一日喜欢小孩子，便收养一个，等年迈走不动时，也可有人照顾床前。
[EN] Her mind was set for life now: no romance, no marriage, pour into her passions, cherish close friends, and maybe adopt a child one day if she wanted company in old age.
[ZH] 陈浩宇的堂哥陈浩东听说堂弟与朱家的亲事，既不解又担心，来到父亲陈江树的房中。
[EN] Meanwhile inside Chen Manor, hearing his cousin Haoyu had gotten betrothed to the Zhus stirred both puzzle and alarm in Chen Haodong, who went straight to his father Chen Jiangshu's room.
[ZH] 陈江树刚从商铺回来，换了衣裳，茶还没喝一口，见儿子进来，问道：“你今儿怎么有空来我这里？为何不在铺子待着好好学习做生意？”
[EN] Just home from inspecting shops and still waiting on his first sip of tea, Chen Jiangshu looked up: "Free today? Why aren't you at the shop learning the ropes?"
[ZH] “爹不用担心，儿子学着呢。陈家这么大的产业，儿子不敢不用心。儿子今儿来是听说大伯给浩宇定下朱家的亲事，不知父亲可知？”陈浩东在父亲面前表现出毕恭毕敬的样子。
[EN] "Studying hard, Father—I'd never slack on an empire this big. Came over because I heard Eldest Uncle betrothed Haoyu to the Zhu family—did Father know?" Standing stiff and humble, Chen Haodong played the model son.
[ZH] 陈江树喝口茶，放下茶盏：“这么大的事，我岂能不知？两家亲上加亲，你大伯看上去很高兴。”
[EN] Taking a sip and setting his cup down, Chen Jiangshu nodded: "How could I miss news that big? Doubling our bond with the Zhus—your Eldest Uncle looks thrilled."
[ZH] “我怎么听说那日知府大人绑了浩宇，手里还拿着鞭子冲进陈府，之后这婚事就定下，爹不觉得蹊跷？”
[EN] "Yet servants saw the Prefect haul Haoyu in bound in ropes with a whip right before the betrothal got sealed—doesn't that smell off, Father?"
[ZH] “是听下人说了，也觉纳闷，侧面向你大伯打听过，他含糊其辞，说不出所以然，也就没再多问。”
[EN] "Heard the rumors and wondered too. Probed your Eldest Uncle gently, got dodgy answers, so I dropped it."
[ZH] “爹，您说朱家姑娘被传成那样，谁家还愿意娶？为何大伯会同意给自家儿子娶那样的？会不会是浩宇做下什么错事，被朱家抓住把柄，大伯才不得不同意？”
[EN] "Think about it, Father: with Miss Zhu's reputation dragged through the mud, who else wanted her? Why did Eldest Uncle jump at her? Bet Haoyu screwed up bad, handed the Zhus dirt on him, and forced Eldest Uncle's hand!"
[ZH] “因为什么与我们无关，关键是，浩宇娶朱家姑娘对我们来讲利弊如何。”
[EN] "Why doesn't matter—all that matters is whether Haoyu marrying Miss Zhu helps or hurts US."
[ZH] “爹考虑的是，按说与朱家结亲对浩宇继承陈家产业有利，可朱家姑娘在外名声不好，浩宇娶她似乎又不利。儿子还听说，朱家姑娘拳头比男子厉害，还与浩宇结下梁子，这两人要是成亲，以后可有好戏看。”
[EN] "Dead right, Father! On paper, tying closer to the Prefect boosts Haoyu's claim to inherit the Chen empire—yet Miss Zhu's rotten name cuts the other way! Plus I heard Miss Zhu packs a meaner punch than most men and already has bad blood with Haoyu—watch sparks fly if they actually wed!"
[ZH] “没想到我儿考虑问题越来越全面。”陈江树赞赏完，问道，“你怎么知道朱家姑娘跟浩宇结下梁子？”
[EN] "Sharp analysis—my boy's seeing the whole board now!" Beaming, Chen Jiangshu asked, "How'd you learn Miss Zhu and Haoyu have bad blood?"
[ZH] 陈浩东笑道：“爹有所不知，浩宇身边那些跟他鬼混的人均是些见钱眼开之人，儿子稍微使些钱财，他们什么话都对我讲。”
[EN] Chen Haodong smirked: "Father wouldn't know—those freeloaders trailing Haoyu sell their mothers for a coin! Grease a palm and they sing like birds."
[ZH] “懂得使手段达到目的，不错，算我没白教你。我问你，你是想浩宇与朱家的亲事成还是不成？”
[EN] "Smart using tools to get intel—didn't waste my lessons on you! Tell me: do you want Haoyu's wedding to happen or blow up?"
[ZH] 陈浩东想想说：“既怕成又想成。”
[EN] Pausing a beat, Chen Haodong answered: "Both dread it and root for it."
[ZH] “怎么个讲法？”
[EN] "Explain."
[ZH] “怕成是因为担心两家结亲，浩宇继承陈家产业多了筹码。想成是因为浩宇平日太跋扈自由，儿子实在看不惯，凭什么都是陈家子弟，他整日浪荡在外自由自在？还可能成为陈家产业的继承人；若娶朱家姑娘，他的日子就不那么好过，不仅会被人嘲笑，还可能会被家暴，儿子想着都开心。”
[EN] "Dread it because marrying the Prefect's daughter might boost Haoyu's standing for the inheritance. Root for it because I'm sick of watching Haoyu live free and wild while still sitting first in line—marry Miss Zhu and he'll get mocked citywide AND battered by his own wife! Makes me grin just picturing it!"
[ZH] “这几日，为父也在想此事，想来想去觉得你大伯是被逼无奈才答应他的儿子娶朱家姑娘，至于什么原因，你悄悄打探。若真是被逼无奈，说明两家亲上加亲并不会增加浩宇继承陈家产业的筹码，说不定还会降低筹码。”
[EN] "Been chewing on this myself—bet your Eldest Uncle only agreed because his hand got forced! Dig quiet and confirm why. If his hand really got forced, this match won't add one ounce to Haoyu's standing—might even sink him!"
[ZH] 陈浩东答应道：“儿子定帮父亲打听清楚。”
[EN] Chen Haodong bowed: "I'll dig up the whole truth for Father."
[ZH] “不是帮我，是帮你，要让陈家所有长辈觉得只有你才是陈家产业最佳的后继之人。不妨再从浩宇身边那些混吃混喝的人下手。”
[EN] "Not for me—for YOU! Make every Chen elder see YOU are the one true heir to the Chen fortune. Start with Haoyu's freeloading pack."
[ZH] “是，父亲，我这就去打探。”
[EN] "On it right now, Father!"
[ZH] 陈浩东出了陈府，先去茶庄忙活一阵，忙活完后，又往春香楼而去。
[EN] Leaving Chen Manor, Chen Haodong stopped by the tea shop for a spell of work, then headed straight for Chunxiang Brothel.

#CH 148
[ZH] 陈浩东来到春香楼外，不一会儿，从里面出来一位商妓，身着冬装，依然掩饰不住浑身上下的媚态。
[EN] Waiting outside Chunxiang Brothel, Chen Haodong watched a courtesan emerge moments later, heavy winter silk failing to hide her sultry sway.
[ZH] 那商妓抬手向陈浩东打招呼，迎上去：“陈大公子，我在楼上瞧见您站在外面，急忙下来迎接，外面冷，要不跟我进去快活快活？”商妓说完去拉陈浩东的胳膊。
[EN] Waving as she sidled close, she cooed, "Spotted you from upstairs, Eldest Young Master Chen, and hurried right down! Freezing out here—come inside and let me warm you up?" Her fingers reached for his sleeve.
[ZH] 陈浩东向后退去：“大街上，金蝉姑娘还是不要离我太近，明日亥时直接去老地方。”
[EN] Stepping back sharp, Chen Haodong warned, "Not out on a public street, Miss Jinchan—meet me at our usual spot tomorrow at the hour of hai."
[ZH] 金蝉娇嗔着声音，不满道：“陈大公子真会装，这一点不如您那堂弟，他可是明目张胆花大价钱买了一位黄花大姑娘，这会儿正快活着呢。”
[EN] Pouting with a playful whine, Jinchan teased, "Always putting on airs, Eldest Young Master—nothing like your cousin! He just dropped a small fortune buying a fresh virgin's first night and is living it up upstairs right now!"
[ZH] “我怎能与他相比？需小心谨慎。你来得正好，帮我把他身边跟着的人叫出来。”
[EN] "I'm not him—I play careful. Good timing, though: go fetch one of the guys tagging along with him."
[ZH] “陈家二公子身边跟了好几位，大公子找哪个？”
[EN] "Second Young Master brought half a crowd—which one?"
[ZH] “脸上有刀疤的。”
[EN] "The one with the knife scar across his cheek."
[ZH] “哦，是疤子，等着，我去替您叫。”
[EN] "Oh, Scarface! Wait right here."
[ZH] “要保密，不许被陈浩宇知道。”
[EN] "Keep it quiet—don't let Chen Haoyu catch wind."
[ZH] “放心，他正快活，顾不上其他。”
[EN] "Relax, he's way too busy upstairs to notice a thing."
[ZH] 金蝉进入春香楼后没多久，陈浩宇身边的一位小弟出来，见到陈浩东，立刻上前拜见。
[EN] Moments after Jinchan ducked inside, one of Chen Haoyu's cronies stepped out and hurried up to bow to Chen Haodong.
[ZH] “大公子有何吩咐？”
[EN] "What can I do for Eldest Young Master?"
[ZH] 陈浩东拿出一袋银子：“给你的，把你知道的都告诉我。”
[EN] Tossing him a pouch of silver: "Yours. Spill everything you know."
[ZH] 疤子接过钱袋，笑嘻嘻地说：“我知道陈公子与朱家姑娘一个不想娶，一个不想嫁。”
[EN] Weighing the silver with a greasy grin, Scarface whispered: "First off, neither Young Master Chen nor Miss Zhu wants this wedding."
[ZH] “你可知为何两家会订亲？”
[EN] "Do you know why the two families got betrothed?"
[ZH] “本来陈公子不想告诉我们，结果到春香楼一高兴，我再哄哄他，便全说了。”
[EN] "Young Master Chen kept mum at first—then once wine flowed at Chunxiang Brothel and I flattered him right, he spilled the whole pot!"
[ZH] “因为什么？”
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] “陈公子散播谣言之事被知府大人知道，把他抓起来，逼他娶朱家姑娘。”
[EN] "Prefect Zhu found out Young Master Chen paid for those smears against Miss Zhu, nabbed him, and forced him to marry her!"
[ZH] “散播谣言？难道朱家姑娘的谣言是他散播的？你为何之前不告诉我？”
[EN] "Wait—he started the rumors about Miss Zhu?! Why didn't you tell me sooner?!"
[ZH] “是陈公子散播的，我还是帮凶，之所以没告诉大公子，是因为此事闹得太大，知府大人又在查，所以不敢告知任何人，现在也算有个了结，不用再藏着掖着。”
[EN] "His idea, and I helped run it! Kept my mouth shut while it was hot and the Prefect was hunting heads—now that it's settled, no harm talking."
[ZH] “原来如此。”打听到这么重大的消息，陈浩东十分满意，“以后记着多打听，银子少不了你的。”
[EN] "Aha!" Delighted with that goldmine, Chen Haodong patted his arm: "Keep your ears peeled—plenty more silver where that came from!"
[ZH] 疤子很乐意：“陈大公子放心，您那堂弟好哄着呢，只要把他哄得开心，拿我们当真兄弟，毫无保留。”
[EN] Scarface grinned: "Count on me, Eldest Young Master! Your cousin's easy to work—stroke his ego and he treats us like blood brothers, hiding nothing!"
[ZH] 陈浩东叮嘱疤子继续将陈浩宇哄好，打探到有用的消息，随时告诉他。
[EN] Charging Scarface to keep buttering Chen Haoyu up and bring any fresh intel straight to him, Chen Haodong dismissed the thug.
[ZH] 陈浩东回到陈府，又来到父亲居住的院子。
[EN] Returning to Chen Manor, Chen Haodong went straight to his father's courtyard.
[ZH] 陈江树正打算去找大哥商议开拓绸缎庄生意之事，被陈浩东挡在门口。
[EN] Just stepping out to visit his eldest brother about expanding their silk trade, Chen Jiangshu paused as his son blocked the doorway.
[ZH] “爹，儿子打听到重大消息。”
[EN] "Huge news, Father!"
[ZH] 陈江树转身走进里屋，陈浩东紧随其后。
[EN] Chen Jiangshu turned back into the inner room, Chen Haodong stepping close on his heels.
[ZH] “什么重大消息？”
[EN] "What huge news?"
[ZH] “父亲可知朱家姑娘的流言是谁人散播？”
[EN] "Guess who started all those rumors about Miss Zhu?"
[ZH] “谁？”
[EN] "Who?"
[ZH] “浩宇。”
[EN] "Haoyu!"
[ZH] 陈江树恍然大悟：“难怪那日知府大人将浩宇五花大绑，估计是知道此事。哼！终于明白大哥为何会同意与朱家订亲，他是被逼无奈，自家儿子惹下大麻烦，总得想办法解决。”
[EN] Everything clicked in Chen Jiangshu's head: "No wonder the Prefect hauled Haoyu in bound in ropes! Hmph—no mystery anymore why Eldest Brother agreed to this match! Pure damage control for his idiot son's mess!"
[ZH] 陈浩东笑道：“呵呵……，看来这门亲事不会成为浩宇继承产业的筹码，反而是他想甩掉的累赘。”
[EN] Chen Haodong chuckled: "Haha, looks like this wedding won't give Haoyu a single bargaining chip for the inheritance—just a millstone he's dying to shake off!"
[ZH] “呵呵……”陈江树也笑道，“我儿不用再理会浩宇的亲事，把心用在生意上，在陈家长辈面前留下好印象，未来陈家的产业自然不会旁落他人。”
[EN] Chen Jiangshu laughed right along: "Forget Haoyu's wedding then! Pour your heart into the business, win over the clan elders, and the Chen fortune lands straight in your lap!"
[ZH] 陈浩东依然充满忧虑：“我听说奶奶对这门亲事也满意，她可是陈家长辈之一，对产业的继承人有否决权，她疼爱浩宇，肯定愿意浩宇成为继承人，难道她就不怕婚事影响浩宇的前程？”
[EN] Still, a shadow lingered in Chen Haodong's eyes: "Heard Grandmother approves of this match too—and she holds veto power over the heir! Spoiling Haoyu the way she does, wouldn't she worry this wedding hurts his shot?"
[ZH] 陈江树帮儿子仔细分析：“你奶奶喜欢浩宇不假，可面对陈家产业她一点儿都不含糊，我猜她同意浩宇娶朱家姑娘，肯定是知道传播谣言之人是他的心肝宝贝，不敢得罪朱家，只好答应。她能答应，也说明浩宇在她心中并非继承产业的最佳人选。”
[EN] Breaking it down cool for his son, Chen Jiangshu said, "Your grandmother dotes on Haoyu, sure, yet when it comes to the Chen empire she's sharp as a razor. Bet she only signed off because she knew her darling boy started the rumors and dared not cross the Prefect—and signing off proves even she knows Haoyu isn't cut out to lead the clan!"
[ZH] “爹说得有道理，儿子以后定会更加努力。”父亲的话令陈浩东高兴了一会儿，很快又忧虑道，“可儿子听说浩宇跟朱家姑娘联合起来，想说服长辈退婚，只怕会闹出大事，将婚事搅黄。”
[EN] "Makes total sense, Father—I'll work twice as hard!" Cheered for a beat, another snag crossed his mind: "Wait—heard Haoyu and Miss Zhu teamed up to push both families into breaking the betrothal! What if they raise a storm and blow it up?"
[ZH] 陈江树想想说：“没想到朱家姑娘想法独特，并不把姑娘家的名声当回事。也对，名声都坏成那样，再坏些，也没什么。按说两家大人定下亲事，不可能出现退婚之事。可也需以防万一，先按兵不动，暗地里悄悄关注此事，如若出现不利的迹象，再采取行动不迟。”
[EN] Chen Jiangshu mused: "Bold girl, that Miss Zhu—doesn't even care about a maiden's reputation! Fair enough, once mud sticks, what's a little more? A betrothal sealed by both houses doesn't get undone on a whim—still, stay alert. Sit tight and watch from the shadows; if trouble brews, we step in."
[ZH] “儿子记下。”
[EN] "Understood, Father."
[ZH] “去吧，我还要找你大伯商议正事，再侧面打听浩宇的亲事。”
[EN] "Run along—I'm off to talk silk expansion with Eldest Brother and sound out Haoyu's wedding on the side."
[ZH] 儿子走后，陈江树来到大哥居住的院子。
[EN] After his son left, Chen Jiangshu walked over to his eldest brother's courtyard.
[ZH] 陈江林正在陪姚轻琰说话，两人聊得甚是开心，姚轻琰不时露出笑容。一旁的丫鬟也被两人感染，充满歆羡之情。
[EN] Inside, Chen Jianglin sat chatting warm with Yao Qingyan, drawing bright laugh after bright laugh while attending maids looked on in quiet envy.
[ZH] 有丫鬟进来报告：“老爷，二爷来访。”
[EN] A maid stepped in to announce: "Master, Second Master is here to see you."
[ZH] 陈江林说：“我陪夫人说会儿话，都要被打搅，什么时候将这一身责任交出去，每天陪夫人到处逛逛，时时刻刻陪你说话，那该多好。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin groaned with a smile: "Can't even chat with my wife without interruptions! Wish I could hand this empire off tomorrow and spend every day strolling and talking with you!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰十分理解丈夫：“老爷是忙大事的人，一闲下来就陪我，我已心满意足。二叔找你肯定有正事，我先退下。”
[EN] Smiling warm, Yao Qingyan stood: "You've got big responsibilities—giving me every spare hour is already heaven. Second Brother must have business; I'll leave you two."
[ZH] 陈江林点点头：“还是夫人体贴，等我与江树谈完事，再陪夫人说话。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin nodded: "Always so thoughtful! Once Jiangshu and I wrap up, I'm right back to you."
[ZH] 姚轻琰起身，带着丫鬟从后门出去，她打算在府内到处走走，顺便去看望婆婆。
[EN] Slipping out the rear door with her maid, Yao Qingyan went for a walk around the manor and a visit with her mother-in-law.
[ZH] 陈江树进屋后，不用大哥招呼，自然坐在他身旁的椅子上。丫鬟很懂事，很快端上一盏茶。
[EN] Entering without standing on ceremony, Chen Jiangshu took the chair beside his brother as a sharp maid brought out a bowl of tea.
[ZH] 等丫鬟退去，陈江林问：“二弟找我可是为拓展绸缎庄生意之事？”
[EN] Once the maid withdrew, Chen Jianglin asked, "Here about expanding our silk shops, Second Brother?"
[ZH] “大哥真是料事如神，是为此事。”陈江树端起茶盏，品了一口。
[EN] "Reads minds as ever, Eldest Brother! Right on target." Taking a sip of tea, Chen Jiangshu smiled.
[ZH] “哪是我料事如神？近期陈家生意上拓展绸缎庄的生意头等是大事，又是你负责，你这人有个毛病，找我从不聊家常，只说正事。我当然猜到你是为此事而来，怎么？已经想好在哪里拓展？”
[EN] "No mind-reading needed—silk expansion is our top move right now, and you never drop by for small talk, only business! Picked your target markets yet?"
[ZH] 陈江树放下茶盏：“还是大哥了解我，呵呵，弟确实已想好，所以来找大哥商议。”
[EN] Setting down his tea bowl: "Nobody knows me like Eldest Brother! Yes, mapped it out and came to run it past you."
[ZH] “说来听听。”
[EN] "Let's hear it."
[ZH] “陈家生意已拓展到各地，但都在大宋的范围之内，我们可否大胆些，再往大宋的北边和东北方想想。”
[EN] "We've already covered every corner of the Great Song—what if we push bold, northward and northeast?"
[ZH] “你想将生意拓展到辽与金的领地？”
[EN] "Into Liao and Jin territory?"
[ZH] 陈江树点点头：“据弟了解，辽和金的百姓对大宋的丝绸很感兴趣，若是拓展到那些地方，生意必然好。”
[EN] Chen Jiangshu nodded: "Word is folk in both Liao and Jin go crazy for Song silk—open shops up there and profits will soar!"
[ZH] 陈江林沉默一会儿：“陈家生意之所以能做大，除资金雄厚，还在于一个‘稳’字，大宋北边局势不稳，只怕刚拓展过去，会血本无归。”
[EN] After a thoughtful pause, Chen Jianglin shook his head: "What built the Chen empire wasn't just deep pockets—it was one word: steady. The northern frontier is sitting on a powder keg; open up there and we risk losing our shirts."
[ZH] “大哥为何会如此考虑？”陈江树很不理解，觉得大哥是多虑。
[EN] Baffled, thinking his brother overcautious, Chen Jiangshu asked, "Why so worried, Eldest Brother?"
[ZH] “大宋与金结下海上之盟，目的是为共同对付辽，辽必然成为金囊中之物，故而北边与西北方局势不稳，陈家的生意不适合往那里拓展，你可以考虑一下西夏和吐蕃。”
[EN] "The Great Song just struck the Alliance Conducted at Sea with the Jin to smash the Liao. Once the Jin swallow the Liao, the whole north and northwest will burn with war—no place for Chen shops! Look toward Western Xia and Tubo instead."
[ZH] 想法被大哥否定，陈江树不满。大哥是主事人，他不好说什么，只得点头同意：“大哥考虑得极是，就按大哥说的办。”
[EN] Stung at getting shot down yet unable to defy the family head, Chen Jiangshu forced a nod: "Wise as ever, Eldest Brother—we'll go with Western Xia and Tubo."
[ZH] 正事已说完，不见二弟离开，陈江林无法与夫人聊天儿，催道：“二弟可还有事？”
[EN] Business done yet seeing his brother lingering—keeping him from his wife—Chen Jianglin nudged, "Anything else, Second Brother?"
[ZH] 陈江树大胆问出订婚一事：“大哥为浩宇定下朱家姑娘，弟甚是不解，朱家姑娘在外名声不好，浩宇若娶她，岂不被人笑话？”
[EN] Venturing onto thin ice, Chen Jiangshu asked, "Betrothing Haoyu to Miss Zhu puzzles me, Eldest Brother. With her name dragged through the mud, won't Haoyu marrying her make us a laughingstock?"
[ZH] “二弟过虑，朱家姑娘不仅相貌好，又有才学，浩宇能娶到她也算幸运。至于外面议论朱家姑娘的谣言，我从来不信。文心对她的小姑子常常赞不绝口，我不信别人，还能不信她？若真成亲后，被人笑话，也无妨，自己的日子自己过，与他人无关。”
[EN] "Don't sweat it, Second Brother. Miss Zhu is gorgeous and brilliant—Haoyu's lucky to get her! As for street rumors, I don't buy a syllable. Wenxin raves about her sister-in-law nonstop; if I can't trust my own daughter, who can I trust? Even if outsiders laugh after the wedding, who cares—we live our own lives, not theirs."
[ZH] 大哥一脸真诚，陈江树无话可劝，本也不想劝。心想：“浩宇犯下大错，想挽回都难，大哥如此说，不过是怕得罪知府大人。哼！我还是耐心等着看浩宇的笑话吧。”
[EN] Facing his brother's genuine calm, Chen Jiangshu dropped it—he was not trying to stop it anyway! "Haoyu dug his own grave and Eldest Brother's just covering for him before the Prefect—sit back and enjoy the circus!" he sneered inside.
[ZH] “大哥的眼界和心胸难有人及，弟受教。事已讲完，弟去忙拓展绸缎庄生意之事。”
[EN] "Nobody matches Eldest Brother's vision and generosity—lesson taken! Off to plan our silk expansion."
[ZH] “去吧，有什么事，随时与我沟通。”陈江林说完又想起什么，“慢着。”
[EN] "Go on—keep me posted." Pausing, Chen Jianglin added: "Wait a second."
[ZH] 陈江树刚站起来要走，又停下：“大哥有何吩咐？”
[EN] Halting mid-rise: "Yes, Eldest Brother?"
[ZH] “近期浩东表现不错，好几位长辈在我面前提起过，没事让他过来陪我说说话。”
[EN] "Haodong's been shining lately—several clan elders praised him to me. Tell him to drop by and chat with me whenever he's free."
[ZH] 大哥如此说，明显是要培养浩东，陈江树更是喜出望外：“浩东是陈家子弟，又在年轻一辈中年长些，自然要给弟弟们带个好头。弟回去，定将大哥的话转达给他，让他没事来陪大哥聊聊家常，这孩子最擅长与人拉家常。”
[EN] Clear signal Eldest Brother meant to groom Haodong! Elation surged in Chen Jiangshu: "Haodong's the eldest of his generation, duty-bound to set an example for his younger cousins! I'll pass word right away—that boy loves talking shop and family with you."
[ZH] “二弟有个好儿子令大哥羡慕，浩宇与浩东比起来差远了，都是二弟教育得好，教育孩子这一点，你比大哥强。”
[EN] "Envy you raising a son like that, Second Brother—Haoyu isn't half the man Haodong is! You beat me hands down at raising kids."
[ZH] “怎能怪大哥？老太太宠着，大哥也没办法。”
[EN] "Hardly your fault, Eldest Brother—Mother spoils him rotten; your hands are tied."
[ZH] “说得也是。”
[EN] "True enough."
[ZH] 陈江树不想再与大哥聊下去：“大哥，弟先退下。”
[EN] Eager to carry the triumph home, Chen Jiangshu bowed: "I'll take my leave, Eldest Brother."
[ZH] 陈江林点点头，看着二弟离去的身影，想着儿子的婚事，姚轻琰已将母亲的意思转达给他。耗着！亏母亲想得出。
[EN] Nodding him out, Chen Jianglin's mind drifted back to his own son's wedding—and Yao Qingyan telling him Mother wanted to stall it! What a stunt!
[ZH] 母亲年纪大，他不愿违背母亲的意思，可要耗着婚事，陈江林老大不愿意，又不好前去劝说老人家，只好装作默认。反正离成亲还有几个月，到时说不定老太太的想法会改变。
[EN] Unwilling to cross his aging mother straight-on yet hating the idea of stalling, he had stayed quiet for now—months remained before spring, plenty of time for Old Madam Xia to cool down.
[ZH] 近期申简身心难安，主要因朱家与陈家的婚事。
[EN] Meanwhile, Shen Jianchen had not known a moment's peace since learning of the Zhu-Chen betrothal.
[ZH] 忧虑难以向人启齿，憋在心里十分难受，难道眼睁睁看着千里寻嫁给一个浪荡公子？申简辰绝对不允许这种事情发生。
[EN] Unable to breathe a word to anyone, agony gnawed at him inside—how could he stand by watching Qianli Xun marry a worthless rake?! Over his dead body!
[ZH] 可他身为王爷，总不能无缘无故阻止别人家的亲事。关键是千里寻根本不愿接受他，若她愿意，他会利用王爷的身份逼迫陈家退婚，再说服乔梦容，让她答应纳淑真为妾。他相信梦容定会答应。
[EN] Yet even a royal prince could not smash another family's betrothal without cause—and Qianli Xun would not give him an inch! Had she said yes, he would have used royal power to kill the Chen match and talked Qiao Mengrong into accepting Shuzhen—sure Mengrong would agree.
[ZH] 千里寻不愿意，搞得申简辰无计可施。他又不愿什么都不做就这么等下去，他决定举行一场诗会。
[EN] With Qianli Xun shutting him out cold, he stood cornered—yet refused to sit idle! An idea hit: host a poetry salon!
[ZH] 他被自己的想法逗乐，不得已才参加诗会的他，竟然会想到用这种方式见日思夜想之人。他决定要好好利用这次诗会，把该请的人都请到。
[EN] He laughed at himself—the guy who dreaded poetry salons now throwing one just to see the woman occupying every waking thought! And he would invite every player on the board!
[ZH] 申简辰来到乔梦容居住的院子，身边并未带白风。今日白风又跑去望月轩与苦荞约会，申简辰羡慕不已。为何他身边的侍卫想去望月轩就去？而自己堂堂王爷，却不能随心所欲。
[EN] Strolling over to Qiao Mengrong's courtyard alone—Bai Feng had run off to Wangyue Pavilion again to see Kuqiao, leaving Shen Jianchen green with envy that his own bodyguard strolled to Wangyue Pavilion whenever he pleased while a royal prince stood banned!
[ZH] 哥哥前来，乔梦容很开心，哥哥现在每日都来看她，也不常走出幽林别居，能看得出，他身边并无别的女子。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong beamed seeing him walk in. Lately he visited every day and rarely left Youlin Retreat, proving no other woman waited outside.
[ZH] 哥哥现在进入她的闺房，也从不让人禀报，乔梦容觉得失忆的哥哥终于把她当成更亲的人看待。
[EN] Even better, he no longer waited to be announced before entering her bedchamber—proof his amnesiac heart was warming closer to her!
[ZH] 乔梦容正在写诗，她停下笔：“哥哥，你今日想吃什么？我让厨房安排。”
[EN] Setting down her brush mid-poem, she smiled: "What does Brother feel like eating today? I'll tell the kitchen."
[ZH] 申简辰并未回答，而是问道：“你在写什么？”
[EN] Skipping the question, he asked, "What are you writing?"
[ZH] “随便写两句诗。”乔梦容担心哥哥要看。
[EN] "Just scribbling two lines of verse." Shy, she hoped he would not ask to read it.
[ZH] 申简辰并不感兴趣：“你好好写，等举行诗会时，让大家也品品你的诗。”
[EN] Uninterested, Shen Jianchen said, "Keep at it—let everyone admire your poem at the poetry salon!"
[ZH] “难道哥哥要举行诗会？”
[EN] "Brother is hosting a poetry salon?!"
[ZH] “我打算后日在幽林别具举办一场诗会，把想请的人都请来。”
[EN] "Day after tomorrow right here at Youlin Retreat—inviting the whole crowd."
[ZH] “也请淑真吗？”
[EN] "Inviting Shuzhen too?"
[ZH] “当然要请幽栖居士，她可是江南女词人，我的诗会怎能少得了她？”
[EN] "Of course Youqi Jushi! She's Jiangnan's foremost female poet—can't hold a salon without her!"
[ZH] “太好了，我又能与淑真见面。不过我写的诗只能自己读读，不适合拿到诗会中显摆，尤其是在淑真面前。”
[EN] "Wonderful! I get to see Shuzhen again! Though my little verses are just for myself—no way I'm showing them off in front of Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 申简辰犹豫一会儿：“我担心幽栖居士不愿来，要不你出面帮哥哥请她？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen hesitated a beat: "Afraid Youqi Jushi might turn me down—could you invite her for me?"
[ZH] 乔梦容自信道：“没问题，我不用出面，只需差喜兰去，她定会答应。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong smiled with confidence: "Easy! Don't even have to go myself—just send Xilan and she'll say yes on the spot!"
[ZH] “好，幽栖居士交给你，其他人哥哥来安排。”
[EN] "Deal—Youqi Jushi's on you, I'll handle everyone else."
[ZH] “太好了，幽林别居好久没热闹过，我要吩咐下人好好安排，保准让哥哥满意。”
[EN] "Yay! Youlin Retreat hasn't buzzed with life in ages—I'll make the servants prepare everything spotless so Brother is proud!"
[ZH] 梦容的善良与毫不犹豫的支持，令申简辰内心愧疚，他决定以后加倍对梦容好，当然也只是哥哥对妹妹的好而已，即使以后不得已娶她，婚后他也只会把她当妹妹看待。
[EN] Watching Mengrong's sweet, instant devotion stabbed Shen Jianchen with fresh guilt. He vowed to spoil her twice as much for life—even if forced to wed her, he would cherish her like a treasured little sister forever.

#CH 149
[ZH] 千里寻接到喜兰送来的请帖，挺开心，也挺疑惑。
[EN] Receiving the invitation card Xilan delivered left Qianli Xun equal parts pleased and puzzled.
[ZH] 开心的是终于有理由走出望月轩与众人接触，呼吸呼吸新鲜空气。
[EN] Pleased to get an excuse to step out of Wangyue Pavilion, see friendly faces, and breathe fresh air.
[ZH] 疑惑的是仪王怎么会想着举行诗会？他看上去对诗没有多少兴致。
[EN] Puzzled why on earth Prince Yi was throwing a poetry salon when he showed so little interest in verse!
[ZH] 千里寻吩咐苦荞将喜兰请进屋，待喜兰站定后，问道：“仪王还请了哪些人参加诗会？”
[EN] Having Kuqiao usher Xilan inside, Qianli Xun asked once the maid stood still, "Who else did Prince Yi invite?"
[ZH] 喜兰回道：“还有乔公子、苗姑娘、东方公子、曹公子、安公子，以及陈公子。”
[EN] Xilan listed them: "Young Master Qiao, Lady Miao, Young Master Dongfang, Young Master Cao, Young Master An, and Young Master Chen."
[ZH] “为何请陈浩宇？他根本不懂诗词。”千里寻猜不透仪王是怎么想的。
[EN] "Why invite Chen Haoyu?! Guy doesn't know one poem from a radish!" She could not fathom Prince Yi's angle.
[ZH] 喜兰回道：“王爷的心思，我们做下人的不好打听，只管按照吩咐做事。”
[EN] Xilan replied, "Servants don't dare guess His Highness's mind—we just carry out orders."
[ZH] 千里寻开始犹豫，文渊与苗知瑜会到场，她还没有做好面对他们的心理准备。
[EN] Hesitation crept into Qianli Xun: Wenyuan and Miao Zhiyu would both be there, and she was not sure her heart was armored enough to face them yet.
[ZH] 可仪王与郡主是贵人，盛情邀请不好拒绝，郡主又是她的闺中密友，多日不见，甚是想念。
[EN] Still, turning down an invitation from royalty was rude—and she truly missed her close friend Princess Jiahe.
[ZH] “辛苦回禀郡主，我会按时参加。”
[EN] "Thank Princess for me—tell her I'll be there on time."
[ZH] 喜兰拜别而去。
[EN] Xilan bowed out.
[ZH] 苦荞猜出小姐的心思，小心说：“小姐若不想去，拒绝便可，仪王与佳和郡主都是明事理的人，绝不会因为小姐拒绝，对您有意见。”
[EN] Reading her mistress's face, Kuqiao murmured gentle: "If Miss doesn't feel up to going, just decline—Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe are reasonable and won't hold it against you."
[ZH] “迟早要面对不愿见又怕见的人，既然仪王为我提供面对的机会，不妨去一趟。”
[EN] "Sooner or later I have to face the people I dread seeing. Since Prince Yi set the stage, might as well go."
[ZH] 苦荞不再说什么，继续研磨：“小姐写的故事是不是该结尾了？”
[EN] Dropping the subject, Kuqiao went back to grinding ink: "Is Miss's story hitting its finale today?"
[ZH] 千里寻提起笔，边写边说：“没错，今日结尾。”
[EN] Dipping her brush, Qianli Xun smiled: "Yep—wrapping it up today."
[ZH] “说话艺人让我转达，希望您继续写新故事，老百姓都等着听呢。”
[EN] "The storyteller asked me to beg for a new story—crowds are hooked and waiting!"
[ZH] “你送书稿时，告诉说话艺人，你家小姐我肚子里的故事多着呢，这个故事写完，下个故事很快奉上。”
[EN] "When you drop off this script, tell him your mistress has plenty more stories up her sleeve—once this wraps, the next one's right behind it."
[ZH] “是我家公子。”苦荞笑着强调道。
[EN] "Your Young Master," Kuqiao corrected with a grin.
[ZH] “对，是公子。看我这脑子，千万要注意，不能暴露。”
[EN] "Right, right—Young Master! Look at my slip-up—gotta stay careful not to blow cover!"
[ZH] 夏果从外面进来：“小姐，化雨前来传话，说东方公子明日要去幽林别居参加诗会，问您去不去？”
[EN] Just then Xiaguo stepped in: "Miss, Huayu's outside passing word that Young Master Dongfang is going to Youlin Retreat's poetry salon tomorrow and asks if you're going."
[ZH] 刚说过有诗会叫上自己，东方尘泽就来邀请，实在够朋友。
[EN] Just asked him to include her in salons and he checked in right away—true friend!
[ZH] 千里寻停下笔：“让化雨转达东方公子，仪王和郡主也邀请我，明日必然去。”
[EN] Pausing her brush, Qianli Xun said, "Tell Huayu to let Young Master Dongfang know Prince Yi and Princess invited me too, so I'll be there for sure."
[ZH] 夏果回声“是”，并未立刻离去。
[EN] Xiaguo answered "Yes," yet stayed planted by the door.
[ZH] 苦荞问道：“你为什么不去回话？”
[EN] Kuqiao frowned: "Why aren't you going out to answer him?"
[ZH] 夏果犹豫一会儿，大胆问：“小姐，乔公子可去？”
[EN] Hesitating a beat, Xiaguo worked up her nerve: "Miss... is Young Master Qiao going?"
[ZH] 苦荞批评道：“夏果！你怎能问小姐这个问题？”
[EN] Kuqiao snapped, "Xiaguo! How dare you ask Miss that?!"
[ZH] 千里寻又停下笔，并未抬头：“文渊也被邀请，你想去明日跟着。”
[EN] Pausing her brush without looking up, Qianli Xun answered cool: "Wenyuan was invited too. Tag along tomorrow if you want."
[ZH] “谢谢小姐！”夏果这才出去给化雨回话。
[EN] "Thank you, Miss!" Beaming, Xiaguo hurried out to answer Huayu.
[ZH] 千里寻继续写书稿，仿佛未发生任何事一般。
[EN] Qianli Xun resumed writing as if nothing had ruffled her.
[ZH] 苦荞很不满：“小姐，您为何要让夏果跟着？她去了，只怕又给乔公子跪下，求着做人家的妾室，给小姐丢脸。”
[EN] Kuqiao muttered in irritation: "Why let Xiaguo come along, Miss?! She'll just drop to her knees begging Young Master Qiao to make her a concubine again and embarrass you!"
[ZH] “每个人都有追求幸福的权利，只要夏果觉得高兴，我又何必计较？”
[EN] "Everyone has the right to chase happiness. If it makes Xiaguo happy, why fuss over it?"
[ZH] “我家小姐人就是好，只盼夏果不要再执迷不悟。”
[EN] "Miss is way too good-hearted—just hope Xiaguo snaps out of her delusions."
[ZH] 墨已研好，苦荞见小姐写得专心，不便打扰，坐在茶桌边开始做茶。
[EN] Finished grinding ink and seeing her mistress deep in focus, Kuqiao moved to the tea table in silence to whisk tea.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 第二天，天气晴朗，是入冬以来少有的晴朗之日。
[EN] Next morning dawned bright and clear—a rare crisp blue sky in dead winter.
[ZH] 冬季太阳的温度没有多么强烈，可只要它挂在天上，将光辉撒到人间，看到的人内心也不由自主暖洋洋的。
[EN] Weak though winter sunlight felt, just seeing golden rays spill across the earth warmed every soul who stepped outside.
[ZH] 时间一点一点疗愈着内心的伤痛，今日的阳光又给予充足的能量，千里寻觉得自己有足够的勇气面对文渊和苗知瑜。
[EN] Time had been stitching her heart back together bit by bit, and today's bright sun poured in fresh courage—Qianli Xun felt ready to face Wenyuan and Miao Zhiyu head-on.
[ZH] 马车向幽林别居进发，好些时日没来到街上。千里寻掀开马车的窗帘，向外瞧去，街上依旧热闹。
[EN] Rolling out toward Youlin Retreat, she lifted the carriage curtain to take in the bustling avenue after weeks indoors.
[ZH] 与往日不同的是，春节的气息越来越浓，街旁有些人家和商铺已贴上春联，时不时传来小孩子们放爆竹的声音。
[EN] New Year spirit already spiced the air: red spring couplets adorned shops and gates, while bursts of children's firecrackers popped down side lanes.
[ZH] 又要过年，千里寻对过年没有任何期待。过一年会长一岁，快二十一了，在这个时代的确是老姑娘。
[EN] Another New Year coming—zero excitement for Qianli Xun. Turning close to twenty-one made her a certified old maid in the Song dynasty!
[ZH] 千里寻觉得无论在哪个时代，她都摆脱不了成为老姑娘的事实。她突然感到高兴，是否说明从穿越而来那天，她已走上千里寻的命运之路，只是她不知道而已。
[EN] Funny—no matter which century she lived in, she could not shake the "old maid" tag! A wry spark of cheer hit her: maybe from day one of crossing over, she had been walking Qianli Xun's own destiny without realizing it.
[ZH] 也许不是，也许与文渊的感情才是命运的转折点，要不从不涉足爱情的她，怎么穿越而来突然想谈恋爱，爱情失败后，又回归到穿越前的心理。
[EN] Or maybe meeting Wenyuan was the real turning point—why else had a career woman sworn off romance fallen head over heels, only to rebound right back to her old independent self once love crashed?
[ZH] 千里寻越想越觉得是这样，她在心里默默告诉自己：“既如此，在这个时代好好过属于千里寻的日子。”
[EN] Convinced that made total sense, she promised inside: "Fine—then I'll live Qianli Xun's best life right here in the Song!"
[ZH] 想起与陈家定亲一事，又在心中问道：“难道这段亲事也是我必须经历的？”该如何解决，她毫无头绪，只好走一步看一步。
[EN] Then the Chen betrothal poked back into mind: "Wait—is that part of my fate too?" With zero clue how to untangle it, she would take it one step at a time.
[ZH] 又想起仪王，他无缘无故举办诗会，还把与她有尴尬关系的人请来，不知葫芦里卖的是什么药？此人不仅花心会撩妹，还是个极有心机之人，千里寻叮嘱自己要小心提防，不要跳进仪王挖好的大坑内，被他彻底俘获。
[EN] And Prince Yi—throwing a random poetry salon stuffing every awkward romantic rival under one roof! What game was that slick, scheming heartbreaker playing? Locking her shields tight, she swore not to fall into whatever trap he had dug.
[ZH] 到达幽林别居时，千里寻才发现她是最后一个到达，本以为出来得挺早，没想到其他人比她还早。
[EN] Arriving at Youlin Retreat, Qianli Xun discovered she was dead last—thinking she had left plenty early, yet everyone else had beaten her!
[ZH] 诗会选在乔文仙曾住过的院子，因院外有木质高台，诗会之余，众人可登高远望。
[EN] The salon was set in Qiao Wenxian's old courtyard, complete with its outdoor wooden viewing platform for gazing over the winter woods between verses.
[ZH] 一大早起来，申简辰安排下人收拾院子和屋子。
[EN] Up at dawn, Shen Jianchen had ordered servants to scrub the courtyard and hall spotless.
[ZH] 打扫干净后，下人们又将屋子里的桌椅重新摆放一番，再放上三个火盆，把火烧旺，先将屋子烘暖和，客人到来之后不会觉得冷。
[EN] Tables and chairs stood arranged neat around three roaring braziers pre-heating the room so no guest felt a chill.
[ZH] 哥哥第一次在幽林别居举办诗会，乔梦容自然不能闲着，她提前写好菜谱差喜兰送到后厨，再安排丫鬟将果品、蜜饯、桂花酿，以及上好的茶叶准备齐全，再一一摆放在每个人的座位上。
[EN] For his very first salon at Youlin Retreat, Qiao Mengrong pitched in full tilt—writing out the kitchen menu for Xilan to deliver, and having maids lay fruit, candied preserves, osmanthus wine, and prime tea at every place setting.
[ZH] 一切齐备后，乔文仙第一个赶来，他不为别的，只为能尽快看到淑真。他的确比上次胖一些，精神状态也好许多，可内心的痛还在。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had arrived first of all—driven by one sole wish, to lay eyes on Shuzhen a minute sooner. He had put a bit of flesh back on and looked steadier, yet the wound still bled inside.
[ZH] 雪天在神柳下与淑真匆匆分手，他难以释怀，总觉得还需与淑真说些什么，才能彻底死心。
[EN] That hasty breakup in the snow beneath the willow left too much unsaid; he needed one more word with Shuzhen before his heart could truly die.
[ZH] 听说今日诗会她要来，他早早起床，衣裳换了好几身，最后才换上与淑真第一次在西湖约会时穿的衣裳。他认为那时两人相爱，这身衣裳多多少少沾些喜气，必然能带来好运。
[EN] Hearing she was coming today, he had woken at dawn and tried on half his wardrobe before choosing the exact robe from their very first West Lake date—hoping a shred of that old magic lingered in its silk.
[ZH] 只是这身衣裳是夏季的长衫，单薄许多。他只能给里面穿厚些，再将长衫套在最外面。
[EN] Because it was a thin summer gown, he had layered heavy winter clothes underneath and worn the summer robe right on top.
[ZH] 申简辰先陪文渊坐一会儿，瞧他心神不安的样子，便知还未放下幽栖居士。
[EN] Shen Jianchen sat with Wenyuan first, reading in his restless fidgeting that he still had not let Youqi Jushi go.
[ZH] 接着苗知瑜到来，自从与乔家订亲，她还未见过乔公子。今日诗会她也充满期待，期待乔文仙对待她不同往日。
[EN] Next came Miao Zhiyu—her first time seeing Young Master Qiao since their betrothal, hoping he might treat her warmer now.
[ZH] 当苗知瑜带着丫鬟走进屋拜见仪王时，乔文仙甚觉尴尬。直到苗知瑜很有礼貌地问候声：“乔公子。”他才投以微笑，并未开口说话。
[EN] As Miao Zhiyu walked in with her maid to bow to Prince Yi, awkwardness froze Qiao Wenxian; only when she greeted him with polite grace—"Young Master Qiao"—did he offer a silent smile without speaking.
[ZH] 苗知瑜专门挑乔文仙身旁的座位坐了。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu chose the seat right beside him on purpose.
[ZH] 申简辰瞧到，甚觉好笑，今儿这场诗会应该比较热闹。
[EN] Shen Jianchen hid a smirk—today's salon was going to be pure fireworks!
[ZH] 他并非看热闹之人，不过是想多见见千里寻而已，再借此机会窥探陈浩宇和文渊对千里寻的态度。
[EN] Not that he was here for cheap drama: he just wanted another hour near Qianli Xun, and a chance to size up Chen Haoyu's and Wenyuan's real feelings for her.
[ZH] 当然更重要的是，他要找机会单独跟千里寻说话，告诉她，他不是仪王赵朴，而是申简辰。
[EN] More important still, he meant to catch Qianli Xun alone and reveal he was not Prince Yi Zhao Pu—he was Shen Jianchen!
[ZH] 希望千里寻知道他是申简辰后，因共同穿越而来的情谊愿意同他在一起。
[EN] Once she knew he was Shen Jianchen, maybe their shared journey across time would move her heart toward him.
[ZH] 陈浩宇第三个到来，他想不通仪王举行诗会为何邀请他？要知道他对诗词一窍不通。送请帖的人正是揍他的白风，他不敢多问，满口应下。
[EN] Third through the door came Chen Haoyu—baffled why Prince Yi invited a poetry illiterate! Since Bai Feng—the exact guy who pounded him flat—delivered the card, he dared not ask and said yes fast.
[ZH] 陈浩宇是万万不敢得罪仪王，为留个好印象，自然不敢迟到，带上青瓜尽快赶来，被下人带进屋，拜见过仪王，选在乔文仙对面最下位的桌子坐了。
[EN] Terrified of crossing Prince Yi again, he showed up super early with Qinggua, bowed low to Prince Yi, and tucked himself into the lowest seat right across from Qiao Wenxian.
[ZH] 他有自知之明，他不懂诗词，还是躲着些好。坐下后，自然瞧到对面的乔文仙和苗知瑜，主动打招呼：“二位成亲时可要告诉我，我定送上一份大礼。”
[EN] Knowing his own limits, keeping low beat making a fool of himself over poetry. Spotting Qiao Wenxian and Miao Zhiyu opposite him, he tossed out a cheeky greeting: "Send me an invite when you two tie the knot—I'll bring a massive gift!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜立刻红脸，不好搭话。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu flushed crimson, too shy to reply.
[ZH] 乔文仙心里念着淑真，更不愿搭理陈浩宇。
[EN] Mind stuck on Shuzhen, Qiao Wenxian ignored him cold.
[ZH] 申简辰实在想笑，不如趁此机会让文渊死心：“陈公子别光顾着说文渊，我听说你也快成亲，不知日子定在什么时候？”
[EN] Holding back a laugh, Shen Jianchen decided to deal Wenyuan a reality check right now: "Quit teasing Wenyuan, Young Master Chen—heard you're getting married soon too! When's the big day?"
[ZH] 仪王问话，陈浩宇不好不回答：“这门亲事，我很不满意，能不能成还不得而知。”
[EN] Unable to dodge Prince Yi's question, Chen Haoyu answered honest: "Hate this match—whether it ever happens is anyone's guess."
[ZH] 申简辰对陈浩宇的回答很满意，他不希望有人与他一样整日惦记他心爱的女子：“看来陈公子的确对这门亲事不满意。”
[EN] Pleased—the last thing Shen Jianchen wanted was another man pining over Qianli Xun—he remarked, "Sounds like Young Master Chen really dislikes this betrothal."
[ZH] “唉，我与乔文仙都是可怜人，他满意的人我可看不上，仪王您说说，为何我们都不能如愿以偿？”
[EN] "Sigh—Qiao Wenxian and I are two poor saps! The one he wants, I wouldn't touch with a pole—tell me, Prince Yi, why does neither of us get what we want?"
[ZH] 还没等仪王回答陈浩宇的问题，乔文仙感觉不对劲：“陈浩宇，你刚才的话什么意思？什么是我满意的人你看不上？”
[EN] Before Prince Yi could reply, alarm snapped Qiao Wenxian upright: "What did you just say, Chen Haoyu?! What 'one I want' won't you touch?!"
[ZH] “看来你还不知道，不妨告诉你，朱家和陈家已订亲，我的成亲对象就是你喜欢的朱淑真，我可是一点儿都不愿意娶她。你说你，怎么那么没出息，要是你把她娶了，就没有与我订亲这回事。”
[EN] "Whoa, didn't you hear? Let me fill you in: the Zhus and Chens sealed a betrothal—my bride-to-be is your precious Zhu Shuzhen! And I want zero part of marrying her! Honestly, how spineless are you—if you'd married her, I wouldn't be stuck with this betrothal!"
[ZH] 乔文仙想起身把陈浩宇打一顿，凭什么他心心念念之人要嫁给他？转念一想，此事怎能怪他，怪只怪天意弄人。他已无话可说，只顾沉默。
[EN] Fury surged in Qiao Wenxian to jump across the table and smash Chen Haoyu's face—why on earth was his beloved Shuzhen promised to this clown?! Then helplessness dragged him back down; blame cruel fate, not him. Clamping his jaw, he sank into dead silence.
[ZH] 苗知瑜自然欢喜，幽栖居士也有了下家，她嫁给乔公子便不会觉得太亏欠她，终于忍不住开口说话：“陈家与朱家联姻，实属亲上加亲，陈公子能娶到幽栖居士也是几世修来的福气。”
[EN] Quiet joy warmed Miao Zhiyu—now that Youqi Jushi had a new match, marrying Young Master Qiao carried less guilt! Speaking up smooth: "Linking the Chen and Zhu houses doubles a fine bond—marrying Youqi Jushi is a rare blessing for Young Master Chen."
[ZH] 陈浩宇很不屑：“苗姑娘即将嫁给心仪之人，哪能体会到我的痛处？我不仅成了林觉的接盘侠，以后还有可能被朱淑真家暴，你说我惨不惨？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu snorted: "Easy for Miss Miao to say, marrying your dream man! How could you feel my pain? Not only am I Lin Jue's fallback guy, I'm signing up for daily wife-beatings from Zhu Shuzhen—isn't my life tragic?!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜不便再接话，继续端坐着，时不时用余光观察乔文仙，他依旧沉默。开春后，他们就要成亲，苗知瑜充满期待。
[EN] With nothing polite left to say, Miao Zhiyu fell quiet, casting sidelong glances at silent Qiao Wenxian, her heart fluttering toward their spring wedding.
[ZH] 陈浩宇的话显然不尊重千里寻，申简辰很不满，提醒道：“怪只怪流言，不早不晚传播开来，搞得所有人不开心，陈公子觉得呢？”
[EN] Irritated at Chen Haoyu's disrespect toward Qianli Xun, Shen Jianchen dropped a pointed barb: "Blame those rumors breaking out right on cue to ruin everyone's happiness—don't you agree, Young Master Chen?"
[ZH] 仪王的提醒充满批评，陈浩宇被噎得无话可说，露出尴尬的笑容，他深切体会到什么是因果报应。
[EN] Feeling the sting of Prince Yi's warning, Chen Haoyu froze with a sheepish grin, tasting sweet karma straight in his teeth.
[ZH] 还好三位公子来到，省得被人注意他一脸尴尬。
[EN] Saved by the bell—the three young masters walked in right on time, pulling eyes off his red face!
[ZH] 三位公子在城外碰到，便一起前来，他们也不敢迟到，也不敢踩着点儿来。被仪王邀请参加诗会何等荣幸，定要早到，给足仪王面子。
[EN] Bumping into each other outside town, all three had ridden in together—arriving extra early to show utmost respect for Prince Yi's invitation.
[ZH] 刚走进来，发现有三位已坐在位置上，仪王也端在正位，才知道他们来得并不算早，立刻上前行礼：“拜见仪王。”
[EN] Seeing three guests already seated alongside Prince Yi in the host's chair, they hurried forward bowing: "Greetings, Prince Yi!"
[ZH] 申简辰点头回礼：“三位随便坐，今日座位不分尊卑。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded warm: "Sit wherever you like—no strict seating rank today."
[ZH] 东方尘泽选择陈浩宇旁边的位置，曹班和安明轩则选择苗知瑜身旁的两个位置。
[EN] Dongfang Chenze slid into the seat beside Chen Haoyu, while Cao Ban and An Mingxuan took the two seats next to Miao Zhiyu.
[ZH] 化雨站在自家公子身后，对乔公子身后的南双挤挤眼，南双悄悄向他挥挥手，算是打过招呼。
[EN] Standing behind his master, Huayu winked at Nan Shuang behind Young Master Qiao, who gave a subtle wave back.
[ZH] 化雨很自然瞅到陈浩宇，想不通仪王为何会邀请他？再瞅瞅他身后的青瓜，觉得这个跟班一点儿书卷气都没有，看上去一肚子草包，不由心生不屑。
[EN] Noticing Chen Haoyu, Huayu puzzled why a rake was invited to a poetry salon—then sized up Qinggua behind him, spotting zero scholarly polish and pegging him a total blockhead with quiet disdain.
[ZH] 东方尘泽坐好后，带头说：“仪王，我们三人本想早来，没想到有人比我们还早，倒显得我们三个不积极，还望仪王不要见怪。”
[EN] Once seated, Dongfang Chenze spoke for the trio: "We tried arriving early, Prince Yi—didn't expect half the room to beat us! Forgive us if we looked slack."
[ZH] “东方公子多虑，你们来得不早不晚，刚刚合适，我为何要怪？再说还有人未到，我们这些来早的人且耐心等待。”
[EN] "Nonsense, Young Master Dongfang—right on time, neither early nor late! Others haven't even arrived yet, so let's wait in comfort."
[ZH] 仪王说的定是佳和郡主和幽栖居士，佳和郡主就在幽林别居，想过来也是分分钟的事，唯独幽栖居士，千万不要迟到。
[EN] Meaning Princess Jiahe and Youqi Jushi—Princess lived right here and could pop over in a breath, so everyone hoped Youqi Jushi would not show up too late.
[ZH] 三位公子分别与其他人打过招呼后，对陈浩宇的到来也深感疑惑，当着仪王的面不好说什么，大家保持沉默等待即将到来之人。
[EN] After trading nods around the hall—all three privately baffled at Chen Haoyu's presence yet mute before royalty—everyone sat waiting in hushed anticipation.

#CH 150
[ZH] 有丫鬟进来，为来客摆上果品和茶水，又为每个人斟茶，然后悄声退下。
[EN] Maids filed in setting out fresh fruit and tea, pouring a cup for every guest before withdrawing in silence.
[ZH] 大家刚品几口茶，佳和郡主带着喜兰前来，众人见礼后，乔梦容说：“哥哥，后厨的酒菜已就绪，是先用饭？还是先品诗词？”
[EN] Barely had everyone taken a sip when Princess Jiahe arrived with Xilan. Once guests finished greeting her, Qiao Mengrong asked, "Brother, the kitchen's feast is ready—dine first, or appraise poetry first?"
[ZH] 申简辰举行诗会目的本不在诗会，故而说道：“已到饭点儿，等人到齐，先用饭，边用饭边欣赏歌舞，用过饭后，再听书，最后品鉴诗词。”
[EN] Never caring a whit about poetry in the first place, Shen Jianchen announced, "It's mealtime already. Once everyone's here, we eat first with music and dance, then listen to storytelling, and save poetry appraisal for last."
[ZH] 除陈浩宇，在场其他人难以理解，不是诗会吗？怎么品诗词成了附带？面对仪王，大家不敢说什么，只能任由安排。
[EN] Every guest except Chen Haoyu blinked in disbelief—a poetry salon where poetry got shoved to the dessert menu?! Before Prince Yi, nobody dared peep, nodding along.
[ZH] 陈浩宇总算明白，仪王为何请他来，原来品诗词不过是最后一项，吃过饭，再听听说书，到了品鉴诗词的环节，随便找个理由向仪王辞别，也省得在众人面前显得无所事事。
[EN] Chen Haoyu lit up—now he got why Prince Yi invited him! Feast first, listen to storytelling, then cook up an excuse to duck out before poetry started—zero awkwardness!
[ZH] “好的，我这就安排。”乔梦容对喜兰说，“告诉后厨，人到齐后，立刻上酒菜。”
[EN] "Right away!" Qiao Mengrong instructed Xilan: "Tell the kitchen to bring out wine and dishes the second everyone is seated."
[ZH] 喜兰领命而去。
[EN] Xilan hurried off.
[ZH] 乔梦容很自然地在乔文仙对面的桌子坐下，扫视一遍在座的人，只有淑真还未到。
[EN] Taking the table straight across from Qiao Wenxian, Qiao Mengrong scanned the hall—only Shuzhen remained.
[ZH] 今日大家到得极早，就连哥哥到的也早。哥哥是身份尊贵的王爷，本应最后一个到，可乔公子一来，他便陪着乔公子坐在这里等待众人。
[EN] Everyone had turned up super early today—even Prince Yi himself! By rights a royal host arrived dead last, yet he had been sitting right here waiting since Young Master Qiao stepped in.
[ZH] 若不是因为乔公子，梦容绝对会误会哥哥想要早些见到某个参加诗会之人。
[EN] If not for Young Master Qiao keeping him company, Qiao Mengrong might almost have wondered if he was eager to see another guest.
[ZH] 正想着，淑真携两个丫鬟进来……
[EN] Right on cue, Shuzhen walked through the doorway with both maids trailing behind...
[ZH] 众人向幽栖居士瞧去。
[EN] Every pair of eyes swiveled toward Youqi Jushi.
[ZH] 乔梦容觉得淑真的气色比上次见时好许多，她应该已从伤痛中走出。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong smiled seeing bright color back in Shuzhen's cheeks—she had stepped out of the worst of her heartbreak.
[ZH] 她不由观察乔公子，在淑真进来那一刻，他的双眼依然放光，只是那光芒中带有些许失落。
[EN] Glancing across at Young Master Qiao, she saw his eyes flare bright at Shuzhen's entrance, shadowed by raw sorrow.
[ZH] 再观察苗知瑜，她瞧过淑真后，扭头看了一眼身旁的乔公子，然后低下头，拿起茶盏，装作喝茶的样子。
[EN] Glancing next at Miao Zhiyu, she watched her look from Shuzhen to Qiao Wenxian beside her, then lower her eyes and lift her tea bowl to hide behind a sip.
[ZH] 哥哥举办诗会，乔梦容开心，可想不通，哥哥为何要把有感情纠葛的人都叫到一起，难道是为看热闹？可哥哥从来不是喜欢看热闹的人，哪怕是失忆后的哥哥也并非是对热闹感兴趣之人。
[EN] Happy as Qiao Mengrong was about the salon, she could not fathom why Prince Yi had packed every tangled love rival into one room—he had never cared for cheap gossip, before amnesia or after.
[ZH] 乔梦容只顾关注他人，却没有认真观察他的哥哥。幽栖居士走进时，她的哥哥双眼中的光芒并不比乔文仙差。
[EN] So busy reading everyone else's faces, she missed her own fiancé's eyes—shining no less ardent than Qiao Wenxian's the instant Youqi Jushi walked in!
[ZH] 直到千里寻行礼问候：“仪王，佳和郡主，淑真这厢有礼。”
[EN] At last Qianli Xun stepped forward and bowed in greeting: "Greetings, Prince Yi, Princess Jiahe."
[ZH] 申简辰才担心大家看出他的心思，不得已收回眼神。
[EN] Afraid the whole room might read his heart, Shen Jianchen forced himself to pull his gaze away.
[ZH] “幽栖居士最后一个来，比本王架子还大，今日既然是诗会，就罚幽栖居士现场做首词供大家鉴赏。”申简辰故意批评，只是想多与心爱的姑娘说句话而已
[EN] "Dead last to arrive, Youqi Jushi—bigger airs than a royal prince! Since this is a poetry salon, your penalty is composing a fresh ci on the spot for everyone!" Shen Jianchen teased harsh on purpose just to trade an extra sentence with her.
[ZH] “淑真虽是最后一个到达，并未迟到，王爷不该罚我。既然是诗会，我作首词也是应该，不必王爷以此作为惩罚的借口。”
[EN] "Last to arrive doesn't mean late, Prince Yi—no grounds to penalize me! Still, at a poetry salon composing a ci is only proper; no need to dress it up as punishment."
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“仪王愿意早到，跟老娘有什么关系！其他人看你是王爷的份上，给你面子，早早来到，才显得我有些晚。你却抓住这点当众说我，实在过分！”
[EN] Qianli Xun fumed inside: "Not my fault you camped out at dawn! Everyone else kissed up to a prince showing up early—now you pick on me in public?!"
[ZH] 朱淑真被仪王批评，陈浩宇很开心，也佩服她竟然敢怼仪王。陈浩宇更觉得这个女子娶不得，一旦娶回家，天天被她怼，多没面子。
[EN] Watching Prince Yi needle Zhu Shuzhen delighted Chen Haoyu, though he had to admire her nerve talking back to royalty—making him even surer she was poison to marry! Imagine getting roasted like that by your own wife every day!
[ZH] 令陈浩宇没想到的是，仪王竟然一点儿不生气，让朱淑真挑位置坐下，还说很期待她的词作，跟那天打他时完全不一样。
[EN] To Chen Haoyu's shock, Prince Yi did not flare up one bit—inviting Zhu Shuzhen to sit and beaming that he looked forward to her ci! Night and day from the prince who had him beaten black and blue!
[ZH] 仪王既然吩咐坐下，千里寻自然归座，只剩下东方尘泽与梦容之间的座位，她没得挑，乖乖坐在那里。
[EN] Directed to take a seat, Qianli Xun found only one spot left—sandwiched between Dongfang Chenze and Mengrong—and settled in.
[ZH] 坐下时与梦容相视一笑，扭过头，自然看到代替她即将嫁给文渊的苗知瑜，千里寻以微笑与苗知瑜打招呼。
[EN] Trading a warm smile with Mengrong, she glanced across to see Miao Zhiyu, the bride taking her place beside Wenyuan, and offered a courteous smile.
[ZH] 千里寻觉得此时的微笑与刚才面对梦容的微笑不同，她尽量让自己显得并不介意、并不尴尬。
[EN] Very different from the smile she gave Mengrong—polished to look effortless, unbothered, free of awkwardness.
[ZH] 苗知瑜也以微笑回应，她看上去很开心，成功得到文渊，又怎能不开心。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu returned a bright smile—winning Wenyuan had her glowing!
[ZH] 还是无意中接触到不愿接触又难免要接触的目光，文渊直愣愣地盯着她，眼神中充满痛苦和不舍。
[EN] Then came the gaze Qianli Xun dreaded yet could not dodge: Wenyuan staring straight through her, eyes drenched in agony and longing.
[ZH] 千里寻不敢看下去，她怕自己会旧情复燃，赶紧低下头，盯着桌面的果品。
[EN] Afraid looking one second longer would reignite old ashes, Qianli Xun dropped her eyes straight onto her plate of fruit.
[ZH] 乔梦容担心大家尴尬，说道：“哥哥，人已到齐，该摆饭了。”
[EN] Sensing tension thickening the room, Qiao Mengrong nudged, "Everyone's here, Brother—time to serve lunch."
[ZH] 仪王竟然愣住心神，根本没听到梦容说话。
[EN] Lost in a daze, Prince Yi did not even hear her.
[ZH] 梦容从哥哥的眼神判断，他看向淑真。难道哥哥是帮乔公子观察淑真的心意？乔梦容大声唤道：“哥哥！”
[EN] Following his locked gaze straight to Shuzhen—assuming he was gauging Shuzhen's mood on Young Master Qiao's behalf—Qiao Mengrong called louder: "Brother!"
[ZH] 申简辰才大梦初醒，将目光从千里寻身上挪开：“哦，什么事？”
[EN] Snapping awake, Shen Jianchen tore his eyes off Qianli Xun: "Oh—what is it?"
[ZH] 乔梦容强调道：“人已到齐。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong repeated: "Everyone is here."
[ZH] 申简辰说：“开饭吧。”
[EN] "Serve the feast, then!"
[ZH] 早有下人在门外听候，清楚地听到仪王的传令，迅速跑去后厨。没多久，丫鬟们开始在每位客人面前摆上酒菜。
[EN] Servants stationed outside heard the command and sprinted to the kitchen; moments later maids streamed in laying wine and dishes before every guest.
[ZH] 摆酒菜的过程，夏果在小姐身后一直观察乔公子，他胖了些，可瞧那样子，根本未放下小姐。
[EN] Standing behind her mistress during the service, Xiaguo kept her eyes glued to Young Master Qiao—he had gained a little weight, yet one look told her he still had not let Miss go.
[ZH] 南双见夏果一直盯着自家公子，没看他一眼，心中着实不满。
[EN] Watching Xiaguo stare nonstop at his master without sparing him half a glance burned Nan Shuang raw.
[ZH] 苦荞瞅瞅夏果，果然一进屋，眼中只有乔公子。她很不满，觉得夏果是丫鬟出身，能跟南双已不错，竟然把心思放在乔公子身上，实在不该。
[EN] Glancing over at Xiaguo, Kuqiao frowned too—sure enough, eyes glued straight on Young Master Qiao! Born a maid, Nan Shuang was already a fine match; chasing Young Master Qiao was sheer folly.
[ZH] 她就不会有不符身份的想法，安心喜欢白风。想到白风，不由向仪王身旁瞧去，白风也看向她。
[EN] Not like Kuqiao, happy with her own match—glancing toward Prince Yi's elbow, she caught Bai Feng looking right back at her!
[ZH] 三位公子面对尴尬的气氛，选择沉默不语。既然前来，主人怎么安排，就怎么享受，他们决定到品鉴诗词环节再开口不迟。
[EN] Sensing the electric awkwardness humming across the hall, the three young masters stayed mute, figuring they would enjoy the feast and wait until poetry time to speak up.
[ZH] 美食摆好后，申简辰对众人说：“大家随便用，不必拘束。”然后向一旁的白风示意。
[EN] Once every dish stood set, Shen Jianchen invited: "Dig in, everyone—no standing on ceremony!" Then he nodded toward Bai Feng.
[ZH] 白风明白王爷的意思，拍了拍手掌，立刻从两边走来舞伎，舞乐响起，舞伎们开始扭动腰肢跳起曼妙的舞姿。
[EN] Clapping his hands, Bai Feng signaled dancers to glide out from both wings as music struck up, slender waists swaying in graceful rhythm.
[ZH] 千里寻心想，仪王该不会不懂什么叫诗会？连个诗词的影子都没见到，又是美食，又是舞蹈，这不就是举行宴会吗？又何必以诗会的名头邀请大家前来？
[EN] Qianli Xun wondered if Prince Yi even knew what a poetry salon was—zero poetry, nonstop food and dancing! Why not just call it a banquet?!
[ZH] 吃了一会儿，欣赏一会儿舞蹈，千里寻不想再面对文渊的眼神，决定出去走走，透透气，她悄声对一旁的梦容说：“我出去方便一下。”
[EN] After eating a spell and watching a dance, eager to escape Wenyuan's burning stare for fresh air, Qianli Xun leaned close to Mengrong: "Stepping out to freshen up."
[ZH] 乔梦容小声说：“快去快回。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong whispered back: "Don't take long."
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，起身往出走，苦荞自然跟上，拿上小姐的披风为她披好。夏果不愿离去，谁让乔公子就坐在对面。千里寻并不管她，带着苦荞从后门出去。
[EN] Nodding, Qianli Xun rose and headed out, Kuqiao hurrying right behind to drape her winter cape over her shoulders, while Xiaguo stayed planted—no way she was walking away from Young Master Qiao! Leaving Xiaguo behind, Qianli Xun slipped out the rear door with Kuqiao.
[ZH] 来到外面，呼吸到新鲜空气，心情舒畅许多。冬季，幽林别居的树木变得光秃秃，萧条的景色中倒有几分别致。
[EN] Stepping into crisp outdoor air cleared the heaviness from her lungs. Bare winter branches stretched stark over Youlin Retreat, lending bleak charm to the grounds.
[ZH] 光秃的树木无法阻挡冬日的阳光，时不时投射在千里寻的身上，她觉得冬日阳光带来的温暖比屋内火盆的暖意更加自然清新。
[EN] With leaves stripped away, pale winter sunlight poured down onto her shoulders, cleaner and gentler than indoor braziers.
[ZH] 她顺着小径，慢慢散步，享受冬日的光辉。走着走着，几株正在开放的梅花映入眼帘。幽林别居到处是树木，梅花倒成此处少有的景致。
[EN] Strolling unhurried along the garden path enjoying the sun, a cluster of blooming winter plum trees caught her eye—a rare splash of life amid the bare woods.
[ZH] 千里寻兴致萌生，上前欣赏起来，顺便将鼻子凑近，去嗅梅花清幽之香，不敢说沁人心脾，也令人头脑清晰。
[EN] Delighted, she walked closer and leaned in to breathe the crisp, cool plum fragrance, sharp enough to clear her mind.
[ZH] 她本以为已完全放下文渊，见到他，才发现完全放下没那么容易，可她必须装作已放下，不装又能如何？
[EN] She had fooled herself thinking she was over Wenyuan; one glimpse today proved letting go was not that simple—still, she had to keep acting over him. What else could she do?
[ZH] 肯定是时间太短，到明年春季，或者夏季，或者更长时间，她肯定能完全放下文渊。
[EN] Just needed more time—by spring, or summer, or a year down the line, she would get past him for good.
[ZH] 嗅完梅花的清香，千里寻继续行走在小径中。苦荞不愿打扰小姐，默默跟在身后……
[EN] Having drunk in the plum scent, she strolled further down the path while Kuqiao trailed quiet behind...
[ZH] 乔文仙见淑真出去，也起身往外走。申简辰分外紧张，尽力掩饰，装作轻松的样子享受美食和舞蹈。
[EN] Back indoors, watching Shuzhen step out, Qiao Wenxian rose and headed outside too. Tension gripped Shen Jianchen, though he forced an easy mask, keeping his eyes on the food and dancers.
[ZH] 陈浩宇在心中笑道：“你们两个最好旧情复燃，再闹出些事来，本公子就可以此为借口退婚。呵呵……”无意中瞥到失落的苗知瑜，举起酒杯，示意她干杯。
[EN] Chen Haoyu smirked inside: "Go on—rekindle that flame and cause a scandal so I get a free ticket out of this wedding! Heheh..." Catching sight of Miao Zhiyu's downcast face, he lifted his cup in a cheeky silent toast.
[ZH] 陈浩宇的动作，苗知瑜根本不理会，自顾忧虑。乔公子已与她有婚约，不仅见面一句话不说，还时刻用目光关照幽栖居士，见她出去，也跟着出去，令她苗知瑜情何以堪？
[EN] Ignoring Chen Haoyu's antics, Miao Zhiyu stewed in humiliation: engaged to her, yet Young Master Qiao had not spoken one word to her, kept staring at Youqi Jushi, and chased straight outside the second she left! Where did that leave her pride?!
[ZH] 三位公子也甚是担忧，又无可奈何，只好在心中祈祷，千万不要发生不该发生的事。
[EN] The three young masters traded anxious looks, praying no disaster broke out.
[ZH] 乔公子既然已离开，夏果没必要再站着，也走出去，尾随在乔公子身后，见周围没人，大胆唤道：“乔公子！”
[EN] The instant Young Master Qiao stepped out, Xiaguo saw no point standing indoors and slipped after him. Catching up where nobody else stood around, she called bold: "Young Master Qiao!"
[ZH] 乔文仙停下脚步，与南双转过身。南双问：“你唤我家公子干什么？”
[EN] Halting, Qiao Wenxian turned around with Nan Shuang, who snapped, "What do you want with my master?"
[ZH] 夏果并未理会南双，靠近后说：“乔公子已有婚约，不要再盯着我家小姐不放，再说我家小姐已将乔公子彻底放下，您就不要再给她制造麻烦。”
[EN] Ignoring Nan Shuang flat out, Xiaguo stepped close: "You're an engaged man now, Young Master Qiao—stop clinging to my mistress! Miss has already let you go for good; stop bringing her trouble!"
[ZH] “制造麻烦？是淑真让你对我这么说的？”乔文仙显得很痛苦。
[EN] "Bringing her trouble? Did Shuzhen tell you to say that?" Pain twisted Qiao Wenxian's face.
[ZH] 夏果强调道：“我天天跟着小姐，她心里怎么想，我尽知。乔公子的未婚妻也在，您偏偏追着我家小姐出来，苗姑娘会怎么想？您有没有考虑过？”
[EN] Xiaguo pressed firm: "I'm beside Miss every hour—I know every thought in her head! Your fiancée is sitting right inside while you chase after my mistress—did you spare one thought for how Miss Miao feels?!"
[ZH] “我管不了那么多，只想跟淑真说话。”乔文仙转身打算继续去追。
[EN] "I don't care about any of that—I just need to speak with Shuzhen!" Turning sharp, Qiao Wenxian started back down the path.
[ZH] 夏果快走几步，挡在面前：“乔公子，醒醒吧！您与我家小姐已不可能，放了她吧！放下，您轻松，我家小姐也轻松。”
[EN] Darting ahead to block him, Xiaguo cried, "Wake up, Young Master Qiao! You and Miss are impossible—let her go! Let go, and both you and Miss get peace!"
[ZH] “放下？哪有那么容易？你让开！”
[EN] "Let go? Think it's that easy?! Step aside!"
[ZH] 夏果没有办法，只能将婚约道出：“乔公子可能还不知，我家小姐已与陈公子订亲。”
[EN] Out of ammo, Xiaguo dropped her trump card: "Maybe Young Master Qiao hasn't heard—Miss is already betrothed to Young Master Chen!"
[ZH] “此事我已知，订亲又如何？我不也已订亲？”
[EN] "I already know! So what—aren't I betrothed too?!"
[ZH] 夏果见此法无用，瞎编道：“乔公子有所不知，我家小姐曾跟她的嫂嫂聊天时，说她已爱上陈公子，心甘情愿嫁到陈家……”
[EN] Seeing truth fail, Xiaguo spun a brazen lie: "What Young Master Qiao doesn't know is Miss told her sister-in-law she's already fallen in love with Young Master Chen and is marrying into the Chen house with all her heart..."
[ZH] 这个消息对乔文仙来讲如同晴空惊雷，他已顾不上去追淑真，原路返回，来到屋内。
[EN] A thunderbolt out of a clear sky! Forgetting all about chasing Shuzhen, Qiao Wenxian spun round and stormed straight back into the hall.
[ZH] 乐舞声掩盖住他的怒气，所有人并未注意到。直到他端起桌上的一盏茶，泼在陈浩宇的脸上，大家才惊讶不已。
[EN] Drowned out by music and dancing, nobody noticed his fury—until he snatched a tea bowl off the table and flung its contents straight into Chen Haoyu's face! Gasps froze the entire hall.
[ZH] 申简辰摆摆手，舞乐停下，舞伎们纷纷退出。
[EN] At a wave from Shen Jianchen, the music died and the dancers scurried out.
[ZH] 陈浩宇无缘无故被泼茶水，甚是生气：“乔文仙，你抽哪门子风？为何用茶水泼我？”
[EN] Drenched out of nowhere, Chen Haoyu exploded: "What the hell's gotten into you, Qiao Wenxian?! Splashing tea in my face?!"
[ZH] 乔文仙抓住陈浩宇的衣领：“谁让你跟淑真订亲？”
[EN] Seizing Chen Haoyu by the collar, Qiao Wenxian roared: "Who gave you the right to get betrothed to Shuzhen?!"
[ZH] 三位公子感到惊讶，幽栖居士竟然和纨绔子弟订婚，简直难以置信。
[EN] Shocked murmurs rippled through the three young masters—Youqi Jushi betrothed to a notorious rake?! Unthinkable!
[ZH] “奇怪？刚才你已知，也不见打我，怎么出去一趟这么生气？”陈浩宇疑惑的同时，被乔文仙气到，也想气气他，继续笑道，“你跟苗知瑜都有婚约，有什么权利管我家淑真？我与淑真是越打感情越深，见你们不成，赶紧求我爹上朱家提亲，没成想一提一个准儿，知府大人满口应下，还夸我是乘龙快婿。”
[EN] "Weird—knew about it ten minutes ago and didn't touch me, step outside two minutes and now you go feral?!" Furious himself and itching to twist the knife, Chen Haoyu sneered: "You're engaged to Miao Zhiyu—what right do YOU have meddling with my Shuzhen?! Fighting just made Shuzhen and me fall harder for each other! Seeing you two flop, I begged my dad to propose to the Zhus—and boom, bull's-eye! The Prefect jumped at it and called me the son-in-law of his dreams!"
[ZH] 申简辰在心中冷笑道：“纨绔子弟！满嘴谎言！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen sneered inside: "Shameless dandy—lying through his teeth!"
[ZH] 乔文仙无法忍受，举起拳头就要打下去，被陈浩宇用手挡住。乔文仙的力气不如陈浩宇，拳头自然难以落下。
[EN] Snapping past reason, Qiao Wenxian cocked a fist to swing, only for Chen Haoyu to catch his wrist cold—stronger by far, locking Qiao Wenxian's punch dead in mid-air.
[ZH] 申简辰看不下去，大声说：“文渊是我兄弟，请陈公子担待些。”
[EN] Unable to sit by, Shen Jianchen barked loud: "Wenyuan is my brother—show some restraint, Young Master Chen!"
[ZH] 仪王发话，陈浩宇不好再与乔文仙敌对下去，一把推开他：“看在仪王的面子上，我不跟你计较，记着，朱淑真以后是我的人，你不要再惦记，你的未婚妻在那里，你的做法会令她伤心。”
[EN] At Prince Yi's command, Chen Haoyu could not push further and shoved Qiao Wenxian back: "For Prince Yi's sake I'll let this slide. Just remember: Zhu Shuzhen belongs to me now, so keep your eyes off her! Your own fiancée is sitting right there—acting like this only hurts her!"
[ZH] 乔文仙趔趄了一下，被南双扶住。当着仪王的面不好再闹下去，他怔怔地站在原地，痛苦地品味着陈浩宇刚才说的“淑真以后是我的人，你不要再惦记”，早已忘记陈浩宇说的那些不满朱陈两家订亲的话，他忽然之间觉得整个世界都失去色彩……
[EN] Staggering back into Nan Shuang's arms, Qiao Wenxian could not brawl before Prince Yi. Frozen where he stood, Chen Haoyu's taunt—"Shuzhen belongs to me now, keep your eyes off her"—hammered through his skull, wiping out every earlier complaint Chen Haoyu had made about hating the match until the whole world drained gray...
[ZH] 苗知瑜站起来，走到乔文仙身边，对陈浩宇说：“陈公子此言差矣，乔公子的做法并不会伤到知瑜，相反我很理解。幽栖居士与乔公子的事知瑜从头到尾尽知，知瑜也希望他们能走到一起，怎奈乔家与苗家已订亲，知瑜也毫无办法。”
[EN] Rising from her chair, Miao Zhiyu stepped beside Qiao Wenxian and faced Chen Haoyu with calm poise: "Young Master Chen is mistaken. Young Master Qiao's feelings don't hurt me at all—quite the opposite, I understand entirely. I know every chapter of Youqi Jushi and Young Master Qiao's bond and wished they could end up together; only because our families sealed this betrothal were our hands tied."
[ZH] 苗知瑜一段至情至理的话，令在场所有人对她刮目相看，就连乔文仙也对她投去赞赏的目光，莫名的愧疚感油然而生。
[EN] Her generous, gracious speech drew admiring glances from every corner of the room—even Qiao Wenxian looked at her with fresh respect, a quiet wave of guilt stirring in his chest.
[ZH] 夏果心想，苗知瑜通情达理，定是个好相处的主母，若成为乔公子的妾室，以后的日子应该好过些。
[EN] Watching from the side, Xiaguo beamed inside: reasonable and gracious—Miao Zhiyu would make an easy primary wife to live under once Xiaguo became Young Master Qiao's concubine!
[ZH] 苗知瑜见已俘获众人之心，对乔文仙说：“乔公子还是坐下继续享用美食，我想幽栖居士应该是出去方便，一会儿便回，你若有话与她说，待她回来，再与她讲。”
[EN] Knowing she had won every heart in the hall, Miao Zhiyu turned warm toward Qiao Wenxian: "Sit back down and finish lunch, Young Master Qiao. Youqi Jushi only stepped out to freshen up and will be right back—whatever you need to tell her, tell her once she returns."
[ZH] 面对此种状况，乔文仙只能坐回位置，本来心中有千言万语想对淑真说，被苗知瑜这么一劝，他倒难以启齿。
[EN] Backed into grace by Miao Zhiyu, Qiao Wenxian sank back into his chair—a thousand words that had burned to burst out at Shuzhen now caught silent in his throat.
